1, 1
kailāsaśikharāsīnaṃ kālakandarpanāśanam |
prasannaṃ parameśānaṃ jagadānandakāraṇam || 1,1.1 ||
praṇamya parayā bhaktyā bhairavī stutimātanot |
devadeva mahādeva janmadāridryanāśana || 1,1.2 ||
prasīda karuṇāmūrte prasanna parameśvara |
divyāgamarahasyāni kulakaulādikāni ca || 1,1.3 ||
jāne tava prasādena māyāmaṅgalavigraha |
śrotumicchāmi sarveśa tava divyarasāyanam || 1,1.4 ||
jarājanmāmayaghnaṃ ca khecaratvādisiddhidam |
dāridryaduḥkhaśamanaṃ brahmatvādivarapradam || 1,1.5 ||
īśvara uvāca |
sādhu sādhu mahābhāge sarvalokopakāriṇi |
tatsarvaṃ jāyate sūtācchuddhātmaprāṇavallabhe || 1,1.6 ||
sarvalokopakārārthaṃ guhyātguhyatamaṃ hitam |
rasendrasya samutpattiṃ lakṣaṇaṃ ca surārcite || 1,1.7 ||
tatsarvaṃ sampravakṣyāmi śṛṇu bhairavi samprati |
<rasotpattiḥ>
surendrairmunibhirdivyaiḥ gandharvoragakiṃnaraiḥ || 1,1.8 ||
prārthito bhavatyāṃ tu kumārotpattimicchadbhiḥ |
tārakāsuranāśāya lokānāṃ rakṣaṇāya ca || 1,1.9 ||
tadā himālayagireḥ guhāyāṃ suciraṃ priye |
saṃkrīḍamānayoḥ kālo gato naḥ putralipsayā || 1,1.10 ||
trijagatkṣobhi surataṃ tyajituṃ preṣitaḥ suraiḥ |
kapotarūpamāsthāya vahniḥ prāgādgavākṣataḥ || 1,1.11 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā lajjayā devi visṛṣṭaṃ surataṃ mayā |
nikṣiptaṃ vadane vahnerāvayosteja ujjvalam || 1,1.12 ||
tena dandahyamāno'gnirgaṅgāyāṃ tannyamajjayat |
tena gaṅgāpi saṃtaptā tadbahirvisasarja ca || 1,1.13 ||
tatdvidhābhūdbahiḥ prāpya caikaṃ skandaprasūtaye |
anyacchuddharaso jāto hyāpadaṃbudhipāradaḥ || 1,1.14 ||
śatayojananimneṣu pañcakūpeṣu saṃsthitaḥ |
tanmalā dhātavo jātā maṇayo divyavastu ca || 1,1.15 ||
tattatkṣetraviśeṣeṇa nāmavarṇādikān guṇān |
bhinnaḥ prāpto rasendro'yaṃ janmadāridryabhañjanaḥ || 1,1.16 ||
te kūpāḥ pralaye pañca saṃjñā jātādi vai mukhāḥ |
sasaṃcavarṇāṃ nivṛttyā nikṣiptā niyutāni hi || 1,1.17 ||
pañcavarṇāni deveśi sarvasattvayutāni ca |
pūrvasyāṃ pāradaḥ śveto nānāvarṇairgadāpahaḥ || 1,1.18 ||
cañcalo dakṣiṇasyāṃ tu rasendro nīlavarṇavān |
doṣahīno'tirūkṣaśca sutarāṃ capalaḥ priye || 1,1.19 ||
tena jātā bhujaṅgendrā jarājanmagadoṣojjhitāḥ |
paścimasyāṃ tu sūtākhyaḥ pītavarṇo'tirūkṣakaḥ || 1,1.20 ||
sarvadoṣayuto'sau tu śuddho'ṣṭādaśakarmabhiḥ |
sarvasiddhiprado devi dehalohādisiddhidaḥ || 1,1.21 ||
uttarasyāṃ diśo raktaḥ sarvadoṣavivarjitaḥ |
rasāyanaṃ tu tenaiva devā janmajarojkṣitāḥ || 1,1.22 ||
madhye tu miśrako jñeyaḥ sarvavarṇasamanvitaḥ |
bahiścandrārkācchāyo raso doṣasamanvitaḥ || 1,1.23 ||
sa cāṣṭādaśasaṃskāraiḥ śuddhaḥ siddhiprado bhavet |
amarāṇāṃ svarūpaṃ tu rasendro hi maheśvaraḥ || 1,1.24 ||
pūrayāmāsatustau ca stokato hyatidurlabhau |
anyeṣu sarvakāryeṣu siddhado'pi hi karmabhiḥ || 1,1.25 ||
aṣṭādaśabhiratyantaṃ śuddhaḥ siddhiprado drutam |
rasāvatāraṃ yo vetti sa tu dhārmikasattamaḥ || 1,1.26 ||
āyuṣyasukhasaṃtānadhanārogyamavāpnuyāt |
<rasaparyāyāḥ tanniruktaista>
janmarogajarāmṛtyudāridryāṃbhonidheḥ param || 1,1.27 ||
pāraṃ dadāti tenaiva pāradaḥ parikīrtitaḥ |
rasoparasalohādikartṛtvācca rasendrakaḥ || 1,1.28 ||
mama pratyaṅgasūtatvātsūta ityabhidhīyate |
sūte yasmātsarvasiddhiṃ tasmātsūta iti smṛtaḥ || 1,1.29 ||
mama deharaso yasmātrasastasmātprakīrtitaḥ |
jarāmaraṇadāridryaroganāśāya śasyate || 1,1.30 ||
tasmāśrasa iti prokto dhātutvācca varānane |
sarvadhātūnrasatyeṣaḥ tasmācca rasa īritaḥ || 1,1.31 ||
mitrakārī savarṇatvātsarvasiddhipradāyakaḥ |
pārado vyādhisaṃhartā rasendro rasakarmaṇi || 1,1.32 ||
dhātukarmasu sūtaḥ syādrasendro rasasādhane |
sarvakarmārhatatayā sarvatayā priye || 1,1.33 ||
sarvasiddhiprado devi miśrako'yamudāhṛtaḥ |
<rasagatā doṣāḥ>
amoghasiddhimamalaṃ sarvasiddhipradaṃ rasam || 1,1.34 ||
ālokya tridaśāḥ sarve brahmaviṣṇupuregamāḥ |
māmabhiprārthayāmāsuḥ stotraiśca vividhaiḥ priye || 1,1.35 ||
rasendradarśanādeva narapakṣimṛgādayaḥ |
siddhiṃ nānāvidhāṃ yānti tannivāraya śaṃkara || 1,1.36 ||
tasmāttridhā kañcukābhirdoṣaiścāsau niyojitaḥ |
tadā prabhṛti doṣaiśca kañcukābhiśca varjitaḥ || 1,1.37 ||
śuddho'ṣṭādaśasaṃskāraiḥ sūto bhavati siddhidaḥ |
<rasāvasthāḥ>
dhūmaściṭiciṭiścaiva maṇḍūkaplutireva ca || 1,1.38 ||
sakaṃpaśca vikaṃpaśca pañcāvasthā rasasya tu |
<rasagatayaḥ>
rasasya gatayaḥ pañca jale syājjalavadgatiḥ || 1,1.39 ||
dhūme dhūmagatiḥ sūtaḥ staraṇe haṃsavadgatiḥ |
kiṭṭe kiṭṭanibhā devi pañcamo jīvavadgatiḥ || 1,1.40 ||
catasro gatayo dṛśyāḥ adṛśyā paṃcamī gatiḥ |
yantrauṣadhādyai roddhavyāścatasro gatayaḥ priye || 1,1.41 ||
dhyānamantrauṣadhādyaiśca roddhavyā pañcamī gatiḥ |
<naisargikā doṣāḥ>
pārthivaśca tathaivāpya āgneyaścānilastathā || 1,1.42 ||
nābhaso gajacarmākhyaḥ puṇḍarīko visarpakṛt |
hāridro raktacarmākhyo nāraṅgo raktabindukaḥ || 1,1.43 ||
asahyāgniśca maṇḍūko malā naisargikāḥ smṛtāḥ |
<aupadhikā doṣāḥ>
kālikā malinī caiva kapotī raktakañcukā || 1,1.44 ||
salomī girijā caiva piṅgalī sapta kañcukāḥ |
aupādhikā imā jñeyāḥ pārade kīrtitāḥ priye || 1,1.45 ||
<yaugikā doṣāḥ>
viṣaṃ nāgaśca vaṅgaśca yaugikaśca trayaḥ smṛtāḥ |
<doṣāṇāṃ svarūpam>
bhaumaḥ kuṣṭhakaraścāpyo doṣodrekaṃ karoti saḥ || 1,1.46 ||
āgneyaḥ kurute dāhaṃ vāyavyaḥ śūlakṛdbhavet |
bādhiryaṃ nābhaso doṣo gajatvaggajacarmakṛt || 1,1.47 ||
puṇḍarīko dadrukaro visarpaśca visarpakṛt |
hāridraḥ pāḍukṛtprokto raktacarmākṣipāṭalam || 1,1.48 ||
nāraṅgo dumbaraṃ kuṣṭhaṃ raktabījo masūrikāḥ |
asahyāgnirmohakārī maṇḍūkaścarmakīlakṛt || 1,1.49 ||
malo mūrcchākaro devi khyātā doṣodbhavāstathā |
kālikā kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ ca malinī malasagraham || 1,1.50 ||
kapotī svarasādaṃ ca visphoṭaṃ raktakañcukā |
salomī vamanaṃ kuryātgirijā jāḍyakāriṇī || 1,1.51 ||
piṅgalī netraghnī doṣāḥ kacukajāḥ smṛtāḥ |
viṣaṃ mṛtyuprado nāgo jāḍyaṃ vaṅgassurārcite || 1,1.52 ||
kurute pūtigandhatvaṃ gadā yaugikasambhavāḥ |
<rasasya sthānāntaragatiḥ>
prathamārtavasusnātā surūpā śubhalakṣaṇā || 1,1.53 ||
śuddhāmbaradharā mālyagandhanliptā subhūṣitā |
uttamāśvasamārūḍhā ratisaṅgavivarjitā || 1,1.54 ||
abhyarcya gaṇanāthaṃ ca bhairavaṃ ca guruṃ purā |
rasendrabhairavaṃ dhyātvā kūpasthaṃ pāradaṃ priye || 1,1.55 ||
paśyecchīghraṃ tato gacchetna punaḥ pṛṣṭhamīkṣayet |
eṣā yojanamātroā kumārī hayasādhanā || 1,1.56 ||
tadānīmāharettatu kumārāsaṃjighṛkṣayā |
kūpamadhyātsamutpatya so'nudhāvati tāṃ prati || 1,1.57 ||
yāvadyojanamāgatya punaḥ kūpe viśetkṣaṇāt |
paritaḥ kṛtagarteṣu teṣu teṣu ca saṃsthitam || 1,1.58 ||
taṃ rasendraṃ śucirbhūtvā gṛhṇīyādrasadeśikaḥ |
gauravādagnivadanātpatito daradāhvaye || 1,1.59 ||
deśe sa sūto bhūlīnaḥ tantrajñai rasakovidaḥ |
nikṣipya mṛttikāyantre pātanākhye samāgataḥ || 1,1.60 ||
pārado gṛhyate devi doṣahīnassa ucyate |
evameva tatra tatra siddhavidyādharaissadā || 1,1.61 ||
nikṣepitaḥ pāradendro vidyate devi siddhidaḥ || 1,1.62 ||


āk, 1, 2
śrībhairavī |
rasopadeśadātāraṃ tacchiṣyaṃ kākinīstriyam |
rasapūjāṃ tu pṛcchāmi yathāvatkathayasva me || 1,2.1 ||
śrībhairavaḥ |
<rasopadeśakalakṣaṇam>
śṛṇu bhairavi tatsarvamapūrvaṃ kathayāmi te |
ācāryo jñānavāndakṣaḥ śīlavān guṇavān śuciḥ || 1,2.2 ||
dharmajñaḥ satyasaṃdhaśca rasaśāstraviśāradaḥ |
vedavedāntatattvajño nirmalaḥ śivavatsalaḥ || 1,2.3 ||
devībhaktaḥ sadā śānto rasamaṇḍapakovidaḥ |
mantrasiddho mahāvīro devatāyāgatatparaḥ || 1,2.4 ||
rasadīkṣāvidhānajño mantrauṣadhamahārasān |
rāgasaṃkhyāṃ bījakalpaṃ dvandvamelāpanaṃ biḍam || 1,2.5 ||
rañjanaṃ sāraṇāṃ tailaṃ dalāni krāmaṇāni ca |
varṇotkarṣaṃ mṛdutvaṃ ca jāraṇaṃ bālavṛddhayoḥ || 1,2.6 ||
khecarīṃ bhūcarīṃ caiva yo vetti sa gururbhavet |
<śiṣyalakṣaṇam>
gurubhaktaḥ sadācāro lobhamāyāvivarjitaḥ || 1,2.7 ||
nispṛho nirahaṅkāraḥ satyavāṅniyamasthitaḥ |
nirālasyaḥ svadharmajñaḥ ṣaṭkarmanirataḥ sudhīḥ || 1,2.8 ||
dambhahiṃsādinirmuktaḥ śivācāreṣu dīkṣitaḥ |
atyantasādhakaḥ śānto mantrānuṣṭhānatatparaḥ || 1,2.9 ||
dāntaḥ śiṣyaḥ sa vijñeyaḥ śaktimān gatamatsaraḥ |
<kākinīlakṣaṇam>
ākuñcitasnigdhakeśā padmapatrāyatekṣaṇā || 1,2.10 ||
dīrghottuṅgaghrāṇakeśā vidrumādharaśobhitā |
dāḍimībījadaśanā kambugrīvonnatastanī || 1,2.11 ||
śirīṣamālāmṛdulabāhupāśavirājitā |
saṃkīrṇorasthalā nimnanābhiḥ sūkṣmā calāṅgakā || 1,2.12 ||
talodarī romarājivalitrayavibhūṣitā |
viśālajaghanopetā rambhoruḥ subhagā priyā || 1,2.13 ||
kādalopamajaṅghā ca pāṭalāṅghrisaroruhā |
śoṇāmalanakhopetā rājahaṃsagatiḥ śubhā || 1,2.14 ||
kalakokilanidhvānakalakaṇṭhādimañjulā |
pippalachadasaṃkāśasmaramandiramaṇḍitā || 1,2.15 ||
yuvatī śyāmalā snigdhā surūpā śubhalakṣaṇā |
cumbanasparśanāśleṣaratikarmavicakṣaṇā || 1,2.16 ||
bahule yā puṣpavatī pakṣe sā kākinī smṛtā |
svasvavarṇasadbhūtā grāhyā vāpyanyavarṇajā || 1,2.17 ||
rasakarmaṇi dīkṣāyāṃ prayoge ca rasāyane |
<kākinītvāpādanam>
kākinyabhāve taruṇī surūpānyāthavā bhavet || 1,2.18 ||
gandhakaṃ goghṛtopetaṃ niṣkaṃ triḥ saptavāsaram |
prātardadyāttu sā nārī kākinīsadṛśā bhavet || 1,2.19 ||
dhārmikaḥ pāpabhīruśca balavān jñānavittamaḥ |
nyāyaśreṣṭhaḥ sarvasamo rasāgamaviśāradaḥ || 1,2.20 ||
bhūpatiścāsya mantrī ca sarvaśāstraviśāradaḥ |
sarvadharmarataḥ śreṣṭho nyāyamārgapravartakaḥ || 1,2.21 ||
<rasaśālānirmāṇam>
rasaśālāṃ pravakṣyāmi rasendrasya varānane |
nirṇayecca nirātaṅke deśe ca nirupaplave || 1,2.22 ||
āstikaprāṇisubhage dhanadhānyasamākule |
saurājye pūrṇavibhave nirapāye hyanindake || 1,2.23 ||
nagare sarvavarṇāḍhye mahāmāheśvarāvṛte |
tatrāpi vijaye sthāne pavitre ca surakṣite || 1,2.24 ||
savyālapaśupakṣyādisaṃbādhaparivarjite |
samasthale ca prākāraparighārgalabhūṣite || 1,2.25 ||
nāraṅgadāḍimaḍahajambīraphalapūrake |
tālahintālavakulanārikelāmlapāṭale || 1,2.26 ||
aśokajambūpanasasālapuṃnāgamaṇḍite |
kapitthapūgasaraladevadārusubilvake || 1,2.27 ||
dhātrīnyagrodhavaraṇamadhūkāmrātamaṇḍite |
ketakīmallikājātīyūthikāmālatīyute || 1,2.28 ||
māgadhīkundakuravatilake śatapatrake |
kumudotpalakalhārapuṇḍarīkāṃbujotpalaiḥ || 1,2.29 ||
haṃsasārasakāraṇḍacakravākavirājitaiḥ |
sarobhiḥ śītalajale paritaḥ pariveṣṭite || 1,2.30 ||
śītānilābhilulitalalitaprasavāñcite |
citrodyāne'tirucire rasaśālāṃ prakalpayet || 1,2.31 ||
ṣoḍaśastambharucirāṃ caturaśrāṃ samāyatām |
manojñāṃ devadeveśi mattavāraṇasaṃyutām || 1,2.32 ||
vātāyanasamopetāṃ kavāṭārgalarakṣitām |
kakṣapatākāsaṃyuktāṃ sarvopakaraṇojjvalām || 1,2.33 ||
samālikhitadikpālāṃ samarcitavināyakām |
pratiṣṭhitomāmāheśāṃ dvārapālaiśca rakṣitām || 1,2.34 ||
bherīkāhalaghaṇṭādidivyavādyavināditām |
vāstulakṣaṇasaṃyuktāṃ rasaśālāṃ prakalpayet || 1,2.35 ||
śālāyāṃ vedikā kāryā sudṛḍhā darpaṇopamā |
iṣṭakaiḥ khacitā ramyā sasopānā salakṣaṇā || 1,2.36 ||
kapilāgomayāliptā hiraṇyakalaśāvṛtā |
<rasendrapūjāvidhiḥ>
vedikāyāṃ rasendrasya bhadrapīṭhaṃ prakalpayet || 1,2.37 ||
bhadrapīṭhe likhetsamyaksindūreṇa dvihastakam |
ṣaṭkoṇaṃ vasupatraṃ ca tadbahiścāṣṭapatrakam || 1,2.38 ||
kamalaṃ caturaśraṃ ca caturdvāropaśobhitam |
rasaliṅgaṃ nyasedyantre puṣpādyaiśca samarcayet || 1,2.39 ||
<rasasandhyā>
atha sandhyāṃ pravakṣyāmi rasakarmaphalapradām |
praviśedrasaśālāṃ ca snātaḥ śuddho'nulepitaḥ || 1,2.40 ||
śuklamālyāmbaradharaḥ saṃyatātmā jitendriyaḥ |
yatavāggurubhaktaśca satyavādī dṛḍhavrataḥ || 1,2.41 ||
upaviśya samācāmetmṛdule citrakambale |
vāṅmāyāśrīyutaṃ toyamātmavidyāśivātmakam || 1,2.42 ||
tattvaṃ ca śodhayāmīti trivāraṃ vidhivatpibet |
dhyāyedvaradagāyatrīṃ trivāraṃ prajapetpriye || 1,2.43 ||
rasabhairavagāyatrīṃ śṛṇu bhairavi tattvataḥ |
aiṃ raseśvarāya vidmahe rasāṅkuśāya dhīmahi |
tannaḥ sūtaḥ pracodayāt |
koṭisūryapratīkāśaṃ daśakoṭīndusaṃnibham || 1,2.44 ||
śubhraṃ pañcamukhaṃ devaṃ trinetraṃ candraśekharam |
aṣṭādaśabhujaṃ śāntaṃ pañcakṛtyaparāyaṇam || 1,2.45 ||
śuklamālyāṃbaradharaṃ nāgayajñopavītinam |
sphaṭikasaṅkāśaṃ pretārūḍhaṃ raseśvaram || 1,2.46 ||
nīlakaṇṭhaṃ ca sarvajñaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam |
varadaṃ rasaśāstraṃ ca pāradaṃ bhujaṅgaṃ sudhām || 1,2.47 ||
śaṅkhabhaiṣajyakodaṇḍaśūlakhaṭvāṅgasāyakam |
ḍamaruṃ karavālaṃ ca gadāṃ pāvakamīśvari || 1,2.48 ||
ārogyaṃ divyamālāṃ ca hyabhayaṃ dvinavaiḥ karaiḥ |
rasāṅkuśīṃ nijotsaṅge bibhrāṇaṃ vṛṣavāhanam || 1,2.49 ||
kapardabhāraruciraṃ mandahāsānanaṃ śivam |
rasabhairavamācintya tasyotsaṅge rasāṅkuśīm || 1,2.50 ||
aṅkuśaṃ cākṣamālāṃ ca dadhatīṃ dakṣahastayoḥ |
pāśābhayaṃ ca bibhrāṇāṃ dakṣiṇetarahastayoḥ || 1,2.51 ||
caturbhujāṃ raktavarṇāṃ triṇetrāminduśekharām |
raktāmbaradharāṃ devīṃ kambugrīvāṃ kṛpāmayīm || 1,2.52 ||
ratnālaṅkārarucirāṃ mandahāsavirājitām |
dhyāyedrasāṅkuśīṃ devīṃ devasyābhimukhīṃ śivām || 1,2.53 ||
tato bhasma samādāya vāmahastena dhārayet |
naivādhaḥpatitaṃ pātre gṛhītvā gomayaṃ sudhīḥ || 1,2.54 ||
viśoṣya pradahetkuṇḍe krameṇa dhārayetpriye |
punaruddhāraṇaṃ caiva sadyojātādibhirbhavet || 1,2.55 ||
nirīkṣaṇaṃ prokṣaṇaṃ ca tāḍanābhyukṣaṇaṃ tathā |
sadyojātādibhirmantrairabhimantrya ca tatpunaḥ || 1,2.56 ||
mūrdhni vaktre ca hṛdaye guhye caraṇayoḥ kramāt |
īśānādyaiḥ pañcamantraistattatsthāneṣu nikṣipet || 1,2.57 ||
sarvāṅgoddhūlanaṃ kuryātbhasmanā pañcabhiśca taiḥ |
saṃkalpaṃ vidhivatkuryātrasācāryo maheśvaraḥ || 1,2.58 ||
prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kṛtvā mūlamantreṇa pārvati |
prokṣayenmantritaṃ toyaṃ mūrdhni vāmakarasthitam || 1,2.59 ||
ācamya ca punastoyaṃ mūlamantreṇa mantritam |
tridhā dakṣiṇahastasthaṃ pibedācamanaṃ tataḥ || 1,2.60 ||
arghyaṃ mūlena dattvā triḥ punarācamya pārvati |
aṣṭottaraśataṃ japtvā mūlamantramudāradhīḥ || 1,2.61 ||
samarpayecca sūtāya rasasandhyeyamīśvari |
trikālamevaṃ kurvīta sandhyāṃ sarvāghanāśinīm || 1,2.62 ||
<rasāṃkuśarasāṃkuśīmūlamantroddhāraḥ>
vakṣye'haṃ mūlamantrasya śṛṇūddhārakramaṃ priye |
vāṅmāyā kamalā cātha caturthyanto raseśvaraḥ || 1,2.63 ||
tato mahākālapadaṃ mahābalapadaṃ tataḥ |
aghorabhairavapadaṃ vajravīrapadaṃ tataḥ || 1,2.64 ||
krodhakālapadaṃ caitatsarvaṃ sambuddhisaṃyutam |
kaṣaṣṭavargāntayutaḥ sādhodantaḥ sabindukaḥ || 1,2.65 ||
idaṃ pañcākṣaraṃ kūṭaṃ tataḥ karṣaṇasaṃyutaḥ |
savisargo'pyayaṃ kūṭaścaturvarṇātmakaḥ priye || 1,2.66 ||
rasendrabhairavasyāyaṃ mantro dvātriṃśadarṇakaḥ |
gopitaḥ sarvatantreṣu rahasyo'tyantadurlabhaḥ || 1,2.67 ||
rasāṅkuśāmantramahaṃ vakṣyāmi śṛṇu bhairavi |
vāṅmāyā bhuvaneśī ca ramā makaraketanaḥ || 1,2.68 ||
rasāṅkuśā caturthyāṃ ca namo'nto dvādaśārṇakaḥ |
mantro rasāṅkuśāyāśca tava devyāḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 1,2.69 ||
rasabhairavasya chando'nuṣṭhupprakīrtitaḥ |
nandikeśa ṛṣiḥ prokto devaḥ śrīrasabhairavaḥ || 1,2.70 ||
raṃ bījaṃ laṃ bhavetkīlamantyārṇaḥ śaktirīritaḥ |
rasāṃkuśāyāḥ skandasyārdraṃ bījaṃ kīlakaṃ rasaḥ || 1,2.71 ||
namaḥ śaktiriti khyātaṃ rahasyaṃ mantramuttamam |
<rasaliṅgārcanā>
rasaliṅgārcanaṃ vakṣye bhairavi śṛṇu tattvataḥ || 1,2.72 ||
vāmapādaṃ puraskṛtya praviśedyāgamandiram |
āsane mṛdule sthitvā śuciḥ saṃyatamānasaḥ || 1,2.73 ||
prāṇānāyamya saṃkalpya śrīguruṃ śirasi sthitam |
śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ praśāntaṃ varadābhayam || 1,2.74 ||
smarettannāma pūrvaṃ ca tadanujñāmavāpya ca |
divi sthitāśca ye bhūtā bhūmisthā vighnakāriṇaḥ || 1,2.75 ||
pātāle ye mahābhūtāḥ te naśyantu śivājñayā |
anenaiva ca mantreṇa digvidikṣvakṣatān kṣipet || 1,2.76 ||
puṣpamastrāya phaḍiti nikṣipedantarikṣake |
sarvaśatrupramathanī ceti dakṣiṇapārṣṇikām || 1,2.77 ||
trivāraṃ ghātayedbhūmau prāṇāyāmo bhavetpunaḥ |
rasendrabhadrapīṭhasya dakṣiṇe caturaśrakam || 1,2.78 ||
maṇḍalaṃ hastamātraṃ ca sārdhaṃ kuryātsuśobhanam |
trikoṇavṛttaṣaṭkoṇacaturaśraṃ bhavetkramāt || 1,2.79 ||
indrādilokapālāṃśca tattannāmapuraḥsaram |
praṇavādinamo'ntaiśca pūjayetkusumākṣataiḥ || 1,2.80 ||
vāṅmāyākamalābījairhṛdayādīn prapūjayet |
prācyāmavācyāṃ kramaśaḥ pratīcyottarayoḥ śive || 1,2.81 ||
īśānalāsuramarutkoṇe netre prapūjayet |
astraṃ sarvāsu kāṣṭhāsu caturthyantaṃ phaḍantakam || 1,2.82 ||
choṭikā darśayedaṣṭa paścādādhāramāharet |
tatraivāvāhayedagnimaṇḍalaṃ bījamūlakam || 1,2.83 ||
agnerdaśa kalāstatra yā gandhākṣataiḥ kramāt |
tataḥ pātraṃ samādāya bhāvayetsūryamaṇḍalam || 1,2.84 ||
mārtaṇḍadvādaśakalāstatrārcyāścandanākṣataiḥ |
gandhottamānvitaṃ toyaṃ pūrayedarghyapātrakam || 1,2.85 ||
cintayedaindavaṃ bimbaṃ tāstatra pūjayet |
madhye trikoṇamālikhya śaktibījaṃ samullikhet || 1,2.86 ||
tatra kṣipetgandhapuṣpaṃ mūlamantraṃ tridhā japet |
pūrvādidikṣu catasṛṣu catuḥpātrāṇi dhārayet || 1,2.87 ||
pūrayetpūrvavattāni gandhapuṣpākṣatairyajet |
prokṣayenmūlamantreṇa madhyapātrodakena ca || 1,2.88 ||
pātrāṇi nijagātrāṇi pūjādravyāṇi sarvataḥ |
svāgataścaturaśraṃ ca maṇḍalaṃ parikalpayet || 1,2.89 ||
pūrvavatpañcapātrāṇi pūrayecchuddhavāriṇā |
nikṣipenmadhyage sūtaṃ ratnaṃ dakṣiṇake kṣipet || 1,2.90 ||
svarṇaṃ tadvāmapātre ca tatsamīpe kuśaṃ nyaset |
ratnapātrasamīpasthe pātre syādaṣṭagandhakam || 1,2.91 ||
pratipātre trikoṇaṃ ca likhenmāyāsamanvitam |
nyasedgandhākṣataṃ puṣpaṃ tridhā mūlena mantrayet || 1,2.92 ||
saptadhā rasapātraṃ ca mūlamantreṇa mantrayet |
rasapātrodakenaiva pūjādravyāṇi cātmanaḥ || 1,2.93 ||
śarīramuttamāṅgaṃ ca prokṣayetpīṭhadīpakam |
āsanasya ṛṣirmeruḥ sutalaṃ chanda īritam || 1,2.94 ||
kūrmarūpī tu bhagavān devatā parikīrtitā |
pṛthvi tvayā dhṛtā lokā devi tvaṃ viṣṇunā dhṛtā |
tvaṃ ca dhāraya māṃ devi pavitraṃ kuru cāsanam |
anenaiva tu mantreṇa cāsane kusumaṃ kṣipet || 1,2.95 ||
<bhūtaśuddhiḥ>
bhūtaśuddhiṃ pravakṣyāmi pṛthvī pītā savajrakā |
kaṭhinā laṃbījayutā jānvantāṅguṣṭhamūlataḥ || 1,2.96 ||
dhyeyā jānvādikaṭyantamuparyasyārdhacandrakā |
śubhrā vaṃ bījasahitā vicintyā dravarūpikā || 1,2.97 ||
kaṭyādi hṛdayāntaṃ ca bahirdhyeyastrikoṇagaḥ |
raṃ bījaṃ raktavarṇaśca svastikena vibhūṣitaḥ || 1,2.98 ||
hṛdādikaṇṭhaparyantaṃ vāyuḥ smaryo'ñjanaprabhaḥ |
yaṃbījasahito devi ṣaḍbinduparibhūṣitaḥ || 1,2.99 ||
ṣaṭkoṇaḥ karṇadeśācca brahmarandhrāntamīśvari |
vartulaṃ haṃbījayutaṃ mahākṛṣṇaṃ viyatsmaret || 1,2.100 ||
pṛthivīmapsu ca tāstvagnau sa ca vāyau sa so'mbare |
melayitvā dvādaśānte sthāpayetparameśvari || 1,2.101 ||
yaṃbījenātmano dehaṃ mūlādhārādviśoṣayet |
ṣaḍādhārāṇi nirbhidya yāvatbrahmabilaṃ priye || 1,2.102 ||
raṃ bījena daheddehaṃ sarvamāplāvayettataḥ |
vaṃbījena dravībhūtaṃ brahmarandhrendumaṇḍalāt || 1,2.103 ||
syandamānāmṛtenaiva tanumāplāvayecchive |
ākāśādvāyumādadyāttasmādvahniṃ samāharet || 1,2.104 ||
vahnerāpaḥ samādeyāstābhyo bhūmiṃ samāharet |
evaṃ pañca ca bhūtāni svasvasthāne niyojayet || 1,2.105 ||
vāmakukṣisthitaṃ pāpapuruṣaṃ kṛṣṇarūpiṇam |
dhyāyettaṃ nirgataṃ devi khaḍgacarmadharaṃ param || 1,2.106 ||
nyāsaṃ rasāṅkuśenaiva kṛtvāṅgulihṛdādiṣu |

aiṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ kṣmauṃ kṣmaṃ hrāṃ hrīṃ hrūṃ hraiṃ hrauṃ hraḥ phaṭraseśvarāya mahākālabhairavāya śikhāyai vaṣaṭ |
raudrarūpāya kṛṣṇapiṅgalalocanāya avatara 2 avatāraya 2 jalpa 2 jalpaya 2 śubhāśubhaṃ kathaya 2 kathāpaya 2 mama mahārakṣāṃ kuru kuru kāraya 2 mama rasasiddhiṃ dehi dehi 1888 |
mahākālabhairavāya hṛdayāya namaḥ |
pūrvavanmahābalabhairavāya śirase svāhā |
pūrvavataghorabhairavāya śikhāyai vaṣaṭ |
pūrvavatvajravīrabhairavāya kavacāya hum |
pūrvavatkrodhabhairavāya netratrayāya vauṣaṭ |
pūrvavatkālabhairavāya astrāya phaṭ |
evamaṅgulinyāsaḥ |

pratibījaiḥ samuccārya caturthaṃ rasāṃkuśam || 1,2.107 ||
hṛdādipañcasthāneṣu vinyasedbījapañcakam |
tataścāstrāya phaḍiti coṭikāṃ dikṣu darśayet || 1,2.108 ||
mātṛkāṃ ca kalānyāsaṃ pājyonyāsaṃ ca kolakam |
prakurvīta tato devi cāntaryajanamācaret || 1,2.109 ||
kumbhakena hṛdambhoje samphullaṃ vidadhāti hi |
hṛtpadmakarṇikāyāṃ ca rasamūrtiṃ ca cintayet || 1,2.110 ||
mānasairgandhapuṣpādyairupacāraiḥ prapūjayet |
svadehaṃ gandhapuṣpādyairalaṃkuryādyathāsukham || 1,2.111 ||
antaryajanamevaṃ syādbahiryajanamācaret |
astreṇa liṅgaṃ samprokṣya liṅgaśuddhiṃ vidhāya ca || 1,2.112 ||
vastrena toyena pūritāṃ vardhanīṃ priye |
dakṣapārśve saṃniveśya pūjayettāṃ vidhānataḥ || 1,2.113 ||
pīṭhe sindūrarajasā vidadhyāccaturaśrakam |
ṣaṭkoṇaṃ vilikhettasminṛjuṃ svarṇaśalākayā || 1,2.114 ||
vasupatraṃ cāṣṭapatraṃ sacaturdvārabhūgṛham |
caturaśraṃ bahirbhāge dikpālānpūjayetpriye || 1,2.115 ||
nandinaṃ ca mahākālaṃ bhṛṅgiṃ riṭiṃ mahābalam |
kumbhakarṇaṃ ca sugrīvaṃ bhṛṅgakaṃ ca dṛḍhāyudham || 1,2.116 ||
caturdvāre tu vinyasya dvau dvau prāgādipūjitau |
rukmaṃ raupyaṃ tāmrasīsaṃ vaṅgakāntaśaṭhaṃ hyayaḥ || 1,2.117 ||
prāgādidvārṣu sampūjya kramāddve dve maheśvari |
bhūgṛhābhyantare prācyāṃ śukramagnau grahaṃ yajet || 1,2.118 ||
dakṣiṇasyāṃ yajedrudramāsure ca samīraṇam |
paścimasyāṃ śivaṃ vahniṃ vāyavyāmuttare hyumām || 1,2.119 ||
yajetpāvakamaiśānyāṃ tataścāṣṭadalāgrake |
prāgādau gandhakaṃ tālaṃ kāsīsaṃ ca manaḥśilām || 1,2.120 ||
kaṃkuṣṭhaṃ mākṣikaṃ caiva nṛpāvartaṃ ca gairikam |
lepikā kṣepikā caiva krāmikā rañjikā tathā || 1,2.121 ||
lohaṭī bandhakārī ca bhūcarī mṛtyunāśinī |
vibhūtiḥ khecarī caiva daśa dūtyaḥ krameṇa ca || 1,2.122 ||
pūjyāstvaṣṭadale padme hyūrdhvādhastāddaleṣu ca |
māṇikyamuktāvaiḍūryanīlagāruḍavidrumāḥ || 1,2.123 ||
gomedaḥ puṣparāgaśca maṇayaḥ sarvasiddhidāḥ |
dvitīyavasupatrasya dalāgreṣu prapūjayet || 1,2.124 ||
aṣṭādaśabhujā rudrāḥ pañcavaktrāstriyambakāḥ |
candrārdhaśobhimakuṭāḥ nīlagrīvā vṛṣadhvajāḥ || 1,2.125 ||
svasvavarṇadharāḥ sarve tvaṣṭavidyeśvarāstu te |
pūjanīyā maheśāni dvitīye'ṣṭadalāmbuje || 1,2.126 ||
rasakaṃ vimalaṃ tāpyaṃ capalaṃ tutthamañjanam |
ṣaṭkoṇasya dalāgreṣu sakhyaḥ syuḥ sarvasiddhidāḥ || 1,2.127 ||
vajravaikrāntavajrābhrakāntapāṣāṇaṭaṅkaṇam |
bhūnāgaḥ śaktayaścaitāḥ ṣaḍasreṣu prapūjayet || 1,2.128 ||
hiṃgulaṃ sasyakaṃ caiva śilājatvagnijārakam |
tatkarṇikāyāṃ pūrvādau parāśakticatuṣṭayam || 1,2.129 ||
hiṃgulaṃ mālinī śaktiḥ parā śaktiśca sasyakam |
śilājatvaparā śaktiragnijāraḥ parāparā || 1,2.130 ||
praṇavādinamo'ntaiśca tattannāmapuraḥsaram |
sacaturthyā yajedetānrasāvaraṇasaṃsthitān || 1,2.131 ||
madhye tu divyaśaktīnāṃ pūjayedrasabhairavam |
saptaviṃśatiniṣke tu drāvite śuddhahāṭake || 1,2.132 ||
navaniṣkaṃ sūtarājaṃ vinyaseccaikamānasaḥ |
madhūcchiṣṭakṛte yantre liṅgākāre sulakṣaṇe || 1,2.133 ||
niṣṭapte hāṭake sūtaṃ pratapte prakṣipecchive |
muhūrtātsvāṅgaśītaṃ taṃ rasaliṅgaṃ payo'ntare || 1,2.134 ||
sthāpayetpunarādāya hyuparisthaṃ malaṃ haret |
saṃghṛṣya ca samīkṛtya liṅgaṃ saṃprokṣayettataḥ || 1,2.135 ||
evaṃ raupyādilohairvā vyastairvātha samastakaiḥ |
abhrakādimasattvairvā liṅgaṃ kuryātsvaśaktitaḥ || 1,2.136 ||
rasaliṅgaṃ dvidhā proktaṃ sakalaṃ niṣkalaṃ priye |
pūrvoktaṃ sakalaṃ liṅgaṃ niṣkalaṃ kevalo rasaḥ || 1,2.137 ||
bubhukṣūṇāṃ hi sakalaṃ niṣkalaṃ mokṣamicchatām |
rasaliṅgaṃ hemabaddhaṃ brahmaviṣṇvīśavahnayaḥ || 1,2.138 ||
apūjayan rūpyabaddhaṃ gaṇeśaskandanandinaḥ |
tvayābhrasatvasambaddhaṃ rasaliṅgaṃ prapūjitam || 1,2.139 ||
tāmrabaddhaṃ ramāvāṇīkuberendrajalādhipāḥ |
kāntabaddhaṃ yamamarunnairṛtāḥ samapūjayan || 1,2.140 ||
vaṅgabaddhaṃ siddhasādhyavidyādharamaharṣayaḥ |
nāgabaddhaṃ nāgavasugandharvoragakinnarāḥ || 1,2.141 ||
tīkṣṇabaddhaṃ bhadrakālīyakṣabhūtapiśācakāḥ |
muṇḍabaddhaṃ ca durgāmbā cāpsaroguhyakāsurāḥ || 1,2.142 ||
niṣkalaṃ rasaliṅgaṃ tu bhṛṅgyādyā yogino'rcayan |
uktāni rasaliṅgāni mānavāḥ phalakāṅkṣiṇaḥ || 1,2.143 ||
pūjayeyuḥ prayatnena nityaṃ sarvārthasiddhaye |
uktānāṃ rasaliṅgānāṃ prāṇasaṃsthāpanaṃ śṛṇu || 1,2.144 ||
prāṇānāyamya vidhivatprāṇaśaktiṃ smarecchive |
śoṇitāmbhodhimadhyastharaktāmbhojāsanāṃ parām || 1,2.145 ||
raktāṃbaradharāṃ raktāṃ nānāratnakirīṭinīm |
vimalendukalājūṭāṃ ravivahnīndulocanām || 1,2.146 ||
karṇatāṭaṅkakiraṇāruṇīkṛtakapolakām |
śrīparṇīkusumākāranāsāvaṃśavirājitām || 1,2.147 ||
pravālapadmarāgābhabimbādharavirājitām |
kuruvindadalākāraślakṣṇadantābhiśobhitām || 1,2.148 ||
śaṅkhābhirāmakaṇṭhasthamuktāhāravirājitām |
aṅgulīyakakeyūrakaṭakādivibhūṣitām || 1,2.149 ||
pāśāṅkuśekṣukodaṇḍapuṣpabāṇābhayapradām |
rudhirāpūrṇapātreṇa śobhamānakarāmbujām || 1,2.150 ||
pīnastanataṭodbhāsihārakuṅkumamaṇḍitām |
kāñcīmañjīrakaṭakapādāṅgulivibhūṣitām || 1,2.151 ||
prāṇapratiṣṭhāṃ liṅgasya kurvītaikāgramānasaḥ |
sṛṇiṃ mañca pāśaśca haṃsaḥ so'hamiti priye |
amuṣya liṅgasya prāṇa iha prāṇaḥ |
amuṣya liṅgasya jīva iha sthitaḥ |
amuṣya liṅgasya sarvendriyāṇi vāṅmanaścakṣuḥśrotrajihvāghrāṇā ihāgatya sukhaṃ ciraṃ tiṣṭhantu so'haṃ haṃsaḥ svāhā |
yaralavaśaṣasahom |
kroṃ hrīmām |
etanmantraṃ samuccārya prāṇānāvāhayetpriye |
asya śrīprāṇapratiṣṭhāmantrasya brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarā ṛṣayaḥ |
ṛgyajuḥsāmāni chandāṃsi |
śrīparāśaktirdevatā |
āṃ bījam |
hrīṃ śaktiḥ kroṃ kīlakam |
amuṣya liṅgasya prāṇapratiṣṭhāyāṃ viniyogaḥ |
āsanaṃ pūjayetpūrvaṃ gandhapuṣpākṣatādibhiḥ || 1,2.152 ||
hāmādhāraśaktyai namaḥ |
hrīṃ kūrmāya namaḥ |
hrūmanantāya namaḥ |
hraiṃ pṛthivyai namaḥ |
hrauṃ kandāya namaḥ |
aṅkurāya nālāya karṇikebhyaḥ dalebhyaḥ kesarebhyaḥ karṇikāyai tanmadhye dharmāya jñānāya vairāgyāya aiśvaryāya adharmāya ajñānāya avairāgyāya anaiśvaryāya namaḥ |
hṛdambhojasthitaṃ devaṃ saha devyā maheśvari |
āvāhayetsāvaraṇaṃ svāṅgaśaktisamanvitam || 1,2.153 ||
pīṭhe saṃsthāpayelliṅgaṃ mūlenārghyaṃ kuśodakaiḥ |
pādyaṃ gandhodakairdadyāthaimenācamanaṃ bhavet || 1,2.154 ||
ratnodakairbhavetsnānaṃ sarvaṃ mūlena śāmbhavi |
ekādaśena rudreṇa pavamānena pārvati || 1,2.155 ||
camakaiḥ puruṣasūktaiśca phalaiḥ pañcāmṛtairapi |
nālikerekṣusalilaiḥ sugandhodakavāriṇā || 1,2.156 ||
saṃsnāpya ca tato liṅgaṃ pīṭhe saṃsthāpayecchive |
liṅgasya paritaḥ oṃ vāmadevāya namaḥ jyeṣṭhāya rudrāya kālāya kalavikaraṇāya balāya balavikaraṇāya balapramathanāya sarvabhūtadamanāya ityaṣṭāsu dikṣu manonmanāya iti śivasannidhau puṣpairabhyarcya |
oṃ hrāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ |
oṃ hrīṃ śirase svāhā |
oṃ hrūṃ śikhāyai vaṣaṭ |
oṃ hraiṃ kavacāya hum |
oṃ hrauṃ netratrayāya vauṣaṭ |
oṃ hraḥ astrāya phaṭ |
hrāmīśānāya namaḥ |
hrīṃ tatpuruṣāya namaḥ |
hrūmaghorāya namaḥ |
hraiṃ vāmadevāya namaḥ |
hrauṃ sadyojātāya namaḥ |
mūlamantreṇa devāya dadyātpuṣpāñjaliṃ priye || 1,2.157 ||
mūlamantraṣaḍaṅgena nyāsaṃ kuryācchivasya ca |
liṅgasya dakṣiṇe bhāge ṛgādīnnigamānapi || 1,2.158 ||
brahmāṇaṃ pṛthivīṃ toyaṃ viṣṇuṃ rudraṃ hutāśanam |
īśvaraṃ pavanaṃ caivamākāśaṃ ca sadāśivam || 1,2.159 ||
śrīkaṇṭhamamṛtāṃśuṃ ca bhairavaṃ bhāskaraṃ tathā |
paraṃ śivaṃ parātmānaṃ pūjayenmatimān kramāt || 1,2.160 ||
liṅgasya vāmapārśve tu brāhmyādīḥ sapta mātaraḥ |
mahālakṣmīṃ mahākālīṃ durgāṃ skandavināyakau || 1,2.161 ||
yoginyaḥ kṣetrapālāśca śaktayaścādipīṭhakāḥ |
oḍyāṇaśaktirjālaṃdhrī tathā pūrṇā giriḥ priye || 1,2.162 ||
kāmeśvarīti saṃpūjyā rudraśaktiḥ surārcite |
lopāmudrā kubjikā ca kālasaṃkarṣaṇī tathā || 1,2.163 ||
madhurādirasā gandhāḥ sarvadhānyāni pārvati |
divyauṣadhyaḥ sudhā hālā viṣaṃ carmādidhātavaḥ || 1,2.164 ||
indriyāṇi mano buddhirahaṅkāraśca cetanaḥ |
śabdādyā viṣayāḥ pūjyā dehāṅgāni ca śāmbhavi || 1,2.165 ||
liṅgasya paścime bhāge samudrāḥ sarito nadāḥ |
parvatā merumukhyāśca bhāskarādyā nava grahāḥ || 1,2.166 ||
jyotiścakraṃ dhruvaścaiva śiśumāraḥ prajāpatiḥ |
kalāḥ kāṣṭhāśca ghaṭikā muhūrtāḥ praharā dinam || 1,2.167 ||
rātriḥ pakṣaśca māsaśca ṛtukālāyanāni ca |
saṃvatsarā yugāḥ kalpā diśastārāstithiḥ śive || 1,2.168 ||
pātālāni ca nāgendrāstathā kālāgnirudrakāḥ |
yakṣāḥ piśācabhūtāśca rākṣasā danujāstathā || 1,2.169 ||
guhyakā duṣṭavetālā rājayakṣmādayo gadāḥ |
mantrāḥ śastrāṇi cāstrāṇi sampūjya kramaśaḥ priye || 1,2.170 ||
liṅgasya pūrvabhāge tu saṃpūjyā gurupaṅktayaḥ |
siddhāḥ sādhyāśca munayo yogino brahmavādinaḥ || 1,2.171 ||
vidyādharāḥ kinnarāśca gandharvaścāśvinau tathā |
vasavo'psarasāṃ mukhyā śṛṅgārādirasā nava || 1,2.172 ||
gītaṃ nṛttāni tadbhedāḥ kāśyapādyāstathā daśa |
rudrāḥ samanavaḥ pūjyā bhāskarā vahnayastrayaḥ || 1,2.173 ||
viśve devāśca pitaro mātaraḥ pitṛdevatāḥ |
kulācāryā rasācāryāḥ pūrvācāryāḥ śivārcakāḥ || 1,2.174 ||
matsyādidaśajanmāni jino lokāyatastathā |
narakādyāḥ sarvalokā garuḍā garuḍāṇḍakāḥ || 1,2.175 ||
brahmāṇḍāḥ khecarāḥ sarve bhūcarāśca jalecarāḥ |
sampūjya devatāḥ sarvāḥ praṇavādinamo'ntakaiḥ || 1,2.176 ||
tattannāmacaturthyantaiḥ puṣpagandhākṣataiḥ priye |
devāya vastrayugalamupavītadvayaṃ tathā || 1,2.177 ||
madhuparkaṃ divyagandhamakṣatān kusumāni ca |
sadā kaitakavarjyāni divyāni vidalāni ca || 1,2.178 ||
tatpuṣpāṇi ca kahlārakamalotpalakairavam |
jātīcampakapunnāgapūganāraṅgakaiḥ || 1,2.179 ||
māgadhīmādhavīmallīyūthikāprabhṛtīni ca |
damanī maruvaṃ caiva rasendrāya samarpayet || 1,2.180 ||
dhūpaṃ daśāṅgaṃ dīpaṃ cāpūpānnaṃ ghṛtapāyasam |
saṣaḍrasāni śākāni śuddhagandhottamānvitam || 1,2.181 ||
lehyaṃ coṣyādi naivedyaṃ pāradendrāya darśayet |
hastaprakṣālanaṃ devi dadyādgandhodakena ca || 1,2.182 ||
suvāsitāni pūgāni ghanasārayutāni ca |
avadātāni sāgrāṇi nāgavallīdalāni ca || 1,2.183 ||
muktāphalodbhavaṃ cūrṇaṃ tāmbūlaṃ ca samarpayet |
darpaṇaṃ cāmaradvandvaṃ chatraṃ nṛttaṃ ca gītakam || 1,2.184 ||
upacāreṣu sarveṣu dadyādācamanaṃ priye |
sarvopacārānmūlena dadyātstotraṃ japecchive || 1,2.185 ||
rasendrabhairavaṃ devaṃ dhyātvā devīṃ rasāṃkuśām |
māheśvarāṃśca vinatāṃ gandhapuṣpādibhiryajet || 1,2.186 ||
aṅguṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ ca dadyādgandhottamānvitam |
bindumūlena devāya devyai ca gurupaṅktaye || 1,2.187 ||
kramādāvaraṇasthebhyo devībhyo'pi ca tarpayet |
hrīmānandaśivamūrtimārādhayāmi |
hrīṃ parānandaśivamūrtimārādhayāmi |
hrīṃ parāparānandaśivamūrtimārādhayāmi |
hrīmānandasiddhamūrtimārādhayāmi |
hrīṃ parānandasiddhamūrtimārādhayāmi |
hrīṃ parāparānandasiddhamūrtimārādhayāmi |
klīmānandagurumūrtimārādhayāmi |
klīṃ parānandagurumūrtimārādhayāmi |
klīṃ parāparānandagurumūrtimārādhayāmi |
tebhyo dattvā prasādaṃ ca tairanujñāpitaḥ svayam || 1,2.188 ||
kuṇḍalyāmātmatattvaṃ ca vidyātattvaṃ ca nābhigam |
hṛdaye śivatattvaṃ ca sarvatattvaṃ hi tālugam || 1,2.189 ||
tritattvamevaṃ saṃcintya prasādaṃ ca samantrakam |
tataḥ prajvalitaṃ smṛtvā jyotirantargataṃ priye || 1,2.190 ||
svīkurvīta prasādaṃ ca yatheṣṭaṃ surasevite |
prīṇanti pitaro harṣānmūrchayā sarvadevatāḥ || 1,2.191 ||
ajñānā [... au3 Zeichenjh] vaprītiḥ sa evāhaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
<rasendrabalikramaḥ>
gandhottamaudanaṃ śuddhakṣatajaṃ ca ghṛtaṃ madhu || 1,2.192 ||
saṃyojya pañcakhaṇḍaṃ ca kṛtvā dadyātkrameṇa ca |
dakṣiṇottarapūrvāsu paścimāyāṃ ca madhyame || 1,2.193 ||
tāṃstānmantrānsamuccārya baliṃ dadyāttato naraḥ |
praṇavaṃ māyāṃ kamalāṃ devīṃ putrapradaṃ tataḥ || 1,2.194 ||
tato vaṭukanāthākhyaṃ kapilaṃ jaṭābhārabhāsuraṃ ca tataḥ piṅgala triṇetra imāṃ baliṃ pūjāṃ gṛhṇa gṛhṇa svāhā |
oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ siddhayoginībhyaḥ sarvamātṛbhyo namaḥ |
oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ sarvabhūtebhyaḥ sarvabhūtapatibhyo namaḥ |
oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ sthānakṣetrapāla imāṃ baliṃ pūjāṃ gṛhṇa gṛhṇa svāhā |
oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ rasendra kṣetrapāla rājarājeśvara imāṃ baliṃ pūjāṃ gṛhṇa gṛhṇa svāhā |
baliṃ dadyāditi śive sarvavighnopaśāntaye |
tataścāṣṭottaraśataṃ japenmūlaṃ rasāgrataḥ || 1,2.195 ||
tataḥ stotraṃ japeddevi rasabhairavatuṣṭaye |
saṃtuṣṭāḥ sarvadevāḥ syuḥ tuṣṭe tu rasabhairave || 1,2.196 ||
ayaṃ nityārcanavidhiḥ proktaḥ sarvatra durlabhaḥ |
<pāradapūjāphalam>
viddhi māṃ pāradaṃ devi tatsmartā matsamaḥ priye || 1,2.197 ||
pāradastvadhiko matto madīyatvācca sārataḥ |
rasasya smaraṇaṃ puṇyaṃ darśanaṃ sparśanaṃ tathā || 1,2.198 ||
pūjanaṃ bhakṣaṇaṃ dānaṃ ṣoḍhā puṇyamudīritam |
sāttvikaṃ rājasaṃ caiva tāmasaṃ trividhaṃ vapuḥ || 1,2.199 ||
sāttvikaṃ muktidaṃ śubhraṃ bālaṃ dvibhujamaṇḍitam |
pūrṇapātraṃ sūkṣmadaṇḍaṃ dvinetraṃ kiṅkiṇīsrajam || 1,2.200 ||
rājasaṃ bhuktidaṃ raktaṃ kirīṭaśaśiśekharam |
caturbhujaṃ ca taruṇaṃ nīlakaṇṭhaṃ trilocanam || 1,2.201 ||
sarvaiśvaryapradaṃ dhyāyetśūlapātravarābhayam |
tāmasaṃ kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ ca jvaladūrdhvaśiroruham || 1,2.202 ||
aṣṭādaśabhujaṃ tryakṣaṃ pūrvoktaṃ cintayedrasam |
sarvasiddhipradaṃ devi sarvakāmaphalapradam || 1,2.203 ||
smaraṇaṃ rasarājasya sarvopadravanāśanam |
hṛdambhojasthitaṃ dhyāyennaśyantyenāṃsi bhairavi || 1,2.204 ||
darśanaṃ pāradendrasya hanti pāpaṃ trikālajam |
setukedāraparyantadivyaliṅgaughadarśane || 1,2.205 ||
yatpuṇyaṃ labhate martyaḥ tatkoṭiguṇitaṃ bhavet |
pṛthivyāṃ sarvatīrtheṣu sāgarānteṣu darśanāt || 1,2.206 ||
snānapānādibhiḥ puṇyaṃ tatkoṭiguṇitaṃ bhavet |
gohatyāniyutaṃ devi bhrūṇahatyāśatāni ca || 1,2.207 ||
brahmahatyāsahasrāṇi naśyanti rasadarśanāt |
gavāṃ koṭipradānena svarṇakoṭiśatena ca || 1,2.208 ||
aśvamedhasahasreṇa yatpuṇyaṃ tacca darśanāt |
rasasya sparśanaṃ hanyādagamyāgamanaṃ priye || 1,2.209 ||
paradravyāpahāraṃ ca pāpaṃ prāṇivadhārjitam |
saptadvīpe dharaṇyāṃ ca pātāle gagane divi || 1,2.210 ||
yānyarcayati liṅgāni tatpuṇyaṃ rasapūjayā |
svāyaṃbhuveṣu liṅgeṣu divyeṣu kṛtapūjayā || 1,2.211 ||
yatpuṇyaṃ rasaliṅgasya pūjayā tatphalaṃ bhavet |
yogino'ṣṭāṅganiratā yatpadaṃ brahmavādinaḥ || 1,2.212 ||
tatpadaṃ samavāpnoti rasaliṅgasya pūjayā |
kuṃkumāgarukastūrīkarpūrāntargataṃ rasam || 1,2.213 ||
mūrchitaṃ rasaliṅgāya dadyādiṣṭārthasiddhaye |
bhakṣaṇādrasarājasya sarvapāpaṃ vinaśyati || 1,2.214 ||
anādimāyāmalinamātmānaṃ ca viśoṣayet |
bhavarogaṃ harecchīghraṃ tritāpaṃ ca vināśayet || 1,2.215 ||
rasarājasya dānena caturabdhyantamedinīm |
mahāmerusamaṃ svarṇaṃ dattvā yattatphalaṃ bhavet || 1,2.216 ||
brahmarūpī śuddharaso viṣṇurūpī sa mūrchitaḥ |
bhasmīkṛto rudrarūpī baddhaḥ sūtaḥ sadāśivaḥ || 1,2.217 ||
kāmarūpī jalūkābhaḥ kālarūpī ca doṣavān |
mūrchito bhasmito baddhaḥ pāradendro maheśvari || 1,2.218 ||
vyādhimṛtyudaridratvaṃ harate'sau kṛpākaraḥ |
<rasendrastotram>
dadāsi tvaṃ rasendrāśu śabdasparśanadhūmataḥ || 1,2.219 ||
svarṇamarbudakoṭyantaṃ trāhi khecarasiddhida |
namaste kālakālāya namaḥ sarvaguṇātmane || 1,2.220 ||
valīpalitanāśāya mahāvīryapradāyine |
namaḥ śastrāstrahantre ca māyāmbhonidhipārada || 1,2.221 ||
pāradendra namastubhyaṃ kāminīkelimanmatha |
sudhārūpa tridoṣaghna namaste ṣaḍrasātmane || 1,2.222 ||
ghanatvātpṛthivīrūpa dravatvājjalarūpiṇe |
tejo'dhikatvāttejasvin cāñcalyādvāyurūpiṇe || 1,2.223 ||
sūkṣmatvādrasarūpāya namaste bhūtarūpiṇe |
namaste yogayogyāya prasīdāmaravandita || 1,2.224 ||
vedagodvijarājendraguruvīrādihiṃsayā |
sambhūtakṣayakuṣṭhādīnrogānharasi pāvana || 1,2.225 ||
naṣṭendriyārthānajñānān trātā tvamasi pārada |
sarvauṣadhībhyo'pyadhiko hyaṇumātropayogataḥ || 1,2.226 ||
eko'pi sarvadoṣaghno'rucināśana te namaḥ |
tvayā sṛṣṭā mahendrādyāḥ surā brahmādayaḥ kalāḥ || 1,2.227 ||
candrārkagrahanakṣatraśivabhairavaśaktayaḥ |
jñānājñānaguṇā lokā gandharvoragarākṣasāḥ || 1,2.228 ||
divyauṣadhāni sarvāṇi sarvasiddhikarāṇyapi |
sūtendra bhavataḥ śrīman kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm || 1,2.229 ||
pratyakṣaśambho sūtendra kimanyairbhavati sthite |
aṇimādiguṇopete nānāsiddhipradāyake || 1,2.230 ||
yogino hemamukhyāni lohānyakṣayatāṃ sadā |
amṛtatvaṃ prāpnuvanti sevayā tatra kojvala || 1,2.231 ||
dṛśyādṛśyasvarūpaṃ tvāṃ bhogamokṣapradaṃ param |
yo na jānāti sūtendra sa māṃ vetti kathaṃ naraḥ || 1,2.232 ||
gururmātā pitā bhrātā dhanaṃ vidyā gatiḥ suhṛt |
rasa tvameva bhūtānāṃ tvattaḥ ko'nyo hitaṃkaraḥ || 1,2.233 ||
rasendra tava yoge ca śāstre stotre rasāyane |
yastu bhaktiyuto loke sa evāhaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 1,2.234 ||
brahmajñāne'pi niṣṇāto yastvāṃ nindati pārada |
tyājyo'sau sarvalokeṣu naivāhaṃ rakṣituṃ kṣamaḥ || 1,2.235 ||
yastvāṃ nāstīti ca vadettasya siddhirna kutracit |
yastvāmastīti ca vadettasya siddhirbhavetsadā || 1,2.236 ||
rasendraṃ yastu loke'sminkulīno vādhamānvayaḥ |
saṃskṛtaṃ kurute sūta tvāmaṣṭādaśakarmabhiḥ || 1,2.237 ||
sa puṇyaḥ sa vratī dhanyaḥ sa ślāghyaḥ sa ca buddhimān |
sa pumānsa ca sarvajñaḥ sa siddhaḥ sa ca daivatam || 1,2.238 ||
tatkulaṃ pāvanaṃ bhūmiḥ puṇyā rājā jayānvitaḥ |
tatpuraṃ sotsavaṃ nityaṃ kṛtārthā ca tadambikā || 1,2.239 ||
tasya saṃdarśanaṃ puṇyaṃ sparśanaṃ bhāṣaṇaṃ tathā |
pratyakṣādipramāṇena pārada tvaṃ sadāśivaḥ || 1,2.240 ||
tvatto dehādi lohāni vajratvaṃ yānti hematām |
rasendra tvatprasādena māninyo mṛgalocanāḥ || 1,2.241 ||
cakravākasamottuṅgapīnavṛttastanojjvalāḥ |
cañcalā manmathāsaktā vidrumādharaśobhitāḥ || 1,2.242 ||
śukatuṇḍapratīkāśavakratīkṣṇanakhāṅkurāḥ |
śirīṣamālāsubhujāḥ campakasraksamaprabhāḥ || 1,2.243 ||
nimnanābhisamudbhūtaromarājivirājitāḥ |
sarvāṅgasauṣṭhavāḥ kāntāḥ snigdhā vaśyā bhavanti ca || 1,2.244 ||
sūtendra śuṣkakāṣṭhānāṃ rāśimagniryathā dahet |
tathaiva janmadāridryamṛtyuduḥkhamahāmayān || 1,2.245 ||
jāraṇārthe'gnimadhye tvāṃ dhārayetkati vāsarān |
tāvatkalyasahasrāṇi śivaloke sukhaṃ vaset || 1,2.246 ||
tava kāryeṣu sūtendra kiṃciddravyaṃ vyayennaraḥ |
teṣāṃ dadāsi tadvittaṃ koṭikoṭiguṇādhikam || 1,2.247 ||
paramā pāradī vidyā sarvalokeṣu durlabhā |
bhogamokṣapradā puṇyā putrārogyapravardhanī || 1,2.248 ||
ye tvāṃ nindanti sūtendra mārjālakharavāyasāḥ |
jambūkā ṛṣabhāḥ śvānaḥ klībāndhabadhirā jaḍāḥ || 1,2.249 ||
mūkāḥ kubjāḥ paṅgavaśca vyaṅgā vandhyāśca rogiṇaḥ |
kaivartāḥ pāpakarmāṇo jāyante'nekajanmasu || 1,2.250 ||
bhavato nindakaiḥ sārdhaṃ bhāṇḍapakvānnamiśraṇam |
yaḥ kuryādāsanaṃ śayyāṃ bhojanālāpasaṃgatim || 1,2.251 ||
sa pāpiṣṭho bhavettyājyaḥ sarvadharmabahiṣkṛtaḥ |
iti stotraṃ mayā proktaṃ pāradendrasya bhairavi || 1,2.252 ||
yaḥ paṭhetśṛṇuyādbhaktyā trisandhyaṃ rasasiddhaye |
brahmahatyādipāpaiśca pātakairupapātakaiḥ || 1,2.253 ||
trikarmakalibhirdevi mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
ghore yuddhe mahāraṇye digbhrame śatrusaṃkaṭe || 1,2.254 ||
nadīprataraṇe devi coravyāghrādisaṅkaṭe |
japetstotramidaṃ devi dhyātvā śrīrasabhairavam || 1,2.255 ||
iti stutvā sūtarājaṃ sarvaṃ karma samarpayet |
raso dātā raso bhoktā rasaḥ kartā ca kāraṇam || 1,2.256 ||
raso hotā ca havyaṃ ca sarvavyāpī rasaḥ sadā |
anenaiva ca mantreṇa rasendrāya samarpayet || 1,2.257 ||
tato nyāsaṃ ca mūlena kuryāddhyātvā ca pūrvavat |
saṃhāramudrāṃ kṛtvātha vahennadyāṃ raseśvaram || 1,2.258 ||
hṛtpadme sthāpayeddevaṃ sāṅgāvaraṇaśaktikam |
śivo'hamiti sadbhāvaṃ cintayedrasadeśikaḥ || 1,2.259 ||
pūjāsthānaṃ ca saṃmārjya prokṣayedudakena ca |
tato homālayaṃ prāpya samācamya ca deśikaḥ || 1,2.260 ||
śālāyāmagnidigbhāge yonikuṇḍe trimekhalam |
darbhaiḥ prāgudagagraiśca paristīrṇaṃ yathāvidhi || 1,2.261 ||
nyāsaṃ mūlena saṃkalpya prāṇāyāmaṃ vidhāya ca |
pañcabhūsaṃskṛtaṃ kṛtvā svastriyā vā dvijena vā || 1,2.262 ||
ānīya vahniṃ nikṣipya kuṇḍe prajvālayettataḥ |
vahniṃ piṅgajaṭājūṭaṃ trinetraṃ śuklavāsasam || 1,2.263 ||
raktāmbhojasthitaṃ devaṃ mālyābharaṇabhūṣitam |
saptahastaṃ catuḥśṛṅgaṃ dviśīrṣaṃ ca tripādakam || 1,2.264 ||
tridhā baddhaṃ ca ṛṣabhaṃ śaktyādyāyudhadhāriṇam |
dhyātvā tasyārghyapādyādi kuryātsarvopacārakam || 1,2.265 ||
gandhākṣatasupuṣpaiśca pūjayejjātavedasam |
tasminnāvāhayeddevaṃ saṃcintya rasabhairavam || 1,2.266 ||
nyāsaṃ devasya kurvīta mūlamantreṇa homayet |
tilājyavrīhibhirmantraṃ śatamaṣṭottaraṃ priye || 1,2.267 ||
aṣṭāviṃśati vā kuryātmūlātpūrvāhutiṃ hunet |
prāṇāyāmaṃ tato nyāsaṃ kṛtvā ca hṛdi dhāraṇam |
evaṃ nityārcanavidhiḥ proktastava surārcite || 1,2.268 ||


āk, 1, 3
<rasadīkṣākramaḥ>
atha dīkṣāṃ pravakṣyāmi sarvasiddhipradāyinīm |
sā ca pañcavidhā proktā samayādivibhedataḥ || 1,3.1 ||
samayā prathamā dīkṣā sādhakākhyā dvitīyakā |
nirvāṇākhyā tṛtīyā ca caturthyācāryasaṃjñakā || 1,3.2 ||
pañcamī siddhasaṃjñā ca prāyaśo durlabhā nṛṇām |
pūrvoktalakṣaṇopetaḥ śiṣyo bhaktinataḥ śuciḥ || 1,3.3 ||
guruṃ praṇamya cāṣṭāṅgaṃ stutvā ca bahudhā tataḥ |
anugṛhṇīṣva me dīkṣāmiti vijñāpayedgurum || 1,3.4 ||
dīkṣāṃ ca vidhivatprāpya śiṣyaḥ sarvārthadṛgbhavet |
<dīkṣāyogyāḥ śubhatithyādayaḥ>
aśvinīmūlarevatyo mṛgaḥ puṇyaḥ punarvasuḥ || 1,3.5 ||
rohiṇī śravaṇo maitraṃ hastaḥ syāduttarātrayam |
maghāśvinī śreṣṭhatamā dīkṣākarmaṇi tārakāḥ || 1,3.6 ||
tṛtīyā pañcamī caiva saptamī ca caturdaśī |
aṣṭamī paurṇamāsī ca daśamī ca trayodaśī || 1,3.7 ||
etāstu tithayo mukhyāḥ saumyendugurubhārgavāḥ |
āditya iti vāreṣu rasadīkṣā suśobhanā || 1,3.8 ||
<samayādīkṣākramaḥ>
śuklapakṣe suyoge ca karaviṣṭivivarjite |
candratārābalopetaviṣuvāyanasaṃkrame || 1,3.9 ||
grahaṇe śivarātre ca janmarkṣe sumuhūrtake |
śubhagrahe siddhayoge dadyāddīkṣāṃ gurūttamaḥ || 1,3.10 ||
rasaśālāṃ praviśyātha sarvopakaraṇojjvalām |
puṣpamālāsamākīrṇāmuttoraṇapatākinīm || 1,3.11 ||
nityapūjāgnikāryaṃ ca kṛtvā dīkṣākramaṃ bhajet |
devasya dakṣiṇe pārśve vistare taṇḍulān śritān || 1,3.12 ||
caturaśraṃ ca tanmadhye kṣipetprasthaṃ sutaṇḍulam |
tadūrdhvaṃ vinyasetkumbhaṃ sitasūtreṇa veṣṭitam || 1,3.13 ||
vāsobhyāṃ veṣṭitaṃ sūtapañcaratnasamanvitam |
pañcamṛtpallavatvagbhiḥ pāvitaṃ jalapūritam || 1,3.14 ||
dūrvāgandhottamatilahemadarbhākṣatānvitam |
śivasaṃjñamidaṃ kumbhaṃ śivarūpaṃ vicintayet || 1,3.15 ||
tadvāmabhāge saṃsthāpya vardhanīsaṃjñakaṃ ghaṭam |
śaktirūpaṃ ca pūrvoktasarvadravyasamanvitam || 1,3.16 ||
paritaḥ sarvatobhadraṃ śrīgaurītilakaṃ likhet |
nandyāvartaṃ svastikaṃ ca pañcavarṇavirājitam || 1,3.17 ||
prakṣālya pādau pāṇī ca samācamya gurūttamaḥ |
nijāsanaṃ samāsādya prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ tathā || 1,3.18 ||
śiṣyasyāsyāyurārogyasaṃpatsaṃtānavṛddhaye |
muktaye cāṣṭasiddhyai ca saṃkalpyābhyarcayecchivam || 1,3.19 ||
gandhapuṣpākṣairdhūpairdīpairnaivedyadarśanaiḥ |
śivakumbhaṃ vardhanīṃ ca pūjayetsopacārakam || 1,3.20 ||
rasāṅkuśīṃ mūlamantraṃ prajapecca sahasrakam |
taddaśāṃśaṃ hunetkuṇḍe trikoṇe lakṣaṇānvite || 1,3.21 ||
phalaṃ trimadhusaṃyuktaṃ tato gandhottamānvitam |
kramādāvṛtidevāṃśca caturthyāṃ svasvanāmakam || 1,3.22 ||
svāhāntaṃ praṇavādiṃ ca samuccārya huneddhaviḥ |
śivatrayaṃ purā devi tataḥ siddhatrayaṃ yajet || 1,3.23 ||
tato gurutrayaṃ devi kulavṛddhān gurūttamaḥ |
yoginīṃ pūjayitvā tu labdhānujño gurustataḥ || 1,3.24 ||
sa śaktimānandaśivamūrtimārādhayāmi ca |
tathā parānandaśivamūrtimārādhayāmi ca || 1,3.25 ||
evaṃ trisiddhamūrtiṃ ca śrībījasahitaṃ yajet |
evaṃ trigurumūrtiṃ ca mārabījayutaṃ yajet || 1,3.26 ||
tattvatrayaṃ gṛhītvā ca punarnyāsaṃ samācaret |
svayaṃ śivatanurbhūtvā śivo'hamiti bhāvayet || 1,3.27 ||
hṛṣṭaṃ śaktiyutaṃ śiṣyaṃ śivadraṣṭā vilokayet |
svavāmapārśve saṃsthāpya tanmūrdhani vinikṣipet || 1,3.28 ||
mūlābhimantritaṃ bhasma tannetraṃ vastrarodhitam |
kṛtvā tanmūrdhni saṃprokṣya śivavardhanivāriṇā || 1,3.29 ||
tridhā svadakṣiṇe haste śivaṃ sāvaraṇaṃ yajet |
vijñeyaḥ śivahasto'yaṃ bhavapāśanikṛntanaḥ || 1,3.30 ||
aṅkuśīmūlamantraṃ ca trivāraṃ ca samuccaret |
śiṣyadakṣiṇakarṇe ca kuryānmantropadeśakam || 1,3.31 ||
pūrvaṃ gaṇapatermantraṃ kṣetrapālamanuṃ tataḥ |
ṛṣistu gaṇakaśchando nyṛcidgāyatrikā smṛtam || 1,3.32 ||
devo gaṇapatirjñeyo nyāsaṃ bījākṣareṇa ca |
ādivargatṛtīyārṇaṃ sārdhacandraṃ gaṇākhyakam || 1,3.33 ||
patiṃ caturthyā saṃyuktaṃ namo'ntaṃ manumuccaret |
pāśāṅkuśaṃ modakaṃ ca bibhrāṇaṃ svavipāṇakam || 1,3.34 ||
gajānanaṃ pravālābhaṃ makuṭendukalādharam |
tundilaṃ gaṇanāthaṃ ca dhyātvā lakṣaṃ japenmanum || 1,3.35 ||
daśāṃśaṃ tarpaṇaṃ homaṃ saguḍaṃ satilaṃ hunet |
kaṣabinduyutaṃ kṣetrapālaṃ ca sacaturthikam || 1,3.36 ||
namo'ntaṃ manumuccārya nyāsaṃ bījākṣareṇa ca |
chandaḥ paṅktirmunirjñeyo bhṛgurdevaśca bhairavaḥ || 1,3.37 ||
caturbhujaṃ śūlapātravaradābhayaśobhitam |
kālāmbudanibhaṃ nāgabhūṣaṇaṃ kiṅkiṇīsrajam || 1,3.38 ||
trinetraṃ vakradaṃṣṭraṃ ca dhyātvā lakṣaṃ japenmanum |
daśāṃśaṃ tarpaṇaṃ homaṃ pāyasaṃ trimadhuplutam || 1,3.39 ||
hunediti samādiśya sāmayācārikaṃ bhajet |
devatāparicaryāṃ ca śuśrūṣāṃ ca guroḥ kuru || 1,3.40 ||
atandrito japaṃ kuryātśāstraśravaṇamādarāt |
pūrvoktācāramārgeṇa nirato bhava saṃtatam || 1,3.41 ||
prahvaḥ kṛtāñjalirbhūtvā kariṣyāmi tathā prabho |
dhanyo'haṃ kṛtakṛtyo'smi śrīnātha tvatprasādataḥ || 1,3.42 ||
iti vijñāpayecchiṣyaḥ samayācārabṛṃhitaḥ |
prātastarāṃ samutthāpya gurūṃśca śirasi smaret || 1,3.43 ||
nivedyāvaśyakaṃ karma snātaḥ saṃdhyāṃ samācaret |
nadyāstīrthe taṭāke vā puṣkariṇyāṃ śucau jale || 1,3.44 ||
upaspṛśya ca saṃkalpya nityatarpaṇamācaret |
ādau śivatrayaṃ vighnanāthaṃ kṣetrādhipaṃ harim || 1,3.45 ||
brahmāṇaṃ bhāskarādīṃśca grahān durgāṃ ca mātṛkāḥ |
bhairavānasitāṅgādīn tataḥ siddhatrayaṃ vadet || 1,3.46 ||
ādināthaṃ mīnanāthaṃ gorakṣaṃ koṅkaṇeśvaram |
jālandhreśaṃ kandhanīśamoḍḍīśaṃ ciñciṇīśvaram || 1,3.47 ||
cauraṅgimetānnāthākhyānnava saṃtarpayettataḥ |
cauraṅgiṃ carpaṭiṃ ghoḍācūliṃ rāmadvayaṃ tataḥ || 1,3.48 ||
bholagovindasiddhaṃ ca vyāḍiṃ nāgārjunaṃ tathā |
koraṇḍaṃ śūrpakarṇaṃ ca muktāyīṃ revaṇaṃ tathā || 1,3.49 ||
siddhaṃ kukkurapādaṃ ca śūrpapādaṃ kaṇairikam |
siddhaṃ kiṅkiṇikākhyaṃ ca siddhān ṣoḍaśa tarpayet || 1,3.50 ||
tato gurutrayaṃ tarpya mātaraṃ pitaraṃ gurum |
ācāryaṃ bhrātaraṃ mitraṃ tarpya cānyānkulodbhavān || 1,3.51 ||
tarpayedvidhinācamya devatārādhanaṃ tataḥ |
nadyāstīre taṭāke vā goṣṭhe devālaye tathā || 1,3.52 ||
śucisthale vā saṃstīrya saikataṃ vartulākṛtim |
āvāhayeddinādhīśaṃ pāṭalaṃ śivarūpiṇam || 1,3.53 ||
dvinetrapadmayugaladhāriṇaṃ dvibhujaṃ param |
raktasragambarālepabhūṣāpadmāsanojjvalam || 1,3.54 ||
dhyātvārcayedraktapuṣpaistato vighneśabhairavau |
dhyātvā pūrvoktavattau dvau raktapuṣpaiḥ samarcayet || 1,3.55 ||
aṣṭāṅgadaṇḍapraṇatiṃ kuryātdvādaśavārakam |
punaḥ saṃsthāpayetsvānte sevāyai svagurorvrajet || 1,3.56 ||
ityuktā samayā dīkṣā sādhakā kathyate'dhunā |
<sādhakadīkṣā>
samayācāranirataṃ sacchiṣyaṃ pakvamānasam || 1,3.57 ||
dīkṣayetsudine lagne dīkṣayā sādhakākhyayā |
kuryānnaimittikīṃ pūjāṃ śiṣyamāvāhayedguruḥ || 1,3.58 ||
svayaṃ śivatanuḥ śiṣyaṃ gaurīgarbhagataṃ smaret |
bhūmau vibhūtiṃ saṃstīrya tasminkoṇatrayaṃ likhet || 1,3.59 ||
tatropaveśya śiṣyaṃ ca bandhayitvā vilocane |
garbhādhānaṃ puṃsavanaṃ sīmantonnayanaṃ tataḥ || 1,3.60 ||
jātakarma ca dīkṣāṅgaṃ nāmadheyaṃ ca kalpayet |
vratakarmaṇi samprokṣya mūlamantreṇa bhasmanā || 1,3.61 ||
gaurīputraṃ bhāvayitvā tasya dīkṣāṃ prakalpayet |
śivadṛṣṭyā vilokyāmuṃ śivabhasma ca mūrdhani || 1,3.62 ||
nidhāya mūlamantraṃ ca pūjayeddaśavārakam |
tasya dakṣiṇakarṇe ca upadiśyāddaśākṣaram || 1,3.63 ||
<rasagāyatrī>
rasendrabhairavaṃ mantraṃ gāyatrīṃ rasasaṃjñikām |
sa binduhutabhugbījaṃ rasendrasya padaṃ tataḥ || 1,3.64 ||
bhairavaṃ ca caturthyantaṃ karmāstraṃ ca daśāṃśakam |
vahnibījaṃ dīrghayuktaṃ karanyāsaṃ samācaret || 1,3.65 ||
aghorākhya ṛṣiśchando virāḍdevo maheśvaraḥ |
pūrvasevājapaṃ lakṣaṃ daśāṃśaṃ homatarpaṇam || 1,3.66 ||
tilājyavrīhibhiḥ kuryāddhomaṃ niścalamānasaḥ |
pūrvoktāṃ rasagāyatrīṃ japellakṣaṃ ca tarpaṇam || 1,3.67 ||
huneddaśāṃśamājyena satilavrīhibhiḥ priye |
dadyācchiṣyāyākṣamālāṃ rasaliṅgaṃ rasāgamam || 1,3.68 ||
pūrvoktācāravān bhūtvā rasaliṅgaṃ samarcayet |
svaśāstrārthāvagamanaṃ kuryātsūtendrasaṃskṛtim || 1,3.69 ||
gurusevārataṃ nityaṃ śiṣyamājñāpayediti |
śiṣyo baddhāñjalirnamrastavājñāmācarāmyaham || 1,3.70 ||
ityuktā sādhakā dīkṣā nirvāṇākhyā praśasyate |
<nirvāṇadīkṣā>
kṛtanityakriyaḥ pūrvaṃ kṛtanaimittikārcanaḥ || 1,3.71 ||
pūrvavacchivakumbhādi vardhanīṃ sthāpayetkramāt |
svavāme bhasma saṃstīrya ṣaṭkoṇaṃ bhūgṛhaṃ likhet || 1,3.72 ||
śiṣyaṃ saṃveśya kumbhasthajalaiḥ saṃprokṣayedguruḥ |
mūlābhimantritaṃ bhasma tasya mūrdhni nikṣipet || 1,3.73 ||
gandhapuṣpākṣataṃ dadyātbadhnīyāddakṣiṇe kare |
kaṅkaṇaṃ caraṇāṅguṣṭhabrahmarandhrāntamānakam || 1,3.74 ||
sūtraṃ tanmūlamantreṇa mantritaṃ surabhīkṛtam |
śivahastaṃ ca tanmūrdhni nidhāya gurusattamaḥ || 1,3.75 ||
rasāṃkuśāṃ dvādaśārṇāmupadiśyānmanuṃ param |
rasabhairavadaivatyaṃ dadyācchiṣyāya deśikaḥ || 1,3.76 ||
ṛṣiśchando devatā ca nyāsaṃ pūrvoktavatpriye |
tarpaṇaṃ cātha homaṃ ca dhyānaṃ kuryācca pūrvavat || 1,3.77 ||
utthāya ca guruṃ natvā bahuśaḥ stotramuccaret |
guruḥ śiṣyāya vai dadyādvyāghracarmāsanaṃ ca yatra || 1,3.78 ||
bhastrikāṃ vastrasampūrṇāṃ dadyādbhaiṣajyabhastrikām |
rasāgamaṃ pāṭhayitvā bhava śiṣya cikitsakaḥ || 1,3.79 ||
rudrarūpī bhavān jātaḥ sarvabhūtahito bhava |
ityādiśedguruḥ śiṣyaṃ śiṣyo hṛṣṭamanāstataḥ || 1,3.80 ||
kṛtakṛtyo'smi pūto'smi tīrṇasaṃsārasāgaraḥ |
iti vijñāpayecchiṣyaḥ stutvā ca bahudhā gurum || 1,3.81 ||
proktā nirvāṇadīkṣeyamathācāryābhiṣecanam |
<ācāryadīkṣā>
nirvāṇadīkṣitasyāsya śiṣyasyāpi mahātmanaḥ || 1,3.82 ||
ācāryadīkṣāṃ kurvīta yathāvadgurusattamaḥ |
yathā nirvāṇadīkṣāyāstathā sarvaṃ prakalpayet || 1,3.83 ||
viśeṣācchivavardhanyoḥ purataḥ sthāpayedghaṭam |
rūpyagandhādilohāśādivyauṣadhagaṇānvitam || 1,3.84 ||
śivakumbhavadanyacca sarvamasmin vinikṣipet |
gurukumbhaṃ japetspṛṣṭvā mūlamaṣṭottaraṃ śatam || 1,3.85 ||
gurukumbhamimaṃ viddhi gurumantreṇa pūjayet |
āvāhanāsanārghyādyairupacāraiśca ṣoḍaśaiḥ || 1,3.86 ||
śivādipaṅktiṃ tatraiva pūjayedguruṇā saha |
yathāpūrvaṃ samāsīnaṃ śiṣyaṃ caikāgramānasam || 1,3.87 ||
pūrvavacchivavardhanyorambunā pariṣecayet |
pūrvavacchivahastaṃ ca mūlamantropadeśakam || 1,3.88 ||
kṛtvā taduttamāṅge ca gurupaṅktiṃ guruṃ tathā |
āvāhanādyaiḥ sampūjya gandhasragdīpakaiḥ || 1,3.89 ||
phalagandhottamopetairnaivedyaiḥ pūjayetkramāt |
guruḥ kumbhajalaiḥ kuryātprokṣaṇaṃ pañcapallavaiḥ || 1,3.90 ||
mūlamantreṇa śatadhā śivapaṅktyā trisaptadhā |
tathaiva siddhapaṅktyā ca gurupaṅktyā tathaiva ca || 1,3.91 ||
śiṣyāya gurubhaktāya śaktiyuktāya dhīmate |
hārakeyūrakaṭakamudrikāmakuṭāni ca || 1,3.92 ||
vāhanāsanasacchatracāmaravyajanāni ca |
maṇikuṇḍalayugmaṃ ca bhūtyāḍhyaṃ pātramakṣayam || 1,3.93 ||
saśirastrāṇamuṣṇīṣaṃ paṭṭakūrpāsamuttamam |
yogapaṭṭaṃ yogadaṇḍaṃ tathaivoḍyāṇabandhanam || 1,3.94 ||
dadyāttasmai tathaivājñāṃ tvamācāryo'si saṃprati |
īśvaro'syadya dhanyo'si sarveṣāṃ deśiko bhava || 1,3.95 ||
rasāgamānāṃ divyānāṃ vyākhyātā bhava tattvavit |
ityādiśedguruḥ śiṣyaṃ praṇataṃ guruvatsalam || 1,3.96 ||
iti cācāryadīkṣeyaṃ siddhadīkṣādya kathyate |
<siddhadīkṣā>
navaṃ saptarātraṃ vā pañcarātraṃ trirātrakam || 1,3.97 ||
vīrāḥ śaktiyutāḥ pūjyāḥ ekaviṃśati cānvaham |
tatsaṃkhyā yoginaḥ pūjyāḥ kumārāḥ kanyakāḥ striyaḥ || 1,3.98 ||
gandhapuṣpākṣataiḥ puṣpaiḥ phalagandhottamārpaṇaiḥ |
toṣayitvā pradadyācca vastrābharaṇadakṣiṇāḥ || 1,3.99 ||
athāsanasamāsīnaṃ śiṣyaṃ bhaktiyutaṃ śucim |
spṛṣṭvā karābhyāṃ tacchīrṣaṃ dakṣiṇe śravaṇe manum || 1,3.100 ||
vahniṃ sabījaṃ śītāṃśuṃ savisargaṃ ca dīkṣayet |
mūlādhārātsamudbhūtaṃ rephaṃ vahniśikhopamam || 1,3.101 ||
ṣaḍādhārāmbujaṃ tīrtvā brahmarandhrāntagaṃ smaret |
brahmarandhrāddravībhūtaṃ sakāraṃ somarūpiṇam || 1,3.102 ||
āplāvayantaṃ sarvāṅgaṃ siddhaḥ saṃcintayenmanum |
ucchvāsaniśvāsabhavaṃ saṃhārasthitikāraṇam || 1,3.103 ||
jñātvetthaṃ manasā nityaṃ sarvamantrātmakaṃ sadā |
sarvadevamayaṃ puṇyaṃ sarvasiddhipradāyakam || 1,3.104 ||
jīvato muktidaṃ śuddhaṃ śivaśaktyātmakaṃ param |
rasamantramimaṃ viddhi cāgnīṣomātmakaṃ bhaja || 1,3.105 ||
ekaviṃśatisāhasraṣaṭśatādhikasaṃkhyakam |
pravartitaṃ divārātraṃ manastatra nidhehi ca || 1,3.106 ||
mantrasyāsya ṛṣirjīvaśchandetyuktāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
raso devastu raṃ bījaṃ saṃ śaktistu prakīrtitaḥ || 1,3.107 ||
rephadīrghayutaṃ kuryātkarāṅganyāsamācaret |
dhyāyeddhṛdambuje devi koṭisūryendusannibham || 1,3.108 ||
śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ rasaṃ saṃsārapāradam |
evaṃ niṣkalarūpaṃ taṃ dhyātvā tanmayatāṃ vrajet || 1,3.109 ||
<niṣkalarasastotram>
tvaṃ rasastvaṃ śivastvaṃ hi śaktistvaṃ bhairaveśvaraḥ |
tvaṃ brahmā tvaṃ hi rudraśca sūryastvaṃ śītadīdhitiḥ || 1,3.110 ||
tvaṃ bhūstvaṃ salilastvaṃ ca vahnistvaṃ ca sadāgatiḥ |
tvaṃ vyoma tvaṃ parākāśaḥ tvaṃ jīvastvaṃ parā gatiḥ || 1,3.111 ||
tvaṃ siddhastvaṃ prabuddhastvaṃ sugandhastvaṃ ca manmathaḥ |
tvaṃ lokapālastvaṃ tārā mātṛkāstvaṃ gajānanaḥ || 1,3.112 ||
tvaṃ skandastvaṃ gurustvaṃ ca tvaṃ sarvajanakaḥ śuciḥ |
tvaṃ yajvā tvaṃ haristvaṃ ca tretāgnistvaṃ ca ṛtvijaḥ || 1,3.113 ||
yena tvamarcyase tena pūjitāḥ sarvadevatāḥ |
tvayi tuṣṭe ca saṃtuṣṭā brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ || 1,3.114 ||
tvatpādodakapānena sarvatīrthaphalaṃ labhet |
yastvāṃ paśyati sadbhaktyā tamālokayate śivaḥ || 1,3.115 ||
yastvāṃ saṃkīrtayettasya sarvamantraphalaṃ bhavet |
yatra tvaṃ kṣaṇamātre ca puṇyakṣetraṃ taducyate || 1,3.116 ||
tvaṃ yaṃ paśyati siddhendra sa brahmā viṣṇurīśvaraḥ |
yatra bhuṅkṣe'nnakavalaṃ tatra trailokyamati hi || 1,3.117 ||
yena tvaṃ dhriyase nātha tvayāyaṃ striyatekṣaṇam |
sa eva citsadānandaḥ paramātmā dhruvaṃ bhavet || 1,3.118 ||
evaṃ niścitacittasya siddhasya tava yoginaḥ |
varṇāśramasadācārāḥ kṛtyākṛtyavivekataḥ || 1,3.119 ||
pāpaṃ puṇyaṃ sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ śītamuṣṇaṃ priyāpriye |
snānāsnānaṃ hānivṛddhī śuddhāśuddhaṃ bhayābhayam || 1,3.120 ||
grāhyāgrāhyaṃ kṣudhā tṛṣṇā na kiṃcidiha vidyate |
svecchāhāravihāraśca sukhī saṃhṛṣṭamānasaḥ || 1,3.121 ||
nirmamaḥ sarvakāryeṣu kalatrādiṣu bandhuṣu |
evamācāryavacanamaṅgīkṛtya ca dīkṣitaḥ || 1,3.122 ||
adya prabhṛti vandyastvaṃ mā kuru praṇatiṃ kvacit |
matsamāno'si siddho'si jīvanmukto'si saṃprati || 1,3.123 ||
ityuktvācāryavaryo'pi siddhamāliṅgya ca kṣaṇam |
siddhadīkṣeyamākhyātā śivasāyujyadāyinī || 1,3.124 ||
durlabhā sarvatantreṣu tava prītyā prakāśitā |
devītthameva mantavyaṃ durlabhaṃ samayeṣu ca || 1,3.125 ||


āk, 1, 4
<rasasaṃkārāḥ>
śrībhairavī |
rasasaṃskāramīśa tvaṃ yathāvatkathayasva me |
śrībhairavaḥ |
aṣṭādaśa syuḥ saṃskārā rasasya parameśvari || 1,4.1 ||
tānsiddhasādhakābhyarcye yathāvatkathayāmi te |
athādau svedanaṃ karma dvitīyaṃ mardanaṃ priye || 1,4.2 ||
mūrchā tṛtīyamutthānaṃ caturthaṃ pātanaṃ śive |
pañcamaṃ rodhanaṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ niyāmaṃ saptamaṃ smṛtam || 1,4.3 ||
dīpanaṃ cāṣṭamaṃ devi navamaṃ cānuvāsanam |
daśamaṃ cāraṇaṃ devi jāraṇaṃ rudrasaṃkhyakam || 1,4.4 ||
garbhadrutirdvādaśī syātbāhyadrutistrayodaśī |
caturdaśaṃ syādrāgākhyaṃ sāraṇā daśapañcamī || 1,4.5 ||
anusāraṇā ṣoḍaśī ca vikhyātā pratisāraṇā |
saptadaśī cāṣṭadaśaṃ vedhanaṃ dehalohayoḥ || 1,4.6 ||
<rasasaṃskāraprakāraḥ>
atha vakṣyāmi saṃskārān rasarājasya pārvati |
liṅgasya dakṣiṇe bhāge likhetṣaṭkoṇamaṇḍale || 1,4.7 ||
tadbahiścāṣṭapatraṃ ca kamalaṃ caturaśrakam |
digdvāraśobhitaṃ tasya karṇikāyāṃ nyasecchive || 1,4.8 ||
tāmrajaṃ kāntajaṃ vāpi khalvaṃ tu svarṇarekhitam |
tanmadhye rasarājaṃ tu palānāṃ śatamātrakam || 1,4.9 ||
tadardhaṃ vā tadardhaṃ vā kṣiptvā bhaktyā prapūjayet |
sādhako rasarājasya tataḥ saṃskāramācaret || 1,4.10 ||
<svedanam, dhānyāmlasandhānaprakāraḥ>
yavagodhūmakalamamāṣamudgacaṇādibhiḥ |
śyāmākakodravādyaiśca nānādhānyaiśca nistuṣaiḥ || 1,4.11 ||
dhānyaṃ caturguṇajale mṛdghaṭe nikṣipetpriye |
prāpnoti yāvadamlatvaṃ tāvadyatnena rakṣayet || 1,4.12 ||
tanmadhye haṃsapādīṃ ca mīnākṣīṃ dvipunarnavām |
citrakaṃ bhṛṅgarājaṃ ca viṣṇukrāntāṃ śatāvarīm || 1,4.13 ||
sarpākṣīṃ girikarṇīṃ ca muṇḍīṃ ca sahadevikām |
triphalāṃ khaṇḍaśaḥ kṛtvā sarvānetānvinikṣipet || 1,4.14 ||
dhānyasya ca caturthāṃśān dhānyāmlamidamuttamam |
svedanādiṣu kāryeṣu pāradasya viśiṣyate || 1,4.15 ||
ātapte kāntaje khalve rasarājaṃ vinikṣipet |
cūrṇaṃ ca gṛhadhūmaṃ ca citrakaṃ triphalāṃ guḍam || 1,4.16 ||
dagdhorṇāmiṣṭakāṃ kanyāṃ lavaṇaṃ bṛhatīdvayam |
māgadhīṃ rājikāṃ caiva vandhyāṃ karkoṭakīṃ tathā || 1,4.17 ||
etānkalāṃśānsūtasya tasminkṣiptvā vimardayet |
dattvā dattvātha dhānyāmlamevaṃ kṛtvā dinatrayam || 1,4.18 ||
samyaksoṣṇāranālena rasaṃ prakṣālayetpriye |
paṭuṃ rājīṃ trikaṭukaṃ mūlakaṃ citrakaṃ varām || 1,4.19 ||
punarnavāṃ meṣaśṛṅgīṃ meghanādaṃ durālabhām |
ārdrakaṃ rajanīṃ nāgabalāṃ ca navasārakam || 1,4.20 ||
samaṃ dhānyāmlakaiḥ piṣṭvā ślakṣṇaṃ vastre pralepayet |
yāvadaṅgulimātraṃ ca tasminpūrvarasaṃ kṣipet || 1,4.21 ||
tadbaddhvā tāmraje pātre kṣiptvā dhānyāmlapūrite |
ghaṭe dolākhyayantre ca svedayettamaharniśam || 1,4.22 ||
tamuddhṛtya punaḥ soṣṇairāranālaiḥ śarāvake |
prakṣālayetsūtarājamevaṃ kuryāttrisaptadhā || 1,4.23 ||
prasveditaṃ sūtarājaṃ tataḥ saṃmardayetpriye |
<mardanam>
athāto mardanaṃ karma vakṣyāmi śṛṇu bhairavi || 1,4.24 ||
cūrṇādipūrvavaddravyaṃ viśālāṃ rājavṛkṣakam |
aṅkolaṃ kṛṣṇadhuttūraṃ trikaṭuṃ ca samaṃ samam || 1,4.25 ||
sarvaṃ sūtakalāṃśaṃ ca taptakhalve rasaṃ kṣipet |
mardayetpūrvadhānyāmlairdivārātraṃ punaśca tam || 1,4.26 ||
kṣālayenmṛṇmaye pātre tataḥ soṣṇāranālakaiḥ |
mardanaṃ kṣālanaṃ caivamekaviṃśativāsaram || 1,4.27 ||
evaṃ vimarditaṃ sūtaṃ samādāyātha mūrchayet |
abhrajīrṇo'thavā bījajīrṇaḥ sūto'pi mardyate || 1,4.28 ||
cūrṇādipūrvadravyaiśca rāgān gṛhṇāti nirmalaḥ |
<mūrcchā>
atha mūrchāṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu tvaṃ sāvadhānataḥ || 1,4.29 ||
rājikā meṣaśṛṅgī ca citrakaṃ ca phalatrayam |
kṣīrakandaṃ sūraṇaṃ ca sarpākṣī kākamācikā || 1,4.30 ||
nīlā vacā balā kanyā kṛṣṇonmattastathākulī |
śvetāparājitāṅkolaṃ gojihvā garuḍī tathā || 1,4.31 ||
eteṣāṃ svarasairmardyo ravikṣīreṇa pāradaḥ |
vyastānāṃ vā samastānāṃ rasaireṣāṃ dināvadhi || 1,4.32 ||
mardayettaptakhalve ca mūṣāyāṃ taṃ rasaṃ kṣipet |
nirudhya bhūdhare yantre vipacettaṃ punaḥ priye || 1,4.33 ||
rājikādyauṣadhabhavai rasaistriḥ saptavāsaram |
mardanaṃ bhūdhare pākaṃ kuryātsaṃmūrchito rasaḥ || 1,4.34 ||
bhavettadutthānavidhiṃ prakurvīta surārcite |
<utthāpanavidhiḥ>
evaṃ saṃmūrchitaṃ sūtaṃ kṣālayetkāṃjikena ca || 1,4.35 ||
soṣṇena vāriṇā vāpi sūtamutthāpayetpriye |
pātanāyantrayoge vā rasasyotthāpanaṃ bhavet || 1,4.36 ||
<pātanam>
pātanaṃ trividhaṃ proktaṃ rasarājasya pārvati |
ūrdhvapātamadhaḥpātaṃ tiryakpātanamucyate || 1,4.37 ||
<ūrdhvapātanavidhiḥ>
rasasya pātanaṃ vakṣye pāṭhā brāhmī ca citrakam |
śāṅgerī kākamācī ca maṇḍūkī girikarṇikā || 1,4.38 ||
kumārī ca jayā bhṛṅgī gojihvā śaṅkhapuṣpikā |
bhūpāṭalī ca nirguṇḍī kākajaṅghā śatāvarī || 1,4.39 ||
ārdrakaṃ devadālī ca tilaparṇī ca nīlikā |
āragvadhaḥ kṣīrakandamaṅkolo devadāru ca || 1,4.40 ||
eṣāṃ rasaiḥ samastānāṃ vyastānāṃ vā dinaṃ rasam |
mardayettaptakhalve tatśulbaṃ sūtacaturthakam || 1,4.41 ||
taṃ piṣṭvā pātayedyantre hyūrdhvapātanake dinam |
ūrdhvalagnaṃ rasaṃ śuddhaṃ tamādāyātha mardayet || 1,4.42 ||
pūrvoditauṣadharasaiḥ pūrvavatśulbasaṃyutam |
pātayenmardayedevaṃ saptadhaivaṃ punaḥ punaḥ || 1,4.43 ||
ūrdhvapātanasaṃśuddhaṃ pāradaṃ pātayedadhaḥ |
<adhaḥpātanavidhiḥ>
athādhaḥpātanaṃ vakṣye tryūṣaṇaṃ lavaṇaṃ varām || 1,4.44 ||
citrakaṃ rājikāṃ śigruṃ sarvaṃ sūtasamaṃ kṣipet |
mardayettaptakhalve ca rasaṃ dhānyāmlakena ca || 1,4.45 ||
tatkalkena lipedūrdhvaṃ bhāṇḍaṃ samyaknirodhayet |
ūrdhvabhāṇḍasya pṛṣṭhe tu dadyāllaghupuṭaṃ tataḥ || 1,4.46 ||
ityadhaḥpātanaṃ kuryātsaptavāraṃ punaḥ punaḥ |
adhaḥpātanaśuddhasya tiryakpātanamācaret || 1,4.47 ||
<tiryakpātanavidhiḥ>
vakṣyāmi tiryakpatanaṃ sūtaṃ dhānyābhrakaṃ samam |
dhānyāmlairmardayedyāmaṃ tiryakpātanayantrake || 1,4.48 ||
caṇḍāgninā pacedevaṃ saptavāraṃ punaḥ punaḥ |
evaṃ tridhā pātitaṃ ca tataḥ sūtaṃ nirodhayet || 1,4.49 ||
abhrakaṃ vātha gandhaṃ vā mākṣikaṃ vimalāmapi |
svarṇaṃ vā rajataṃ vāpi kāntaṃ vā tīkṣṇameva vā || 1,4.50 ||
pratyekaṃ śodhitaṃ deyaṃ tāmravatpādamātrakam |
pāṭhādikarasaiḥ kuryānmardanaṃ pātanaṃ kramāt || 1,4.51 ||
yadyaddravyānvitaḥ sūtastattaddravyaguṇapradaḥ |
pātane tāmrayogena nāgavaṅgau tyajedrasaḥ || 1,4.52 ||
athavā dīpikāyantre śuddhaḥ syātpātito rasaḥ |
<nirodhanam>
kadarthito bhavetsūtaḥ svedādyaiḥ pañcakarmabhiḥ || 1,4.53 ||
āpyāyanārthaṃ sūtasya karma kuryānnirodhanam |
atha karma nirodhākhyaṃ peṣayellavaṇaṃ jalaiḥ || 1,4.54 ||
sthālīmadhye rasaṃ kṣiptvā tadūrdhvaṃ lavaṇaṃ kṣipet |
kiṃcijjalaṃ ca nivapettaṃ śarāveṇa rodhayet || 1,4.55 ||
kuryātsamyaksandhilepaṃ tadūrdhvaṃ ca puṭellaghu |
evaṃ nirodhanaṃ karma vidadhyātsaptadhā priye || 1,4.56 ||
āpyāyito bhavetsūto'nena ṣaṇḍatvavarjitaḥ |
nirodhakarmasiddho'sau vīryavān suniyamyate || 1,4.57 ||
<niyāmanam>
atho niyāmanaṃ karma kathayāmi varānane |
yavaciñcā kṣīrakandaṃ sarpākṣī paṭu bhṛṅgarāṭ || 1,4.58 ||
vandhyā karkoṭakī nimbaḥ sarvaṃ dhānyāmlapeṣitam |
kṛtvāloḍyāranālena taddravaiḥ svedayeddinam || 1,4.59 ||
yantre niyāmake saptavāsaraṃ taṃ ca dīpayet |
<dīpanam>
athāto dīpanaṃ karma vadāmi tava pārvati || 1,4.60 ||
svarṇapuṣpī ca marīcaṃ trikṣāraṃ pañcapuṣpikā |
bhūkhagaḥ pañcalavaṇaṃ rājikā śigrumūlakam || 1,4.61 ||
kāsīsaṃ vijayā kanyā pātālagaruḍī kaṇā |
kṣīrakandaṃ ca karkoṭī girikarṇī jayā madhu || 1,4.62 ||
citrakaṃ kākajaṅghā ca sarvamamlagaṇena ca |
kalkayetpāradaṃ tena mardayettaddravairapi || 1,4.63 ||
ḍolāyantre pacedekadinamevaṃ ca saptadhā |
mardanaṃ pācanaṃ kuryātgrāsārthī dīpito rasaḥ || 1,4.64 ||
evaṃ pradīpitaṃ sūtaṃ śuddhaṃ tamanuvāsayet |
<anuvāsanam>
athānuvāsanaṃ karma mṛtpātre dīpitaṃ rasam || 1,4.65 ||
kṣiptvā jambīrajadrāvaistīvragharme'nuvāsayet |
evaṃ saptadinaṃ kuryāttataścāraṇamācaret || 1,4.66 ||
navabhiḥ svedanādyaiśca śuddhaḥ syātkarmabhiḥ priye |
pāradaḥ sarvarogaghnaḥ saptakañcukavarjitaḥ || 1,4.67 ||
śrībhairavī |
svedanādīni karmāṇi śrutāni vada śaṅkara |
tvatprasādādasaṃdehaṃ saṃśṛṇve cāraṇādikam || 1,4.68 ||
śrībhairavaḥ |
sādhu pṛṣṭaṃ mahābhāge śṛṇu taccāraṇādikam |
<cāraṇavidhiḥ>
athābhracāraṇaṃ karma vakṣyāmi parameśvari || 1,4.69 ||
samukhe vā nirmukhe vā rase vā vāsanāmukhe |
gaganaṃ cārayedgarbhapiṣṭigrāsakramaiḥ kramāt || 1,4.70 ||
<samukhacāraṇam>
atrādau kathyate devi samukhaṃ cāraṇaṃ sphuṭam |
samukho grasati grāsaṃ nirmukho grasanākṣamaḥ || 1,4.71 ||
tasmātparaṃ rasendrasya mukhīkaraṇamācaret |
<prathamo mukhīkaraṇaprakāraḥ>
iṣṭakāmadhyabhāge tu gambhīraṃ vartulaṃ samam || 1,4.72 ||
gartaṃ kṛtvā tatra sūtaṃ prakṣipedanuvāsitam |
nirundhyātsvacchavastreṇa rasasya daśamāṃśakam || 1,4.73 ||
gandhaṃ tadūrdhvaṃ nikṣipya śarāveṇa nirodhayet |
tatpṛṣṭhe'lpapuṭaṃ dadyātgandhe jīrṇe punaḥ punaḥ || 1,4.74 ||
kṣiptvā kṣiptvā śataguṇaṃ cārayetpātayediti |
caturguṇe sāndravastre gālayettaṃ raseśvaram || 1,4.75 ||
veṇau kṣiptvā nirudhyāsyaṃ pacedgomūtrapūrite |
bhāṇḍe trisaptadivasaṃ punastaṃ taptakhalvake || 1,4.76 ||
jambīramātuluṅgāmlavetasairbhūkhagaistryaham |
bhūnāgaiśca tryahaṃ mardyaṃ taṃ paścādiṣṭakodare || 1,4.77 ||
ekaikaṃ gartamādadyāddhānyābhraṃ gandhakaṃ tathā |
pratyekaṃ daśaniṣkaṃ ca yāmaṃ jambīramarditam || 1,4.78 ||
anena lepayedgartadvayamekeṣṭakāntare |
garte kṣiptvātha taṃ taṃ viṃśanniṣkaṃ tadūrdhvataḥ || 1,4.79 ||
aparāmiṣṭakāṃ dadyāttatsandhiṃ loṇamṛtsnayā |
dṛḍhaṃ liptvātha dīpāgnau pacetsaptadināvadhi || 1,4.80 ||
iṣṭakāṃ svāṅgaśītāṃ tāmavatārya haredrasam |
kiṭṭahīnaṃ punarapi kuryāditthaṃ trivārakam || 1,4.81 ||
evaṃ kṛte rasendrasya sukhaṃ syāddevasaṃjñakam |
<dvitīyo mukhīkaraṇaprakāraḥ>
punaranyaṃ pravakṣyāmi mukhīkaraṇamuttamam || 1,4.82 ||
rasendraṃ jīvibhūnāgasamaṃ saṃmardayettryaham |
tatkṣipetbhūlatāliptamūṣāyāṃ saṃniveśayet || 1,4.83 ||
tadūrdhvaṃ bhūlatākalkaṃ kṣiptvā ruddhvā viśoṣayet |
ārdragomayaliptāṃ tāṃ pādamagnāṃ niveśayet || 1,4.84 ||
tadgarte ca puṭaṃ deyaṃ tuṣakārīṣavahninā |
dinānte tatsamuddhṛtya pūrvavanmardayetpacet || 1,4.85 ||
triṃśadvāraṃ punaḥ kuryāditthaṃ vahnimukho rasaḥ |
grasate guhyasūto'yaṃ sarvasiddhiprado bhavet || 1,4.86 ||
<tṛtīyo mukhīkaraṇaprakāraḥ>
punaranyaṃ pravakṣyāmi mukhīkaraṇamuttamam |
gomūtrapūrite bhāṇḍe ḍolāyantre pacedrasam || 1,4.87 ||
triḥ saptavāraṃ samyakca randhritaṃ veṇunālake |
mukhaṃ bhavati sūtasya cāraṇārhaṃ varānane || 1,4.88 ||
<caturtho mukhīkaraṇaprakāraḥ>
iṣṭakādvayamadhye ca gartaṃ tu caturaṅgulam |
daśaniṣkaṃ śuddhagandhaṃ dhānyābhraṃ daśaniṣkakam || 1,4.89 ||
jambīranīraiḥ saṃmardya yāmaṃ kalkena tena ca |
gartadvayaṃ samāṃśena liptvā garte vinikṣipet || 1,4.90 ||
vāsitaṃ rasarājaṃ taṃ viṃśanniṣkaṃ tadūrdhvataḥ |
iṣṭakāmaparāṃ nyasya ślakṣṇamṛllavaṇairdṛḍham || 1,4.91 ||
lepayedatha dīpāgnimadhaḥ prajvālayetpriye |
anusyūtaṃ saptarātraṃ svāṅgaśītaṃ samuddharet || 1,4.92 ||
kiṭṭaṃ vihāya taṃ sūtaṃ pūrvavacca trivārakam |
kuryādevaṃ hi sūtasya mukhaṃ bhavati śobhanam || 1,4.93 ||
<nirmukhacāraṇāprakāraḥ>
divyauṣadhiprabhāvena rasaścarati nirmukhaḥ |
athavā vajravaikrāntasparśādabhrādikaṃ caret || 1,4.94 ||
pāradasya catuḥṣaṣṭiniṣkaṃ vaikrāntabhasma ca |
caturniṣkaṃ vajrabhasma niṣkaṃ divyauṣadhidravaiḥ || 1,4.95 ||
sarvaṃ saptadinaṃ mardya sa raso'bhrādikaṃ caret |
<vāsanāmukhacāraṇam>
gandhakaṃ jārayetsūte dolākhye pūrvabhāṣite || 1,4.96 ||
yantre ca ṣoḍaśaguṇaṃ rasaḥ syādvāsanāmukhaḥ |
<abhrakābhiṣekaḥ cāraṇīyābhrakasya saṃskāraḥ>
cāraṇārhābhrakasyāhamabhiṣekaṃ vadāmi te || 1,4.97 ||
<prathamaḥ prakāraḥ>
mṛtamabhraṃ tu rudhiraiḥ kṣārairjalakaṇairapi |
sṛṣṭitrayaistumburubhirmardayedamlavargakaiḥ || 1,4.98 ||
saptāhaṃ taccaretsūtaḥ samukho nirmukho'thavā |
<dvitīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
mṛtamabhraṃ ca kadalī mūlakaṃ ca śatāvarī || 1,4.99 ||
punarnavā meghanādo yavaciñcā ca śigrukaḥ |
sūraṇaṃ ca rasaireṣāṃ mardayedbhāvayetpriye || 1,4.100 ||
saptāhaṃ taccaretsūtaḥ samukho nirmukho'thavā |
<tṛtīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
śulvapātre tu lavaṇasarjiṭaṅkaṇabhūkhagān || 1,4.101 ||
kṣiptvāranālaṃ tridinaṃ kuryātparyuṣitaṃ yathā |
tasminnāgaṃ tu vidrāvya ḍhālayecchatadhā priye || 1,4.102 ||
raupyakarmaṇi vaṅgasya taddravairbhāvayedghanam |
saptāhaṃ taccaretsūtaḥ samukho nirmukho'thavā || 1,4.103 ||
<caturthaḥ prakāraḥ>
dhānyābhramarkakṣīreṇa mardayedekavāsaram |
nirudhya saṃpuṭe pacyātkapotākhye puṭe punaḥ || 1,4.104 ||
caturvāraṃ puṭedevaṃ mardayecca punastathā |
rambhādravaistridhā mardyaṃ mūlakasya dravaistridhā || 1,4.105 ||
kākamācī meghanādo matsyākṣī ca punarnavā |
apāmārgaścitrakaṃ ca vidārī bhṛṅgarāṇmuniḥ || 1,4.106 ||
caturvāraṃ pacedevaṃ mardayecca punastathā |
etairekaikadhā mardyaṃ puṭapākaṃ pṛthakpṛthak || 1,4.107 ||
tadabhrakaṃ bhāvayecca musalī yavaciñcikāḥ |
taṇḍulī kadalī śigrurvarī cārkaḥ punarnavā || 1,4.108 ||
ekaikaiśca dravairbhāvyamekaikaṃ divasaṃ pṛthak |
tadabhrakaṃ pāradendraścaratyeva surārcite || 1,4.109 ||
<pañcamaḥ prakāraḥ>
dhānyābhraṃ ravidugdhena mardayedyāmamātrakam |
tadabhraṃ saṃpuṭe ruddhvā kapotākhyapuṭe pacet || 1,4.110 ||
vārāṃścaturdaśaivaṃ syāttato rambhārkapīlukāḥ |
nāgavallī ca musalī kuberākṣī ca śigrukaḥ || 1,4.111 ||
tumbī tumbururaṅkolabalāpāmārgakāḥ priye |
gṛhītvaiṣāmekarasaṃ savyoṣaṃ ca sakāṃjikam || 1,4.112 ||
bhāṇḍamadhye vinikṣipya ḍolāyantre pacettryaham |
pūrvābhraṃ ca tatastasmātsamuddhṛtyātha sādhayet || 1,4.113 ||
ṭaṅkaṇaṃ tuvarī sindhukāsīsaṃ ca samaṃ samam |
abhrakasya daśāṃśaṃ tu sarvametadvimardayet || 1,4.114 ||
tadabhrakaṃ caretsūtaḥ samukho nirmukho'thavā |
<ṣaṣṭaḥ prakāraḥ>
muṇḍīdrave tu śatadhā drutaṃ nāgaṃ praḍhālayet || 1,4.115 ||
taddraveṇa ca dhānyābhraṃ mardayetsaṃpuṭe kṣipet |
ruddhvā pacetkapotākhye puṭe caivaṃ tu saptadhā || 1,4.116 ||
ārdrakasya dravairevaṃ saptadhā citrakadravaiḥ |
tatsamaṃ gandhakaṃ dattvā mardayedbhāvayetkramāt || 1,4.117 ||
prāguktamuṇḍīsvarasairbījapūrarasena ca |
stanyairekadinaṃ bhāvyaṃ tadabhraṃ ca caredrasaḥ || 1,4.118 ||
evaṃ sarvāṇi bījāni sattvaṃ cābhrakasya ca |
svarṇādisarvalohāni satvāni vividhāni ca || 1,4.119 ||
dvandvāni sarvaratnāni yadyatsyāccāraṇārhakam |
tattatpiṣṭvābhravatkāryaṃ tattaccarati pāradaḥ || 1,4.120 ||
<saptamaḥ prakāraḥ>
tilaparṇīrasaiḥ piṣṭvā dhānyābhraṃ puṭayettridhā |
saptadhā bhāvayedasya mardanena ca pāradaḥ || 1,4.121 ||
piṣṭatāṃ yāti sahasā gaganaṃ carati kṣaṇāt |
<aṣṭamaḥ prakāraḥ>
trikṣāraṃ pañcalavaṇaṃ kāṅkṣī kāsīsaṭaṅkaṇam || 1,4.122 ||
bhūkhagaṃ hemamākṣīkaṃ saurāṣṭrī vatsanābhakam |
tuvarī vetasāmlaṃ ca sarvametatsamāṃśakam || 1,4.123 ||
tatsamāṃśaṃ tu dhānyābhraṃ dhānyāmlairmardayeddinam |
arkakṣīraṃ bhṛṅgarājo meghanādaḥ punarnavā || 1,4.124 ||
mūlakaṃ citrakaṃ raṃbhā kākamācī śatāvarī |
apāmārgastālamūlī vidārī taṇḍulī muniḥ || 1,4.125 ||
mīnākṣī śigru bṛhatī kumārī yavaciñcikā |
balā tumbī kuberākṣī vyoṣaṃ tumburupīlunī || 1,4.126 ||
aṅkolaḥ phaṇivallī ca sarvametatsamāṃśakam |
saṃpeṣya kalkayeddevi dhānyāmle taccaturguṇe || 1,4.127 ||
drutaṃ nāgaṃ śataṃ vārānkṣipettasminpunaḥ punaḥ |
kalkadraveṇa pūrvābhraṃ yāmaṃ saṃmardayeddṛḍham || 1,4.128 ||
kapotākhye puṭe pacyādevaṃ viṃśativārakam |
tattulyaṃ gandhakaṃ dattvā pūrvoktairauṣadhadravaiḥ || 1,4.129 ||
sastanyairbījapūrotthairdravairbhāvyaṃ trivāsaram |
etadabhraṃ tu sūtasya cāraṇe paramaṃ hitam || 1,4.130 ||
dvaṃdvitaṃ vyomasatvaṃ ca bījāni vividhāni ca |
hemādisarvalohāni ratnāni vividhāni ca || 1,4.131 ||
dvaṃdvitaṃ vajrabījaṃ ca cāraṇāyāṃ vidhīyate |
samastametatpūrvoktaprakāreṇaiva kārayet || 1,4.132 ||
tadā rasendraścarati tatsarvaṃ cāraṇocitaḥ |
<navamaḥ prakāraḥ>
pātre tu kāsīsaṃ kāṃkṣī mākṣīkagandhakam || 1,4.133 ||
kṣāratrayaṃ pañcaloṇamamlayuktaṃ kṣaṇaṃ kṣipet |
divyābhiṣekayogoktaṃ śatadhā ḍhālayedahim || 1,4.134 ||
vaṅgaṃ vānena vastūni cāraṇārhāṇi bhāvayet |
varī punarnavā raṃbhā gavākṣī yavaciñcikā || 1,4.135 ||
śigrukā meghanādaśca rasaireṣāṃ vibhāvayet |
vajrābhrakaṃ krameṇaiva saptāhaṃ suravandite || 1,4.136 ||
tadabhrakaṃ nāgavallyā koberyā śigrukasya vā |
bāṇāṅkolabalāraṃbhāsphotānāṃ pīlukasya vā || 1,4.137 ||
alambuṣāhimarucāmusalītumburorapi |
athavā kharamañjaryāstiktaśākasya vā priye || 1,4.138 ||
ekenaiṣāṃ rasenaiva vyoṣasarṣapasaṃyutam |
svinnaṃ tridivasaṃ kuryāttasminṭaṅkaṇasaindhavam || 1,4.139 ||
kāsīsaṃ tuvarīṃ kṣiptvā pūrvābhiṣavayogataḥ |
etadabhraṃ caretsūtaḥ samukho nirmukho'thavā || 1,4.140 ||
tadabhrakaprabhāvena golakaḥ siddhido bhavet |
<daśamaḥ prakāraḥ>
dhānyāmlairamlavargaiśca svedayedabhrakaṃ dinam || 1,4.141 ||
tataḥ snukkṣīrato mardyaṃ kapotākhye puṭe dahet |
munitrayaṃ meghanādaṃ rambhākandaṃ ca mūlakam || 1,4.142 ||
matsyākṣī śṛṅgiveraṃ ca kākamācī punarnavā |
apāmārgastvarūpūgaścaiteṣāṃ tu rasena ca || 1,4.143 ||
ekaikena punardadyādekaikaṃ pūrvamabhrakam |
ḍolāyantre snuhīkṣīrayuktamekadinaṃ pacet || 1,4.144 ||
tataśchāyāgate śuṣke ghane'sminnavasārakam |
nimbaṃ vacāṃ ca kāsīsaṃ caṇakāmlaṃ pṛthakpṛthak || 1,4.145 ||
abhrasya ṣoḍaśāṃśaṃ ca nikṣipettāmrabhājane |
gharṣayettaccaretsūto lohapātrasya yogataḥ || 1,4.146 ||
<ekādaśaḥ prakāraḥ>
dhānyābhraṃ mardayetsomavallītoyairdinaṃ dṛḍham |
puṭedevaṃ tridhā paścātsomavallyā rasena ca || 1,4.147 ||
bhāvayetsaptadhā devi rasaścarati tadghanam |
<dvādaśaḥ prakāraḥ>
sāranāle tu śatadhā niṣicya tamanena ca || 1,4.148 ||
śatadhā plāvayedabhraṃ tāmravāsanayā saha |
khalalyāṃ taccaretsūto nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 1,4.149 ||
<gandhakabhāvanā>
cāraṇārthasya gandhasya bhāvanāṃ śṛṇu pārvati |
saurāṣṭraṃ cājamodaṃ ca sarjīṃ sīsameva ca || 1,4.150 ||
mardayecchigrutoyena gandhakaṃ tena bhāvayet |
saptāhaṃ marditaṃ gandhaṃ cārayetpāradasya ca || 1,4.151 ||
rāgaṃ dhatte rasendrasya bījānāṃ pākajāraṇe |
pakṣacchede rasendrasya śreṣṭhaḥ syātsarvakarmaṇi || 1,4.152 ||
palāśapuṣpasvarasaiḥ śākavṛkṣacchadadravaiḥ |
viṣṇukrāntādravairgandhaṃ bhāvayetsaptavāsaram || 1,4.153 ||
taṃ gandhaṃ cārayetsūte iṣṭakāyantrayogataḥ |
<amracāraṇe śreṣṭhā divyamūlyaḥ>
athābhracāraṇe śreṣṭhā divyamūlīrvadāmi te || 1,4.154 ||
vajrī raṃbhā haṃsapadī ciñcikā vyāghrapādikā |
maṇḍūkī bṛhatī puṅkhā kumārī lāṅgalī tathā || 1,4.155 ||
sarpākṣī cāgnidhamanī śaṅkhapuṣpīndravāruṇī |
ṣaṇḍajārī ca karkoṭī vikhyātā divyamūlikā || 1,4.156 ||
<gandhakābhrakacāraṇam>
athātaścāraṇaṃ karma pravakṣyāmi samāsataḥ |
dhānyābhrakaṃ drute gandhe samaṃ kṣiptvā niyojayet || 1,4.157 ||
khyāto gandhābhrayogo'yaṃ śreṣṭhaścāraṇakarmaṇi |
samukhaṃ nirmukhaṃ vāpi pāradaṃ daśaniṣkakam || 1,4.158 ||
gandhābhrayoganiṣke dve piṣṭaṃ kuryādyathā tathā |
taṃ piṣṭaṃ nāgapiṣṭyātha samayā pariveṣṭayet || 1,4.159 ||
hemakarmaṇi vaṅgasya piṣṭyā rajatakarmaṇi |
tāmrapiṣṭyā dehasiddhau veṣṭayetparameśvari || 1,4.160 ||
tāṃ piṣṭiṃ vidrute gandhe vipaceddivasatrayam |
tāṃ piṣṭiṃ dīpikāyantre pācayetpātayedadhaḥ || 1,4.161 ||
taṃ rasaṃ pūrvavatpiṣṭiṃ kuryādveṣṭanavarjitām |
tridinaṃ vidrute gandhe vipacetpātayedadhaḥ || 1,4.162 ||
taṃ rasaṃ taptakhalve tu catuḥṣaṣṭiguṇaṃ kṣipet |
bhāgaikamabhiṣiktābhraṃ mardyaṃ divyauṣadhairdinam || 1,4.163 ||
tatkṣipeccāraṇāyantre jambīrarasasaṃyute |
tīvrātape dinaṃ sthāpyaṃ sūtaścarati tadghanam || 1,4.164 ||
evaṃ punaḥ punargarbhapiṣṭīgrāsakramātmakam |
pattrābhracāraṇaṃ proktamevaṃ satvābhracāraṇam || 1,4.165 ||
evaṃ kuryādyathāśakyaṃ cāraṇaṃ parameśvari |
rase'bhraṃ cārayetpūrvaṃ catuḥṣaṣṭitamāṃśakam || 1,4.166 ||
dvātriṃśattamabhāgaṃ syāttadetatṣoḍaśāṃśakam |
aṣṭamāṃśaṃ caturthāṃśaṃ dvitīyāṃśaṃ samaṃ kramāt || 1,4.167 ||
dviguṇaṃ caturguṇaṃ cāṣṭaguṇaṃ ṣoḍaśakaṃ guṇam |
dvātriṃśadguṇamevaṃ syātcatuḥṣaṣṭiguṇaṃ kramāt || 1,4.168 ||
pakṣacchedastvadoṣatvaṃ laghutā grāsayogyatā |
mukhitāgnisahatvaṃ ca vyāpitvaṃ gatihīnatā || 1,4.169 ||
rasendrasyānavasthatvaṃ ca bhavedabhrakacāraṇāt |
<jāraṇā>
yasyāhamapi tuṣṭaḥ syāṃ tasya sūtendrajāraṇe || 1,4.170 ||
matirbhavenna sandeho jāraṇā bhuktimuktidā |
durlabhā jāraṇā devi vinā bhāgyaṃ na labhyate || 1,4.171 ||
jāraṇā dvividhā bālajāraṇā vṛddhajāraṇā |
<jāraṇe kramaḥ>
gaganaṃ jārayetpūrvaṃ sarvasatvānyataḥ param || 1,4.172 ||
dvaṃdvāni sarvalohāni pakvabījāni bhairavi |
padmarāgādiratnāni jārayecca yathākramam || 1,4.173 ||
<jāraṇāya abhrakasatvanirmāṇaprakāraḥ, prathamaḥ prakāraḥ>
jāraṇārhaṃ vyomasatvaṃ rasarājasya vakṣyate |
dhānyābhrakaṃ ca vidhivanmārayedvajrasaṃjñakam || 1,4.174 ||
abhiṣekaṃ pūrvavatsyāttasmin tāpyaṃ daśāṃśakam |
pañcamāhiṣagavyaśca mardayetkarṣasannibhāḥ || 1,4.175 ||
kurvīta vaṭikāḥ koṣṭhīyantre satvaṃ vipātayet |
satvasya ca caturthāṃśaṃ tāpye mūṣāgataṃ dhamet || 1,4.176 ||
evaṃ kṛtvā caturvāraṃ tatsatvaṃ rasajāraṇe |
rasāyane ca bhaiṣajye śreṣṭhaṃ sakalakarmaṇi || 1,4.177 ||
<dvitīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
mṛtābhraṃ taccaturthāṃśaṃ nāgaṃ tatpādamākṣikam |
pañcabhirmāhiṣairgavyaiḥ pātayetsattvamujjvalam || 1,4.178 ||
tatsatvaṃ taccaturthāṃśaṃ mākṣikaṃ tatsamāṃ śilām |
dhametpraliptamūṣāyāṃ satvaṃ syāddhemasannibham || 1,4.179 ||
evaṃ kuryāccaturvāraṃ tatsatvaṃ grasate rasaḥ |
<tṛtīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
mṛtābhrakaṃ ṣoḍaśāṃśaṃ taccaturthāṃśakaṃ trapu || 1,4.180 ||
tatsamaṃ tālakaṃ kṣiptvā pañcamāhiṣagavyataḥ |
sattvaṃ nipātayettacca caturthaṃ haritālakam || 1,4.181 ||
kṣiptvā kṣiptvā caturvāraṃ tatsatvaṃ grasate rasaḥ |
<caturthaḥ prakāraḥ, abhrakasattvamṛdūkaraṇam>
vajramūṣāgataṃ kṛtvā ghanasatvaṃ ca tatsamam || 1,4.182 ||
kacaṃ ca ṭaṅkaṇaṃ kṣiptvā ruddhvā tīvrāgninā dhamet |
tatkṣipedamlavargeṇa ciñcābījaṃ snuhīdalam || 1,4.183 ||
piṣṭvā tasminpunastadvatkācaṭaṅkaṇayogataḥ |
dhamedevaṃ saptavāraṃ secanaṃ ca punaḥ punaḥ || 1,4.184 ||
tadabhrasattvaṃ śubhraṃ syānmṛdu syāccāraṇoditam |
<pañcamaḥ prakāraḥ>
rasendraṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ tāpyaṃ bhāgaikaikaṃ pṛthakpṛthak || 1,4.185 ||
tribhāgaṃ śodhitaṃ nāgaṃ mṛtābhraṃ daśabhāgakam |
yavakṣāraṃ ca dagdhorṇāṃ sarjakṣāraṃ ca guggulum || 1,4.186 ||
ajāpañcāṅgasaṃyuktamamlamūtragaṇairyutam |
mardayetpūrvavatsatvaṃ pātayenmṛdu nirmalam || 1,4.187 ||
<dvandvamelāpanāya mūṣālepaḥ: prathamaḥ prakāraḥ>
dvandvamelāpane mūṣālepaṃ vakṣyāmi pārvati || 1,4.188 ||
meṣaśṛṅgaṃ khuraṃ guñjā ṭaṅkaṇaṃ ca viṣaṃ samam |
sthūlabhekasya vasayā piṣṭvā mūṣāṃ pralepayet || 1,4.189 ||
milanti sarvasatvāni mūṣāyāṃ cūrṇitāni ca |
gugguluṃ dhātakīpuṣpaṃ guḍaṃ kṣāratrayaṃ samam || 1,4.190 ||
stanyaiḥ piṣṭvātha mūṣāyāṃ lepanaṃ dvandvamelanam |
<dvitīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
aśmabhedī lāṅgalī ca bhūlatā ca marīcakaḥ || 1,4.191 ||
kārpāsasya phalaṃ śakragopaścaitāṃśca mardayet |
nārīstanyena tenaiva mūṣāmantaḥ pralepayet || 1,4.192 ||
tasyāṃ milanti satvāni sarvāṇi ca mahārasāḥ |
hemamukhyāni lohāni caṇḍāgnidhamanena ca || 1,4.193 ||
<tṛtīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
cucundaryāśca piśitaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ ca viṣaṃ samam |
mardayitvā ca mūṣāntarlepayettatra nikṣipet || 1,4.194 ||
dvandvayogyaṃ tu yadyatsyāttatsarvaṃ dhamanānmilet |
<caturthaḥ prakāraḥ>
bhasmīkṛtamapāmārgaṃ kaṅguṇītailamarditam || 1,4.195 ||
mūṣāyāṃ lepayettena dvandvadravyaṃ vinikṣipet |
milettīvrāgnidhamanāttattanmārakavāpanāt || 1,4.196 ||
<dvandvamelāpanam>
ata eva pravakṣyāmi dvandvamelāpanaṃ śṛṇu |
varṣābhūkadalīkandakākamācīpunarnavam || 1,4.197 ||
svarṇaṃ narakapālaṃ ca guñjā ṭaṅkaṇamabhrakam |
satvaṃ mūṣāgataṃ dhmātaṃ hemābhraṃ milati kṣaṇāt || 1,4.198 ||
abhrakaṃ cūrṇayitvā tu kākamācīrasaplutam |
kapitthatoyasaṃyuktaṃ sūtaṭaṅkaṇasaṃyutam || 1,4.199 ||
vaṅgapatrāntare nyastaṃ dhmātaṃ vaṅgābhrakaṃ milet |
āvartya kaṭutailena bhasmāpāmārgajaṃ kṣipet || 1,4.200 ||
hemābhraṃ nāgatāpyena tārābhraṃ haritālakāt |
gandhakena tu śulvābhraṃ tīkṣṇābhraṃ sindhuhiṅgulāt || 1,4.201 ||
vaṅgābhraṃ haritālena nāgābhraṃ śilayā milet |
lāṅgalī camarīkeśāḥ kārpāsāsthi kulatthakam || 1,4.202 ||
bhūlatā cāśmadamanī strīstanyaṃ suragopakaḥ |
dvandve praliptamūṣāyāṃ sudhmātāstīvravahninā || 1,4.203 ||
kāntābhraśailavimalāḥ milanti sakalāḥ kṣaṇāt |
atha cucchundarīmāṃsaṃ viṣaṭaṅkaṇayojitam || 1,4.204 ||
mūṣāpralepātkurute sarvadvandveṣu melanam |
jaurabhramaraśṛṅgaṃ ca maṇḍūkasya vasāmiṣam || 1,4.205 ||
guñjāṭaṅkaṇalepena sarvasatveṣu melanam |
ṭaṅkaṇorṇāgirijatukarṇākṣīmalakarkaṭaiḥ || 1,4.206 ||
milanti sarvadravyāṇi strīstanyaparipeṣitaiḥ |
dhātakīgugguluguḍasarjayāvakaṭaṅkaṇaiḥ || 1,4.207 ||
strīstanyapeṣitaṃ śastaṃ dvandvaṃ syāttu rasāyane |
sūkṣmacūrṇaṃ tu yatkiṃcitpūrvakalkena saṃyutam || 1,4.208 ||
andhamūṣāgataṃ dhmātaṃ saṃkaraṃ milati kṣaṇāt |
vāpitaṃ tāpyarasakasasyakairdaradena ca || 1,4.209 ||
sa satvaṃ syādanibiḍaṃ dṛḍhaṃ dhmātaṃ milettataḥ |
rasoparasalohāni sarvāṇyekatra melayet || 1,4.210 ||
<prathamaḥ prakāraḥ>
anyonyaṃ dvaṃdvatāṃ yānti dravanti salilaṃ yathā |
vajrābhrasattvacūrṇaṃ tu yatkiṃcillohacūrṇakam || 1,4.211 ||
dvayaṃ samaṃ tayostulyamarivargaṃ vinikṣipet |
dvandvamelanamūṣāyāṃ dhamanānmelanaṃ bhavet || 1,4.212 ||
<dvitīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
mardayeṭṭaṅkaṇaṃ rambhākandatoyena lepayet |
mūṣāṃ śvetābhrakaṃ vaṅgaṃ dhamanānmilati priye || 1,4.213 ||
<tṛtīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
vanaśigruṣṭaṅkaṇaśca guñjā ca nṛkapālakam |
piṣṭvā yoṣitstanyanīraistena mūṣāṃ pralepayet || 1,4.214 ||
vaṅgaśvetābhrasatvaṃ ca cūrṇayitvā kṣipeddhamet |
asaṃśayaṃ milantyeva śreṣṭhaṃ rajatakarmaṇi || 1,4.215 ||
<caturthaḥ prakāraḥ>
śvetābhrasatvaṃ saṃcūrṇya taccaturthāṃśapāradam |
sūtatulyaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ ca mardayetkākamācijaiḥ || 1,4.216 ||
dravaistadgolakaṃ kṛtvā vaṅgapatreṇa veṣṭayet |
śvetābhrasatvatulyena liptamūṣāgataṃ dhamet || 1,4.217 ||
bhavetsammelanaṃ samyakmukhyaḥ syādrūpyakarmaṇi |
<vajrahemamelāpane mūṣālepaḥ:  prathamaḥ prakāraḥ>
athāto vajrakanakadvandvamelāpralepanam || 1,4.218 ||
guñjā kāntamukhaṃ tulyaṃ dvandvayordviguṇaṃ balim |
stanyena yoṣitāṃ piṣṭvā mūṣāyāmantare tathā || 1,4.219 ||
melayedvajramelāpo'yaṃ kathito mayā |
<dvitīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
śilādhātuḥ kāntamukhaṃ śaśadantāśca gandhakaḥ || 1,4.220 ||
amlavetasakaṃ tulyaṃ piṣṭvā mūṣāṃ pralepayet |
hemavajraṃ milatyeva mūṣāyāṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ || 1,4.221 ||
<tṛtīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
kulīrāsthi śilādhātu mahiṣīkaṇadṛṅmalam |
ṭaṅkaṇorṇāsamaṃ sarvaṃ kāntāstanyena mardayet || 1,4.222 ||
anena lepayenmūṣāṃ hemavajraṃ milatyalam |
<caturthaḥ prakāraḥ>
kāntāsyaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ bālavatsaviḍbhramarāsthi ca || 1,4.223 ||
manuṣyacikurāsthīni strīstanyena vimardayet |
anena liptamūṣāyāṃ hemavajraṃ mileddhruvam || 1,4.224 ||
<pañcamaḥ prakāraḥ>
tāpyaṭaṅkaṇabhūnāgakāntakācamadhūni ca |
kulīrāsthi snuhīkṣīramarkakṣīraṃ samaṃ samam || 1,4.225 ||
stanyena mardayetsarvaṃ mūṣālepaṃ tu kārayet |
mūṣālepo bhavedeṣa vajrahāṭakamelanaḥ || 1,4.226 ||
vajraṃ bhasmībhavedyaistairmūṣāmantarvilepayet |
dhamettīvrāgninā hemavajramelāpanaṃ bhavet || 1,4.227 ||
<vajrahemamelāpanam; prathamaḥ prakāraḥ>
athāto melanaṃ vakṣye vajrahemnoḥ sureśvari |
svarṇaṃ dvādaśabhāgaṃ syāttadardhaṃ śuddhapāradam || 1,4.228 ||
rasendrārdhaṃ nāgabhasma nāgārdhaṃ mṛtavajrakam |
etaccatuṣṭayaṃ cāmlairmardayettaptakhalvake || 1,4.229 ||
uddhṛtya dvandvamelāpe mūṣāyāṃ rodhayeddhamet |
haṭhāttacca milatyetannātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 1,4.230 ||
<dvitīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
bhāvayecchārivākṣīre mṛtaṃ vajraṃ dinaṃ tataḥ |
mardayedgolakaṃ kṛtvā śeṣayeccaikabhāgakam || 1,4.231 ||
tribhāgaṃ rasarājaṃ ca caturbhāgaṃ suvarṇakam |
amlena mardayetpiṣṭiṃ kṛtvā tadgolakam || 1,4.232 ||
tatpiṣṭvā veṣṭayettacca bhūrjapatreṇa veṣṭayet |
pakṣaṃ nyaseddhānyarāśau taduddhṛtya punaḥ punaḥ || 1,4.233 ||
dhameddhaṭhānmilatyeva nātra kāryā vicāraṇā |
<tṛtīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
vajraṃ ṣoḍaśabhāgena hemapatreṇa veṣṭayet || 1,4.234 ||
tatkṣipelliptamūṣāyāṃ svarṇāṃśaṃ śvetakācajam |
cūrṇaṃ vā nṛkapālaṃ vā cūrṇaṃ kṛtvā vinikṣipet || 1,4.235 ||
nirudhya ca dhamettīvramevaṃ kuryātpunaḥ punaḥ |
kṣiptvā kṣiptvā śvetakācaṃ nṛkapālamathāpi vā || 1,4.236 ||
saptadhaivaṃ milatyeva punarevaṃ vidhīyate |
asya hemnaḥ ṣoḍaśāṃśaṃ mṛtaṃ vajraṃ vinikṣipet || 1,4.237 ||
pūrvavalliptamūṣāyāṃ kācaṃ vā nṛkapālakam |
kṣiptvā kṣiptvā dhamettīvraṃ saptavāraṃ milatyalam || 1,4.238 ||
evaṃ tṛtīyavāraṃ ca kuryādevaṃ caturthakam |
<dvandvitānāmabhiṣekaḥ>
athāto dvaṃdvitānāṃ ca pravakṣyāmyabhiṣecanam || 1,4.239 ||
kāsīsaṃ pañcalavaṇaṃ mākṣīkaṃ gandhakaṃ tathā |
kāṃkṣī kṣāratrayaṃ tulyamāranālena mardayet || 1,4.240 ||
tatkalkamātape tāmrapātre saṃsthāpayettryaham |
tasminkṣipeddrutaṃ nāgaṃśatadhā taddrutaṃ drutam || 1,4.241 ||
tāmrapātrasthasauvīrairbhāvayecchatavārakam |
dvaṃdvitaṃ taccaretsūto yatkiṃcijjāraṇārhakam || 1,4.242 ||
rūpyakarmaṇi vaṅgaṃ syānnāgaṃ syāddhemakarmaṇi |
<pakvabījavidhānam>
athātaḥ sampravakṣyāmi pakvabījaṃ surārcite || 1,4.243 ||
<prathamaṃ vidhānam>
suvarṇe vidrute tulyaṃ nāgābhraṃ dvaṃdvitaṃ priye |
vāhayeddvādaśaguṇaṃ svarṇaśeṣaṃ yathā dhamet || 1,4.244 ||
pakvabījamidaṃ khyātaṃ jārayetpārade kramāt |
<dvitīyaṃ vidhānam>
pītābhrasatvaṃ svarṇaṃ ca samāṃśaṃ dvaṃdvitaṃ dhamet || 1,4.245 ||
vāhayeddvādaśaguṇaṃ svarṇaśeṣaṃ yathā dhamet |
punaḥ pītābhrasatvaṃ ca vaheddaśaguṇaṃ śanaiḥ || 1,4.246 ||
svarṇaśeṣaṃ bhavedyāvatpakvabījamidaṃ bhavet |
<tṛtīyaṃ vidhānam>
nāgārkavyomarasakaṃ catustrirdvyekabhāgikam || 1,4.247 ||
cūrṇayelliptamūṣāyāmandhayecca dhameddṛḍham |
mākṣikeṇa ca tatkhoṭaṃ saṃpeṣyāmlaiḥ puṭe pacet || 1,4.248 ||
punastatsamamākṣīkamamlaiḥ piṣṭvā puṭe pacet |
evaṃ pañcapuṭaṃ dattvā taddrute hemni vāhayet || 1,4.249 ||
dhameddaśaguṇaṃ yāvattāvatsyātpakvabījakam |
<caturbījakramaḥ>
athātaḥ sampravakṣyāmi caturbījaṃ varānane || 1,4.250 ||
hematārāhikuṭilabījānyetāni tattvataḥ |
hemabījaṃ nāgabījaṃ śasyate hemakarmaṇi || 1,4.251 ||
tārabījaṃ vaṅgabījaṃ kathite rūpyakarmaṇi |
hemabījaṃ tārabījaṃ bhavetāṃ ca rasāyane || 1,4.252 ||
nāgabījaṃ vaṅgabījaṃ na rasāyanakarmaṇi |
catvāryetāni bījāni bhaveyū rogaśāntaye || 1,4.253 ||
anyathā naiva yojyāni bījānyetāni śāmbhavi |
<hemabījavidhānam, prathamaṃ hemabījam>
gandhakaṃ sasyakaṃ vāpi mākṣīkaṃ vātha tālakam || 1,4.254 ||
śilāṃ ca vimalāṃ vāpi vaikrāntaṃ vāñjanaṃ ca vā |
kharparaṃ vā kāntamukhaṃ vā cūrṇayitvā samāṃśakam || 1,4.255 ||
śatavārāndrute hemni vāhayecca punaḥ punaḥ |
<dvitīya nāgabhasmasiddhaṃ hemabījam>
sūtahiṃgulakaṃkuṣṭhalohaparpaṭikābhrakam || 1,4.256 ||
mṛtārkamākṣīkaśilāvimalāṃśca samāṃśakān |
etānnāgakalābhāgān snuhyarkapayasā priye || 1,4.257 ||
mardayettena patrāṇi śuddhanāgasya lepayet |
puṭeddvātriṃśatipuṭe nāgo bhasmati tadvahet || 1,4.258 ||
samaṃ samaṃ ca śatadhā vidrute pūrvavāhite |
suvarṇe ca tathā hemaśeṣaṃ tāvaddhameddṛḍham || 1,4.259 ||
<tṛtīyaṃ vaṅgabhasmasiddhaṃ hemabījam>
athavā bhasmayedvaṅgaṃ tālaśaṅkhābhrapāradaiḥ |
ciñcākṣāraistrapusamaiḥ snuhyarkakṣīramarditaiḥ || 1,4.260 ||
puṭetṣoḍaśabhistacca śatadhā hemni vāhayet |
<caturthaṃ tāmrabhasmasiddhaṃ hemabījam>
