athavā mārayecchulbamūrdhvādho gandhakaṃ samam || 1,4.261 ||
kṣiptvā puṭedaṣṭavāraṃ tattāmraṃ hemni vāhayet |
<pañcamaṃ tīkṣṇabhasmasiddhaṃ hemabījam>
athavā mārayettīkṣṇaṃ tīkṣṇatulyaṃ ca hiṃgulam || 1,4.262 ||
strīstanyairmardayitvā tu puṭedatha kalāṃśakam |
tīkṣṇasya hiṃgulaṃ dattvā mardayetpuṭayetkramāt || 1,4.263 ||
evaṃ tatṣoḍaśapuṭaiḥ tattīkṣṇaṃ hemni vāhayet |
śatadhā hema tadiśaṃ vidrute hemni vāhayet || 1,4.264 ||
<ṣaṣṭhaṃ hemabījam>
taddhemni dviguṇaṃ tāpyaṃ pūrṇitaṃ taddhameddṛḍham |
taccūrṇayeddvitripuṭaiḥ pañcabhirmāritaṃ bhavet || 1,4.265 ||
tadvāhayeddaśaguṇaṃ drute hemni dhaman dhaman |
tadeva jāyate divyaṃ sarvasiddhipradāyakam || 1,4.266 ||
hemabījamidaṃ khyātaṃ hitaṃ syādrasajāraṇe |
<saptamaṃ hemabījam>
sasyakābhrakavaikrāṃtasatvaṃ svarṇamahiṃ ravim || 1,4.267 ||
tīkṣṇaṃ ca cūrṇayelliptamūṣāyāṃ cāndhritaṃ dhamet |
punaḥ prakaṭamūṣāyāṃ dhamettasmindrute sati || 1,4.268 ||
mākṣikaṃ nikṣipetkiṃcitkiṃciddattvā punaḥ punaḥ |
yāvatsvarṇāvaśeṣaṃ syāttatastasmin vinikṣipet || 1,4.269 ||
svarṇaṃ nāgaṃ raviṃ tīkṣṇaṃ pūrvavacca dhametkramāt |
vāhayenmākṣikaṃ tadvaddhametsvarṇāvaśeṣakam || 1,4.270 ||
hemabījamidaṃ khyātaṃ hitaṃ syādrasajāraṇe |
<aṣṭamaṃ hemabījam>
samāṃśaṃ cūrṇayedabhraṃ satvaṃ tāmramayaṃ śubham || 1,4.271 ||
abhrakāddviguṇaṃ dhautaṃ tāpyaṃ sarvaṃ dhameddhaṭhāt |
mūṣāyāṃ dvandvaliptāyāṃ tatkhoṭaṃ cūrṇayettataḥ || 1,4.272 ||
mardyaṃ divyauṣadhirasaiḥ saṃpuṭe rodhayeddṛḍham |
pacedgajapuṭe tadvaccūrṇayenmardayetpuṭet || 1,4.273 ||
evaṃ pañcapuṭaiḥ pakvaṃ tadvahetkanake drute |
yāvaddaśaguṇaṃ tāvanmūṣāyāṃ prakaṭe dhamet || 1,4.274 ||
tatastasmin śuddhatāpyaṃ kṣiptvā kṣiptvā dhameddhamet |
yāvatsvarṇāvaśeṣaṃ syātsvarṇabījamidaṃ priye || 1,4.275 ||
<navamaṃ hemabījam>
tīkṣṇārkanāgabhasmāni tāpyacūrṇaṃ samaṃ samam |
etaddrute vaheddhemni yāvaddaśaguṇaṃ bhavet || 1,4.276 ||
tāvatsvarṇāvaśeṣaṃ syātsvarṇabījamidaṃ priye |
<daśamaṃ hemabījam>
mṛtatārārkatīkṣṇāyaḥ samaṃ sarvaṃ dhameddṛḍham || 1,4.277 ||
mūṣāyāṃ dvandvaliptāyāṃ tatkhoṭaṃ cūrṇayettataḥ |
taccūrṇaṃ drāvite svarṇe vāhayecca śanaiḥ śanaiḥ || 1,4.278 ||
yāvaddaśaguṇaṃ paścāttāpyacūrṇaṃ kṣipankṣipan |
dhametsvarṇāvaśeṣaṃ syāddhemabījamidaṃ priye || 1,4.279 ||
<ekādaśaṃ hemabījam>
nāgamekaṃ catustāmraṃ sattvaṃ rasakasambhavam |
mūṣāyāṃ dvandvaliptāyāṃ sarvaṃ dhmātaṃ vicūrṇayet || 1,4.280 ||
taccūrṇaṃ vāhayetsvarṇe vidrute ṣaḍguṇaṃ śanaiḥ |
svarṇaśeṣaṃ bhavedyāvattāvatsyātsvarṇabījakam || 1,4.281 ||
<dvādaśaṃ hemabījam>
tāpyena mārayettāmraṃ tannāge vāhayecchanaiḥ |
yāvacchataguṇaṃ tāpyaṃ cūrṇaṃ kṣiptvā dhamandhaman || 1,4.282 ||
tadvāhayeddhameddhemni kramāddvātriṃśataṃ guṇam |
svarṇaśeṣaṃ bhavedyāvattāvatsyāddhemabījakam || 1,4.283 ||
<trayodaśaṃ hemabījam>
bhāgaikaṃ kharparīsatvaṃ dvibhāgaṃ cābhrasatvakam |
tribhāgaṃ tāmracūrṇaṃ ca liptamūṣāgataṃ dhamet || 1,4.284 ||
tatkhoṭaṃ cūrṇayettāpyaṃ tulyamamlairvimardayet |
tatkṣipetsaṃpuṭe ruddhvā puṭellaghupuṭe punaḥ || 1,4.285 ||
evaṃ pañcapuṭaiḥ pakvaṃ taccūrṇaṃ vāhayeddrute |
sahasraguṇitaṃ hemni yāvatsvarṇāvaśeṣitam || 1,4.286 ||
tāvattāpyaṃ vahedyuktyā syādidaṃ hemabījakam |
<caturdaśaṃ hemavījam>
bhāgaikamabhrasatvaṃ ca dvibhāgaṃ śuddhatāmrakam || 1,4.287 ||
cūrṇayelliptamūṣāyāṃ kṣiptvā ruddhvā dhameddṛḍham |
tatastaṃ cūrṇayettāpyaṃ tulyamamlena mardayet || 1,4.288 ||
tadruddhvā saṃpuṭe pacyātpuṭe taccūrṇayetpunaḥ |
pūrvavanmākṣike kṣiptvā mardayetpātayetpriye || 1,4.289 ||
evaṃ pañcapuṭe kārye taccūrṇaṃ vāhayetpriye |
svarṇe śataguṇaṃ yāvattāvatsyāddhemabījakam || 1,4.290 ||
<pañcadaśaṃ hemabījam>
mākṣikaṃ gandhapāṣāṇaṃ haritālaṃ manaḥśilām |
vaikrāntakaṃ kāntamukhaṃ sasyakaṃ vimalāṃjane || 1,4.291 ||
rasakaṃ cāpi śataśaścūrṇitaṃ hemni vāhayet |
lohaparpaṭikātāpyakaṅkuṣṭhavimalābhrakaiḥ || 1,4.292 ||
mṛtaśulbaśilāsūtasnuhyarkakṣīrahiṅgulaiḥ |
nāgo nirjīvatāṃ yāti yogaiḥ punaḥ punaḥ || 1,4.293 ||
rasatālakaśuddhaciṃcākṣāraistathā trapu |
mṛtaṃ nāgaṃ mṛtaṃ vaṅgaṃ śulbaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ ca vā mṛtam || 1,4.294 ||
ekaikamuttame hemni vāhayetsuravandite |
niruddhe pannage hemni nirvyūḍhe śatasaṃguṇaiḥ || 1,4.295 ||
<hemni nāgādinirvahaṇena varṇavyatyāsaḥ>
nāgajīrṇaṃ rocanābhaṃ hārītaṃ tāmravāhitam |
tīkṣṇajīrṇaṃ ravisamaṃ vaṅgajīrṇaṃ japānibham || 1,4.296 ||
<prathamaṃ tārabījam>
śvetābhrasatvaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ ca tāpyaṃ ca vimalāṃ tathā |
kharparaṃ ca samaṃ sarvaṃ kuṭilaṃ ca caturguṇam || 1,4.297 ||
dhametsarvaṃ cūrṇayecca bhasmayetpuṭapañcakaiḥ |
tāre drute śataguṇaṃ vāhayecca śanaiḥ śanaiḥ || 1,4.298 ||
tārabījamidaṃ khyātaṃ jāraṇe paramaṃ hitam |
<dvitīyaṃ tārabījam>
yathā tāpyena kuṭilaṃ tathā tāpyena mārayet || 1,4.299 ||
tadbhāgaṃ ca samaṃ tālaṃ tatsamaṃ rajate drute |
triṃśadguṇaṃ bhavedyāvattāvadvāhyaṃ krameṇa ca || 1,4.300 ||
tārabījamidaṃ khyātaṃ pārade tacca jārayet |
<tṛtīyaṃ tārabījam>
tīkṣṇaṃ vaṅgaṃ ca vimalāṃ samāṃśaṃ cūrṇayetpriye || 1,4.301 ||
dvandvamelopaliptāyāṃ dhamettatkhoṭakaṃ bhavet |
tatkhoṭaṃ cūrṇayedamlairmardayetpuṭayediti || 1,4.302 ||
pañcavāraṃ prakurvīta bhasma tajjāyate priye |
tadbhasma vidrute tāre vāhayecca samaṃ samam || 1,4.303 ||
dhaman dhaman daśaguṇaṃ yāvadbhavati bhairavi |
tārabījamidaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ rasarājasya jāraṇe || 1,4.304 ||
<caturthaṃ tārabījam>
śvetābhrasatvaṃ kuṭilaṃ tāramākṣīkasatvakam |
trayaṃ samāṃśaṃ saṃcūrṇya liptamūṣāgataṃ dhamet || 1,4.305 ||
taccūrṇaṃ vāhayettāre tāratulyaṃ vinikṣipet |
yāvaddaśaguṇaṃ tāvattāramākṣīkavāpataḥ || 1,4.306 ||
tārabījamidaṃ proktaṃ jāraṇe paramaṃ hitam |
<pañcarmaṃ tārabījam>
tridvyekabhāgān deveśi tīkṣṇatālāmalān kramāt || 1,4.307 ||
cūrṇitān liptamūṣāyāṃ kṣiptvā tīvrāgninā dhamet |
tatkhoṭaṃ cūrṇayedamlairmardayetpuṭayediti || 1,4.308 ||
saptadhā taddrute tāre vāhyaṃ daśaguṇaṃ tataḥ |
kṣiptvā kṣiptvā tālacūrṇaṃ tāraśeṣaṃ yathā dhamet || 1,4.309 ||
tārabījamidaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ jāraṇe paramaṃ hitam |
<ṣaṣṭhaṃ tārabījam>
kharparī tālaśubhrābhrasattvakaṃ tāramākṣikam || 1,4.310 ||
tulyaṃ sarvasamaṃ vaṅgaṃ sarvaṃ mūṣāgataṃ dhamet |
cūrṇayenmardayedamlaiḥ puṭedevaṃ tu saptadhā || 1,4.311 ||
taccūrṇaṃ vāhayettāre drute daśaguṇaṃ dhaman |
tāraśeṣaṃ bhavedyāvattāvatsyāttārabījakam || 1,4.312 ||
<saptamaṃ tārabījam>
śvetābhrasatvaṃ vaṅgaṃ ca dvandvaṃ kuryācca pūrvavat |
taccūrṇe tālakaṃ dattvā pādamamlairvimardayet || 1,4.313 ||
puṭettasminkṣipettālaṃ pūrvavanmardanaṃ puṭam |
evaṃ pañcapuṭaṃ kuryāttaccūrṇaṃ vāhayeddrute || 1,4.314 ||
rajate dvādaśaguṇaṃ tadbhavettārabījakam |
<aṣṭamaṃ tārabījam>
śvetābhrasatvaṃ vaṅgaṃ ca vaṅgārdhaṃ tīkṣṇacūrṇakam || 1,4.315 ||
tīkṣṇāṃśā tāravimalā sarvaṃ mūṣāgataṃ dhamet |
tatastaccūrṇayedamlaiḥ piṣṭvā ruddhvā puṭetpacet || 1,4.316 ||
pañcavāraṃ punastacca vāhayedrajate drute |
yāvacchataguṇaṃ tāvattālakaṃ ca kṣipan dhamet || 1,4.317 ||
tāraśeṣaṃ bhavedyāvattāvatsyāttārabījakam |
<tāmrabījam>
tāmrabījaṃ pravakṣyāmi jāraṇārthaṃ rasasya tu || 1,4.318 ||
svarṇaṃ tāmraṃ samaṃ devi tayostulyaṃ ca mākṣikam |
ūrdhvādho nikṣipelliptamūṣāyāṃ taddhameddṛḍham || 1,4.319 ||
dhamedevaṃ ca daśadhā dattvā dattvātha mākṣikam |
tāmrabījamidaṃ khyātaṃ bhāsvatkiraṇasaṃnibham || 1,4.320 ||
<prathamaṃ nāgabījam>
abhrakaṃ rasakaṃ tulyaṃ nāgabhasma caturguṇam |
dhamayeccūrṇayettacca mākṣikeṇa ca mārayet || 1,4.321 ||
etatsamaṃ śilācūrṇamekīkṛtyātha vāpayet |
taddhametsadṛśe hemni vidrute śatadhā priye || 1,4.322 ||
nāgabījamidaṃ proktametatsūte tu jārayet |
<dvitīyaṃ nāgabījam>
nāgabījaṃ pravakṣyāmi śreṣṭhaṃ tadrasajāraṇe || 1,4.323 ||
rasakābhrakatāmre'hiṃ bhāgavṛddhyā dhamettataḥ |
mākṣikeṇa hataṃ tacca bījaṃ nirvāhayetpriye || 1,4.324 ||
dvātriṃśāṃśaguṇaṃ hemni vaṅgaṃ tāpyahataṃ vahet |
triṃśadguṇaṃ śilāvāpyaṃ nāgabījamudāhṛtam || 1,4.325 ||
<vaṅgabījakramaḥ>
bījaṃ pravakṣyāmi vaṅgaṃ tālaṃ ca tāpyakam |
samaṃ dhameccūrṇayecca tatsamaṃ tāpyatālakam || 1,4.326 ||
mardayetpuṭayedamlairbhasma syātṣoḍaśaiḥ puṭaiḥ |
tāre drute śataguṇaṃ vāhayedvaṅgabhasma ca || 1,4.327 ||
tattāre tālakaṃ devi dvātriṃśadguṇamāvahet |
vaṅgabījamidaṃ jñeyametatsūte tu jārayet || 1,4.328 ||
<jāraṇe biḍayogāḥ>
viḍayogānpravakṣyāmi jāraṇārhān surārcite |
<1. vaḍavānalaviḍaḥ>
sauvīraṃ gandhakaṃ kāṃkṣī kāsīsaṃ vyoṣasaindhavam || 1,4.329 ||
sauvarcalaṃ sarjikā ca mālatītīrasambhavam |
śigrumūlarasaiḥ sarvaṃ bhāvayetsaptavāsaram || 1,4.330 ||
biḍo'yaṃ jāraṇe śreṣṭho nāmnā ca vaḍabānalaḥ |
<2. vaiśvānaraviḍaḥ>
arkakṣīrairdagdhaśaṅkhaṃ bhāvayetpuṭayetpriye || 1,4.331 ||
śatadhāyaṃ viḍaḥ prokto nāmnā vaiśvānaro mahān |
<3. jvālāmukhabiḍaḥ>
gandhakaṃ śaṅkhacūrṇaṃ ca saindhavaṃ ca viṣaṃ samam || 1,4.332 ||
śatavāraṃ gavāṃ mūtraiḥ śigrumūlarasaistathā |
bhāvito'yaṃ biḍaḥ prokto nāmnā jvālāmukhaḥ smṛtaḥ || 1,4.333 ||
<4. agnijihvakabiḍaḥ>
palāśasya rasairbhāvyaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ śatadhā priye |
lohānāṃ jāraṇe śreṣṭho biḍo nāmnāgnijihvakaḥ || 1,4.334 ||
<5 vaḍabābiḍaḥ>
kāntāsyaṃ gandhakaṃ cūlī caikaikaṃ lohajārakam |
cūlīgandhakasindhūtthatālabhūkhagaṭaṅkaṇān || 1,4.335 ||
sakṣāramūtrairvipacennāmnāyaṃ vaḍabāmukhaḥ |
<6. mahāvaiśvānaro viḍaḥ>
devadāliṃ mokṣakaṃ ca niculaṃ ca punarnavām || 1,4.336 ||
palāśaṃ kāñcanaṃ vāsāmeraṇḍaṃ vāstukaṃ tilam |
kadalīmapi sarvāṅgaṃ khaṇḍitaṃ nāti śoṣayet || 1,4.337 ||
mūtravarge cāṣṭaguṇe prakṣipettadanantaram |
samūlaṃ mūlakaṃ dagdhvā tilakāṇḍaṃ ca tatsamam || 1,4.338 ||
kṣāradvayaṃ pūrvarase kṣiptvā sthāpyaṃ tryahaṃ khare |
ātape tadvastrapūtaṃ lohapātre dravaṃ pacet || 1,4.339 ||
bahavo budbudā bāṣpā udbhavanti yadā yadā |
trikṣāraṃ trikaṭuṃ gandhaṃ kāsīsaṃ loṇapañcakam || 1,4.340 ||
saurāṣṭrīṃ rāmaṭhaṃ cūliṃ samaṃ cūrṇīkṛtaṃ kṣipet |
pracālayellohadarvyā guḍapāko yathā bhavet || 1,4.341 ||
samuttārya kṣipellohasaṃpuṭe saptavāsaram |
bhūgarbhe dhānyarāśau ca saptāhaṃ dhārayetpunaḥ || 1,4.342 ||
saptāhaṃ dhārayedgharme mahāvaiśvānaro biḍaḥ |
<7. vahnibiḍaḥ>
jambīrāmlena śatadhā vanaśigrurasena ca || 1,4.343 ||
gandhakaṃ bhāvayedeṣa biḍaḥ syādvahnisaṃjñakaḥ |
navasāro'pi ca tathā gandhavatsa biḍo bhavet || 1,4.344 ||
<8. citrabhānubiḍaḥ>
gandhakaṃ niculakṣāraṃ guṃjābījaṃ ca ṭaṅkaṇam |
mūlabījaṃ devadālyāḥ samaṃ sarvaṃ ca bhāvayet || 1,4.345 ||
tiktakośātakīnīraiḥ saptadhāmlagaṇaistathā |
biḍo'yaṃ citrabhānuḥ syātpradhānaṃ hemajāraṇe || 1,4.346 ||
<9. mahābiḍaḥ>
mūlakaṃ śṛṅgiveraṃ ca vahniṃ dagdhvā trayaṃ samam |
gomūtre nikṣipettacca vastrapūtaṃ ca kārayet || 1,4.347 ||
anena bhāvito gandhaḥ śatadhā syānmahābiḍaḥ |
<10. anyo mahābiḍaḥ>
tālaṃ śilā ṭaṅkaṇaṃ ca śaṅkhaṃ lavaṇaśuktike || 1,4.348 ||
mūtrāmlairvipacenmandaṃ vahnāveṣa biḍo mahān |
<11. vajrānalabiḍaḥ>
arkakṣīrairdagdhaśaṅkhaṃ śatadhā bhāvayettataḥ || 1,4.349 ||
mardayitvāmlavargeṇa ruddhvā pañcapuṭaiḥ pacet |
tatsamaṃ ṭaṅkaṇakṣāraṃ kṣiptvāmlagaṇabhāvitam || 1,4.350 ||
kṛtvā manaḥśilāṃ gandhaṃ daradaṃ vidrumaṃ tataḥ |
nṛpāvartaṃ tu pañcānāṃ śaṃkhacūrṇaṃ samaṃ priye || 1,4.351 ||
bhāvayedamlavargeṇa tridinaṃ syānmahābiḍaḥ |
nāmnā vajrānalaḥ proktaḥ khoṭaḥ sūtasya jāraṇe || 1,4.352 ||
dvandvamelāpamūṣāyāṃ sāraṇe yojayetsadā |
pāradastu kṣaṇādeva vajrādīnyapi jārayet || 1,4.353 ||
<12. anyo viḍaḥ>
nirdagdhaśaṅkhacūrṇaṃ ca ravikṣīraśatāplutam |
puṭitaṃ bahuśo devi praśasto jāraṇe biḍaḥ || 1,4.354 ||
<13. baḍabāmukho biḍaḥ>
śataśo vā plutaṃ cūrṇaṃ gandhakasya gavāṃ jalaiḥ |
nirdagdhaśaṅkhacūrṇaṃ tu śigrumūlāmbubhāvitam || 1,4.355 ||
śataśo viṣasindhūtthasaṃyutaṃ vaḍabāmukhaḥ |
<14. sarvajārakabiḍaḥ>
gandhakaṃ bhāvayetkanyādhuttūrakaravīrajaiḥ || 1,4.356 ||
dravaistu śatadhā gharme viḍo'sau sarvajārakaḥ |
<15. anyo biḍaḥ>
tāmravallīdalarasaiḥ plāvayedgandhasaindhavam || 1,4.357 ||
saptāhena bhavedenaṃ niyuñjyāddhemajāraṇe |
<16. siddhabiḍaḥ>
saindhavaṃ kunaṭī gandhaṃ pratyekaṃ ca palaṃ palam || 1,4.358 ||
bhūlatā tripalaṃ sarvaṃ mardayecchoṣayetpacet |
evaṃ kuryādaṣṭavāramayaṃ siddhabiḍaḥ smṛtaḥ || 1,4.359 ||
<abhrakajāraṇam>
athābhrajāraṇaṃ karma vakṣyāmi śṛṇu pārvati |
taptakhalve catuḥṣaṣṭiniṣkasūtaṃ sucāritam || 1,4.360 ||
bhāvitaṃ gaganaṃ caikaṃ niṣkaṃ tatra vinikṣipet |
trikṣāraṃ pañcalavaṇaṃ bhūkhagāmlajavetasān || 1,4.361 ||
ṣoḍaśāṃśān rasendrasya kṣipejjambīravāriṇā |
mardayedyāmamātraṃ tu kūrmayantre viḍānvite || 1,4.362 ||
paceddinaṃ vinikṣipya jīrṇagrāso bhavedrasaḥ |
etatsūtaṃ taptakhalve kṣiptvā rājīguḍorṇakam || 1,4.363 ||
iṣṭikāṃ gṛhadhūmaṃ ca saindhavaṃ pāradasya tu |
etatsarvaṃ ṣoḍaśāṃśaṃ mardyamamlagaṇairdinam || 1,4.364 ||
caṇḍātape piṇḍitaṃ tadrase baddhvātha poṭalam |
amlavargeṇa bharite ḍolāyantre paceddinam || 1,4.365 ||
pratigrāse tvidaṃ kuryātpunargrāsamapekṣate |
grāso'jīrṇo yadi bhavetpācyaḥ kacchapayantrake || 1,4.366 ||
evaṃ grāsakrameṇaiva jārayedgaganādikam |
catuḥṣaṣṭitamāṃśādi catuḥṣaṣṭiguṇāvadhi || 1,4.367 ||
rasendre jārayedgrāsaṃ dattvā dattvā punaḥ |
<jīrṇābhrakarasalakṣaṇam>
catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśake sūto vyomasatve tu jārite || 1,4.368 ||
daṇḍadhārī jalūkābho vaiṣa dvātriṃśadaṃśake |
ṣoḍaśāṃśe vāyasasya viṣṭhātulyo'ṣṭamāṃśake || 1,4.369 ||
dadhimaṇḍasamo devi caturthāṃśe tu jārite |
navanītasamo dvyaṃśe golābho jārito bhavet || 1,4.370 ||
abhrake samajīrṇe tu śatavedhī bhavedrasaḥ |
ghane dviguṇajīrṇe tu sahasrāṃśena vedhayet || 1,4.371 ||
caturguṇe'bhrake jīrṇe tvayutāyurbhavetpriye |
gagane'ṣṭaguṇe jīrṇe brahmāyurlakṣavedhakaḥ || 1,4.372 ||
viṣṇvāyuḥ ṣoḍaśaguṇe jīrṇe'bhre koṭivedhakaḥ |
dvātriṃśadguṇite jīrṇe rudrāyurdaśakoṭibhiḥ || 1,4.373 ||
catuḥṣaṣṭiguṇe jīrṇe nityāyuḥ śatakoṭibhiḥ |
abhrake samajīrṇe tu raso doṣānvimuñcati || 1,4.374 ||
jahāti svagatānsarvān sarvalohāni bhakṣayet |
<pakṣaccheda>
yantrādadho na patati naivotpatati cordhvataḥ || 1,4.375 ||
nodgārī kapilo varṇe vahnau tiṣṭhati niścalaḥ |
vipruṣo muñcate devi chinnapakṣo bhavedrasaḥ || 1,4.376 ||
samajīrṇe tu bālaḥ syādyuvā jīrṇacaturguṇaḥ |
vyomaṣaḍguṇajīrṇastu vṛddhasaṃjño bhavedrasaḥ || 1,4.377 ||
bālo vidhyati kalkena yuvā patrapralepataḥ |
jāritastu rasendro'yaṃ vṛddho lohāni vidhyati || 1,4.378 ||
bālaḥ sūto rujaṃ hanti na samartho rasāyane |
yuvā rasāyane dakṣo vṛddhaḥ syāddehalohayoḥ || 1,4.379 ||
<anyajāraṇaprakāraḥ>
abhāve ghanasatvasya kāntasatvaṃ pradīyate |
tadabhāve bhavettīkṣṇamevaṃ dvandvāni jārayet || 1,4.380 ||
ghanajīrṇasya sūtasya tato dvandvāni jārayet |
rasendro dvandvarahitaṃ na caredabhrasatvakam || 1,4.381 ||
tasmāddhemādilohena yuktamabhraṃ careddhruvam |
nāgābhraṃ vāpi vaṅgābhraṃ tāmrābhraṃ vā surārcite || 1,4.382 ||
tārābhraṃ vāpi hemābhraṃ tāpyābhraṃ vāpi jārayet |
ghanasatvaṃ yathā jīrṇaṃ tathā dvandvāni jārayet || 1,4.383 ||
dvandvabījarasasyātha pakvabījāni jārayet |
<jāraṇāphalam>
khalvaṃ tu pīṭhikā devi rasendro liṅga ucyate || 1,4.384 ||
mardanaṃ candanaṃ tasya grāsaḥ pūjā vidhīyate |
kṣīyamāṇe pātakaughe sulabhā rasajāraṇā || 1,4.385 ||
jāraṇāyāṃ ca labdhāyāṃ jñānaṃ kaivalyadaṃ bhavet |
tāvanmuktiḥ kutaḥ kānte yāvanno vetti jāraṇām || 1,4.386 ||
jāraṇā sādhakendrasya muktivyaktikarā priye |
jāraṇārthaṃ raso yāvaddinaṃ vahnau tu dhāryate || 1,4.387 ||
śivaloke sukhaṃ bhuṅkte tāvatkalpasahasrakam |
dhārayedyo rasaṃ vahnāvekāhaṃ vā tadardhakam || 1,4.388 ||
kṣīyante tasya pāpāni bahujanmārjitāni ca |
suvarṇaretaso mitraṃ svarṇadehastathā rasaḥ || 1,4.389 ||
asahatvaṃ sūtavahnau toye maitraṃ suvarṇataḥ |
caturguṇena vastreṇa pīḍito nirmalaśca saḥ || 1,4.390 ||
gālanakriyayā grāse sati niḥśeṣanirgate |
sa bhaveddaṇḍadhārī ca jīrṇagrāsastadā rasaḥ || 1,4.391 ||
hrāṃ hrīṃ hrūṃ hraiṃ hrauṃ hraḥ phaṭraseśvarāya sarvasattvopahārāya grāsaṃ gṛhṇa gṛhṇa hrīṃ svāhā |
<garbhadrutiḥ; garbhadrāvaṇayogyabījasiddhiḥ; prathamaḥ prakāraḥ>
garbhadrutiṃ pravakṣyāmi jāritasya rasasya ca |
garbhadrāvaṇayogyāni bījāni śṛṇu bhairavi || 1,4.392 ||
nāgabhasma ca mākṣīkaṃ gandhakaṃ ca samaṃ samam |
cūrṇitaṃ vāhayetsvarṇe triguṇaṃ drāvite dhaman || 1,4.393 ||
pūtibījamidaṃ sūtagarbhe dravati tatkṣaṇāt |
<dvitīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
svarṇe nāgaṃ samāvartya śilācūrṇaṃ kṣipankṣipan || 1,4.394 ||
nāgakṣaye punarnāgaṃ dattvā dattvā trivārakam |
svarṇaśeṣaṃ bhavedyāvattāvaddhāmyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ || 1,4.395 ||
etadbījaṃ dravatyeva rasagarbhe tu mardanāt |
<tṛtīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
suvarṇaṃ tāpyasatvaṃ tu samaṃ mūṣāgataṃ dhamet || 1,4.396 ||
tasmindrute tāpyacūrṇaṃ tulyaṃ tulyaṃ kṣipankṣipan |
mākṣīkasatvaṃ triguṇamevaṃ vāhyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ || 1,4.397 ||
etadbījaṃ rasendrasya garbhe dravati mardanāt |
<caturthaḥ prakāraḥ>
suvarṇasya samaṃ tāpyasatvamāvartayettataḥ || 1,4.398 ||
kuryātkaṇṭakavedhyāni patrāṇi ca vilepayet |
lavaṇenāmlapiṣṭena gandhatulyena pārvati || 1,4.399 ||
tāni patrāṇi ca puṭe paceddhemāvaśeṣitam |
etadbījaṃ rasendrasya garbhe dravati mardanāt || 1,4.400 ||
<pañcamaḥ prakāraḥ>
tāpyatulyaṃ ca sindhūtthaṃ mardayedamlakena ca |
tatsaṃpuṭe puṭe pacyātpunaḥ saṃmardayetpuṭet || 1,4.401 ||
punaḥ punardviṣaḍvāramasya tulyaṃ ca nāgajam |
bhasma saṃmardayedamlairanena svarṇapatrakam || 1,4.402 ||
liptvā liptvā dhametsaptavāraṃ tadvāhayettataḥ |
drutaṃ taṃ vāpayetpūrvaṃ kalkitaiḥ saptavārakam || 1,4.403 ||
etadbījaṃ rasendrasya garbhe dravati mardanāt |
<ṣaṣṭhaḥ prakāraḥ>
kāsīsaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ gandhaṃ tāpyaṃ sauvarcalaṃ śilām || 1,4.404 ||
tulyaṃ saṃmardayetsarvāṃścaṇakāmlairdināvadhi |
etadbījaṃ rasendrasya garbhe dravati mardanāt || 1,4.405 ||
<garbhadrāvaṇe mūṣāyantrasiddhiḥ>
suvarcalaṃ ca kāsīsaṃ yavakṣāraṃ ca ṭaṅkaṇam |
mākṣikaṃ saindhavaṃ cullī sāmudraṃ rājikā tathā || 1,4.406 ||
tulyaṃ kāṃkṣīṃ ca karpūramarkasnukkṣīramarditam |
anena lepayenmūṣāṃ biḍenāṃgulamātrakam || 1,4.407 ||
mūṣāyantramidaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ garbhadrāvaṇakarmaṇi |
<garbhadrutiprakārāḥ; prathamaḥ prakāraḥ>
svarṇaṃ tāmraṃ mṛtaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ rañjitaṃ pakvabījakam || 1,4.408 ||
garbhadrāvaṇabījaṃ ca sarvamāvartayeddṛḍham |
drute'smiṃstāpyacūrṇaṃ ca kṣiptvā kṣiptvā dhamandhaman || 1,4.409 ||
yāvatkṣayaṃ gate tāmratīkṣṇe tāvatsureśvari |
etadbījaṃ rasendrasya garbhe dravati mardanāt || 1,4.410 ||
<dvitīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
hema gandhahataṃ nāgaṃ pakvabījasya sādhanam |
taddvayaṃ liptamūṣāyāmandhrayitvā dhameddṛḍham || 1,4.411 ||
taccūrṇamabhiṣiktaṃ ca rasagarbhe dravatyalam |
<tṛtīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
suvarṇe vidrute śulbaṃ mṛtaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ śanaiḥ śanaiḥ || 1,4.412 ||
vāhayetṣaḍguṇaṃ yāvattadrase dravati priye |
<caturthaḥ prakāraḥ>
mahārasānanurasānkṣīṇalohāni cākṣaye || 1,4.413 ||
samāṃśaṃ samamākṣīkagandhakāvāpayogataḥ |
śataśo vāhayedetadakṣīṇāṃśāvaśeṣitam || 1,4.414 ||
samāṃśaṃ rasarājasya garbhe dravati niścitam |
<pañcamaḥ prakāraḥ>
jāritasya rasendrasya catuḥṣaṣṭitamāṃśakam || 1,4.415 ||
garbhadrāvaṇabījaṃ ca taptakhalve vinikṣipet |
śilārucakamākṣīkagandhakāsīsaṭaṅkaṇaiḥ || 1,4.416 ||
mardayeccaṇakāmlena sarvametaddināvadhi |
rasasyaitatṣoḍaśāṃśaṃ dattvā bījaṃ ca dāpayet || 1,4.417 ||
mucyate yatra yatraiva tattaddravati tatkṣaṇāt |
<ṣaṣṭhaḥ prakāraḥ>
apāmārgapalāśotthabhasmakṣāraṃ samāharet || 1,4.418 ||
ṭaṅkaṇaṃ sayavakṣāraṃ kāsīsaṃ ca suvarcalam |
sāmudraṃ saindhavaṃ rājīṃ mākṣikaṃ navasārakam || 1,4.419 ||
karpūraṃ mākṣikaṃ tulyaṃ snuhyarkakṣīramarditam |
mūṣālepamanenaiva kṛtvā kuryādbiḍena ca || 1,4.420 ||
lepamaṅgulamānena prāksūtaṃ cātra nikṣipet |
ruddhvā svedyaṃ dinaikaṃ tu karīṣāgnau dravatyalam || 1,4.421 ||
ityevaṃ drāvitaṃ jāryaṃ yāvadbījaṃ tu ṣaḍguṇam |
<13. bāhyadrutiḥ>
śive vakṣyāmi te bāhyadrutikarma yathākramam || 1,4.422 ||
yenopāyenābhrakādidrutirbhavati tacchṛṇu |
<abhrakadrutiḥ>
athābhrakadrutiṃ vakṣye kañcukīkandameva ca || 1,4.423 ||
kapitindau keśataile pratyekaṃ tu tridhā vapet |
mūṣāyāṃ drāvayitvā tadabhrasatvaṃ drutirbhavet || 1,4.424 ||
<svarṇadrutiḥ>
phalapāṃśurdevadālyāḥ śakagopo'śvalālakāḥ |
ṭaṅkaṇaṃ jālinīnīrairbhāvayedbahudhā priye || 1,4.425 ||
drāvayetkāñcanaṃ tatra vapetsūtasamā drutiḥ |
<sarvaratnadrutiḥ>
trikṣāraṃ caṇakāmlaṃ ca rāmaṭhaṃ cāmlavetasam || 1,4.426 ||
tathā jvālāmukhīkṣāraṃ sthalakumbhīrasena ca |
piṣṭvā snuhyarkayoḥ kṣīraistadgole mṛdu hīrakam || 1,4.427 ||
nikṣipettacca jambīre ḍolāyantre tryahaṃ pacet |
evaṃ kṛte hīrakasya drutirbhavati sūtavat || 1,4.428 ||
padmarāgādiratnānāṃ drutireva kṛte bhavet |
<drutimelāpanam>
drutīnāṃ melanaṃ vakṣye śṛṇu bhairavi tattvataḥ || 1,4.429 ||
kṛṣṇāgaruśca kastūrī brahmabījaṃ ca mākṣikam |
nārīpuṣpaṃ viṣaṃ hiṃgu laśunaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ niśā || 1,4.430 ||
etaiḥ samaṃ drutiṃ sūtaṃ taptakhalve vimardayet |
pāṭhā vandhyā tālamūlī nīlī trividhacitrakam || 1,4.431 ||
padmakandaṃ kṣīrakandaṃ balā guñjāmṛtārdrakam |
aśvalālā nimbapatraṃ strīstanyaṃ ca samaṃ samam || 1,4.432 ||
eteṣāṃ grāhayetsvacchaṃ rasaṃ vastreṇa gālitam |
etaddrutiyute sūte kṣiptvā kṣiptvā vimardayet || 1,4.433 ||
milanti drutayaḥ sarvāḥ pārade nātra saṃśayaḥ |
drutayo militā yena yantraṃ tenaiva kacchapam || 1,4.434 ||
limpecca biḍayogena melayejjārayetkramāt |
ityevaṃ rasarājasya ṣaḍguṇāṃ jārayeddrutim || 1,4.435 ||
<14. rañjanam>
drutijīrṇasya sūtasya rañjanaṃ śṛṇu pārvati |
tasmādrañjakabījānāṃ rañjanaṃ ca vadāmi te || 1,4.436 ||
<rañjanatailavidhiḥ>
palāśapuṣpaṃ mañjiṣṭhā lohitaṃ karavīrajam |
puṣpaṃ ca khadiraṃ raktacandanaṃ kukkuṭī tathā || 1,4.437 ||
niśādvayaṃ ca saralaṃ devadāruṃ japāsumam |
anyāni raktapuṣpāṇi lākṣātoyena mardayet || 1,4.438 ||
etaccaturguṇaṃ tailaṃ tailādraktaprasūnajam |
dravaṃ caturguṇaṃ deyaṃ tailaśeṣaṃ yathā bhavet || 1,4.439 ||
tasminniṣecayedbījamekaviṃśativārakam |
rañjayetpakvabījāni sarvāṇyevaṃ surārcite || 1,4.440 ||
<nāgabījarañjanam>
kharpare nāgamādāya caṇḍāgniṃ jvālayedadhaḥ |
palāśaparṇabījāni kṣipettasminpracālayet || 1,4.441 ||
palāśadaṇḍenāmardyaṃ caturyāmena bhasmati |
tadbhasma gandhakaṃ tulyamamle yāmaṃ prapeṣayet || 1,4.442 ||
ruddhvā puṭedgajapuṭe svāṅgaśītaṃ taduddharet |
taccaturthāṃśakaṃ gandhaṃ dattvā piṣṭvā puṭe pacet || 1,4.443 ||
evaṃ dvisaptavāreṇa nāgaṃ syādraktavarṇakam |
vāśāpañcāṅgacūrṇaṃ ca taccūrṇaṃ kakubhasya ca || 1,4.444 ||
śigrukiṃśukakoraṇḍaśākapuṣpāṇi nāginīm |
ahimāraṃ kumārīṃ ca nāgakanyāṃ ca cūrṇayet || 1,4.445 ||
etatsarvaṃ caikabhāgaṃ dvibhāgaṃ ca manaḥśilām |
bhāṇḍe sarvaṃ vinikṣipya gavāṃ mūtre caturguṇe || 1,4.446 ||
pacetpādāvaśeṣaṃ tu pūrvanāge kṣipankṣipan |
cullyāṃ pacedbrahmadaṇḍaiścālayetsaptavāsaram || 1,4.447 ||
idaṃ nāgaṃ pakvabīje drute nirvāhayettridhā |
rañjitaṃ jāyate bījaṃ mukhyaṃ syādrasarañjane || 1,4.448 ||
<bījarañjanaprakāraḥ>
tāpyasatvaṃ ca kṛṣṇābhrasatvacūrṇaṃ dhametsamam |
tasmindrute tāpyacūrṇaṃ mṛtaśulbaṃ kramādvahet || 1,4.449 ||
triguṇaṃ tadbhavedbījaṃ rañjakaṃ parameśvari |
<anyaḥ prakāraḥ>
kunaṭī mākṣikaṃ gandhaṃ daradaṃ peṣayetsamam || 1,4.450 ||
raktavargasya gomūtre peṣitasya rase priye |
peṣayecchoṣayetsaptavāraṃ taccūrṇayetkṣipet || 1,4.451 ||
dvaṃdvite tīkṣṇatāmre ca tulyaṃ kṣiptvā dhameddṛḍham |
jyotiṣmatītailayukte raktavarge niṣecayet || 1,4.452 ||
punaśca pūrvacūrṇaṃ tu tulyaṃ kṣiptvā dhametpriye |
secayetsaptadhā tvevaṃ syādbījaṃ rasarañjakam || 1,4.453 ||
<anyaḥ prakāraḥ>
tāraṃ ca vimalāsattvaṃ samāṃśaṃ drāvayettataḥ |
saraktavarge gomūtre bhāvitaṃ daradaṃ tridhā || 1,4.454 ||
tattulyaṃ nikṣipettasmin dhamedevaṃ tu saptadhā |
kṣiptvā kṣiptvā ca daradaṃ syādbījaṃ rasarañjakam || 1,4.455 ||
<anyaḥ prakāraḥ>
ghanasattvamahiṃ svarṇaṃ samāṃśaṃ dvaṃdvitaṃ dhamet |
kharpare raktavargaṃ ca mākṣikaṃ gairikaṃ śilām || 1,4.456 ||
samaṃ saṃcūrṇayettasmin vāpayecca samaṃ samam |
daśavāraṃ bhavedetadbījaṃ sūtendrarañjakam || 1,4.457 ||
<anyaḥ prakāraḥ>
yavaciñcārasairbhāvyā raktavarṇā manaḥśilā |
viṃśadvāraṃ prayatnena tena kalkena lepayet || 1,4.458 ||
nāgapatraṃ puṭe pacyādyāvaccūrṇamupāgatam |
rasakasya tu bhāgāṃstrīn bhāgaikaṃ daradasya ca || 1,4.459 ||
śilāgandhaviṣāṇāṃ ca trayāṇāmekabhāgakam |
secayenmātuluṅgāmlaiḥ tena kalkena lepayet || 1,4.460 ||
mūṣāgarbhe kṣipettattatpūrvanāgaṃ dhamettataḥ |
drutaṃ yāvatsamuddhṛtya liptvā mūṣāṃ punardhamet || 1,4.461 ||
ityevaṃ saptadhā dhāmyaṃ nāgaṃ svarṇanibhaṃ bhavet |
<anyaḥ prakāraḥ>
pītābhrakasya satvaṃ tu pūrvanāgaṃ ca tatsamam || 1,4.462 ||
dvaṃdvitaṃ pūrvayogena hyabhiṣiktaṃ ca kārayet |
anena dvandvayogena vāpo deyo drutasya ca || 1,4.463 ||
pakvabījasya vārāṃstrīn tadbījaṃ rañjitaṃ śubham |
<tārabījarañjanam>
athātastārabījānāṃ rañjanaṃ śṛṇu pārvati || 1,4.464 ||
nānābhūruhasambhūtaśvetapuṣparase priye |
caturbhāge caikabhāgaṃ kaṅguṇītailakaṃ kṣipet || 1,4.465 ||
tailāvaśeṣaṃ vipacettārabījāni tatra vai |
bhūyo bhūyo drāvayitvā secayedekaviṃśatim || 1,4.466 ||
bījāni rañjitānyevaṃ bhaveyū rasarañjane |
evaṃ vaṅgasya bījāni rañjayetparameśvari || 1,4.467 ||
<rañjanakramaḥ>
drutijīrṇasya sūtasya catuḥṣaṣṭitamāṃśakam |
nikṣipedrañjakaṃ bījaṃ taptakhalve dināvadhi || 1,4.468 ||
mardayettaptakhalve tu pacetkacchapayantrake |
evaṃ grāsakrameṇaiva ṣaḍguṇaṃ jārayetpriye || 1,4.469 ||
<15. - 17. sāraṇatrayam; sāraṇārthe vajrabījasādhanam; prathamaḥ prakāraḥ>
rañjitasya rasendrasya pravakṣye sāraṇātrayam |
sāraṇā yogyabījāni divyāni ca surārcite || 1,4.470 ||
svarṇaṃ dvādaśabhāgaṃ syāttadardhaṃ śuddhapāradam |
rasendrārdhaṃ nāgabhasma caturbhāgaṃ mṛtaṃ pavim || 1,4.471 ||
etaccatuṣṭayaṃ cāmlairmardayettaptakhalvake |
uddhṛtya dvandvamelāpamūṣāyāṃ rodhayeddhamet || 1,4.472 ||
haṭhāttacca milatyeva vajrabījamidaṃ priye |
<dvitīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
bhāvayecchāribākṣīrairmṛtaṃ vajraṃ dinaṃ tataḥ || 1,4.473 ||
mardayedgolakaṃ kṛtvā śoṣayeccaikabhāgakam |
tribhāgaṃ rasarājaṃ ca caturbhāgaṃ suvarṇakam || 1,4.474 ||
amlena mardayetpiṣṭiṃ kṛtvā tadvajragolakam |
tatpiṣṭyā veṣṭayettacca bhūrjapatreṇa veṣṭayet || 1,4.475 ||
pakṣaṃ nyaseddhānyarāśau taduddhṛtya punaḥ priye |
dhameddhaṭhānmilatyeva vajrabījamidaṃ bhavet || 1,4.476 ||
vajraṣoḍaśabhāgena svarṇapatreṇa veṣṭayet |
tatkṣiptvā liptamūṣāyāṃ svarṇāṃśaṃ śvetakācajam || 1,4.477 ||
cūrṇaṃ vā nṛkapālaṃ vā cūrṇaṃ kṛtvā vinikṣipet |
nirudhya ca dhamettīvramevaṃ kuryātpunaḥ punaḥ || 1,4.478 ||
kṣiptvā kṣiptvā śvetakācaṃ nṛkapālamathāpi vā |
saptadhaivaṃ milatyeva vajrabījamidaṃ bhavet || 1,4.479 ||
<sāraṇātailam>
ataḥ paraṃ sāraṇāyāṃ pravakṣye tailasādhanam |
jyotiṣmatīkarañjākṣakaṭutumbīsamudbhavam || 1,4.480 ||
tailaṃ kūrmavarāhādimeṣamatsyasamudbhavam |
jalūkābhekajātā ca vasā grāhyā vidhānataḥ || 1,4.481 ||
raktavargaḥ pītavargaḥ kvāthyaḥ kṣīraiścaturguṇaiḥ |
puṣpāṇāṃ raktapītānāmanekānāṃ dravaṃ haret || 1,4.482 ||
pāṭalīkākatuṇḍyutthamahārāṣṭrīdravaṃ tathā |
ekāṃśaṃ tailamekāṃśā vasā kvāthaścaturguṇaḥ || 1,4.483 ||
dvyaṃśaiḥ puṣpadravairdvyaṃśān pāṭalyādidravānapi |
sarvaṃ tāmramaye pātre mṛdunā vahninā pacet || 1,4.484 ||
tasminkṣipetṣoḍaśāṃśaṃ bhūlatāmalatāpyakam |
vikhyātaṃ sāraṇātailaṃ rasarājasya jāraṇe || 1,4.485 ||
rañjitaṃ rasarājaṃ tu sāraṇātailasaṃyutam |
nikṣipetsāraṇāyantre nālamūṣāṃ gataṃ drutam || 1,4.486 ||
bījaṃ tu ḍhālayetsūte catuḥṣaṣṭitamāṃśakam |
uddhṛtya taptakhalve tu paṭvamlairmardayeddinam || 1,4.487 ||
tataḥ kacchapayantre tu savivye pācayeddinam |
ityevaṃ ṣaḍguṇaṃ sāryaṃ sārito jāyate rasaḥ || 1,4.488 ||
evaṃ dvitīyavāre tu kṛte sūto'nusāritaḥ |
tathā tṛtīyavāre tu rasaḥ syātpratisāritaḥ || 1,4.489 ||
<18. vedhaḥ>
sāritasya rasendrasya haritālaṃ samāṃśakam |
mardayeddhūrtatailaiśca dinaṃ divyauṣadhidravaiḥ || 1,4.490 ||
tata uddhṛtya kṣipedvajramūṣāyāṃ vipaceddinam |
karīṣāgnāviti punarmardyaṃ pācyaṃ tridhā priye || 1,4.491 ||
anena karmaṇā devi sūto baddhamukho bhavet |
baddhavaktrasya sūtasya bhāgamekaṃ surārcite || 1,4.492 ||
sutāratāmratīkṣṇānāṃ cūrṇānāmekabhāgakam |
sarvaṃ saṃmardayeddevadālīnīrairdinaṃ tataḥ || 1,4.493 ||
madhvājyaiśca dinaṃ mardyaṃ tena kuryātsugolakam |
nikṣipedvajramūṣāyāṃ ruddhvā tīvrāgninā dhamet || 1,4.494 ||
sa rasaḥ khoṭabaddhaḥ syāttatkhoṭaṃ kācaṭaṅkaṇaiḥ |
tejaḥpuñjo bhavedyāvattāvatkuryāddhaman dhaman || 1,4.495 ||
taṃ rasaṃ yojayeddehe lohe roge ca pārvati |
<dehavedhakramaḥ>
dehavedhaṃ pravakṣyāmi sāvadhānaṃ śṛṇu priye || 1,4.496 ||
pācanasnehanasvedavamanārecanaiḥ kramāt |
śarīraṃ śodhayelloṇakṣārāmlādivivarjitaḥ || 1,4.497 ||
tatastvāroṭakaṃ sūtaṃ bhakṣayetparameśvari |
kṣetraṃ kṛtvā śarīraṃ tu tataḥ siddharasaṃ kṣipet || 1,4.498 ||
śatavedhirasaṃ pūrvaṃ tataḥ sāhasravedhakam |
daśasāhasravedhaṃ ca lakṣavedhakaraṃ rasam || 1,4.499 ||
vedhakaṃ daśalakṣasya koṭivedhaṃ surārcite |
gandhakena yutaṃ sūtaṃ krameṇānena suvrate || 1,4.500 ||
sa rasaḥ kramate dehe siddhayaḥ sambhavanti hi |
rasāyane tu yāḥ proktā mūlikā dehasiddhidāḥ || 1,4.501 ||
tābhiryukto rasendrastu dehe saṃkramate priye |
krāmati tanau hi sūto janayati putrāṃścadevatāgarbhān || 1,4.502 ||
khe gamanena ca nityaṃ saṃcaraṇaṃ sakalabhuvaneṣu |
dhātā bhuvanatritaye sraṣṭādyo brahmayoniriva || 1,4.503 ||
viṣṇuriva pālanārthe saṃhartā rudradevavatsatkalam |
<lohavedhakramaḥ>
krāmaṇaṃ lohavedhasya vakṣyāmi śṛṇu bhairavi || 1,4.504 ||
daradaṃ mākṣikaṃ kāntaṃ śilāṃ gandhaṃ viṣaṃ ghanam |
rasakaṃ bhūlatāṃ hiṅgu kāntāsyaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ priye || 1,4.505 ||
gandhakena hataṃ tāmraṃ bhujaṅgaṃ śilayā hatam |
daradena hataṃ tīkṣṇaṃ mahiṣīkarṇasambhavam || 1,4.506 ||
malaṃ vāyasaviṣṭhāṃ ca prathamārtavaraktataḥ |
anena veṣṭayetsiddhasūtaṃ loheṣu vedhayet || 1,4.507 ||
tārakṛṣṭyaṣṭanavatiḥ svarṇamekaṃ tathā rasaḥ |
śatavedhī tu vikhyātastvevaṃ sāhasravedhakaḥ || 1,4.508 ||
vedhayeddaśasāhasraṃ lakṣaṃ koṭimathārbudam |
jāraṇāyāṃ balaṃ yāvattāvallohāni vedhayet || 1,4.509 ||
<1. krāmaṇayogaḥ>
mākṣikaṃ bhūlatāṃ sūtaṃ kunaṭīṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ tathā |
strīstanyaraktaṃ sampiṣṭaṃ krāmaṇaṃ kṣepalepayoḥ || 1,4.510 ||
<2. krāmaṇayogaḥ>
hiṅgulaṃ rasakaṃ kāntamindragopaṃ viṣaṃ tathā |
mardayettailaraktābhyāṃ krāmaṇaṃ kṣepalepayoḥ || 1,4.511 ||
<3. krāmaṇayogaḥ>
gaṇḍolaviṣabhekāsyamahiṣīnetrajaṃ malam |
rudhireṇa samāyuktaṃ rasasaṃkrāmaṇe hitam || 1,4.512 ||
<4. krāmaṇayogaḥ>
rocanaṃ gugguluṃ stanyamindragopaṃ viṣaṃ tathā |
sarvaṃ saṃmardayeddevi rasasaṃkrāmaṇe hitam || 1,4.513 ||
<5. krāmaṇayogaḥ>
viṣṇukrāntā madhūcchiṣṭaṃ māhiṣaṃ karṇajaṃ malam |
bhūlatā kākaviṣṭhā ca lāṅgalī dvipadīrajaḥ || 1,4.514 ||
nararaktaṃ brahmasomā surasā śailajaṃ priye |
śṛṅgī ca lakṣmaṇā gṛdhrakarṇī ca krāmaṇaṃ param || 1,4.515 ||
<6. krāmaṇayogaḥ>
śrīveṣṭanimbaniryāsastrīstanyaviṣaṭaṅkaṇaiḥ |
goghṛtena samāyukto lohe saṃkrāmate rasaḥ || 1,4.516 ||
<7. krāmaṇayogaḥ>
paramaṃ krāmaṇaṃ vaṅgaṃ mṛganābhaṃ ca pārvati |
mātṛvāhaḥ kulīraśca śaṅkhābhyantarajo malaḥ || 1,4.517 ||
tathā kapitthaniryāso rasasaṃkrāmaṇaṃ param |
krāmaṇaṃ rasarājasya vedhakāle pradāpayet || 1,4.518 ||


āk, 1, 5
<viśeṣajāraṇākramaḥ>
śrībhairavī |
sāmānyajāraṇā proktā tvayā pūrvaṃ sadāśiva |
viśeṣajāraṇaṃ brūhi yathā jānāmyahaṃ prabho || 1,5.1 ||
śrībhairavaḥ |
śṛṇu devi vakṣyāmi bhūcarākhyaṃ tu jāraṇam |
<bhūcarī jāraṇā>
kṛṣṇaṃ pītaṃ tathā raktaṃ śulbe tīkṣṇe ca melayet || 1,5.2 ||
śulbaṃ śuddhaṃ yadā jīrṇaṃ dvāviṃśatiguṇaṃ mate |
gandhanāgaṃ tato'rdhaṃ tu krameṇaiva tu melayet || 1,5.3 ||
hemnā tu saha dātavyaṃ sūtakaikena ṣoḍaśa |
gandhanāgaṃ yadā jīrṇaṃ tadā baddho bhavedrasaḥ || 1,5.4 ||
hemni jīrṇe tato'rdhena mṛtalohena rañjayet |
gandhakena hataṃ śulbaṃ mākṣikaṃ daradāyasam || 1,5.5 ||
puṭena mārayecchuddhaṃ hema dadyāttu ṣaḍguṇam |
sūtake hemabījaṃ ca yadā jīrṇaṃ caturguṇam || 1,5.6 ||
baddharāgaṃ vijānīyāddhemābho jāyate rasaḥ |
sāraṇāyantramadhyasthaṃ tenaiva saha sārayet || 1,5.7 ||
tribhāgaṃ sāritaṃ kṛtvā punastatraiva jārayet |
jāritaḥ sāritaścaiva punarjāritasāritaḥ || 1,5.8 ||
saptaśṛṅkhalikāyogātkoṭivedhī bhavedrasaḥ |
bhūcarī jāraṇā proktā khecarīṃ jāraṇāṃ śṛṇu || 1,5.9 ||
<khecarī jāraṇā>
hīnarāgāni ratnāni rasocchiṣṭāni kārayet |
kaṭutuṃbasya bījāni tasyārdhena tu dāpayet || 1,5.10 ||
mahājārasamāyuktaṃ kalkaṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ |
vajramūṣāmukhe caiva tanmadhye sthāpayedrasam || 1,5.11 ||
katakaṃ kanakaṃ caivamekīkṛtya vimardayet |
padmarāgapralepaṃ tu rase grāsaṃ tu dāpayet || 1,5.12 ||
bhakṣitavyaṃ prayatnena natvā ca gurudevayoḥ |
sarvasiddhānnamaskṛtya devatāśca viśeṣataḥ || 1,5.13 ||
mūrcchāṅgadāhaśca tato jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
ātmānamutthitaṃ paśyeddivyadeho mahābalaḥ || 1,5.14 ||
śaṅkhakāhalanirghoṣaiḥ siddhavidyādharaiḥ saha |
icchayā vicarellokān kāmarūpī vimānagaḥ || 1,5.15 ||
devā vai yatra līyante siddhastatraiva līyate |
<anyo jāraṇāprakāraḥ>
punaranyaṃ pravakṣyāmi jāraṇāyogamuttamam || 1,5.16 ||
sughṛṣṭaṃ pātitaṃ sūtaṃ sarvadoṣojjhitaṃ tataḥ |
śākapallavasāreṇa viṣṇukrāntārasena ca || 1,5.17 ||
pālāśapuṣpatoyena bhāvitaṃ gandhakaṃ samam |
samaṃ kṛṣṇābhrasatvaṃ ca rasakaṃ cāṣṭakaṃ guṇam || 1,5.18 ||
tīkṣṇaśulboragaṃ caiva kūrmayantreṇa jārayet |
kāñcanaṃ jārayetpaścādbiḍayogena pārvati || 1,5.19 ||
tataḥ siddhaṃ vijānīyāddvaidhaṃ śulvasya dāpayet |
karmasaṃkhyāpramāṇena nāgo bhavati kāñcanam || 1,5.20 ||
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi jāraṇākramamuttamam |
bījacūrṇāni tailena bhāvayitvā punaḥ punaḥ || 1,5.21 ||
ṣoḍaśāṃśena taṃ grāsamaṅgulyā mardayecchanaiḥ |
ārdrakādi tato yogāddātavyaṃ ṣoḍaśāṃśataḥ || 1,5.22 ||
bhūrje dattvā tato deyaṃ ḍolāyantre vinikṣipet |
ahorātreṇa tadbījaṃ sūtako grasati priye || 1,5.23 ||
samuddhṛtya rasaṃ devi khalve saṃmardayettataḥ |
tato yantre vinikṣipya divārātraṃ dṛḍhāgninā || 1,5.24 ||
taptaṃ samuddhṛtaṃ yantrāttaptakhalve vimardayet |
mardayitvārdrake piṇḍe kṣiptvātha tripuṭaṃ dahet || 1,5.25 ||
tato garbhe patatyāśu jarate tatsukhena tu |
ḍolāyantre tato dadyādārdrapiṇḍena saṃyutam || 1,5.26 ||
tṛtīyadivase sūto jarate grasate tataḥ |
samajīrṇaṃ tato yāvatḍolāyantre vicakṣaṇaḥ || 1,5.27 ||
paścātkacchapayantreṇa samajīrṇaṃ tu pārvati |
tāmrāṃśadvādaśāṃśena kacchapena tu jārayet || 1,5.28 ||
prāgvadārdrakayogaṃ ca garbhadrāvaṇameva ca |
paścāttaṃ devi nikṣipya puṭaṃ dadyādvicakṣaṇaḥ || 1,5.29 ||
aṣṭāṃśena tato grāsaṃ garbhadrāvaṃ ca pūrvavat |
kandodare sūraṇasya taṃ vinikṣipya sūtakam || 1,5.30 ||
puṭayedvārtikastāvatyāvatkando na dahyate |
pādāṃśena tu mūṣāyāṃ grāsaḥ sūte vidhīyate || 1,5.31 ||
pūrvavacca biḍaṃ dadyādgarbhadrāvaṇameva ca |
evaṃ caturguṇe jīrṇe sūtako balavān bhavet || 1,5.32 ||
tataḥ śalākayā grāsamagnistho grasate rasaḥ |
tato ratnāni jāryāṇi vakṣyamāṇakrameṇa tu || 1,5.33 ||
abhrakaṃ bhrāmakaṃ caiva śaṅkhanābhiṃ tathaiva ca |
rasānuparasāndattvā mahājārasamanvitam || 1,5.34 ||
vajrakandalatāṃ brāhmīmeṣaśṛṅgyamṛtāyasam |
kaṭutumbasya bījāni mṛtalohāni pācayet || 1,5.35 ||
sarvāṇi samabhāgāni śikhiśoṇitamarditam |
tāvattaṃ mardayetprājño yāvatkarma dṛḍhaṃ bhavet || 1,5.36 ||
mūṣā mallākṛtiścaiva kartavyācchādanaiḥ saha |
tanmadhye sthāpayetsūtamadhovātena dhāmayet || 1,5.37 ||
ādau tāvatprakartavyaṃ vajramauṣadhalepitam |
gṛhyate nātra sandeho yathā tīvrahutāśane || 1,5.38 ||
kuliśādi bhaveddagdhaṃ karīṣā tena mardayet |
yāvadekādaśaguṇaṃ kuliśaṃ jārayedbudhaḥ || 1,5.39 ||
saṃdagdhā śaṅkhanābhiśca mātuluṅgarasaplutā |
muktāphalaṃ tato deyaṃ vajrajīrṇe tu sūtake || 1,5.40 ||
anena kramayogena hyekādaśaguṇaṃ bhavet |
kevalaṃ śikhipittaṃ ca nīlīniryāsamiśritam || 1,5.41 ||
nīlotpalāni liptāni nikṣiptāni tu sūtake |
rasaḥ pibenmahārāgān hīnarāgān parityajet || 1,5.42 ||
ratnāni śikhipittaṃ ca mahāratnasamanvitam |
sadratnaṃ lepayettena prakṣipedrasamadhyataḥ || 1,5.43 ||
rajanī caiva kaṅkuṣṭhaṃ brahmaniryāsabhāvitam |
jāraṇaṃ puṣparāgasya tenaiva saha dāpayet || 1,5.44 ||
bahuratneṣu jīrṇeṣu bhṛṅgarājeṣu suvrate |
rasendro dṛśyatāṃ devi nīlapītāruṇacchaviḥ || 1,5.45 ||
<dhūmavedhīrasaḥ>
śuddhāni hemapatrāṇi śatāṃśenānulepayet |
puṭena mārayedetadindragopanibhaṃ bhavet || 1,5.46 ||
saṃsparśādvedhayetsarvamidaṃ hema mṛtaṃ priye |
tribhāgaṃ sūtakendrasya teneva saha kārayet || 1,5.47 ||
mūṣāmadhye sthite tasmin punastenaiva jārayet |
dhūmavedhī bhaveddevi punaḥ punaḥ prasāritaḥ || 1,5.48 ||
anena kramayogena yadi jīrṇā triśṛṅkhalā |
vedhayennātra sandeho giripāṣāṇabhūtalam || 1,5.49 ||
pārśve jyotiḥ pradṛśyeta ūrdhvaṃ dṛśyeta tena vai |
bhūcaraṃ taṃ vijānīyādrasendraṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ || 1,5.50 ||
tenāśrāntagatirdevi yojanānāṃ śataṃ vrajet |
divyatejā mahākāyo divyadṛṣṭirmahābalaḥ || 1,5.51 ||
sarvarogavinirmukto jīveccandrārkatārakam |
tasya mūtrapurīṣaṃ tu sarvalohāni vidhyati || 1,5.52 ||
<anye vedhaprakārāḥ>
samajīrṇena vajreṇa hemnā ca sahitena ca |
agnistho jārayellohān bandhamāyāti sūtakaḥ || 1,5.53 ||
sārayettena bījena sahasramapi vedhayet |
sāritaṃ jārayetpaścātlepyaṃ kṣepyaṃ sahasrataḥ || 1,5.54 ||
sārayettena bījena lakṣavedhamavāpnuyāt |
anena kramayogena koṭivedhī bhavedrasaḥ || 1,5.55 ||
kevalaṃ tu yadā vajraṃ samajīrṇaṃ tu kārayet |
baddhaḥ sūtastadā jñeyo niṣkampo nirupadravaḥ || 1,5.56 ||
agnistho jāyate sūtaḥ śalākāṃ grasate kṣaṇāt |
haṭhāgninā dhāmyamāno grasate sarvamādarāt || 1,5.57 ||
grasate jarate sūtamāyurdravyapradāyakaḥ |
mūṣāsthaṃ dhamayetsūtaṃ haṭhāgnau naiva kampayet || 1,5.58 ||
jārayetsarvaratnāni baddhaḥ khecaratāṃ nayet |
aṣṭalohe'ṣṭaguṇite jīrṇe syādrasabandhanam || 1,5.59 ||
lohāni sarvāṃstriguṇaṃ triguṇaṃ kanakaṃ tathā |
dhūmāvaloke vedhī syātbhavennirvāṇado'mbaraḥ || 1,5.60 ||
ādāvaṣṭaguṇaṃ jāryaṃ vyomasattvaṃ mahārase |
samaṃ hemadaśāṃśena vajraratnāni jārayet || 1,5.61 ||
samaṃ ca jārayedvajraṃ tadāsau khecaro rasaḥ |
krame pradakṣiṇāvartaḥ koṭivedhī ca jāyate || 1,5.62 ||
<jīrṇadravyamānabhedādvedhe viśeṣaḥ>
same tu pannage jīrṇe daśavedhī bhavedrasaḥ |
dviguṇe śatavedhī syātsahasraṃ triguṇe bhavet || 1,5.63 ||
caturguṇe'yutaṃ devi krameṇānena vardhayet |
uttarottaravṛddhyā tu jārayettatra pannagam || 1,5.64 ||
kuṭilaṃ pannagaṃ jāryaṃ navasaṃkhyākrameṇa tu |
dattvā krameṇa deveśi koṭivedhī bhavedrasaḥ || 1,5.65 ||
gandhakādimapāṣāṇe ṣaḍguṇe jīrṇatāṃ gate |
rogahartā rasaḥ syāttu samukhe tavadā bhavet || 1,5.66 ||
tasmin śataguṇe jīrṇe rugjarāmṛtyuhā bhavet |
abhrakādimapāṣāṇasattvānyātmasamaṃ grasan || 1,5.67 ||
rugjarā maraṇaṃ jitvā śatavedhī raso bhavet |
śatāyurdviguṇe jīrṇe sūtaḥ sāhasravedhakaḥ || 1,5.68 ||
caturguṇe sahasrāyuḥ pārado'yutavedhakaḥ |
rasaścāṣṭaguṇe jīrṇe lakṣāyurlakṣavedhakaḥ || 1,5.69 ||
brahmāyuḥ ṣoḍaśaguṇe koṭivedhī bhavedrasaḥ |
dvātriṃśadguṇite jīrṇe khecaratvādisiddhidaḥ || 1,5.70 ||
viṣṇorāyurbalaṃ datte pāradaḥ sparśavedhakaḥ |
catuḥṣaṣṭiguṇe jīrṇe śivāyuḥ śabdavedhakaḥ || 1,5.71 ||
<ṣaḍguṇābhrakajāraṇena sarvadoṣanāśaḥ>
rasasya sarvadoṣāstu ṣaḍguṇenābhrakeṇa tu |
jīrṇena nāśamāyānti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 1,5.72 ||
tadā grasati lohāni tyajecca gatimātmanaḥ |
<pañcāvasthā>
dhūmaściṭiciṭiścaiva maṇḍūkaplutireva ca || 1,5.73 ||
sakampaśca vikampaśca pañcāvasthā rasasya tu |
samajīrṇo bhavedbālo yauvanasthaścaturguṇaḥ || 1,5.74 ||
vṛddhaḥ ṣaḍguṇajīrṇastu sarvakarmakaraḥ śubhaḥ |
<jāraṇe kramaḥ>
gandhakaṃ jārayedādau sarvasattvānyataḥ param || 1,5.75 ||
tataḥ sarvāṇi lohāni dvandvāni vividhāni ca |
pakvabījāni ratnāni drutisattve ca jārayet || 1,5.76 ||
catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśake pūrvaṃ dvātriṃśāṃśe tṛtīyakaḥ |
tṛtīyaḥ ṣoḍaśāṃśe tu caturthaścāṣṭamena ca || 1,5.77 ||
pañcamo'tha caturthāṃśe ṣaṣṭho dvyaṃśe prakīrtitaḥ |
śatāṃśe saptamo jñeyo grāsamānaṃ rasasya tu || 1,5.78 ||
<grāsamānabhedena svarūpabhedaḥ>
catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśake grāse daṇḍadhārī bhavedrasaḥ |
jalūkābho dvitīye tu grāsayoge sureśvari || 1,5.79 ||
grāse rasāttṛtīye ca kākaviṣṭhāsamo bhavet |
caturtho golakākāraḥ pañcame dahanaprabhaḥ || 1,5.80 ||
ṣaṣṭhe sūryaprabhaḥ sūtastejaḥpuñjaśca saptame |
rasarājasya deveśi kramājjīrṇasya lakṣaṇam || 1,5.81 ||
samāṃśaṃ dviguṇaṃ grāsaṃ tataḥ sūte caturguṇam |
tathā cāṣṭaguṇaṃ devi jārayecca kalāguṇam || 1,5.82 ||
dvātriṃśadguṇitaṃ devi catuḥṣaṣṭiguṇaṃ kramāt |
<saṃskārairjāyamānā guṇāḥ>
tīvratvaṃ janayetsvedādamalatvaṃ ca mardanāt || 1,5.83 ||
mūrchanāddoṣarāhityamutthānātpūtināśanam |
rasāyanaṃ pātanena rodhādāpyāyanaṃ bhavet || 1,5.84 ||
acāpalyaṃ niyamanāddīpanātsamukho jvalet |
vāsanādyogasāṃgatyaṃ cāraṇādbalacāritā || 1,5.85 ||
jāraṇādbandhanaṃ samyagekatvaṃ drāvaṇadvayāt |
raktatvaṃ rañjanāttasya vyāpitvaṃ sāraṇātrayāt || 1,5.86 ||
krāmitvaṃ krāmaṇāddehaloheṣvapi ca vedhanāt || 1,5.87 ||


āk, 1, 6
<dehavedhakramaḥ>
praṇamya śirasā śambhuṃ papraccha girijātmajā |
śrībhairavī |
dehavedhastvayā pūrvaṃ saṃkṣepātkathitaḥ prabho || 1,6.1 ||
taṃ dehavedhamācakṣva samyakjānāmyahaṃ yathā |
śrībhairavaḥ |
devi pravakṣyāmi devānāmapi durlabham || 1,6.2 ||
yogināṃ bhogayuktānāṃ dehavedhaṃ sureśvari |
pācanādi prakurvīta pañcakarmavidhānataḥ || 1,6.3 ||
<pañcakarmāṇi; 1. pācanam>
lavaṇādisamutkliṣṭān doṣān saṃśodhayetkramāt |
tato jīrṇarasaṃ cādyātkramādevaṃ sureśvari || 1,6.4 ||
laghvāhāro divā bhūtvā kṣudrādhānyākanāgaram |
etattrayaṃ palonmeyamudakeṣvavaloḍayet || 1,6.5 ||
aṣṭāvaśiṣṭaṃ saṃkvāthya pratirātraṃ pibettryaham |
anantaraṃ varākvāthaṃ pūrvavattridinaṃ pibet || 1,6.6 ||
iti pācanamātanyādatha snehanamācaret |
<2. snehanam>
ghṛtaudanaṃ chāgarasaṃ divā bhuñjīta mātrayā || 1,6.7 ||
ājyānnaṃ vā mudgarasaṃ laghvāśī syāddine sudhīḥ |
rātrau pibedghṛtaṃ gavyaṃ saindhavena samanvitam || 1,6.8 ||
niṣkamekaṃ saindhavaṃ ca ghṛtaṃ niṣkacatuṣṭayam |
bhṛṅgāmalakatailena sarvāṅgamabhiṣecayet || 1,6.9 ||
evaṃ saptadinaṃ kuryātsnehanaṃ paramaṃ hitam |
<3. svedanam>
matsyamāṃsaṃ māṣatilayavajāmalasaktavaḥ || 1,6.10 ||
sarvametadbhavetprasthamekāṣṭhīlāgarūbalā |
rāsnā vyāghrī ghanāpatraṃ kauśikātiviṣā niśā || 1,6.11 ||
sarvametaddvayapalaṃ takrakṣīrāmbukāṃjikam |
dvyāḍhake nikṣipetsarvaṃ mṛtpātre kvāthayetsudhīḥ || 1,6.12 ||
kuryāttasyoṣmaṇā gātraṃ svinnaṃ dvighaṭikāvadhi |
evaṃ saptadinaṃ kuryātsvedanaṃ paramaṃ hitam || 1,6.13 ||
<4 vamanam>
madanasya phalaṃ caikaṃ pāṭhā ṣoḍaśikaṃ jalam |
pādāvaśiṣṭaṃ saṃkvāthya tajjale vastraśodhite || 1,6.14 ||
pippalīndrayavā yaṣṭilavaṇaṃ karṣamātrakam |
nikṣipecca pibetprātarvāntiḥ syātsarvarogahā || 1,6.15 ||
<5. virecanam>
sūtaṭaṅkaṇagandhāśma trikaṭuṃ triphalāṃ samam |
etaiḥ samaṃ tu jepālaṃ ślakṣṇaṃ tatparimardayet || 1,6.16 ||
guñjādvitayamātraṃ tu guḍena saha bhakṣayet |
virecanamiti proktaṃ sarvavyādhivināśanam || 1,6.17 ||
pañcakarmeti kathitaṃ kramātkuryādvirecane |
<doṣaśodhakayogāḥ>
ketakīstanajambīraṃ pratyahaṃ kuḍuvaṃ pibet || 1,6.18 ||
saptavāsaraparyantaṃ prātarlavaṇadoṣahṛt |
salile ṣoḍaśapale triphalaikapalaṃ kṣipet || 1,6.19 ||
kvāthayetpādaśeṣaṃ tattasmin śīte madhoḥ palam |
pibetprabhāte tridinaṃ kṣāradoṣaharaṃ param || 1,6.20 ||
viḍaṅgaṃ savacākuṣṭhaṃ ketakīstanasaṃyutam |
kvathitaṃ tridinaṃ pītamamladoṣaharaṃ pibet || 1,6.21 ||
athavā tintriṇīkṣārasalilaṃ palamātrakam |
yavakṣārasitākarṣaṃ saṃyojya tridinaṃ pibet || 1,6.22 ||
amladoṣavināśo'yaṃ kathitaśca rasāyane |
vacābiḍālapālāśabījajantughnakarṣakam || 1,6.23 ||
karṣaṃ guḍaṃ ca tatsarvaṃ bhakṣayeduṣṇavāri ca |
pibetprātastridivasaṃ bhavettatkrimipātanam || 1,6.24 ||
śyāmāvahniviḍaṅgāni vāśā tryūṣaṃ phalatrayam |
saindhavaṃ devadāruṃ ca mustā caitatsamaṃ samam || 1,6.25 ||
ghṛtaiḥ karṣaṃ lihetprātaḥ saptāhātsarvarogajit |
evaṃ kṛtvā dehaśuddhiṃ śālyannaṃ kṣīrabhojanam || 1,6.26 ||
samyagjātabalo bhūtvā tataḥ kuryādrasāyanam |
<rasāyanopayogaḥ>
kṣīrājyāmalakarasakṣaudraiḥ suratarūdbhavam || 1,6.27 ||
tailaṃ nirmathya tatsarvaṃ dvipalaṃ pratyahaṃ pibet |
māsena kāntirmedhā ca bhavatyeva na saṃśayaḥ || 1,6.28 ||
dvitīyamāsi pūrvoktaṃ tatpibettaccatuḥpalam |
tena śāmyanti doṣāśca vikārā netrasambhavāḥ || 1,6.29 ||
punastṛtīye māse tu sevyaṃ ṣaṭpalamātrakam |
tenāmaravapurbhūyānmahātejā bhaveddhruvam || 1,6.30 ||
<āroṭarasasevākramaḥ>
athāroṭarasaḥ sevyaḥ krameṇa parameśvari |
ayaḥśulbābhratāpyebhyaḥ pātito māritaḥ kramāt || 1,6.31 ||
ayamāroṭakaraso dehasiddhikaraḥ paraḥ |
svedanādyaiśca saṃskāraiḥ saptabhiḥ saṃskṛto rasaḥ || 1,6.32 ||
mūrchito rañjito devi sūtastvāroṭakaḥ smṛtaḥ |
rasāyane rogaśāntyāṃ śreṣṭhaḥ sarvaguṇapradaḥ || 1,6.33 ||
kāntābhrasatvāroṭāśca samaṃ guñjādvayonmitam |
madhvājyatriphalābhiśca māsamekaṃ bhajediti || 1,6.34 ||
evaṃ krameṇa saṃsevyaṃ dvitrivedeṣuṣaṭkramāt |
evaṃ ṣoḍaśamāsāntaṃ guñjāṣoḍaśamātrakam || 1,6.35 ||
āroṭakarase cetthaṃ kuryānmatprāṇavallabhe |
valīpalitanirmukto jīvecca śaradaḥ śatam || 1,6.36 ||
<āroṭarasaḥ>
kāntasattvābhrasattvaṃ ca svarṇajīrṇarasastathā |
sarvametatsamīkṛtya bhajedāroṭakaṃ tathā || 1,6.37 ||
kṣetrīkaraṇametaddhi sahasrāyuṣyakārakam |
abhūmau yojitaḥ sūto na prarohati kutracit || 1,6.38 ||
tasmātkṣetramakṛtvaiva yojayedyastu sūtakam |
na prarohedasya śubhaṃ bījamivoṣare || 1,6.39 ||
<khoṭabaddharasasevākramaḥ>
pūrvoktakhoṭabaddhasya sūtasya vidhimuttamam |
krāmaṇaṃ ca kramādvakṣye tathā vidhiniṣedhanam || 1,6.40 ||
śṛṇu pārvati yatnena tvatprītyā kathayāmyaham |
kāntābhrasvarṇavajrāṇi rasasya krāmaṇaṃ param || 1,6.41 ||
krāmaṇena vinā sūto na sidhyeddehalohayoḥ |
guñjāmātraṃ khoṭabaddhaṃ krāmaṇakṣaudrasaṃyutam || 1,6.42 ||
māsamekaṃ bhajeditthaṃ punarvidhikrameṇa vai |
māsaṣoḍaśaparyantaṃ yathāroṭarasastathā || 1,6.43 ||
sa śatāyuṣyamāpnoti sarvarogavivarjitaḥ |
palamātropayogena vyādhibhirnābhibhūyate || 1,6.44 ||
dvitīye śukravṛddhiḥ syāttṛtīye balavān bhavet |
caturthe palitaṃ hanti valiṃ jayati pañcame || 1,6.45 ||
ṣaṣṭhe śrutidharo vāgmī saptame netrarogahṛt |
aṣṭame tārkṣyadṛṣṭiḥ syādbrahmāyurbrahmavikramaḥ || 1,6.46 ||
upayuñjyānnavapalaṃ siddhimelāpakaṃ bhavet |
viniyuñjyāddaśapalaṃ dvitīyaḥ śaṅkaro bhavet || 1,6.47 ||
sahasrāyuṣyakaraṃ sūtaṃ māṣamātraṃ bhajennaraḥ |
ayutāyuṣkaraṃ sūtaṃ yavamātraṃ bhajetpriye || 1,6.48 ||
lakṣāyuṣyakaraṃ sūtaṃ vrīhimātraṃ bhajetsudhīḥ |
koṭyāṃ bhajetsūtaṃ khādayanmudgamātrakam || 1,6.49 ||
etatsarvaṃ rasānāṃ tu krāmaṇaṃ pūrvavadbhavet |
sahasrāyuḥpradaḥ sūto brahmatvaṃ vidadhāti saḥ || 1,6.50 ||
ayutāyuṣkaraḥ sūto viṣṇutāṃ pradadāti ca |
lakṣāyuṣyakaraḥ sūto rudratvamupapādayet || 1,6.51 ||
koṭyāyuṣyapradaḥ sūtaḥ śivatvaṃ vidadhāti ca |
mākṣīkajīrṇasūtastu dhanadatvaṃ dadāti vai || 1,6.52 ||
indratvaṃ vimalājīrṇastvabhrajīrṇo'rkatāṃ dadet |
brahmatāṃ śulbajīrṇaśca viṣṇutāṃ tārajāritaḥ || 1,6.53 ||
rudratā hemajīrṇe syādīśatvaṃ vajrajārite |
sadāśivatvaṃ ca dadetpārado drutijāritaḥ || 1,6.54 ||
saccidānandarūpatvaṃ sūtako bījajāritaḥ |
sāmānyena tu tīkṣṇena rudratvaṃ prāpnuyānnaraḥ || 1,6.55 ||
evaṃ yo lohajīrṇaṃ tu bhakṣayedbhasmasūtakam |
jalena jalarūpī syātsthalena sthalatāṃ vrajet || 1,6.56 ||
tejastvaṃ tejasā devi vāyunā vāyurūpabhāk |
kartā hartā svayaṃ sākṣācchāpānugrahakārakaḥ || 1,6.57 ||
yatra mūtrapurīṣaṃ tu sādhakastu parityajet |
pāṣāṇaṃ mṛṇmayaṃ tatra spṛṣṭaṃ bhavati kāñcanam || 1,6.58 ||
prasvedāttasya gātrasya lohānyaṣṭau ca vedhayet |
tatsarvaṃ kanakaṃ divyaṃ bhakṣite dvādaśe pale || 1,6.59 ||
athavā tārajīrṇaṃ tu bhakṣayedbhasmasūtakam |
śulbāraṃ vaṅgaghoṣaṃ ca tatsvedāttāratā bhavet || 1,6.60 ||
athavā tīkṣṇajīrṇaṃ tu bhakṣayedbhasmasūtakam |
mūtreṇa tasya spṛṣṭaṃ tu vaṅgaṃ vrajati tāratām || 1,6.61 ||
ekaikena niṣekena stambhanaṃ nāgavaṅgayoḥ |
guñjāmātraṃ rasaṃ devi hemajīrṇaṃ tu bhakṣayet || 1,6.62 ||
dviguñjaṃ tārajīrṇasya ravijīrṇasya ca trayam |
tīkṣṇābhrakāntamāṣaikaṃ guṃjaikā dve'thavā bhavet || 1,6.63 ||
vajravaikrāntajīrṇaṃ tu bhakṣayetsarṣaponmitam |
nāgavaṅgavasākīṭaviṣopaviṣasaṃyutam || 1,6.64 ||
mūtrayuktaṃ haṭhādbaddhaṃ tyajetkalkaṃ rasāyane |
hematārapraveśena jāto yo'gnisahaḥ kramāt || 1,6.65 ||
baddhaśca rasarājo'yaṃ dehasiddhiprado bhavet |
vajrāyaskāntamākṣīkavaikrāntābhrakakāñcanam || 1,6.66 ||
etairjīrṇo yathālābhaṃ rasaḥ śasto rasāyane |
ṣaḍevoparasāścaiva bhakṣaṇārthaṃ rasāyane || 1,6.67 ||
bhasmanastīkṣṇajīrṇasya palamekaṃ tu bhakṣayet |
daśavarṣasahasrāṇi vajrakāyaḥ sa jīvati || 1,6.68 ||
evaṃ ca dvādaśapalaṃ tīkṣṇajīrṇasya bhakṣayet |
evaṃ jīvanmahākalpaṃ pralayānte śivaṃ vrajet || 1,6.69 ||
bhasmanaḥ śulbajīrṇasya palena brāhmamāyuṣam |
dvipale vaiṣṇavāyuṣyaṃ rudrāyustripalena tu || 1,6.70 ||
caturthe tu pale devi śivatvaṃ prāpnuyānnaraḥ |
hemajīrṇe bhasmasūte tripale bhakṣite kramāt || 1,6.71 ||
aṣṭāśītisahasrāṇi yoginyo madadarpitāḥ |
tasya tiṣṭhanti kiṃkaryaḥ kāmarūpī bhavennaraḥ || 1,6.72 ||
yadyadbhāvayate rūpaṃ tattadrūpadharo bhavet |
yatra yatra vilīyante siddhistatraiva līyate || 1,6.73 ||
mite pale dvādaśabhirhemabhasmani bhakṣite |
guṃjaikamātraṃ deveśi jñātvā cāgnibalaṃ nijam || 1,6.74 ||
ghṛtena madhunā cādyāttāmbūlaṃ kāminīṃ bhajet |
eko hi doṣaḥ sūkṣmo'pi bhakṣite bhasmasūtake || 1,6.75 ||
triḥsaptāhādvarārohe kāmāndho jāyate naraḥ |
kāminīnāṃ sahasraṃ tu kṣobhayeddivasāntare || 1,6.76 ||
nārīsaṅgādvarārohe dehe krāmati sūtakaḥ |
nārīsaṅgādvinā devi hyajīrṇaṃ tasya jīryate || 1,6.77 ||
maithunāccalite śukle trisaptāhādadhaḥ kṛtāt |
jāyate prāṇasaṃdehastāvanmaithunaṃ tyajet || 1,6.78 ||
yuvatyā jalpanaṃ kāryaṃ yuvatyā cāṅgamardanam |
yasyāḥ sparśanamātreṇa rasaḥ krāmati vigrahe || 1,6.79 ||
yathā tathā hlādayate sustrīrūpanirīkṣaṇam |
tathā krāmati deveśi sūtako'sau tataḥ kṣaṇāt || 1,6.80 ||
aśvatthasadṛśo yasyā ādhāraśca samaḥ śubhaḥ |
tādṛśastu bhago devi bhājane tu rasāyane || 1,6.81 ||
nirmāṃsaścaiva dīrghaśca bhagaḥ śuṣkaśirāstathā |
duḥkhadāridryakartāraṃ varjayettaṃ rasāyane || 1,6.82 ||
pakṣe śukle śubhadine candratārābalānvite |
sumuhūrte cintya śivānalavipragurūndvijaḥ || 1,6.83 ||
saṃtuṣṭaḥ sumanā bhūtvā rasaṃ seveta siddhidam |
payasā hemaśuṇṭhībhyāṃ nasyaṃ krāmaṇamuttamam || 1,6.84 ||
hemādi ṣaḍlohakṛtamañjanaṃ krāmaṇaṃ param |
<rasasevāyāṃ pathyāpathyam>
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi rasasevāhitāhitam || 1,6.85 ||
raktaśālyannagodhūmaṃ gavyaṃ kṣīraṃ ghṛtaṃ dadhi |
jāṅgalaṃ palalaṃ mudgaṃ śarkarā madhu saindhavam || 1,6.86 ||
haṃsodakaṃ vāstukaṃ ca meghanādaḥ punarnavā |
paṭolālābukadalīdhānyakekṣukadāḍimam || 1,6.87 ||
nālikerāmbu viśvaṃ ca sadā tāmbūlacarvaṇam |
gandhasāraṃ kuṅkumaṃ ca mṛganābhiṃ vilepayet || 1,6.88 ||
surabhīṇi supuṣpāṇi mṛduśayyeṣṭakāminīḥ |
śivātmajñānakathanaṃ mṛdubhāṣā sukhāsikā || 1,6.89 ||
divyāmbarāṇi śuddhāni chāyā cālpaviparyaṭam |
mṛtyuñjayajapo harṣo mandahāsaśca śuddhatā || 1,6.90 ||
nartanālokanaṃ gītaśravaṇaṃ śivapūjanam |
devāgniguruviprāṇāṃ vandanaṃ śrutipālanam || 1,6.91 ||
samādhiḥ prāṇikaruṇā satyavākyaṃ hitaṃ priyam |
vāgdehamanasā ceṣṭā yathā duḥkhanirodhinī || 1,6.92 ||
etatsarvaṃ rasendrasya krāmaṇaṃ kathitaṃ priye |
ahitaṃ rasasevāyāḥ kathayāmi śṛṇu priye || 1,6.93 ||
atyaśanaṃ cātipānamatinidrātijāgaram |
akāle bhojanaṃ strīṇāmatisaṅgo'pyasaṅgitā || 1,6.94 ||
asthānamadahāsāśca harṣaḥ kopo'dhikaspṛhā |
bahujalpo jalakrīḍā duḥkhamatyantacintanam || 1,6.95 ||
kaliṅgaṃ kāravellaṃ ca kūṣmāṇḍaḥ karkaṭī tathā |
kākamācī kulatthaṃ ca kārkoṭī ca kusumbhikā || 1,6.96 ||
tilātasītailamāṣakapotakamasūrakāḥ |
takrabhaktaṃ ca sauvīraṃ tiktakaṭvamlalāvaṇam || 1,6.97 ||
kṣāraṃ kṣaudraṃ picchilaṃ ca pittalaṃ ca parūṣakam |
māhiṣīḥ kṣīravikṛtīrvidaraṃ sahakārakam || 1,6.98 ||
nāraṅgabilvalikucaśigrunaivedyabhojanam |
agnisparśanamaṅghribhyāṃ tāḍanaṃ godvijanmanoḥ || 1,6.99 ||
kumārībālaturagapaśvādīnāṃ ca tāḍanam |
pātakaṃ prāṇihiṃsā ca chedanaṃ bhūruhāmapi || 1,6.100 ||
akṣādisaptavyasanaṃ chāyāśvatthakapitthayoḥ |
liṅgastrīnarakhaṭvānāṃ tathā dvīpivibhītayoḥ || 1,6.101 ||
catuṣpathātinirviktasthāne viṇmūtramocanam |
śivadvijagurustrīṇāṃ vīrayoniyatātmanām || 1,6.102 ||
samayaśrutisūtānāṃ nadītīrthāmbhasāṃ nṛṇām |
palāṇḍuhiṅgulaśunarājikābṛhatīdvayam || 1,6.103 ||
niṣpāvalaṅghanodvartiniśāśābharaṇanditā |
suptirmadyāsavau tāmracūḍaśca jalajāmiṣam || 1,6.104 ||
tīkṣṇoṣṇaguruviṣṭambhirūkṣaśuṣkāmiṣaṃ madhu |
kadalīpatrakāṃsyeṣu bhojanaṃ gharmasevanam || 1,6.105 ||
etāni dravyajālāni niṣiddhāni rasāyane |
<apathyajanitā rogāḥ>
seveta cetpramādena vikṛtirjāyate kṣaṇāt || 1,6.106 ||
rasājīrṇe bhavetkānte śoṣo mūrcchā bhramo jvaraḥ |
klamaḥ kampaḥ śakṛnmūtrarodhanaṃ śūlavepathuḥ || 1,6.107 ||
anidrālasyahikkā ca kāsaśvāsavijṛmbhikāḥ |
arocakātisāraśca liṅgastambho'kṣikukṣiṣu || 1,6.108 ||
vakṣaḥkarṇodarāṅghrau ca meḍhre śirasi sandhiṣu |
dāho'ṅgabhaṅgasarve'nye vyādhayaḥ sambhavanti vai || 1,6.109 ||
rasājīrṇapraśāntyarthaṃ yogo'yaṃ kathyate mayā |
rājakośātakī puṅkhā garuḍī kāravallikā || 1,6.110 ||
kārkoṭī kākamācī ca devadālī parājitā |
sarvametaccaikapalaṃ gomūtre tu catuṣpale || 1,6.111 ||
kvāthayetpādaśeṣaṃ tu kārṣikaṃ svacchasaindhavam |
nikṣipettatpibetkvāthaṃ rasājīrṇe hitaṃkaram || 1,6.112 ||
sukhībhavettridivase rasasya krāmaṇaṃ bhavet |
raso vyathayate tattadaṅgaṃ saṃparimardayet || 1,6.113 ||
vā stanena ca tanvaṅgyā nirvyathaḥ kramate rasaḥ |
<saptadhātuvedhaḥ>
tvagvedhaḥ prathamaṃ devi raktavedho dvitīyakaḥ || 1,6.114 ||
tṛtīyo māṃsavedhaḥ syānmedovedhaścaturthakaḥ |
asthivedhaḥ pañcamaḥ syātṣaṣṭho majjātmako bhavet || 1,6.115 ||
saptamaḥ śuklavedhaḥ syātnāḍīvedhastathāṣṭamaḥ |
navamaścakṣuṣo vedho daśamaḥ sarvavedhakaḥ || 1,6.116 ||
rasavedhena bhujagaḥ svarṇaṃ syādraktavedhataḥ |
trapu svarṇaṃ bhavenmāṃsavedhāttīkṣṇaṃ ca kāñcanam || 1,6.117 ||
medovedhena śulbaṃ tu svarṇaṃ syādasthivedhataḥ |
rūpyaṃ svarṇaṃ bhavenmajjavedhāllohāni kāṃcanam || 1,6.118 ||
śuklavedhena mṛtpātraṃ kāñcanaṃ bhavati priye |
vajrajīrṇo vīryavedhī majjāmabhrakajāritaḥ || 1,6.119 ||
hemajīrṇastvasthivedhī rūpyajīrṇo rasaṃ priye |
medovedhī tāpyajīrṇo māṃsavedhī tu pāradaḥ || 1,6.120 ||
śulbajīrṇo raktavedhī kāntajīrṇo rasātmakaḥ |
tīkṣṇabaddhena nīlābhastāmreṇāruṇasaprabhaḥ || 1,6.121 ||
rajatenendusaṃkāśo hemnā kāñcanasaprabhaḥ |
dhūmāvalokī vaktrastho māsātkhecaratāṃ nayet || 1,6.122 ||
sparśavedhī tu vaktrastho dvimāsātsiddhidāyakaḥ |
śatakoṭistribhirmāsaiścaturbhirdaśakoṭikṛt || 1,6.123 ||
pañcabhiḥ koṭivedhī syādaṣṭabhiścāyutaṃ punaḥ |
sāhasraṃ navabhirmāsairdaśamāse śataṃ punaḥ || 1,6.124 ||
tasya mūtrapurīṣaistu śleṣmaṇāṅgamalaistathā |
lepāddhematvamāyānti yāni lohāni bhūtale || 1,6.125 ||
catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśato vedhī māsamekādaśasya tu |
dvātriṃśakavedhī tu varṣāddehaṃ tu vedhayet || 1,6.126 ||
sarvarogairvinirmukto valīpalitavarjitaḥ |
nāsau chidyeta śastraiśca pāvakena na dahyate || 1,6.127 ||
vāyuvegī mahātejāḥ kāmadeva ivāparaḥ |
icchayā jāyate dṛśyo'pyadṛśyacaiva jāyate || 1,6.128 ||
yasya saṃsparśamātreṇa sarvalohāni kāñcanam |
tasminnekārṇave ghore naṣṭe sthāvarajaṅgame || 1,6.129 ||
devā yatra vilīyante siddhastatraiva līyate || 1,6.130 ||


āk, 1, 7
<padmarāgādiratnotpattiḥ>
purā kailāsaśikhare gahvare'drisuteśvarau |
ciramāstāṃ krīḍamānau jigūṣū tau parasparam || 1,7.1 ||
tadā retaḥ sūtarūpapravāho'bhūttayormahān |
tatpravāhaprabhāvena vedhitā dṛṣado'bhavan || 1,7.2 ||
padmarāgādimaṇayo lohā hemādayastathā |
gandhakādyāśca pāṣāṇā divyā oṣadhayo latāḥ || 1,7.3 ||
nānāvarṇā bahuvidhā nānāsiddhipradāyakāḥ |
pūrvoktānāṃ samastānāṃ vajraṃ śreṣṭhatamaṃ mahat || 1,7.4 ||
tajjātilakṣaṇaṃ devi saṃskāraṃ bhajanaṃ phalam |
kramādvakṣyāmi te devi sāvadhānaṃ śṛṇu priye || 1,7.5 ||
<vajralakṣaṇaṃ vajrabhedāṃśca>
dvijanmānaśca rājanyā ūravyā vṛṣalāḥ kramāt |
śubhraśoṇaharidrābhāḥ kālā nānāvidhāstathā || 1,7.6 ||
strīpuṃnapuṃsakāścaiva jñātavyāste kramātpriye |
śuklāḥ phalakasampūrṇā jyotiṣmanto mahattarāḥ || 1,7.7 ||
pumāṃsaste tu vijñeyā rekhābījavivarjitāḥ |
balāḍhyāḥ satvasahitā lohakramaṇahetavaḥ || 1,7.8 ||
rasabandhakarāḥ kṣipraṃ sarveṣāṃ siddhidāyakāḥ |
rasāyanakarāḥ sthūlā hyaṣṭasiddhipradāyakāḥ || 1,7.9 ||
ṣaṭkoṇabījarekhābhirujjvalāstāḥ striyaḥ smṛtāḥ |
dehajyotiḥprajananā bhogakāntisukhapradāḥ || 1,7.10 ||
dīrghāstryasrāśca tanavo niboddhavyā napuṃsakāḥ |
kṣīṇasatvāḥ svalpavīryāḥ krāmakāḥ suranāyike || 1,7.11 ||
klībā napuṃsake strīṇāṃ striyaḥ sarvahitā narāḥ |
jātyānurūpāścotsāhaṃ yathā satvaṃ tathā guṇam || 1,7.12 ||
yathā kāntistathā śīlaṃ kurvantyevaṃ dvijātayaḥ |
valīpalitarogaghnāḥ kṣatriyā mṛtyuhāriṇaḥ || 1,7.13 ||
dehasthairyakarā vaiśyā lohānāṃ vedhakāriṇaḥ |
vayaḥstaṃbhakarāḥ śūdrā nānāmayavināśanāḥ || 1,7.14 ||
<vajrasaṃskāraḥ>
vajrasaṃskāramadhunā kathayāmi śṛṇu priye |
śyāmā śamī meghanādā varṣābhūdhūrtakodravāḥ || 1,7.15 ||
meṣaśṛṅgyākhukarṇī ca kulutthaṃ cāmlavetasaḥ |
madanāgastyanirguṇḍī caiteṣāṃ svarasairyute || 1,7.16 ||
ḍolāyantre pācayecca vyāghrīkandagataṃ dinam |
uddhṛtya māhiṣaśakṛlliptaṃ kārīṣavahninā || 1,7.17 ||
dahedrātrau caturyāmaṃ rātryante pariṣecayet |
hayamūtrasnuhīkṣīrakulatthakarasaistathā || 1,7.18 ||
pācayeddāhayedevaṃ secayetsaptavārakam |
evaṃ dvijātijātīyāḥ śodhitāḥ syuśca siddhidāḥ || 1,7.19 ||
<vajrarasāyanam>
rasāyanārhaṃ vakṣyāmi vajrabhasma sureśvari |
vaikrāntatāpyagandhāśmaśilāmākṣikatālakam || 1,7.20 ||
kāntāsyaṭaṅkaṇakṣārakulīrāsthīni peṣayet |
stanyena golake kṣiptvā mūṣāyāṃ nikṣipettataḥ || 1,7.21 ||
dhameddṛḍhataraṃ pūrvaṃ paścādgajapuṭe pacet |
tadeva vajramādāya tasminṣoḍaśamātrake || 1,7.22 ||
ekāṃśaṃ pāradaṃ tasmin pakvabījena jāritam |
stanyena mardayettau dvau punargajapuṭe pacet || 1,7.23 ||
evaṃ tatṣoḍaśapuṭādbījajīrṇarasānvitam |
dṛḍhaṃ kurvīta tadvajraṃ bhavedbhasma rasāyanam || 1,7.24 ||
śṛṇu rudrāṇi vakṣyāmi divyaṃ vajrarasāyanam |
pūrvoktavatprakurvīta dehaśuddhyādikaṃ vidhim || 1,7.25 ||
śubharkṣe sumuhūrte ca candratārābalānvite |
śivāgnigurugoviprabhiṣajaḥ pūjayetpurā || 1,7.26 ||
tataḥ seveta tadbhasma yavamātraṃ varānane |
kāntābhrasattvaṃ sauvarṇabhasma guñjaikamātrakam || 1,7.27 ||
triphalāmadhusarpirbhiḥ prātaḥ śuddhavapurlihet |
evaṃ ṣoḍaśamāsāntamekadvitricatuṣṭayam || 1,7.28 ||
pratimāsaṃ vardhayitvā yāvadyāvakaṣoḍaśam |
kāntādibhasmatritayaṃ guñjāvṛddhikrame tathā || 1,7.29 ||
dviniṣkaṃ triphalācūrṇaṃ sarpirmadhu yathāsukham |
pūrvoktavadvidhiṃ tyājyaṃ prakuryānmama vallabhe || 1,7.30 ||
sevitaṃ palamātraṃ tu vajrabhasma dinaṃ kramāt |
valīpalitarogaghnaṃ śatāyuṣyaṃ dadāti ca || 1,7.31 ||
dvipalaṃ ca sahasrāyurayutaṃ tripalaṃ tathā |
catuṣpalaṃ lakṣamāyurdaśalakṣaṃ ca pañcamam || 1,7.32 ||
koṭyāyuṣyaṃ ṣaṭpalaṃ ca surendrāyuśca saptamam |
brahmāyuṣyaṃ cāṣṭapalaṃ viṣṇutvaṃ navamaṃ palam || 1,7.33 ||
rudratvaṃ daśamaṃ devi īśvaratvaṃ tataḥ param |
sadāśivatvaṃ dvādaśyāṃ vajrabhasma dadātyalam || 1,7.34 ||
sarvajñatvaṃ sarvagatvaṃ svecchāviharaṇaṃ tathā |
<vajraudanam>
vajraudanaṃ pravakṣyāmi rasāyanahitaṃ param || 1,7.35 ||
pūrvavacchodhite vajre mṛdukarma samārabhet |
mātṛvāhakamadhyasthaṃ tittirīmāṃsaveṣṭitam || 1,7.36 ||
tīkṣṇakāntānanarasaiḥ punastatpariveṣṭayet |
kulatthatriphalānīrakodraveṣu pṛthakpṛthak || 1,7.37 ||
tridinaṃ pācayedevaṃ tadvajraṃ veṣṭayeddalaiḥ |
trivarṣārūḍhatāmbulyāstathā kārpāsakasya vā || 1,7.38 ||
ceṣṭitaṃ jānumadhyasthaṃ vajraṃ yāmadvayānmṛdu |
vajraudanamidaṃ proktaṃ vajradrutirathocyate || 1,7.39 ||
<vajradrutiḥ>
trikṣāraṃ caṇakāmlaṃ ca rāmaṭhaṃ cāmlavetasam |
jvālāmukhī cekṣurakaṃ sthalakumbhīrasena ca || 1,7.40 ||
piṣṭvā snuhyarkayoḥ kṣīraistadgole mṛdu hīrakam |
nikṣipettacca jambīre dolāyantre tryahaṃ pacet || 1,7.41 ||
evaṃ kṛte hīrakasya drutirbhavati sūtavat |
<anyadrutyatideśaḥ>
padmarāgādiratnānāṃ drutirevaṃ kṛte bhavet || 1,7.42 ||
abhrakādimapāṣāṇā dravantyevaṃ kṛte dhruvam |
dravanti rasaratnāni mauktikaṃ cāmlavetasam || 1,7.43 ||
saptāhaṃ bhāvayedgharme kṣipejjambīrake tataḥ |
dhānyarāśau trisaptāhaṃ puṭapāke dravatyalam || 1,7.44 ||
<vajrabhasmaguṇāḥ>
vajrabhasma yathā tadvaddrutimārdavayorapi |
bhavedbhasma varārohe jarādāridryamṛtyujit || 1,7.45 ||
rasāyane bhavetsaukhyaṃ bhasma drutimṛdūdbhavam |
rasāyane tu strīpuṃsau krāmaṇe tu napuṃsakaḥ || 1,7.46 ||
ṣaḍrasaṃ himavīryaṃ ca sarvāmayavināśanam |
sarvadoṣapraśamanaṃ sarvasaukhyaṃ rasāyanam || 1,7.47 ||
vidyutprabhāṃ dehadārḍhyaṃ svacchaṃ snigdhaṃ ca lekhanam |
saundaryaṃ balamāyuṣyaṃ grahālakṣmīvināśanam || 1,7.48 ||
śuddhirmṛdudrutībhasma vaikrāntasya tu vajravat |
<svarṇaṃ tadbhedāḥ>
atha vakṣye śreṣṭhatamaṃ śṛṇu hemarasāyanam || 1,7.49 ||
hemotpattiḥ purā proktā tathāpi trividhā bhavet |
rasavedhādbhaveccaikamitaratkṣetrasambhavam || 1,7.50 ||
lohasaṅkarajaṃ cānyacchreṣṭhamadhyakanīyasaḥ |
krameṇa pītarakte ca pītaraktaṃ ca varṇataḥ || 1,7.51 ||
nikarṣachedadāheṣu kuṅkumaśvetaśoṇitam |
<svarṇaśuddhiḥ>
divyamevaṃvidhaṃ svarṇaṃ paṭṭakaṃ kaṇaśaḥ kṛtam || 1,7.52 ||
halinīkañcukīkandasnuhyarkāgnikarañjakam |
dhūrtaguñjeṅgudīcīramūlatālāśvagandhikāḥ || 1,7.53 ||
piṣṭvendravāruṇī caiṣā mūlāni mathitena vai |
lepayetsvarṇapaṭṭāni tāpayejjātavedasi || 1,7.54 ||
mūtre mathite taile kulutthāmbhasi kāñjike |
jambīrārkapayomadhye pratyekaṃ saptadhā kṣipet || 1,7.55 ||
lepayettāpayetsiñcyādbhūyo bhūyo'pi saptadhā |
valmīkamṛddhūmasāramiṣṭakāpaṭugairikam || 1,7.56 ||
piṣṭvā jambīranīreṇa liptvā patraṃ puṭīkṛtam |
caturvarṇābhrakaṃ tāpyaṃ daradaṃ tālakaṃ śilām || 1,7.57 ||
kāntakaṅkuṣṭharasakavimalāsūtabhūlatāḥ |
piṣṭvāmlena vaṭīḥ kuryānniṣkamātrāḥ sureśvari || 1,7.58 ||
palādūnaṃ na kartavyamadhikaṃ ca catuṣpalāt |
puṭitaṃ hema śulbaṃ ca drāvayecca samīkṛtam || 1,7.59 ||
tanmadhye taddravībhūte caikaikāṃ vaṭikāṃ kṣipet |
svarṇāddaśaguṇā vāhyā vaṭikāḥ śoṣitāḥ kramāt || 1,7.60 ||
yāvaccheṣaṃ bhavetsvarṇaṃ tāvadevaṃ dhamecchanaiḥ |
śuddhaṃ tajjāyate divyaṃ jarāmaraṇarogahṛt || 1,7.61 ||
<svarṇabhasma>
ciñcāpatranibhaṃ svarṇaṃ patraṃ śuddhaṃ ca pāradam |
ekāntaṃ mardayedamlaistatpiṣṭimapi lepayet || 1,7.62 ||
samagandhakatāpyābhyāṃ piṣṭiṃ mūṣāgatāṃ dhamet |
ṣoḍaśāṃśaṃ sūtabhasma hemacūrṇe vinikṣipet || 1,7.63 ||
amlena mardayedgāḍhaṃ pacettadbhūdhare puṭe |
evaṃ ṣoḍaśaparyantaṃ rasaṃ pratipuṭaṃ kṣipet || 1,7.64 ||
nirutthaṃ hemabhasmedaṃ jāyate sarvasiddhidam |
<hemarasāyanam>
virekavamanādyaiśca śuddhadehaḥ śubhe dine || 1,7.65 ||
arcayedīśaviprāgniguruvaidyapuraḥsarān |
seveta śuddhahṛdayo divyaṃ hemarasāyanam || 1,7.66 ||
kāntābhrayoḥ sattvabhasma caikaguñjāpramāṇakam |
māṣonmitaṃ hemabhasma varāmadhvājyayuglihet || 1,7.67 ||
pūrvoktavadvajramāsaṣoḍaśikāvadhi |
pūrvoktavadvidhiṃ tyājyaṃ prakurvīta rasāyanī || 1,7.68 ||
śṛṇu citrāṃ girisute sevāṃ hemarasāyane |
palamātropayogena sarvarogavivarjitaḥ || 1,7.69 ||
tato dvipalayogena valīpalitavarjitaḥ |
vāksiddhirdivyadṛṣṭiśca tripalena bhavetpriye || 1,7.70 ||
catuḥpalopayogena viṣavyāghrāhibhīrna hi |
tathā pañcapalenaiva divyātmajñānitā bhavet || 1,7.71 ||
devi ṣaṭpalayogena siddhasādhyapadaṃ bhavet |
tathā saptapalenāpi vidyādharapadaṃ bhavet || 1,7.72 ||
tathāṣṭapalayogena svecchāviharaṇe paṭuḥ |
palena navamenāpi cāṣṭaiśvaryaguṇānvitaḥ || 1,7.73 ||
tathā daśapalenāpi mahendratvamavāpnuyāt |
ekādaśapalairdevi brahmatvaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ || 1,7.74 ||
dvādaśabhiḥ palaiḥ kānte viṣṇutvaṃ samupaiti ca |
trayodaśapalenāpi raudraṃ padamavāpnuyāt || 1,7.75 ||
tathā caturdaśapalairaiśvaraṃ labhate padam |
palaiḥ pañcadaśaireti sadāśivapadaṃ param || 1,7.76 ||
tathā syātṣoḍaśapalaiḥ saccidānandavigrahaḥ |
sarvajñaḥ sarvakartā ca hartā goptā sa sarvagaḥ || 1,7.77 ||
<hemadrutiḥ>
hemadrutiṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu saṃprati pārvati |
phalapāṃśurdevadālyāḥ śakragopo'śvalālikā || 1,7.78 ||
ṭaṅkaṇaṃ jālinīnīrairbhāvayedbahudhā priye |
drāvayetkāñcanaṃ tatra vapetsūtasamā drutiḥ || 1,7.79 ||
imāṃ drutiṃ kāñcanavadbhasma sevāṃ ca kalpayet |
svādu tiktaṃ kaṣāyaṃ ca śītamuṣṇaṃ rasāyanam || 1,7.80 ||
hṛdyaṃ kāntipradaṃ śuddhaṃ cakṣuṣyaṃ guru lekhanam |
dhṛtismṛtyāyurārogyanayavāksiddhidāyakam || 1,7.81 ||
kṣayonmādādirogāṇāṃ nāśanaṃ doṣaśāntikṛt |
snigdhaṃ rucyaṃ dīptiṃ vīryakṛdbalavardhanam || 1,7.82 ||
<kāntaṃ tadbhedā lakṣaṇañca>
atha kānte pravakṣyāmi kāntaṃ kāntarasāyanam |
divyā auṣadhayaḥ santu sarvasiddhipradāyikāḥ || 1,7.83 ||
tebhyaḥ kāntaṃ śreṣṭhatamaṃ valīrukpalitāpaham |
kalpānte tāṇḍavaṃ śaṃbhuḥ śarvāṇīsahitaḥ svayam || 1,7.84 ||
uccaṇḍaṃ vyatanottatra saṃjātāḥ svedabindavaḥ |
tatra tatra gatāste'pi hyayaskāntatvamāyayuḥ || 1,7.85 ||
jāṅgale bahavo jātā deśe sādhāraṇe kvacit |
jalaprāye'sti vā nāsti jātyā pañcavidhāḥ smṛtāḥ || 1,7.86 ||
bhrāmakaṃ cumbakaṃ devi karṣakaṃ drāvakaṃ tathā |
romakaṃ ca tathaikadvitricatuḥ sarvatomukhāḥ || 1,7.87 ||
teṣāṃ pañcavidhānāṃ tu pītaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ ca śoṇitam |
miśraṃ pṛthakpṛthakteṣāṃ kramātsyuradhidevatāḥ || 1,7.88 ||
brahmaviṣṇvīśabhūtātmamātṛkāḥ parikīrtitāḥ |
pītavarṇaṃ sparśavedhi kṛṣṇaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ rasāyane || 1,7.89 ||
rasabandhakaraṃ raktaṃ miśraṃ sarvarujāpaham |
romakaṃ grahabhītighnaṃ drāvakaṃ cottamottamam || 1,7.90 ||
uttamaṃ madhyamaṃ nīcaṃ karṣakaṃ cumbakaṃ kramāt |
bhrāmakaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ vacmi lohaṃ tu bhrāmayettataḥ || 1,7.91 ||
tasmādbhrāmakamityuktaṃ cumbakaṃ lohacumbakam |
karṣakaṃ karṣayellohaṃ drāvakaṃ drāvayedyataḥ || 1,7.92 ||
sphuṭanādromajananaṃ tadromakamudāhṛtam |
atyuttamaṃ bahumukhaṃ catuḥpañcāsyamuttamam || 1,7.93 ||
madhyamaṃ syāddvitrimukhaṃ nīcamekamukhaṃ bhavet |
bhrāmakaṃ cumbakaṃ sarvarogāṇāṃ nāśane hitam || 1,7.94 ||
karṣakaṃ drāvakaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ rasayoge rasāyane |
rasadvipāṅkuśamidaṃ kāntaṃ pañcavidhaṃ priye || 1,7.95 ||
samīraṇātapaspṛṣṭaṃ na grāhyaṃ tatkadācana |
hastadvayaṃ samutkhāya kāntaṃ grāhyaṃ varānane || 1,7.96 ||
<kāntasattvapātanam>
ādau sampūjya durgāṃ ca gaṇeśaṃ bhairavaṃ priye |
tīvrāgninā dahetkāntaṃ secayettriphalāmbunā || 1,7.97 ||
tatkāntaṃ cūrṇayetsūkṣmaṃ kācasāmudraṭaṅkaṇaiḥ |
lākṣāgugguluguñjābhirūrṇājyamadhusikthakaiḥ || 1,7.98 ||
śaśāsthikṣudramīnaiśca bhallātatilakalkakaiḥ |
sarjakṣārayavakṣārarālaiściragulaistathā || 1,7.99 ||
nyaṅkusairibhadantīnāṃ viṣāṇaiśca samāṃśakaiḥ |
etatsamāṃśaṃ tatkāntamajākṣīreṇa mardayet || 1,7.100 ||
melayetpañcamāhiṣyairdadhyādyairgomayāntakaiḥ |
ślakṣṇaṃ piṣṭvā vaṭīḥ kuryātkarṣamātrāḥ sureśvari || 1,7.101 ||
koṣṭhīyantre vaṅkanāle khadirāṅgārakairdṛḍham |
pañca pañca vaṭīstatra nikṣipecca śanaiḥ śanaiḥ || 1,7.102 ||
triyāmamathanādeva kāntātsatvaṃ patecchuci |
tatsarvaṃ kaṇaśaḥ kṛtvā kācādyairauṣadhaiḥ samaiḥ || 1,7.103 ||
mūṣāgataṃ dhamettīvramevaṃ dhāmyaṃ tridhā priye |
evaṃ kṛtaṃ tu tatkāntasatvaṃ sattvatamaṃ bhavet || 1,7.104 ||
<kāntasvarūpam>
kāntalohasya pātrasthajale tailasya bindavaḥ |
na sarpanti caṇāstvetāḥ kṛṣṇāḥ syurnātra saṃśayaḥ || 1,7.105 ||
ghṛṣṭahiṅgorna gandhaḥ syātkṣīrormiḥ śikharākṛtiḥ |
na kadācidbhuvi patecchivā muñcati tiktatām || 1,7.106 ||
tallohakṣiptamuṣṇāmbu śītalaṃ bhavati kṣaṇāt |
<lohaśuddhiḥ>
ūnaṃ pañcapalānnaiva nordhvaṃ pañcadaśātpalāt || 1,7.107 ||
triphalā ṣoḍaśapalā taccaturguṇamambu ca |
kvāthayetpādaśeṣaṃ tu tasminpatrāṇi ḍhālayet || 1,7.108 ||
śaśaraktapraliptāni śoṣayedātape punaḥ |
tāpitāni punastakratailasauvīrasarpīṃṣi || 1,7.109 ||
kṣaudrārkakṣīrajambīrakulatthakaraseṣvapi |
lepanaṃ śoṣaṇaṃ dāhaṃ secanaṃ ca kramātpriye || 1,7.110 ||
pratyekaṃ saptadhā kuryādgiridoṣaṃ tyajedayaḥ |
<lohasaṃskāramantrāḥ>
ādau mantrastataḥ karma kartavyo mantra ucyate || 1,7.111 ||
udbhavodbhavaśabdaḥ syāccaturthyanto'mṛtātparaḥ |
svāhāntaḥ praṇavaścādau mantro'yaṃ mardane sthitaḥ || 1,7.112 ||
omamṛtodbhavodbhavāya svāhā |
rakṣāyai lohanarayorayamekodbhavo manuḥ |
vinā svāhāpadaṃ sthāne phaḍantaṃ yaḥ samuccaret || 1,7.113 ||
omamṛtodbhavāya phaṭpraṇavordhvaṃ namaścaṇḍavajrapāṇaya ityapi |
tataḥ paraṃ mahāyakṣasenādhipataye namaḥ || 1,7.114 ||
dviruktaṃ suruśabdasya mahāvidyābalāya ca |
tataḥ svāheti mantro'yaṃ balikarmaṇi kīrtitaḥ || 1,7.115 ||
oṃ namaścaṇḍavajrapāṇaye mahāyakṣasenādhipataye namaḥ suru suru mahāvidyābalāya svāhā |
<lohabhasma>
etāni lohapatrāṇi sūkṣmaṃ saṃcūrṇayettataḥ |
ayaḥpātre kāntacūrṇaṃ nikṣipya triphalārasaiḥ || 1,7.116 ||
secayettadayodarvyā cālayanpācayediti |
piṇḍībhūtaṃ tadādāya mṛttikāpaṭale śubhe || 1,7.117 ||
saṃpuṭe nikṣipetsamyakṣoḍaśāṅgulagartake |
nidhāyāraṇyakārīṣamadhyagaṃ kāriṣaṃ dahet || 1,7.118 ||
ahorātraṃ prakurvīta vātātapavivarjitam |
punaḥ prātaḥ samādāya varākvāthena mardayet || 1,7.119 ||
sthālīsaṃpuṭayorevaṃ kuryātpākapuṭaiḥ kramāt |
evaṃ vidadhyātsaptāhaṃ tasminkṣepyaṃ ca hiṅgulam || 1,7.120 ||
lohasya ṣoḍaśāṃśaṃ ca nārīstanyena mardayet |
sthālyāṃ pākaṃ saṃpuṭe ca puṭaṃ kurvīta ṣoḍaśa || 1,7.121 ||
nirutthaṃ tadbhavedbhasma devi yojyaṃ rasāyane |
<lhohabhasmano'mṛtīkaraṇam>
yāvatsyāttriphalā tasmādbhavedvāri caturguṇam || 1,7.122 ||
aṣṭāvaśeṣaṃ kvāthayetkvāthasya sadṛśaṃ ghṛtam |
ghṛtatulyaṃ kāntabhasma tāmrapātre pacetpriye || 1,7.123 ||
lohadarvyā pracalayan yāvacchuṣyati tadrasaḥ |
tāvanmṛdvagninā pācyaṃ lohatulyāṃ sitāṃ kṣipet || 1,7.124 ||
amṛtīkaraṇamityuktaṃ sarvayogavahaṃ param |
<lohapākabhedāḥ>
lohapākaṃ pravakṣyāmi tadbhavettrividhaṃ priye || 1,7.125 ||
mṛdumadhyakharāḥ proktā jambīrasadṛśo mṛduḥ |
madhyamaḥ piṇḍasadṛśo vālukāsadṛśaḥ kharaḥ || 1,7.126 ||
<kāntaloharasāyanasevākramaḥ>
atha sevāṃ pravakṣyāmi kāntalohasya bhasmanaḥ |
sumuhūrte śubhadine pūjiteśādidevataḥ || 1,7.127 ||
seveta siddhidaṃ divyaṃ kāntabhasma rasāyanam |
śuddhadeho virekādyairabhrabhasma purā priye || 1,7.128 ||
māsamekaṃ tu seveta tasmācchuddhavapurbhavet |
māṣamātraṃ kāntabhasma guñjāmātrābhrabhasma ca || 1,7.129 ||
dviniṣkaṃ triphalācūrṇaṃ madhusarpiḥsamanvitam |
lihetprātarviśuddhātmā snānadānādikarmabhiḥ || 1,7.130 ||
anupeyaṃ tato lohādgavyaṃ ṣaṣṭiguṇaṃ payaḥ |
dhāroṣṇaṃ tadabhāve tu kṛtvā kṣīraṃ samāmbukam || 1,7.131 ||
yāvatkṣīṇajalaṃ kvāthyaṃ kṣīraṃ cānupibetpriye |
tadasātmye varākvāthaṃ guḍūcyā vā kaṣāyakam || 1,7.132 ||
ityekamāsaṃ seveta kramāddvitricatuṣṭayam |
pratimāsaṃ māṣaguñjāvṛddhiḥ kāntābhrabhasmanoḥ || 1,7.133 ||
evaṃ ṣoḍaśamāsāntaṃ vṛddhiḥ ṣoḍaśamātrakāḥ |
pūrvoktavadvidhiṃ tyājyaṃ kuryātsiddhimavāpnuyāt || 1,7.134 ||
asya vatsarayogena kṣudrāmayavināśanam |
etaddvivarṣayogena mahārogapraṇāśanam || 1,7.135 ||
trivarṣātpalitaṃ hanti caturvarṣādvaliṃ haret |
pañcamādapamṛtyughnaṃ ṣaṣṭhe syādāyuṣaḥ śatam || 1,7.136 ||
saptābde sūryavaddīptiṃ daśame siddhimelanam |
vaidyādharaṃ samāpnoti varṣe caikādaśe priye || 1,7.137 ||
trayodaśābde devatvamindratvaṃ ca caturdaśe |
tathā pañcadaśābde ca sarvalokapriyo bhavet || 1,7.138 ||
ṣoḍaśābde'ṣṭasiddhiḥ syātsatyaṃ satyaṃ maheśvari |
<kāntadrutikramaḥ>
suvarṇadrutivatkāntadrutiḥ syātpūrvavatpriye || 1,7.139 ||
kāntadrutirbhavedbhasma kāntalohasya bhasmavat |
<lohabhedāstadguṇāśca>
kiṭṭaṃ muṇḍaṃ tathā tīkṣṇaṃ kāntaṃ pūrvottarottaram || 1,7.140 ||
guṇāḍhyāḥ syurdaśaśatasahasraṃ lakṣakaṃ kramāt |
śatādhiko bhavetkāntastvauṣadhānāṃ rasāyanam || 1,7.141 ||
tridoṣaśamanaṃ divyaṃ sarvarogāpahārakam |
kāntiṃ rucyaṃ ca cakṣuṣyaṃ hṛdyamāyuṣyadaṃ śuci || 1,7.142 ||
sthairyaṃ dārḍhyaṃ ca dhātūnāṃ balavacca rasāyanam |
<abhrakotpattiḥ tadbhedā lakṣaṇañca>
athābhrakaṃ pravakṣyāmi tadutpattyādikaṃ kramāt || 1,7.143 ||
purādiśaktirbhavatī śvetadvīpe manorame |
indrāṇīkamalāvāṇīgāyatrīpramukhaiḥ saha || 1,7.144 ||
arundhatīmukhairdivyamunidāraiśca saṃyutā |
saṃcikrīḍe mahāmāyā citsadānandarūpiṇī || 1,7.145 ||
ānandāśrukaṇāḥ kṣaume nyapatanbahavo dṛśoḥ |
tadā ṛtumatī jātā tataḥ svādu saritpateḥ || 1,7.146 ||
tīre tyaktvā ca tadvastraṃ snātvā kailāsamāyayau |
vātyāvaśena tadvastraṃ kṣīrābdhau nyapatattadā || 1,7.147 ||
devāsurairmathyamānāttasmātkṣīrāmbudhestadā |
samabhūttadrajovastraṃ caturdhābhrakasaṃjñakam || 1,7.148 ||
kṛṣṇābhrakaṃ sāñjanābhaṃ pītaṃ devyaṅgalepanāt |
raktaṃ tasya rajorūpaṃ kṣaumaṃ śvetamabhūditi || 1,7.149 ||
caturvidhaṃ tadālokya rasabandhanakāraṇam |
rasāyanaṃ rogaharaṃ sarvasiddhipradāyakam || 1,7.150 ||
ityūcire surāḥ sarve sākṣādamṛtasaṃmitam |
śvetaṃ rajatakāryeṣu trayamanyatsuvarṇake || 1,7.151 ||
sarvaṃ rasāyane yojyaṃ raktapītasitāsitam |
śvetācchataguṇaṃ raktaṃ raktātpītaṃ sahasrakam || 1,7.152 ||
pītāllakṣaguṇaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ jñeyaṃ lohe rasāyane |
pratyekaṃ tāni jāyante caturdhā jātitaḥ kramāt || 1,7.153 ||
pinākaṃ darduraṃ nāgaṃ vajraṃ ceti caturvidham |
vajraṃ samāharenmukhyaṃ trīṇyanyāni vivarjayet || 1,7.154 ||
pinākamagninikṣiptaṃ muñcate dalasaṃcayam |
tatsevitaṃ malaṃ baddhvā mārayedrogakāraṇam || 1,7.155 ||
darduraṃ vahninikṣiptaṃ kurute bhekaniḥsvanam |
tadutpādayate rogamaśmaryākhyamasādhyakam || 1,7.156 ||
nāgamagnau vinikṣiptaṃ phūtkāraṃ vitanoti yat |
tatsevayā mahākuṣṭhaṃ maṇḍalākhyaṃ bhaveddhruvam || 1,7.157 ||
<grāhyalakṣaṇam>
etattritayasevābhirjāyate rogasaṃcayaḥ |
vahnisthitaṃ tu vajrābhraṃ vikārānnācaretkvacit || 1,7.158 ||
tasmādvajrābhrakaṃ sevyaṃ valīpalitamṛtyujit |
rājahastātparaṃ grāhyaṃ khanitrena tadākarāt || 1,7.159 ||
<vajrābhra:: parīkṣā:: badṝuality>
bhārayuktaṃ pṛthudalaṃ snigdhaṃ mocyadalaṃ sukham |
kācacandrakakiṭṭābhaṃ na yojyaṃ tatkadācana || 1,7.160 ||
<dhānyābhrakam>
pūrvoktalakṣaṇayutaṃ vajrābhraṃ dhamayeddṛḍham |
arkakṣīrāranāle ca gomūtre triphalārase || 1,7.161 ||
meghanādarase cetthaṃ nikṣipetteṣu tattridhā |
samyagdalaṃ mocayitvā meghanādāmlayordravaiḥ || 1,7.162 ||
bhāvayedātape tīvre ślakṣṇaṃ piṣṭvātha vastrake |
nikṣipya dhānyasahitaṃ baddhvā tatkāñjike punaḥ || 1,7.163 ||
karābhyāṃ ghaṭṭayedgāḍhaṃ sūkṣmaṃ tatkāñjike sravet |
tatkuryādātape śuṣkametaddhānyābhrakaṃ smṛtam || 1,7.164 ||
<abhrakasatvapātanam>
kāntavatsatvapatanamabhrakasya bhavetpriye |
hema rūpyaṃ mākṣikaṃ ca vaikrāntaṃ madhu ṭaṅkaṇam || 1,7.165 ||
guñjā guḍaṃ ghṛtaṃ bhallamekaikaṃ daśaniṣkakam |
piṣṭvārkapayasā kāryā vaṭikāḥ karṣamātrakāḥ || 1,7.166 ||
śataniṣke'bhrasatve'smin vidrute vaṭikāḥ kṣipet |
ekaikaghaṭikāmātraṃ vaṭyaḥ kṣepyāḥ śanaiḥ śanaiḥ || 1,7.167 ||
yāvattatsattvaśeṣaṃ syāttāvaddhāmyamatandritaiḥ |
idamabhrakasattvaṃ tu devayogyaṃ rasāyanam || 1,7.168 ||
<abhrakasatvabhasma>
cūrṇīkṛtyābhrasattvaṃ tanmeghanādaḥ punarnavā |
sūraṇo'rkadalaṃ mustā jambīrastālamūlikā || 1,7.169 ||
triphalā vajravallī ca śāṅgerī maricaṃ tathā |
amlavargo vaṭajaṭā kārpāsamuniśigrukam || 1,7.170 ||
ekavīrā kokilākṣī sarpākṣī tulasī vacā |
peṭārī vākucī kanyā caikaikasya rasaiḥ pṛthak || 1,7.171 ||
mardanaṃ gharmapākaṃ ca sthālīpākaṃ puṭaṃ kramāt |
ekaikāhaṃ prakurvīta tato daradamākṣike || 1,7.172 ||
satvaṣoḍaśabhāgena yojyaṃ peṣyaṃ varārasaiḥ |
puṭaṣoḍaśaparyantaṃ kuryādevaṃ hi bhairavi || 1,7.173 ||
pūrvoktavatsyādamṛtīkaraṇaṃ kāntasatvavat |
nirutthaṃ bhasma bhavati cāyurārogyadāyakam || 1,7.174 ||
<abhrakasatvasevākramaḥ>
atha sevāṃ pravakṣyāmi ghanasatvarasāyane |
vamanādiviśuddhātmā pūjitasveṣṭadevataḥ || 1,7.175 ||
guñjāmātrābhrasatvaṃ ca dviniṣkaṃ triphalārajaḥ |
madhvājyābhyāṃ lihetprātarekamāsaṃ bhajediti || 1,7.176 ||
evaṃ ṣoḍaśamāsāntaṃ māsavṛddhikrameṇa vai |
guñjāvṛddhirbhaveddevi sevetetthaṃ rasāyanam || 1,7.177 ||
pūrvoktavadvidhiṃ tyājyaṃ prakurvīta sureśvari |
varṣeṇa dehaśuddhiḥ syāddvivarṣādāmayāccyutaḥ || 1,7.178 ||
trivatsarānmahāroganāśanaṃ bhavati dhruvam |
caturvarṣānmahākāntibalavīryapravardhanam || 1,7.179 ||
pañcavarṣāddehadārḍhyaṃ ṣaṣṭhe vāksiddhimeti hi |
saptame divyadṛṣṭiḥ syādaṣṭame viṣanāśanam || 1,7.180 ||
navābde siddhatāmeti tato vidyādharo bhavet |
ekādaśābde tvindratvaṃ dvādaśe brahmatā bhavet || 1,7.181 ||
trayodaśābde viṣṇutvaṃ rudratvaṃ ca caturdaśe |
īśatvaṃ pañcadaśake ṣoḍaśābde sadāśivaḥ || 1,7.182 ||
aṇimādiyutaḥ svairī sarvajñaḥ sarvakṛdbhavet |
<abhrakadrutiḥ>
athābhrakadrutiṃ vakṣye kañcukīkanda eva ca || 1,7.183 ||
kapitindau keśataile pratyekaṃ tu tridhā vapet |
mūṣāyāṃ drāvayitvā tadabhrasatvadrutirbhavet || 1,7.184 ||
bhasmīkuryātprayatnena drutimabhrakasatvavat |
<abhrakaguṇāḥ>
ṣaḍrasaḥ sarvarogaghnastridoṣaśamanaḥ paraḥ || 1,7.185 ||
vīryāyuṣyabalaprajñākāntirūpavivardhanaḥ |
vapurdārḍhyasthairyayukto valīpalitamṛtyuhā || 1,7.186 ||
rucyo vṛṣyo laghuḥ śīto medhyaḥ snigdho rasāyanam |
abhrakaṃ dīpanaṃ grāhi śreṣṭhaṃ pāradabandhanam || 1,7.187 ||
pakṣajitsūtarājasya bhasmībhūtābhrasatvakaḥ |
abhrasya patraṃ rogaghnaṃ tacca satvaṃ dṛḍhaṃkaram || 1,7.188 ||
cetasatvaṃ mṛdu hareddrutistāṃśca daridratām || 1,7.189 ||


āk, 1, 8
<rasāyanasevā>
sūto vajraṃ suvarṇaṃ ca kāntalohaṃ tathābhrakam |
pañcaitānyamṛtāni syuḥ kalpitāni nṛṇāṃ mayā || 1,8.1 ||
mattakāśini yo vetti so'pi sākṣānmaheśvaraḥ |
ahaṃ raso rasaścāhamāvayorantaraṃ na hi || 1,8.2 ||
tena yuktāstu vajrādyāḥ siddhidāḥ syurna saṃśayaḥ |
puruṣaṃ rasasevārhaṃ vadāmi śṛṇu pārvati || 1,8.3 ||
strīklībabālakānāṃ ca na hi yojyaṃ rasāyanam |
bālāḥ pañcadaśābdā ye kumārāṃstriṃśadabdakāḥ || 1,8.4 ||
pañcāśadvarṣadeśīyā yuvānaḥ parikīrtitāḥ |
ataḥ paraṃ ca sthavirāḥ bhaveyuste rasāyane || 1,8.5 ||
yathoktakāle siddhiḥ syātkumārasya rasāyanāt |
tasmāddviguṇakālena yūnaḥ siddhirbhaveddhruvam || 1,8.6 ||
tasmāttriguṇakālena vṛddhaḥ siddhimavāpnuyāt |
jñātavyāḥ kramaśo devi hyuttamo madhyamo'dhamaḥ || 1,8.7 ||
<rasāyanasevākramaḥ>
pūrvamabhrakamaśnīyāttataḥ kāntarasāyanam |
atha kāntābhrakaṃ devi paścāddhemarasāyanam || 1,8.8 ||
athābhrakaṃ svarṇayogaṃ kāntahemarasāyanam |
ghanakāntasvarṇayogaṃ tato vajrarasāyanam || 1,8.9 ||
athābhravajrasevāṃ ca kāntavajramataḥ param |
ghanāyaskāntahīraṃ ca hemavajraṃ tato bhavet || 1,8.10 ||
paścādabhrasvarṇavajraṃ kāntāṣṭāpadavajrakam |
ghanakāntaṃ hemavajramataḥ pāradabhakṣaṇam || 1,8.11 ||
tathābhrarasayogaṃ syāttataḥ kāntarasaṃ bhavet |
ghanakāntarasaṃ devi paścāddhemarasaṃ bhavet || 1,8.12 ||
ghanahemarasaṃ paścātkāntahemarasaṃ tataḥ |
ghanakāntaṃ hemasūtamato vajrarasaṃ bhavet || 1,8.13 ||
ghanavajrarasaṃ devi kāntavajrarasaṃ tataḥ |
ghanakāntaṃ vajrasūtaṃ hemavajrarasaṃ tataḥ || 1,8.14 ||
ghanahemapavīsūtaṃ kāntahemapavīrasam |
ghanakāntasvarṇavajrarasamasmātparaṃ na hi || 1,8.15 ||
uttarottarataḥ sarve caite sarvaguṇottarāḥ |
rasāyanāni pañca syurekaikāni varānane || 1,8.16 ||
rasā dvidvyauṣadhabhavāstritryauṣadhabhavā daśa |
catuścaturbhavāḥ pañca tvekaḥ pañcauṣadhodbhavaḥ || 1,8.17 ||
ekatriṃśadvarārohe rasāyanamiti priye |
ghanaṃ kāntaṃ hema vajraṃ pāradaścetaram || 1,8.18 ||
pūrvottarottaraguṇā bhavanti prāṇavallabhe |
rasasya krāmaṇaṃ vajraṃ hemakāntaghanaṃ bhavet || 1,8.19 ||
vajrasaṃkrāmaṇaṃ hema kāntaṃ vyoma bhavetpriye |
hemnaḥ kāntabhavaṃ yojyaṃ kāntasya krāmaṇaṃ ghanam || 1,8.20 ||
abhrasya krāmaṇaṃ cābhrasattvaṃ siddhipradaṃ yataḥ |
ekaikauṣadhasevāyāṃ yathoktaṃ krāmaṇaṃ bhavet || 1,8.21 ||
yukto dvitricatuḥpañcabhaiṣajyaiḥ krāmaṇaṃ na hi |
triphalāmadhusarpīṃṣi sāmānyakrāmaṇaṃ bhavet || 1,8.22 ||


āk, 1, 9
<rasāyanārharasasaṃskāraḥ; prathamaḥ prakāraḥ>
śrībhairavī |
rasāyanārhāṃ sūtasya saṃskṛtiṃ vada me vibho |
śrībhairavaḥ |
śṛṇu pārvati yatnena saṃskāraṃ śodhanādikam || 1,9.1 ||
punarnavārasaiḥ peṣyaṃ dhānyābhraṃ pāradaṃ samam |
taptakhalve dinaṃ devi vajramūṣāgataṃ rasam || 1,9.2 ||
pacedbhūdharayantre ca punaḥ saṃmardayecca tam |
pūrvadravairyathāpūrvaṃ mardanaṃ pācanaṃ punaḥ || 1,9.3 ||
kṛtvaivaṃ daśavāraṃ taṃ pātyaṃ pātanayantrake |
śuddhaḥ syātpārado devi yojyo yoge rasāyane || 1,9.4 ||
<dvitīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
daśamāṃśaṃ rasādgandhaṃ taptakhalve vinikṣipet |
varājambīrakanyāgnidravairyāmaṃ vimardayet || 1,9.5 ||
pātayetpātanāyantre kuryādevaṃ tu saptadhā |
sagandhakaṃ mardanaṃ ca pātanaṃ bhavati priye || 1,9.6 ||
śuddhaḥ syātpārado devi yogyo yoge rasāyane |
<tṛtīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
tilatailairmāhiṣikairmūtrairmardyāmlakena ca || 1,9.7 ||
gomāṃsairhiṅgulaṃ pācyaṃ lohapātre kramāgninā |
saptāhaṃ lohadaṇḍena cālayansadravaṃ muhuḥ || 1,9.8 ||
sārdramāyūrapittena bhāvayedātape dinam |
pācayetpātanāyantre daradaṃ kharavahninā || 1,9.9 ||
śuddho bhaveccaturyāmātpāradaḥ syādrasāyane |
<rasabhasmakramaḥ; prathamaḥ prakāraḥ>
ūrdhvādhastātkhajīrṇasya sūtasya samagandhakam || 1,9.10 ||
nikṣipetpakvamūṣāyāṃ garte dvābhyāṃ caturguṇam |
kākamācīdravaṃ dattvā nirudhyaināṃ kramāgninā || 1,9.11 ||
pacedguṇḍakayantrasthāṃ mūṣāṃ yāmacatuṣṭayam |
bhasmasājjāyate sūto yojayettu rasāyane || 1,9.12 ||
<dvitīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
jīrṇasūtaṃ snukkṣīraiḥ samaṃ gandhaṃ vimardayet |
dinaṃ tato garbhayantre puṭe bhasmati pūrvavat || 1,9.13 ||
<tṛtīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
khajīrṇasūtaṃ tulyāṃśaṃ lākṣorṇāmadhuṭaṅkaṇam |
guñjāṃ bhṛṅgarasaiḥ sarvaṃ dinamekaṃ vimardayet || 1,9.14 ||
andhritaṃ vajramūṣāyāṃ dhamedbhasmati pāradaḥ |
<caturthaḥ prakāraḥ>
ghanajīrṇaṃ rasaṃ gandhaṃ tryahaṃ tulyaṃ vimardayet || 1,9.15 ||
ahimāryā rasairvātha hyajāmāryā rasena vā |
rasena kīṭamāriṇyāḥ śvetāṅkolarasena vā || 1,9.16 ||
divārātraṃ karīṣāgnau mṛṇmaye saṃpuṭe dahet |
tuṣāgninā vā tridinādbhasmībhavati pāradaḥ || 1,9.17 ||
taptakhalve vajrabhasma jīrṇe sūte samaṃ tryaham |
haṃsapādīrasairmardyaṃ bījairdivyauṣadhodbhavaiḥ || 1,9.18 ||
lepayedvajramūṣāyāṃ tatra pūrvaṃ rasaṃ kṣipet |
tryahaṃ tuṣāgninā pācyaṃ haṃsapādīdravaiḥ punaḥ || 1,9.19 ||
mardayettridinaṃ gāḍhaṃ tadgolaṃ pūrvavatpacet |
bhasmībhavati sūtendraḥ śaṅkhakundendusannibhaḥ || 1,9.20 ||
<jāritarasamāraṇam; prathamaḥ prakāraḥ>
atha vakṣyāmi deveśi jāritaṃ rasamāraṇam |
prathamaṃ jāraṇaṃ kuryātsabījaṃ suranāyike || 1,9.21 ||
ajāritaṃ rasaṃ mūḍhaḥ pramādānmārayedyadi |
mama drohī sa pāpiṣṭho mahāpātakavān bhavet || 1,9.22 ||
bhrūṇahā gurughātī ca goghnaḥ strībālaghātakaḥ |
tasmātsarvaprayatnena kuryājjāraṇapūrvakam || 1,9.23 ||
māraṇaṃ rasarājasya mama bījasya pārvati |
dharāyāṃ gomayaṃ devi sthāpayitvā tata upari || 1,9.24 ||
pakvamūṣāyāṃ vinikṣipya tanmadhye kaṭutumbijam |
tailaṃ nidhāya sūtaṃ ca nikṣipecca tata upari || 1,9.25 ||
kākamācīraso deyastailatulyo varānane |
śuddhaṃ gandhaṃ vrīhimātraṃ dattvā mūṣāṃ nirodhayet || 1,9.26 ||
tadūrdhvaṃ khādirāṅgārapūritaṃ śrāvakaṃ nyaset |
jñātvā tatsvāṅgaśītatvaṃ punargandhaṃ ca tailakam || 1,9.27 ||
kākamācīdravaṃ dattvā vahnimevaṃ punaḥ punaḥ |
adhastādgomayaṃ sāndramupariṣṭācca pāvakam || 1,9.28 ||
kākamācīdravaḥ sūtastailaṃ caitattrayaṃ samam |
jārayetṣaḍguṇaṃ gandhaṃ rasasyetthaṃ mukhīkṛtiḥ || 1,9.29 ||
dinaṃ jambīranīreṇa mardayettaṃ rasaṃ punaḥ |
catuḥ ṣaṣṭyaṃśakaṃ hemnaḥ patraṃ kaṇṭakabhedanam || 1,9.30 ||
mayūrapittaṃ tailaṃ ca lipetsarṣapasaṃbhavam |
abhāve tailapittasya cūlikālavaṇaṃ balim || 1,9.31 ||
jambīrasya rasairmardyaṃ taṃ rasaṃ hemapatrakam |
pācayetpūrvavadvahnau jambīrasya rasaiḥ punaḥ || 1,9.32 ||
sampūrṇāṃ rodhayenmūṣāṃ pūrvavajjārayetpunaḥ |
dvātriṃśadaṃśakaṃ hema ṣoḍaśāṃśaṃ tata upari || 1,9.33 ||
mardayejjārayedevaṃ tataḥ sūtasya māraṇam |
samūlāṃ kumbhimādāya gavāṃ mūtreṇa peṣayet || 1,9.34 ||
dinamekaṃ tadrasena mardayetpāradaṃ sudhīḥ |
saṃpuṭe kāntaje kṣiptvā cordhvādhaśca niyāmakān || 1,9.35 ||
cullyāṃ mṛdvagninā pācyaṃ dinaṃ bhasma bhavedrasaḥ |
akṣīṇo mṛtyunāśī syājjarādāridryanāśanaḥ || 1,9.36 ||
<dvitīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
vāsitaṃ pāradaṃ karṣamaṣṭaguñjāḥ suvarṇakam |
mṛtaṃ vajraṃ caturguñjaṃ mardyaṃ haṃsapadīrasaiḥ || 1,9.37 ||
taptakhalve vajramūṣāgataṃ kṛtvā nirodhayet |
pacedbhūdharayantre ca punarādāya taṃ rasam || 1,9.38 ||
haṃsapādīdravairmardyaṃ taptakhalve dinatrayam |
pūrvavadbhūdhare yantre pacetsūtaṃ punaḥ punaḥ || 1,9.39 ||
evaṃ śatapuṭaṃ kṛtvā bhasma syādraktavarṇakam |
etāni rasabhasmāni śastāni ca rasāyane || 1,9.40 ||
<rasāyanayogāḥ; abhrakarasāyanam>
ekadvitricatuḥpañcayogayuktaṃ rasāyanam |
kramādvakṣyāmi deveśi tatrāpyabhrakasevanam || 1,9.41 ||
vamanādiviśuddhātmā tyaktakṣārāmlakādikaḥ |
pūrvavadbhasmayedvyomasatvaṃ bhṛṅgavarārasaiḥ || 1,9.42 ||
triḥ saptadhā bhāvanīyaṃ prātarguñjādvayaṃ bhajet |
