sasitaṃ tadanu kṣaudraṃ ghṛtaiḥ satriphalaṃ lihet || 1,9.43 ||
dvātriṃśadguñjakā vṛddhiḥ krāmaṇaṃ karṣamātrakam |
eṣa ṣoḍaśamāsānte sarvarogādvimucyate || 1,9.44 ||
sa bhavetkāntasevārhastvevamabhrakasevayā |
<kāntarasāyanam>
pūrvavadbhasmayetkāntaṃ varānirguṇḍibhṛṅgajaiḥ || 1,9.45 ||
rasaistrisaptavārāṇi mardayedbhāvayetkramāt |
kṣaudrājyābhyāṃ lihetprātarnityaṃ guñjādvayonmitam || 1,9.46 ||
anu bhakṣyaṃ sinduvāravarābhṛṅgabhavaṃ rajaḥ |
madhvājyābhyāṃ karṣamātraṃ śuddhāṅgo vamanādibhiḥ || 1,9.47 ||
dvātriṃśadguñjikā vṛddhiḥ paramāvadhirīśvari |
bhavetṣoḍaśamāsāntājjarāvyādhibhirujjhitaḥ || 1,9.48 ||
kāntasya sevayā paścādbhavetkāntābhrakārhakaḥ |
<kāntābhrarasāyanam>
mṛtaṃ kāntaṃ ghanaṃ tulyaṃ dhātrībhṛṅgapunarnavāḥ || 1,9.49 ||
dravaireṣāṃ pṛthaṅmardyaṃ kalye guñjādvayaṃ lihet |
śarkarāmadhusarpirbhiḥ śuddhātmā vamanādibhiḥ || 1,9.50 ||
bhṛṅgāmalakavarṣābhūcūrṇaṃ krāmaṇamuttamam |
madhvājyairanu lehyaṃ taddvātriṃśadguñjakāvadhi || 1,9.51 ||
māsaṣoḍaśayogena valīpalitajidbalī |
kāntābhrasatvabhajanāddhemasevārhako bhavet || 1,9.52 ||
<svarṇarasāyanam>
pūrvavadbhasmayetsvarṇaṃ daśamūlakaṣāyataḥ |
ekaviṃśativāraṃ taṃ mardayedbhāvayetpriye || 1,9.53 ||
madhvājyābhyāṃ mudgamānaṃ lihetprātarviśuddhadhīḥ |
guḍūcītriphalākvāthaṃ palaṃ cānupibetsudhīḥ || 1,9.54 ||
aṣṭaguñjāvadhirvṛddhirbhavetṣoḍaśamāsataḥ |
tejasvī balavāndhīmāṃścakṣuṣmān rogavarjitaḥ || 1,9.55 ||
valīpalitanirmuktastrikālaviṣajidbhavet |
<svarṇābhrakarasāyanam>
pūrvavanmārayetsvarṇaṃ tasmāddviguṇamabhrakam || 1,9.56 ||
daśamūlavarābhṛṅgīkvāthairbhāvyaṃ trisaptadhā |
prātarmāṣatrayaṃ lehyaṃ madhvājyaiḥ krāmaṇaṃ lihet || 1,9.57 ||
palaṃ guḍūcītriphalākvāthaṃ tadanu pārvati |
caturviṃśatiguñjāsya vṛddhiḥ syātparamāvadhiḥ || 1,9.58 ||
māsaṣoḍaśayogena divyatejā mahābalaḥ |
ghanakāñcanayogena svarṇakāntārhako bhavet || 1,9.59 ||
<svarṇakāntarasāyanam>
pūrvavanmārayetsvarṇaṃ kāntaṃ svarṇadvibhāgakam |
dhātrīphalairdvisaptaiva bhāvayenmardayetkramāt || 1,9.60 ||
saśarkaraṃ bhajetprātastrimāṣaṃ ca pibedanu |
dhātrīsatvaṃ ghṛtaṃ kṣaudraṃ karṣamātraṃ sureśvari || 1,9.61 ||
caturviṃśatikā guñjā vṛddhiḥ syātparamāvadhiḥ |
māsaṣoḍaśayogena valīpalitavarjitaḥ || 1,9.62 ||
taptakāñcanasacchāyaḥ pañcabāṇa ivāparaḥ |
hemakāntāsvādanena kāntābhrakanakārhakaḥ || 1,9.63 ||
<svarṇābhrakāntarasāyanam>
pūrvavanmārayetsvarṇaṃ kāntasattvābhrasatvakam |
samāni trīṇi caitāni bhāvayecca trisaptakam || 1,9.64 ||
muṇḍinirguṇḍikābhṛṅgavarānīreṇa pārvati |
māṣatrayonmitaṃ prātarlihetkṣaudraghṛtāplutam || 1,9.65 ||
muṇḍīcūrṇaṃ karṣamātraṃ madhvājyābhyāṃ lihedanu |
gokṣīraṃ palamātraṃ ca pibettadanu pārvati || 1,9.66 ||
valladvādaśasaṃkhyātā parā vṛddhirbhavetpriye |
māsaṣoḍaśayoge divyakāyo bhavennaraḥ || 1,9.67 ||
hemābhrakāntabhajanādyogyaḥ syādvajrabhakṣaṇe |
<vajrarasāyanam>
pūrvavadbhasmayedvajraṃ dhātrīnīreṇa mardayet || 1,9.68 ||
bhāvayetsaptadhā dhīmānvrīhimātraṃ lihetpriye |
ghṛtakṣaudrayutaṃ cānupibeddhātrīrasaṃ palam || 1,9.69 ||
vallāvadhirbhavedvṛddhirmāsaṣoḍaśayogataḥ |
cirajīvī vajrakāyo divyadṛṣṭirmahābalaḥ || 1,9.70 ||
vajrasya sevayā yogyo bhavedvajrābhrabhakṣaṇe |
<vajrābhrarasāyanam>
pūrvavanmārayedvyoma vajraṃ vajrāccaturguṇam || 1,9.71 ||
ghanaṃ dhātryāḥ śatāvaryā rasairbhāvyaṃ trisaptadhā |
yavamātraṃ lihetprātarmadhvājyābhyāṃ lihedanu || 1,9.72 ||
dhātrīśatāvarīnīraṃ krāmaṇaṃ palamātrakam |
caturguñjā parā vṛddhirbhavetṣoḍaśamāsataḥ || 1,9.73 ||
vāksiddhirdivyadṛṣṭiḥ syāddevatāsadṛśaprabhaḥ |
sevayā ghanavajrasya kāntavajrārhako bhavet || 1,9.74 ||
<kāntavajrarasāyanam>
pūrvavadbhasmayedvajraṃ kāntaṃ vajrāccaturguṇam |
kāntaṃ trisaptadhā bhāvyamaśvagandhāvarārasaiḥ || 1,9.75 ||
yavamātraṃ kṣaudraghṛtairyuktaṃ lehyaṃ dinodaye |
aśvagandhāvarācūrṇaṃ karṣaṃ gopayasā pibet || 1,9.76 ||
caturguñjāvadhirjñeyā vṛddhiḥ syātparamāvadhiḥ |
māsaṣoḍaśayogena bālasūryasamadyutiḥ || 1,9.77 ||
mahābalo mahātejā vāksiddhaḥ siddhatāṃ vrajet |
sevayā vajrakāntasya vajrakāntābhrakārhakaḥ || 1,9.78 ||
<vajrābhrakarasāyanam>
pūrvavanmārayedvajraṃ kāntābhrāṇi ca hīrakāt |
caturguṇe ca kāntābhre varābhṛṅgakuraṇḍajaiḥ || 1,9.79 ||
rasaistrisaptadhā bhāvyaṃ yavamātraṃ lihetpriye |
samadhvājyaṃ bhṛṅgavarākuraṇḍakarajoyutam || 1,9.80 ||
palaṃ pibecca gokṣīraṃ caturguñjāparāvadhiḥ |
māsaṣoḍaśayogena gṛdhradṛṣṭirmahābalaḥ || 1,9.81 ||
avyāhatagatirdhīraḥ siddhasaṃghena vartate |
kāntābhravajrabhajanādyogyaḥ syāddhemavajrayoḥ || 1,9.82 ||
<svarṇavajrarasāyanam>
pūrvavanmārayedvajraṃ svarṇaṃ vajrāddvibhāgakam |
suvarṇavajraṃ varṣābhūrasairbhāvyaṃ trisaptadhā || 1,9.83 ||
vrīhimeyaṃ lihetkṣaudraghṛtābhyāṃ ca punarnavām |
karṣaṃ palaṃ ca gokṣīramanupeyaṃ sureśvari || 1,9.84 ||
dviguñjāvadhi vṛddhiḥ syānmāsaṣoḍaśayogataḥ |
cirakālaṃ bhavejjīvī valīpalitavarjitaḥ || 1,9.85 ||
vajrahemopayogena svarṇābhrakuliśārhakaḥ |
<svarṇābhravajrarasāyanam>
pūrvavadbhasmayeddhemaghanavajrāṇi pārvati || 1,9.86 ||
hīrakāddviguṇaṃ svarṇaṃ svarṇādabhraṃ dvibhāgakam |
musalīkandasāreṇa bhāvanīyaṃ trisaptadhā || 1,9.87 ||
yavamātraṃ lihetprātarmadhvājyābhyāṃ pibedanu |
karṣaṃ syānmusalīcūrṇaṃ gokṣīraṃ palamātrakam || 1,9.88 ||
caturguñjāvadhirvṛddhirmāsaṣoḍaśayogataḥ |
mṛtyuṃ jayejjarāhīno vajrakāyo mahābalaḥ || 1,9.89 ||
etasya sevayā vajrahemakāntārhako bhavet |
<svarṇavajrakāntarasāyanam>
vajrakāntasuvarṇāni pūrvavanmāritāni ca || 1,9.90 ||
vajrātsuvarṇaṃ dviguṇaṃ svarṇātkāntaṃ dvibhāgakam |
dhātrībhṛṅgarasairmardyamekaviṃśativārakam || 1,9.91 ||
yavamātraṃ lihetprātarmadhvājyābhyāṃ pibedanu |
dhātrībhṛṅgabhavaṃ cūrṇaṃ karṣaṃ godugdhakaṃ palam || 1,9.92 ||
guñjācatuṣṭayī vṛddhirmāsaṣoḍaśayogataḥ |
valīpalitanirmukto divyatejā mahābalaḥ || 1,9.93 ||
etasya sevayā kāntasvarṇavajrābhrakārhakaḥ |
<svarṇakāntavajrābhrakarasāyanam>
bhasmayetsvarṇakāntābhravajrāṇi ca yathoktavat || 1,9.94 ||
jyotiṣmatīrasairbhāvyamekaviṃśativārakam |
yavamātraṃ lihetprātarmadhvājyābhyāṃ pibedanu || 1,9.95 ||
aśvagandhākandacūrṇaṃ karṣaṃ godugdhakaṃ palam |
guñjācatuṣṭayī vṛddhiḥ paramāvadhirīśvari || 1,9.96 ||
māsaṣoḍaśayogena sākṣādindrasamo bhavet |
etasya sevayā devi rasaṃ sevitumarhati || 1,9.97 ||
<rasabhasma>
dhānyābhraṃ pāradaṃ mardyaṃ taptakhalve dinatrayam |
triphalāragvadhaniśākumārīkṛṣṇadhūrtajaiḥ || 1,9.98 ||
punarnavāgnijaṃbīrabhavairnīrairvimardayet |
pātayetpātanāyantre tvevaṃ mardanapātanam || 1,9.99 ||
kuryāttrivāraṃ śuddhaḥ syātpārado doṣavarjitaḥ |
niyāmakairmardayettaṃ mūṣālepaṃ niyāmakaiḥ || 1,9.100 ||
kṛtvā taṃ marditaṃ sūtaṃ mūṣāyāṃ nikṣipetsudhīḥ |
andhrayitvā bhūdharākhye yantre pācyaṃ sureśvari || 1,9.101 ||
evaṃ ca saptadhā kuryānmardanaṃ puṭapācanam |
rasabhasma bhavecchubhraṃ yojyaṃ yoge rasāyane || 1,9.102 ||
<āroṭakarasāḥ; abhrakajīrṇarasabhasma>
atha cāroṭakarasaṃ dehasiddhyekakāraṇam |
māṣamātraṃ lihetprātarmadhvājyatriphalaiḥ saha || 1,9.103 ||
dhāroṣṇaṃ gopayaḥ peyaṃ mātramanu priye |
aṣṭaguñjāvadhiṃ vṛddhiṃ kṛtvā ṣoḍaśamāsakam || 1,9.104 ||
valīpalitanirmukto jīvedācandratārakam |
etasya sevayā devi hyabhrasūtārhako bhavet || 1,9.105 ||
mukhīkṛte rase devi catuḥṣaṣṭikaniṣkake |
abhrasatvaṃ niṣkamātraṃ kṣiptvā jambīrajairdravaiḥ || 1,9.106 ||
taptakhalve mardayecca dinaṃ kacchapayantrake |
sabiḍaṃ ca pacetpaścāttamādāya vimardayet || 1,9.107 ||
abhrasatvaṃ dviniṣkaṃ ca jambīrāmlena pārvati |
taptakhalve kacchapākhye yantre pacanakarma ca || 1,9.108 ||
tṛtīye ca caturniṣkaṃ caturthe pañcame kramāt |
aṣṭaniṣkābhrasatvaṃ ca dadyātpañcamapātane || 1,9.109 ||
ghanaṣoḍaśaniṣkaṃ ca ṣaṣṭhe dvātriṃśadaṃśakam |
sattvaṃ saptame syātsamameva hi pārvati || 1,9.110 ||
pārade'bhrakasatvasya jāraṇā bhavati priye |
evaṃ jāritasūtendraṃ bhasmīkuryācca pūrvavat || 1,9.111 ||
palaṃ caitatsūtabhasma vyomasatvaṃ catuṣpalam |
bhṛṅgarājadravairmardyaṃ mūṣāyāmandhayeddṛḍham || 1,9.112 ||
kukkuṭākhye puṭe pacyātsvāṅgaśītaṃ samāharet |
triphalābhṛṅgajairnīrairbhāvayettattrisaptadhā || 1,9.113 ||
madhvājyābhyāṃ guñjamātraṃ lihetprātarviśuddhadhīḥ |
pibedanu varākvāthaṃ palaṃ niyatamānasaḥ || 1,9.114 ||
guñjāṣoḍaśikā vṛddhiḥ sevyaṃ ṣoḍaśamāsataḥ |
ācandratārakaṃ jīvedvalīpalitavarjitaḥ || 1,9.115 ||
etasya sevayā kāntasūtasevārhako bhavet |
<kāntajīrṇarasabhasma>
mukhīkṛte sūtarāje yathābhūdabhrajāraṇā || 1,9.116 ||
tathaiva kāntasatvasya jāraṇā bhavati priye |
mārayetpūrvavatsūtaṃ kāntasatvena jāritam || 1,9.117 ||
evaṃvidhaṃ sabhasma bhāgamekaṃ bhavetpunaḥ |
caturbhāgaṃ kāntabhasma mardayettriphalāmbunā || 1,9.118 ||
mūṣāyāmandhrayetpaścātpacetkaukkuṭike puṭe |
tamādāya varānīrairbhāvayecca trisaptadhā || 1,9.119 ||
madhvājyābhyāṃ lihedguñjāmātraṃ prātaratandritaḥ |
anupeyaṃ ca gokṣīraṃ palamātraṃ sureśvari || 1,9.120 ||
guñjāṣoḍaśikā vṛddhirmāsaṣoḍaśayogataḥ |
divyadṛṣṭiṃ khecaratvaṃ prāpnuyādaindrakaṃ padam || 1,9.121 ||
etasya sevayā sūtaghanakāntārhako bhavet |
mukhīkṛtarase kuryātpūrvavadvyomajāraṇam || 1,9.122 ||
<kāntābhrakajīrṇarasabhasma>
samaṃ sūtasya deveśi paścātkāryaṃ vicakṣaṇaiḥ |
pūrvavatkāntasatvasya jāraṇā sūtarāṭsamā || 1,9.123 ||
kāntasatvābhrasatvābhyāṃ jāritaḥ pārado yadi |
sa rasaḥ pakṣahīnaḥ syāccāñcalyarahitaḥ śivaḥ || 1,9.124 ||
taṃ rasaṃ pūrvavaddivyairauṣadhairmāritaṃ rasam |
palaṃ taddviguṇaṃ vyomasatvabhasma ca tatsamam || 1,9.125 ||
bhasma vaikrāntasatvasya mardyamevaṃ trayaṃ tridhā |
bhṛṅgadhātrīphalarase tato laghupuṭe pacet || 1,9.126 ||
bhṛṅgadhātrīphalarasaiśchāyāyāṃ bhāvayedrasam |
triphalāmadhusarpirbhirguñjāmātraṃ lihedanu || 1,9.127 ||
pibetpalaṃ ca gokṣīraṃ vṛddhiḥ ṣoḍaśaguñjikā |
māsaṣoḍaśayogena jīvedācandratārakam || 1,9.128 ||
etasya sevayā sūtasvarṇasevārhako bhavet |
<svarṇajīrṇarasabhasma>
hemajīrṇaṃ sūtarājaṃ bhasmīkuryācca pūrvavat || 1,9.129 ||
evaṃvidhaṃ sūtabhasma palaṃ svarṇapaladvayam |
bhṛṅgadhātrīphalarasaiśchāyāyāṃ bhāvayettridhā || 1,9.130 ||
ruddhvā taṃ saṃpuṭe pacyātkukkuṭākhye puṭe pacet |
punastrisaptadhā bhṛṅgadhātrīnīraiśca bhāvayet || 1,9.131 ||
māṣamātraṃ lihetprātarmadhvājyābhyāṃ yutaṃ rasam |
anupeyaṃ ca gokṣīraṃ vṛddhiḥ ṣoḍaśamāṣakam || 1,9.132 ||
māsaṣoḍaśayogena devatulyaścirāyuṣaḥ |
sevayā svarṇasūtasya ghanahemarasārhakaḥ || 1,9.133 ||
<svarṇābhrakajīrṇarasabhasma>
mukhīkṛtarase cābhrasatvaṃ jāryaṃ samaṃ purā |
tataḥ svarṇaṃ samaṃ jāryaṃ taṃ sūtaṃ bhasmayettataḥ || 1,9.134 ||
pūrvoktavatsūtabhasma palaṃ svarṇapaladvayam |
catuṣpalaṃ vyomasatvabhasma caikatra yojayet || 1,9.135 ||
nīlīpatrarase mardyaṃ varākvāthe dinaṃ priye |
ruddhvā taṃ saṃpuṭe pacyādanyairviṃśatigomayaiḥ || 1,9.136 ||
nīlīrasavarākvāthairbhāvayettaṃ trisaptadhā |
guñjonmeyaṃ lihetkalye madhvājyābhyāṃ pibedanu || 1,9.137 ||
palamātraṃ varākvāthaṃ vṛddhiḥ ṣoḍaśaguñjikā |
māsaṣoḍaśayogena jīvedācandratārakam || 1,9.138 ||
etasya sevayā kāntahemasūtārhako bhavet |
<svarṇakāntajīrṇarasabhasma>
samukhe pārade kāntasatvaṃ jāryaṃ yathoktavat || 1,9.139 ||
jārayecca tathā svarṇaṃ dvābhyāṃ jīrṇaṃ samaṃ samam |
vidhinā bhasmayetsūtaṃ kāntahāṭakajāritam || 1,9.140 ||
taṃ rasaṃ palamekaṃ ca hemabhasma paladvayam |
calaṃ kāntasatvabhasma yuñjyādyathāvidhi || 1,9.141 ||
śatāvarīvarānīre dinaṃ saṃmardya saṃpuṭe |
ruddhvā taṃ kukkuṭapuṭe pacedādāya taṃ rasam || 1,9.142 ||
triḥ saptadhā śatāvaryā rasairbhāvyaṃ varārasaiḥ |
guñjāmātraṃ ghṛtakṣaudrayuktaṃ lehyaṃ pibedanu || 1,9.143 ||
śatāvarīrasaḥ peyaḥ palamātraṃ sureśvari |
guñjāṣoḍaśikā vṛddhirmāsaṣoḍaśayogataḥ || 1,9.144 ||
valīpalitanirmuktaḥ pralayāntaṃ ca jīvati |
etasya sevayā svarṇakāntābhrarasabhugbhavet || 1,9.145 ||
<svarṇakāntābhrajīrṇarasabhasma>
ghanakāntasuvarṇāni caikaikāni samāni vai |
mukhīkṛtasya sūtasya jārayetkramaśaḥ priye || 1,9.146 ||
ghanādijāritaṃ sūtaṃ mārayetpūrvavatsudhīḥ |
tasmādbhasmīkṛtātsūtāddviguṇaṃ hemabhasma ca || 1,9.147 ||
hematulyaṃ mṛtaṃ kāntaṃ kāntatulyaṃ mṛtaṃ ghanam |
etatsarvaṃ varākvāthamuṇḍībhṛṅgarasairdinam || 1,9.148 ||
mardayetsaṃpuṭe ruddhvā pacetkaukkuṭike puṭe |
tadādāya varābhṛṅgamuṇḍinīraiśca bhāvayet || 1,9.149 ||
triḥ saptavāraṃ chāyāyāṃ prātarguñjonmitaṃ lihet |
śarkarāmadhusarpirbhiranupeyaṃ ca gopayaḥ || 1,9.150 ||
guñjāṣoḍaśikā vṛddhirmāsaṣoḍaśayogataḥ |
valīpalitanirmukto jīvedācandratārakam || 1,9.151 ||
etasya sevayā sūtavajrasevārhako bhavet |
<vajrajīrṇarasabhasma>
samukhe pārade kuryātpūrvavadvajrajāraṇām || 1,9.152 ||
vajrajīrṇaṃ rasaṃ devi bhasmīkuryācca pūrvavat |
bhasmībhūtarasādvajrabhasma ca dviguṇaṃ priye || 1,9.153 ||
kanyābhṛṅgavarānīrairdinaṃ mardyaṃ ca saṃpuṭe |
kukkuṭākhye puṭe pacyāttamādāyātha bhāvayet || 1,9.154 ||
kanyābhṛṅgavarānīrairbhāvayecca trisaptadhā |
yavamātraṃ lihetprātarmadhvājyābhyāṃ pibedanu || 1,9.155 ||
gokṣīraṃ palamātraṃ tu vṛddhirguñjācatuṣṭayam |
māsaṣoḍaśayogena jīvedācandratārakam || 1,9.156 ||
etasya sevayā vajrasūtavyomārhako bhavet |
<vajrābhrakajīrṇarasabhasma>
ghanajīrṇaṃ vajrajīrṇaṃ bhasmīkuryādrasaṃ sudhīḥ || 1,9.157 ||
bhasmībhūtādrasādvajraṃ dviguṇaṃ vyomabhasma ca |
caturguṇaṃ ca tatsarvamekīkṛtya vimardayet || 1,9.158 ||
varākvāthe rasairdinaṃ saṃpuṭake pacet |
kṛte laghupuṭe paścādbhāvyaṃ bhṛṅgavarārasaiḥ || 1,9.159 ||
trisaptadhā samadhvājyaṃ māṣamātraṃ pibedanu |
gavyaṃ kṣīrapalaṃ peyaṃ vṛddhirguñjāṣṭakāvadhiḥ || 1,9.160 ||
evaṃ ṣoḍaśamāsena jīvedācandratārakam |
etasya sevayā kāntavajrasūtārhako bhavet || 1,9.161 ||
<kāntavajrajīrṇarasabhasma>
kāntaṃ vajraṃ samaṃ jāryaṃ samukhe pārade priye |
kāntahīrakajīrṇaṃ taṃ pāradaṃ mārayetsudhīḥ || 1,9.162 ||
pūrvoktavidhinā kānte etatpāradabhasma ca |
pāradāddviguṇaṃ vajraṃ vajrātkāntaṃ caturguṇam || 1,9.163 ||
palāśapuṣpasvarasairdinaṃ mardyaṃ ca saṃpuṭe |
ruddhvā kukkuṭake paścāttamādāyātha bhāvayet || 1,9.164 ||
palāśapuṣpanīreṇa bhāvayettaṃ trisaptadhā |
samadhvājyaṃ māṣamātraṃ lihedanu pibetpayaḥ || 1,9.165 ||
aṣṭaguñjāvadhirvṛddhirbhavetṣoḍaśamāsataḥ |
valīpalitanirmukto jīvedācandratārakam || 1,9.166 ||
etasya sevayā vajrarasakāntābhrakārhakaḥ |
<kāntavajrābhrakajīrṇarasabhasma>
samukhe pārade vyomakāntavajrāṇi ca kramāt || 1,9.167 ||
samāni jārayetpaścājjīrṇaṃ taṃ mārayedrasam |
pūrvavatpāradādvajraṃ samaṃ vajracaturguṇam || 1,9.168 ||
kāntaṃ kāntasamaṃ vyomasatvaṃ caitāni mardayet |
triphalābhṛṅgajarasai ruddhvā saṃpuṭake pacet || 1,9.169 ||
laghunāgnipuṭenaiva tamādāyātha bhāvayet |
varābhṛṅgarasairekaviṃśatiṃ vāramātape || 1,9.170 ||
māṣamātraṃ samadhvājyaṃ lihetprātarviśuddhadhīḥ |
anupeyaṃ ca gokṣīraṃ vṛddhirguñjāṣṭakaṃ bhavet || 1,9.171 ||
māsaṣoḍaśayogena jīvedācandratārakam |
etasya sevayā hemavajrasūtārhako bhavet || 1,9.172 ||
<svarṇavajrajīrṇarasabhasma>
samukhe pārade tulye hema vajraṃ ca jārayet |
taṃ rasaṃ bhasmayeddevi rasaṃ vajraṃ samaṃ samam || 1,9.173 ||
tayoḥ samaṃ mṛtaṃ hema varākvāthena mardayet |
tatsarvaṃ saṃpuṭe kṣiptvā bhūdharākhye puṭe pacet || 1,9.174 ||
tamādāya varākvāthairbhāvayecca trisaptadhā |
māṣamātraṃ lihetprātastilājyamadhusaṃyutam || 1,9.175 ||
gokṣīramanupeyaṃ syādaṣṭaguñjāvadhi kramāt |
māsaṣoḍaśaparyantaṃ jīyādācandratārakam || 1,9.176 ||
etasya sevayā vyomahemavajrarasārhakaḥ |
<svarṇavajrābhrakajīrṇarasabhasma>
samukhe pārade vyomahemavajrāṇi jārayet || 1,9.177 ||
pāradasya samāṃśāni tataḥ sūtaṃ vimārayet |
rasatulyaṃ mṛtaṃ vajraṃ tayostulyaṃ ca hāṭakam || 1,9.178 ||
sarvatulyaṃ rasaṃ vyoma tatsarvaṃ brahmabījakaiḥ |
tailaistryahaṃ peṣayitvā saṃpuṭedbhūdhare puṭe || 1,9.179 ||
taṃ palāśakaṣāyeṇa bhāvanāścaikaviṃśatiḥ |
taṃ rasaṃ māṣamātraṃ ca tailaṃ brahmadrubījakam || 1,9.180 ||
karṣamātraṃ pibeccānu yāvadguñjāṣṭakāvadhi |
māsaṣoḍaśayogena siddho bhavati śāśvataḥ || 1,9.181 ||
etasya sevayā kāntahemavajrarasārhakaḥ |
<svarṇakāntavajrajīrṇarasabhasma>
mukhīkṛte sūtarāje kāntaṃ svarṇaṃ samaṃ samam || 1,9.182 ||
jārayedbhasmayettaṃ ca tatsamaṃ vajrabhasma ca |
tayostulyaṃ mṛtaṃ hema kāntaṃ sarvasamaṃ priye || 1,9.183 ||
varābhṛṅgarase piṣṭvā bhūdhare saṃpuṭe pacet |
tato bhṛṅgavarānīrairekaviṃśatibhāvanāḥ || 1,9.184 ||
varākṣaudrairlihetprātarmāṣamātraṃ rasaṃ sudhīḥ |
gavyaṃ payaḥ palaṃ peyaṃ vṛddhiḥ ṣoḍaśamāṣikā || 1,9.185 ||
māsaṣoḍaśayogena bhavetsiddhasamaḥ prabhuḥ |
etasya sevayā vyomakāntahemapavīrasam || 1,9.186 ||
sevituṃ jāyate'rho'sau saṃyatātmā maheśvaraḥ |
<svarṇakāntavajrābhrakajīrṇarasabhasma>
ghanakāntasvarṇavajraṃ jārayetsamukhe rase || 1,9.187 ||
pratyekaṃ pāradasamameva kāryaṃ sureśvari |
tatastaṃ mārayedyuktyā rasatulyaṃ mṛtaṃ pavim || 1,9.188 ||
tayostulyaṃ mṛtaṃ hema sarvatulyaṃ mṛtaṃ ghanam |
ghanatulyamayaskāntaṃ sarvatulyaṃ suradrujaiḥ || 1,9.189 ||
tailaistataḥ saṃpuṭe ca kṣiptvā bhūdharake pacet |
guñjāmātraṃ rasaṃ caitaṃ lihetprātaḥ śuciḥ sudhīḥ || 1,9.190 ||
devadārujatailena karṣamātraṃ tu pārvati |
dhāroṣṇaṃ gopayaḥ peyaṃ palamātramanu priye || 1,9.191 ||
guñjāṣoḍaśaparyantaṃ māsaṣoḍaśayogataḥ |
ya imaṃ pāradaṃ divyaṃ sevate pathyabhuksadā || 1,9.192 ||
vyādhijanmajarāmṛtyuvarjitaḥ sarvasiddhibhāk |
sarvajñaḥ sarvagaḥ siddhaḥ sṛjatīva pitāmahaḥ || 1,9.193 ||
viṣṇuvattrāyate viśvaṃ haratīva haraḥ svayam |
vāñchitārthān svayaṃ datte kalpadruma ivāparaḥ || 1,9.194 ||
kandarpa iva kāmākṣīḥ sahasraṃ ramayetkṣaṇāt |
saccidānandarūpo'yaṃ rasasevī bhaveddhruvam || 1,9.195 ||
ghanādipañcayogotthamekatriṃśadrasāyanam |
bhavatsnehena kathitaṃ rahasyaṃ devadurlabham || 1,9.196 ||


āk, 1, 10
<ghuṭikāsiddhiḥ>
praṇamya parayā bhaktyā bhairavaṃ bhairavī śivam |
uvāca vinayenedaṃ lokānāṃ hitakāṃkṣiṇī || 1,10.1 ||
śrībhairavī |
devadeva kṛpāmūrte sarvānugrāhaka prabho |
tvatprasādānmayā jñātaṃ rahasyamatidurlabham || 1,10.2 ||
kāntābhrahemakuliśarasabhasma rasāyanam |
ataḥ paraṃ mahādeva śrotumicchāmi bhairava || 1,10.3 ||
etairghanādyai racitaghuṭikānāṃ rasāyanam |
śrutvā savinayaṃ vākyaṃ bhairavyāstvādibhairavaḥ || 1,10.4 ||
sthitvetyuvāca vacanaṃ tadvakṣyāmi rasāyanam |
śrībhairavaḥ |
<1. sañjīvanī ghuṭikā>
śṛṇu pārvati yatnena sāvadhānena sāmpratam || 1,10.5 ||
prathamaṃ jāraṇaṃ kāryaṃ paścātpāradabandhanam |
tasmājjāraṇabījāni ghanādīnāṃ vadāmi te || 1,10.6 ||
gandhatālaśilātutthakharparīhiṅgulāmalāḥ |
bhūnāgatāpyakāsīsanṛpāvartābhragairikam || 1,10.7 ||
kāntatrikṣāravaikrāntāstvete cūrṇasamāṃśakaḥ |
siddhacūrṇamidaṃ khyātaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ syādbījakarmaṇi || 1,10.8 ||
dvandvamelopaliptāyāṃ mūṣāyāṃ nikṣipetpriye |
kṛṣṇasatvābhrasatvaṃ ca drāvayetsamaśulbakam || 1,10.9 ||
samitrapañcakaṃ tasminsiddhacūrṇaṃ muhurmuhuḥ |
ghanāddaśaguṇaṃ kṣepyaṃ dhamedgāḍhaṃ varānane || 1,10.10 ||
ghanasatvāvaśiṣṭaṃ syāddhamanīyaṃ ca tāvatā |
kālenedaṃ vyomasatvabījaṃ jāryaṃ rasāyane || 1,10.11 ||
samukhe pārade bījaṃ catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśake kṣipet |
taptakhalve'mlavargeṇa mardayeddivasatrayam || 1,10.12 ||
taṃ sūtaṃ kacchape yantre sabiḍe jārayetpriye |
tato dvātriṃśadaṃśaṃ ca ṣoḍaśāṃśaṃ rasasya tu || 1,10.13 ||
aṣṭamāṃśaṃ caturthāṃśaṃ dvyaṃśaṃ caiva samāṃśakam |
pārade jārayedevaṃ kramādbījaṃ sureśvari || 1,10.14 ||
sūtamevaṃvidhaṃ vyomasatvaṃ bījasamaṃ priye |
taptakhalve'mlavargeṇa mardayedekavāsaram || 1,10.15 ||
piṣṭiṃ pāradarājaṃ taṃ jambīrāntarvinikṣipet |
dolāyantre'mlayukte taṃ vipacetsaptavāsaram || 1,10.16 ||
tamādāya baliṃ kṣiptvā siddhacūrṇena pārvati |
tatastaṃ bhūdhare yantre pacellaghupuṭena ca || 1,10.17 ||
yāvatkaṭhinatāṃ yāti tāvatkāryaṃ muhurmuhuḥ |
dolāpākaṃ siddhacūrṇalepaṃ bhūdharake puṭam || 1,10.18 ||
mṛtasañjīvanī nāmnā ghuṭikā sarvasiddhidā |
vaktrāntare gale mūrdhni bāhau karṇe'thavā vahet || 1,10.19 ||
hemnā suveṣṭitā samyagvalīpalitanāśinī |
vaktrasthā mṛtyuhā varṣādviṣavigrahanāśinī || 1,10.20 ||
śuddhaṃ gandhaṃ karṣamātraṃ gavyaṃ kṣīraṃ palaṃ pibet |
anena kramaṇenaiva sūtaḥ saṃkramate tanum || 1,10.21 ||
<2. divyā ghuṭikā>
kāntamlecchamukhaṃ rūpyaṃ liptamūṣāntare samam |
drāvayitvā siddhacūrṇaṃ kāntāddaśaguṇaṃ śanaiḥ || 1,10.22 ||
vahenmuhurmuhurdrāvyaṃ yāvatkānto'vaśiṣyate |
kāntabījamidaṃ proktaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ jāryaṃ rasāyane || 1,10.23 ||
samukhe pārade cedaṃ bījaṃ cāryaṃ ca pūrvavat |
catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśakaṃ pūrvaṃ samāṃśaṃ taṃ krameṇa vai || 1,10.24 ||
evaṃvidhaṃ sūtarājaṃ kāntabījaṃ dvayaṃ samam |
mardayettaptakhalve ca sāmlavarge dinatrayam || 1,10.25 ||
piṣṭībhūtaṃ ca jambīre kṣiptvāmlagaṇapūrite |
dolāyantre pacetsamyagevamā saptavārakam || 1,10.26 ||
tamādāya lihedbāhye siddhacūrṇena bhairavi |
tato divyauṣadhairlipte saṃpuṭe bhūdhare pacet || 1,10.27 ||
yāvatkaṭhinatāṃ yāti tāvatkuryātkrameṇa ca |
dolāpākaṃ siddhacūrṇalepaṃ bhūdharapācanam || 1,10.28 ||
puṭe tau jārayeddivyanāmnātha parameśvari |
vaktrāntare gale mūrdhni bāhau karṇe'thavā vahet || 1,10.29 ||
hemnā suveṣṭitaṃ dhāryaṃ valīpalitanāśanam |
pūrvavatphaladā puṇyā dhāryā yatnena mānavaiḥ || 1,10.30 ||
pūrvavatkrāmaṇaṃ kāryaṃ dehe saṃkramate rasaḥ |
<3. kāmeśvarī ghuṭikā>
samukhe pārade vyomakāntabījaṃ ca mārayet || 1,10.31 ||
pratyekaṃ pāradasamaṃ pūrvavacca śanaiḥ śanaiḥ |
catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśakaṃ cādau samāṃśaṃ taṃ kramādrase || 1,10.32 ||
asya sūtasya tulyāṃśaṃ kāntabījābhrabījakam |
taptakhalve'mlavargeṇa mardayedvāsaratrayam || 1,10.33 ||
piṣṭīkṛtaṃ ca jambīre kṣiptvā dolāgataṃ pacet |
punarādāya tāṃ piṣṭiṃ siddhacūrṇena lepayet || 1,10.34 ||
divyauṣadhigaṇairlipte saṃpuṭe bhūdhare pacet |
yadā kaṭhinatāṃ yāti dvidhā kuryātkrameṇa tu || 1,10.35 ||
dolāsvedaṃ cūrṇalepaṃ kṛtvā bhūdharapācanam |
kāmeśvarīyaṃ ghuṭikā vaktrasthā sarvasiddhidā || 1,10.36 ||
pūrvavatkrāmaṇaṃ kāryaṃ rasendraḥ krāmate tanum |
<4. hemasundarī ghuṭikā>
svarṇaṃ rūpyaṃ mlecchamukhaṃ kāntasatvābhrasatvakam || 1,10.37 ||
dvandvamelopaliptāyāṃ mūṣāyāṃ tadvinikṣipet |
tasmindrute siddhacūrṇaṃ svarṇāddaśaguṇaṃ kṣipet || 1,10.38 ||
yāvatsvarṇāvaśeṣaṃ syāttāvaddrāvyaṃ śanaiḥ śanaiḥ |
hemabījamidaṃ khyātaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ rasarasāyane || 1,10.39 ||
samukhe pārade devi bījaṃ rasasamaṃ kramāt |
jārayetpūrvavaddevi catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśakādikam || 1,10.40 ||
etadrasaṃ hemabījaṃ samaṃ khalve ca tāpite |
mardayedamlavargeṇa tryahātpiṣṭirbhavedrasaḥ || 1,10.41 ||
piṣṭiṃ jambīrajāṃ kṛtvā dolāyantre'mlapūrite |
ṣaḍahaṃ pācayeddevi siddhacūrṇapralepanam || 1,10.42 ||
tathā saṃpuṭalepaṃ ca tathā bhūdharapācanam |
yāvatkaṭhinatāṃ yāti tāvatkuryācca pūrvavat || 1,10.43 ||
iyaṃ tu ghuṭikā divyā vikhyātā hemasundarī |
vaktrasthā siddhidā nḥṇāṃ jarāmṛtyuviṣāpahā || 1,10.44 ||
pūrvavatkrāmaṇaṃ kāryaṃ sūtaḥ saṃkramate punaḥ |
<5. madanasundarī ghuṭikā>
mukhīkṛte rase vyomabījaṃ hāṭakabījakam || 1,10.45 ||
samaṃ samaṃ kramājjāryaṃ catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśakādikam |
ityevaṃ jāritaṃ sūtaṃ bījaṃ gaganarukmayoḥ || 1,10.46 ||
mardayetpāradasamaṃ taptakhalve'mlavargataḥ |
tridinājjāyate piṣṭiḥ punarjambīragāṃ kuru || 1,10.47 ||
dolāyantre'mlabharite pacettāṃ saptavāsaram |
lepanaṃ siddhacūrṇasya tathā saṃpuṭalepanam || 1,10.48 ||
tathā bhūdharapākaśca yāvatkaṭhinatāṃ vrajet |
siddhidā ghuṭikā hyeṣā nāmnā madanasundarī || 1,10.49 ||
āsyāntarasthitā kuryātsarvasiddhīścirāyuṣaḥ |
vyālavyāghragajādīnāṃ rājñāṃ vaśyaṃ striyāmapi || 1,10.50 ||
pūrvavatkrāmaṇaṃ kāryaṃ kāyamākrāmate rasaḥ |
<6. khecarī ghuṭikā>
mukhīkṛte rase kāntahemabījaṃ samaṃ samam || 1,10.51 ||
rasonmite pṛthakjāryaṃ catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśakādikam |
itthaṃ jīrṇaṃ rasaṃ bījaṃ kāntahemno rasonmitam || 1,10.52 ||
pūrvavatpiṣṭikāṃ kṛtvā dolāyantre ca pācanam |
yāvatkaṭhinatāṃ yāti tāvatkāryaṃ muhurmuhuḥ || 1,10.53 ||
ghuṭikā jāyate nāmnā khecarī sarvasiddhidā |
pūrvavatkrāmaṇaṃ kāryaṃ sugandhaṃ gopayaḥ pibet || 1,10.54 ||
tena saṃkramate dehaṃ ghuṭikāntargato rasaḥ |
<7. vajreśvarī ghuṭikā>
samukhe pārade vyomakāntahemnāṃ samaṃ samam || 1,10.55 ||
bījaṃ pṛthakpṛthakjāryaṃ catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśakādikam |
itthaṃbhūtasya sūtasya samaṃ vyomādibījakam || 1,10.56 ||
trayaṃ tāpitakhalvena tryahamamlena mardayet |
sa sūtaḥ piṣṭimāpnoti tāṃ piṣṭiṃ pūrvavatpriye || 1,10.57 ||
dolāpākaṃ siddhacūrṇalepaṃ bhūdharapācanam |
yāvatkaṭhinatāṃ yāti tāvatkāryamatandritaiḥ || 1,10.58 ||
vajreśvarīti ghuṭikā vaktrasthā sarvasiddhidā |
pūrvavatkrāmaṇaṃ kāryaṃ gātraṃ vyāpnoti pāradaḥ || 1,10.59 ||
<8. mahāvajreśvarī ghuṭikā>
śuddhaṃ svarṇaṃ vajrabhasma lohacūrṇābhrasatvakam |
etaccatuḥsamaṃ nāgaṃ mūṣāyāṃ cāndhritaṃ dhamet || 1,10.60 ||
dvandvamelopaliptāyāṃ punarādāya taṃ priye |
ekībhūtaṃ ca mūṣāyāṃ prakaṭaṃ ca dhametpunaḥ || 1,10.61 ||
dhautasattvaṃ mākṣikaṃ ca svalpaṃ svalpaṃ muhuḥ kṣipet |
vajrahemāvaśeṣaṃ tu yāvatsyāttata uddharet || 1,10.62 ||
etasmin vyomasatvāyaścūrṇanāgāṃśca pūrvavat |
dvandvamelopaliptāyāmandhrayitvā dṛḍhaṃ dhamet || 1,10.63 ||
ekībhūtaṃ samādāya mūṣāyāṃ prakaṭaṃ dhamet |
hemavajrāvaśeṣaṃ syādyāvattatpunarāharet || 1,10.64 ||
vyomasatvamayaścūrṇaṃ nāgaṃ vāhyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ |
pūrvavatkramayogena mākṣikaṃ dhautasattvakam || 1,10.65 ||
muhuḥ kṣipya dhamettaṃ tu bāhyamevaṃ tu ṣaḍguṇam |
vajrabījamidaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ jāryaṃ rasarasāyane || 1,10.66 ||
samukhe pārade jāryaṃ vajrabījaṃ rasonmitam |
catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśakādyaṃ ca kramātsaṃkrāmayetpriye || 1,10.67 ||
itthaṃ jāritasūtasya samaṃ kuliśabījakam |
taptakhalve'mlavargeṇa mardayeddivasatrayam || 1,10.68 ||
sa piṣṭirjāyate sūtastāṃ piṣṭīṃ ca samāharet |
dolāpākaḥ siddhacūrṇalepaḥ sampuṭabhūdharam || 1,10.69 ||
kurvīta pūrvavatsarvaṃ yāvatkaṭhinatāṃ vrajet |
mahāvajreśvarī nāmnā ghuṭikā siddhidāyinī || 1,10.70 ||
vaktrasthā krāmaṇaṃ kāryaṃ pūrvavatkrāmate rasaḥ |
<9. vajrakhecarī ghuṭikā>
mukhīkṛte rase vyomavajrabījaṃ rasonmitam || 1,10.71 ||
pṛthakpṛthakjāraṇīyaṃ catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśakādikam |
amuṣya jīrṇasūtasya samaṃ vajrābhrabījakam || 1,10.72 ||
taptakhalve'mlavargeṇa tridinaṃ mardayeddṛḍham |
sa raso jāyate piṣṭistāṃ punaḥ pūrvavatpriye || 1,10.73 ||
dolāsvedaṃ siddhacūrṇalepaṃ bhūdharapācanam |
yāvatkaṭhinatāṃ yati tāvadevaṃ muhurmuhuḥ || 1,10.74 ||
ghuṭikā jāyate divyā nāmneyaṃ vajrakhecarī |
vaktrasthā siddhidā nṛṇāṃ pūrvavatkrāmaṇaṃ priye || 1,10.75 ||
<10. kālavidhvaṃsinī ghuṭikā>
mukhīkṛte rase kāntavajrabījaṃ rasonmitam |
pṛthakpṛthagjāraṇīyaṃ catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśakādikam || 1,10.76 ||
asya jīrṇasya sūtasya samaṃ kuliśakāntayoḥ |
bījaṃ saṃtaptakhalve'mlavarge mardyaṃ dinatrayam || 1,10.77 ||
piṣṭirbhavatyeṣa sūtaḥ piṣṭiṃ tāṃ punarāharet |
dolāsvedaḥ siddhacūrṇalepaḥ saṃpuṭabhūdharam || 1,10.78 ||
yāvatkaṭhinatāṃ yāti tāvadevaṃ muhurmuhuḥ |
kālavidhvaṃsinī nāmnā ghuṭikā jāyate śubhā || 1,10.79 ||
mukhasthā siddhidā divyā pūrvavatkrāmaṇaṃ pibet |
<11. gaganeśvarī ghuṭikā>
mukhīkṛte sūtarāje vajrakāntābhrabījakam || 1,10.80 ||
pṛthakpṛthaksūtarāje catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśakādikam |
rasasya tasya sadṛśaṃ vajrabījābhrabījakam || 1,10.81 ||
taptakhalve'mlavargeṇa tryahātpiṣṭiḥ sumardanāt |
dolāpākaḥ siddhacūrṇalepaḥ saṃpuṭabhūdharam || 1,10.82 ||
yāvatkaṭhinatāṃ yāti tāvatkuryānmuhurmuhuḥ |
gaganeśvarīyaṃ ghuṭikā vaktrasthā sarvasiddhidā || 1,10.83 ||
pūrvavatkrāmaṇaṃ kāryaṃ dehe krāmati sūtakaḥ |
<12. vajraghaṇṭeśvarī ghuṭikā>
mukhīkṛte sūtarāje bījaṃ vajrasuvarṇayoḥ || 1,10.84 ||
pṛthakpṛthaksamaṃ jāryaṃ catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśakādikam |
asya sūtasya sadṛśaṃ hemno vajrasya bījakam || 1,10.85 ||
taptakhalve'mlavargeṇa tryahātpiṣṭirbhavedrasaḥ |
tāṃ piṣṭiṃ punarādāya pūrvavatparikalpayet || 1,10.86 ||
dolāpākaṃ siddhacūrṇalepaṃ bhūdharapācanam |
yāvatkaṭhinatāṃ yāti tāvatkuryānmuhurmuhuḥ || 1,10.87 ||
vajraghaṇṭeśvarī hyeṣā ghuṭikā siddhidāyinī |
mukhasthā siddhidā divyā pūrvoktaṃ krāmaṇaṃ pibet || 1,10.88 ||
<13. vajrabhairavī ghuṭikā>
mukhīkṛte sūtarāje hemavajrābhrabījakam |
pṛthakpṛthaksamaṃ jāryaṃ catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśakādikam || 1,10.89 ||
asya sūtasya sadṛśaṃ hemavajrābhrabījakam |
mardayedamlavargeṇa taptakhalve dinatrayam || 1,10.90 ||
piṣṭirbhavetpunastāṃ ca samādāya tataḥ priye |
dolāpākaṃ siddhacūrṇalepaṃ bhūdharapācanam || 1,10.91 ||
yāvatkaṭhinatāmeti tāvatkāryaṃ muhurmuhuḥ |
ghuṭikā jāyate divyā nāmneyaṃ vajrabhairavī || 1,10.92 ||
mukhasthitā siddhakarī krāmaṇaṃ pūrvavatpriye |
<14. tripurabhairavī ghuṭikā>
mukhīkṛte rase kāntavajrahāṭakabījakam || 1,10.93 ||
jāryaṃ pṛthaksūtasamaṃ catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśakādikam |
jīrṇasūtasamaṃ kāntavajrakāñcanabījakam || 1,10.94 ||
taptakhalve'mlavargeṇa mardayeddivasatrayam |
jāyate pāradaḥ piṣṭiḥ pūrvavattāṃ samāharet || 1,10.95 ||
dolāpākaṃ siddhacūrṇalepaṃ bhūdharapācanam |
yāvatkaṭhinatāmeti tāvatkāryaṃ muhurmuhuḥ || 1,10.96 ||
ghuṭikā jāyate divyā nāmnā tripurabhairavī |
mukhasthā siddhidā hyeṣā pūrvavatkrāmaṇaṃ priye || 1,10.97 ||
<15. mahābhairavī ghuṭikā>
mukhīkṛte rase kāntavajrābhrasvarṇabījakam |
pṛthaksūtasamaṃ jāryaṃ catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśakādikam || 1,10.98 ||
asya sūtasya jīrṇasya samaṃ vyomādibījakam |
mardayedamlavargeṇa taptakhalve dinatrayam || 1,10.99 ||
piṣṭirbhavati sūtendrastāṃ piṣṭiṃ punarāharet |
dolāpākaṃ siddhacūrṇalepaṃ saṃpuṭabhūdhare || 1,10.100 ||
yāvatkaṭhinatāmeti tāvatkāryaṃ muhurmuhuḥ |
nāmnā mahābhairavīyaṃ ghuṭikā sarvasiddhidā || 1,10.101 ||
dhārayenmukhamadhye tatpūrvavatkrāmaṇaṃ pibet |
<krāmaṇāni>
triphalā musalī muṇḍī bhṛṅgarājaśca vākucī || 1,10.102 ||
nīlī kanyā kākamācī hayagandhā śatāvarī |
uttarā vāruṇī devadālī citrā punarnavā || 1,10.103 ||
nirguṇḍī sahadevī ca rudantī gajakarṇikā |
bhūtāvarātabhallātakākatuṇḍāmṛtālatāḥ || 1,10.104 ||
palāśaḥ kukkurūṭaśca dhātrīphalarasaḥ payaḥ |
palāśabījakaṃ tailaṃ ghṛtaṃ madhu śivāmbu ca || 1,10.105 ||
bilvamajjā ca tailaṃ ca tailaṃ jyotiṣmatībhavam |
etāni krāmaṇārhāṇi cauṣadhāni bhavanti hi || 1,10.106 ||
ete cāṣṭārdhagaditāḥ krāmaṇārthe prayogataḥ |
rasabhasmaprayoge ca ghuṭikānāṃ rasāyane || 1,10.107 ||
<pañcadaśaghuṭikāphalam>
priye pañcadaśānāṃ ca ghuṭikānāṃ phalaṃ śṛṇu |
abhrabījena racitaghuṭikā rasasaṃyutā || 1,10.108 ||
mukhasthitā dvādaśābdaṃ sarvarogavināśanī |
sa jīvedvatsaraśataṃ pumāṃśca parameśvari || 1,10.109 ||
kāntabījena racitā ghuṭikā rasasaṃyutā |
āsyasthā sarvarogaghnī dvādaśābdaṃ varānane || 1,10.110 ||
dhātudārḍhyaprajananī sahasrāyuṣyadāyinī |
kāntābhrabījaracitā rasayugghuṭikā śubhā || 1,10.111 ||
dvādaśābdaṃ mukhāntasthā jarāmayavināśinī |
ayutāyuṣyadā divyā mahābalavivardhinī || 1,10.112 ||
hemabījayutā sūtaghuṭikā mukhamadhyagā |
ā dvādaśābdaṃ dehasya valīpalitarogahā || 1,10.113 ||
āyuṣyapradā puṇyā nāgāyutabalapradā |
hemābhrabījaghaṭitā ghuṭikā yuktapāradā || 1,10.114 ||
vaktrāsthitārkasaṃkhyābdaṃ daśalakṣābdajīvadā |
divyabuddhiprajananī divyasatvapradāyinī || 1,10.115 ||
kāntakāñcanabījābhyāṃ sasūtā ghuṭikā kṛtā |
dvādaśābdaṃ mukhāntā koṭyāyuṣyavivardhinī || 1,10.116 ||
mahātejaḥprajananī bilanidhyādidarśinī |
kāntābhrahemaghaṭitā ghuṭikā rasasaṃyutā || 1,10.117 ||
mukhasthā dvādaśābdāntāddaśakoṭyabdajīvadā |
hālāhalādisaṃvartakhecaratvapradāyinī || 1,10.118 ||
nirmuktavajrabījena rasayugghuṭikā śubhā |
mukhasthā dvādaśābdāntaṃ sarvalokagatipradā || 1,10.119 ||
yāvadbhūmiḥ sthiratarā tāvadāyuḥpravardhinī |
taruṇaḥ sarvadā kāmaḥ kāntānāṃ suratakṣamaḥ || 1,10.120 ||
vajrābhrabījaracitā ghuṭikā rasagarbhitā |
ghuṭikā rasasaṃkhyābdaṃ mukhasthā sarvasiddhidā || 1,10.121 ||
brahmāyuṣyapradā puṃsāṃ jagatsṛṣṭuṃ kramātprabhuḥ |
pūjyate brahmavaddevairvedavedāṅgapāragaḥ || 1,10.122 ||
sarasvatyā ca sāvitryā sevyate sarvalokagaḥ |
aṣṭābhiḥ siddhibhiryukto hyaṇimādibhirīśvari || 1,10.123 ||
vajrakāñcanabījena sasūtā ghuṭikā kṛtā |
mukhasthā dvādaśābdāntaṃ viṣṇvāyuṣyapradā nṛṇām || 1,10.124 ||
sa ca viṣṇutvamāpnoti viṣṇuvatpālituṃ kṣamaḥ |
sevyate sanakādyaiśca śriyā yukto mahābalaḥ || 1,10.125 ||
svecchāgatirmahendrādyairnirjaraiḥ sevyate sadā |
hemābhravajrabījena racitā ghuṭikā priye || 1,10.126 ||
sapāradā mukhāntaḥsthā dvādaśābdaṃ varānane |
rudrāyuṣpradā nḥṇāṃ rudratvaṃ sā dadāti hi || 1,10.127 ||
saṃhartā rudravallokaṃ viṣṇvindrādyaiśca sevyate |
aṇimādyaiśca sahitaḥ sarvajñaḥ sarvalokagaḥ || 1,10.128 ||
sevyate pramathaśreṣṭhairdivyaśaktyā samanvitaḥ |
yogīndrairdhyāyate dhīro mahātejā mahābalaḥ || 1,10.129 ||
kāntakāñcanavajrāṇāṃ bījaiḥ sūtayutā kṛtā |
ghuṭikā bhānusaṃkhyābdaṃ mukhasthā siddhidā nṛṇām || 1,10.130 ||
īśvarāyuṣyamāpnoti khecaratvaṃ ca mānavaḥ |
tirodhatte svayaṃ lokādbrahmendrādyabhivanditaḥ || 1,10.131 ||
koṭisūryapratīkāśo mahāmārutasattvavān |
mahākalpāntakāle'pi vināśaṃ na vrajeddhruvam || 1,10.132 ||
yogakrīḍānuṣaktātmā dhyāyate yogavittamaiḥ |
kāntābhrahemavajrāṇāṃ kṛtā bījai rasātmikā || 1,10.133 ||
ghuṭikā ravisaṃkhyābdavaktrasthā yasya bhairavi |
sadāśivāyuḥ sa bhavetsarvānugrāhakaḥ prabhuḥ || 1,10.134 ||
sadāśivatvamāpnoti devānāmadhipastathā |
vahnau vahnirjale vāri mārute mārutātmakaḥ || 1,10.135 ||
pṛthivyāṃ pṛthivīrūpaḥ śūnye śūnyātmako bhavet |
tasyākṣiṇīnduvahnyarkā brahmendrādyāśca sevakāḥ || 1,10.136 ||
gāyakā nāradādyāśca nartakyaścāpsaro'ṅganāḥ |
tadājñayaiva brahmendrāḥ sṛṣṭisthitivināśakāḥ || 1,10.137 ||
saccidānandakaḥ śaktaḥ sarvagaḥ sarvavicchivaḥ |
parāśaktiyutaḥ puṇyo nirmāyo niṣkalaḥ param || 1,10.138 ||
evaṃ guṇāḥ prakathitā ghuṭikānāṃ mayā priye |
śastrastambhakaraścāsāṃ sarvāsāmapi vidyate || 1,10.139 ||
rasāyanasya sarvasya siddhido'yaṃ maheśvari |
<ghuṭikāsiddhau mantraprayogaḥ>
vakṣyate mantrarājo'yaṃ sarvasiddhipradāyakaḥ || 1,10.140 ||
aiṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ klīṃ sauḥ amṛteśvarabhairava amṛtaṃ kuru amṛteśvarabhairavāya hum |
sauḥ klīṃ śrīṃ hrīmaiṃ huṃ phaṭsvāhā |
sauḥ klīṃ śrīṃ hrīmaim |
pumānanena mantreṇa śīghraṃ siddhimavāpnuyāt || 1,10.141 ||


āk, 1, 11
<jīvata eva divyadehasiddhiḥ; bhūtakālāntakarasaḥ>
śrībhairavī |
devadeva mahādeva kathitāni tvayādhunā |
rasāyanāni divyāni siddhidāni maheśvara || 1,11.1 ||
cirakālena dehānāṃ kalpānāṃ siddhidāni hi |
sadyaḥ siddhiryathā deva jāyate parameśvara || 1,11.2 ||
tadbrūhi nyūnābhyadhikahīnakṣīṇāṅgināmapi |
atha gadgadamūkānāṃ kubjānāmatha kuṣṭhinām || 1,11.3 ||
andhapaṅgvabalānāṃ ca jarājarjaritātmanām |
sadā rogārtaṣaṇḍānāṃ kṛśānāṃ bhrāntacetasām || 1,11.4 ||
bhūtapretapiśācāpasmāronmattayujāmapi |
pramādājjīvaśeṣāṇāṃ dehasiddhipradaṃ nṛṇām || 1,11.5 ||
śrībhairavaḥ |
śrutvā tadbhairavīvākyaṃ sādhu pṛṣṭaṃ tvayā priye |
tathāvidhāṃ pravakṣyāmi dehasiddhiṃ varānane || 1,11.6 ||
pañcabhūtātmikāḥ pañca kartavyā ghuṭikāḥ priye |
jīvātmikā bhavetṣaṣṭhī ghuṭikā piṇḍakoṣṭayuk || 1,11.7 ||
kāle tu yā kanyā kuryātsaṃbhogamāyatam |
tadvarāṅgasthitaṃ raktaṃ śuklaṃ syādvyomasatvakam || 1,11.8 ||
tasyāḥ sadyaḥ prasūtasya viṣṭhā putrasya mārutam |
tattvaṃ syātkevalaṃ tasyā rajastejātmasattvakam || 1,11.9 ||
tadapatyasya rudhiraṃ jalatattvaṃ prakīrtitam |
tatsūtasya vapuḥ sarvaṃ pārthivaṃ tattvamucyate || 1,11.10 ||
sūtasevakaśuklaṃ ca jīvatattvaṃ bhavetpriye |
koṭivedhakaraṃ sūtaṃ karṣaṃ karṣaṃ niyojayet || 1,11.11 ||
ekaikatattvamadhye tu prasekaṃ tāni mardayet |
teṣāṃ ca golakānkṛtvā ṣaḍrasaṃ sthāpayetpṛthak || 1,11.12 ||
nṛmānamunnataṃ kāyamāyāmaṃ tu tadardhakam |
kaṭāhaṃ tāmraghaṭitaṃ piṇḍasthaulyaṃ ṣaḍaṅgulam || 1,11.13 ||
caturmukhamayaṃ koṣṭhaṃ tasyopari kaṭāhakam |
dhārayenniścalaṃ samyaktadantaḥ pūrayetpriye || 1,11.14 ||
goghṛtaṃ ca mahātailaṃ samabhāgamidaṃ dvayam |
arcayeddaśadikpālān yoginīśca kumārikām || 1,11.15 ||
śrīguruṃ siddhacakraṃ ca bhairavaṃ bhairavīṃ tathā |
gaṇādhipaṃ kṣetrapālaṃ nijeṣṭadaivataṃ tathā || 1,11.16 ||
navagrahāgniviprāṃśca daivajñān bhiṣaguttamān |
tathāntarāyakartḥṃśca bhūtapretapiśācakān || 1,11.17 ||
yakṣarākṣasagandharvānmantrajñānsvajanānapi |
tarpayenmadyamāṃsaiśca vastrabhūṣaṇakāñcanaiḥ || 1,11.18 ||
tattatpriyakarairdivyairbaliṃ dikṣu vinikṣipet |
caturbhirvaṅkanālaiśca dhamayetkhadirāgninā || 1,11.19 ||
phenahīnamadhūmaṃ ca saṃtaptaṃ ca yadā bhavet |
śrīguruṃ nijadevaṃ ca candrasūryāgnitārakāḥ || 1,11.20 ||
bhuvanāni namaskṛtya kaṭāhe nikṣipettanum |
jñātvā samyagdrutaṃ dehaṃ pārthivākhyaṃ rasaṃ kṣipet || 1,11.21 ||
dhamedgāḍhaṃ prayatnena tatkalkaṃ ca yadā bhavet |
tato'ptattvākhyarasakaṃ nikṣipedraktatāṃ nayet || 1,11.22 ||
tejastattvarasaṃ paścātkṣipettanmāṃsatāṃ vrajet |
nikṣipedvāyutattvākhyaṃ rasaṃ tatra vinikṣipet || 1,11.23 ||
śubhravarṇatvamāpnoti tataścākāśatattvakam |
nikṣipejjīvatattvākhyaṃ rasatattvaṃ ca pārvati || 1,11.24 ||
rasaṃ kṣipettvandhritasya svarṇasya tatsamaṃ bhavet |
sajīvo jāyate siddho huṃkāratrayamuccaret || 1,11.25 ||
yathodito bhānubimbo mahābuddhiparākramaḥ |
mahāvapurmahātejā nāgāyutamahābalaḥ || 1,11.26 ||
manmathopamarūpāḍhyo vācā vāgīśvarīsamaḥ |
buddhyā jīvasamaḥ śrīmānviṣṇuvaddhanadopamaḥ || 1,11.27 ||
tyāge roṣe ca kālāgnirgāmbhīryeṇa mahodadhiḥ |
sraṣṭā hartā ca goptā ca sarvānugrāhakaḥ prabhuḥ || 1,11.28 ||
divyadṛṣṭirvajradehaḥ sa sākṣādbhairavastvayam |
ardhayojanavistīrṇahemakiṅkiṇimaṇḍitam || 1,11.29 ||
caladbhramarasaśobhaṃ nānāmāṇikyamaṇḍitam |
hemamālāpariṣvaktaṃ ghaṇṭānādamanoharam || 1,11.30 ||
divyadīptamahānādiśaṅkhakāhalasaṃkulam |
vīṇāveṇumṛdaṅgādyairvāditrairmurajaiḥ samam || 1,11.31 ||
tālairbahuvidhaiścānyairdivyaghoṣaiḥ samākulam |
gāyatkinnaragandharvaistathā kiṃpuruṣairyutam || 1,11.32 ||
lasanmāṇikyakeyūrahārakaṅkaṇamudrakāḥ |
kācanūpurasaṃyuktā divyābharaṇabhūṣitāḥ || 1,11.33 ||
divyamālāpariṣkārā divyagandhānulepanāḥ |
divyāmbarāścārurūpā mattamanmathavihvalāḥ || 1,11.34 ||
divyāṅganāstadā cainaṃ samāśritya bruvanti ca |
kiṃ karma siddhasaṃkhedāstvādeśo deva dīyatām || 1,11.35 ||
upāsate siddhakanyāḥ paraḥ śatasahasrakam |
yatra yāsyati tatraiva cānuyāmo vayaṃ vṛtāḥ || 1,11.36 ||
divyālayāṃśca vividhānhemamāṇikyamaṇḍitān |
divyāni snānapānāni svīkurvāṇo muhurmuhuḥ || 1,11.37 ||
vajryādisarvalokeṣu svecchayā viharatyasau |
pūjyate devasiddhaughaiḥ yathāyaṃ bhairavastathā || 1,11.38 ||
bhuñjānaḥ sarvabhogāṃśca kṣutpipāsāvivarjitaḥ |
yoginīśatasāhasraṃ bhoktā saṃcintayan sukham || 1,11.39 ||
mahākalpāntakāle'pi prakṣīṇe'sminvarānane |
līyate parame vyomni līyante yatra devatāḥ || 1,11.40 ||
bhūtakālāntako nāma rasasyāsya prabhāvataḥ |
abhedyo'yamakhaṇḍyaśca tvadāhyo bhavati priye || 1,11.41 ||
rasāyanasya sarvasya siddhido'yaṃ maheśvari |
vakṣyate mantrarājo'yaṃ rasasiddhipradāyakaḥ || 1,11.42 ||
aiṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ klīṃ sauḥ śrībhairava || 1,11.43 ||


āk, 1, 12
<śrīśaile siddhilābhaḥ>
śrībhairavī |
śrīśaile vividhā siddhiḥ sadyaḥ pratyayakāriṇī |
sulabhā śrūyate deva tāṃ brūhi vividhāṃ prabho || 1,12.1 ||
śrībhairavaḥ |
vakṣyāmi śṛṇu tatsarvaṃ sadyaḥ siddhikaraṃ priye |
kailāsānmandarānmerorvindhyādreśca himālayāt || 1,12.2 ||
mahendrānmalayādreśca sahyādṛśyagirerapi |
śreṣṭhaḥ śrīparvato divyaḥ siddhiyogīndrasevitaḥ || 1,12.3 ||
tatra tīrthāni sarvāṇi sarāṃsi saritaḥ priye |
siddhipradāni liṅgāni latāpāṣāṇapādapāḥ || 1,12.4 ||
mṛttikākandatoyāni patrapuṣpaphalāni ca |
evamādīni vidyante sarvasiddhikarāṇi ca || 1,12.5 ||
śrīparvato'hamīśāni tvahaṃ sākṣātsa parvataḥ |
sthāvaraṃ māmakaṃ rūpaṃ viddhi taṃ surasevitam || 1,12.6 ||
asminyadasti nānyatra yadanyatra sthitaṃ ca tat |
divyaliṅgasparśanīyaṃ jyotirliṅgamanāmayam || 1,12.7 ||
śrīmallikārjunamiti prakhyātaṃ parameśvari |
vāmapārśve'sya liṅgasya ghaṇṭāsiddheśvaraḥ sthitaḥ || 1,12.8 ||
ghaṇṭā vilambate dvāre tīrthakuṇḍaṃ ca vidyate |
upoṣitaistribhiḥ kāryaṃ jāgarūkairatandritaiḥ || 1,12.9 ||
niśi kṛṣṇacaturdaśyāmajasraṃ snāpayecchivam |
ekaḥ samānayettoyaṃ kuṇḍasthamaparaḥ priye || 1,12.10 ||
ghaṇṭāṃ ninādayedanyaścaturyāmāvadhi priye |
ghaṇṭāsiddheśvarastuṣṭo dadyāttebhyo'pi khe gatim || 1,12.11 ||
ghaṇṭāsiddheśvarasyāsya dakṣiṇe nikhanetpriye |
jānudaghnaṃ tu tatraiva dṛśyate rocanaprabhā || 1,12.12 ||
mṛttikā tāṃ samāhṛtya karṣamātraṃ pibetpriye |
kṣīrayuktāṃ ca saptāhaṃ sa sākṣādamaro bhavet || 1,12.13 ||
śrīgirīśasya purato gajākārā mahāśilā |
sravatyeva divārātraṃ divyagandhaṃ suguggulum || 1,12.14 ||
taṃ gṛhṇīyātpalāśasya darvyālābukapātrake |
prakṣipedgandhakayutaṃ bhakṣayetkarṣamātrakam || 1,12.15 ||
pratyahaṃ māsaparyantaṃ tataḥ siddhimavāpnuyāt |
sadānando yuvā dhīro jīvedācandratārakam || 1,12.16 ||
vidrute mlecchavadane gugguluṃ taṃ vinikṣipet |
koṭimaṃśaṃ tatastāmraṃ divyaṃ bhavati kāñcanam || 1,12.17 ||
<candrodaka bei ṃllikārjuna>
mallikārjunadevasya candravāpyasti paścime |
vaiśākhapūrṇimāyāṃ tu sādhayetsādhakottamaḥ || 1,12.18 ||
nirbhayo nirvikalpaśca vasaṃstoyasamīpataḥ |
japenmṛtyuñjayaṃ mantraṃ rātrau vāsovivarjitaḥ || 1,12.19 ||
niśīthe candrasalilaṃ candraspṛṣṭaṃ bhavedyadā |
tattīrthacchidradeśe ca svāñjaliṃ prakṣipedataḥ || 1,12.20 ||
spṛṣṭvā candro yadā gacchettadā tattoyamāharet |
pibecca sahasā dhīro jīvedācandratārakam || 1,12.21 ||
vajrakāyaḥ saumyarūpaḥ śāntātmā sa bhavennaraḥ |
<ṭripurāntaka>
pūrvadvāre śrīgirestu vidyate tripurāntakaḥ || 1,12.22 ||
devasya nikaṭe deśe cottare tintriṇītaruḥ |
dṛśyate tatra mūle tu svayaṃ śrībhairavaḥ prabhuḥ || 1,12.23 ||
nṛmātrāṃ nikhanedbhūmiṃ tadagre dṛśyate tadā |
taptakuṇḍaṃ nīlajalaṃ divyasiddhipradāyakam || 1,12.24 ||
tattintriṇīkapatrāṇi vastre baddhvā vinikṣipet |
kuṇḍe tasmiṃstadā tāni sthūlamīnā bhavanti ca || 1,12.25 ||
gṛhītvā tintriṇīkāṣṭhaiḥ pacedevaṃ kramātsudhīḥ |
teṣāṃ śiraḥkaṇṭakāni khāni ca vivarjayet || 1,12.26 ||
bhakṣayeccheṣamakhilaṃ sādhakaḥ siddhikāṅkṣayā |
kṣaṇamātraṃ bhavenmūrcchā tena paścādvibudhyate || 1,12.27 ||
vasudhāyāṃ bilaṃ paśyejjīveddivyāyutābdakam |
<maṇipalli>
paścime tripurāntasya gavyūtidvayamātrake || 1,12.28 ||
maṇipalliriti grāmastasya paścimabhāgataḥ |
vidyate kaścana giristatpaścādekavāṭakam || 1,12.29 ||
dṛśyate praviśettatra pūrvāsyaśca tato vrajet |
dakṣiṇābhimukhaḥ paścāddaśacāpāntamātrakam || 1,12.30 ||
tatra cāmraphalākārān pāṣāṇāñjvalanaprabhān |
gṛhītvā bandhayedvastre tadvastraṃ raktatāṃ vrajet || 1,12.31 ||
pāṣāṇayuktaṃ tadvastraṃ kṣīramadhye vinikṣipet |
tataḥ kṣīraṃ raktavarṇaṃ syātpibetsādhakottamaḥ || 1,12.32 ||
saptāhājjāyate siddhirvajrakāyo mahābalaḥ |
jīvedācandratāraṃ ca ā kalpamavilpakam || 1,12.33 ||
<kokilābila>
tripurāntasyodagbhāge kokilābilamuttamam |
jagatprakāśaṃ tatrāste kṛtvā dehaviśodhanam || 1,12.34 ||
sādhako vamanādyaiśca tadbilaṃ praviśetsudhīḥ |
daśacāpāvadhistatra pāṣāṇāḥ kokilopamāḥ || 1,12.35 ||
santi gṛhītvā tatpṛṣṭhe tilāḥ kṣiptāḥ sphuṭanti ca |
ityetatpratyayaṃ dṛṣṭvā tāśca ghṛṣya parasparam || 1,12.36 ||
dugdhāntaḥ prakṣipettāṃśca tatkṣīraṃ kṛṣṇatāṃ vrajet |
tatkṣīraṃ māsamātraṃ ca pibeddivyatanurbhavet || 1,12.37 ||
brahmaṇastridinaṃ jīvedvalīpalitavarjitaḥ |
vege samīrasadṛśaśchidraṃ paśyati bhūtale || 1,12.38 ||
tripurāntasya pūrvasyāṃ diśi yojanamātrake |
asti svargapurīnātho devastasyāgrataḥ khanet || 1,12.39 ||
jānumātraṃ ca vasudhāṃ tatra sarpaphaṇopamāḥ |
sparśasaṃjñāstu pāṣāṇā nirgacchanti varānane || 1,12.40 ||
tripurāntakadevasya paścime'rdhārdhayojane |
asti divyabiladvāraṃ tatra cāpatrayāntaram || 1,12.41 ||
vrajetkharjūravṛkṣo'sti kṛṣṇavarṇaḥ phalānvitaḥ |
tatphalānāṃ rasaṃ pītvā tena mūrcchā bhavetkṣaṇam || 1,12.42 ||
prabuddho'sau bhavetsiddho jīvedā candrabhāskaram |
śrīgirerdakṣiṇadvāre vajreśvarasureśvarau || 1,12.43 ||
tiṣṭhato nikhanedbhūmiṃ tatra śrīphalasannibhāḥ |
pāṣāṇāḥ sparśabhedāḥ syuḥ saṃgrāhyāste sureśvari || 1,12.44 ||
tatra rāmeśvarākhyo'sti nikhanettasya sannidhau |
rudrākṣākārapāṣāṇāḥ sparśabhedā bhavanti te || 1,12.45 ||
<āvartaka>
jyotiḥsiddhavaṭasthāne dakṣiṇe caikapādapaḥ |
tiṣṭhatyāvartako nāma tadāsanne tu paścime || 1,12.46 ||
parvato vidyate tatra khanettālaphalopamāḥ |
pāṣāṇāstāndhamedgāḍhaṃ tatsarvaṃ kāñcanaṃ bhavet || 1,12.47 ||
tasyaiva dakṣiṇe dvāre vidyate kuṇḍaleśvaraḥ |
tannikṛṣṭe jānumātraṃ khanedbhūmiṃ samuddharet || 1,12.48 ||
raktābhāḥ śrīphalākārāḥ pāṣāṇāḥ sparśavedhakāḥ |
puruṣeśvaradevasya samīpe kuṇḍamasti hi || 1,12.49 ||
guñjāriḍḍhau ca vidyete vṛkṣau tatpattramaśnīyāt |
saptāhājjāyate siddho jarāmaraṇavarjitaḥ || 1,12.50 ||
<hastiśilā>
tatra hastiśilā dakṣe khaneddhastapramāṇataḥ |
tatra jambūphalākārā grāhyāste sparśasaṃjñakāḥ || 1,12.51 ||
khyātā hastiśironāmnā khyātā hastiśileti sā |
<chāyāchattra>
ekayojanamātre tu tasyā dakṣiṇabhāgataḥ || 1,12.52 ||
śivarūpaṃ śivaproktaṃ chāyāchattraṃ tu vidyate |
vartulaṃ śatahastaṃ tu chidraṃ tasyāpyadho vrajet || 1,12.53 ||
na kaiściddṛśyate so'pi siddho bhavati tatkṣaṇāt |
yadṛcchayā rudratulyaḥ krīḍatyeva jagattraye || 1,12.54 ||
siddhyaṣṭakaṃ sādhayedvā sarvasiddhipradāyakaḥ |
kāntābhrasatvakanakasūtāḥ kramaguṇottarāḥ || 1,12.55 ||
amlena mardayedgāḍhaṃ golaṃ kṛtvā tu veṣṭayet |
tadgolaṃ dīrghavaṃśāgre baddhvā śrīkālikāmanum || 1,12.56 ||
japenniveśayettatra chāyāchattreṇa tena ca |
vaṃśāgrabaddhagolāntarjāyate ghuṭikā śubhā || 1,12.57 ||
vaktrāntardhārayettāṃ tu tatkṣaṇātkhecaro bhavet |
śilāṃ tālaṃ vastrabaddhaṃ kṛtvā vaṃśāgraveṣṭitam || 1,12.58 ||
chāyāchattre nivasyaitattābhyāṃ netre samañjayet |
nidhiṃ paśyati bhūmisthaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 1,12.59 ||
vaṃśāgrabaddhakhaḍgaṃ ca chāyāchattre vinikṣipet |
mantrayetkālikāmantraṃ taṃ khaḍgaṃ dhārayetkare || 1,12.60 ||
trailokyavijayī dhīro bhavedvīro jagattraye |
vaṃśāgre rocanaṃ baddhvā chāyāchattre niveśayet || 1,12.61 ||
lalāṭe tilakaṃ tena kṛtvā lokaṃ vaśaṃ nayet |
srotoñjanāñjane tadvacchāyācchatre niveśayet || 1,12.62 ||
tenāñjanenāñjito'sau devairapi na dṛśyate |
pāduke pūrvavadbaddhvā chāyācchatre nidhāpayet || 1,12.63 ||
pādābhyāṃ pāduke dhṛtvā yatra yatrābhivāñchati |
prayātuṃ tatra tatraiva nayatastaṃ ca pāduke || 1,12.64 ||
pūrvavadraktavastraṃ ca chāyācchatre niveśayet |
āveṣṭayettaṃ ca paṭamadṛśyo bhavati kṣaṇāt || 1,12.65 ||
samujjhite paṭe paścāddṛśyate'sau na saṃśayaḥ |
kāntaṃ vyoma hema sūtamekīkṛtya vimardayet || 1,12.66 ||
golībhūtaṃ tu vastreṇa vaṃśāgre bandhayetsudhīḥ |
chāyācchatre sthāpayettatkālīmantreṇa mantrayet || 1,12.67 ||
sparśavedhī bhavedetatsatyaṃ satyaṃ varānane |
śrīśailapaścimadvāre devo brahmeśvaraḥ sthitaḥ || 1,12.68 ||
durgā devī ca tatrasthā sopānaṃ navamaṃ ca yat |
tuṅgabhadrāyāṃ ca nadyāṃ tato brahmeśvarasya ca || 1,12.69 ||
dvāradeśe mudgavarṇaṃ catvāraḥ sparśakā amī |
ciñcāvanaṃ ca nairṛtye sthitaṃ brahmeśvarasya hi || 1,12.70 ||
kuṇḍaṃ ca vidyate tatra ciñcādhaścaṇḍikā sthitā |
ekapādena satataṃ tacciñcāphalamāharet || 1,12.71 ||
vastreṇa bandhayetkuṇḍe kṣiptvā snānaṃ samācaret |
snānānte tāni gṛhṇīyāttāvanmatsyā bhavanti hi || 1,12.72 ||
tadindhanaiḥ pacettāśca hyasthipucchaśirastyajet |
dadyāddevāyaikamaṃśamatithīnāṃ dvitīyakam || 1,12.73 ||
tṛtīyāṃśaṃ svayaṃ bhakṣenmūrchito bhavati kṣaṇāt |
divyo bhavati siddho'yaṃ bilaṃ paśyati bhūtale || 1,12.74 ||
jarāmaraṇanirmukto hyabadhyo nirjarairapi |
alampurottare grāmo vidyate bhīmapādukaḥ || 1,12.75 ||
pārśve tu tasya grāmasya hastamātraṃ dharāṃ khanet |
pāṣāṇā hi phaṇākārāḥ sparśasaṃjñā bhavanti te || 1,12.76 ||
yogīśvarīti devyasti tatrālaṃpuradevatā |
tasyāgrato ramyaguhā tasyā madhye khanedbhuvam || 1,12.77 ||
pāṣāṇā bhedasaṃkāśā grāhyā mārjālaviṣṭhayā |
saṃmiśrya nikṣipedvaṅge drute tattāratāṃ vrajet || 1,12.78 ||
madhvājyābhyāṃ pibettāṃśca pāṣāṇānsādhakottamaḥ |
divyo bhavati siddho'yaṃ valīpalitamṛtyujit || 1,12.79 ||
śrīparvatottaradvāre devo nāmnā maheśvaraḥ |
tiṣṭhati bhramarāmraśca tatra tatphalamāharet || 1,12.80 ||
sphoṭayecca tadantasthā niryānti bhramarāstathā |
sajīvā atha tānsarvānbhramarāṃstānvivarjayet || 1,12.81 ||
tatphalāni pacetkṣāre yāvacchakyaṃ payaḥ pibet |
kṣaṇaṃ mūrcchā bhavettena mūrcchānte ca payaḥ pibet || 1,12.82 ||
evaṃ kuryātprayatnena caikaviṃśativāsaram |
tena vajraśarīraḥ syādvalīpalitavarjitaḥ || 1,12.83 ||
vedavedāṅgatattvajño jīvedādityatārakam |
tarasā vāyunā tulyastatkṣaṇādbhavati priye || 1,12.84 ||
tadāmrasya phalenaiva tyaktabhramarakena ca |
vaṅgasya palasāhasraṃ drāvitaṃ rajataṃ bhavet || 1,12.85 ||
randhramāpādayedāmraphale bhṛṅgaṃ vivarjayet |
tanmadhye nikṣipetsūtaṃ karṣaṃ kṛṣṇābhrasatvakam || 1,12.86 ||
nirudhya vaktraṃ samṛdā gomayena ca lepayet |
chāyāyāṃ śoṣayetkāṣṭhaistadīyaiḥ praharaṃ pacet || 1,12.87 ||
svabhāvaśītalaṃ kṛtvā vaktrasthāṃ kārayetsudhīḥ |
khecaratvamavāpnoti valīpalitavarjitaḥ || 1,12.88 ||
jīveccandrārkaparyantaṃ vāgmitvaṃ brahmaṇā samam |
sūtaṃ kṛṣṇābhrasatvaṃ cāmraphale pūrvavatkṣipet || 1,12.89 ||
pralipya gośakṛnmṛdbhyāṃ puṭedāraṇyakopalaiḥ |
svaśītaṃ pāradaṃ grāhyaṃ madhusarpirbhiryutaṃ lihet || 1,12.90 ||
guñjāmātraṃ ca māsāntaṃ bālo bhavati mānavaḥ |
navanāgopamaḥ satve jīvedbrahmaikavāsaram || 1,12.91 ||
uttare śrīgirīśasya vidyate śuklaparvate |
pañcopacāraiḥ sampūjya triṣu lokeṣu viśrutaḥ || 1,12.92 ||
oṃ huṃ phaṭkāramantreṇa nirvikalpena sādhakaḥ |
paśyetpaścimadigbhāgamantarikṣe vimānakam || 1,12.93 ||
divyaṃ hyanekaruciraṃ sadyaḥ pratyayakārakam |
tataḥ paścimadigbhāge vrajettīrtvā mahānadīm || 1,12.94 ||
dṛśyate ca guhā tatra praviśetpaścimānanaḥ |
triyojanaṃ vrajettatra vibhīreko'vikalpakaḥ || 1,12.95 ||
kadalīkānanaṃ tatra dṛśyate pañcayojanam |
acchodapūrṇā sarasī tanmadhye bhadrapīṭhakam || 1,12.96 ||
tatpīṭhaṃ tu mahaddivyaṃ paśyetsphaṭikasannibham |
uccārayenmantramimaṃ daṇḍavatpraṇatiṃ bhajet || 1,12.97 ||
oṃ hrīṃ vidyāvāgīśvarādhipataye hrīmoṃ svāhā |
tatra snātvā japenmantraṃ lakṣamekaṃ varānane |
kandamūlāśano vā yatheṣṭaṃ sādhakaḥ priye || 1,12.98 ||
siṃhāsanāntare devaṃ paśyetsphaṭikasannibham |
caturbhujaṃ triṇetraṃ ca viśadendukalādharam || 1,12.99 ||
nīlagrīvaṃ nāgabhūṣaṃ paraśvathamṛgāyudham |
varābhayaṃ vareṇyaṃ taṃ viśiṣṭavibudhārcitam || 1,12.100 ||
namaskuryācca sāṣṭāṅgaṃ mantraiḥ stotrairmuhurmuhuḥ |
tataḥ prasannaḥ sa śivo varaṃ datte yathepsitam || 1,12.101 ||
śatamāyatanaṃ tatra kūpaṃ navaśataṃ yathā |
ārāmāścāpi tāvanti nandanāni vanāni ca || 1,12.102 ||
vāpyo navaśataṃ santi vivaraṃ caiva tatsamam |
kalpavṛkṣāśca tāvanti ete sarve'pi siddhidāḥ || 1,12.103 ||
tatrāste mohalī nāmnā prathitā yakṣiṇīṣṭadā |
brūte sā dehi me bhuktiṃ siddhiṃ tvaṃ yadi vāñchasi || 1,12.104 ||
devi dāsyāmyahaṃ bhuktiṃ vaktavyamiha cāmunā |
kandamūlaṃ phalaṃ tasyai pāyasaṃ vā samarpayet || 1,12.105 ||
sā yakṣiṇī punarvakti yāvadbhuñje sutaṃ mama |
dhārayervakṣasā tāvadvaraṃ dāsyāmi te mahat || 1,12.106 ||
visṛjestvaṃ yadi tadā bhavantaṃ hanmi nirdayā |
evaṃ gatabhayaścīraḥ kuryāccetsiddhibhāgbhavet || 1,12.107 ||
vrajedudīcīdigbhāge tatsaro yojanārdhake |
tatrāste candanaṃ divyaṃ puṣpapūrṇaṃ maheśvari || 1,12.108 ||
tatpuṣpāghrāṇamātreṇa kṣutpipāsā na bādhate |
bhakṣayedathavā tasya phalamekaṃ yathocitam || 1,12.109 ||
vajrakāyo bhavettasya bhakṣaṇādeva mānavaḥ |
sarodakṣiṇadigbhāge gacchedyojanapādakam || 1,12.110 ||
nandanaṃ dṛśyate tatra dāḍimīvṛkṣasaṃkulam |
tatphalaṃ bhakṣayedyastu jīvedyugasahasrakam || 1,12.111 ||
tasyaiva sarasaḥ pūrvabhāge krośārdhamātrakam |
gaccheddhātrīphalairyuktaṃ vidyate nandanaṃ vanam || 1,12.112 ||
aśnīyāttatphalaṃ dhīro jīvetsaṃvartakatrayam |
sarasastasya bhāge ca paścime yojanaṃ vrajet || 1,12.113 ||
tatra śrīphalasampūrṇaṃ nandanaṃ vidyate vanam |
adyate tatphalaṃ yena jīvatyācandratārakam || 1,12.114 ||
tatraiva nandanavane liṅgaṃ syānnīlavarṇakam |
tasyodagdvāramārgeṇa viśennāgo mahābalaḥ || 1,12.115 ||
tatra saptaphaṇopetastūgrabhītikaro mahān |
evaṃvidhaṃ mahānāgaṃ huṃhuṃkāraṃ vadanmuhuḥ || 1,12.116 ||
namaskuryātprayatnena sādhakaḥ siddhikāṅkṣayā |
vadatyevaṃ mahānāgastvadṛśyatvaṃ dadāmi te || 1,12.117 ||
yāhi svaṃ paścimadvāraṃ divyā kanyāsti tatra vai |
samuccaranmahāmantraṃ vrajettatraiva sādhakaḥ || 1,12.118 ||
dadāti hāraṃ sā kanyā praveśaṃ na dadāti ca |
hāraṃ gale dhārayettaṃ tena sārasvataṃ bhavet || 1,12.119 ||
tasmāddakṣiṇadigdvāre vrajedbhītikaraṃ param |
tatra paśyenmuktakeśaṃ jihmanetraṃ digambaram || 1,12.120 ||
gadāhastaṃ nīlavarṇaṃ dṛṣṭvā śrīkṣetrapālakam |
vandeta mantramuccārya stotrairvighnanivāraṇam || 1,12.121 ||
hāhāhehehuṃhuṃkāraṃ phaṭhuṃ svāhāntameva ca |
kṣetrapālo'pyanenaiva mantreṇāśu prasīdati || 1,12.122 ||
svāgatvaṃ vitaratyeṣa praveśaṃ na dadāti hi |
atha vrajetpūrvadiśo dvāraṃ tatra gaṇādhipam || 1,12.123 ||
sthitaṃ prapūjayettatra praviśetsādhakottamaḥ |
dṛśyate divyaliṅgaṃ ca cāpastatra manoharaḥ || 1,12.124 ||
oṃ huṅkāreṇa mantreṇa pūjayecca tamīśvaram |
upavāsena tatraiva divārātraṃ japetsudhīḥ || 1,12.125 ||
pratyakṣo bhavatīśāno dadāti hi varaṃ param |
ityevamādayaḥ santi siddhayaḥ kadalīvane || 1,12.126 ||
tatraiva sarasaḥ pūrvabhāge yojanamātrake |
akṣarairlikhitaṃ dvāri tatra padmāvatībilam || 1,12.127 ||
vamanādyairviśuddhātmā praviśettatra sādhakaḥ |
tatra cāpāntaraśataṃ gacchettatra mṛdaṅgakam || 1,12.128 ||
ālokya vādayettaṃ ca taddhvaniśravaṇāttadā |
padmāvatī svayaṃ yāti hyamṛtaṃ ca dadāti ca || 1,12.129 ||
tasya pānena siddho'yamamaratvaṃ labheta ca |
tataḥ sainaṃ samāgamya prārthayettvaṃ mamāntikam || 1,12.130 ||
samāgacchālaye divye kanyāpañcaśatākule |
sthitvā mama patirbhūyā brahmāyuṣyāvadhi prabho || 1,12.131 ||
tadante śāśvataṃ sthānaṃ gamiṣyasi na saṃśayaḥ |
tasya pūrvataṭākasya cāgneyyāṃ diśi vidyate || 1,12.132 ||
kadambeśvaradevo'pi vṛkṣaḥ kādambako'grataḥ |
tatra patrāṇi cādāya kaṭutailena lepayet || 1,12.133 ||
tadbījasaṃbhavaistailairlimpedvātha pacetsudhīḥ |
tatkāṣṭhaistāni matsyāḥ syustāmrapātre vinikṣipet || 1,12.134 ||
varjyamasthi śiraḥ pucchaṃ kṣaudrasarpiryutaṃ bhajet |
tatsevayā bhavetsiddho rudratulyo mahābalaḥ || 1,12.135 ||
tadvṛkṣaśca nadītīre kuṇḍaleśasya sannidhau |
vidyate pūrvavadyuktyā siddhirbhavati nānyathā || 1,12.136 ||
astyuttare puṣpagiriḥ kapoteśaśca vidyate |
triḥ pradakṣiṇamātanyāttasya mūrdhānamāvrajet || 1,12.137 ||
khecaratvaṃ bhavettasya sādhakasya na saṃśayaḥ |
tasya pūrvottare bhāge chedikīdvārakaṃ sthitam || 1,12.138 ||
udaṅmukhaṃ viśettatra tricāpāntaramādarāt |
mūṣikākārapāṣāṇaṃ takre piṣṭvā pibennaraḥ || 1,12.139 ||
kṣipraṃ mūrcchā bhavettasya jīvedbrahmadinatrayam |
vedhayetsarvalohāni sparśamātrānna saṃśayaḥ || 1,12.140 ||
upoṣya ca divā naktaṃ devāgre siddhimāpnuyāt |
vidyate devatāyugmaṃ kapoteśvaradakṣiṇe || 1,12.141 ||
ā nābhimātraṃ nikhaneddevatādvayamadhyataḥ |
gorocanopamāstatra pāṣāṇāḥ santi tānharet || 1,12.142 ||
piṣṭvā kṣaudraghṛtābhyāṃ ca pibedyaḥ so'maro bhavet |
kapoteśasya vāyavye nikhaneddhastamātrakam || 1,12.143 ||
tatra pārāvatākārapāṣāṇāḥ sparśasaṃjñakāḥ |
devatārādhanaṃ kṛtvā teṣāmekaṃ samāharet || 1,12.144 ||
aiśānyāṃ śrīgirīśasya hyastyeva bhṛgupātanam |
tatsamīpe divyakuṇḍaṃ mṛdaṃ tasmātsamāharet || 1,12.145 ||
gavyapañcakayuktāṃ tāṃ dhametkhadiravahninā |
tasmāllohaṃ patennīlaṃ madhvājyābhyāṃ pratāpitam || 1,12.146 ||
tatsecayetsaptadhaute tadgolaṃ nikṣipenmukhe |
viṣṇutulyo bhavetsiddhaḥ sarvajñaḥ sarvagaḥ sukhī || 1,12.147 ||
bhṛgupātanaśailāgrātkrośe dadhikavāṭakam |
acchatailagirirnāmnā tadagre vidyate sadā || 1,12.148 ||
bilaṃ tatpaścime hyasti tanmadhye cāpapañcake |
gate paśyettatra kuṇḍaṃ tatra lākṣāsamaṃ rasam || 1,12.149 ||
alābupātre gṛhṇīyātsa sūtaḥ koṭivedhakaḥ |
prākāraścandraguptasya śrīgirīśasya paścime || 1,12.150 ||
tanmadhye vidyate veśma caityaṃ tatpūrvataḥ sthitam |
caityātpūrve śilā divyā mudgābhā sparśasaṃjñakā || 1,12.151 ||
tatrāste mohano dantī tamāruhya samāhitaḥ |
pṛṣṭhāttasya tṛṇaṃ grāhyaṃ tatsarvaṃ kāñcanaṃ bhavet || 1,12.152 ||
tadgajasyottare pārśve jānudaghnāṃ khaneddharām |
jambūphalābhāḥ pāṣāṇāḥ sparśasaṃjñā bhavanti te || 1,12.153 ||
kṣitiṃ khanedgajasyādho jānumātraṃ labhettataḥ |
lakṣavedhakarā siddhā trikoṇe ghuṭikā parā || 1,12.154 ||
mallināthasya vāyavye natvā devahradaḥ paraḥ |
tīrthaṃ tatrāsti pāṣāṇā mudgābhāḥ sparśasaṃjñaḥ || 1,12.155 ||
liṅgaṃ kūrmopamaṃ tatra sparśasaṃjñaṃ śubhaṃ priye |
pūrṇimāyāṃ kṛttikāyāṃ pūjayitvā samāharet || 1,12.156 ||
tasmādgavyūtiyugalātsmṛtā nīlavanī parā |
tasyā liṅgaṃ ca salilaṃ nīlavarṇaṃ praśasyate || 1,12.157 ||
aśvāmrakākasaṅkāśāḥ pāṣāṇāḥ sparśavedhakāḥ |
devālayāntarnikhanejjānumātrāntarāddharām || 1,12.158 ||
mūṣikākārapāṣāṇāḥ sparśasaṃjñā bhavanti te |
śrīgirīśasya nairṛtyāṃ khyātā guṇḍīprabhākhyayā || 1,12.159 ||
tasyāgragarte pītābhāṃ mṛttikāṃ ca puṭe dahet |
tasyānniḥsarati svarṇaṃ śuddhadevārhakaṃ param || 1,12.160 ||
tatrāste bhṛṅgacūto'pi pūrvavatsiddhidāyakaḥ |
tathā tambīpure cāsti tīrthe ca vipule śubham || 1,12.161 ||
sadāphalaṃ tu vikhyātaṃ tasyāḥ pūrvottare sudhīḥ |
śrīgirernairṛte bhāge mahānandeti viśrutaḥ || 1,12.162 ||
vrajedghaṇṭāpathenaiva tasya pūrvottare sudhīḥ |
tatrāste kālakaṇṭheśo nāmnāgre tasya kuṇḍakam || 1,12.163 ||
tatrendragopasaṅkāśaṃ siddhiḥ sūtasya vidyate |
bhavanti sapta lohāni svarṇaṃ taptāni sekataḥ || 1,12.164 ||
aiśānye śrīgirīśasya cāsti śrīmālinīśvaraḥ |
tasyottaradiśi khyātastūmāparvatasaṃjñakaḥ || 1,12.165 ||
tasyāgre ca triśūlābhāḥ santi darbhāḥ śubhaṅkarāḥ |
tadadho nikhanennābhimātraṃ nīlāṃ mṛdaṃ haret || 1,12.166 ||
akṣakāṣṭhaiḥ pacettāṃ ca devātithyagnaye kramāt |
ekaikabhāgaṃ kalpeta caturthāṃśaṃ svayaṃ bhajet || 1,12.167 ||
jīvetkalpāyutaṃ siddho mahābalaparākramaḥ |
ileśvarasya nikaṭe tatra koṭīśvaraḥ sthitaḥ || 1,12.168 ||
puṣpaṃ patraṃ tadagre'sti sparśavedhakaraṃ bhavet |
tatrācaleśvaro devaḥ sparśavedhakaraḥ paraḥ || 1,12.169 ||
tasya yojanamātre ca dakṣiṇe cāmareśvaraḥ |
tataḥ svarṇaśilā cāsti tadūrdhvaṃ naramāṃsakam || 1,12.170 ||
gomāṃsaṃ vā kṣipetkāṣṭhairbādarairjvālayetsudhīḥ |
prātaḥ kāñcanasaṅkāśā dṛśyate sā śilā tadā || 1,12.171 ||
pratyagrapuṣpavatyāśca rajasā bhāvitāṃśukam |
loḍayettacchilāyāṃ ca kṣipedalpāmbupātrake || 1,12.172 ||
tasya saṃsparśamātreṇa lehaḥ syātkāñcanaṃ param |
śrīgurau yatra tatrāpi piṇḍabhūmisthitopalāḥ || 1,12.173 ||
karṣamātraṃ tu taccūrṇaṃ bhakṣayettriphalā samam |
valīpalitajinmāsājjīvedācandratārakam || 1,12.174 ||
śrīgirīndrasya nairṛtyāṃ paṭāhakarṇa īśvaraḥ |
tasya pūrvottare bhāge pañcaviṃśaticāpake || 1,12.175 ||
dvihastamātrordhvaśilā khanettatra dvihastakam |
śarāvasaṃpuṭākārān dṛṣadastānsamāharet || 1,12.176 ||
kurvīta tānagnivarṇān siñcyātkūṣmāṇḍajairdravaiḥ |
tanmadhyānnavanītaṃ tu dadyāddevāya bhāgakam || 1,12.177 ||
bhāgaikamatitherdeyaṃ bhāgamekaṃ svayaṃ lihet |
divā naktaṃ bhavenmūrcchā vinidro bhavati svayam || 1,12.178 ||
valīpalitasaṃvarjyo jīvedvarṣāyutaṃ naraḥ |
sparśakāntasya sopānaṃ dvitīyaṃ bhavati priye || 1,12.179 ||
taddevapārśvayoḥ sarve pāṣāṇāḥ śvetapītakāḥ |
sparśāḥ sarvā bhavantyete teṣu caikaṃ samāharet || 1,12.180 ||
prācyāṃ hi tasya devasya samīpe kūpamasti ca |
maṇḍūkābhāśca pāṣāṇāḥ santi ca sparśasaṃjñakāḥ || 1,12.181 ||
tasyeśvarasya codīcyāṃ liṅgādrirvidyate mahān |
taduttarasyāṃ diśyasti taṭinī pūrvavāhinī || 1,12.182 ||
taṭinyāḥ paścimāśāyāṃ piṅgābhaṃ liṅgamasti hi |
pūrṇaśailodakaṃ kuṇḍaṃ tatra syātkṣaṇavedhakam || 1,12.183 ||
dviyojane maheśasya dakṣiṇe caṇḍikā sthitā |
piṇḍikākhyā suvikhyātā tasyā vāyavyakoṇataḥ || 1,12.184 ||
andhurasti hi tanmadhye mudgavarṇāstathopalāḥ |
kecitpathyānibhāḥ santi te sarve sparśasaṃjñakāḥ || 1,12.185 ||
atratyānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ sparśānāṃ vidhirucyate |
yadi sthūlaṃ peṣayettaṃ ślakṣṇaṃ mūṣāntareṇa hi || 1,12.186 ||
kuryātsarvāṇi lohāni tasyāmāvartayetpriye |
tatsarvaṃ jāyate svarṇaṃ kuryādevaṃ yatheṣṭakam || 1,12.187 ||
sūkṣmaścetsarvalohānāṃ drutānāmantare kṣipet |
tatsparśātsarvalohāni kāñcanatvaṃ prayānti hi || 1,12.188 ||
vāyavyāṃ mallināthasya tīrthe sarveśvarākhyakam |
tasya dakṣiṇadigbhāge rājamārge dviyojane || 1,12.189 ||
gate ḍoṃgalikā tatra dṛśyate tasya mūrdhani |
kṛṣṇadhātrīphalānyeva santi tāni ca bhakṣayet || 1,12.190 ||
saptavāsaraparyantamā tṛptiṃ vajravigrahaḥ |
jarāmaraṇanirmukto jīvetkalpaśatatrayam || 1,12.191 ||
tasmācca dakṣiṇe bhāge kākalārīmahāvane |
tatrāste stambakadalī praviśettatra sādhakaḥ || 1,12.192 ||
krośārdhamātraṃ gacchecca rasakuṇḍaṃ ca dṛśyate |
alābupātre saṃsthāpya koṭivedhī bhavettu saḥ || 1,12.193 ||
śilāmayo nīlavarṇaḥ śikhaṇḍī tatra dṛśyate |
tanmukhāgre'sti kuṇḍaṃ vitastimātrātinīlakam || 1,12.194 ||
śuṣkavaṃśaṃ ca tanmadhye kṣaṇaṃ kṣiptaṃ navaṃ bhavet |
patraṃ puṣpaṃ dinaikena bhavatyeva na saṃśayaḥ || 1,12.195 ||
tatra prasthaṃ rasaṃ kṣiptvā tāvanmātraṃ jalaṃ haret |
madhutulyaṃ pibettaṃ ca mūrcchā bhavati tatkṣaṇāt || 1,12.196 ||
kṣaṇena labdhajñānaḥ syājjīvedyugasahasrakam |
śrīgirervahnidigbhāge ghaṭikāsiddhakhecaraḥ || 1,12.197 ||
asti tasya puro bhūmau pañcahastaṃ khanetsudhīḥ |
badarākārapāṣāṇā vidyante khagatipradāḥ || 1,12.198 ||
ekaṃ vaktre sadā dhāryaṃ siddhaiḥ khegatilipsubhiḥ |
<siddhikramaḥ>
śrīśaile sarvasiddhīnāmuktānāṃ vidhirucyate || 1,12.199 ||
mantranyāsaṃ purā kṛtvā paścāllakṣaṃ japenmanum |
aghorāntena vai śīghraṃ tattatsiddhimavāpnuyāt || 1,12.200 ||
<atha nyāsaḥ>
oṃ hrīṃ mahāparvatavāsinyai hṛdayāya namaḥ |
oṃ hrīṃ jyeṣṭhāyai śirase svāhā |
oṃ hrīṃ raudryai śikhāyai vaṣaṭ |
oṃ hrīṃ kanakakuṇḍalinyai kavacāya hum |
oṃ hrīṃ saṃjīvinyai netrebhyo vauṣaṭ |
oṃ hrīṃ mālinyai astrāya phaṭ |
oṃ huṃ śivāya namaḥ pādayornyaset |
oṃ vaṃ vāmadevāya namaḥ jaṅghayoḥ |
oṃ paṃ padminyai namaḥ guhye |
oṃ kāṃ kanyākumāryai namaḥ nābhau |
oṃ glauṃ namaḥ kukṣau |
oṃ drāṃ hrīṃ durgāyai namaḥ urasi |
oṃ huṃ vindhyavāsinyai namaḥ kaṇṭhamūle |
oṃ laṃ namaḥ grīvāyām |
oṃ hrīṃ śrīmaṣṭabhujāyai namaḥ bhujayoḥ |
oṃ huṃ sarvatomukhinyai namaḥ mukhe |
oṃ prakāśikāyai namaḥ netrayoḥ |
oṃ mahiṣamardinyai namaḥ karṇayoḥ |
oṃ hrāṃ sarvāṅgasundaryai namaḥ mūrdhni nyaset |
evamaṅgarakṣāṃ kṛtvā kṣetraṃ pūjayet |
oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ namo bhagavati sarveśvari devi namo maṇḍalavāsini krāṃ krīṃ krūṃ hana hana paca paca matha matha śīghramāveśaya śīghramāveśaya ehyehi bhuvanavandite svāhā |
anena mantreṇa devatāmāvāhayet |
oṃ hrāṃ hrīṃ hrūṃ tribhuvaneśvaryai namaḥ sāṃnidhyaṃ kuru kuru svāhā |
anena mantreṇa tatkṣetradevatāṃ pūjayet |
oṃ kṛṣṇavarṇini aparāmukhyai namaḥ pūrvasmin |
oṃ sudhāvarṣiṇyai namaḥ āgneye |
oṃ parvatākṛṣṇavarṇinīraudramukhyai namaḥ dakṣiṇe |
oṃ bālārkavarṇinīmahālakṣmyai namaḥ nairṛtye |
oṃ khaṃ raktaparvatavarṇinī |
mahāmahiṣāsuramardinyai namaḥ paścime |
oṃ māṃ sarvavarṇinīhuṅkāravāgdevyai namaḥ vāyavye |
oṃ sihmavāhinyai namaḥ uttare |
oṃ śivāśivāśivātriśūlahastāyai namaḥ aiśānye |
oṃ hrāṃ hrīṃ klīṃ manonmanyai namaḥ ākāśe |
oṃ hrāṃ hrīṃ mahāmohinyai namaḥ pātāle |
oṃ hrāṃ hrīṃ hrūṃ hraiṃ hroṃ hrauṃ hraṃ mahāvidye jambhaya jambhaya mohaya mohaya darśaya darśaya mūrchaya mūrchaya klīṃ matha matha ākarṣayākarṣaya huṃ phaṭsvāhā |
madhye ca pūjayet |
evaṃ nyāsaṃ rakṣāṃ pūjāṃ kṛtvā tatkarmaṇi lakṣamekamaghoraṃ japet |
tataḥ siddhimavāpnoti |
mantraṃ yathā |
oṃ hrāṃ hrīṃ hramaghoratara prasphura prasphura prakaṭa prakaṭa kaha kaha śama śama jāta jāta daha daha pātaya pātaya oṃ hraiṃ hrāṃ hramadhorāstrāya phaṭ |
amuṃ mantraṃ lakṣaṃ japet |
tataḥ svapne pratyakṣībhūya devatā varaṃ dadāti || 1,12.201 ||


āk, 1, 13
<gandhakaḥ>
śrībhairavī |
praṇamya śirasā śambhumastauṣītparameśvarī |
śrutaṃ tava prasādena divyaṃ sarvarasāyanam || 1,13.1 ||
utpattiṃ gandhakasyāpi jātiṃ saṃśodhanaṃ tathā |
sevāṃ rasāyanaphalaṃ kramādbrūhi maheśvara || 1,13.2 ||
śrībhairavaḥ |
devi śṛṇu pravakṣyāmi gandhakasya rasāyanam |
śvetadvīpe purā devi latākalpadrumojjvale || 1,13.3 ||
nānāmaṇigaṇākīrṇe nānāpuṣpaphalākule |
siddhavidyādharastrībhiḥ kinnaryapsarasāṃ gaṇaiḥ || 1,13.4 ||
anekanirjarastrībhiḥ krīḍantī tvaṃ madollasā |
veṇuvīṇāvinodena vādyanādairmanoharaiḥ || 1,13.5 ||
tālairjhallarikāḍhakkāninādaiḥ karatālakaiḥ |
atyānandena deveśi nṛtyantī tvaṃ surārcite || 1,13.6 ||
tadā ṛtumatī jātā susrāva ca rajo mahat |
sugandhinā tadrajasā tvadvastraṃ raktatāṃ yayau || 1,13.7 ||
vihāya tadraktavastraṃ snātvā kṣīramahodadhau |
anekāmarakāntābhirvṛtā kailāsamāvrajaḥ || 1,13.8 ||
vāyunā tadrajovastraṃ kṣiptaṃ kṣīramahodadhau |
magnaṃ tatraiva tadvastraṃ cirakālaṃ sureśvari || 1,13.9 ||
mathyamānānmahāmbhodheramṛtena sahodbhavam |
ślāghyena nijagandhena modayannasurānsurān || 1,13.10 ||
tenaiva nāmnā ūcuste śakrādyāḥ surapuṅgavāḥ |
gandhako'yaṃ bhavatvasya nāmnā jātaṃ tu matpriye || 1,13.11 ||
caturvidhaṃ śoṇitaṃ te divyaṃ balakaraṃ param |
samartho'yaṃ gandhakastu rasabandhe ca jāraṇe || 1,13.12 ||
rasendre ye guṇāḥ santi te guṇāḥ santi gandhake |
ityūcurindrapramukhā hṛṣṭā gandhakagandhataḥ || 1,13.13 ||
tadā prabhṛti loke'sminkhyāto'yaṃ gandhakaḥ priye |
japākusumasaṅkāśaḥ kṣatriyaścottamaḥ priye || 1,13.14 ||
pīto vaiśyo madhyamaḥ syācchveto vipro'dhamaḥ smṛtaḥ |
kṛṣṇavarṇo bhavecchūdraḥ priye syādadhamādhamaḥ || 1,13.15 ||
raktavarṇaṃ lohavedhi pītavarṇaṃ rasāyanam |
śvetavarṇaṃ ca bhaiṣajye kṛṣṇaṃ kuṣṭhādilepane || 1,13.16 ||
<gandhakaśuddhiḥ>
atha vakṣye gandhakasya śodhanaṃ siddhidāyakam |
tilaparṇyajamodāśca brāhmī bhṛṅgī ca dhutturaḥ || 1,13.17 ||
eteṣāṃ ca rasairbhāvyaṃ cūrṇitaṃ gandhakaṃ dinam |
kāntapātre vinikṣipya samāṃśājyena pācayet || 1,13.18 ||
mṛdvagninā tu tatpaktvā hyajākṣīre vinikṣipet |
gokṣīrājyaiḥ kiṃcidūnaṃ sthālyā vaktraṃ tu bandhayet || 1,13.19 ||
svacchāṃśukena baddhasya cūrṇaṃ tadupari kṣipet |
tadūrdhvaṃ śrāvakaṃ ruddhvā tatsthāpyaṃ gartakāntare || 1,13.20 ||
vanotpalairmṛdupuṭaṃ dattvā dravati gandhakam |
punaśca tilaparṇyādyairbhāvayecchoṣayettridhā || 1,13.21 ||
drāvayettatsamādāya matsyapittena saptadhā |
bhāvayejjālinībījacūrṇaistatparipeṣayet || 1,13.22 ||
tato bhṛṅgadravairbhāvyaṃ saptakṛtvastape khare |
jalaiḥ prakṣālya tatsamyakśoṣayettatpunaḥ pacet || 1,13.23 ||
mṛdvagninā kāntapātre ghṛtākte tu kṣaṇaṃ tataḥ |
bhṛṅgarājadrave kṣiptvā śuddhaṃ tadgandhakaṃ bhavet || 1,13.24 ||
rase rasāyane yoge yojyaṃ sukhakaraṃ bhavet |
<gandhakarasāyanam>
pūrvoktavaddehaśuddhiṃ kuryādvāntivirecanaiḥ || 1,13.25 ||
sumuhūrte sunakṣatre pūjayitvā paraṃ śivam |
gaṇādhipaṃ kṣetrapālaṃ śrīguruṃ bhiṣajaṃ kramāt || 1,13.26 ||
niṣkaikaṃ triphalācūrṇaṃ niṣkamekaṃ ca guggulum |
guñjonmeyaṃ śuddhagandhaṃ lihederaṇḍatailataḥ || 1,13.27 ||
guñjāvṛddhikrameṇaiva sevyaṃ tatprativāsaram |
dinaṣoḍaśaparyantaṃ guñjāṣoḍaśamātrakam || 1,13.28 ||
tadā ṣoḍaśaghasrānte sevyaṃ tatprativāsaram |
guñjāṣoḍaśikonmeyamapūrvaṃ triphalāṃ param || 1,13.29 ||
etasmādadhikā vṛddhirna kāryā siddhilipsunā |
evaṃ māsaprayogeṇa kuṣṭhamaṣṭādaśaṃ haret || 1,13.30 ||
tathā pramehān gulmāni kāsaśvāsakṣayajvarān |
vraṇānbhagandarādīṃścāśītivātodbhavāngadān || 1,13.31 ||
māsadvayaprayogeṇa bahukāntiṃ prayacchati |
sarvavyādhipraśamanaṃ bhavenmāsatrayātpriye || 1,13.32 ||
tataḥ ṣaṇmāsayogena vṛddho yauvanamāpnuyāt |
evamabdaprayogeṇa siddho bhavati śāmbhavi || 1,13.33 ||
tanmūtramalayogena tāmraṃ kāñcanatāṃ vrajet |
vidhiṃ ca pratiṣedhaṃ ca kuryātpūrvoktavatsudhīḥ || 1,13.34 ||
rasādi puṇyamamalaṃ yaḥ seveta rasāyanam |
sa eva kṛtakṛtyaḥ syāddaivatairapi pūjyate || 1,13.35 ||
sarvatīrtheṣu saṃsnātaḥ sarvavrataphalānvitaḥ |
aśvamedhādiyajñānāṃ sarveṣāṃ phalamāpnuyāt || 1,13.36 ||
dhanyaḥ puṇyatamaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ ślāghanīyo manīṣibhiḥ |
sa yogavijñaḥ sarvajñaḥ sarvānugrāhakaḥ prabhuḥ || 1,13.37 ||
śuddhaḥ sarvagataḥ śāntaḥ śūraḥ satvaguṇojjvalaḥ |
tasya dehalayo nāsti hyadṛśyaḥ śivatāṃ vrajet || 1,13.38 ||


āk, 1, 14
<viṣam; viṣotpattiḥ>
śrībhairavī |
śrutaṃ sarvaṃ mayā deva divyaṃ sarvarasāyanam |
viṣotpattiṃ ca jātiṃ ca sevāṃ vada ca tatphalam || 1,14.1 ||
śrībhairavaḥ |
kṣīrodadhau mathyamāne manthanīkṛtamandaraiḥ |
siddhakinnaraguhyendrapiśācoragarākṣasaiḥ || 1,14.2 ||
brahmādyairakhilairdevairekato danujādhipaiḥ |
daityairanyatra vipulairmahābalaparākramaiḥ || 1,14.3 ||
tato jātāni ratnāni kaustubhādīni kāmagauḥ |
kalpavṛkṣo mahālakṣmīrairāvatamahāgajaḥ || 1,14.4 ||
uccaiḥśravāstathā cendurdhanvantaribhiṣagvaraḥ |
amṛtaṃ ca latā divyā divyasiddhipradāyakāḥ || 1,14.5 ||
mandarabhramaṇaśrāntanāgarājamukhāttataḥ |
viṣajvālā samutpannā tīvrā lokabhayaṃkarī || 1,14.6 ||
mamajja kṣīrajaladhāvatyantamathanātpunaḥ |
kālakūṭaṃ mahākṣveḍaṃ kalpāntayamasannibham || 1,14.7 ||
saṃvartāgnipratīkāśaṃ vīkṣya bhītāḥ surāstadā |
mamāntikaṃ samājagmuḥ stuvanto dīnamānasāḥ || 1,14.8 ||
svāminsarvottama trāhi śaraṇāgatapālaka |
ityādi bahudhā stotraṃ kurvāṇāste surāsurāḥ || 1,14.9 ||
ahaṃ smitamukhaṃ kṛtvā kālakūṭaṃ tadāpibam |
tatra tatrāvaśiṣṭaṃ tadbahudhā samabhūdbhuvi || 1,14.10 ||
kvacinmūlasvarūpeṇa kvāpi tvagrūpataḥ priye |
kutrāpi parṇarūpeṇa toyarūpeṇa kutracit || 1,14.11 ||
evaṃ bahuvidhaṃ jātaṃ tatra tatra viṣaṃ gatam |
teṣāṃ śreṣṭhaṃ kandaviṣamaṣṭādaśaviṣaṃ priye || 1,14.12 ||
<poison:: 10 kinds>
kramādbhedaṃ pravakṣyāmi kālakūṭaṃ ca darduram |
hālāhalaṃ meṣaśṛṅgaṃ mohadaṃ granthi karkaṭam || 1,14.13 ||
raktaśṛṅgi haridraṃ ca kesaraṃ daśamaṃ smṛtam |
ete daśavidhāḥ kṣveḍā na prayojyā rasāyane || 1,14.14 ||
<poison:: 8 kinds>
śvetaśṛṅgī vatsanābhaṃ sarṣapaṃ śṛṅgi vālukam |
mustakaṃ saktukaṃ devi cāṣṭamaṃ kardamaṃ bhavet || 1,14.15 ||
rasāyane yojanīyāstvaṣṭau bhedāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
kālakūṭaṃ kākacañcuprabhaṃ dardurasannibham || 1,14.16 ||
darduraṃ nīlavarṇaṃ ca hālāhalaviṣaṃ samam |
meṣaśṛṅgī tu meṣasya śṛṅgābhaṃ mohadaṃ punaḥ || 1,14.17 ||
raktanīlaprabhaṃ devi granthikaṃ granthisaṃyutam |
karkaṭaṃ kapilābhaṃ ca raktaśṛṅgi mahāviṣam || 1,14.18 ||
kṛṣṇapiṅgaṃ ca raktābhaṃ haridrābhaṃ haridrakam |
kesaraṃ padmakiñjalkaprabhaṃ daśavidhaṃ tviti || 1,14.19 ||
śvetaśṛṅgībhadantābhaṃ vatsanābhaṃ ca pāṇḍuram |
sarṣapākṛtibhiryuktaṃ bindubhiḥ sarṣapaṃ bhavet || 1,14.20 ||
śṛṅgī śṛṅgākṛtirjñeyo vālukaṃ vālukākṛti |
mustākṛtirmustakaṃ syācchvetavarṇaṃ tu saktukam || 1,14.21 ||
madhūcchiṣṭākṛtirdevi kardamaṃ viṣamuttamam |
viṣakhaṇḍeṣu bhagneṣu dṛśyante yeṣu bindavaḥ || 1,14.22 ||
śvetaraktapītavarṇāḥ kṛṣṇā viprādayaḥ kramāt |
śvetā rasāyane raktā vaśye pāradakarmaṇi || 1,14.23 ||
kṣudrakarmaṇi pītāḥ syuḥ kṛṣṇavarṇāstu māraṇe |
viṣaṃ yuktyāmṛtaṃ devi tadayuktyā viṣaṃ bhavet || 1,14.24 ||
tasmādyuktyā viṣaṃ sevyamayuktyā na kadācana |
rasābhrahemakāntānāṃ vīryeṇa sadṛśaṃ viṣam || 1,14.25 ||
<viṣaśuddhiḥ>
raktasarṣapatailena lipte vastre ca bandhayet |
tridinānte samuddhṛtya ṭaṅkaṇena samaṃ viṣam || 1,14.26 ||
mardayecchlakṣṇacūrṇaṃ tatkārayecca viśudhyati |
roge rasāyane yojyaṃ siddhidaṃ syādvarānane || 1,14.27 ||
<viṣarasāyanam>
śuddhadeho virekādyaiḥ pathyāśī viṣabhugbhavet |
krameṇa sevanīyaṃ tatsarṣapaṃ rājasarṣapam || 1,14.28 ||
mudgavrīhiyavā māṣā guñjāmātraṃ parāvadhiḥ |
śarkarāsahitaṃ sevyaṃ kṣvelaṃ tadamṛtaṃ bhavet || 1,14.29 ||
kaṭvamlalavaṇāstyājyā vyāyāmoṣṇātapādayaḥ |
tailasarṣapakhādyāśca varjyā viṣarasāyane || 1,14.30 ||
gavyaṃ kṣīraṃ ghṛtaṃ takraṃ snigdhaṃ dadhi ca śarkarā |
sevyā svādurasadravyā nocedvikṛtikāraṇam || 1,14.31 ||
<viṣopadravāstaccikitsā ca>
mātrayā sevitaṃ kṣvelamamṛtaṃ bhavati priye |
atimātraṃ yadi bhavedamṛtaṃ ca viṣāyate || 1,14.32 ||
pramādādatimātraṃ cettadā vegā bhavanti ca |
kaṣṭaṃ prathamavege syādvepathuśca dvitīyake || 1,14.33 ||
tṛtīye ca tṛṣā dāhaścaturthe gatibhañjanam |
phenilāsyaḥ pañcame syātṣaṣṭhe kandharabhañjanam || 1,14.34 ||
saptame ca nirutthānaṃ maraṇaṃ cāṣṭame bhavet |
tasmācchīghraṃ pratīkāraḥ karaṇīyo varānane || 1,14.35 ||
kiṃcinmātrādhikaṃ kṣvelaṃ nānārogānkaroti tat |
bhrāntivismṛtiśūlāni vāntyatīsārakaṃ param || 1,14.36 ||
svarasādaṃ gadgadatvaṃ dāhaṃ dṛṣṭibhramaṃ tathā |
karṇarukśvāsakāsādīnanyān vātodbhavān gadān || 1,14.37 ||
yadātimātraṃ cihnāni dṛśyante vapuṣi priye |
tadā tu ṭaṅkaṇaṃ niṣkamājyaṃ cāpi caturguṇam || 1,14.38 ||
peyaṃ tatparihārārthaṃ jalairvā taṇḍulīyakam |
majjāṃ vā putrajīvasya phalānniṣkaṃ jalānvitam || 1,14.39 ||
tutthaṃ paṇadvayonmeyaṃ nṛmūtrairvā haridrakam |
vacāṃ vā devadālīṃ vā sarpākṣīṃ vātha sārbuṇīm || 1,14.40 ||
paṭolīṃ giriparṇīṃ nṛmūtrairviṣajitpṛthak |
lāṃ yāṃ vāṃ hāṃ caturvarṇairgāruḍaṃ saṃpuṭīkṛtam || 1,14.41 ||
stambhastobhananirvāhanirviṣīkaraṇaṃ tathā |
lāṃ proṃ lāmanena mantreṇa stambhanam |
śītajalaghaṭamabhimantrya viṣāturasya mastake jalaṃ ḍhālayet |
viṣaṃ nākrāmati |
yāṃ proṃ yāmanena stobhanamantreṇa viṣāturasya śikhinamandhayet |
vāṃ proṃ vāmanena nirvāhamantreṇārkadaṇḍaṃ vā dhuttūrakāṣṭhadaṇḍaṃ vābhimantrya viṣāturasya sarvāṅgaṃ spṛṣṭvā viṣaṃ nirvāhayet |
hāṃ proṃ hāmanena nirviṣīkaraṇamantreṇa nirviṣīkaraṇārthaṃ viṣāturasya sarvāṅgaṃ daṇḍenāpāmārjayetsvastho bhavati |
jīrṇaṃ kṣvelaṃ śarīre cetpittāntaṃ vamanaṃ priye || 1,14.42 ||
āmāntaṃ recanaṃ kāryaṃ taṇḍulīyakamūlabhuk |
seveta māṃsaṃ saghṛtaṃ viṣadoṣaharaṃ bhavet || 1,14.43 ||
pūrvoktamātrāsevī yo mahāvyādhervimucyate |
devi ṣaṇmāsayogena jarāpalitakhaṇḍanam || 1,14.44 ||
saṃvatsaropayogena jīveccandrārkatārakam |
himaśītavasanteṣu yojyaṃ viṣarasāyanam || 1,14.45 ||
grīṣmavarṣaśaratsvetanna prayojyaṃ kadācana |
vātasthaviragarbhiṇyaḥ kṣuttṛḍgharmādhvapīḍitāḥ || 1,14.46 ||
napuṃsakā gadakṣīṇāḥ pittakrodhādhikāstathā |
yakṣmiṇo naiva yogyāḥ syuḥ sadā viṣarasāyane || 1,14.47 ||


āk, 1, 15
<divyauṣadhirasāyanāni>
śrībhairavī |
malamāyāvihīneśa jarājanmagadāpaha |
tvatprasādena viditaṃ rasādīnāṃ rasāyanam || 1,15.1 ||
itaḥparamapi svāmin śuśrūṣe kimapi prabho |
sukhopāyopayogyaṃ ca divyauṣadhirasāyanam || 1,15.2 ||
brūhi me tadvidhaṃ divyaṃ sarvasiddhipradāyakam |
śrībhairavaḥ |
<1. brahmakalpaḥ; brahmavṛkṣatailakalpaḥ>
vakṣyāmi brahmavṛkṣādi divyauṣadhirasāyanam || 1,15.3 ||
tatrādau brahmavṛkṣasya śṛṇu devi rasāyanam |
brahmavṛkṣasya bījāni vidadhyānnistuṣāṇi ca || 1,15.4 ||
pariśoṣyātape tīvre sūkṣmacūrṇāni kārayet |
dhātrīphalena saptāhaṃ bhāvayetpayasāthavā || 1,15.5 ||
cakrayantre kṣipettāni tatastattailamāharet |
itthamutthāpitaṃ tailaṃ doṣaghnaṃ ca rasāyanam || 1,15.6 ||
brahmabījajatailasya prasthamājyaṃ ca tatsamam |
nikṣiptaṃ snigdhabhāṇḍe ca dhānyarāśau vinikṣipet || 1,15.7 ||
māsārdhamāsaṃ deveśi tasmāttailaṃ samāharet |
śuddhadeho virekādyairarcitāgnigurudvijaḥ || 1,15.8 ||
dvipalaṃ ca gavāṃ kṣīraṃ tattailaṃ niṣkamātrakam |
saṃmiśrya ca pibetprātaḥ pathyāśī syājjitendriyaḥ || 1,15.9 ||
evaṃ dvitīye'pi dine hyekāhāntarite kramāt |
niṣkavṛddhirbhavedevaṃ yāvatṣoḍaśaniṣkakam || 1,15.10 ||
etattailasya paramā mātrā hyasyaivamīritā |
pītamātre kṣaṇaṃ mūrcchā jāyate siñcayenmukham || 1,15.11 ||
prabuddhe saghṛtaṃ dadyāddugdhānnaṃ śarkarānvitam |
evaṃ tailopayogena māsājjñānī bhavennaraḥ || 1,15.12 ||
sakṛdgrāhī sukāntaśca ṣoḍaśābda iva sthitaḥ |
dvitīye nāgabalavān sarvavyādhivivarjitaḥ || 1,15.13 ||
indropamabalo dhīraścaturthe māsi ca kramāt |
vajradeho divyadṛṣṭiḥ pañcame khecaro bhavet || 1,15.14 ||
aṇimādiguṇopetaḥ sarvagaḥ sarvakālikaḥ |
ṣaṣṭhe māsi svayaṃ sraṣṭā bhoktā hartā trimūrtivat || 1,15.15 ||
varṣaikasevanāddevi bhavetsākṣātsadāśivaḥ |
yāvattailopajīvī syāttāvatkṣīraudanāśanaḥ || 1,15.16 ||
vamanādiviśuddhāṅgaḥ kṛtvā vaidyadvijārcanam |
prātargokṣīrakuḍubaṃ tailaṃ kiṃśukabījajam || 1,15.17 ||
kuḍubaṃ pūrvavajjātamekīkṛtya dvayaṃ pibet |
tatpānamātre mūrchā syātkuryāttaṃ bhasmaśāyinam || 1,15.18 ||
saptarātre prabuddhaḥ syādbaddhavacchayane sthitaḥ |
nāṅgāni cālayedeṣa jāyate vicalekṣaṇaḥ || 1,15.19 ||
taile jīrṇe samāpanne saṃjñā bhavati bhairavi |
gokṣīraṃ tasya dātavyaṃ pratyahaṃ daśavāsaram || 1,15.20 ||
sa tvacaṃ ca tyajeddehātkañcukaṃ bhujago yathā |
kṣīrāhārī bhavennityamekaviṃśativāsaram || 1,15.21 ||
vācāṃ patirbhaveddhīraḥ śrutaṃ dhārayate kṣaṇāt |
dūraśrāvī divyadṛṣṭirjīvedbrahmadinaṃ sudhīḥ || 1,15.22 ||
<brahmavṛkṣapallavakalpaḥ>
puṇyarkṣe brahmavṛkṣasya pallavāni samāharet |
krimikīṭavihīnāni komalāni śubhāni ca || 1,15.23 ||
ātape śoṣayettīvre cūrṇitaṃ vastragālitam |
kuryānnūtanabhāṇḍe ca nikṣipecca prayatnataḥ || 1,15.24 ||
vamanādyairviśuddhāṅgo brahmacārī jitendriyaḥ |
kṣārāmlavarjitāhāraḥ kṣīraśālyannabhojanaḥ || 1,15.25 ||
koṣṇaṃ jalaṃ pibennityaṃ nivāte śayanaṃ bhajet |
karṣamātraṃ ca seveta māsaṃ gotakrasaṃyutam || 1,15.26 ||
dvitīye ca tṛtīye ca vṛddhiḥ karṣādhikā kramāt |
evaṃ ṣoḍaśamāsāntaṃ satakraṃ kuḍubaṃ pibet || 1,15.27 ||
evaṃ nityopasevī yaḥ kuñcitasnigdhakuntalaḥ |
mattamātaṅgabalavān jīvedbrahmadinaṃ naraḥ || 1,15.28 ||
<brahmavṛkṣapuṣpakalpaḥ>
brahmavṛkṣasya puṣpāṇi chāyāyāṃ śoṣayetsudhīḥ |
cūrṇayedgālayedvastre navabhāṇḍe vinikṣipet || 1,15.29 ||
viṃśatpalaṃ puṣpacūrṇaṃ caturviṃśatigoghṛtam |
palamekatra saṃmiśraṃ dhānyarāśau niveśayet || 1,15.30 ||
taduddharecca māsānte kṛtvā bhāgāṃścaturdaśa |
ekaikaṃ pratyekaṃ sevyamevaṃ māsatrayaṃ bhavet || 1,15.31 ||
bhuñjīta śulbapātre ca lavaṇāmlādivarjitam |
pāyasāśī kaṣāyaṃ tṛṣārtaḥ khādiraṃ pibet || 1,15.32 ||
trimāsājjāyate gātraṃ vajravannātra saṃśayaḥ |
nāgāyutabalo dhīro vāyuvegagatirbhavet || 1,15.33 ||
asya varṣopayogena purīṣamapi mūtrakam |
vedhayetsarvalohāni kāñcanāni ca kārayet || 1,15.34 ||
jīvedbrahmadinaṃ sākṣāddaivataiḥ saha modate |
<brahmavṛkṣabījakalpaḥ>
brahmavṛkṣasya bījāni cūrṇayennikṣipedghaṭe || 1,15.35 ||
madhvājyaśarkarāyuktaṃ dhānyarāśau vinikṣipet |
māsādūrdhvaṃ samāhṛtya pratyahaṃ karṣamātrakam || 1,15.36 ||
upayuñjīta śuddhātmā gokṣīraṃ ca pibedanu |
yāvatpalaṃ bhavedvṛddhistāvadevādhikaṃ na hi || 1,15.37 ||
evaṃ saṃvatsarātsiddhirjāyate mṛtyuvarjitā |
jīvedbrahmadinaṃ śuddho mataṅgajabalopamaḥ || 1,15.38 ||
<brahmavṛkṣavalkalakalpaḥ>
sūkṣmacūrṇaṃ prakurvīta brahmavṛkṣasya valkalam |
bhāvayedgavyapayasā palaṃ cānudinaṃ pibet || 1,15.39 ||
jitendriyaśca pathyāśī bhavedā vatsaraṃ sudhīḥ |
daśavarṣasahasrāṇi jīvetsiddho bhaveddhruvam || 1,15.40 ||
<brahmavṛkṣaniryāsakalpaḥ>
brāhmī ca madhukaṃ sājyaṃ niryāsaṃ brahmavṛkṣajam |
samabhāgāni seveta palaṃ cānupibetpayaḥ || 1,15.41 ||
evamekābdayogena śubhaṃyudarśanaḥ śuciḥ |
jīvedbrahmadinaṃ jñānī valīpalitavarjitaḥ || 1,15.42 ||
<brahmavṛkṣapañcāṅgakalpaḥ>
pañcāṅgaṃ brahmavṛkṣasya vidhivattatsamāharet |
chāyāśuṣkaṃ prakurvīta cūrṇitaṃ paṭaśodhitam || 1,15.43 ||
karṣamātraṃ lihecchuddhaḥ kṣaudrājyābhyāṃ dinodaye |
valīpalitanirmukto vatsarājjāyate naraḥ || 1,15.44 ||
<brahmavṛkṣamūlaguptadhātrīkalpaḥ>
atisthūlataraṃ dīrghaṃ bhedayedbrahmabhūruham |
mūle trihastaśeṣaṃ ca tadagre gartamāracet || 1,15.45 ||
dhātrīphalāni pakvāni tatra sampūrayetpriye |
tacchinnakāṣṭhaśeṣeṇa tadgartaṃ ca nirodhayet || 1,15.46 ||
kuśaiḥ saṃveṣṭayetsamyagā mūlāgraṃ ca lepayet |
mṛdgomayābhyāṃ matimāṃstato vastreṇa veṣṭayet || 1,15.47 ||
punarmṛdgomayābhyāṃ ca lepayecchoṣayetsudhīḥ |
samyakśuṣke ca paritaḥ karīṣaiḥ paripūrayet || 1,15.48 ||
dahettaṃ śītalībhūte sāndraṃ tatphalamāharet |
bhāṇḍe kṣaudrājyabharite kṣipeddhātrīphalaṃ ca tat || 1,15.49 ||
bhūgṛhe vā nivāte vā pradeśe ca vasansukhī |
yatheṣṭaṃ bhakṣayennityaṃ kṣīrāhārī jitendriyaḥ || 1,15.50 ||
ṣaṇmāsātpaśyati chidraṃ nidhānāni ca bhūtale |
tyajettvacaṃ sarpa iva jīvedbrahmayugaṃ naraḥ || 1,15.51 ||
<2. śvetabrahmavṛkṣakalpaḥ; śvetabrahmavṛkṣapañcāṅgakalpaḥ>
cūrṇayecchvetapālāśapañcāṅgaṃ śoṣayetpriye |
chāyāyāṃ vastragalitaṃ madhunā ca lihetpriye || 1,15.52 ||
karṣamātraṃ trimāsāntarjarāmṛtyuṃ jayennaraḥ |
saṃvatsarācca brahmāyuḥ siddhaḥ sarvagato bhavet || 1,15.53 ||
<śvetabrahmabījakalpaḥ>
śvetapālāśabījāni caikaikāni samāharet |
ajāghṛtenānudinaṃ māsānmṛtyuṃ jarāṃ jayet || 1,15.54 ||
saṃvatsarādbrahmayugaṃ jīvetsiddhipurogamaḥ |
ye proktā raktapālāśatailatvakparṇakādiṣu || 1,15.55 ||
te guṇāḥ śvetapālāśe santi sādhakasiddhidāḥ |
<brahmavṛkṣakalpasiddhiḥ>
vakṣyāmi brahmavṛkṣasya kalpasiddhiṃ manum || 1,15.56 ||
oṃ hrīmamṛtaṃ kuru kuru amṛtamālinyai namaḥ |
pūrvaṃ viṃśatisāhasraṃ puraścaraṇamācaret |
sudhākumbhavarākṣasragjñānamudrāṃ karāṃbujaiḥ || 1,15.57 ||
bibhrāṇāṃ mauktikaṃ pāśaṃ dhyāyedamṛtamālinīm |
grahaṇe'ṣṭottaraśataṃ bhakṣayetsaptavārakam || 1,15.58 ||
candrasūryoparāgādikāleṣvaṣṭasahasrakam |
mantraṃ vinā na siddhiḥ syātkalpakoṭiśatairapi || 1,15.59 ||
samantre śīghrasiddhiḥ syāccarenmantrapuraḥsaraḥ |
<3. muṇḍīkalpaḥ>
atha muṇḍīṃ pravakṣyāmi sādhakeṣṭārthadāyinīm || 1,15.60 ||
caturvidhā bhavetca raktā pītā sitāsitā |
puṣyārke pūrṇamāsyāṃ vā revatyāṃ śravaṇe'pi vā || 1,15.61 ||
uparāgādikāle vā siddhayogeṣu vā haret |
kaidārīṃ muṇḍinīṃ tāṃ ca balipūjanapūrvakam || 1,15.62 ||
samāharetkṛtasnāno maunī mantraṃ samuccaran |
omamṛtodbhavāya amṛtaṃ kuru kuru svāhā hrīṃ saḥ |
mantraṃ dvādaśasāhasraṃ puraścaryāṃ samācaret || 1,15.63 ||
mūlaṃ nālaṃ phalaṃ puṣpaṃ patraṃ pañcāṅgamīritam |
sa pañcāṅgaṃ harenmuṇḍīṃ samyakśītena vāriṇā || 1,15.64 ||
prakṣālya śoṣayettāṃ ca chāyāyāṃ cūrṇayettataḥ |
vastreṇa śodhayetkumbhe nūtane sthāpayetkramāt || 1,15.65 ||
viśuddhadehastaccūrṇaṃ karṣaṃ gopayasā saha |
pibejjīrṇe ca bhaiṣajye ghṛtamādau pibennaraḥ || 1,15.66 ||
tato bhuñjīta lavaṇatailāmlādivivarjitam |
ghṛtapakvaṃ samadhuraṃ ṣaṣṭikānnaṃ payo'dhikam || 1,15.67 ||
ṣaṇmāsājjāyate siddhistvacaṃ sarpa iva tyajet |
palitādivinirmukto divyadṛṣṭirdṛḍhaṃ vapuḥ || 1,15.68 ||
mattanāgabalo dhīro yuvā darpavigrahaḥ |
saṃvatsarātsarvasiddhirbhavedbrahmāyuṣo naraḥ || 1,15.69 ||
ghṛtopayuktā sā muṇḍī pūrvaphaladā bhavet |
<4. devadālīkalpaḥ>
atha vakṣyāmyahaṃ devi devadālīrasāyanam || 1,15.70 ||
śvetā kṛṣṇā ca pītā ca devadālī tridhā matā |
śvetā rogapraśamanī kṛṣṇā pītā viśeṣataḥ || 1,15.71 ||
rasāyane ca phaladā lohakarmaṇi pārvati |
candrasūryoparāgeṣu pūrṇimāyāṃ surārcite || 1,15.72 ||
trayodaśyāṃ kṛṣṇapakṣe pañcamyāṃ vā yathāvidhi |
śubhāhe śubhanakṣatre cāharenmantrapūrvakam || 1,15.73 ||
omamṛtagaṇarudragaṇāntāya svāhā |
aṣṭottaraśataṃ japtvā balipūrvaṃ samāharet |
oṃ namo bhagavate rudrāya phaṭsvāhā |
sādhakasya śikhābandhanamantraḥ |
devadālyāśca pañcāṅgaṃ chāyāyāṃ śoṣayetsudhīḥ || 1,15.74 ||
vastreṇa śodhayetsamyaggavyakṣīreṇa bhakṣayet |
pacettāṃ lauhapātreṇa tato mandāgninā sudhīḥ || 1,15.75 ||
madhvājyābhyāṃ lihetkarṣaṃ śuddhātmā saptavāsaram |
tasya divyā bhavetprajñā rogahṛnmāsasevayā || 1,15.76 ||
dvimāsabhakṣaṇenaiva nidhyādiṃ paśyati dhruvam |
dhātrīphalarasaṃ kṣaudraṃ devadālīrasaṃ ghṛtam || 1,15.77 ||
pratyekaṃ karṣamātraṃ syātpratyahaṃ ca pibellaghu |
ekaviṃśaddinādūrdhvaṃ medhāvī śrutadhārakaḥ || 1,15.78 ||
pañcāṅgaṃ cūrṇayeddevadālyā vastreṇa śodhayet |
śivāmbunā vā payasā gomūtrairvā pibetsadā || 1,15.79 ||
pūrvavatsiddhidā sā syātsarvakuṣṭhāpahāriṇī |
kāmilāplīhapavanaśūlaṃ hanti bhagandarān || 1,15.80 ||
tadraso gandhakopetaḥ sarvalohaṃ vilāpayet |
badhnāti ca rasaṃ samyaksamaṃ mūrchati tatkṣaṇāt || 1,15.81 ||
gandharvalajjāgāndhārīdevadālīrasastathā |
lepanaṃ meṣatailena stambhayedagnimujjvalam || 1,15.82 ||
bījāni devadālyāśca saguḍāni ca mardayet |
tena vartiṃ prakurvīta arśoghnī syādgudāṅkure || 1,15.83 ||
rasena devadālyāśca nayanaṃ tvañjayennaraḥ |
paśyatyasau bhūtajālaṃ tasmai sarvaṃ prayacchati || 1,15.84 ||
ghṛtaṃ dhātrīphalarasaṃ devadālīrasaṃ madhu |
rasaṃ ca lakṣmaṇāyāśca sarvamekapalaṃ pibet || 1,15.85 ||
tridinamṛtukāle tu strīṇāṃ putrapradāyakam |
devadālīrasaṃ kṣīraṃ madhvājyābhyāṃ samaṃ lihet || 1,15.86 ||
puṃsaḥ komalabījānāṃ bījaṃ garbhapradaṃ bhavet |
bhṛṅgarāḍvākucī vahniḥ sarpākṣī devadālikam || 1,15.87 ||
śivāmbunā cānudinaṃ pibetkarṣaṃ maheśvari |
dharitryāḥ paśyati chidraṃ varṣānmṛtyuṃ jarāṃ jayet || 1,15.88 ||
punarnavādevadālyoḥ palaṃ kṣīrayutaṃ pibet |
