śivāmbunā devadālīṃ sasarpākṣīpalaṃ pibet || 1,15.89 ||
pūrvavajjāyate siddhiḥ śīghrameva varānane |
śivāṃbunā devadālyā nirguṇḍyāśca palaṃ pibet || 1,15.90 ||
saṃvatsarājjarāṃ hanyājjīvedācandratārakam |
omamṛtaṃ kuru kuru amṛteśvarāya svāhā |
etanmantraṃ japedādau devadālyupayogake || 1,15.91 ||
<5. śvetārkakalpaḥ; śvetārkamūlakalpaḥ>
atha śvetārkamūlasya kalpaṃ vakṣyāmyahaṃ śṛṇu |
śvetārkamūlaṃ puṣyarkṣe gṛhītvā kāṣṭhavarjitam || 1,15.92 ||
śuddhāṃ tvacaṃ ca chāyāyāṃ śoṣayetpaṭaśodhitam |
cūrṇaṃ kṛtvā karṣamekaṃ sevyaṃ gopayasā saha || 1,15.93 ||
paladvayena ṣaṇmāsātsarvavyādhīñjarāṃ haret |
saṃvatsarādbrahmadinatrayaṃ jīvenna saṃśayaḥ || 1,15.94 ||
<śvetārkaparṇakalpaḥ>
śvetārkaparṇasvarasaṃ bhṛṅgarājarasaṃ samam |
ekīkṛtyātape śoṣyaṃ yāvaccūrṇatvamāpnuyāt || 1,15.95 ||
caturguṇe gavāṃ kṣīre tatpacenmṛduvahninā |
yāvatpiṇḍaṃ bhavettāvatsevyaṃ tatkarṣamātrakam || 1,15.96 ||
gavāṃ kṣīraṃ palaṃ peyaṃ pūrvavatphalamāpnuyāt |
omāṃ haṃsamālini svāhā ayaṃ bhakṣaṇamantraḥ |
<6. hastikarṇīkalpaḥ; hastikarṇīpatrakalpaḥ>
athenduvārasaṃyuktatrayodaśyāṃ samāharet || 1,15.97 ||
hastikarṇīpalāśāni chāyāśuṣkāṇi cūrṇayet |
palaṃ sevyaṃ gavāṃ kṣīraṃ varṣānmṛtyuṃ jarāṃ jayet || 1,15.98 ||
jīvedbrahmāyuṣaṃ martyaḥ siddhasādhyādisevitaḥ |
<hastikarṇīpañcāṅgakalpaḥ>
pañcāṅgaṃ hastikarṇyāśca kuryācchāyāviśoṣitam || 1,15.99 ||
māsaikamudakaiḥ sārdhaṃ karṣaṃ pratyahamaśnuyāt |
sauvīradadhidugdhājyatakrakṣaudrairyathākramam || 1,15.100 ||
ekaikaṃ pratimāsaṃ ca sevyaṃ varṣācca sidhyati |
jīvedbrahmadinaṃ siddho vajrakāyo mahābalaḥ || 1,15.101 ||
omamṛtāya amṛtaṃ gṛhṇāmi svāhā |
ayaṃ grahaṇamantraḥ |
amṛtakuṭījātānāmamṛtaṃ kuru kuru svāhā |
anena mantreṇa pūjayet |
omamṛtodbhavāya amṛtaṃ kuru kuru nityaṃ namo namaḥ |
ayaṃ bhakṣaṇamantraḥ |
<7. rudantīkalpaḥ>
atha vakṣyāmyahaṃ divyaṃ rudantīkalpamuttamam |
caṇapatropamaiḥ patraiḥ puṣpairapi ca tādṛśaiḥ || 1,15.102 ||
rudantī nāma vikhyātā hyadhastājjalavarṣiṇī |
roditīva janāndṛṣṭvā mriyamāṇāngadākulān || 1,15.103 ||
caturvidhā ca sā jñeyā pītā raktā sitāsitā |
śuklapakṣe śubhadine rudantīṃ tāṃ samāharet || 1,15.104 ||
samūlāṃ śoṣayeddhīmān chāyāyāṃ vastraśodhitām |
viśuddhadehaḥ seveta biḍālapadamātrakam || 1,15.105 ||
kṣaudrājyābhyāmanudinaṃ bhuktiḥ kṣīrājyasaṃyutā |
jīrṇāyāṃ saṃyamī bhūtvā varṣāttejobalānvitaḥ || 1,15.106 ||
paramāyurbhavenmartyo jarāvyādhivivarjitaḥ |
tailaṃ katakamūlotthaṃ kuryātpātālayantrake || 1,15.107 ||
garbhayantre'thavā kṣālyaṃ pañcāśadvāramambunā |
nālikerāmbunā vātha kṛtvetthaṃ tailaśodhanam || 1,15.108 ||
pañcāṅgaṃ ca rudantyāśca chāyāśuṣkaṃ vicūrṇayet |
tadardhaṃ musalīcūrṇaṃ musalyardhapalatrayam || 1,15.109 ||
etattricūrṇaṃ saṃmiśraṃ karṣaṃ katakatailataḥ |
lihedanudinaṃ śuddhastadvṛddhiḥ syātpalāvadhi || 1,15.110 ||
saṃvatsarādvajrakāyaḥ sa jīvedbrahmaṇo dinam |
<8. nirguṇḍīkalpaḥ; nirguṇḍīmūlakalpaḥ>
atha vacmi śubhaṃ divyaṃ nirguṇḍīkalpamuttamam || 1,15.111 ||
prātaḥ puṣyaravau grāhyā nirguṇḍīmūlasaṃbhavā |
tvakśoṣaṇīyā chāyāyāṃ taccūrṇaṃ karṣamātrakam || 1,15.112 ||
palamātrājamūtreṇa pibecchuddho'nuvāsaram |
ṣaṇmāsāddivyadehaḥ syānmattanāgabalānvitaḥ || 1,15.113 ||
madhvājyakṣīralulitaṃ taccūrṇaṃ snigdhabhāṇḍake |
pidhāya dhānyarāśau tu māsamekaṃ tu nikṣipet || 1,15.114 ||
māsānte tatsamuddhṛtya śuddhāṅgo dvipalaṃ sadā |
seveta varṣaparyantaṃ jīvedācandratārakam || 1,15.115 ||
athavārdhapalaṃ cūrṇaṃ tadīyaṃ saghṛtaṃ pibet |
pūrvavajjāyate siddhirjarārogavivarjitaḥ || 1,15.116 ||
vacāmuṇḍīnimbavarāguḍūcībhṛṅgarāṭsamāḥ |
eṣāṃ samāṃśaṃ saṃyojya nirguṇḍīmūlacūrṇataḥ || 1,15.117 ||
kṣaudrājyābhyāṃ liheddhastādanvahaṃ palamātrakam |
āyurviriñcitridinaṃ bhavenmṛtyujarojjhitam || 1,15.118 ||
nirguṇḍīmūlacūrṇaṃ tu krimighnakaṭukaiḥ samam |
palamātramajākṣīrairnityaṃ śuddhaḥ pibedbudhaḥ || 1,15.119 ||
valīpalitanirmukto nirgadaḥ syāttrimāsataḥ |
<nirguṇḍīpatrakalpaḥ>
nirguṇḍīpatrajadrāvaṃ bhāṇḍe mṛdvagninā pacet || 1,15.120 ||
guḍavatpākamāpannaṃ taṃ pibenniṣkamātrakam |
dine dine niṣkavṛddhiryāvaddvipalikaṃ bhavet || 1,15.121 ||
tena vāntirvirekaḥ syānniryānti krimayaḥ param |
apānato dehagatā mukhanāsākṣikarṇataḥ || 1,15.122 ||
kṣayakuṣṭhādirogāśca naśyanti munivāsarāt |
māsatraye jarāṃ hanti jīvedvarṣaśatatrayam || 1,15.123 ||
<nirguṇḍīpañcāṅgakalpaḥ>
pañcāṅgacūrṇaṃ nirguṇḍyāstilatailena sevitam |
niṣkādipalaparyantaṃ trimāsena jarāṃ jayet || 1,15.124 ||
aśītiṃ vātajānrogān kuṣṭhānapi galāmayān |
āsyārtyupakuśādīṃśca jayettatparṇacarvaṇāt || 1,15.125 ||
sindhuvārakapañcāṅgacūrṇaṃ madhughṛtaplutam |
palamātraṃ lihetprātastato jīrṇe gavāṃ payaḥ || 1,15.126 ||
pibedyathānalabalaṃ bhuñjītāgnau krameṇa ca |
nirvāte nivaseddhīmānsiddhimāpnoti vatsarāt || 1,15.127 ||
siddhadehasya pūrveṇa mriyete viṣapāradau |
kāle kāle yathāprāptaṃ pañcāṅgaṃ sindhuvārakāt || 1,15.128 ||
chāyāyāṃ śoṣitaṃ cūrṇaṃ trimadhutriphalāyutam |
snigdhabhāṇḍe dhānyarāśau trimāsaṃ sthāpayettataḥ || 1,15.129 ||
niṣkādipalaparyantaṃ sevetākarṣakaṃ sudhīḥ |
sarvavyādhivinirmukto jarāmaraṇavarjitaḥ || 1,15.130 ||
oṃ namo māyāgaṇapataye kuberāya svāhā |
ayaṃ bhakṣaṇamantraḥ |
<9. śunakaśālmalīkalpaḥ>
atha vakṣyāmi deveśi kalpaṃ śunakaśālmaleḥ |
aṣṭamyāṃ kṛṣṇapakṣasya snātvā rātrau samāhitaḥ || 1,15.131 ||
āveṣṭya kṛṣṇasūtraiśca vṛkṣaṃ śunakaśālmalim |
tatrāghoraṃ japeddhīraḥ sahasravasusaṃmitam || 1,15.132 ||
<9. śunakaśālmalīmūlakalpaḥ>
tanmūlavalkalaṃ grāhyaṃ chāyāyāṃ śoṣayettataḥ |
cūrṇaṃ kṛtvā tu madhvājyaiḥ khādetprātaḥ palaṃ palam || 1,15.133 ||
varṣādvalijarāmuktaḥ sa jīvedbrahmaṇo dinam |
<śunakaśālmalīpuṣpakalpaḥ>
tasyāṣṭādaśa puṣpāṇi gokṣīre'ṣṭapale pacet || 1,15.134 ||
puṣpavarjyaṃ pibetkṣāramevaṃ ca prativāsaram |
māsena divyadehaḥ syājjīvedbrahmadinatrayam || 1,15.135 ||
<śunakaśālmalīphalakalpaḥ>
tatphalaṃ tu gavāṃ kṣīre pācayetkāntapātrake |
phalaṃ tyaktvā pibetkṣīraṃ dīpte'gnau kṣīrabhojanam || 1,15.136 ||
māsaṣaṭkaprayogeṇa divyadeho bhavennaraḥ |
jīvetkalpāntaparyantaṃ vāyuvegī mahābalaḥ || 1,15.137 ||
tasya mūtrapurīṣābhyāṃ śulbaṃ svarṇaṃ bhaveddhruvam |
<10. pathyākalpaḥ; pathyotpattiḥ>
athātaḥ sampravakṣyāmi pathyākalpamanūpamam || 1,15.138 ||
yena rogā vinaśyanti sidhyanti ca manorathāḥ |
pītvāmṛtaṃ śacīnāthaḥ prītyā śacyai samāpibat || 1,15.139 ||
tayorhṛṣṭamukhāmbhojagalitāmṛtabindavaḥ |
saptadhā bhuvi te jātā mahāvīryāḥ sthirāyuṣaḥ || 1,15.140 ||
te saptadeśeṣūtpannāḥ saptadhā nāma dhārakāḥ |
vindhyadeśe kānyakubje saurāṣṭre himavadgirau || 1,15.141 ||
gaṅgātaṭe ca kāśmīre vainyadeśe yathākramam |
vijayā rohiṇī caiva pūtā syāttrivṛtāmṛtā || 1,15.142 ||
jīvantī tvabhayā jātā deśe deśe yathākramam |
uttamā madhyamā nīcāḥ svayaṃ pakvāstu śobhanāḥ || 1,15.143 ||
harītakyāṃ rasāḥ pañca vidyante lavaṇojjhitāḥ |
svādvamlatiktakaṭukatuvarāśca yathākramam || 1,15.144 ||
syurmajjasnāyuvṛntatvaṅmāṃseṣu kramaśo rasāḥ |
alābukarṇī vṛttā ca caturaṅgyalpacarmakā || 1,15.145 ||
pañcāsrā māṃsalā svarṇavarṇā kṛṣṇā smṛtāḥ kramāt |
yā majjantī jale grāhyā gurvī snigdhā ghanāghanāḥ || 1,15.146 ||
dvikarṣamātrā sā śreṣṭhā karṣamātrā tu madhyamā |
ardhakarṣā bhavennīcā pathyā phalamudāhṛtam || 1,15.147 ||
krimijuṣṭā vahnidagdhā naṣṭā paṅkajalārdritā |
sphuṭitā coṣarasthā ca varjanīyā harītakī || 1,15.148 ||
<pathyākalpaḥ>
śuddhadeśe puṇyadine grahaṇe candrasūryayoḥ |
gandhādyaiḥ pūjayitvādau prārthayitvā japetsudhīḥ || 1,15.149 ||
gāyatrīśatamāvṛttya pathyāmevaṃ samāharet |
śuṇṭhyā vātha guḍenāpi saindhavenāthavā śivām || 1,15.150 ||
seveta dīpanaṃ tena karotyāmavināśanam |
kṣaudreṇa vā guḍenāpi pippalyā nāgareṇa vā || 1,15.151 ||
saindhavenāthavā sevyā śuddhāṅgaḥ prativāsaram |
dve dve pathye dvādaśābdaṃ tena jīvecchataṃ samāḥ || 1,15.152 ||
dviniṣkacūrṇaṃ pathyāyā niṣkaikaṃ lohabhasma ca |
lolayitvā goghṛtena kāntapātre ca lepayet || 1,15.153 ||
adhomukhīkṛtaṃ pātraṃ sthāpayettaddivāniśam |
prātaḥ śuddhavapurlehyaṃ jarāṃ mṛtyuṃ jayetsudhīḥ || 1,15.154 ||
śālmalīṃ chidrayitvādau tadantarnikṣipecchivāḥ |
ā ṣaṇmāsaṃ parastāstu grāhyā caikaikaśo'nvaham || 1,15.155 ||
seveta pathyāṃ śuddhāṅgastasya mṛtyurjarā na hi |
<11. āmalakīkalpaḥ>
atha vakṣyāmi deveśi kalpamāmalakībhavam || 1,15.156 ||
caitrādau grāhayeddhātryāḥ supakvāni phalāni ca |
harītakīvadgrāhyāni chāyāśuṣkāṇi cūrṇayet || 1,15.157 ||
karṣaṃ jalena vājyena madhunā vā phalaṃ niśi |
vardhayejjāṭharaṃ vahnimindriyāṇāṃ prasādakṛt || 1,15.158 ||
dhātrīcūrṇaṃ lohabhasma kṣaudrājyālulitaṃ lihet |
valīpalitamṛtyughnaṃ vatsarātpūrvavadvidhiḥ || 1,15.159 ||
kṣīrapakvaṃ ca tadbhakṣyaṃ pūrvavatsiddhidāyakaḥ |
dhātrīcūrṇaṃ bhṛṅgacūrṇaṃ madhvājyasahitaṃ lihet || 1,15.160 ||
valīpalitanirmukto vatsarādbhavati dhruvam |
niṃbāmalakayoścūrṇaṃ madhvājyasahitaṃ lihet || 1,15.161 ||
pūrvavacca phalaṃ devi nātra kāryā vicāraṇā |
<12. triphalākalpaḥ>
atha bravīmi te devi triphalāyā rasāyanam || 1,15.162 ||
ekaṃ harītakībhāgaṃ dvibhāgaṃ ca vibhītakam |
caturbhāgaṃ tathā dhātrī sarvamekatra cūrṇayet || 1,15.163 ||
pippalyā vā tugākṣīryā saindhavairmadhukena vā |
kṣaudreṇa vā sarpiṣā vā vacayā sitayāthavā || 1,15.164 ||
suvarṇai rajataiḥ śulbairvaṅgairnāgaistathāyasaiḥ |
saṃyuktā triphalā līḍhā jarāmaraṇanāśinī || 1,15.165 ||
triphalāyāḥ samāṃśāstu yojyāścaikaikaśastu tāḥ |
pippalyādyāstu sauvarṇabhasmādīnāṃ ca kathyate || 1,15.166 ||
sauvarṇaṃ paṇamātraṃ ca dvipaṇaṃ rājataṃ bhavet |
paṇārdhaṃ śulbacūrṇaṃ ca dvipaṇaṃ nāgavaṅgajam || 1,15.167 ||
cūrṇaṃ tathāyasaṃ proktaṃ paṇapañcakamātrakam |
evaṃ yastriphalāsevī jīvedācandratārakam || 1,15.168 ||
ekāṃ harītakīṃ prātarbhukteḥ prāgdvivibhītakam |
caturāmalakaṃ rātrau sarve te ghṛtapācitāḥ || 1,15.169 ||
sevetāvatsaraṃ dhīmānvalīpalitavarjitaḥ |
annodakena saṃpeṣya kāntapātre lihenniśi || 1,15.170 ||
sakṣaudraṃ tallihetprātarāyurārogyavardhanam |
triphalāṃ bhāvayetpūrvaṃ khadirāsanayūṣataḥ || 1,15.171 ||
triḥ saptavāraṃ deveśi tataḥ kṣaudraghṛtāplutām |
sevetānudinaṃ karṣaṃ varṣādindrāyuṣo bhavet || 1,15.172 ||
viḍaṅgabhṛṅgakhadirabrahmavṛkṣarasaiḥ pṛthak |
pṛthaksaptadinaṃ gharme bhāvayettriphalāṃ priye || 1,15.173 ||
bhakṣayedguḍasarpirbhyāṃ karṣaṃ varṣātsuropamaḥ |
triphalāyāḥ śataphalaṃ cūrṇaṃ bhṛṅgarasaiḥ purā || 1,15.174 ||
triḥ saptavāsaraṃ bhāvyaṃ lihenmadhughṛtānvitam |
palārdhaṃ pratyahaṃ prātarjīrṇe'dyāddadhibhaktakam || 1,15.175 ||
varṣātprasannadṛṣṭiśca jīvetṣaṭśatavatsaram |
mahābalaḥ kṛṣṇakeśaḥ smṛtibuddhisamanvitaḥ || 1,15.176 ||
yuvā śatastrīrantā ca dhīraḥ sa subhago bhavet |
<13. śuṇṭhīkalpaḥ>
atha vakṣyāmi girije divyaṃ śuṇṭhīrasāyanam || 1,15.177 ||
śreṣṭhaṃ nāgaramādāya cūrṇayetpaṭagālitam |
guḍaṃ cāsya samaṃ yojyaṃ madhunā goghṛtena ca || 1,15.178 ||
snigdhabhāṇḍe vinikṣipya dhānyarāśau suyantritam |
dvimaṇḍalātsamāhṛtya śuddhāṅgaḥ puṇyavāsare || 1,15.179 ||
karṣaṃ karṣaṃ lihennityaṃ saptāhādrogavarjitaḥ |
ṣaṇmāsamupabhuñjāno jīvedvarṣaśatadvayam || 1,15.180 ||
buddhyā vācaspatisamaḥ purāṇāgamaśāstravit |
mayānubhūtaṃ deveśi kathitaṃ tava sauhṛdāt || 1,15.181 ||
<14. pippalīkalpaḥ>
atha priye pravakṣyāmi pippalīnāṃ rasāyanam |
pippalīṃ śodhayetpūrvaṃ kiṃśukakṣāravāriṇā || 1,15.182 ||
saptadhā ca tataḥ kuryāttāsāṃ ca ghṛtabharjanam |
tataḥ seveta śuddhāṅgaḥ pañcāṣṭau daśa saptadhā || 1,15.183 ||
varṣamekaṃ tu madhvājyai rogāstasya na santi ca |
valīpalitanirmukto jīvecca śaradaḥ śatam || 1,15.184 ||
prātaḥ prāgbhojanātpaścādbhakṣayettritripippalīḥ |
varṣādvyādhiṃ jarāṃ hanti śatāyuṣyamavāpnuyāt || 1,15.185 ||
ekadvitrikrameṇaiva vardhayeddaśavāsaram |
hrāsayetpippalīstadvaddhrāsavṛddhī punaḥ punaḥ || 1,15.186 ||
gokṣīreṇa yutaṃ yāvatpippalīnāṃ sahasrakam |
tāvatseveta śuddhāṅgo jarārogavivarjitaḥ || 1,15.187 ||
ajākṣīreṇa saṃpeṣya pibedyuktā balādhikā |
kṣīraśṛtā madhyaphalā pūrvavaddhrāsavṛddhayaḥ || 1,15.188 ||
yāvatsahasradvitayaṃ tāvatsevyaṃ rasāyanam |
kāsaśvāsakṣayāḥ pāṇḍuplīhaśoṇitamārutāḥ || 1,15.189 ||
mehārśograhaṇīśophahidhmavamigalagrahāḥ |
rasāyanena pippalyā naśyanti viṣamajvarāḥ || 1,15.190 ||
jīvedvarṣaśataṃ pūrṇaṃ valīpalitavarjitaḥ |
<15. citrakakalpaḥ>
atha citrakakalpaṃ te vakṣyāmi śṛṇu pārvati || 1,15.191 ||
citrakastrividho jñeyaḥ kṛṣṇo raktaḥ sitaḥ śive |
kṛṣṇo rasāyane rakto lohe roge sito bhavet || 1,15.192 ||
śreṣṭhamadhyakanīyāṃso na hemante samāharet |
vasante kṛṣṇapañcamyāṃ kṛṣṇāmbaradharaḥ śuciḥ || 1,15.193 ||
kṛṣṇagandhākṣataiḥ puṣpaiḥ pūjayetkṛṣṇasūtrakaiḥ |
pariveṣṭya ca pūrvedyurdadyātkṛṣṇaudanaṃ balim || 1,15.194 ||
prātarmaunī khanecchuddhaḥ samūlaṃ citrakaṃ haret |
na goghrātaṃ padā spṛṣṭaṃ śucau deśe vinikṣipet || 1,15.195 ||
kṣiptaṃ kṣīrāntare kṛṣṇaṃ vahnau kṣīraṃ tu kṛṣṇati |
raktacitraṃ parīkṣyaivaṃ kṣīraṃ raktatvamāpnuyāt || 1,15.196 ||
evaṃ ca raktacitrasya raktapuṣpākṣatādibhiḥ |
kṛṣṇaṃ vā lohitaṃ vāpi samūlamapi khaṇḍayet || 1,15.197 ||
saptadhāmalakāmbhobhirbhāvayecchoṣayetkramāt |
saṃcūrṇya tārkṣītālatvakcitrakatriphalāḥ samāḥ || 1,15.198 ||
eṣāṃ samaṃ cāgnicūrṇaṃ sarvaṃ bhāṇḍe nave kṣipet |
tataḥ śuddhaḥ śubhadine niṣkaṃ niṣkaṃ ghṛtāplutam || 1,15.199 ||
bhakṣayenmāsamātreṇa vraṇakuṣṭhādikṛntanam |
dantakeśanakhā yānti patanaṃ ca punarbhavam || 1,15.200 ||
valīpalitanirmukto jīvecca śaradaḥ śatam |
evaṃ ca raktacitrasya yojanā ca phalaṃ tathā || 1,15.201 ||
<16. bhallātakīkalpaḥ>
atha bhallātakīkalpaṃ śṛṇu vakṣyāmi pārvati |
pakvaṃ navaṃ sutīkṣṇaṃ ca bhallātakaphalaṃ haret || 1,15.202 ||
gokṣīre phalamekaṃ tu sūcībhinnaṃ pacetsudhīḥ |
madhvājyaśarkarāyuktaṃ phalaṃ tyaktvā payaḥ pibet || 1,15.203 ||
ekadvitrikrameṇaivaṃ vardhayedekaviṃśatim |
phalāni ca dinānyekaviṃśatiḥ kramaśaḥ priye || 1,15.204 ||
sarve rogā vinaśyanti ṣaṇmāsāddivyavigrahaḥ |
<17. bhūmikadambakalpaḥ>
atha bhūmikadambasya kalpaṃ vakṣyāmi pārvati || 1,15.205 ||
trividhaḥ sa tu vijñeyaḥ śveto raktaśca mecakaḥ |
śuklapakṣe ca puṣyārke vidhivattaṃ samāharet || 1,15.206 ||
samūlaṃ śoṣayettaṃ ca chāyāyāṃ cūrṇayettataḥ |
taccūrṇaṃ karṣamājyena lihecchuddhavapuḥ priye || 1,15.207 ||
tasmiñjīrṇe prakurvīta kṣīrānnaṃ vijitendriyaḥ |
naśyanti valayastasya ṣaṇmāsātpalitāni ca || 1,15.208 ||
saṃvatsaraprayogeṇa jīvedvarṣaśatatrayam |
<18. punarnavākalpaḥ>
divyaṃ punarnavākalpaṃ vakṣyāmi śṛṇu pārvati || 1,15.209 ||
punarnavākhyā dvividhā lohitā dhavalā tathā |
rase rasāyane śvetā roge raktā praśasyate || 1,15.210 ||
puṣyārke śvetaśophaghnīṃ samūlāmāharetsudhīḥ |
chāyāśuṣkaṃ cūrṇayitvā gokṣīreṇa palārdhakam || 1,15.211 ||
palaṃ vātha pibetprātaḥ śuddhāṅgo vatsarāvadhi |
yuvā bhavati vṛddho'pi sa jīveccharadaḥ śatam || 1,15.212 ||
gokṣīreṇa ca tanmūlaṃ kṣīrakṣīṇaṃ pacetpriye |
cūrṇīkṛtya ca tanmūlaṃ pakvīkṛtaguḍe samam || 1,15.213 ||
goghṛtaṃ ca kṣipettatra tvekīkṛtyāvatārayet |
palaṃ cānudinaṃ lehyaṃ kṣīrānnāśī jitendriyaḥ || 1,15.214 ||
saṃvatsaraprayogeṇa valīpalitavarjitaḥ |
tatsevakasya naśyanti viṣāṇi vividhāni ca || 1,15.215 ||
tvagdoṣaḥ kaphapāṇḍvādyā audarā gulmapāyujāḥ |
naśyanti sakalā rogāśchardihikkākṣikarṇajāḥ || 1,15.216 ||
puṣpitāṃ phalitāṃ pakvāmādāya ca punarnavām |
khaṇḍitāṃ nāgarakvāthasusvinnāṃ kṣīrapeṣitām || 1,15.217 ||
pibeddivā ca cūrṇānte niśi kṣīraghṛtāśanaḥ |
ṣaṇmāsātsiddhimāpnoti yoṣicchatarato bhavet || 1,15.218 ||
<19. bhṛṅgarājakalpaḥ>
atha śrībhṛṅgarājasya kalpaṃ vacmi maheśvari |
samūlaṃ tacca puṣyārke samāhṛtyātha śoṣayet || 1,15.219 ||
chāyāyāṃ pūrṇitaṃ kṛtvā karṣaṃ sauvīraloḍitam |
prātaḥ pibecchuddhadeho māsādrogānvyapohati || 1,15.220 ||
tataḥ ṣaṇmāsayogena valīpalitakhaṇḍanam |
ghṛtakṣīrāśano nityaṃ jīvedvarṣaśatatrayam || 1,15.221 ||
yuktaḥ kṛṣṇatilairardhairbhṛṅgarājasya pallavam |
upayuñjīta ṣaṇmāsaṃ valīpalitakṛntanam || 1,15.222 ||
bhṛṅgarājaphalaṃ puṣpaṃ patraṃ mūlaṃ ca cūrṇayet |
etaccūrṇasya sadṛśāṃścitrādyānparikalpayet || 1,15.223 ||
citraviśvakaṇābilvapathyādhātrīviḍaṅgakam |
marīcaṃ vyādhighātaṃ ca cakramardasya bījakam || 1,15.224 ||
lodhrasarṣaparājyaśca cūrṇitāśca samāhṛtāḥ |
ekīkṛtyaiva bhṛṅgasya rasena paribhāvayet || 1,15.225 ||
saptadhā ca tataḥ sarvaṃ cūrṇīkṛtya punaḥ priye |
karṣaṃ gopayasā sārdhaṃ śuddhakoṣṭho lihetpriye || 1,15.226 ||
ṣaṇmāsājjāyate siddhirvalīpalitavarjitaḥ |
pūrvoktavadvidhiḥ proktaḥ svarṇabhṛṅgasya pārvati || 1,15.227 ||
oṃ namo bhagavate rudrāya tiṣṭha tiṣṭha saṃgṛhāṇa svāhā |
ayaṃ grahaṇamantraḥ |
oṃ namo rudrāya amṛtātmane svāhā |
ayamāloḍanamantraḥ |
omuttiṣṭhottiṣṭha kalyāṇi svāhā |
ayaṃ bhakṣaṇamantraḥ |
<20. kumārīkalpaḥ>
atha vakṣyāmyahaṃ devi kumārīkalpamuttamam |
nadītīre'thavā grāme nagare'bdhitaṭe'thavā || 1,15.228 ||
mṛdukarṇakapatrāṇi pītānyahisamāni ca |
pravālasamapuṣpāṇi sakledahastīni ca || 1,15.229 ||
yasyāḥ patrāṇi tiṣṭhanti sā kumārīti kathyate |
śuciḥ puṇyadine kanyāṃ sopavāsaḥ samāharet || 1,15.230 ||
arcayitvā baliṃ dattvā kumārīṃ siddhidāyinīm |
śuddhakoṣṭhaḥ prage khādeccaturaṅgulamātrataḥ || 1,15.231 ||
evaṃ māsaṃ tataḥ khādyaṃ yathā vṛddhiḥ śanaiḥ śanaiḥ |
atyantaṃ varjayenmadhyaṃ khādyaṃ bhavati sarvadā || 1,15.232 ||
svaśaktyanuguṇaṃ sevyaṃ svecchāhāravihāravān |
snigdhairmāṃsarasakṣīrairyuktyā sevyā kumārikā || 1,15.233 ||
haratyakhilarogāṃśca rājayakṣmādikānpriye |
kinnaraiḥ sadṛśaṃ gāyedgṛdhradṛṣṭirmahābalaḥ || 1,15.234 ||
snigdhakeśaśca matimān bālādityasamaprabhaḥ |
daśanāgabalopetaḥ samīrasadṛśo gatau || 1,15.235 ||
valīpalitanirmuktaḥ ṣoḍaśābdavayā bhavet |
evaṃ varṣaprayogeṇa bhavatyetairguṇairyutaḥ || 1,15.236 ||
yastu dvādaśavarṣāntaṃ seveta sa surāsuraiḥ |
mahoragairavadhyaśca triṃśannāgabalānvitaḥ || 1,15.237 ||
mayūradṛṣṭiḥ sarvajñaḥ sarvaśāstraviśāradaḥ |
vidyādharo bhavenmartyo nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 1,15.238 ||
kumāryā dalamādāya hastarkṣe sādhake'hani |
prātaḥ pratyahamāsvādya śuciragnibalaṃ yathā || 1,15.239 ||
tanmajjāṃ vā lihedyastu madhvājyavyoṣasaṃyutām |
māsaṃ seveta niyamātsarvarogaiḥ pramucyate || 1,15.240 ||
ṣaṇmāsamupayuñjāno valīpalitavarjitaḥ |
tasyāḥ kledaṃ samānīya vyoṣakṣaudrājyasaṃyutam || 1,15.241 ||
svinnaṃ mṛdvagninā sevyaṃ kṣīrāhāro jitendriyaḥ |
māsādrogā vinaśyanti dvimāsāddṛḍhavigrahaḥ || 1,15.242 ||
divyadṛṣṭiśca tejasvī dvimāsāddviṣahṛdbhavet |
ṣaṇmāsayogādvṛddho'pi yuvā syātsatyamīśvari || 1,15.243 ||
evaṃ varṣopayogena jīveddviśatavatsaram |
tasyā dalaṃ yugaiḥ sārdhaṃ bhakṣayecchuddhakoṣṭhavān || 1,15.244 ||
valīpalitanāśaḥ syādvatsarānnātra saṃśayaḥ |
<21. nīlīkalpaḥ>
atha devi pravakṣyāmi mahānīlīrasāyanam || 1,15.245 ||
puṣyārke śucirādadyānmahānīlīṃ vidhānataḥ |
mūlapuṣpaphalopetāṃ chāyāyāṃ śoṣayetpriye || 1,15.246 ||
paṭacūrṇaṃ dviniṣkaṃ syānniṣkamabhraṃ ca yojayet |
sarvaṃ ca madhunāloḍya snigdhabhāṇḍe vinikṣipet || 1,15.247 ||
dhānyarāśau nyasenmāsamuddhṛtya ca tataḥ priye |
śuddhakoṣṭho lihetprātarbiḍālapadamātrakam || 1,15.248 ||
ṣaṇmāsājjāyate martyo valīpalitavarjitaḥ |
mahānīlīṃ ca kṛṣṇābhraṃ musalīmamṛtālatām || 1,15.249 ||
samāṃśaṃ cūrṇitānkṛtvā madhunā karṣamālihet |
evaṃ varṣopayogena jīrṇo'pi taruṇāyate || 1,15.250 ||
balavānmatimāndhīro jīvedvarṣaśatadvayam |
abhrakaṃ ca kumārīṃ ca kākajaṅghāṃ śatāvarīm || 1,15.251 ||
etatsamāṃ mahānīlīṃ cūrṇayetsnigdhabhāṇḍake |
madhunā sahitaṃ saptarātryante ca samuddharet || 1,15.252 ||
karṣaṃ lihetkṣīrabhojī varṣādvalijarāṃ jayet |
<22. musalīkalpaḥ>
atha bravīmi deveśi musalīkalpamuttamam || 1,15.253 ||
svarṇapuṣpī ca kharjūrapatravatpatraśobhitā |
antaḥ śvetā ca nārācamūlā sā musalī smṛtā || 1,15.254 ||
tanmūlaṃ ca samuddhṛtya chāyāśuṣkaṃ ca cūrṇayet |
karṣaṃ madhvājyalulitaṃ prātaḥ śuddhatanurlihet || 1,15.255 ||
jīrṇe dugdhānnabhojī syādrūkṣānnaṃ varjayetpriye |
ṣaṣṭhe māsi bhavedbālo vṛddho'pi balabuddhimān || 1,15.256 ||
payasā musalīcūrṇaṃ dadhnā vāpi pibecchuciḥ |
varṣādyauvanamāpnoti śatastrīgamane paṭuḥ || 1,15.257 ||
<23. indravallīkalpaḥ>
athendravallīkalpaṃ ca vyākhyāmi śṛṇu pārvati |
mayoditā surendrasya daityānāṃ vijayāya ca || 1,15.258 ||
tadā prabhṛti lokeṣu khyātā sā cendravallikā |
tāmimāṃ bhajatāṃ puṃsāṃ dehasiddhirbhaveddhruvam || 1,15.259 ||
samūlāṃ tāṃ samuddhṛtya chāyāśuṣkāṃ vicūrṇayet |
taccūrṇaṃ dvādaśapalaṃ vacācūrṇaṃ ca tatsamam || 1,15.260 ||
vraṇaghnīcūrṇametasya samāṃśaṃ trikaṭūdbhavam |
ṣaṭpalaṃ sarvamekatra kṛtvā bhāṇḍe vinikṣipet || 1,15.261 ||
karṣaṃ karṣaṃ madhuyutaṃ prātaḥ prātarlihetsadā |
ṣaṇmāsātsarvarogaghnaṃ vatsarāddehasiddhidam || 1,15.262 ||
bhūyo bhūyaśca ṣaṇmāsānnavaṃ cūrṇaṃ prakalpayet |
<24. jyotirdrumakalpaḥ>
atha jyotirdrumasyāpi pañcāṅgānyāharetpriye || 1,15.263 ||
triḥ saptarātraṃ sakṣaudrāṇyāśrayeddhānyarāśike |
bhakṣayedroganirmuktastejasvī dehasiddhibhāk || 1,15.264 ||
<25. aśvagandhākalpaḥ>
athāśvagandhākandaṃ ca pautrīkoraṇṭayoḥ samam |
cūrṇitaṃ madhusarpirbhyāṃ karṣaṃ prātarlihecchuciḥ || 1,15.265 ||
saṃvatsaraprayogeṇa triśatāyurbhavennaraḥ |
<26. jyotiṣmatīkalpaḥ>
atha jyotiṣmatīkalpaṃ vakṣyāmi śṛṇu pārvati || 1,15.266 ||
divyā jyotiṣmatī vallī taptakāñcanasannibhā |
bahupratānā svarṇābhā phalabījā śubhapradā || 1,15.267 ||
āṣāḍhe śuklapakṣe ca śubharkṣe śubhavāsare |
kuṅkumāktena sūtreṇa tāṃ vallīṃ pariveṣṭayet || 1,15.268 ||
raktagandhākṣataiḥ puṣpairarcayitvā praṇamya ca |
raktamālyāmbaradharaḥ kuṅkumāgarucarcitaḥ || 1,15.269 ||
tāṃbūlacarvaṇaṃ kurvanhṛṣṭo niyatamānasaḥ |
abhīṣṭadevatāmantraistārāmantreṇa vārcayet || 1,15.270 ||
abhīṣṭadevatāṃ dhyātvā tataścodaṅmukhaḥ priye |
pakvabījāni gṛhṇīyādātape śoṣayettataḥ || 1,15.271 ||
tvagvarjyāni nidhāyādau cūrṇitāni viśeṣataḥ |
tebhyastailaṃ samāhṛtya tattailasadṛśaṃ payaḥ || 1,15.272 ||
pādāṃśaṃ madhu saṃyojya tailaśeṣaṃ pacecchanaiḥ |
tasminkarpūratakkolatvagjātīphalameva ca || 1,15.273 ||
etāni samabhāgāni cūrṇayitvā ca kovidaḥ |
tattailaṃ ṣoḍaśapalaṃ taccūrṇaṃ palamātrakam || 1,15.274 ||
ekīkṛtyāvaloḍyātha snehabhāṇḍe vinikṣipet |
triḥ saptavāsaraṃ dhānyarāśau bhāṇḍe vinikṣipet || 1,15.275 ||
daśāhamathavā triṃśatṣaṣṭirvā navatistathā |
adhikaṃ vā yathāyogaṃ paścāttailaṃ samāharet || 1,15.276 ||
śuddhakoṣṭhaḥ śubhadine kṛtadevadvijārcanaḥ |
sūryodaye pibeddhīro binduvṛddhyā kramātpriye || 1,15.277 ||
palāntaṃ sādhakastena niḥsaṃjñatvamavāpyate |
mandaṃ mandaṃ labdhasaṃjño muhuḥ krandati nandati || 1,15.278 ||
rodityeva muhuḥ kṛcchrāllabdhasaṃjño bhavennaraḥ |
gokṣīraṃ pāyayetpaścātsakṣaudraṃ śrāntacetase || 1,15.279 ||
kramādevaṃ bhavetsvasthaḥ sādhako'sau na saṃśayaḥ |
ṣaṣṭikānnaṃ sagokṣīraṃ taile jīrṇe ca bhojanam || 1,15.280 ||
evaṃ kuryātpratidinaṃ māsamekaṃ nirantaram |
māsātsūryasamaḥ sākṣāttejasā mantraśāstravit || 1,15.281 ||
sāṅgopāṅgaśrutiṃ vetti tvanadhīto'pi mānavaḥ |
madhyāhne bhāskaraṃ paśyetpūrṇacandramivāparam || 1,15.282 ||
vainateyasamā dṛṣṭirdivā paśyati tārakāḥ |
annavajjīryate kṣvelaṃ supto'pyākarṇayeddhvanim || 1,15.283 ||
aṣṭādaśavidhaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ sarvarogānvināśayet |
phalaṃ dvitīyamāsasya śṛṇu vakṣyāmi bhairavi || 1,15.284 ||
gandharvoragarakṣobhiḥ sevyate tailasevakaḥ |
mahāpātakakartāro devatāpitṛnindakāḥ || 1,15.285 ||
samayācārarahitāsta ete muktakilbiṣāḥ |
amuṣya sevayā muktāḥ prayānti paramāṃ gatim || 1,15.286 ||
phalaṃ tṛtīyamāsasya śṛṇu pārvati vakṣyate |
devāśca divyā ṛṣayo vasavo'ṣṭau mahoragāḥ || 1,15.287 ||
amuṣya sevāṃ kurvanti priyaṃ nityahitaṃ tathā |
tasya mūtraṃ tāmraghaṭe pūrayitvā khanedbhuvam || 1,15.288 ||
tadgarte vihitaṃ kṛtvā ṣaṇmāsātkanakaṃ bhavet |
caturthamāsasya phalaṃ śarvāṇi śṛṇu sāṃpratam || 1,15.289 ||
adṛśyo'sau bhavenmartyaḥ sarvaiśvaryayuto balī |
daśabrahmadinaṃ jīvetsarvalokagatau paṭuḥ || 1,15.290 ||
tanmūtramalagharmāmbūdvartanaistāmralepanam |
kuryādvahnau pratapanaṃ tacchulbaṃ kāñcanaṃ bhavet || 1,15.291 ||
atha pañcamamāse tu sadehaḥ khecaro bhavet |
sākṣādbrahmā bhaveddevi śṛṇu ṣāṇmāsikaṃ phalam || 1,15.292 ||
mahāsiddhaiḥ parivṛtaḥ sarvalokān yadṛcchayā |
vicarecchivatulyaḥ syānmahātejā mahāyaśāḥ || 1,15.293 ||
atha saptamamāsasya phalaṃ kalyāṇi vakṣyate |
jitāriṣaḍvargamanāḥ sarvabhūtahite rataḥ || 1,15.294 ||
viṣṇvāyuṣyamavāpnoti puṇyāpuṇyavivarjitaḥ |
vicarecca mahāvīryaḥ śrīmān viṣṇurivāparaḥ || 1,15.295 ||
aṣṭamāsaphalaṃ vacmi śṛṇu tvaṃ sarvamaṅgale |
matpriyo divyakarmā ca sarvajño muktimāpnuyāt || 1,15.296 ||
phalaṃ navamamāsasya śṛṇu kātyāyani priye |
dvitīyacintāmaṇivadvīro'sau kāmarūpavān || 1,15.297 ||
brahmaviṣṇvindracandrāṇāṃ rūpaṃ sṛjati sādhakaḥ |
manasā cintitaṃ yadyattattatkartuṃ svayaṃ prabhuḥ || 1,15.298 ||
etadrasāyanaṃ divyaṃ sukaraṃ mokṣasādhanam |
puṇyaṃ paramaguhyaṃ ca gopanīyaṃ tvayā priye || 1,15.299 ||
etasya sadṛśaṃ nāsti devānāmapi durlabham |
bahunātra kimuktena tvatpriyārthaṃ mayoditam || 1,15.300 ||
rasāyanamidaṃ divyaṃ na kurvanti ca mānavāḥ |
ajñānopahatāḥ kecitkecinnāstīti śaṅkayā || 1,15.301 ||
antarāyeṇa kecicca tadalābhena kecana |
tatkalpājñānataḥ kecitkecidguruvivarjitāḥ || 1,15.302 ||
kecidrogākulā devi tvitthaṃ tairapi vighnitāḥ |
yatra jyotiṣmatī vallī sā bhūmiḥ puṇyabhūmikā || 1,15.303 ||
tatraiva sarvatīrthāni siddhayo vividhā api |
jyotiṣmatīphalabhavaṃ sevyaṃ divyarasāyanam || 1,15.304 ||
<27. guggulukalpaḥ>
atha guggulukalpaṃ ca śṛṇu tvaṃ caṇḍike'dhunā |
caturvidho gugguluḥ syātkumudaḥ padmakastathā || 1,15.305 ||
mahiṣākṣaṃ ca hemākhyastattadvarṇasamujjvalaḥ |
hemākhyaṃ ca manuṣyāṇāṃ rasāyanamudāhṛtam || 1,15.306 ||
kaṇaśo gugguluṃ kṛtvā goghṛtena śanaiḥ pacet |
ḍolāyantre yuktiparo ghṛtabharjitamāṃsavat || 1,15.307 ||
pathyāvibhītakau dhātrī gandhakaśca pṛthakpṛthak |
samāṃśaṃ tānvicūrṇyaiva etatsarvasamaṃ puram || 1,15.308 ||
śuddhakoṣṭho lihetprātastailaṃ hyeraṇḍasaṃbhavam |
lolayitvā niṣkamātraṃ vṛddhiḥ karṣāvadhirbhavet || 1,15.309 ||
evaṃ varṣaprayogeṇa śītavātavraṇāstathā |
piḍakāgaṇḍamālādyā vinaśyanti mahārujaḥ || 1,15.310 ||
dvādaśābdaprayogeṇa vajrakāyo bhavennaraḥ |
yadyaddravyaṃ ca sātmyaṃ syāttattaddravyeṇa vā bhajet || 1,15.311 ||
yasya yasya ca rogasya yadyadbheṣajamīritam |
taistaiḥ sahaiva seveta tattadrogaharaṃ bhavet || 1,15.312 ||
<28. vijayākalpaḥ; vijayotpattiḥ>
śrībhairavī |
devadeva jagannātha surāsuranamaskṛta |
pañcakṛtyapradhāneśa namastubhyaṃ parātpara || 1,15.313 ||
sarve'pyauṣadhakalpāśca matprītyā kathitāstvayā |
āścaryaṃ bhūtadeveśa sadyaḥ siddhipradāyakam || 1,15.314 ||
sukhasevyaṃ sukhakaraṃ jñānadaṃ bhuktidaṃ śucim |
asmatsāyujyadaṃ brūhi prītyā mama rasāyanam || 1,15.315 ||
tataḥ smitamukho bhūtvā śaṅkaro lokaśaṃkaraḥ |
śrībhairavaḥ |
devi vakṣyāmyahaṃ kalpaṃ spṛhaṇīyaṃ surāsuraiḥ || 1,15.316 ||
siddhaiśca munibhiḥ strībhiḥ sarvavarṇaiśca yogibhiḥ |
bālairvṛddhaiśca rugṇaiśca ṣaṇḍhaiśca bahuyoṣitaiḥ || 1,15.317 ||
śṛṇu tvamamṛtodbhūtaṃ sāvadhānena cetasā |
āgneyo māmako'ṃśaḥ syātsaumyāṃśastāvakaḥ priye || 1,15.318 ||
agnīṣomātmakaṃ sarvamauṣadhaṃ jaladhau surāḥ |
asurāḥ prākṣipaṃścakrurmathanaṃ mandarādriṇā || 1,15.319 ||
atīva mathanāttatra devyāgneyo mamāṃśakaḥ |
jvalanmahāviṣaṃ ghoraṃ jātaṃ hālāhalākhyakam || 1,15.320 ||
tena vyākulitā mlānā gatavegāḥ surāsurāḥ |
kṛpayā tanmayā sarvaṃ viṣaṃ ca kabalīkṛtam || 1,15.321 ||
tato devā ditisutā mamanthurhṛṣṭamānasāḥ |
tato lakṣmīprabhṛtayaḥ prādurāsaṃstato'mṛtam || 1,15.322 ||
tadudbhūtaṃ paraṃ divyaṃ jarāmaraṇakṛntanam |
tadā tadā mahāviṣṇurmama hastāmbuje'rpayat || 1,15.323 ||
trivāraṃ bindavo dattāḥ parasmai paramātmane |
parāśaktiyuje paścāttubhyaṃ datte mayā priye || 1,15.324 ||
pītaṃ tvayā ca me dattaṃ madhusūktamudīrya ca |
svīkṛtaṃ ca mayā kānte cidānandātmakāmṛtam || 1,15.325 ||
sāndrānandena ca mayā sphūtkṛtirlajjayā kṛtā |
patitā bindavaḥ sūkṣmāstebhyo jātā mahauṣadhiḥ || 1,15.326 ||
tāmālokya śubhāṃ divyāṃ muditāḥ paryapālayan |
matkarāṃbujanītena pīyūṣeṇa vivardhitam || 1,15.327 ||
tasmāttriśūlasadṛśaparṇaṃ ca paṭalaṃ bahu |
tasmānmahauṣadhirjātā madādiṣṭairguṇairvṛtā || 1,15.328 ||
kadācidbhairavo dṛṣṭvā hṛṣṭastāṃ prārthayanmayā |
dattā tasmai bhairavo'pi yoginībhyaḥ samarpipat || 1,15.329 ||
tāśca prītāḥ svabhaktebhyo bhaktebhyaḥ pradaduśca te |
ninyuśca bhūtalaṃ divyāmauṣadhiṃ sarvasiddhidām || 1,15.330 ||
<vijayābhedāḥ>
śrībhairavī |
kiṃnāmnī sā kathaṃvīryā sevanīyā kathaṃ prabho |
kīdṛgvarṇā kiyadbhedā kīdṛgrūpaguṇā ca sā || 1,15.331 ||
kathaṃ vā siddhidā sā syātkramānme brūhi vallabha |
śrībhairavaḥ |
atha tasyāścaturvarṇā yugadharmāśritāḥ priye || 1,15.332 ||
śuklavarṇā kṛtayuge tretāyāṃ śoṇitaprabhā |
dvāpare pītavarṇā ca nīlavarṇā kalau yuge || 1,15.333 ||
ekaparṇā triparṇā ca pañcaparṇā municchadā |
daśaparṇā rudraparṇā trayodaśadalānvitā || 1,15.334 ||
navaparṇeti vijñeyā bhedā hyete sureśvari |
<vijayālakṣaṇam>
strīrūpā saphalā vallī puṃrūpā ca drumākṛtiḥ || 1,15.335 ||
saphalā tu madaṃ mūrchāṃ sukhaṃ satvaṃ karoti ca |
yogavāhā tiktarasā sā kaṭuścūgragandhinī || 1,15.336 ||
śivamūlī ca vijayā bhaṅgī gañjā vimardinī |
divyā siddhā siddhidā ca siddhamūlī manonmanī || 1,15.337 ||
madhudravā cidāhlādā paśupāśavināśinī |
kālaghnī sarvarogaghnī nāmānyetāni pārvati || 1,15.338 ||
śivo mūlaṃ bhavedyasyāḥ śivamūlīti kathyate |
trilokānariṣaḍvargān vijayākhyā jayediti || 1,15.339 ||
tāpatrayādiduḥkhānāṃ bhañjanādbhaṅginī smṛtā |
gañjeti mādayatyeṣā madirāsthānakāriṇī || 1,15.340 ||
sadṛśaṃ marśanaṃ yasyāḥ saiva proktā vimarśinī |
krīḍāmodadyutimadakāntidatvācca divyakā || 1,15.341 ||
svataḥsiddheti siddhākhyā diṣṭasiddhipradāyikā |
siddhideti mahāsiddhaiḥ sā nītā siddhamūlikā || 1,15.342 ||
manaścintitakāryāṇāṃ sādhanācca manonmanā |
saṃvidānandadatvācca cidāhlādeti kīrtitā || 1,15.343 ||
brahmarandhrasthitasudhādrāvaṇācca madhudravā |
jantūnāṃ pāśanāśatvātpaśupāśavināśinī || 1,15.344 ||
mṛtyuñjayatvātkālaghnī rogaghnī sārthanāmikā |
<vijayāvardhanakramaḥ>
vardhanakramamācakṣe śṛṇu lokaśivaṃkari || 1,15.345 ||
śilāloṣṭādito varjyā vālukāśarkarāditaḥ |
bhūmiḥ kṛṣṇā pāṃsulā ca mṛdulā sakarīṣakā || 1,15.346 ||
bhujaṅgamāṃsasahitā tatra bījāni vāpayet |
ahivaktreṣu satvañci snigdhāni ca gurūṇi ca || 1,15.347 ||
puṣyārke siddhayoge vā śravaṇe śuklapakṣake |
snāto liptatanurgandhaiḥ kṛtanyāsajapārcanaḥ || 1,15.348 ||
udaṅmukhaḥ prāṅmukho vā dhyātvā ca gurupādukām |
sakṣīrāṅkolatailena jalena pariṣecayet || 1,15.349 ||
aṅkure ca samutpanne siñcetsaghṛtavāriṇā |
parṇe kīṭe samutpanne sāmudraṃ vāri secayet || 1,15.350 ||
tato'syāṃ vitatāyāṃ ca śiraśchittvā ca randhrayet |
tatra sūtaṃ kṣipenniṣkaṃ niṣkaṃ pratyekamādarāt || 1,15.351 ||
nirodhayettanmukhāni kauśikena prayatnataḥ |
puṣpite phalite madyamāṃsāmbu ca niṣecayet || 1,15.352 ||
ekaikapakṣaparyantaṃ kṣīrādi pariṣecayet |
asyāṃ baddhvā jaṭāmāṃsīṃ sakṣaudraṃ vāri secayet || 1,15.353 ||
śeṣaṃ madyena vā vārā vardhanīyā mahauṣadhiḥ |
ādau vā paramaṃ mantraṃ paścātsevanamantrakam || 1,15.354 ||
oṃ kṣāṃ kṣīṃ kṣūṃ kṣetrapālāya namaḥ savīryaṃ kuru kuru siddhiṃ dehi dehi svāhā |
ayaṃ sthāpanamantraḥ |
oṃ śrīṃ hrīṃ klīṃ yaralavaśaṣasaha amṛteśvari amṛtaṃ kuru kuru āṃ hrāṃ kroṃ svāhā |
ayaṃ sevanamantraḥ |
phālgune kṛṣṇapakṣasya caturdaśyāṃ ca sādhakaḥ |
snātaḥ śuddhāmbaro gandhapuṣpabhūṣaṇasaṃyutaḥ || 1,15.355 ||
arcayedbhairavaṃ tatra nandīśaṃ ca krameṇa ca |
madyamāṃsopahāreṇa raktapītasitāsitaiḥ || 1,15.356 ||
tantubhirveṣṭayeddevi tanmantraṃ ca nigadyate |
oṃ glauṃ sauṃ hrīṃ khecarabhūcaradivyayogini imāṃ rakṣa rakṣa sarvaśatrupramathini svāhā |
ayaṃ tantubandhanamantraḥ |
sahasraṃ pratyahaṃ japyamaghoraṃ mantranāyakam || 1,15.357 ||
evaṃ saptadinaṃ kāryaṃ pañcamyāmamṛteśvarīm |
dhyātvā kundendukarpūrasaṃkāśāṃ dhavalāṃbarām || 1,15.358 ||
sitamālyānulepārdrāṃ muktābharaṇamaṇḍitām |
dakṣiṇe'kṣaguṇaṃ vāme sudhāpūrṇaghaṭaṃ tataḥ || 1,15.359 ||
tadadho dakṣiṇe vāme saṃvitpustakadhāriṇīm |
tatra sthitāṃ tataḥ kuryāllavanaṃ ca samantrakam || 1,15.360 ||
snigdhāni ca sabījāni saparṇāni samāharet |
oṃ klīṃ vaṃ saṃ krauṃ śivānandāmṛtodbhave tribhuvanavijaye vijayaṃ prayaccha svāhā |
ayaṃ lāvanamantraḥ |
mandātape śuddhadeśe śoṣayetsaptavāsaram || 1,15.361 ||
nikṣipennūtane bhāṇḍe tatparṇāni sucūrṇayet |
sthālīdvayaṃ pratāpyādāvekasyāṃ prathamaṃ puṭet || 1,15.362 ||
tato'parasyāṃ puṭayedevaṃ kāryaṃ muhurmuhuḥ |
yāvatsupākatāṃ yāti tajjñastāvadvipācayet || 1,15.363 ||
guñjonmattaśca vallī ca tāsāṃ parṇarasairmuhuḥ |
sthālyau pṛthakpṛthakdevi saptadhā saptadhā priye || 1,15.364 ||
muṇḍī brāhmī kumārī ca varī dhātrī kaṭudvayam |
sugandhikā kramājjñeyā caikaikapalamānataḥ || 1,15.365 ||
yojanīyāścūrṇitāśca tatsarvasadṛśā jayāḥ |
sitā sarvasamā yojyā tatsamaṃ gopayaḥ kṣipet || 1,15.366 ||
kṣīramadhye sitāṃ kṣiptvā tantupākaṃ bhavedbhiṣak |
muṇḍādivijayāntaṃ ca taccūrṇaṃ tatra nikṣipet || 1,15.367 ||
lolayitvāvatāryaivaṃ śaitye kṣaudraṃ sitārdhakam |
nikṣipya goghṛtaṃ tulyaṃ kṣaudrasya parimelayet || 1,15.368 ||
pakṣadvayaṃ dhānyarāśau nidhāya pratyahaṃ japet |
sahasraṃ vā yathāśaktyā mahāvaṭukamantrakam || 1,15.369 ||
hrīṃ śrīṃ mahākālāgnibhairavāya sarvasiddhimātṛbrahmā liṅgitavigrahāya sarvāpadāṃ śoṣakāya huṃ phaṭṭham |
ayamagnipākamantraḥ |
māsāduddhṛtya vidhivadgrasedāmalakopamam |
nivātamandire sthāyī kṣīrānnāśī jitendriyaḥ || 1,15.370 ||
gurūpadiṣṭadevaṃ vā tattvaṃ vā paramaṃ smaret |
dvikālaṃ vā trikālaṃ vā yathā sevābalaṃ kramāt || 1,15.371 ||
evaṃ trivarṣājjarayā valībhiśca vivarjitaḥ |
bhūcarīsiddhimāpnoti jīvettriśatavatsaram || 1,15.372 ||
vārāhī triphalā citramaśvagandhā krameṇa ca |
evaṃ dvitricaturbhāgamaśvagandhādi yojayet || 1,15.373 ||
etatsamā ca vijayā madhunā lolayetsukham |
ātape pakṣaparyantaṃ sthāpayetpratitāpayet || 1,15.374 ||
dhātrīphalopamaṃ sevyaṃ sūryābhaścāṣṭamāsataḥ |
sādhako jāyate varṣājjarāmaraṇavarjitaḥ || 1,15.375 ||
japanmantramimaṃ bhaktyā dehasiddhyai sureśvari |
hrīṃ śrīṃ mahāvyomabhāskarāya dīpimātṛkāliṅgitavigrahāya tejasāṃ nidhiṃ kuru kuru ṭham |
iti sūryapākamantraḥ |
palatrayaṃ jayāyāśca yaṣṭīcūrṇaṃ paladvayam || 1,15.376 ||
palamelābhadracūrṇaṃ citramūlaṃ palārdhakam |
śrīkhaṇḍacūrṇaṃ ca palaṃ caitatsarvasamā sitā || 1,15.377 ||
svarṇacūrṇaṃ ca niṣkārdhaṃ niṣkaṃ ca himavālukam |
sarvamājyena lulitaṃ jyotsnāyāṃ nikṣipetsudhīḥ || 1,15.378 ||
śuklapañcamīmārabhya pakṣāntaṃ śaśibhāvanā |
vidhivatpūjayitvā tu śuddhakoṣṭho lihetpriye || 1,15.379 ||
asya mantraṃ punarvacmi sarvasiddhipradāyakam |
hrīṃ śrīṃ mahāśaśāṅkakiraṇavisphārabhairavāya amṛteśvarī mātṛkāliṅgitavigrahāya visphuraṇaṃ kuru kuru huṃ phaṭṭham |
iti candrapākamantraḥ |
evaṃvidhasya kalpasya trividhaṃ mantramīritam || 1,15.380 ||
ekaikamantramayutaṃ puraścaraṇamācaret |
vahnipākaṃ caturmāseṣvāṣāḍhādiṣu tanyate || 1,15.381 ||
phālgunādau prakurvīta ravipākaṃ sureśvari |
kārttikādiṣu candrasya pākaḥ syātsiddhidāyakaḥ || 1,15.382 ||
muṇḍīcitrakanirguṇḍīsahitā kuṣṭhanāśinī |
brāhmī kumāryā yuktā cedamapasmāravināśinī || 1,15.383 ||
jayā varāvyoṣayutā kṣayasya kṣayakāriṇī |
jayā kārpāsamatsyākṣīpatrayuktā ca pittanut || 1,15.384 ||
snuhyarkapatrakṣāreṇa saṃyutā gulmaśūlahṛt |
vacādūrvārajoyuktā jñānavṛddhipradāyinī || 1,15.385 ||
yaṣṭīgandhakasaṃyuktā kuṣṭhanutpuṣṭidāyinī |
śālmalīpicchasaṃyuktā sasitā vīryavardhinī || 1,15.386 ||
pāṭhātiktātrikaṭukairyuktā kaphagadāpahā |
śuklaguñjāyutā nḥṇāṃ mahāviṣavināśinī || 1,15.387 ||
vyāghātacūrṇasahitā kuṣṭharogavināśinī |
āraṇyamaricairaṇḍayuktā vātavināśinī || 1,15.388 ||
ete dvādaśayogāśca māseṣu dvādaśeṣu ca |
mantratrayaṃ ca prajapetpratimāseṣu sādhakaḥ || 1,15.389 ||
sādhako'nena yogena jarāmaraṇavarjitaḥ |
bhavatyeva na sandehaḥ satyaṃ satyaṃ varānane || 1,15.390 ||
rasatrigandhakṛṣṇābhrarudrākṣasvarṇabhasma ca |
jayārasena saṃmardyaṃ pakṣamekaṃ tu bhāvayet || 1,15.391 ||
bhūdhare saṃpuṭe yantre puṭayeccūrṇayettataḥ |
etaccaturguṇajayā śarkarāghṛtasaṃyutā || 1,15.392 ||
sevyā niṣkapramāṇena prātaḥ sāyantane'thavā |
mahāvaṭukamantraṃ ca japellakṣāvadhi priye || 1,15.393 ||
ṣaṣṭhamāse bhavetsiddhirjarāmaraṇavarjitaḥ |
varṣānmanojavagatirnāgāyutabalo bhavet || 1,15.394 ||
vajrakāyaśca siddho'sau jīvedācandratārakam |
vaṭāṅkurasya cūrṇena sitāmadhvājyasaṃyutā || 1,15.395 ||
tripakṣātsevitajayā sarvalokavaśaṃkarī |
apāmārgarajoyuktā jayā ghṛtasamanvitā || 1,15.396 ||
mantrajāpī bhavennityaṃ ṣaṇmāsādamaro bhavet |
varā sitajayā citrastrivṛtā trikaṭurvṛṣā || 1,15.397 ||
dūrvā bhṛṅgo marīcaśca madhukājājisaindhavam |
karpūraṃ ca kacoraṃ ca sarvatulyaṃ jayārajaḥ || 1,15.398 ||
madhutrayeṇa lulitaṃ snigdhabhāṇḍe vinikṣipet |
karṣaṃ prabhāte seveta maṇḍalātsiddhibhāgbhavet || 1,15.399 ||
syādvarṣāddevasadṛśo jīveddevadinatrayam |
aśvagandhā vacā vyoṣaṃ jayācūrṇaṃ ca tatsamam || 1,15.400 ||
lihettrimadhurairyuktaṃ trivarṣāddevatāsamaḥ |
gañjācūrṇaṃ cāṣṭabhāgaṃ caturbhāgā ca muṇḍikā || 1,15.401 ||
brāhmīrajo dvibhāgaṃ syādvājigandhaikabhāgikā |
dvibhāgastryūṣaṇasyāpi varābhāgadvayaṃ tathā || 1,15.402 ||
bhāgamekaṃ śilāyāśca bhāgaikaṃ gandhakasya ca |
rasarāḍekabhāgaḥ syāttadardhaṃ svarṇabhasma ca || 1,15.403 ||
tatsamaṃ mauktikaṃ yojyaṃ muktātulyaṃ pravālakam |
madhvājyābhyāṃ vilulitaṃ snigdhabhāṇḍe vinikṣipet || 1,15.404 ||
saptarātraṃ dhānyapākaṃ kṛtvā lakṣatrayaṃ japam |
niṣkamātravaṭī sevyā kṣīrāhāro jitendriyaḥ || 1,15.405 ||
maṇḍalātsarvarogaghnaṃ tvabdātpuṣpavadagnibhāk |
ardhavarṣādbhairavaḥ syājjīvedācandratārakam || 1,15.406 ||
trisugandhivarāvyoṣaiḥ samāṃśā śivamūlikā |
madhvājyābhyāṃ yutā lehyā kāsaghnī vahnivardhanī || 1,15.407 ||
madhuraprāyabhojī cetsakāsaśvāsarogajit |
haridrayā ca sahitā sarvamehavināśinī || 1,15.408 ||
maṇḍūkaparṇyā sahitā vacayā vāṅmatipradā |
drākṣā sitā vallakī ca kharjūrī dugdhapiṇḍikā || 1,15.409 ||
sahitā kandalīnīrasaiḥ sarvasvāduriti smṛtaḥ |
nārikelodakairyuktā karpūrasurabhīkṛtā || 1,15.410 ||
sarvasvāduyutā siddhā pittaghnī vīryavardhanī |
svādoścaturguṇaṃ kṣīraṃ madhupākaṃ yathā bhavet || 1,15.411 ||
tadvastragālitaṃ kṛtvā karpūraṃ tatra nikṣipet |
elāṃ ca sarvamadhuraṃ jayāṃ ca pibatastataḥ || 1,15.412 ||
śatastrīgamane śaktiḥ pittahṛtpuṣṭivardhanam |
samāmbupayasi kṣiptvā jayābījaṃ savastrakam || 1,15.413 ||
avaśiṣṭaṃ caturthāṃśaṃ pacenmṛdvagninā priye |
taddadhīkṛtya vidhivanmanthayettata uddharet || 1,15.414 ||
navanītaṃ kṣipettatra karpūrailā sitā madhu |
puṣṭyāyuṣyadyutikaraṃ balārogyavivardhanam || 1,15.415 ||
śatadhā stanyadhautaṃ tacchatadhautamitīritam |
nāgakesarakacchūralavaṅgailāgaruṃ tathā || 1,15.416 ||
karpūracandanamṛganābhitakkolakuṅkumam |
etatsamaṃ jayābījaṃ gokṣīreṇa ca mardayet || 1,15.417 ||
niṣkaniṣkapramāṇena vidadhyādvaṭikāḥ sudhīḥ |
supuṣpavāsitāḥ kṛtvā satāmbūlaṃ mukhe kṣipet || 1,15.418 ||
prasekamukhavairasyamalapūtiharī śuciḥ |
saugandhyavaiśadyakarī cittaharṣapradāyinī || 1,15.419 ||
gokṣīre cārdhasalile dhātakīkusumaṃ kṣipet |
jātīphalaṃ nāgaraṃ ca tatsarvasadṛśāṃ jayām || 1,15.420 ||
kṣīrāvaśiṣṭaṃ vastreṇa gālayettrimadhuplutam |
siddhayoga iti khyāto vṛṣyāyuṣyabalapradaḥ || 1,15.421 ||
ajamodayutā vāpi niśārajoyutāthavā |
tanmātrasevitā cūrṇā pāmākiṭṭibhanāśinī || 1,15.422 ||
jayā tālayutā hanyātpradaraṃ śvayathuṃ tathā |
kaṇḍūpraśamanī jñeyā vijayā śilayā yutā || 1,15.423 ||
tailena bhāvayetsthālyāṃ jayāṃ paścācca phāṇite |
sāndrapākaṃ bhaveddhīmān saghṛtaṃ bhakṣayetsadā || 1,15.424 ||
sākṣātparyāyamadano'nekayoṣitsukhapradaḥ |
pathyā bhallātakaguḍaṃ tilāścaite samāṃśinaḥ || 1,15.425 ||
tatsamā vijayā hanti gudakīlāṃśca ṣaḍvidhān |
yavānī jīrakayugamajamodā samāṃśinaḥ || 1,15.426 ||
etatsamā ca vijayā sarpiṣā sevyatāṃ budhaiḥ |
sandhivātaṃ bhramaṃ chardimunmādaṃ mastakavyathām || 1,15.427 ||
śarkarāṃ madhu viśvaṃ ca māgadhīṃ tatsamāṃ jayām |
kṛtvājyagalitāṃ golīmimāṃ pañcāgadaṃ viduḥ || 1,15.428 ||
hṛdrogaplīhajaṭharabhagandaranikṛntanīm |
sacitrakā vahnikarī savarā śūlabhañjinī || 1,15.429 ||
savyoṣā vātasaṃhartrī pittaghnī śarkarāyutā |
śvetādrikarṇikābījaṃ vijayāṃ kharamūtrataḥ || 1,15.430 ||
nasyenāpasmṛtiharā bhūtapretādibhañjanī |
jayācūrṇaṃ samadhvājyaṃ bhajedāmalakopamam || 1,15.431 ||
vākpāṭavaṃ vīryavṛddhiṃ kurute vijayā param |
madhunājyena vā dhātryā mustayā svarasena vā || 1,15.432 ||
upayuktā jayā rātrau dṛkprasādakarī nṛṇām |
yauvanasthairyamātanyādāyuṣyaṃ paramaṃ bhavet || 1,15.433 ||
guḍo'bhayā ca vijayā durnāmakulabhañjanī |
jayākāṣṭhena dantānāṃ śodhanaṃ dantadārḍhyakṛt || 1,15.434 ||
tailāktavijayākāṣṭhādrasajñāśodhanaṃ yadi |
jihvāpūtimalān hanyādyathārthā syādrasajñatā || 1,15.435 ||
nālikerabhavakṣīre pacenmṛdvagninā jayām |
trimadhvaktāṃ pibedrātrau tato rāmāśataṃ bhajet || 1,15.436 ||
śuṇṭhī harītakī tulye tatsamā ca jayā tathā |
sarvatulyā sitā yojyā yogastrailokyamohanam || 1,15.437 ||
tryūṣaṇaṃ madhukaṃ cavyaṃ cāturjātaṃ phalaṃ varā |
gostanī pippalīmūlaṃ samāṃśaṃ parikalpayet || 1,15.438 ||
priyālamajjā taistulyā caitatsarvasamā jayā |
triguṇena guḍe pakve teṣāṃ cūrṇaṃ kṣipecchive || 1,15.439 ||
āloḍya modakānkuryātkāmī kandarpamodakān |
sarvarogopaśamanāṃstridoṣaghnānbalapradān || 1,15.440 ||
vṛṣyānpuṣṭikarān dhīmāṃstrisaṃdhyamanuvāsaram |
bhakṣayecchuddhakoṣṭhe tu trivarṣātsiddhirīdṛśī || 1,15.441 ||
sadaivamupayuñjāno jarāmaraṇavarjitaḥ |
marīcaṃ pippalī śuṇṭhī tvagelāṃ patrakaṃ samam || 1,15.442 ||
etaiśca vijayā tulyā samagodhūmapiṣṭakaiḥ |
dviguṇena guḍenaiva modakānparikalpayet || 1,15.443 ||
karṣamātrānpratidinaṃ bhakṣayenniyatendriyaḥ |
balapuṣṭiyuto varṣādvalīpalitavarjitaḥ || 1,15.444 ||
caṇakairmāṣakairmudgairāḍhakairvā tilaistathā |
niṣpāvairvituṣairājyabharjitairguḍasaṃyutaiḥ || 1,15.445 ||
satrijātaiśca savyoṣaiḥ sajīraiḥ samabhāgataḥ |
saṃyojya vijayāṃ kuryānmodakān karṣamātrakān || 1,15.446 ||
ekaikaṃ pratyahaṃ khādetsāyaṃ prātarviśuddhadhīḥ |
sarvarogaharān vṛṣyān buddhīndriyabalapradān || 1,15.447 ||
takkolaṃ candanaṃ sevyaṃ karpūraṃ nāgakesaram |
elālavaṅgamadhukaṃ pippalī maricaṃ tathā || 1,15.448 ||
sarvaiḥ samāṃśā vijayā sitā sarvasamā śubhā |
pañcabāṇābhidhāno'yaṃ cūrṇaṃ sarvarujāpaham || 1,15.449 ||
dvādaśābdopayogena valīpalitahā bhavet |
varā trijātakaścandrakhaṇḍabījaṃ kaṭutrayam || 1,15.450 ||
etaiḥ samā siddhamūlī sarvatulyā ca śarkarā |
madhunā lolitā līḍhā dīpanī dehasiddhidā || 1,15.451 ||
śatāvaryuttamāvyoṣamusalīdviguṇānvitaḥ |
jayā samā samasitā vṛṣyāyuṣyabalapradā || 1,15.452 ||
āyurghṛtayutā dhatte prātaḥ śuṇṭhīsitāyutā |
kṣīrasiddhā jayā vṛṣyā balyā ca niśi sevitā || 1,15.453 ||
madhunā sevitā bhukteḥ pūrvaṃ sā vājigandhayā |
balapuṣṭikarā siddhā rasāyanamidaṃ param || 1,15.454 ||
nistvaṅmarkaṭikābījaṃ māṣacūrṇaṃ samā jayā |
kṣīre ca māhiṣe paktvā rātrau sevyātivṛṣyakṛt || 1,15.455 ||
vidhivacchālmalīpicchavarībhṛṅgāmṛtārasaiḥ |
kāntapātre pratirasaiḥ saptadhā saptadhātape || 1,15.456 ||
kurvīta bhāvanāṃ paścāccūrṇayettatsamāṃ jayām |
madhunā ca lihetkarṣaṃ jarāmaraṇanāśinī || 1,15.457 ||
pañcāṅgaṃ śālmalergrāhyaṃ tatsamaṃ vijayārajaḥ |
madhvājyābhyāṃ lihetkarṣaṃ valīpalitakhaṇḍanam || 1,15.458 ||
atha puṣyaravau hastikandaṃ grāhyaṃ samūlakam |
anātape ca saṃśoṣya cūrṇayettatsamāṃ jayām || 1,15.459 ||
madhunāloḍayetpaścātsnigdhabhāṇḍe vinikṣipet |
ekaviṃśaddinaṃ dhānyarāśau sthāpyaṃ tata uddharet || 1,15.460 ||
pratyahaṃ palamekaṃ tu bhakṣayenmaṇḍaladvayam |
kaṭvamlau varjayettāvanmṛdu yūṣānnabhojanam || 1,15.461 ||
madhuraṃ laghu pittaghnaṃ palalaṃ jāṅgalaṃ hitam |
bālārkābhaśca matimān pikālāpo balānvitaḥ || 1,15.462 ||
śatayojanaparyantaṃ dinenaikena gacchati |
muṇḍīcūrṇaṃ jayācūrṇaṃ samaṃ madhughṛtānvitam || 1,15.463 ||
karṣaṃ prātaḥ pratidinaṃ lihedāyuṣyapuṣṭikṛt |
dvādaśābdaṃ tu seveta bhuñjīta ghṛtasaṃyutam || 1,15.464 ||
gokṣīraṃ ṣaṣṭikānnaṃ ca loṇāmlakṣārahīnakam |
bhavedvalijarāhīno dṛśyaḥ syāllaghudehavān || 1,15.465 ||
patraṃ puṣpaṃ phalaṃ śvetabrahmavṛkṣasya cāharet |
chāyāyāṃ śoṣayeccūrṇaṃ tatsamaṃ vijayārajaḥ || 1,15.466 ||
madhvājyābhyāṃ lihetkarṣamabdādyauvanamāpnuyāt |
jayābījāni ca tilānbharjayetsaguḍānpriye || 1,15.467 ||
vidhāya laḍḍukānprātarbhakṣayedgoghṛtāplutān |
manaḥsaṃmohanakarān kāntāsaṅgamasādhakān || 1,15.468 ||
pākārhavyañjanaiḥ sārdhaṃ jayāpatrāṇi pācayet |
bharjayedgoghṛtenaiva guḍailātīkṣṇajīrakaiḥ || 1,15.469 ||
saṃskṛtaḥ sukhasevyo'yaṃ nāmnā vyañjanayogarāṭ |
taṇḍulānvijayābījaṃ mudgaṃ ca vigatatvacaḥ || 1,15.470 ||
surandhranālikerāntaḥ kṣipedrandhraṃ nirodhayet |
bahirgomayamṛlliptaṃ śoṣayenmṛduvahninā || 1,15.471 ||
pācayennālikerasthaṃ samajjaṃ tatsamāharet |
madhutrayeṇa sahitaṃ bhuñjīta ca yathābalam || 1,15.472 ||
mahāvṛṣyakaro yogaḥ ṣaṇḍhānāmapi puṃstvadaḥ |
gokṣīre vijayābījaṃ vastrabaddhaṃ vinikṣipet || 1,15.473 ||
ardhāvaśiṣṭe tatkṣīre vyajanānilaśītale |
vārāhīmarkaṭībījakarpūrailālavaṅgakam || 1,15.474 ||
trimadhu trisugandhaṃ ca nikṣipecca samāṃśataḥ |
ekīkṛtya pibedrātrau mahāvṛṣyakaraḥ paraḥ || 1,15.475 ||
vārāhīyoga eṣo'yaṃ ṣaṇḍhatvādinikṛntanaḥ |
kṣīraśṛtaṃ ca tadbījaṃ peṣayecchilayā tanu || 1,15.476 ||
rasenottamakanyāyāḥ kāṃsyapātre pralepayet |
tīvrātape dhārayettaṃ tasmātsnehaṃ samāharet || 1,15.477 ||
snehāntarayujā tena nasyakarma samācaret |
jatrūrdhvagatarogāṃśca kāsaśvāsādikānharet || 1,15.478 ||
yasya yasya ca rogasya vihitaṃ yadyadauṣadham |
tena tena yutā siddhā tattadrogaharā bhavet || 1,15.479 ||
guṇā mayā ca kathyante tān śṛṇuṣva maheśvari |
jihvārdratā manaḥsaukhyaṃ tṛptiḥ saṃkalpasiddhatā || 1,15.480 ||
vākpaṭutvaṃ mukhollāso bhavedvyāyāmapeśalaḥ |
snānadhyānārcanaparo madhurāsvādalolupaḥ || 1,15.481 ||
mṛṣṭānnāśī śītavāri pibannālāpatatparaḥ |
yatheṣṭālokanaparaḥ kāminīsaṅgalolupaḥ || 1,15.482 ||
sarvabhūtadayāviṣṭastattvajñānavilīnadhīḥ |
kavitākhyānavijñānopanyāsaikaparāyaṇaḥ || 1,15.483 ||
vādeṣu vijayaṃ kuryātsaṅgrāme mallasaṅgare |
anye'pi bahavaḥ santi guṇāstvanyatra durlabhāḥ || 1,15.484 ||
<vijayāvyāpatsiddhī>
etasyāḥ siddhimūlyāśca bhakṣaṇe tvadhike sati |
jāyante hi vikārāśca śṛṇu tānparameśvari || 1,15.485 ||
āraktalocanaḥ śuṣkajihvauṣṭhapuṭatālukaḥ |
prathame śuṣkanāsāgra uṣṇakṛcchvāsapārśvayoḥ || 1,15.486 ||
dvitīye'kṣinimīlatvaṃ paṭalīkṛtavigrahaḥ |
tṛtīye pādahastākṣidāhakṛdgadgadadhvaniḥ || 1,15.487 ||
caturthe kṣutpipāsārto nidrāghūrṇitalocanaḥ |
pañcame gadgadā vāṇī svaproktaṃ vismaretkṣaṇāt || 1,15.488 ||
ṣaṣṭhe vikāre saṃjāte cittāpasmṛtikāraṇam |
saptame karasādaśca dehe ca rucirāyate || 1,15.489 ||
mahormaya ivollāsā jāyante ca punaḥ punaḥ |
aṣṭame digbhramaḥ śāntirbhrūbhaṅgaścātirodanam || 1,15.490 ||
navame śrotrahuṅkāro mūrcchāpasmṛtikātaraḥ |
udgāraḥ kūjanaṃ bhūmau luṭhanaṃ dainyabhāṣaṇam || 1,15.491 ||
anyathā bhāṣaṇaṃ guhyaṃ bravansaukhyaṃ na vetti ca |
duḥkhaṃ ca mṛdukalpo'yaṃ svinnāṅgaśca bhavetpriye || 1,15.492 ||
anye vikārā bahavaḥ santi teṣāṃ cikitsanam |
vireko'mlarasaḥ śīrṣasnānaṃ śītāmbunā tataḥ || 1,15.493 ||
candanośīrakarpūrahimāmbuparilepanam |
sugandhiśītalībhūtamālālaṃkṛtavigrahaḥ || 1,15.494 ||
mallikājāticāmpeyakamalotpaladhāriṇā |
mṛṇālavalayodbhāsiśayyā ca kadalīdalam || 1,15.495 ||
karpūrailālavaṅgāśca takkolaṃ kaṭukīphalam |
carvayetsaha tāmbūlaṃ tālavṛntena vījanam || 1,15.496 ||
sūkṣmakāṣāyavastrāṇi sugandhīni ca dhārayet |
kaṅkaṇe candramāpaśyañjyotsnāyāṃ dvimuhūrtakam || 1,15.497 ||
pīnottuṅgakucadvandvagāḍhāliṅganatatparaḥ |
śayīta śarkarākṣīraghṛtamāṃsarasādikam || 1,15.498 ||
pānakaṃ mudgayūṣaṃ vā peyaṃ vā śārkaraṃ madhu |
kevalaṃ śayanaṃ kuryādyadyacchītaṃ bhajecca tat || 1,15.499 ||
<29. kañcukīkalpaḥ>
atha vakṣyāmi deveśi kañcukīkalpamuttamam |
saptāṣṭacchadasaṃyuktā mūlakopamakandukā || 1,15.500 ||
kṣīrānvitā meṣaśabdājjāyante pallavānyapi |
sā jñeyā pītaraktābhakāṇḍā śvetatanuḥ priye || 1,15.501 ||
āṣāḍhe kārttike māse caityadeśe prarohati |
piṣṭāṃ palāṣṭake kṣīre tvaṅguṣṭhadvayamātrakam || 1,15.502 ||
kuṭīpraveśaṃ kṛtvādau pītvā paścātpayaḥ pibet |
jīrṇe kṣīraṃ punaḥ peyaṃ peyāṃ vā snehavarjitām || 1,15.503 ||
stokoṣṇalavaṇāmlāṃ tāṃ na spṛśecchītalaṃ jalam |
evaṃ saptadinaṃ sevyaṃ khilakuṣṭhaṃ vināśayet || 1,15.504 ||
tṛtīye saptarātreṇa naśyanti nikhilā gadāḥ |
tṛtīye saptarātreṇa bhujaṅgaḥ kañcukaṃ tathā || 1,15.505 ||
tvakkeśanakhadantāṃśca sa tyajetsvayamevaṃ hi |
jīvetpañcaśataṃ varṣaṃ rūpamedhābalānvitaḥ || 1,15.506 ||
cūrṇitaṃ kañcukīkandaṃ tatkṣīreṇa vibhāvitam |
karṣaṃ gokṣīrakuḍubaiḥ piṣṭvā khaṇḍaṃ palaṃ kṣipet || 1,15.507 ||
nakhakeśāsthidantasthān rogānpānena nāśayet |
kañcukyāstu palaṃ cūrṇaṃ prasthagokṣīravāpitam || 1,15.508 ||
cūrṇaśeṣaṃ pacettāvadgoghṛtena ca bharjayet |
sitayā madhunā lihyātkṣīrānno maṇḍalāvadhi || 1,15.509 ||
mahāgajabalopeto dviraṣṭavayasojjvalaḥ |
jīvetpañcaśataṃ varṣānkāminīkelimanmathaḥ || 1,15.510 ||
<30. kukkuṭīkalpaḥ>
atha vakṣyāmi te devi kukkuṭīkalpamuttamam |
surāsurairmathyamānādabdheramṛtabindavaḥ || 1,15.511 ||
patitāstebhya utpannā kukkuṭī nirjaratvadā |
pattrairmarakatacchāyaiḥ kharjūrīdalasannibhaiḥ || 1,15.512 ||
kandaiśca kukkuṭāsyābhairīṣatkaṭukapicchilaiḥ |
lakṣitā kukkuṭī jñeyā mahārogaharā parā || 1,15.513 ||
viśeṣādvātarogāṃśca gudakīlaviṣāṇi ca |
ghṛtena bharjayetkandaṃ śuddhakoṣṭhaḥ kuṭīṃ vrajet || 1,15.514 ||
guñjāmātraṃ prage sevyaṃ dhāroṣṇaṃ gopayaḥ pibet |
jīrṇe kṣīrānnamaśnīyājjalānnaṃ vā vicakṣaṇaḥ || 1,15.515 ||
yathābalaṃ pratidinaṃ vardhayenmaṇḍalāvadhi |
jatrūrdhvarogān pāṇḍvādīn kṣayakuṣṭhādikānharet || 1,15.516 ||
sthirakeśadvijanakho valīpalitavarjitaḥ |
jīveddviśatavarṣaṃ ca medhādhairyabalānvitaḥ || 1,15.517 ||
śuklapratipadārabhya caikaikaṃ ghṛtavarjitam |
kandaṃ prāśnīya parvāntaṃ vardhayettadyathābalam || 1,15.518 ||
tathaivāsitapakṣādi nūnaṃ kuryāddināddinam |
bhukteḥ prāgupayuñjīta paścātkṣīrāśano bhavet || 1,15.519 ||
darśāntamevaṃ seveta pratimāsaṃ punaḥ punaḥ |
trivarṣātsiddhimāpnoti jīvedvarṣāyutatrayam || 1,15.520 ||
kukkuṭīkandacūrṇaṃ ca gokṣīreṇa yathābalam |
jīrṇe pibedyavāgūṃśca māsātsiddhiḥ prajāyate || 1,15.521 ||
nānāvidhagadānhanti jīvedvarṣaśatadvayam |
<31. somalatākalpaḥ>
atha somalatākalpaṃ divyaṃ vakṣyāmi śaṅkari || 1,15.522 ||
somaḥ svayaṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ hitāyāvanimaṇḍalam |
pratipede ca tannāmnā khyātā somalatā bhuvi || 1,15.523 ||
caturviṃśatidhā proktā sthānanāmādibhedataḥ |
pūrvapratipadārabhya pūrṇāntaṃ prativāsaram || 1,15.524 ||
ekaikaṃ jāyate parṇaṃ tathaivāparapakṣake |
patati kramaśaḥ parṇaṃ darśe saikāvaśiṣyate || 1,15.525 ||
pūrṇamāsyāṃ pañcadaśachadopetā sadā bhavet |
nānāvidhadalopetāśchadapañcadaśātmikāḥ || 1,15.526 ||
kṣīrayuktalatākandāḥ sarvāḥ somalatāḥ smṛtāḥ |
tuṣārādrāvarbude ca sahye devagirau tathā || 1,15.527 ||
śrīparvate ca malaye mahendre pāriyātrake |
vindhye devasahe'drau ca devasūtahṛde tathā || 1,15.528 ||
vitastottaratīre'sti prabhāsākhyo mahīdharaḥ |
sindhuhrado'sti pāñcāle candramāḥ plavate'tra ca || 1,15.529 ||
tatpradeśe ca vāpyasti tvaṃśumānatha muñjavān |
divyaṃ saro'sti kāśmīre khyātaṃ kṣudrakamānasam || 1,15.530 ||
auṣṇiktraiṣṭubhagāyatro jāgatastryaiṣṭubhastathā |
anyāśca pañca santyatra mṛgāṅkābhāsavallikāḥ || 1,15.531 ||
bhāgyahīnā na paśyanti devāgnidvijanindakāḥ |
sarvasomalatānāṃ ca vidhireka upāsane || 1,15.532 ||
candramāḥ svarṇasacchāyo viśado rajataprabhaḥ |
tau raṃbhākṛtikandau tu candramāstu jalecaraḥ || 1,15.533 ||
laśunacchadanaḥ puṇyo muñjavāngaruḍāhṛtaḥ |
śyenacchadanibhaḥ pāṇḍuḥ sarpanirmokavatsadā || 1,15.534 ||
lambate pādapāgreṣu viśeṣaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ smṛtam |
aṃśumāṃścandramāścaiva śonakī karṇakopamaḥ || 1,15.535 ||
dūrvāsomau jāgrataśca kanyābhāsaśca śākvaraḥ |
karavīrastālavṛntaḥ pratānaśca gavīnasaḥ || 1,15.536 ||
pathyaḥ somapradaścaiva garuḍāhṛtanāmakaḥ |
ayaḥprabho'gniṣṭomaḥ syādaukthyo revatakastathā || 1,15.537 ||
aṃśumānsarvamukhyaḥ syādasya sevāṃ pracakṣate |
etadrasāyanajñaiśca prītairmantraviduttamaiḥ || 1,15.538 ||
bhiṣagvaraiḥ suhṛdbhiśca saṃyuktaḥ śuddhakoṣṭhavān |
kuṭīṃ praviśya nivṛtāṃ puṇyarkṣe śubhavāsare || 1,15.539 ||
pūrvedyurdevi viprāgnisiddhasāmāyikāngurūn |
yoginīśca kumārīśca bālakān siddhasantatim || 1,15.540 ||
gandhapuṣpākṣatādyaiśca dhūpairdīpaiśca pūjayet |
bhojanairdakṣiṇābhiśca toṣayecca vidhānataḥ || 1,15.541 ||
kṣīramaṃśumataḥ kandātsvarṇasūcyā vibheditāt |
hemapātre samādāya tatkṣīraṃ kuḍubaṃ pibet || 1,15.542 ||
sakṛdeva pibetsarvaṃ pītaśeṣaṃ jale kṣipet |
divā suhṛdbhirviharedvāgyataśca vaśī bhavet || 1,15.543 ||
dravyadehopadeśajñaiḥ kuṭyāmāstikabuddhibhiḥ |
upavāsī japarataḥ sāyaṃ sandhyārcanāparaḥ || 1,15.544 ||
prasupyāddarbhaśayane kṛṣṇājinatirohite |
pipāsito'pi pānīyaṃ śītalaṃ mātrayā pibet || 1,15.545 ||
prabhāte ca samutthāya kṛtaprāgetanakramaḥ |
vinā somaṃ tathāsīta jīrṇe kṣīre saśoṇitam || 1,15.546 ||
pittāvasānaṃ sakṛmi vamanaṃ bhavati priye |
śṛtaśītaṃ ca gokṣīraṃ sāyaṃ peyaṃ susādhakaiḥ || 1,15.547 ||
vireko jāyate tasya tṛtīyadivase mahān |
tena pramucyate martyaḥ pūrvakairduṣṭabhojanaiḥ || 1,15.548 ||
snānaṃ kuryāddinānte sa śṛtaśītaṃ pibetpayaḥ |
darbhaśayyāṃ sumakṣaumachāditāyāṃ śayīta saḥ || 1,15.549 ||
tataścaturthe divase śvayathustasya jāyate |
aṅgebhyaḥ krimayo bahvyaḥ sūkṣmā niryānti taddine || 1,15.550 ||
goviḍbhasmamayīṃ śayyāṃ śodhitāṃ mṛdulāṃ sudhīḥ |
adhiśeta pipāsā cecchṛtaśītaṃ jalaṃ pibet || 1,15.551 ||
sāyaṃ gavyaṃ śṛtaṃ kṣīraṃ pītvā śiṣyasuhṛdvṛtaḥ |
yathāsukhaṃ prasupyācca niśi pañcamaṣaṣṭhayoḥ || 1,15.552 ||
ahno vidhirayaṃ kāryo dvikālaṃ pāyayetpayaḥ |
saptame divase dehastvagasthisnāyuśeṣitaḥ || 1,15.553 ||
māṃsaraktavihīnaḥ syātsomapaḥ prāṇaśeṣitaḥ |
sukhoṣṇaiḥ siñcayetkṣīrairgavyairetasya vigraham || 1,15.554 ||
śrīkhaṇḍatilayaṣṭyāhvaiḥ piṣṭaistaṃ parilepayet |
kṣīrāśī kaṭaśāyī ca kṣaumamayyāṃ śayīta saḥ || 1,15.555 ||
aṣṭame'hni nakhaṃ śmaśru tvakkeśā radanā api |
patanti sadyojātasya dehavajjāyate vapuḥ || 1,15.556 ||
kṣīrāśī syāttu navame'ṇutailābhyaṅgamācaret |
snāyātsomalatākalpaṃ kvathitena jalena ca || 1,15.557 ||
tatastu navame tvakca sthiratāṃ pratiyāti ca |
tathaivaikādaśadinaṃ prārabhya ṣoḍaśaṃ dinam || 1,15.558 ||
abhyaṅgaṃ nācaretsnānaṃ somavalkakaṣāyajam |
dinātsaptadaśādūrdhvaṃ caturviṃśatikāvadhi || 1,15.559 ||
sthiratvaṃ tvagavāpnoti dantā vajranibhojjvalāḥ |
sthirā dṛḍhatarāḥ snigdhā jāyante samapaṅktayaḥ || 1,15.560 ||
pañcaviṃśattame ghasre ṣaṣṭyannaṃ gopayo'nvitam |
bhuñjīta nakharāstasya śukacañcunibhojjvalāḥ || 1,15.561 ||
tvagasya jāyate snigdhā nīlotpalasamadyutiḥ |
sulakṣaṇātasīpuṣpavaiḍūryāmbudasannibhā || 1,15.562 ||
māsādanantaraṃ kuryāttilośīrakacandanaiḥ |
piṣṭaiḥ pralepanaṃ paścātpayasā snapanakriyā || 1,15.563 ||
rohanti cikurāstasya bhramarāñjanasannibhāḥ |
māsasaptadinādūrdhvaṃ dvitīyāvaraṇasthitiḥ || 1,15.564 ||
punastṛtīyāvaraṇe tiṣṭhetsiddho muhūrtakam |
kuṭīṃ viśedyathāpūrvaṃ balātailājyalepanam || 1,15.565 ||
kuryāddinatrayaṃ paścāccatvāriṃśaddinātparam |
karīṣabhasmanoddhūlya dehaṃ kalkairvilepayet || 1,15.566 ||
ajaśṛṅgītvagudbhūtaiḥ snānaṃ koṣṇajalena ca |
candanośīrakarpūrairliptāṅgo mudgadhātrijaiḥ || 1,15.567 ||
yūṣaiḥ ṣaṣṭyannamaśnīyātkṣīraistrimadhusaṃyutaiḥ |
ghṛtena vā dvitīye tu vasedāvaraṇe sudhīḥ || 1,15.568 ||
divasaṃ pañcakaṃ tatra kiṃcidvātātape sudhīḥ |
madhye madhye ca seveta tathaiva daśavāsaram || 1,15.569 ||
soḍhavātātapastasmāttṛtīyāvaraṇaṃ bhajet |
ekaviṃśaddinaṃ tiṣṭhan sthirīkṛtamanāḥ samut || 1,15.570 ||
tato dvāviṃśatidine dehasiddhiḥ prajāyate |
bahirnirgatya ca tadā pūjayedbhairavaṃ purā || 1,15.571 ||
gurvagnidvijasiddhānāṃ cetāṃsi parimodayan |
sarvatra svecchayā nityaṃ viharedduḥkhavarjitaḥ || 1,15.572 ||
varṣāyutaṃ navaṃ divyaṃ vapurdhatte sukāntimat |
balaṃ gajasahasrāṇāṃ bhavedasya na saṃśayaḥ || 1,15.573 ||
viṣāgnitoyaśastrāstramṛgādibhyo bhayaṃ na hi |
brahmalokādilokeṣu vicaretsvecchayā sadā || 1,15.574 ||
kāntyā dvitīyaścandraḥ syādrūpeṇa makaradhvajaḥ |
sarvāhlādakaraḥ śāntaḥ saṣaḍaṅgapadakramān || 1,15.575 ||
vedānvetti ca śāstrāṇi bhūlokagatinirjaraḥ |
hrasvaśākhāpratānāyāḥ somavallyāḥ samāharet || 1,15.576 ||
kāṇḍāndvātriṃśatiṃ svarṇapātre puṇye yathāvidhi |
raupye pātre'thavā yojyaṃ dvipalaṃ gopayastathā || 1,15.577 ||
somavallīrasaṃ tadvanmelayitvā pibettataḥ |
kuṭīpraveśaḥ pathyaṃ ca pūrvavatsamudāhṛtam || 1,15.578 ||
aṇimādyaṣṭasiddhiḥ syātsa sākṣādamaro bhavet |
<32. guḍūcīkalpaḥ>
athāmṛtalatākalpaṃ vakṣyāmi śṛṇu bhairavi || 1,15.579 ||
sākṣātsudhāmṛtalatā vyādhijanmajarāpahā |
amṛtāyāḥ śatapalaṃ triprasthaṃ madhuratrayam || 1,15.580 ||
cūrṇaṃ lihetkarṣamātramevaṃ saṃvatsarāvadhi |
sarvavyādhipraśamanaṃ jīvecca śaradaḥ śatam || 1,15.581 ||
atha chinnaruhācūrṇaṃ pañcaviṃśatpalaṃ sitā |
dviguṇā ca tavakṣīrī cātmaguptāśvagandhikā || 1,15.582 ||
punarnavājyaṃ pratyekaṃ yuñjyāddaśapalaṃ priye |
mṛdvīkāṣṭapalā yaṣṭī tataḥ pañcapalā tathā || 1,15.583 ||
ekīkṛtya ca tatsarvaṃ snigdhabhāṇḍe vinikṣipet |
pakṣamekaṃ dhānyarāśau nidhāya prativāsaram || 1,15.584 ||
palaṃ palaṃ prayuñjīta māsaṃ syādvyādhivarjitaḥ |
ṣaṇmāsādgṛdhradṛṣṭiḥ syādvarṣaṃ jīvecchatāyuṣam || 1,15.585 ||
amṛtāyāḥ śatapalaṃ cūrṇaṃ ṣaṣṭipalaṃ madhu |
catvāriṃśatpalaṃ sarpirdvimāsaṃ dhānyarāśigam || 1,15.586 ||
pratyahaṃ palamātrāśī māsātsarvagadānharet |
divyadṛṣṭiḥ śataṃ jīvenmedhābalamahādyutiḥ || 1,15.587 ||
<33. tuvarakakalpaḥ>
atha vakṣyāmyahaṃ devi kalpaṃ tuvarakasya ca |
vṛkṣāḥ paścimavārīśataṭe tiṣṭhanti saṃtatam || 1,15.588 ||
taraṅgānilasaṃkṣubdhaśīkārārdritapallavāḥ |
varṣartau ca supakvāni tatphalāni samāharet || 1,15.589 ||
śoṣayitvātha saṃcūrṇya tilavattailamāharet |
athavairaṇḍavattailaṃ prayatnena samāharet || 1,15.590 ||
punarvahnau pacettailaṃ yāvadaṃbhaḥkṣayaṃ bhavet |
tatkarṣaṃ nikṣipetpakṣaṃ tata uddhṛtya prayatnataḥ || 1,15.591 ||
caturbhaktavihīnaḥ san śuddhaḥ karṣaṃ pibetpriye |
śaṅkhacakragadāpāṇistvāmājñāpayadacyutaḥ || 1,15.592 ||
mantreṇānena seveta sarvo doṣo vinaśyati |
evaṃ pañcadinaṃ sevyamahitāni ca varjayet || 1,15.593 ||
māsaṃ mudgarasāśī syātsarvakuṣṭhavivarjitaḥ |
tataḥ kṣaudraghṛtābhyāṃ ca lihetṣāṇmāsikāvadhi || 1,15.594 ||
valīpalitahīnaśca jīvedvarṣaśatadvayam |
tattailaṃ dinapañcāśaṃ nasyeddhīrakavaddvijaḥ || 1,15.595 ||
valīpalitanirmuktaḥ śrutadhārī dṛḍhāṅgavān |
jīveddviśatavarṣaṃ ca medhāvī gṛdhralocanaḥ || 1,15.596 ||
<34. somarājīkalpaḥ>
atha vakṣyāmyahaṃ devi somarājīrasāyanam |
saṃgrahettāṃ puṣyaravāvātape śoṣayettataḥ || 1,15.597 ||
cūrṇitāṃ koṣṇasalilaiḥ pibetprātarviśuddhaye |
tridivaṃ ca tataḥ kṣaudraiḥ karṣamātraṃ lihetprage || 1,15.598 ||
māsena rogā naśyanti ṣaṇmāsātsiddhimāpnuyāt |
<35. vṛddhadārukakalpaḥ>
atha bravīmi deveśi vṛddhadārukakalpakam || 1,15.599 ||
nāgavallīdalakṣaudraiḥ sadugdhairmṛduromakaiḥ |
patrairyutāpi saphalā raktapuṣpā pratānikā || 1,15.600 ||
tiktoṣaṇaṃ kāmbuteti mahiṣākhyalatā smṛtā |
tulonmitaṃ ca tanmūlaṃ cūrṇitaṃ sarṣapāyutam || 1,15.601 ||
dhānyarāśau nyasetpakṣamuddhṛtyonmitakarṣakam |
anupeyaṃ ca gokṣīraṃ jīrṇe kṣīrānnabhojanam || 1,15.602 ||
vṛddho'pi taruṇo bhūyādvarṣātsarvagadojjhitaḥ |
evaṃ dvādaśavarṣāṇi sevetālasyavarjitaḥ || 1,15.603 ||
jīvetṣaṭśatavarṣaṃ ca valīpalitavarjitaḥ |
vṛddhadārukacūrṇaṃ ca śuddhaṃ śatapalonmitam || 1,15.604 ||
vājigandhākandacūrṇaṃ śatatrayapalaṃ ghṛtaiḥ |
āloḍya bhakṣayetkarṣaṃ sa jīveccharadāṃ śatam || 1,15.605 ||
turaṃgamasamo vege bale dantābalopamaḥ |
kāntyā himāṃśusadṛśastejasā bhāskaropamaḥ || 1,15.606 ||
śatāvaryāśvagandhā ca gokṣuro vṛddhadārukaḥ |
samāṃśamakhilaṃ sarpiryuktaṃ karṣaṃ ca khādayet || 1,15.607 ||
saptāhātkinnaradhvāno vatsarātsiddhibhāgbhavet |
vṛddhadārukamūlaṃ ca varīsvarasapeṣitam || 1,15.608 ||
karṣaṃ pibedvatsaraṃ yatsa jīveccharadaḥ śatam |
vṛddhadārukacūrṇaṃ ca dhātrīsvarasabhāvitam || 1,15.609 ||
ekaviṃśatidhā prātarmadhvājyābhyāṃ tu karṣakam |
lihedabdaṃ sa jīvecca varṣāṇi ca śatadvayam || 1,15.610 ||
yovasāpastadhā jīvennīrujaḥ sa mahānbhavet |
godugdhena ca tanmūlaṃ rasaṃ dhātryāśca saṃmitam || 1,15.611 ||
pibetsaṃvatsaraṃ yastu sa jīveccharadaḥ śatam |
tatsiddhaṃ sarpiraśnīyātpūrvavatphalamāpnuyāt || 1,15.612 ||
punarnavā varī śuṇṭhī māgadhī rajanīdvayam |
etatsarvaṃ samaṃ vṛddhadārukaṃ kāñjikānvitam || 1,15.613 ||
palaṃ prātaḥ pibeddhīmān dacchadāddhāramācaret |
māsātsarvāmayān hanti varṣādāyuḥ śataṃ bhavet || 1,15.614 ||
dvīpī śauṇḍī varā viśvaṃ mustamelā samāṃśakam |
etatsaṃyojya siddhaṃ taccūrṇaṃ vellarikaṃ bhavet || 1,15.615 ||
kṣaudrājyalulitaṃ khādetkarṣaṃ varṣeṇa sidhyati |
tatphalaṃ svarasaṃ karṣaṃ ghṛtaṃ kṣaudraṃ ca tatsamam || 1,15.616 ||
pibedanu gavāṃ kṣīraṃ māhiṣaṃ vā palonmitam |
māsena sarvarogaghnaṃ varṣājjīvecchatāyuṣam || 1,15.617 ||
cūrṇaṃ vellarikāmūlaṃ phalaṃ pippalikābhavam |
samavellarikāmūlaphalajaiśca rasaistathā || 1,15.618 ||
guḍūcīsvarasenāpi bhāvayedekaviṃśatim |
śuṣkaṃ ghṛtaplutaṃ bhāṇḍe snigdhe dhānyacaye sthitaḥ || 1,15.619 ||
trisaptāhāttaduddhṛtya karṣaṃ prātarlihennaraḥ |
varṣādvarṣasahasrāyuḥ sarvarogavivarjitaḥ || 1,15.620 ||
<36. vajravallīkalpaḥ>
atha vyākhyāmyahaṃ devi vajravallīrasāyanam |
vajravallīṃ samūlāṃ ca chāyāśuṣkāṃ vicūrṇayet || 1,15.621 ||
varā vraṇārimūlaṃ ca pratyekaṃ bhāskaraṃ palam |
rogānaśeṣān ṣaṇmāsāddhanyātsaṃvatsarādbhavet || 1,15.622 ||
dehasiddhiśca ṣaṇmāsāccūrṇamanyatprakalpayet |
<37. tilakṣīriṇikākalpaḥ>
tilakṣīriṇikākalpaṃ kathayiṣyāmi śāmbhavi || 1,15.623 ||
tilakṣīriṇikāmūlaṃ maunenotpāṭya sādhakaḥ |
koṣṇena vāriṇā piṣṭvā prāṅmukhodaṅmukhaḥ pibet || 1,15.624 ||
giledvā gulikāṃ kṛtvā kṣīrājyānāśayecchuciḥ |
krameṇārdhadvitriniṣkaṃ sevetābdaṃ sa siddhibhāk || 1,15.625 ||
daśāhācchukravṛddhiḥ syāttrimāsātsarvarogajit |
ṣaṇmāsātsiddhimāpnoti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 1,15.626 ||
<38. brāhmīkalpaḥ>
atha vakṣyāmi deveśi brāhmīkalpamanuttamam |
samūlāmuddharedbrāhmīṃ prakṣālya salilena ca || 1,15.627 ||
ulūkhale kuṭṭayitvā pātre tatra samāharet |
juhuyācchāradāmantraistadrasaiśca sahasrakam || 1,15.628 ||
arcayitvā gurūnviprānprāśnīta hutaśeṣakam |
palamātraṃ tadrasaṃ ca jīrṇe kṣīrānnabhojanam || 1,15.629 ||
evaṃ saptadinaṃ sevyaṃ brahmacārī jape rataḥ |
medhāvī brahmavarcasvī vāgviśuddhaḥ prajāyate || 1,15.630 ||
ūrdhvaṃ saptadinātsevyaṃ prabadhnātīcchayā api |
vismṛtānapi vettyeva śrutaṃ śīghraṃ ca dhārayet || 1,15.631 ||
dvitīye saptamādūrdhvaṃ pūrvavattadrasaṃ pibet |
sakṛcchrutaṃ ślokaśataṃ sahasramapi dhārayet || 1,15.632 ||
evaṃ trisaptadivasaṃ pibedbrāhmīrasaṃ śuciḥ |
dehādalakṣmīrniryāti vāṇī viśati śāśvatī || 1,15.633 ||
bhavetpuṃrūpavāgdevī sarvaśāstrārthavedinī |
vedavedāntaviddhīmāñjīvetpañcaśatābdakam || 1,15.634 ||


āk, 1, 16
<1. aṅkolakalpaḥ>
atha bravīmyahaṃ devi kalpamaṅkolabījakam |
caturbhiḥ sādhakairdhīrairviśuddhaiḥ śuddhikāṅkṣibhiḥ || 1,16.1 ||
samavetaḥ sādhakendraiḥ puṇyarkṣeṣvārabhecca tat |
aṅkolamūlaṃ vitṛṇaṃ kṛtvā saṃmārjya śodhanam || 1,16.2 ||
tatra liṅgaṃ ca saṃsthāpya tadagre sthāpayedghaṭam |
ekasūtreṇa badhnīyālliṅgāṅkolaghaṭān priye || 1,16.3 ||
gandhapuṣpādibhiḥ pūjāmaghoreṇa prakalpayet |
pratimāsaṃ divā naktaṃ puṣpādi phalitāvadhi || 1,16.4 ||
gṛhītvā tatphalaṃ pakvaṃ pūrayettadghaṭaṃ phalaiḥ |
yoginībhairavīprītyai mahāpūjāṃ vidhāya ca || 1,16.5 ||
ānīya tadghaṭaṃ gehe śoṣayedātape phalam |
vituṣāṇi ca bījāni kuryāttanmukhagharṣaṇam || 1,16.6 ||
kuryāttato viśālāsye pātre mṛccūrṇalepanam |
ekaikaśaścordhvamukhaṃ ropayedbhujagākṛti || 1,16.7 ||
tatastu ṭaṅkaṇaṃ tadvattattadvaktreṣu lepayet |
tatpātraṃ kāṃsyapātre ca sthāpayettadadhomukham || 1,16.8 ||
ātape dhārayeddhīmānpalalaiśca tirodadhet |
dine dine cyutaṃ tailaṃ gṛhṇīyāddinapañcakam || 1,16.9 ||
tattailaṃ kācakūpyantaḥ sthāpayecca samantrakam |
ardhamātrāṅkolatailaṃ dviguṇaṃ tilatailakam || 1,16.10 ||
nāsārandhradvaye nasyaṃ jarāmṛtyuvināśanam |
sa jīvettriśataṃ varṣaṃ sarvāmayavivarjitaḥ || 1,16.11 ||
rogādijalapāśādyairbhramādyaiśca viṣeṇa ca |
gatāsūnāṃ nṛṇāṃ nasyaṃ kuryātpuṃśuklamiśritam || 1,16.12 ||
mṛtasūtaṃ ca tailaṃ te jīvanti ca na saṃśayaḥ |
mṛtasaṃjīvanī vidyā pūrvoktā kathitānaghe || 1,16.13 ||
<anye rasāyanakalpāḥ>
elābhayā vacā kākatuṇḍīphalamaruṣkaraḥ |
sahadevī nimbapatraṃ lāṅgalīkandameva ca || 1,16.14 ||
samāṃśaṃ śoṣayedyantre pātāle tailamāharet |
tattailaṃ nasyamādadyātsaptāhāntaritaṃ priye || 1,16.15 ||
evaṃ varṣe kṛte nasye sahasrāyurbhavennaraḥ |
nīlikāmūlasaṃyuktaṃ tailaṃ cārdhapalaṃ pibet || 1,16.16 ||
vatsarājjāyate siddho valīpalitavarjitaḥ |
jīvedbrahmadinaṃ tryabdādvāyuvego mahābalaḥ || 1,16.17 ||
bhṛṅgīrasaṃ kṛṣṇajīraṃ prasthaṃ prasthaṃ prakalpayet |
nīlotpalaṃ ca madhukaṃ prasthārdhaṃ ca pṛthagbhavet || 1,16.18 ||
tilatailaṃ pañcapalaṃ pācayetsarvamekataḥ |
tailāvaśiṣṭaṃ tattailaṃ jarā mṛtyuśca naśyati || 1,16.19 ||
ardhaniṣkaṃ caikaniṣkaṃ sārdhaniṣkaṃ krameṇa ca |
tailapramāṇamityuktamabdāñjīvecchatatrayam || 1,16.20 ||
iṣṭikākṣipalaṃ kṣiptvā karṣaikamudakaiḥ pibet |
vatsarātpalitaṃ hanti sahasrāyurbhavennaraḥ || 1,16.21 ||
sarpākṣī kākamācī ca sahadevī ca bhṛṅgarāṭ |
kākatuṇḍīphalaṃ nimbaṃ parṇaṃ vākucibījakam || 1,16.22 ||
samūlāṃ devadālīṃ ca brāhmīmūlaṃ phalatrayam |
mūlaṃ ca vājigandhāyā nīlakoraṇḍapatrakam || 1,16.23 ||
samaṃ samaṃ kanyakāyā dravaiśca paribhāvayet |
saptāhaṃ śoṣayeccūrṇaṃ chāyāyāṃ trimadhuplutam || 1,16.24 ||
dvikarṣaṃ pratyahaṃ sevyaṃ vatsareṇa jarāṃ jayet |
ā candratārakaṃ jīvenmahābalayutaḥ sukham || 1,16.25 ||
<mahākāmeśvarakalpaḥ>
cāturjātakacoracandanajaladrākṣātugāreṇukaṃ kastūrītagarendukuṅkumajaṭākuṣṭhāśvagandhābdakam |
kaṅkolāmaradārucitrakaviṣaṃ dve jīrake saindhavaṃ bhārṅgīgokṣuradevapuṣpamusalīyaṣṭībalāphenakam || 1,16.26 ||
raṃbhākandaphalatrayaṃ trikaṭukaṃ jātīphalaṃ vāśakaṃ tālīśaṃ kaṭukīphalaṃ gajakaṇā māṣāmṛtāśarkarā |
picchābhṛṅgavidārikāmadanakaṃ bījaṃ ca kacchūdbhavaṃ śālmalyaṅghripunarnavāgaruśatāvaryapyatho dīpyakam || 1,16.27 ||
pādāṃśaṃ mṛtamabhrakaṃ ca vijayā tulyauṣadhānāṃ samā sarveṣāṃ sadṛśā sitā ca madhunā cājyena saṃyojitā |
jātīcampakaketakādikusumaiḥ seveta saṃvāsitaṃ karṣārdhaṃ niśi dehasiddhidamahākāmeśvaraḥ kāmadaḥ || 1,16.28 ||
puṃsāṃ śukravivṛddhidārḍhyakaraṇe kṣīrānupānaṃ hitaṃ śālmalyaṅghrijalānupānamathavāpyanyacca yacchukralam |
prauḍhānāṃ sudṛśāṃ sukhātisukhado vaśyo mahādrāvakaḥ saṅge bhaṅgurakāmakautukarasaḥ krīḍākalāmodadaḥ || 1,16.29 ||
saṃbhoge sthavirāgniyoṣiti made yūnāṃ smare darpahā nityānandanidānamādipuruṣairapyāhitāsvādanaḥ |
vācāṃ siddhikaraḥ prakṛṣṭakavitāsaṃdarbhasaṃpādanaḥ saukhyārogyaviśeṣayauvanakalāsāmagryasaṃdhāyakaḥ || 1,16.30 ||
vṛddhatvaṃ harate balaṃ ca kurute mṛtyuṃ nirasyetparaṃ vyādhivrātamapākaroti kurute kāntiṃ nayatyārjavam |
yogābhyāsavidhau ratasya sulabhā siddhiṃ vidhattetarāmaṃhaḥ saṃtatisaṃhṛtiṃ kalayate strīṇāmapatyapradaḥ || 1,16.31 ||
rogāṇāṃ nicayaṃ kṣayaṃ kṣapayati kṣipraṃ mahārudgaṇaṃ vyāhanti śvasanaṃ layaṃ gamayate chinte ca kāsodayam |
durnāmāni ca ṣaḍbhinatti harate sarvārtirogolbaṇaṃ mehaughaṃ ca lunāti śoṇitadaraṃ vidhvaṃsate sevanāt || 1,16.32 ||
kṣīṇe poṣamupādadhāti vipulaṃ pūrṇātijīrṇojjvalaṃ mandāgniṃ grahaṇīṃ nikṛntatitarāṃ doṣānaśeṣānapi |
śreṣṭhaḥ sarvarasāyaneṣu viduṣāṃ bhogārthināṃ yogināṃ siddhiṃ samyagihātanoti vapuṣaḥ saṃsevanādanvaham || 1,16.33 ||
<mūlikākalpeṣu rasābhrakādimelanātideśaḥ>
mahāvṛkṣādikalpeṣu prokteṣveteṣu sādhakaiḥ |
eṣa sādhāraṇo yogaḥ kartavyaḥ siddhikāṅkṣibhiḥ || 1,16.34 ||
guñjāmātraṃ śuddhasūtaṃ vābhrakaṃ vā paṇadvayam |
kāntalohaṃ tathaivopayojayedbhakṣayennaraḥ || 1,16.35 ||
rasābhralohayogena sadyaḥ siddhimavāpnuyāt |
jīvahīno yathā deho gandhahīnaṃ prasūnakam || 1,16.36 ||
tathaiva mūlikākalpā rasahīnā na siddhidāḥ |
tasmādrasena sahitāḥ sadyaḥ siddhipradāyakāḥ || 1,16.37 ||
<abhyaṅgarasāyanam; mahānīlītailam>
mahānīlīrasaṃ prasthaṃ prasthaṃ bhṛṅgarasaṃ priye |
dhātrīphalarasaṃ prasthaṃ kākatuṇḍīphalodbhavam || 1,16.38 ||
prasthaṃ kaṣāyatilajaṃ tailaprasthaṃ ca gopayaḥ |
āḍhakaṃ yojayetsarvaṃ kākatuṇḍīphalaṃ palam || 1,16.39 ||
tripalaṃ cāmalaṃ bhṛṅganīlīpatrarajaḥ palam |
piṣṭvā tasmin kṣipetsarvaṃ pacenmandāgninā priye || 1,16.40 ||
tailāvaśiṣṭaṃ vipacettato vastreṇa śodhayet |
tailenānena cābhyajya śiro'bhyaṅgaṃ samācaret || 1,16.41 ||
pratyahaṃ karṇapūraṃ ca evaṃ kuryādrasāyanam |
keśā bhramarasaṅkāśāḥ śrotraṃ digantapāṭavam || 1,16.42 ||
jatrūrdhvarogā naśyanti tathā pādahitaṃ bhavet |
<candanādyaṃ tailam>
candanāgarukarpūrakastūrīkuṅkumaṃ tathā || 1,16.43 ||
uśīradvayakaṅkolajātīphalalavaṅgakam |
nalikānaladāspṛkkāturuṣkasthāṇulocanam || 1,16.44 ||
hareṇustagaraṃ lākṣā nakhaṃ sthauṇeyakaṃ murā |
naladāmalakaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ corakaṃ kaṭukīphalam || 1,16.45 ||
prapauṇḍarīkaṃ kharjūraṃ padmakaṃ jātipattrikā |
śaileyaṃ dhātakīpuṣpaṃ saralaṃ cailavālukam || 1,16.46 ||
pūgīphalaṃ saptaparṇaṃ tathā tāmalakaṃ priye |
ekaikaṃ karṣamātraṃ syātprasthaṃ tailaṃ ca gopayaḥ || 1,16.47 ||
āḍhakaṃ tatpacetsarvaṃ tailaśeṣaṃ samāharet |
tasminmṛgāṇḍaṃ kastūrīṃ karpūraṃ kuṅkumaṃ kṣipet || 1,16.48 ||
pṛthakkarṣaṃ ca jātyādikusumairvāsayedbudhaḥ |
tenābhyaṅgamadhaḥkāye kuryāddaurgandhyanāśanam || 1,16.49 ||
aśītivarṣadeśīyo'pyasau syātṣoḍaśābdakaḥ |
kāminīlokakandadarpaḥ subhagaḥ śuklavṛddhimān || 1,16.50 ||
yoṣicchataṃ ca ramate sa jīveccharadaḥ śatam |
<udvartanarasāyanam>
athodvartanamākhyāmi valīpalitabhañjanam || 1,16.51 ||
sūtaṃ gandhaṃ samaṃ mardyaṃ stanadugdhena yoṣitaḥ |
muṇḍikā meghanādaśca viṣṇukrāntā munistathā || 1,16.52 ||
sarpākṣī ca dravairāsāṃ dinamekaṃ vimardayet |
etaddaśaguṇaṃ kṣārastilasya ca yavasya ca || 1,16.53 ||
etatsarvaṃ samadhvājyamanenodvartanaṃ vapuḥ |
valīpalitanāśaḥ syādvarṣāttriśatavatsaraḥ || 1,16.54 ||
jīveddivyavapurbhūtvā sādhako nātra saṃśayaḥ |
sanālamutpalaṃ sūtaṃ saptāhaṃ parimardayet || 1,16.55 ||
ātmīyaśivatoyena tataścāṅgaṃ vimardayet |
vatsarājjāyate siddhiḥ pūrvavaddivyavigrahaḥ || 1,16.56 ||
śivāṃbhasā ca sūtendraṃ brahmadaṇḍīyamūlakam |
mardayetsaptadivasaṃ samyagdevi vimardayet || 1,16.57 ||
vatsarājjāyate siddhiḥ pūrvavaddivyavigrahaḥ |
kaṭutailena surabhiṃ bhāvayeddinasaptakam || 1,16.58 ||
palārdhaṃ bhakṣayennityaṃ varṣādvajravapurbhavet |
triśatāyuḥsvarṇavarṇo valīpalitavarjitaḥ || 1,16.59 ||
samadhvājyaṃ kuṣṭhacūrṇaṃ karṣaṃ prātarlihennaraḥ |
śatapuṣpasugandhāṅgo jīvedvalivivarjitaḥ || 1,16.60 ||
<keśarañjanakalpāḥ>
kākatuṇḍīphalaṃ kāntacūrṇamāmrāṇḍatailakam |
āloḍya bhāṇḍe nikṣipya māsaṃ dhānyamaye kṣipet || 1,16.61 ||
uddhṛtya lepayecchīrṣaṃ nasyakarmāmunā bhavet |
tryahātkeśāḥ sunīlāḥ syuḥ ṣaṇmāsātkeśarañjanam || 1,16.62 ||
harītakī niṣkamekaṃ dvipalaṃ kāntacūrṇakam |
palamekaṃ nāgacūrṇamamladadhnā pramardayet || 1,16.63 ||
abhyakto nikṣipenmūrdhni mardayennāḍikārdhakam |
eraṇḍapatraiḥ saṃveṣṭya punarvastreṇa bandhayet || 1,16.64 ||
snāyātpunaśca tridinaṃ pūrvavatkeśarañjanam |
samūlau nīlikābhṛṅgāvayaścūrṇaṃ varāsamam || 1,16.65 ||
cūrṇameḍakamūtreṇa dinamekaṃ ca bhāvayet |
snānādi pūrvavatkṛtvā pūrvavatkeśarañjanam || 1,16.66 ||
nistvagguñjāphalaṃ cailā devadāru ca kuṣṭhakam |
samaṃ cūrṇaṃ bhṛṅgarasairbhāvyamekadinaṃ priye || 1,16.67 ||
anyaccūrṇaṃ tailaṃ pacenmṛdvagninā budhaḥ |
yasya tailaghṛtābhyaṅgātkeśā bhramarasannibhāḥ || 1,16.68 ||
hastidantasya dagdhasya samabhāgaṃ rasāñjanam |
chāgīdugdhena saṃpeṣya lepanātkeśarañjanam || 1,16.69 ||
kṛṣṇamṛttriphalābhṛṅgarasāyaścūrṇakaṃ samam |
āloḍyainaṃ samaṃ bhāṇḍe māsamekaṃ nirodhayet || 1,16.70 ||
tallepāccikurāḥ kṛṣṇā bhaveyuḥ pañcamāsataḥ |
dhātrīphalaṃ japāpuṣpaṃ kiṭṭaṃ piṣṭvā ca mūrdhani || 1,16.71 ||
lepayettridinaṃ tena keśāḥ syurbhramaropamāḥ |
cūrṇaṃ sindūrasadṛśaṃ tayostulyā ca sāṃbraṇiḥ || 1,16.72 ||
piṣṭvāṃbhasā pralepena tatkṣaṇātkeśarañjanam |
gorocanaṃ kṛṣṇatilān śatapuṣpāṃ śivāṃbunā || 1,16.73 ||
kākamācīmidaṃ sarvamayaḥpātre vimardayet |
tenaiva divasādūrdhvaṃ keśarañjanamuttamam || 1,16.74 ||
cūrṇaṃ siddhārthakaṃ sarjo yavakṣāraṃ ca kāñjikaiḥ |
nāgapuṣparasaiḥ peṣyaṃ lepanaṃ keśarañjanam || 1,16.75 ||
kadalīkandacūrṇaṃ ca sindūrāṅgārakau tathā |
rasairjambīrajairlohamuṣṭyāyaḥpātrake piṣet || 1,16.76 ||
dinaikalepanāttena keśānāṃ rañjanaṃ bhavet |
kāsīsaṃ nīlikāpatraṃ dadhibhṛṅgarasastathā || 1,16.77 ||
samāṃśaṃ lohacūrṇaṃ ca lepanātkeśarañjanam |
nāgadaṇḍena patrāṇi koraṇṭasya ca mardayet || 1,16.78 ||
dinatrayaṃ ca tallepātkeśāḥ syurbhramaropamāḥ |
varā bhṛṅgī cūtabījaṃ mṛṇālaṃ ca priyaṅgukam || 1,16.79 ||
nīlī niśā lohanāgau cūrṇitā niṃbatailakaiḥ |
kāntapātre loḍayitvā māsamekaṃ vibhāvayet || 1,16.80 ||
tena liptāḥ kacāḥ kṛṣṇā rañjitā bhramaropamāḥ |
tilāḥ kṛṣṇāḥ kākamācībījāni samabhāgataḥ || 1,16.81 ||
tattailayantrato grāhyaṃ tena syātkeśarañjanam |
karṣaṃ japārasaḥ kṣaudraṃ saptāhaṃ nasyamācaret || 1,16.82 ||
tadūrdhvaṃ rañjayetkeśān sarvanasyottamo hyayam |
bhṛṅgarājarasaiḥ piṣṭvā triphalāṃ lohacūrṇakam || 1,16.83 ||
tailametatsamaṃ yojyaṃ tathā bhṛṅgarasaiḥ punaḥ |
mṛdvagninā pacetsarvaṃ tailaśeṣaṃ yathā bhavet || 1,16.84 ||
snigdhabhāṇḍe ca tattailaṃ māsaṃ bhūmau vinikṣipet |
tailena lepayecchīrṣaṃ kāravallīdalaiḥ purā || 1,16.85 ||
veṣṭayettattu vastreṇa nivāte kṣīrabhojanam |
kṣālayettriphalākvāthaiḥ kuryātsaptadinaṃ priye || 1,16.86 ||
kapālarañjanaṃ kuryādyāvajjīvaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
śriyālīkākatuṇḍyāśca bījaṃ nirguṇḍikārasaiḥ || 1,16.87 ||
japāpuṣparasairbhāvyaṃ pṛthakdinacatuṣṭayam |
pātālayantre tattailaṃ pātayettena lepanam || 1,16.88 ||
kuryānmūrdhni ca gandharvapattraiśca pariveṣṭayet |
vastreṇa ca tato vātaśūnyadeśe vasansudhīḥ || 1,16.89 ||
kṣīrāśī kāñjike snānaṃ kuryātsaptadinaṃ tviti |
keśā bhramarasaṅkāśā yāvajjīvaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 1,16.90 ||
jalamaṇḍanikākākamācībhṛṅgāḥ samāḥ samāḥ |
piṣṭvā tailaṃ pacetsūpaṃ punastailena lepayet || 1,16.91 ||
mūrdhānaṃ lepayettena saptāhaṃ keśarañjanam |
snānabhojanaśayyāṃ ca pūrvavatparikalpayet || 1,16.92 ||
varābhṛṅgarasaiḥ piṣṭvā kāntapātre vilepayet |
avāṅmukhaṃ viniṣṭhāpya prātastaṃ bhṛṅgarāḍrasaiḥ || 1,16.93 ||
punaḥ piṣṭvā lihecchīrṣaṃ kṣālayettriphalāṃbunā |
ekaviṃśaddinaṃ kuryātpūrvavatphalamāpnuyāt || 1,16.94 ||
garalaṃ kṛṣṇajīraṃ ca piṣṭvā ruddhvā ca tanmukham |
gṛhāṅgaṇe paṅkile ca khanitvā sthāpayetsudhīḥ || 1,16.95 ||
ṣaṇmāsāttaddravībhūtaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ sādhu samuddharet |
śiromadhye kṣipetkarṣaṃ pūrvavadveṣṭanādikam || 1,16.96 ||
ā janma rañjayetkeśāṃstacca kāpālarañjanam |
bhṛṅgī nīlī varā kṛṣṇāyasaṃ madanabījakam || 1,16.97 ||
koraṇṭakusumaṃ cūrṇamarjunasya tvacodbhavam |
ambhojamūlaṃ jambvāśca tatsarvaṃ ca samāṃśakam || 1,16.98 ||
lohabhāṇḍagataṃ kuryātpakṣaṃ bhūmau vinikṣipet |
haredbhāṇḍagataṃ kalkaṃ kalkāttailaṃ caturguṇam || 1,16.99 ||
tailāccaturguṇaṃ bhṛṅgarasastasmāccaturguṇaḥ |
varākvāthaḥ sarvamidaṃ mandavahnau vipācayet || 1,16.100 ||
tailāvaśiṣṭaṃ vipacettatpiṣṭvāyasapātrake |
nikṣipecca parīkṣārthaṃ kākapakṣaṃ vilepayet || 1,16.101 ||
tailena yadi kṛṣṇaṃ tadauṣadhaṃ siddhidaṃ bhavet |
sādhako māsamekaṃ tu tasminpātre vipācitam || 1,16.102 ||
tena liptāḥ kacāstasya ṣaṇmāsādbhramaropamāḥ |
ciñcāśvatthapalāśānāṃ vāsāyāśca dravairmuhuḥ || 1,16.103 ||
drutaṃ pātragataṃ nāgaṃ cālayedbhasmatāṃ vrajet |
tāvatpacennāgabhasma palaṃ lohasya cūrṇakam || 1,16.104 ||
dvipalaṃ ca varācūrṇaṃ tripalaṃ dāḍimatvacaḥ |
palaṃ caitatsarvasamaṃ kāñjikaṃ lohabhājane || 1,16.105 ||
loḍayetpācayetkiṃcitkṣaṇaṃ tallohapātrake |
ātape dhārayedbhṛṅgakoraṇṭarasabhāvitam || 1,16.106 ||
ekaviṃśaddinaṃ bhāvyaṃ bhūyo bhūyo dravaṃ dravam |
tenaiva liptāścikurāḥ piñchatāpiñchasannibhāḥ || 1,16.107 ||
kacarañjanayogeṣu kathitākathiteṣu ca |
niśi lepaṃ tathairaṇḍapatraiśca pariveṣṭanam || 1,16.108 ||
prātaḥ snānamidaṃ karma sarvasādhāraṇaṃ smṛtam |
<divyauṣadhigrahaṇayogyasthalanirdeśaḥ>
śmaśāne salile mārge gṛhe devālaye tathā || 1,16.109 ||
malamūtrayutasthāne grāme goṣṭhe taroradhaḥ |
chāyāyāṃ ca viṣaprāye kuśīte cānyabādhite || 1,16.110 ||
evaṃprāyeṣu deśeṣu na grāhyā siddhamūlikā |
araṇye parvate tīre nadyā tapavane śucau || 1,16.111 ||
śilāsaṅgiśucikṣetre siddhakṣetre'bdhirodhasi |
śrīparvate himagirau taṭāke vijane hrade || 1,16.112 ||
gṛhṇīyānmūlikā jātā dehasiddhipradāyikāḥ |
<divyauṣadhigrahaṇavidhiḥ>
sarveṣāṃ siddhamūlānāṃ rakṣābandhanakarmaṇi || 1,16.113 ||
khananeṣūtpāṭaneṣu grahaṇeṣu krameṇa vai |
kathyante manavo divyāḥ sarvasādhāraṇāḥ smṛtāḥ || 1,16.114 ||
pūrvedyureva susnātaḥ kṛtadantaviśodhanaḥ |
anuliptaḥ śuddhavastro hṛṣṭaḥ saṃyatamānasaḥ || 1,16.115 ||
maunī gandhākṣatopeto rakṣābandhanasūtritām |
pūrvamanveṣitāṃ gatvā mūlikāṃ sveṣṭadāyikām || 1,16.116 ||
vitṛṇaṃ paritaḥ kṛtvā khātvāmbu pariṣecayet |
gandhapuṣpākṣataiḥ pūjāṃ kṛtvā sūtreṇa bandhayet || 1,16.117 ||
samantrakaṃ sādhakendraḥ sopavāso jitendriyaḥ |
atraiva tiṣṭha kalyāṇi mama kāryakarī bhava || 1,16.118 ||
mama kāryakarī siddhā tataḥ svargaṃ gamiṣyasi |
oṃ hrīṃ namaste'mṛtasambhūte balavīryavivardhini balamāyuśca me dehi pāpaṃ me jahi dūrataḥ |
hrīṃ phaṭ |
anenaiva tu mantreṇa tridhā sūtreṇa veṣṭitam || 1,16.119 ||
rakṣāṃ badhnīta tāṃ spṛṣṭvā japedaṣṭottaraṃ śatam |
tataḥ prātaḥ samutthāya kṛtvā nityavidhiṃ śuciḥ || 1,16.120 ||
mūlikāṃ tāṃ samāsādya gandhapuṣpākṣatādibhiḥ |
sarvopacāraiḥ sampūjya baliṃ dadyātsahetukām || 1,16.121 ||
samantrakaṃ khaneddhīmānmantro'yamapi kathyate |
yena tvāṃ khanate brahmā yena tvāṃ khanate bhṛguḥ || 1,16.122 ||
yenendro varuṇo viṣṇustena tvāmupacakrame |
tvāmevāhaṃ khaniṣyāmi mantrapūtena pāṇinā || 1,16.123 ||
evaṃ samprārthayitvādau paścānmantraṃ samuccaret |
oṃ śrīṃ hrīṃ tamamṛteśvari ehi ehi mama sakalasiddhiṃ kuru huṃ phaṭ |
anenaiva tu mantreṇa nikhanetsiddhimūlikām || 1,16.124 ||
atha tūtpāṭayettāṃ tu prāñjaliḥ prārthayediti |
mā utpate mā nipate mā ca te cānyathā bhavet |
oṃ hrīṃ caṇḍa huṃ phaṭsvāhā |
utpāṭayedanenaiva mantreṇa parameśvari |
acchinnamūlāmādāya śuddhavastrābhiveṣṭitām || 1,16.125 ||
kṣiptvā paṭalikāyāṃ ca svaveśmani śucisthale |
nicaye hṛṣṭamanasā nidadhyātpāraṇaṃ tataḥ || 1,16.126 ||
oṃ namo bhairavāya mahāsiddhipradāyakāya āpaduttaraṇāya huṃ phaṭ |
anenaiva tu mantreṇa gṛhītvā gṛhamānayet || 1,16.127 ||


āk, 1, 17
<uṣaḥpānarasāyanam>
atha prītamanā devī papraccha parameśvarī |
śrībhairavī |
ādibhairava deveśa sṛṣṭisthityantakāraṇa || 1,17.1 ||
sarvajña śiva lokeśa tvatprasādānmayā vibho |
rasāyanāni divyāni śrutāni vividhāni ca || 1,17.2 ||
susādhyaṃ sulabhaṃ divyaṃ sadyaḥ pratyayakāraṇam |
bālastrīṣaṇḍhavṛddhānāṃ rogārtānāṃ viśeṣataḥ || 1,17.3 ||
yogyaṃ rasāyanārhāṇāṃ brūhi deva kṛpānidhe |
tataḥ smerānanāmbhojo bhairavo bhavabhairavaḥ || 1,17.4 ||
śrībhairavaḥ |
śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi sarvayogyaṃ sukhaṅkaram |
pāvanaṃ sulabhaṃ cāntarbahiraṃhovināśanam || 1,17.5 ||
yasmājjaganti jātāni prāṇāḥ prāṇabhṛtāmapi |
trāyate viśvamakhilaṃ sarvadevātmakaṃ priye || 1,17.6 ||
tridoṣaśamanaṃ saukhyaṃ pathyaṃ sarvarasādhikam |
ṣaḍjīvanādiguṇayugrasayuktaṃ balapradam || 1,17.7 ||
jīvanaṃ ca balaṃ prāyaḥ prāṇināmagnimūlakam |
tasyāgnirindhanaṃ toyaṃ tasmāttoyaṃ pibedbudhaḥ || 1,17.8 ||
vaidyuto bāḍavo vahnirjāṭharaśca trayo'gnayaḥ |
abindhanāḥ syuranyeṣāṃ kāṣṭhādīnīndhanāni hi || 1,17.9 ||
tasmāddevi śivaṃ vacmi śṛṇu toyarasāyanam |
pūrayennūtanaghaṭe prātaḥ śodhyaiśca vāribhiḥ || 1,17.10 ||
gāṅgeyairvātha nādeyaistāṭākairvāpi sārasaiḥ |
kaupairvā cāpi vai gharmairvārṣikairvā yathābhavaiḥ || 1,17.11 ||
tadghaṭaṃ sthāpayenmañce divyabhāskararaśmibhiḥ |
naṣṭadoṣaṃ niśāyāṃ ca candrāmṛtakarāplutam || 1,17.12 ||
elośīrakakarpūracandanairadhivāsitam |
pāṭalīketakījātimallikotpalavāsitam || 1,17.13 ||
uśīratālavṛntena toyasiktena mandrataḥ |
muhurmuhurvījitaṃ tu haṃsodakamudāhṛtam || 1,17.14 ||
anurādhodayātpūrvaṃ nāspṛṣṭamalamūtrakam |
pibettoyaṃ tataḥ kuryānmalamūtravisarjanam || 1,17.15 ||
sandhyāvandanakarmādi kurvansvecchāśano bhavet |
ekābdabālako nityaṃ pibettoyaṃ palaṃ palam || 1,17.16 ||
evaṃ ṣoḍaśavarṣāntaṃ pratyabdaṃ ca palaṃ palam |
vardhayetkramaśo dhīmānpalaṣoḍaśikāvadhi || 1,17.17 ||
ṣoḍaśābdātparaṃ sevyaṃ ṣoḍaśaprasṛtaṃ jalam |
evaṃ viṃśativarṣāntaṃ nityaṃ seveta sādaram || 1,17.18 ||
anena vyādhayaḥ sarve vinaśyanti na saṃśayaḥ |
jīvedvarṣaśataṃ buddhibalendriyasukhānvitaḥ || 1,17.19 ||
prasṛtaṃ ca pibedaṣṭapalaṃ dīrghāyuṣāya ca |
yathā vahnibalaṃ toyaṃ tryahādvā pañcavāsarāt || 1,17.20 ||
saptāhādvā kramātprājñaḥ prasṛtaṃ prasṛtaṃ tathā |
punastu kuḍavaṃ vāpi vardhayettacchanaiḥ śanaiḥ || 1,17.21 ||
ṣoḍaśaprasṛtaṃ yāvattāvatsevyaṃ tataḥ param |
yathā yathāmbuno vṛddhistathā doṣaḥ praśāmyati || 1,17.22 ||
ṣaṇmāsaṃ vā vatsaraṃ vā vādyantaṃ vā pibejjalam |
yathā vṛddhistathā hrāso yāvacculukamātrakam || 1,17.23 ||
upavāsaṃ divā rātrau pathyāśī tatparityajet |
ātyantike ca vyāyāme sati vāñjalimātrakam || 1,17.24 ||
pādamardhaṃ tripādaṃ vā yathā sevāñjaliṃ pibet |
madhvājyatailadugdhājyajalādyanyatamairyutam || 1,17.25 ||
hanyācchīghraṃ vātarogamathavāsyasthabheṣajaiḥ |
tattadrogopaśamanaṃ bhakṣayitvā ca bheṣajam || 1,17.26 ||
tato jalaṃ vā prapibenmuhūrte vā gate sati |
jīrṇe vā salile sevyaṃ bhojanānte'thavā niśi || 1,17.27 ||
jvarādiroge saṃjāta upavāso bhavedyadi |
pibedardhaṃ ca pūrvasmāllaghvāhārī yathāsukham || 1,17.28 ||
upavāso vratārthaṃ cettatkṣīraṃ vā palaṃ niśi |
rātrāvabhojanaṃ devi jaṭhare vāri śuṣyati || 1,17.29 ||
ardhaṃ vā prāñjaliṃ vāri pibennityaṃ prayatnataḥ |
sarvatrāhārasatvāgnisāmyaṃ vāri pibetsudhīḥ || 1,17.30 ||
viṇmūtrotsarjanaṃ brāhmānmuhūrtātpūrvato yadi |
tataḥ pītvaiva salilaṃ viṇmūtraṃ ca visarjayet || 1,17.31 ||
vāri pūtaṃ viṣaṃ tvalpaṃ viṇmūtrotsarjanātparam |
ajñātavele rātrau cetpibettoyaṃ punaḥ prage || 1,17.32 ||
pādaṃ vātha caturthāṃśamañjaliṃ vā pibejjalam |
<jalapānakalpe pathyamapathyañca>
godhūmaṣaṣṭiśālyannaṃ yavajāṅgalajāmiṣam || 1,17.33 ||
gavyaṃ sarpirdadhi kṣīramudaśvinnavanītakam |
madhvikṣudāḍimadrākṣāmocakharjūraśarkarāḥ || 1,17.34 ||
sahakāraphalaṃ dhātrī nālikerajalaṃ navam |
kūṣmāṇḍālāburunimbapatraphalāni ca || 1,17.35 ||
patrapuṣpaphalaṃ śigrormudgayūṣarasau kṛtau |
suniṣaṇṇakagoraṇṭī jīvantīdvayakaṃ tathā || 1,17.36 ||
rājavṛkṣakaśothaghnī cillī vāpi laghucchadā |
śatāvarī taṇḍulī ca matsyākṣī ca vidārikā || 1,17.37 ||
upodakī ca vārttākī kāravallīphalāni ca |
laghukośātakī lakṣmī paṭolaṃ tālamūlakam || 1,17.38 ||
mocāyāḥ kusumaṃ daṇḍaphalāni kṣudradantikā |
sadāphalaṃ ca matsyāṇḍī śṛṅgaveraṃ ca saindhavam || 1,17.39 ||
phalapūrāmlakaṃ pakvadhātrīphalamalarkakaḥ |
vāyasī kuntalī caiva vasukanyālikā tathā || 1,17.40 ||
kustumbarī ca sarasī śārṅgerī cakramardakaḥ |
maṇḍūkaparṇī pālakyāḥ patraṃ gāṅgerukaṃ tathā || 1,17.41 ||
ākhukarṇī kāsamardapatraṃ gāṅgerukaṃ tathā |
saṃskārārthaṃ ca kaiḍaryasaindhavoṣaṇajārakāḥ || 1,17.42 ||
sarve'pi madhuraprāyāḥ svāduśītāḥ sukhapradāḥ |
katakasya ca pathyāyāḥ śalāṭuṃ lavaṇāktakam || 1,17.43 ||
nīlotpalābjayornālapuṣpaśālūkakesarāḥ |
snehayuktaṃ bhajetsnānaṃ śvetāmbaradharaḥ śuciḥ || 1,17.44 ||
kastūrīcandanahimakarpūrairanulepanam |
mandānilaṃ puṣpamālyaṃ kaumudīratnabhūṣaṇam || 1,17.45 ||
pathyaṃ rucyaṃ jalīyaṃ ca svāduprāyaṃ ca sadravam |
ghṛtakṣīrayutaṃ bhaktaṃ bhuñjītodakasevakaḥ || 1,17.46 ||
