tāmbūlacarvaṇaṃ kuryātsakarpūraṃ sukhāsanam |
nārikelajalaṃ sāyaṃ niśi kṣīraṃ saśarkaram || 1,17.47 ||
śālmalī tūlaśayyā ca niśi nidrā yathāsukham |
ūnātimātraviṣṭambhivibandhyuṣṇakharāṇi ca || 1,17.48 ||
rūkṣāhṛdyasthiragurupicchilākālabhojanam |
kulmāṣabṛhatīcoṣadvayālābunirūḍhakam || 1,17.49 ||
kulutthayūṣakāliṅgaguñjāvṛkṣaphalaṃ tathā |
palāṇḍu laśunaṃ sarvakandaṃ siddhārthakaṃ tathā || 1,17.50 ||
ajāṅgalaṃ palaṃ matsyaṃ kiṃcitkṣīrānnabhojanam |
anuktaśākamannaṃ ca phalaṃ kṣīrayutaṃ palam || 1,17.51 ||
sarvakṣīrayutānsarvānrasānamadhurānapi |
tilatailaṃ ca tatkalkametaiḥ saṃyuktamauṣadham || 1,17.52 ||
bahubhāṣyātapachāyāsamacintādhvaroṣaṇam |
śokavāhanabhītiścālasyātyantarataṃ tathā || 1,17.53 ||
mūrdhni bhāravahaścāṃbhastaraṇaṃ copavāsakam |
vyāyāmerṣyādivāsvāpo niśi jāgaraṇaṃ tathā || 1,17.54 ||
purovāto himaṃ dhūlirvātaprāyasthalaṃ tathā |
dhūmaprāyagṛhaṃ talpaṃ kārpāsaparikalpitam || 1,17.55 ||
agniḥ śramāmbujananaṃ tathoṣṇāmbaradhāraṇam |
kauśeyadhāraṇaṃ tūrvostāḍanaṃ kaṭhināsanam || 1,17.56 ||
uṣṇālayakṣārakūpasalilaṃ kaṭutiktakau |
amlaḥ kaṣāyatiktaśca rāmaṭhaṃ ca virūkṣakam || 1,17.57 ||
madirā ca vasā majjā mūtraṃ duṣṭajalāni ca |
madaṃ śītamanārogyaṃ bhojanaṃ tailavarjitam || 1,17.58 ||
abhyaṅgaṃ kāñjikaṃ śītaṃ rogābhāve kaṣāyakam |
vidāhi śoṣaṇaṃ mūrdhni snānamapyuṣṇavāribhiḥ || 1,17.59 ||
kāraṇaṃ doṣakopānāṃ nindyamanyadvivarjayet |
<pītajalajīrṇalakṣaṇam>
svasthaḥ pītvā sukhāsīnaḥ salilaṃ vāgyataḥ śuciḥ || 1,17.60 ||
visarjayenmalaṃ mūtraṃ tāmbūlādīṃśca varjayet |
yāvajjalaṃ pibettāvanmūtraṃ niryāti cettadā || 1,17.61 ||
jalaṃ jīrṇaṃ vijānīyādauṣadhena vinā yadā |
nirgacchetsalilaṃ pītaṃ tadā svasthataro mataḥ || 1,17.62 ||
nirgate pītasalile niḥśeṣe kṣutprajāyate |
tailābhyaṅgaṃ tataḥ kuryādvihitaṃ pathyamācaret || 1,17.63 ||
<jalājīrṇalakṣaṇaṃ tatparihāraśca>
parihāraṃ vinā rogā jāyante nāvicārataḥ |
śramaḥ klamaśca vamanaṃ śirastodaḥ śirobhramaḥ || 1,17.64 ||
salilastambhaśoṣau cābhiṣyando vegarodhanam |
gulmodāvartakau vahnisadanaṃ gātrabhañjanam || 1,17.65 ||
todo bastau mehane ca vakṣaṇe hṛdi locane |
jaṅghodveṣṭanakaṃ śvāso bhuktidveṣastvarocakaḥ || 1,17.66 ||
aśmarī pīnasaḥ kāsastvatisāraśca dustaraḥ |
sakṛdambhoviśoṣaścedrūkṣāyāsādikarmabhiḥ || 1,17.67 ||
sa doṣo na tu vijñeyo doṣastaṃbho'sakṛdyadi |
prapīte salile stabdhe cikitsātra vidhīyate || 1,17.68 ||
kūṣmāṇḍapatrairudaraṃ bastiṃ cācchādayetsudhīḥ |
mūtramocanakṛllepaṃ vidadhyājjalamuktaye || 1,17.69 ||
yadvājyadhātrīliptāṅgasnānaṃ kuryādrasāyanam |
saguḍaṃ vā pibetkṣīraṃ takraṃ vā śarkarānvitam || 1,17.70 ||
nālikerodakairvāpi sasitorvārubījakam |
kaṣṭājjīrṇaṃ jalaṃ yasya bhukteḥ prāksarpirādadet || 1,17.71 ||
yadvā tiṣṭhecciraṃ cāpsu snāyāddhātryādibhirhimaiḥ |
yadvā stabdhe jale pīte svāduśītairviśodhayet || 1,17.72 ||
kṛṣṇāmadanasindhūtthaiḥ kalkairmadhuyutairvamet |
pathyācūrṇaṃ nālikerajalaiḥ pītvā virecayet || 1,17.73 ||
yadvā trijātakavyoṣakrimighnāmalakābdakaiḥ |
trivṛtsarvasamā yojyā hyetatsarvasamā sitā || 1,17.74 ||
sakṣaudragulikāṃ kṛtvā lihedambhovimuktaye |
rātrau varā sevitā cetsarvadoṣavināśinī || 1,17.75 ||
nābheradhaścārdrapaṭaṃ kṣaṇamātraṃ nidhāpayet |
avagāhena śītāmbho mūrdhni vā śītavāri ca || 1,17.76 ||
nikṣipedathavā sārdrasikatātalpaśobhite |
dhārāgṛhe vā cāsīta trapusorvārubījakam || 1,17.77 ||
varīdrākṣāmbunā peyaṃ vā tu kāśmīrajaṃ vṛṣam |
drākṣārasena vā peyaṃ karkaṭībījakāni ca || 1,17.78 ||
taṇḍulakṣālanajalairmadhukaṃ vātha śarkarām |
sadrākṣaṃ ca guḍaṃ dārvīṃ taṇḍulodakayogataḥ || 1,17.79 ||
yadvā dhātrīphalarasairgostanīṃ śarkarānvitām |
varīṃ vā śarkarāyuktāṃ drākṣāṃ vā mastunā saha || 1,17.80 ||
jalena vā guḍaṃ peyaṃ nālikerajalena vā |
tugākṣīrībhavaṃ kalkaṃ vāpi karkārubījakam || 1,17.81 ||
kalpaṃ vā tāmalakyāśca mūlaṃ vā yaṣṭikalkakam |
piśācakadalīpakvaphalaṃ kṣaudrasitānvitam || 1,17.82 ||
nālikerajalairvāpi takrairvā mastunāpi vā |
rambhākandajalairvāpi kṣīrairvāmalakīrasaiḥ || 1,17.83 ||
yadvāmṛtārasairvāpi kūṣmāṇḍasvarasena vā |
dāḍimasya rasairvāpi tvabhīrośca rasaistathā || 1,17.84 ||
alābośca rasairvāpi pibedelāṃ saśarkarām |
kadalīkandayaṣṭyāhvaśvadaṃṣṭrāśca kaśerukāḥ || 1,17.85 ||
tṛṇapañcakamūlāni śukrā caiva śatāvarī |
eteṣāṃ kadalīkandapramukhānāṃ kaṣāyakam || 1,17.86 ||
sakṣaudraṃ sasitaṃ vāpi peyaṃ jalavimuktaye |
<uṣaḥpānaguṇāḥ>
evaṃ brāhme muhūrte yatpītaṃ vāri rasāyanam || 1,17.87 ||
doṣānaśeṣān śamayedrogānapi vināśayet |
raktavātaṃ ca vātāṃśca hṛdrogaṃ śvāsakāsakam || 1,17.88 ||
raktapittaṃ ca pittāni grahaṇīchardyarocakān |
kṣayamūrcchākuṣṭhamehamohagaṇḍārbudān gadān || 1,17.89 ||
gulmaplīhāśmarīśūlānāhājīrṇaviṣūcikāḥ |
krimiṃ pāṇḍuṃ kāmilāṃ ca halīmakavisarpakāḥ || 1,17.90 ||
vraṇaṃ ca śvayathuṃ śvitramagnisādaṃ tridhā viṣam |
jatrūrdhvaguhyakān rogānapasmāraṃ ca vibhramam || 1,17.91 ||
jvarānādhmānajaṭharamūtrāghātagudāṅkurān |
valīpalitanirmukto jīvettriśatavatsaram || 1,17.92 ||
sthiradhīrbalavāndhīraḥ kāntaḥ kāntāmanobhavaḥ |
<nāsāpānarasāyanam>
nāsāpuṭābhyāṃ salilaṃ yastu prātaḥ pibennaraḥ || 1,17.93 ||
śuktimātraṃ pratidinaṃ pathyāśī vijitendriyaḥ |
indriyāṇāṃ paṭutvaṃ ca valīpalitanāśanam || 1,17.94 ||
śatāyuṣyamavāpnoti nāsārandhrarasāyanam || 1,17.95 ||


āk, 1, 19
<ṛtucaryā, kālavibhāgaḥ>
śrībhairavī |
bhagavan tvatprasādena nityācārakramaḥ śrutaḥ |
svāminnaimittikācāraṃ śrotuṃ vāñchāsti me vada || 1,19.1 ||
śrībhairavaḥ |
tacchṛṇu tvaṃ mahādevi brūmi naimittikīṃ vidhim |
kālādhīnaḥ sa tu bhavedanapāyo'mitaḥ sadā || 1,19.2 ||
anādinidhanaḥ sūkṣmaḥ sthūlo vyāptaḥ sadātanaḥ |
brahmādayaśca sūryādyā mahābhūtādayaḥ pare || 1,19.3 ||
kālānusāriṇaḥ sarve prayatne kālarūpiṇaḥ |
kālādeva hi jāyante līyante tatra sarvadā || 1,19.4 ||
yaḥ kālaḥ so'hameveti tvaṃ ca kālapravartinī |
tasmājjanmabhṛtāṃ janmanidhanāya nivedyate || 1,19.5 ||
avibhājyo hi kālo'yaṃ tathāpi pravibhajyate |
tīkṣṇasūcyābdapatraṃ tu yāvatkālena bhidyate || 1,19.6 ||
sa kālo lava ityuktastruṭī triṃśallavairbhavet |
tadyo'yaṃ syāttruṭiḥ kālo mātrā syāttaddvayānvitā || 1,19.7 ||
dṛkpakṣmaṇoḥ parikṣepaḥ sa tu mātrā praśasyate |
mātrāṣṭādaśabhiḥ kāṣṭhā kāṣṭhātriṃśadyutā kalā || 1,19.8 ||
kalātriṃśatkṣaṇaḥ proktaḥ kṣaṇaiḥ ṣaḍbhiśca nāḍikā |
nāḍīdvayaṃ muhūrtaḥ syāttaiścaturbhiśca yāmakaḥ || 1,19.9 ||
yāmaiścaturbhirdivasastathā rātrirbhavetpriye |
evaṃ divāniśaṃ kiṃ tu pūrvāhṇo daśa nāḍikāḥ || 1,19.10 ||
madhyāhne daśa nāḍī syādaparāhṇastathaiva ca |
pūrvarātraṃ cārdharātraṃ tathaivāpararātrakam || 1,19.11 ||
divasaiḥ pañcadaśabhiḥ pakṣaḥ syācchuklasaṃjñakaḥ |
dvitīyaḥ kṛṣṇapakṣaḥ syāddvābhyāṃ māsastu jāyate || 1,19.12 ||
māsābhyāmṛtusaṃjñā syādṛtubhyāṃ kālasaṃjñakaḥ |
ṛtutrayaṃ syādayanaṃ dvābhyāṃ saṃvatsaro bhavet || 1,19.13 ||
aṣṭādaśaprakāreṇa kālastasmādvibhajyate |
māghādimāsāḥ kramaśo bhavanti śiśirādayaḥ || 1,19.14 ||
śiśiraśca vasantaśca grīṣmo varṣā śaraddhimaḥ |
ṛtavo dakṣiṇaṃ teṣāṃ kathayāmi śṛṇu priye || 1,19.15 ||
<hemantartusvarūpam>
tatra sūryo diśaścaiva himānīkaluṣīkṛtāḥ |
dhūmadhūmrarajomando vāyuḥ śītaprabodhanaḥ || 1,19.16 ||
romāñcakārī kaubero lavalyaḥ puṣpitāstadā |
puṃnāgalodhraphalinīpuṣpāmoditaṣaṭpadāḥ || 1,19.17 ||
gajavājīgomahiṣīkākājādyāśca garvitāḥ |
saritprāleyapaṭalasaṃchannaśakunādayaḥ || 1,19.18 ||
soṣṇabāṣpajalāḥ kūpāḥ paśya haimantike ṛtau |
<śiśirartusvarūpam>
evaṃ hi śiśire kāle cayaṃ yāti kaphaḥ svataḥ || 1,19.19 ||
<vasantartusvarūpam>
vasante vimalā dikkāḥ sūryaścāruṇadīdhitaḥ |
malayānilasañcāro navapallavaśobhitāḥ || 1,19.20 ||
navīnatvakpalāśāḍhyā vṛkṣāḥ sarvatra puṣpitāḥ |
sahakāśokabakulamādhavīcampakodbhavaiḥ || 1,19.21 ||
palāśakādimaiḥ puṣpaiḥ pravibhāti vanasthalī |
bhramadbhramaranidhvānakokilālāpasaṃkulā || 1,19.22 ||
śleṣmakopaśca bhavati kālaḥ sarvottamo hyayam |
<grīṣmartusvarūpam>
grīṣme tīkṣṇakaraścaṇḍo'tasīkusumasannibhaḥ || 1,19.23 ||
saṃtāpitamahīdikkaḥ śoṣitāśeṣabhūrasaḥ |
dāvāgninātijvalitā diśo bhūmiśca dhūsarāḥ || 1,19.24 ||
nairṛto vāyuratyugro vātyā cogrāsukhā bhavet |
ātapasvedapavanaiḥ prāṇino jvaritā iva || 1,19.25 ||
saṃtaptakroḍamahiṣamātaṅgairākulīkṛtāḥ |
caṇḍāśukiraṇottaptaśuṣkasvalpajalānvitāḥ || 1,19.26 ||
kallolitāstaṭākādyāḥ saṃtaptajalajantavaḥ |
jīrṇaśuṣkaviśīrṇaiśca parṇaiśca patitairdrumāḥ || 1,19.27 ||
soṣmāśchāyāvihīnāśca vāyuvyākulitā bhṛśam |
śuṣkapatralatāgulmāḥ śirīṣāḥ kusumojjvalāḥ || 1,19.28 ||
śleṣmakṣayaścānudinaṃ tasmādvāyoścayo bhavet |
<varṣartusvarūpam>
varṣartau paścimo vāyurmahī navatṛṇāvṛtā || 1,19.29 ||
bhinnaistathāñjanābhaiśca jaladaiśchāditākhilā |
śakragopāvṛtā pṛthvī sarvasasyamanoharā || 1,19.30 ||
bhūlatānivahacchannā jalaklinnā ca paṅkilā |
mattakekikulakrīḍānṛttavistīrṇapiñchakā || 1,19.31 ||
bhekabhīkaranidhvānabadhirīkṛtadiṅmukhā |
kadambakadalījātikuṭajāmodamedurā || 1,19.32 ||
dhārādharāmbudhārābhighātanaṣṭasaroruhā |
prabhūtasalilāpūrṇasopānā dīrghikā bhṛśam || 1,19.33 ||
patisaṃyogasadṛśamahākārā mahājalāḥ |
pūrṇobhayataṭāntāśca nadyaḥ sakaluṣodakāḥ || 1,19.34 ||
nabhaḥ stanitajīmūtaprodyadvidyutpradīpitam |
anyonyāmbudasaṃghaṭṭajātanirghoṣabhīkaram || 1,19.35 ||
indracāpalasanmeghacchāditārkendumaṇḍalam |
vanaṃ hṛṣṭājamahiṣakiṭicātakasaṅkulam || 1,19.36 ||
śleṣmā kṣīṇataro vāyoḥ kopaḥ pittacayastadā |
<śaradṛtusvarūpam>
śaradi sphuritābhāśca bhānostīkṣṇā marīcayaḥ || 1,19.37 ||
prakṣīṇavārivimalavāridonmuktamārgakāḥ |
jyotsnāmṛtarasāsiktamoditākhilajantavaḥ || 1,19.38 ||
sphuritoḍukulākīrṇā rātrayaśca manoharāḥ |
vimalākāśarucirāḥ kāmalīlotsavapradāḥ || 1,19.39 ||
mahī cāśyānapaṅkā ca kaṇikāpūrṇaśālikā |
sadasyaḥ phullakamalakumudotpalamaṇḍitāḥ || 1,19.40 ||
prahṛṣṭamīnahaṃsālīlīlālolataraṅgikāḥ |
amalāmbusarākīrṇāḥ snānapānahitapradāḥ || 1,19.41 ||
diśaḥ praphullarucirakāśacāmaraśobhitāḥ |
vimalāmoditakroñcamallikāparimaṇḍitāḥ || 1,19.42 ||
saptacchadarajoyogadṛptadantikulaṃ vanam |
prakṣīyate tadā vāyuḥ pittaṃ kupyati pārvati || 1,19.43 ||
<ādānavisargakālasvarūpam>
divāniśādimadhyānte śleṣmapittasamīraṇāḥ |
kupyanti ṛtavaḥ sarve pravartante kramācchive || 1,19.44 ||
dinamāgneyarūpaṃ syādrātriḥ saumyamayī bhavet |
dvau ca pakṣau tadā devi ṛtū hemantaśaiśirau || 1,19.45 ||
tau ṛtū himakālaḥ syāduṣṇau madhunidāghakau |
prāvṛṭśaradṛtū jñeyau varṣākālaḥ sa ucyate || 1,19.46 ||
ṛtubhiḥ śiśirādyaistattribhiḥ syāduttarāyaṇam |
tadā jñeyamiti jñeyamādānaṃ tadbhavetpriye || 1,19.47 ||
sūryānilau svabhāvena bhūmisaumyarasāpahau |
kiṃtu mārgavaśādetāvatyuṣṇakhararūkṣakau || 1,19.48 ||
ādadāte balaṃ tejaḥ prāṇināṃ prativāsaram |
tiktaḥ kaṣāyakaṭukau prabalāḥ syuryathottaram || 1,19.49 ||
prāvṛḍādyaiśca ṛtubhistribhiḥ syāddakṣiṇāyanam |
etatsomātmakaṃ viddhi visargākhyamiti smṛtam || 1,19.50 ||
prakṛtyā śītalā vṛṣṭyā vāyusomāmbudāḥ param |
balinaḥ syuryatastasmātsūryaḥ kṣīṇarucirbhavet || 1,19.51 ||
gate bhūtalatāpe'smin prāṇināṃ prativāsaram |
kālo balaṃ visṛjati prabalāḥ syuryathottaram || 1,19.52 ||
atrāmlalavaṇau svādū rasāḥ snigdhā bhavanti hi |
hemante śiśire pūrṇaṃ madhau śaradi madhyamam || 1,19.53 ||
grīṣme prāvṛṣi śītaṃ syātsarveṣāṃ prāṇināṃ balam |
<hemantartucaryā>
rasasya balinaḥ śītasaṃvṛtatvāddhimāgame || 1,19.54 ||
koṣṭhāgneścāvikīrṇatvātpiṇḍito jaṭhare yataḥ |
tato balīyān koṣṭhāgnistasmin svalpāśano yadi || 1,19.55 ||
vāyunā dīpyate vahniḥ paceddhātūn rasādikān |
tasmāddhimāgame sevyo madhuro lavaṇāmlakaḥ || 1,19.56 ||
atha dairghyānniśānāṃ tu kṣudhā prātastarāṃ bhavet |
dinacaryāprakāreṇa visṛjanmalamūtrakam || 1,19.57 ||
dantakāṣṭhādikaṃ sarvaṃ vidadhyātparameśvari |
tato mūrdhārhavātaghnatailenābhyaṅgamācaret || 1,19.58 ||
gātrābhyaṅgaṃ mardanaṃ ca yāvacchakyaṃ ca yathāsukham |
kuśalairbāhuyuddhaṃ ca yāvacchakyaṃ bhajettataḥ || 1,19.59 ||
godhūmacaṇakairmudgairhṛtatailo yathāvidhi |
snātvā kauśeyeke rakte laghunyuṣṇe susāndrake || 1,19.60 ||
vāsasī paridhāyaiva kastūryā kuṅkumena ca |
kālāgarudraveṇaiva carcāṃ kurvīta vigrahe || 1,19.61 ||
dhūpayeddehacikurānkālāgarujadhūpataḥ |
campakaṃ bakulaṃ puṣpaṃ śatapatraṃ ca kaitakam || 1,19.62 ||
javādyairupaliptāni kṛtvā śirasi dhārayet |
guḍāsavaṃ madyamaṇḍaṃ madyaṃ māṃsaṃ ca meduram || 1,19.63 ||
snigdhaṃ māṃsarasaṃ soṣṇaṃ māṣagodhūmapiṣṭajān |
ikṣukṣīravikārāṃśca soṣṇamannaṃ tilodbhavam || 1,19.64 ||
śauce sukhoṣṇaṃ salilaṃ soṣmalaṃ cāru mandiram |
śayanaṃ kauthakaślakṣṇamṛdulājinakambalaiḥ || 1,19.65 ||
prāvāraiḥ śubhakauśeyaiḥ krameṇāstṛtamujjvalam |
bhajedavṛddhasūryāṃśūn pṛṣṭhabhāgena śāmbhavi || 1,19.66 ||
svedaṃ copānahaṃ nityaṃ hasantītīvratāpite |
garbhagehe nivāsaṃ ca vṛttapīnonnatastanīḥ || 1,19.67 ||
rathāṅgavṛttasuśroṇyo rambhāstambhorumaṇḍitāḥ |
madyapānamadonmattā vastrasraggandhaśobhitāḥ || 1,19.68 ||
priyāḥ prītāḥ samāśliṣyenna bādhā śītadoṣajā |
kastūrī kuṅkumaṃ candraṃ lavaṅgaṃ jātikāphalam || 1,19.69 ||
pratyekaṃ niṣkamekaṃ syātsāraḥ khadirasambhavaḥ |
yuñjyātṣoḍaśaniṣkaṃ ca sarvaṃ peṣyaṃ himāmbunā || 1,19.70 ||
gulikāṃ maricākārāṃ kuryāddaurgandhyanāśinīm |
vātaśleṣmaharā rucyā guṭikaiṣā prakīrtitā || 1,19.71 ||
guṭyanvitaṃ ca tāmbūlaṃ yatheṣṭaṃ bhakṣayetsadā |
evaṃ hemantacaryā syātśiśire'pyamumācaret || 1,19.72 ||
śleṣmā citaḥ syānnitarāṃ bhajeddhaimantikaṃ vidhim |
atrādānabhavaṃ rūkṣaṃ bhavecchīto mahattaraḥ || 1,19.73 ||
<vasantartucaryā>
śiśire tu citaḥ śleṣmā vasante'rkāṃśuvidrutaḥ |
nāśayejjāṭharaṃ vahniṃ svayaṃ toyasvabhāvataḥ || 1,19.74 ||
janayedrogamakhilaṃ tasmācchīghraṃ kaphaṃ jayet |
atha vāsantikāṃ caryāṃ kathayāmi mama priye || 1,19.75 ||
tīkṣṇāñjanacchardinasyairvyāyāmodvartanairapi |
pādābhyāṃ mardanenoṣmavārisnānena taṃ haret || 1,19.76 ||
kāṣāyaraktakausumbhakauśeyavasanāni ca |
paridhāya sakarpūrāgaruṇāṅgāni dhūpayet || 1,19.77 ||
kastūrīkuṅkumahimaiścandanairlepayettanum |
cūtacampakapunnāgapūgakesarapāṭalam || 1,19.78 ||
mādhavīketakīmallikāśokanavamālikāḥ |
javādyairlepitāḥ kṛtvā vahetkalhāramutpalam || 1,19.79 ||
madyaṃ pañcavidhaṃ proktaṃ sarvavyādhiharaṃ param |
mādhavāriṣṭamārdvīkāsavaśīthuritīritāḥ || 1,19.80 ||
kṣaudrairmadakaradravyai racito mādhavaḥ smṛtaḥ |
ciraṃ madakaradravyaṃ khanitvā sthāpitaṃ bhuvi || 1,19.81 ||
ariṣṭo'yamiti jñeyo mārdvīko gostanībhavaḥ |
āsavo madakṛddravyasavanādvā samudbhavaḥ || 1,19.82 ||
śīthurikṣurasājjātaḥ sevyāḥ pañca mayā matāḥ |
kāminīvadanāmbhojavāsitāścittahāriṇaḥ || 1,19.83 ||
puṣpādivāsitā hṛdyāḥ kāntānayanarañjitāḥ |
purāṇāḥ kaṭukāstiktāḥ kaṣāyāḥ śleṣmahāriṇaḥ || 1,19.84 ||
mātuluṅgāmrajambūnāṃ patrapuṣpaphalānvitāḥ |
suhṛdbhiḥ saha jaṃbīrarasārdrakapalāṇḍukam || 1,19.85 ||
jāṅgalaṃ palalaṃ śūlyaṃ tadrasaṃ ca kṛtaṃ navam |
purāṇakṣaudragodhūmayavaṣaṣṭikaśālikān || 1,19.86 ||
bhajetkaphaghnaṃ śākaṃ ca vyañjanaṃ pānakaṃ tathā |
amadyapastu śuṇṭhyambu mustāmbu kṣaudravāri vā || 1,19.87 ||
khadirāsanasārotthakvathitaṃ vāri vā pibet |
udyāne saudhakācādyairmaṇinīkāśarocibhiḥ || 1,19.88 ||
kuṭṭimairmaṇḍite cārumaṇḍape suratocite |
kokilālāparucire sugandhikusumojjvale || 1,19.89 ||
bahupādapasacchāyāvāritoṣṇāśudīdhitau |
samantātsalilāpūrṇakulyābhiḥ śītalīkṛte || 1,19.90 ||
malayānilasañcāraśamitaśramavāriṇi |
nānāprasūnasubhagaśākhinīnālanandite || 1,19.91 ||
lambamānasugandhasraṅmakarandābhiṣecite |
uśīrapāṭalīpaṅktiśobhite bisavistṛte || 1,19.92 ||
puṣpairākīrṇite mandatālavṛntānilojjvale |
samāsīnaḥ priyālāpacaturāḥ kāmalolupāḥ || 1,19.93 ||
kandarpadarpasarvasvāḥ sūkṣmasvacchāṃbarāḥ priyāḥ |
taruṇī ramayan goṣṭhīḥ kurvaṃstābhirmanoharāḥ || 1,19.94 ||
suhṛdbhirāptaiḥ sahito madhyāhnaṃ gamayetsukhī |
atiśītagurusnigdhasvādvamlalavaṇāni ca || 1,19.95 ||
tilekṣukṣīravikṛtidivāsvapnātipicchilam |
matsyaṃ kṣārodakaṃ pūpaṃ śleṣmalaṃ varjayenmadhau || 1,19.96 ||
<grīṣmartucaryā>
grīṣme hyatyarthatīkṣṇaḥ syādgharmabhānurbhuvo rasān |
snigdhān guṇāṃśca kṣapayetkaphastasmātkṣayaṃ vrajet || 1,19.97 ||
tena vāyuścitaḥ syācca pūritaiḥ śītalairjalaiḥ |
candanāgarukastūrīkuṅkumaiśca sugandhibhiḥ || 1,19.98 ||
prasūnairvividhaiḥ phullairvāsite mallikādibhiḥ |
kāminīpīnavakṣojadaghnāmbuni suśodhite || 1,19.99 ||
hrade vā dīrghikāyāṃ vā saṃskṛtāyāṃ yathāvidhi |
ā karṇapūrṇanayanavirājitamukhendubhiḥ || 1,19.100 ||
mṛṇālabhūṣaṇodbhāsiśirīṣamṛdubāhubhiḥ |
karpūramuktākusumamālāmalayajojjvalaiḥ || 1,19.101 ||
sūkṣmakausumbhavasanabaddhamadhyanitambibhiḥ |
svanūpuraravākṛṣṭasārasārāvarañjitaiḥ || 1,19.102 ||
jalakrīḍāticaturaiḥ saṃyuktaḥ kāminījanaiḥ |
saṃkrīḍeta karonmuktavārisiktāṅganāmukhaḥ || 1,19.103 ||
drutiśṛṅgamukhotsṛṣṭavārisecitavigrahaḥ |
tābhirbhujāntaraṃ śliṣyan kurvan līlāṃ muhurmuhuḥ || 1,19.104 ||
uttīrya ca vapurvastrairudvartya cikurānsukham |
svacche sūkṣme kaṣāye ca vasane dhārayettataḥ || 1,19.105 ||
candanāgarukarpūrairdhūpayetkeśavigrahau |
śrīkhaṇḍacandrakastūrīpaṅkacarcitavigrahaḥ || 1,19.106 ||
nepālamālatīmallīpāṭalīśatapatrakāḥ |
saugandhikaṃ maruvakaṃ hrīveraṃ ca mṛgāṇḍajaiḥ || 1,19.107 ||
sugandhatailairliptāni dhārayetkusumāni ca |
atra madyaṃ na peyaṃ syādathavā madyasātmyakaiḥ || 1,19.108 ||
svalpaṃ peyaṃ tu teneṣanna tṛptiścettadā jalaiḥ |
svādubhirbahubhiryuktaṃ pibenmadyaṃ yathāsukham || 1,19.109 ||
tathā nocedbhaveddhātuśoṣo'ntardāhamohabhāk |
śithilāvayavakṣīṇaprāṇabuddhibalaḥ priye || 1,19.110 ||
madhuraṃ śītalaṃ snigdhaṃ dravaṃ laghu hitaṃ bhajet |
sadyaḥ saśarkaraṃ lihyācchāleyaṃ jāṅgalaṃ palam || 1,19.111 ||
ameduraṃ māṃsarasaṃ rasālāṃ pānakaṃ pibet |
pañcasāraṃ ca rāgaṃ ca ṣāḍavaṃ cāthavā hitam || 1,19.112 ||
<rasālāpānake>
śarkarāmaricopetaṃ dadhi hastaviloḍitam |
etadrasālā vikhyātā rambhāpanasacūtajaiḥ || 1,19.113 ||
phalaiḥ saśarkarāmbhobhirnavamṛtpātragaṃ kṛtam |
īṣadamlamiti khyātaṃ pānakaṃ taddhitaṃkaram || 1,19.114 ||
<pañcasāraḥ>
mṛdvīkārājakharjūramadhūkakusumāni ca |
śrīparṇīkṣudrakharjūrīparipakvaphalāni ca || 1,19.115 ||
saśarkarāmbu nikṣipya dṛḍhaṃ hastena peṣayet |
saṃsthāpya navamṛdbhāṇḍe hyanyedyurvastraśodhitam || 1,19.116 ||
pañcasāra iti khyāto hṛdyo vātakaphāpahaḥ |
<rāgaṣāḍavau>
sitākṣaudrādimadhuradravyayuktaṃ phalaṃ ca yat || 1,19.117 ||
svacchaṃ rāga iti jñeyaḥ sarvasantāpanāśanaḥ |
ayameva tu sāndraścellehyaṃ ṣāḍava īritaḥ || 1,19.118 ||
sa ca vastreṇa saṃśuddhaḥ peyaṣāḍava ucyate |
etatpañcavidhaṃ pānaṃ pāṭalīcampakādibhiḥ || 1,19.119 ||
elayā vāsitaṃ śuddhaṃ mṛdbhāṇḍe sthāpite nave |
uśīratālavṛntasya vāyunā śītalīkṛtam || 1,19.120 ||
mocacocadalopetameṣā sāmānyasaṃskṛtiḥ |
atha nūtanabhāṇḍāntaḥ pūritaṃ svādu nirmalam || 1,19.121 ||
pāṭalīketakīpuṣpakarpūrasurabhīkṛtam |
sāndrāṃbareṇa saṃvītaṃ tālavṛntaiḥ suśītalam || 1,19.122 ||
pibejjalaṃ tālasālapūgakharjūrapādapaiḥ |
nālikerāmrapanasajambūpunnāgacampakaiḥ || 1,19.123 ||
karapraceyavyālambiphalapuṣpakagucchakaiḥ |
drākṣāstabakasaṃchannaśākhāntaritarājitaiḥ || 1,19.124 ||
mādhavīstabakālīnabhṛṅgagītābhinanditaiḥ |
nānāsugandhitarubhirvāryamāṇārkadīdhitau || 1,19.125 ||
haṃsasārasakāraṇḍaśobhamānasarovare |
paritaḥ pravahatkulyātaraṅgānilaśītale || 1,19.126 ||
nṛtyatkekikalākīrṇakokilālāpaśobhite |
śārikāśukasaṃlāpamuhyanmānavatījane || 1,19.127 ||
udyāne bālakośīravṛtau salilasecite |
mṛṇālapadmakalhārotpalapallavanirmite || 1,19.128 ||
sugandhipuṣpamālābhirlambamāne suśītale |
ārdrāmbaraiśca racitacchāyānavapaṭālike || 1,19.129 ||
mādhavīmaṇḍape ramye sarvasantāpahāriṇi |
kadalīmṛṇālakusumapallavaiḥ parikalpitām || 1,19.130 ||
cārūttamacchadapaṭāṃ himāmbupariṣecitām |
śayyāmatyantamṛdulāṃ santāpaśramahāriṇīm || 1,19.131 ||
tuṣāraśītalataraiścarmavastrābhipūritaiḥ |
sūkṣmairjalalavaiḥ siktaḥ savayobhiḥ samanvitaḥ || 1,19.132 ||
madhyāhnaṃ gamayedevaṃ tathā dhārāgṛhe'thavā |
dīvyan pustamayīkāntāstanahastamukhacyutaiḥ || 1,19.133 ||
bālośīrāmbubhiḥ śīte svacchasphaṭikapaṭṭake |
karpūracandanālipte luṭhaṃstāpapraśāntaye || 1,19.134 ||
pratyagrasnānaśītāṅgyo muktābharaṇabhūṣitāḥ |
mṛṇālacandanālepā mṛṇālavalayānvitāḥ || 1,19.135 ||
svacchāmbarātirucirāḥ pramadāstāpahāriṇīḥ |
cumbanāliṅganasparśaistoṣayan parihāsayan || 1,19.136 ||
puṣpamālāviracitavitānapariśobhite |
śītāṃśukiraṇasparśadravaccandropalojjvale || 1,19.137 ||
candrikākāntilasite mandānilavirājite |
saudhasthale samāsīnaḥ śaśāṅkakiraṇāhvayān || 1,19.138 ||
bhakṣānaśnīta tārendukiraṇaiḥ śītalīkṛtam |
saśarkaraṃ pibetkṣīraṃ māhiṣaṃ balakṛddhitam || 1,19.139 ||
nidāghahaṃ śarīrasya mālācandanadhāriṇaḥ |
svacchāṃśukāvṛtāṅgasya samāptaratikarmaṇaḥ || 1,19.140 ||
jalārdrāṃśukavātena tālavṛntānilena ca |
vistāritābjapatrasya vījanaiścāmbuvarṣibhiḥ || 1,19.141 ||
mayūratālavṛntaiśca tathā ca haricandanaiḥ |
karpūramallikāmuktāmālābhirmadhuraiḥ priye || 1,19.142 ||
śārikāśukabālānāmālāpairbisabhūṣaṇaiḥ |
phullakalhārakamalanīlotpalavirājitaiḥ || 1,19.143 ||
karpūracandanālepaiḥ kāntaiśca pramadājanaiḥ |
tāpaḥ saṃhriyate cāsya cādānoṣṇābhitāpinaḥ || 1,19.144 ||
saktukaṭvamlalavaṇarūkṣāyāsātapāṃstyajet |
<varṣartucaryā>
athāto vārṣikīṃ caryāṃ śṛṇu vakṣyāmi bhairavi || 1,19.145 ||
ādānakṣīṇadhātūnāṃ narāṇāṃ jaṭharānalaḥ |
kṣīṇo'pi varṣāsamaye doṣaiḥ sīdati satvaram || 1,19.146 ||
savārivāridavrātatirohitadivākare |
vyomni jhañjhāsamīreṇa śītalena tuṣāriṇā || 1,19.147 ||
vātaḥ syātkupito'tyantaṃ prataptāyā nidāghataḥ |
bhūmermeghādisiktāyā bāṣpaiḥ kālasvabhāvataḥ || 1,19.148 ||
amlapākaiśca lūtādimalinaiḥ salilaistathā |
pittaṃ kupyati cātyarthaṃ durdinatvācca jāṭharaḥ || 1,19.149 ||
vahniḥ sīdati tenaiva śleṣmā kupyati dustaraḥ |
evamanyonyaduṣṭāḥ syurdoṣāḥ sādhāraṇaṃ tataḥ || 1,19.150 ||
mardanaṃ ca śiro'bhyaṅgaṃ tailairnārāyaṇairbhajet |
uṣṇodakaiśca bahubhiḥ snātvā saṃmārjayettanum || 1,19.151 ||
raktaṃ cāpyathavā śuklaṃ māñjiṣṭhaṃ vātha dhārayet |
sāndreṇāgarudhūpena śarīraṃ dhūpayecchive || 1,19.152 ||
kastūrīṃ kuṅkumaṃ cāru bhajetkālāgarudravam |
kuṭajaṃ ketakīṃ jātiṃ maruvaṃ karavīrakam || 1,19.153 ||
snigdhajāṅgalamāṃsāni saṃskṛtāṃstadrasānapi |
mudgāḍhakakulutthānāṃ pibedyūṣaṃ ca saṃskṛtam || 1,19.154 ||
ariṣṭākhyaṃ ca maireyaṃ purāṇamathavā pibet |
sauvarcalayutaṃ mastu pañcakolarajoyutam || 1,19.155 ||
bhajeduṣṇakaraṃ sarvaṃ śuddhakoṣṭho bhavennaraḥ |
kuryātkaṣāyabastiṃ ca jīrṇadhānyāśanaṃ bhajet || 1,19.156 ||
divyaṃ vā kaupamudakaṃ śṛtaṃ peyaṃ sukhāvaham |
atyantavātavarṣe'hni prāyeṇa lavaṇāmlakam || 1,19.157 ||
madhuraṃ laghu sakṣaudraṃ śuṣkanistālitāni ca |
vyañjanāni ca tailaṃ ca vaṭakānparpaṭānbhajet || 1,19.158 ||
yathāsukhaṃ ca tāmbūlaṃ kastūrīphalasaṃyutam |
netevanīsyādagarudhūpitāmbaramāvahet || 1,19.159 ||
sugandhāvayavastiṣṭhetsaudhe bhūbāṣpavarjite |
tuṣāraśītarahite suramye'garudhūpite || 1,19.160 ||
divāsuptiṃ nadītoyaṃ saktuṃ jalaghṛtāplutam |
vyāyāmamarkakiraṇān saṃgamātyantikaṃ tyajet || 1,19.161 ||
<śaradṛtucaryā>
athātaḥ śāradīṃ caryāṃ vakṣyāmi śṛṇu vāṅmayi |
varṣartau śītavṛṣṭibhyāṃ sahasaiva raveḥ karaiḥ || 1,19.162 ||
taptāṅgānāṃ nṛṇāṃ pittaṃ citaṃ vṛṣṭau tu śārade |
sutarāṃ kupyati tadā tasmātpittāpanuttaye || 1,19.163 ||
pittaghnatailenābhyaṅgaṃ mardanaṃ mṛdu kalpayet |
sukhoṣṇavāriṇā snānaṃ śuklakāṣāyamambaram || 1,19.164 ||
dhṛtvā cāgarukarpūradhūmenāṅgāni lepayet |
bhadraśrīhimaliptāṅgaḥ ketakībhallakāni ca || 1,19.165 ||
campakāmbujapatrāṇi vahenmṛgamadānvitam |
virecanaṃ sirāmokṣaṃ tiktājyāsvādanaṃ bhajet || 1,19.166 ||
śāligodhūmamudgaṃ ca paṭolakṣaudraśarkarāḥ |
jāṅgalaṃ piśitaṃ tiktaṃ kaṣāyaṃ madhurān bhajet || 1,19.167 ||
bubhukṣitastu laghvannaṃ dhātrīṃ śīthuṃ ghṛtaṃ payaḥ |
pānakaṃ yāvasaṃ cekṣunālikerodakaṃ navam || 1,19.168 ||
agastyodayasaṃśuddhanirviṣaṃ laghu śītalam |
arkendukiraṇottaptaśītaṃ haṃsodakaṃ pibet || 1,19.169 ||
maudgayūṣaṃ pradoṣe tu himacandanacarcitaḥ |
sugandhamālānirdhautavasanālaṃkṛtaḥ sukhī || 1,19.170 ||
muktāmālāpariṣkāraḥ sugandhośīralepitaḥ |
ramyārāmāparivṛte harmye jyotsnātisundare || 1,19.171 ||
samāsīnaścandrapādān seveta taruṇīyutaḥ |
svādan svādaṃścekṣudaṇḍān pittaghnamadirāmapi || 1,19.172 ||
pāyaṃ pāyaṃ sukhaṃ tiṣṭhankrīḍayankāminījanam |
yavakṣārādikān kṣārān himamātṛpti bhojanam || 1,19.173 ||
tilatailaṃ ravikarāndivā nidrāṃ vasāṃ dadhi |
tīkṣṇaṃ madyaṃ tyajedvastu prācīvāyuṃ ca pittakṛt || 1,19.174 ||
<sarvartusādhāraṇī caryā>
atha sādhāraṇī caryā saṃkṣepādvakṣyate śive |
svādvamlalavaṇānprāyo himavarṣāgame bhajet || 1,19.175 ||
tiktoṣaṇakaṣāyāṃśca vasante nitarāṃ bhajet |
nidāghe madhuraprāyaṃ bhajedvarṣātyaye punaḥ || 1,19.176 ||
tiktasvādukaṣāyāṃśca varṣartau ca bhajetkramāt |
madhau rūkṣoṣṇamaśnīyādgrīṣme snigdhaṃ ca śītalam || 1,19.177 ||
prāvṛṭśiśirahemante snigdhaṃ coṣṇataraṃ bhajet |
meghātyaye rūkṣaśītaṃ vidhinānena sevayet || 1,19.178 ||
tattadṛtūktānadhikān rasān seveta cānvaham |
anyānapi rasānsarvānalpamātraṃ yathāruci || 1,19.179 ||
ekasyāntyaṃ ca saptāhamanyasya dinasaptakam |
evaṃ caturdaśadinamṛtusaṃdhiriti smṛtaḥ || 1,19.180 ||
vartamānāmṛtoścaryāṃ mandaṃ mandaṃ samutsṛjet |
āgāmina ṛtoścaryāmṛtusaṃdhau bhajetkramāt || 1,19.181 ||
sātmyadravyavisargācca hyasātmyadravyasevanāt |
rogā bhavanti tasmāttacchīlatyāgau śanairvrajet || 1,19.182 ||
ṛtucaryāmiti bhajannāyurārogyamāpnuyāt |
<annapākakramo dhātumalādyutpattikramaśca>
annapākakramaṃ vakṣye samāsena surārcite || 1,19.183 ||
bhuktamannaṃ ca sakalaṃ koṣṭhakaṃ prāṇavāyunā |
āhṛtaṃ taddravairbhinnasaṃghātaṃ mārdavaṃ punaḥ || 1,19.184 ||
snehena nītaṃ koṣṭhāgnirāmāśayagataṃ pacet |
samānavāyunoddīpto jāṭharastu yathā bahiḥ || 1,19.185 ||
sthālīsthaṃ taṇḍulaṃ toyaṃ pacedannaṃ ca pāvakaḥ |
bhuktamātreṇa tatsarvaṃ ṣaḍrasaṃ madhurāyate || 1,19.186 ||
pacyamānaṃ kaphotpādyaiḥ phenaḥ syānmadhurādibhiḥ |
phenastu kaphatattvaṃ yātyamlatāṃ ca vidāhataḥ || 1,19.187 ||
āmāśayāccyutaṃ tacca pittaṃ bhavati nirmalam |
pakvāśayamanuprāptamagninā pariśoṣitam || 1,19.188 ||
piṇḍitaṃ paripakvaṃ syānmahataḥ pākataḥ kaṭuḥ |
pārthivaścāpya āgneyo vāyavyaśceti nābhasaḥ || 1,19.189 ||
pañcoṣmāṇaḥ pārthivādīnannānanu pacanti ca |
pañcāhāraguṇān pakvāṃstathā bhūtaguṇānapi || 1,19.190 ||
pṛthakpṛthakyathāsvaṃ ca puṣṇantyete yathāsukham |
bhaumo bhaumāṃstathaivānyānanye dehagatānkramāt || 1,19.191 ||
pakvaṃ tadannaṃ dvividhaṃ kiṭṭasāraprabhedataḥ |
kiṭṭaṃ tu dvividhaṃ cācchaṃ mūtraṃ sāndraṃ śakṛdbhavet || 1,19.192 ||
punaḥ sāraṃ pacantyeva yathāsvaṃ sapta vahnayaḥ |
dhātugāstu tato devi rasādraktaṃ bhavettataḥ || 1,19.193 ||
māṃsaṃ māṃsādbhavenmedastasmādasthi prajāyate |
asthno majjā tataḥ śukraṃ śukrādgarbho bhavetpriye || 1,19.194 ||
prasādaśeṣajānvakṣye rasātstanyamasṛktataḥ |
sirāśca kaṇḍarā māṃsātṣaṭtvacaśca vasā bhavet || 1,19.195 ||
medasaḥ snāyusandhī ca śeṣaṃ naśyatyataḥ param |
rasasya kiṭṭaṃ śleṣmā syādasṛjaḥ pittameva ca || 1,19.196 ||
māṃsasya kiṭṭaṃ khamalā medaso gharmavāri ca |
asthno romāṇi keśāḥ syurmajjāyāstvakṣiviṭtvacām || 1,19.197 ||
snehaḥ śuklasya caujaḥ syātkramāddhātumalāḥ smṛtāḥ |
kiṭṭaṃ prasādo bhavati dhātūnāṃ paripākataḥ || 1,19.198 ||
itaretarasaṃstambhā dhātusnehaparamparā |
āhārātsādhu jīrṇādyo jātaḥ sāro raso hi saḥ || 1,19.199 ||
tasmādrasastu dhātūnāṃ raktādīnāṃ ca vardhanaḥ |
narāṇāṃ dhātavaḥ sapta santi strīṇāṃ yathā tathā || 1,19.200 ||
puṃsāṃ śuklaṃ ca śirasi nārīṇāṃ hṛṣṭamānase |
patisaṅge tu tacchuklaṃ sravanti smaramandire || 1,19.201 ||
taccāmbusadṛśaṃ svacchaṃ saumyaṃ garbhāya no bhavet |
bhuktamannaṃ divārātraṃ śuklavṛddhiṃ karoti tat || 1,19.202 ||
saptāhādacchaśuklaṃ syānmāsādgarbhakṣamaṃ hi tat |
evaṃ pākakrameṇaiva bhavetṣāṇmāturāṃbike || 1,19.203 ||
vṛṣyauṣadhaprabhāveṇa sadyaḥ śuklādi jāyate |
caṅkramadbhojyadhātūnāṃ parivṛttiḥ sadā bhavet || 1,19.204 ||
<pācakāgnisvarūpam>
jāṭharo bhautikaścaiva dhātavīyo'gnayaḥ smṛtāḥ |
eteṣu jāṭharaḥ śreṣṭho yenānnaṃ paripacyate || 1,19.205 ||
sa eva mūlaṃ sarveṣāmagnīnāṃ tatkṣaye kṣayaḥ |
tadvṛddhau ca bhavedvṛddhistanmūlaṃ jīvitaṃ balam || 1,19.206 ||
devi tasmāddhitaiḥ sātmyairannapānākhyadārubhiḥ |
pālayettaṃ prayatnena tadāyattā hyarogatā || 1,19.207 ||
caturvidhaḥ sa evāgnistīkṣṇo mandaḥ samo'samaḥ |
pittābhimūrchite vāyau samāne tīkṣṇapāvakaḥ || 1,19.208 ||
asamyagbahu vā bhuktaṃ pacecchīghraṃ tu pittajān |
rogānkuryāttu mandāgniḥ samāne kaphapīḍite || 1,19.209 ||
samyagbhuktaṃ mitaṃ vāpi hitaṃ cānnaṃ cirātpacet |
vaktraśoṣādhmānatāśca gauravaṃ cāntrakūjanam || 1,19.210 ||
āṭopamasakṛtkuryācchleṣmajānāmayānapi |
svasthānasthe samāne tu samo'gnirabhidhīyate || 1,19.211 ||
samyagbhuktaṃ pacetkāle tvārogyaphalado bhavet |
asamo'gniramārgasthe samāne syātsubhojanam || 1,19.212 ||
cirātpacettu durbhuktamacirādvātajān gadān |
kuryāttasmādapramattaḥ samāgniṃ rakṣayetpriye || 1,19.213 ||
yāmadvaye pacettīkṣṇaḥ ṣaḍyāmānmandapāvakaḥ |
kṛcchrādannaṃ samāgnistu caturyāmātpacetsukham || 1,19.214 ||
asamāgniḥ kadācittu śīghraṃ vā mandameva vā |
nābhisthāne sthito vahniḥ sarveṣāṃ prāṇināmapi || 1,19.215 ||
gajoṣṭraturagādīnāṃ vahniraṅguṣṭhamātrakaḥ |
paśumartyamṛgāṇāṃ ca yavamātrānalo bhavet || 1,19.216 ||
gṛdhrolūkabakādīnāṃ jāṭharāgnistilonmitaḥ |
kṛmikīṭādijantūnāṃ keśamātro hutāśanaḥ || 1,19.217 ||
pradīpto jāṭharo vahnirādāvannaṃ pacettataḥ |
annābhāve paceddoṣāndoṣe kṣīṇe pacettataḥ || 1,19.218 ||
dhātūndhātukṣaye prāṇānsaṃharetprāṇināṃ param || 1,19.219 ||


āk, 1, 20
<yogarasāyanam>
śrībhairavī |
devadeva kṛpāmbhodhe kālakandarpanāśana |
aṣṭamūrte mahāmūrte pañcakṛtyaparāyaṇa || 1,20.1 ||
prapañcitaṃ jagatsarvaṃ tryambaka tripurāntaka |
jaṭākalitabhogīndraphūtkāraklāntacandramaḥ || 1,20.2 ||
sūryenduvahninayana smerapañcānana prabho |
kuṇḍalāhiphaṇāratnadyotamānakapolabhūḥ || 1,20.3 ||
kundāgradantasubhagapallavādharaśobhita |
hālāhalāsitagala saptabhogīndrabhūṣaṇa || 1,20.4 ||
mahāhivalayaprodyadaṣṭādaśabhujojjvala |
sarvadivyāyudhopeta varavyāghrājināṃbara || 1,20.5 ||
namatsurāsurādhīśamakuṭotpalaraśmibhiḥ |
nīrājitapadadvandva yogijanmajarāpaha || 1,20.6 ||
saccidānandavibhava prasanna karuṇāmbudhe |
oṅkāragamya vimalātarkyācintyāprameya bhoḥ || 1,20.7 ||
stotā stutyaḥ stutistvaṃ hi kartā kāryaṃ ca kārakaḥ |
sarvo'pi hi tvamevāsi prasīda parameśvara || 1,20.8 ||
tvanmāyayā jagatsarvaṃ sṛṣṭaṃ trātaṃ hataṃ tathā |
tvaṃ bhūstvamāpastvaṃ vahnistvaṃ vāyustvaṃ nabhaḥ śaśī || 1,20.9 ||
ravistvaṃ paramātmā tvaṃ guṇāstvaṃ prakṛtistathā |
puruṣastvaṃ manastvaṃ ca buddhiścittamahaṃkṛtiḥ || 1,20.10 ||
rasāyanaṃ ca sakalamākhyātuṃ ca savistaram |
ājñāpayāhaṃ yadi te hṛdyā prāṇapriyā vibho || 1,20.11 ||
jīvanmuktiḥ kathaṃ nātha yogarūpaṃ ca kīdṛśam |
anugṛhṇīṣva deveśa sukhopāyaṃ bhavāpaham || 1,20.12 ||
śrutvā stutiṃ smitamukho bhairavaḥ parayā mudā |
uvāca devīṃ kalyāṇīṃ parvatādhipanandinīm || 1,20.13 ||
śrībhairavaḥ |
sādhu sādhu mahāmāye sarvaṃ vetsi sanātane |
tathāpi pṛcchasīśāni lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā || 1,20.14 ||
pravakṣyāmi samāsena sāvadhānaṃ śṛṇu priye |
tvatto'nyā vallabhā kā me rahasyārthavibhāṣaṇe || 1,20.15 ||
<jīvanmuktalakṣaṇam>
śṛṇu vakṣyāmi deveśi jīvanmuktasya lakṣaṇam |
kāmaṃ krodhaṃ bhayaṃ lobhaṃ madaṃ mohaṃ ca matsaram || 1,20.16 ||
mānaṃ lajjāṃ kulaṃ śīlaṃ kutsāṃ dambhaṃ ca vañcanām |
avidyāṃ jaḍatāṃ garvaṃ śītamuṣṇaṃ tathātapam || 1,20.17 ||
vātaṃ sukhaṃ ca duḥkhaṃ ca pāpaṃ puṇyaṃ hitāhitam |
tāpatrayaṃ putramitrakalatrādīni yastyajet || 1,20.18 ||
na saktaḥ sarvaviṣaye tattvacintāparāyaṇaḥ |
padmapatramivāmbhobhirnirliptahṛdayo bhavet || 1,20.19 ||
maitrīkṛtātaṭopekṣāmadaitairmaṇḍitāśayaḥ |
aihikāmuṣmikasukhaprāptikāryāviraktadhīḥ || 1,20.20 ||
nityānityavivekajño hyantaḥkaraṇanigrahaḥ |
jarāmaraṇahīnaśca śivasāmarasātmavān || 1,20.21 ||
jīvanmuktaḥ sa vijñeyastīrṇasaṃsārasāgaraḥ |
devadaityādibhirvandyaḥ sa sevyaḥ sa guruḥ śivaḥ || 1,20.22 ||
yathāhaṃ sarvalokeṣu pūjanīyo maheśvari |
tathāsau sarvalokeṣu sarvaiḥ sampūjyate sadā || 1,20.23 ||
dehānteṣu tu muktiryā prāṇināṃ sāprayojanā |
dehānte dehinaḥ sarve muktiṃ yānti na saṃśayaḥ || 1,20.24 ||
bhagasaṃdrāvaṇānmuktirbhaveccedgardabhādayaḥ |
pakṣiṇo vṛṣabhā meṣāḥ kiṃ muktāstripurāmbike || 1,20.25 ||
retoviṇmūtrasevāyāṃ yadi muktirbhavetpriye |
kapiśca sūkarādyāśca kathaṃ muktā bhavanti te || 1,20.26 ||
na kevalāmaratvācca na śivatvādbhavettathā |
taddvayormelanācca syājjīvanmuktiriyaṃ smṛtā || 1,20.27 ||
sarvasminsamaye śāstre muktirastyantakālajā |
na dṛśyate karāntasthamaṇivatsā ca śāmbhavi || 1,20.28 ||
atigopyamavācyaṃ yaddevānāmapi durlabham |
kathayiṣyāmi deveśi dehasthairyaṃ sadātanam || 1,20.29 ||
<jīvanmuktisādhakaśarīrasthairyasaṃpādanopāyaḥ; yogasiddhiḥ>
śivatvaṃ khecaratvaṃ ca sarvasiddhipradaṃ śubham |
dehaṃ vinā na kiṃcitsyāddeho'yaṃ sarvasādhanam || 1,20.30 ||
tasmāddehaṃ prayatnena rakṣayetsarvataḥ sadā |
dehapāte dharmanāśo dharmanāśe kriyācyutiḥ || 1,20.31 ||
kriyācyutau kuto yogo yogabhraṃśe na cidbhavet |
cidabhāve kuto mokṣo mokṣe bhraṣṭe na kiṃcana || 1,20.32 ||
anyopāyaśatenāpi na deho dhāryate sadā |
pāradaḥ pavanaśca syātsarvasiddhida uttamaḥ || 1,20.33 ||
saṃmūrchitau mṛtau baddhāvubhau pavanapāradau |
kramādrogaharau nityaṃ mṛtyughnau khecarapradau || 1,20.34 ||
rasaḥ pūrvaṃ mayā khyāto'dhunā vāyuḥ praśasyate |
vāyoḥ saṃdhāraṇājjñānaṃ jñānānmokṣaḥ prajāyate || 1,20.35 ||
sarveṣāṃ dehamūlaṃ syāttatsthairye pavanaḥ prabhuḥ |
parasmādakṣarāttasmādākāśaṃ samabhūttataḥ || 1,20.36 ||
vāyustasmācca dahanastasmādāpastato mahī |
eteṣāṃ pañcabhūtānāmakṣaraṃ kāraṇaṃ param || 1,20.37 ||
vyoma śabdātmakaṃ vāyuḥ śabdasparśātmako bhavet |
vahniḥ śabdasparśarūpamayaḥ salilamucyate || 1,20.38 ||
śabdarūpasparśarasātmakaṃ bhūmirviśeṣataḥ |
śabdarūparasasparśagandharūpā bhavetpriye || 1,20.39 ||
bhūvāyvagnyanilākāśādhiṣṭhātryaścaiva devatāḥ |
brahmā viṣṇuśca rudraśca maheśvarasadāśivau || 1,20.40 ||
bhūmirnaṣṭāmbusaṃmagnā tāśca grastā mahāgninā |
sa ca caṇḍasamīreṇa śamitaḥ so'pi pārvati || 1,20.41 ||
vyomni līnaḥ kramāttasmādākāśādudbhavanti te |
antarbahiḥ sthitaṃ vyāptaṃ nirādhāraṃ nirāśrayam || 1,20.42 ||
ākāśaṃ cetasā dhyāyan pracchindyādbhavabandhanam |
jagatprāṇamayaṃ vāyuṃ cidānandapradaṃ jalam || 1,20.43 ||
niścalīkurute yuktyā vāyuvatsyātsa khecaraḥ |
anantārkāgnisaṃdīptaṃ dahantaṃ jagatāṃ trayam || 1,20.44 ||
tejo dhyātvā svahṛdaye nāgninā sa tu bādhyate |
sudhātaraṅganikaraplāvyamānamahītalam || 1,20.45 ||
aptattvaṃ bhāvayan svānte vāri taṃ na hi bādhate |
abhūtalacarākrāntaṃ bhūtalaṃ bhūtasaṃplavam || 1,20.46 ||
hṛdaye bhāvayannityaṃ tasya no pārthivaṃ bhayam |
yadyadbhāvayate citte tattadrūpamavāpnuyāt || 1,20.47 ||
yathāgnitvaṃ vrajetkāṣṭhaḥ kīṭo bhramaratāṃ yathā |
<yogāṅgāni>
āsanaṃ prāṇaniyamaḥ pratyāhāraśca dhāraṇā || 1,20.48 ||
dhyānaṃ samādhiḥ ṣoḍhā syuryogāṅgāni krameṇa ca |
<1. āsanavidhiḥ>
caturaśītilakṣāṇi hyāsanāni bhavanti hi || 1,20.49 ||
teṣu mukhyāsane dve ca siddhapadmāsane smṛte |
<siddhāsanam>
svayoniṃ pādamūlena caikena ghaṭayeddṛḍham || 1,20.50 ||
anyaccaraṇamūlaṃ ca mehanopari vinyaset |
avakrāṅgaḥ samāsīno vaśībhūtendriyaḥ priye || 1,20.51 ||
niścalākṣo bhruvormadhyaṃ paśyanniścalamānasaḥ |
siddhāsanamidaṃ jñeyaṃ muktimārgapradāyakam || 1,20.52 ||
<padmāsanam>
dakṣiṇorau padaṃ vāmaṃ vāmorau dakṣiṇaṃ padam |
vinyasya karayugmena pṛṣṭhabhāgagatena ca || 1,20.53 ||
viparītena cāṅguṣṭhaṃ vāmaṃ vāmakareṇa ca |
dakṣiṇaṃ dakṣiṇenaiva dṛḍhaṃ dhṛtvā nijorasi || 1,20.54 ||
vinyasya cubukaṃ dhyāyennāsāgraṃ saṃyatendriyaḥ |
etatpadmāsanaṃ khyātaṃ sarvaroganibarhaṇam || 1,20.55 ||
<2. prāṇāyāmaḥ; ajapāmudrā>
ādhāraṃ tu gudasthāne caturdalasaroruham |
vādisāntākṣaropetaṃ bālāruṇasamaprabham || 1,20.56 ||
svādhiṣṭhānaṃ ṣaḍdalābjaṃ bādilāntākṣarānvitam |
vidyutprabhaṃ tato devi ratnābhaṃ maṇipūrakam || 1,20.57 ||
ḍādiphāntārṇasaṃyuktaṃ nābhau dalaśatātmakam |
anāhataṃ suvarṇābhaṃ dvādaśacchadapaṅkajam || 1,20.58 ||
hṛdaye kādiṭhāntārṇaṃ viśuddhistu praśasyate |
ṣoḍaśāraṃ mahāpadmaṃ ṣoḍaśasvarabhūṣitam || 1,20.59 ||
ājñācakraṃ dvayadalaṃ padmahastavirājitam |
śvetamevaṃ kramāddevi ṣaṭcakraṃ samudāhṛtam || 1,20.60 ||
nābheradhastānmeḍhrasyopariṣṭātkanda ucyate |
vihaṃgamāṇḍasaṅkāśastatra nāḍīsamudbhavaḥ || 1,20.61 ||
saptatidvisahasrāḥ syustāsu mukhyā daśa smṛtāḥ |
iḍā ca prathamā nāḍī piṅgalā ca dvitīyakā || 1,20.62 ||
suṣumnā ca tṛtīyā syādgāndhārī ca caturthikā |
pañcamī hastijihvā syātṣaṣṭhī pūṣā tarasvinī || 1,20.63 ||
saptamyalambuṣā nāḍī cāṣṭamī ca kuhūḥ smṛtā |
navamī śaṅkhinī caiva daśamī ca krameṇa hi || 1,20.64 ||
etāstu prāṇavāhinyo vāyavastu japāḥ smṛtāḥ |
prāṇāpānau tathā vyānodānau caiva samānakaḥ || 1,20.65 ||
nāgaḥ kūrmaśca kṛkalo devadatto dhanañjayaḥ |
sarveṣu nāḍīcakreṣu vartante daśa vāyavaḥ || 1,20.66 ||
vāmadakṣiṇamārgābhyāmadha ūrdhvaṃ ca cañcalāḥ |
prāṇāpānavaśo jīvaḥ pradhāvati na dṛśyate || 1,20.67 ||
hastābhyāmāhato bhūmau kanduko na sthiro yathā |
prāṇāpānaparikṣiptastathā jīvo'pi na sthiraḥ || 1,20.68 ||
prāṇāpānasamākarṣe tathā prāṇamapānataḥ |
bahirgacchaddhakāreṇa sakāreṇāntarāviśet || 1,20.69 ||
haṃsaḥ so'haṃ manumamuṃ sadā jīvo japetpriye |
ekaviṃśatsahasraṃ ca ṣaṭśatādhikamīśvari || 1,20.70 ||
haṃsamantrasya saṃkhyā syādahorātreṇa sarvadā |
haṃsākhyo'yaṃ mahāmantro hyajapeti prakīrtitaḥ || 1,20.71 ||
japākhyeyaṃ ca gāyatrī yamikaivalyadāyinī |
etatsamaṃ tapo jñānaṃ japaḥ puṇyaṃ na kiṃcana || 1,20.72 ||
anuccāryā hyavarṇā ca kuṇḍalinyāḥ samudbhavā |
prāṇasaṃcāriṇī hyeṣā jñātavyā yogibhiḥ sadā || 1,20.73 ||
mātā kuṇḍalinī śaktiḥ kandādūrdhvaṃ pratiṣṭhitā |
aṣṭadhā parivṛttā ca prasuptabhujagākṛtiḥ || 1,20.74 ||
brahmadvāramukhaṃ sā tu svamukhena pidhāya ca |
tāṃ ca prabodhayedādau vahniyogena pārvati || 1,20.75 ||
vāyunā manasā sārdhaṃ madhyanāḍyā vrajecchive |
haṭhādākuñcanādbrahmadvāramudghāṭayettu sā || 1,20.76 ||
yathā nayedguṇaṃ sūcī tadvadbrahmabilaṃ tu sā |
sukhaṃ padmāsanāsīnaḥ pāṇī cottānitau priye || 1,20.77 ||
aṅkamadhye nidhāyaiva cubukaṃ vakṣasi nyaset |
dṛḍhaṃ gudamukhaṃ gāḍhamākuñcyāpānarandhrakam || 1,20.78 ||
muhurmuhurvāyumūrdhvaṃ cālayettvaritaṃ priye |
prāṇaṃ muñcankuṇḍalinyāḥ prabhāvānmokṣavartmajam || 1,20.79 ||
upaiti satprabodhaṃ ca devānāmapi durlabham |
tadodbhūtaiḥ śramajalaiḥ svāṅgāni parimārjayet || 1,20.80 ||
kṣīrāhārī mitānnāśī kaṭvamlalavaṇaṃ tyajet |
jitendriyo brahmacārī kuṭīsthaḥ karmavarjitaḥ || 1,20.81 ||
evamabhyāsanirato vatsarātsiddhimeti saḥ |
<prāṇāyāme mudrāḥ>
jālandharaṃ mūlabandhamoḍḍīyāṇaṃ ca khecarīm || 1,20.82 ||
mahāmudrāṃ ca yaḥ kuryātsa bhaveddehasiddhibhāk |
eṣāṃ vakṣye lakṣaṇāni gopyāni tava śāmbhavi || 1,20.83 ||
<jālandharabandhaḥ>
jālandharaṃ kaṇṭhasirāsamūhānāṃ ca bandhanam |
kṛtvādho namayitvā kaṃ tadā syandati mūrdhataḥ || 1,20.84 ||
nabhastaḥ syandamānā ca sudhā dogdhau patenna ca |
na dhāvati maruttatra karṇasaṃkocane kṛte || 1,20.85 ||
karṇāmayasamūhaghnaṃ mṛtyughnaṃ tatparaṃ bhavet |
<mūlabandhaḥ>
mūlabandhaṃ pārṣṇibhāgādyonisthānaṃ prapīḍayet || 1,20.86 ||
gudamākuñcayedyogī nayedūrdhvamapānakam |
evaṃ kṛte mūlabandhe kṣīyate malamūlakam || 1,20.87 ||
prāṇāpānau ca saṃyuktau syātāṃ vṛddho'pi yauvanam |
prāpnuyānmūlabandhena mṛtyuthopi vivasvatām || 1,20.88 ||
<oḍyāṇabandhaḥ>
oḍḍiyāṇe nābhivivaramūrdhvaṃ jaṭhare dṛḍham |
ākṛṣya paścimaṃ tānaṃ bandhayetpavanastadā || 1,20.89 ||
viśrāntaḥ syānmahāmāye coḍyāṇo'yaṃ prakīrtitaḥ |
mṛtyudāvānalo dīpto jarārogābdhivāḍabaḥ || 1,20.90 ||
<khecarīmudrā>
āsyāntarvivare jihvāṃ tālurandhre praveśayet |
viparītāṃ bhruvormadhye paśyenniścalayā dṛśā || 1,20.91 ||
eṣā hi khecarī mudrā gopanīyātidurlabhā |
jihvā tu khagatā yasmānmanaścarati khe tataḥ || 1,20.92 ||
khecarīti prasiddheyaṃ mṛtyurogajarāpahā |
nidrā kṣudhā tṛṣā nāsti khecaryā mudritasya ca || 1,20.93 ||
mūrcchā bhavati sādhvī ca karmabandhabhayaṃ na hi |
ramaṇyā saṃgatasyāpi reto na patati dhruvam || 1,20.94 ||
yāvacchukraṃ sthiraṃ dehe tāvatkālabhayaṃ na hi |
yena baddhā nabhomudrā bījastasya na gacchati || 1,20.95 ||
yadi gacchettasya bījo hutāśanamupaiti hi |
sa bījaścordhvamāyāti śaktyā pratihataḥ svayam || 1,20.96 ||
yonimudrānibaddhaḥ sansā mudrā tena durlabhā |
bījastu dvividhaḥ proktaḥ śukraṃ caiva mahārajaḥ || 1,20.97 ||
śiraḥsthānagataṃ śukraṃ yonisthānagataṃ rajaḥ |
śukraṃ tu śvetavarṇaṃ syātpravālābhaṃ rajaḥ smṛtam || 1,20.98 ||
śukraṃ candragataṃ nityaṃ rajaḥ sūryeṇa saṅgatam |
śukraṃ śivo rajaḥ śaktistayā yogaḥ sudurlabhaḥ || 1,20.99 ||
marutā śakticāreṇa rajaścordhvaṃ praṇīyate |
aikyaṃ tadbindunā yāti tadā divyaṃ vapurbhavet || 1,20.100 ||
<mahāmudrā>
vāmāṅghrimūlabhāgena yonisthānaṃ prapīḍayet |
dakṣiṇāṅghriṃ ca vitataṃ hastābhyāmabhidhārayet || 1,20.101 ||
hanuṃ vakṣasi nikṣipya vāyunā jaṭharaṃ tataḥ |
āpūrya recayeddevi sthitvā baddhāsano yamī || 1,20.102 ||
eṣā khyātā mahāmudrā malasaṃśodhanī varā |
sūryendū ghaṭayejjihvāśoṣaṇī pāpanāśinī || 1,20.103 ||
tathā dakṣiṇapādena yonisthānaṃ prapīḍayet |
vitatya vāmapādaṃ ca karābhyāṃ dhārayetpriye || 1,20.104 ||
śeṣaṃ pūrvoktavatkuryādevaṃ savyāpasavyayoḥ |
bhāgayoḥ samakālaḥ syādabhyāsastāṃ vivarjayet || 1,20.105 ||
tasya pathyamapathyaṃ ca ṣaḍrasā nīrasā api |
ghoraṃ viṣaṃ vātisukhaṃ pīyūṣamiva jīryate || 1,20.106 ||
gulmodāvartakuṣṭhādyā rogā naśyantyasaṃśayam |
siddhideyaṃ mahāmudrā kālaṃ hanti jarābhayam || 1,20.107 ||
na prakāśyā na deyā ca yasmai kasmaicana priye |
<3. pratyāhāraḥ>
muktāsanasthito yogī ṛjvaṅgagrīvamastakaḥ || 1,20.108 ||
ghoṇāgralocanaḥ svasthaḥ kuṭīsthaḥ praṇavaṃ japet |
trilokaśca trikālaśca tridevāstrīśvarā api || 1,20.109 ||
trijyotīṃṣīndumukhyāni trivedāścāgnayastrayaḥ |
śaktitrayaṃ tripadavī brāhmī caindrī ca vaiṣṇavī || 1,20.110 ||
varṇatrayaṃ ca bhāsante yatra tajjyotiromiti |
tamoṃkāraṃ ca manasā vacasā karmaṇā tu yaḥ || 1,20.111 ||
dhyāyejjapedabhyasecca sa mukto bhavabandhanāt |
gacchaṃstiṣṭhañjapañjāgracchucirvāpyaśuciryadi || 1,20.112 ||
na karmaṇā ca liptaḥ syājjalenābjadalaṃ yathā |
vāyau calati sarve'pi calantīndriyadhātavaḥ || 1,20.113 ||
sthite vāyau sthire sarvaṃ vapuḥprabhṛti śāmbhavi |
tasmādvāyuṃ nibadhnīyātsthire vāte sthiraṃ manaḥ || 1,20.114 ||
sthire manasi jīvaśca sthiro bhavati bhairavi |
yāvatsaṃyamito vāyuryāvacceto'pi susthiram || 1,20.115 ||
bījaṃ yāvadbhruvormadhye tāvatkālabhayaṃ na hi |
ṣaṭtriṃśadvyaṅgulaṃ nityaṃ tataḥ prāṇaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 1,20.116 ||
baddhapadmāsane sthitvā ṛjvaṅgaḥ sthiramānasaḥ |
vāmanāsāpuṭenaiva pūrayetprāṇamārutam || 1,20.117 ||
yāvatṣoḍaśamātraṃ ca kumbhayeddvādaśa priye |
recayeddaśamātraṃ ca dakṣanāsāpuṭena ca || 1,20.118 ||
punaśca sūryamārgeṇa pūrayetpūrvavatpriye |
kumbhayitvā recayecca vāmanāsāpuṭena ca || 1,20.119 ||
prāṇāyāmavidhiḥ proktastrividho yogivandite |
adhamo madhyamo devi hyuttamo'pi yathākramam || 1,20.120 ||
prokto'yamadhamastasmāddviguṇo madhyamaḥ smṛtaḥ |
uttamastriguṇaḥ proktaḥ prāṇāyāmo'yamīśvari || 1,20.121 ||
yadā tu vāmanāsāyāṃ pūrayeccandramakṣaram |
dhyāyedamṛtavārīśamadhyasthaṃ kṣīrasannibham || 1,20.122 ||
yadā tu dakṣanāsāyāṃ pūrayetsūryamakṣaram |
jvalajjvalanasaṅkāśaṃ nābhisthaṃ cintayetsadā || 1,20.123 ||
evaṃ bījadvayaṃ dhyātvā nāsārandhradvayena ca |
pūrayedyastu matimānnāḍīśuddhirato bhavet || 1,20.124 ||
evaṃ māsatrayābhyāsādyatheṣṭaṃ vāyudhāraṇam |
pradīpto jāṭharo vahnirnādavyaktiśca jāyate || 1,20.125 ||
ārogyaṃ prāpnuyāddevi nityamabhyāsayogataḥ |
prathameyaṃ na dharmaḥ syāddvitīye kampate vapuḥ || 1,20.126 ||
uttiṣṭhati tṛtīye tu baddhapadmāsanasthiteḥ |
prāṇāyāmādiṣaṭkena pratyāhāro bhavecchive || 1,20.127 ||
taddviṣaṭkena vidhinā dhāraṇā tu praśasyate |
dhāraṇādvādaśena syāddhyānaṃ taddvādaśātmakaḥ || 1,20.128 ||
samādhiḥ kathyate devi tena dṛśyaṃ parātparam |
tasminparāpare dhāmni kṣīyate karmasaṃcayaḥ || 1,20.129 ||
janmamṛtyū na bhavato jarārogaśca naśyati |
yuktiyuktena yogena cirāyuśca sukhī bhavet || 1,20.130 ||
ayuktyābhyāsanāddhikkākarṇarogaśirovyathā |
śvāsakāsādayo rogā doṣāḥ syurbahavastathā || 1,20.131 ||
<viparītakaraṇīmudrā>
saumyasthānātsamāyātā dvābhyāṃ caikā tu bhujyate |
tatastṛtīyo yaḥ kaścitsa syājjanmajarojjhitaḥ || 1,20.132 ||
prajvalajjvalanākāro nābhimadhye sthito raviḥ |
tālumadhye śaśī bhāti sudhāṃ varṣatyadhomukhaḥ || 1,20.133 ||
tāṃ grasatyūrdhvavadano bhāskaraḥ kiraṇatviṣā |
etasya viparītaṃ yatkaraṇaṃ viparītakam || 1,20.134 ||
kākacañcuvadāsyaṃ ca kṛtvā vāyuṃ sasūtkṛtam |
ādāya nāsārandhreṇa punastaṃ śvasanaṃ tyajet || 1,20.135 ||
śītalīkaraṇākhyo'yaṃ yogastu jvarapittahṛt |
amṛtaṃ śītalaṃ tasya pibataśca jarā na hi || 1,20.136 ||
jihvayā tālumūlena prāṇaṃ yaḥ pibati priye |
tasya ṣaṇmāsataḥ sarve rogā naśyanti yoginaḥ || 1,20.137 ||
rasanāmūrdhvataḥ kṛtvā somaṃ pibati yaḥ priye |
siddhirmāsārdha āyāti rogāstasya na santi hi || 1,20.138 ||
jihvāgreṇa ca saṃpīḍya rasanāntarbilaṃ mahat |
dhyātvāmṛtajharīmambāmardhābdena bhavetkaviḥ || 1,20.139 ||
amṛtāplāvitatanoryogino vatsaratrayāt |
retaścordhvaṃ prayātyeva sidhyanti hyaṇimādayaḥ || 1,20.140 ||
trividhaṃ garalaṃ tasya śarīre ca na saṃkramet |
jarā ca maraṇaṃ nāsti duṣṭasattvabhayaṃ tathā || 1,20.141 ||
<4. dhāraṇam>
uktāsanasamārūḍhaḥ prāṇāyāmarataḥ sadā |
pratyāhāraprasannaḥ sannabhyaseddhāraṇaṃ tataḥ || 1,20.142 ||
cetaso niścalatvaṃ yaddhāraṇā sā smṛtā śive |
pṛthvyādipañcabhūtānāṃ yā pṛthagdhāraṇā hṛdi || 1,20.143 ||
caturaśrā suvarṇā salakārā ca hṛdi sthitā |
brahmaṇā sahitā bhūmirdhyātavyā pañca nāḍikāḥ || 1,20.144 ||
prāṇaṃ tatraiva manasā dhārayetsaha śāṃbhavi |
eṣā stambhakarī vidyā pṛthvījayamavāpnuyāt || 1,20.145 ||
ardhacandrapratīkāśaṃ karpūrahimanirmalam |
kaṇṭhasthānagataṃ nityaṃ sudhāplutavakārayuk || 1,20.146 ||
jalatattvaṃ ca saṃyuktaṃ viṣṇunā tatra dhārayet |
prāṇaṃ cittena sahitaṃ dhyātavyaṃ pañcanāḍikāḥ || 1,20.147 ||
eṣā hi vāruṇī vidyā viṣapittajvarāpahā |
grasane kālakūṭasya mayaiva parikalpitā || 1,20.148 ||
trikoṇaṃ taptahemābhaṃ sarephaṃ rudradaivatam |
tālusthānagataṃ dhyāyedvahnitattvaṃ jvalatprabham || 1,20.149 ||
tatraiva pañcaghaṭikāḥ prāṇaṃ ca manasā saha |
dhārayennihitaṃ yogī hyeṣā vaiśvānarī parā || 1,20.150 ||
viṣāgnibhītisaṃhartrī vātaśleṣmādirogahṛt |
vartulaṃ nīlameghābhaṃ bhruvormadhye pratiṣṭhitam || 1,20.151 ||
sayakāraṃ vāyutattvaṃ nityamīśvaradaivatam |
tatraiva nāḍikāḥ pañca prāṇaṃ ca manasā saha || 1,20.152 ||
dhārayedvāyavīyaiṣā vidyā khagatidāyinī |
mṛgatṛṣṇāmbusaṅkāśaṃ hakāreṇa samanvitam || 1,20.153 ||
sadāśivādidaivaṃ ca brahmarandhragataṃ sadā |
vyomatattvaṃ nirākāśaṃ śāntaṃ sarvagataṃ priye || 1,20.154 ||
tatra prāṇaṃ ca saṃyamya manasā saha dhārayet |
yāvatsyuḥ pañca ghaṭikā nabhovidyeyamīśvari || 1,20.155 ||
kathitā mokṣadā devi yogināṃ duḥkhahāriṇī |
stambhanī pārthivī vidyā plāvanī vāruṇī matā || 1,20.156 ||
tato vaiśvānarī vidyā dāhinīti prakīrtitā |
vāyavī bhrāmaṇī proktā śamanī vyomarūpiṇī || 1,20.157 ||
kramaśaḥ pañcavidyāśca dhāraṇīyāḥ pṛthakpṛthak |
svanāmakarmasadṛśaṃ phalaṃ dadati yoginām || 1,20.158 ||
eṣā pañcavidhā devi dhāraṇā bhuvi durlabhā |
<5. dhyānam>
tattveṣu niścalā cintā yā taddhyānaṃ prakīrtyate || 1,20.159 ||
dhyānaṃ dvidheti vikhyātaṃ saguṇaṃ nirguṇaṃ priye |
saguṇaṃ varṇasahitaṃ nirguṇaṃ varṇavarjitam || 1,20.160 ||
lakṣaṃ ca vājapeyānāmaśvamedhasahasrakam |
sakṛddhyānasya yogasya kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm || 1,20.161 ||
pratyaṅmanā bahirdṛṣṭirṛjuḥ padmāsanasthitaḥ |
dhyānayogaḥ sa vijñeyaḥ sadyaḥ siddhipradaḥ śubhaḥ || 1,20.162 ||
mūlādhāre suvarṇābhe cakre'sminprathame priye |
dhyātvātmānaṃ ca nāsāgre lakṣayeddhanti kilbiṣam || 1,20.163 ||
svādhiṣṭhāne ca ratnābhe hyātmānaṃ paricintayet |
nāsāgralakṣo duḥkhebhyo mucyate yogisattamaḥ || 1,20.164 ||
bālāruṇābhe cātmānaṃ cakre'sminmaṇipūrake |
smṛtvā nāsāgradṛṣṭiḥ saṃstrijagatkṣobhayedyamī || 1,20.165 ||
vidyunmālānibhe cakre'nāhate hṛdayasthale |
prāṇāyāmena bahudhā sādhite parameśvari || 1,20.166 ||
ātmānaṃ cintayedyastu nāsāgragatalocanaḥ |
bhavedbrahmasamo yogī vaśīkṛtamanāḥ priye || 1,20.167 ||
ghaṇṭikāyāṃ viśuddhākhye svarṇacampakasannibhe |
ghrāṇāgradṛṣṭirātmānaṃ dhyātvānandamayo bhavet || 1,20.168 ||
lambikāyāṃ sudhāpūrṇe candramaṇḍalamaṇḍite |
nāsāgradṛṣṭirātmānaṃ dhyātvānandamayo bhavet || 1,20.169 ||
bhruvormadhye'ñjanākāre dhyāyedātmānamīśvari |
jitaprāṇo bhavedyogī yogānandamayo bhavet || 1,20.170 ||
nirguṇaṃ nirapāyaṃ ca śivaṃ śāntaṃ parātparam |
viśvataijasamātmānaṃ dhyāyedbhrūmadhyalocanaḥ || 1,20.171 ||
brahmānandamayo yogī bhavatyeva na saṃśayaḥ |
ājñācakramiti khyātaṃ pare vyomni nirāmaye || 1,20.172 ||
śivamātmānamācintya bhavejjñānamayo vaśī |
gaganākāramamalaṃ mṛgatṛṣṇāmbusannibham || 1,20.173 ||
dhyātvātmānaṃ sarvagataṃ mokṣaṃ vrajati saṃyamī |
apānameḍhrau nābhiśca hṛdayaṃ ghaṇṭikā tathā || 1,20.174 ||
lambikā ca bhruvormadhyaṃ nabhaśca brahmarandhrakam |
tava cakramiti khyātaṃ deyaṃ sthānaṃ ca yoginām || 1,20.175 ||
dhāraṇā pañcaghaṭikā ṣaṇṇāḍī dhyānamucyate |
samādhirdvādaśāhaṃ syātprāṇasaṃyamanātpriye || 1,20.176 ||
<6. samādhiḥ>
jalasaindhavayoryogādekatvaṃ ca yathā bhavet |
cittātmanoḥ sāmarasyaṃ samādhiḥ sa tu kathyate || 1,20.177 ||
manaḥ pralīyate cānte yadā prāṇakṣayo bhavet |
sāmarasyaṃ tadā syācca samādhiḥ sa tu kathyate || 1,20.178 ||
praṇaṣṭākhilasaṅkalpo jīvātmā paramātmanā |
yadekatvaṃ bhajeddevi samādhiḥ sa tu kathyate || 1,20.179 ||
parāpekṣojjhitaṃ cittamindriyeṣu pravartate |
yadā jīvo gataścaikyaṃ tadā naivendriyaṃ manaḥ || 1,20.180 ||
śabdaṃ sparśaṃ ca rūpaṃ ca rasaṃ gandhaṃ paraṃ tathā |
ātmānaṃ ca sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ mānāmānaṃ priyāpriye || 1,20.181 ||
śītāśītaṃ tathā vātamātapaṃ yo na vetti ca |
na bādhyate svakarmaughairna kaiścidapi bādhyate || 1,20.182 ||
na śastrairbādhyate mantrairyantraistantrairna gṛhyate |
nāgninā na jalenāpi vāyunā na ca pīḍyate || 1,20.183 ||
avadhyo dehibhiḥ sarvairmānanīyaḥ surairapi |
āhāre ca vihāre ca nidrāyāmavabodhane || 1,20.184 ||
sarvakarmasu yuktaḥ sansa tattvaṃ vetti yogini |
nirālambaṃ nirākāramanādyantaṃ nirāśrayam || 1,20.185 ||
anālayaṃ niṣprapañcaṃ niṣkriyaṃ nirmalaṃ mahat |
niścalaṃ nirmalaṃ nityaṃ nirguṇaṃ vyoma cinmayam || 1,20.186 ||
sadānandamanantaṃ ca sarvagaṃ vibhu saṃtatam |
etadbrahmapadaṃ tattvaṃ viddhi tvaṃ vindhyavāsini || 1,20.187 ||
anāmaye nirālambe nirātaṅke mahādyutau |
nirābhāse pare tattve yogayuktaḥ pralīyate || 1,20.188 ||
dugdhe dugdhaṃ ghṛte cājyamagnāvagnirjale jalam |
kṣiptaṃ vrajettanmayatvaṃ tathā brahmaṇi līyate || 1,20.189 ||
<yogābhyāsaphalam>
mayoditamidaṃ sarvaṃ divyavāyurasāyanam |
yaḥ seveta sa puṇyātmā kṛtakṛtyo jagattraye || 1,20.190 ||
sarvayajñaphalopetaḥ sa snātaḥ sarvatīrthake |
sa yaśasvī sa yogīndraḥ sa evāmaravanditaḥ || 1,20.191 ||
sa eva siddhaḥ śuddhaśca mama tulyo varānane |
tasminsnihyati me cetastaccittaṃ me nivāsabhūḥ || 1,20.192 ||
saphalaṃ jīvitaṃ tasya pūtaṃ tadubhayaṃ kulam |
tatpadanyāsamātreṇa dharitrī pāvanīkṛtā || 1,20.193 ||
yatroṣitaṃ kṣaṇaṃ tena puṇyakṣetraṃ hi sā mahī |
dhanyā tajjanayitrī ca puṇyastajjanakaḥ priye || 1,20.194 ||
taddarśanātsajīvāḥ syuḥ śūlaprotādayaḥ śavāḥ |
tadvākyenaiva sarve'pi labhante'pi śubhāśubham || 1,20.195 ||
tanmūtramalasaṃsparśāllohā yānti suvarṇatām |
kiṃ punaḥ kathyate devi mama tulyaparākramaḥ || 1,20.196 ||


āk, 1, 21
<yogasiddhidakuṭīnirmāṇavidhiḥ>
śrībhairavī |
kuṭī proktā tvayā pūrvaṃ kathaṃ kāryā ca kīdṛśī |
tatra kālaṃ kiyacchaṃbho vastavyaṃ brūhi me prabho || 1,21.1 ||
śrībhairavaḥ |
vakṣyāmi tāṃ kuṭīṃ samyakśṛṇu tripurasundari |
medinīmunnatīkṛtya punastāṃ suhṛdaṃ priye || 1,21.2 ||
staṃbhāṃśca kramaśaḥ ṣaṭṣaṭpaṅktiśaḥ sthāpayedṛjūn |
tulā upari cāropya dārūṇi sudṛḍhāni ca || 1,21.3 ||
sthāpayediṣṭakāḥ paścātsudhayā sāndramālipet |
parito valabhiṃ kṛtvā bhittiṃ trivalayāṃ śubhām || 1,21.4 ||
kuryātkuṭīṃ ca tanmadhye tṛtīyāvaraṇaiḥ punaḥ |
nikhaneccaturaśraṃ ca daśaprādeśamātrakam || 1,21.5 ||
pañcaprādeśamātre ca tvadhonimnaṃ tathordhvataḥ |
evaṃ daśavitastyābhiracintyāṃ tāṃ manoharām || 1,21.6 ||
prāgdvāraṃ bāhyavalaye dvitīye valaye śive |
yāmyadvāraṃ tṛtīye tu pratyagdvāraṃ vidhīyate || 1,21.7 ||
dvārāṇāṃ ca pramāṇaṃ hi vitastidvayamucyate |
sakavāṭaṃ pratidvāramacchidraṃ cārgalānvitam || 1,21.8 ||
sudhāpralepitaṃ kuryādbhittiṃ ślakṣṇataraṃ sthalam |
dakṣiṇe cottare caiva kuṭyantarvedikādvayam || 1,21.9 ||
sārdhatrayaṃ vitastīnāṃ viśālaṃ cāyataṃ daśa |
prādeśamātramutsedhaṃ madhyaṃ prādeśikatrayam || 1,21.10 ||
tatra gomayasambhūtaṃ bhasma vastreṇa gālitam |
pūrayecca kuṭībhittau citraṃ bahu suvistaram || 1,21.11 ||
bhairavaṃ kālameghābhaṃ jvaladūrdhvaśiroruham |
phālākṣaṃ vakradaṃṣṭraṃ ca nāgakuṇḍalamaṇḍitam || 1,21.12 ||
nāgayajñopavītaṃ ca kiṅkiṇīmuṇḍamālinam |
digambaraṃ tu kākoṭībhūṣitāṅghriśiroruham || 1,21.13 ||
hārakeyūrakaṭakamudrikādivibhūṣitam |
daśahastaṃ ca ḍamarumaṅkuśaṃ khaḍgaśūlakam || 1,21.14 ||
varadaṃ savyahastābjairnāgaṃ pāśaṃ ca ghaṇṭikām |
madhupatraṃ bhayaharaṃ bibhrāṇaṃ vāmabāhubhiḥ || 1,21.15 ||
kṛṣṇāṅgarāgamālāḍhyaṃ sarvavyādhivināśanam |
vyādhibhūtāhiśatrughnaṃ kṣvelādibhayanāśanam || 1,21.16 ||
tato mṛtyuñjayaṃ śāntaṃ vaṭukaṃ vilikhetpriye |
pāradenduhimaśvetaṃ bālaṃ dvibhujaśobhitam || 1,21.17 ||
kiṅkiṇīmālayā baddhakarṇanūpuraśobhitam |
bālaṃ kuṇḍalasacchobhaṃ trinetraṃ nagnarūpiṇam || 1,21.18 ||
śvetamālyānulepaṃ ca pūrṇapātraṃ ca vāmataḥ |
daṇḍaṃ dakṣiṇahastena dadhānaṃ mṛtyunāśanam || 1,21.19 ||
māyābījaṃ ca vaṭukaṃ hyenaṃ prathamamuccaret |
āpaduddhāraṇāyeti likhetpañcākṣaradvayam || 1,21.20 ||
vaṭukāyeti māyāṃ ca vaṭukasya manuḥ smṛtaḥ |
mūrtidvayorayaṃ mantraḥ kathitaḥ suravandite || 1,21.21 ||
mantrasyāsya ca yadyantraṃ tadyantraṃ tatra saṃlikhet |
ramāṃ ca bhuvaneśīṃ ca kāmaṃ cintāmaṇiṃ kramāt || 1,21.22 ||
karṇikāyāṃ likhetpūrvaṃ vaṭukāyeti vīpsitam |
aṣṭapatre likheccheṣāṇyakṣarāṇyaṣṭapatrake || 1,21.23 ||
bahiḥ ṣoḍaśapatreṣu vilikhetṣoḍaśa svarān |
dvātriṃśaddalake kādisāntadvātriṃśadakṣarān || 1,21.24 ||
anye dale halakṣāṃśca vilikhedbhūpuraṃ bahiḥ |
āpaduddhāraṇaṃ yantramapamṛtyunivāraṇam || 1,21.25 ||
rakṣākaraṃ grahārtānāṃ sarveṣāṃ prāṇināmapi |
strīvaśyaṃ rājavaśyaṃ ca puṃvaśyaṃ paśuvaśyakam || 1,21.26 ||
nānāsiddhipradaṃ nityaṃ sarvarogaviṣāpaham |
hārakeyūraruciraṃ kāntyā viśvavimohanam || 1,21.27 ||
mahāmṛtyuñjayaṃ devaṃ bhāvayenmṛtyujidbhavet |
evaṃ dvitīyavargasya tṛtīyaṃ pañcamena ca || 1,21.28 ||
svareṇa bindunā yuktaṃ sontaṃ sādhyapadaṃ tataḥ |
rakṣaśabdayugaṃ paścātpūrvaṃ bījatrayaṃ punaḥ || 1,21.29 ||
pratilomaṃ samuccārya mantraṃ mṛtyuñjayaṃ japet |
tāraṃ madhye sasādhyākhyaṃ digdale bhaṃ samālikhet || 1,21.30 ||
āgneyādidaleṣvantyamakṣaraṃ kramaśo likhet |
bāhye bhūpuramālikhya dikṣu sāndraṃ samālikhet || 1,21.31 ||
catuṣkoṇe ṭhakāraṃ ca yantraṃ mṛtyuñjayātmakam |
apamṛtyujvaravyādhikṣvelamohavināśanam || 1,21.32 ||
tatra cintāmaṇiṃ devaṃ cintitārthapradaṃ likhet |
nīlapravālaruciraṃ triṇetraṃ rucirānanam || 1,21.33 ||
pāśāruṇotpalaṃ vāme dakṣe śūlakapālakau |
dadhānamindumakuṭaṃ dhyāyedardhāmbikeśvaram || 1,21.34 ||
vahniprathamavargādiṣamarephāḥ kramāttataḥ |
prāṇasadyāntasahitaḥ ṣaṣṭhasvarasabindukaḥ || 1,21.35 ||
idaṃ cintāmaṇermantraṃ cintitārthapradaṃ śubham |
ṣoḍaśasvarasaṃvītaṃ cintāmaṇimabhīṣṭadam || 1,21.36 ||
ṭhakārāveṣṭitaṃ kuryājjvarāpasmṛtimṛtyuham |
tataśca śāradādevīmālikhetsiddhidāyinīm || 1,21.37 ||
śaṅkhakundendudhavalāṃ makuṭendukalādharām |
sudhākumbhaṃ varākṣasraksaṃvinmudrāṃ karāṃbujaiḥ || 1,21.38 ||
bibhrāṇāṃ śvetavasanāṃ mauktikābharaṇojjvalām |
vāgbījaṃ bhuvaneśīṃ ca vadavākyadvayaṃ tataḥ || 1,21.39 ||
vāgvādinīṃ sasambuddhimagnipatnīṃ samuccaret |
eṣa śrīmātṛkāmantraḥ proktaḥ sārasvatapradaḥ || 1,21.40 ||
ādibījadvayaṃ hitvā śeṣaṃ pūrvavaduccaret |
daśārṇaśāradāmantro vāgvilāsapradāyakaḥ || 1,21.41 ||
viyadvarṇasukāraṃ ca sadyāntaṃ savisargakam |
karṇikāyāṃ likhetpūrvaṃ kiñjalkeṣu svarānapi || 1,21.42 ||
aṣṭacchadeṣvaṣṭavargānyaśahādyaiḥ paraistribhiḥ |
kādyaiśca pañcabhirvarṇairaṣṭavargāḥ samīritāḥ || 1,21.43 ||
likhedbhūpurakoṇeṣu ṭhakārāndikṣu vinyaset |
idaṃ hi mātṛkāyantraṃ viṣamṛtyugadāpaham || 1,21.44 ||
aghoraṃ vilikheddevi nīlajīmūtasannibham |
krūradaṃṣṭraṃ triṇetraṃ ca nāgendrāṣṭavibhūṣitam || 1,21.45 ||
raktāṅgarāgavasanaṃ raktamālāvirājitam |
paraśvathuṃ ca ḍamaruṃ khaḍgaṃ kheṭamiṣuṃ dhanuḥ || 1,21.46 ||
triśūlaṃ pūrṇapātraṃ ca bibhrāṇaṃ cāṣṭabāhubhiḥ |
bhuvaneśīṃ sphuradvandvaṃ tataḥ prasphuravīpsitam || 1,21.47 ||
ghoraṃ tato'ghorataraṃ tanurūpaṃ caṭadvayam |
prakaṭadviguṇaṃ caiva kahaśabdayugaṃ tataḥ || 1,21.48 ||
vamaśabdadvayaṃ devi bandhaśabdaṃ ca vīpsitam |
ghātayadvitayaṃ devi kavacaṃ ca phaḍantakam || 1,21.49 ||
ekottaro'yaṃ pañcāśadarṇo'ghoraḥ smṛto manuḥ |
vaśyārthaṃ taptahemābhaṃ pūrvoktākṛtisaṃyutam || 1,21.50 ||
muktau mṛtyuñjayārthe tu śvetaṃ pūrvoktavigraham |
sarvārthasiddhidaṃ śāntamaghorāstraṃ likhetpriye || 1,21.51 ||
māyābījaṃ sasādhyaṃ syānmadhye ca svarasaṃyutam |
tadbahiḥkesareṣvevaṃ vilikhedaṣṭavargakam || 1,21.52 ||
tatastvaṣṭadale mantravarṇānguṇamitān likhet |
agraśeṣeṣu tadvattatṣaṭkoṇe kavacāstrakau || 1,21.53 ||
tadbahirbhūpuraṃ lekhyamenadāghorayantrakam |
sphuradvayāvṛtaṃ madhye śaktibījaṃ likhettataḥ || 1,21.54 ||
ṣaṭkoṇe prasphurayugaṃ tataścāṣṭadale kramāt |
ṣaḍbhiścaturbhirvedaiśca rasairvastraiśca gopadaiḥ || 1,21.55 ||
ṛtubhiḥ śiṣṭamantrārṇairamībhirvilikhettataḥ |
ṣaṭkoṇe vahnicāstrābhyāmuddhṛtaṃ vītakoṇakam || 1,21.56 ||
bhūpureṇāvṛtaṃ yantramaghoraṃ vilikhetpriye |
vyālāricorakṣudrāpasmārabhūtagrahāpaham || 1,21.57 ||
atho mahāgaṇapatiṃ likhedvidrumasannibham |
koṭīracandraśakalaṃ gajāsyaṃ locanatrayam || 1,21.58 ||
tundilaṃ raktavasanaṃ raktamālānulepanam |
daśadordaṇḍasubhagaṃ vāmorusthitayoṣitam || 1,21.59 ||
hārakeyūrakaṭakamudrikākuṇḍalojjvalam |
phalapūraṃ gadāmikṣukodaṇḍaṃ ca triśūlakam || 1,21.60 ||
cakraṃ sarasijaṃ pāśamutpalaṃ śālimañjarīm |
svadantatuṇḍayā ratnakalaśaṃ daśabhiḥ karaiḥ || 1,21.61 ||
bibhrāṇaṃ padmakarayā vāmorusthitayā śriyā |
āliṅgitaṃ bhaktalokacintitārthasuradrumam || 1,21.62 ||
praṇavaṃ kamalāṃ māyāṃ kāmarājaṃ vasuṃdharām |
kramādgaṇapaterbījaṃ mahāgaṇapatiṃ tataḥ || 1,21.63 ||
caturthyantaṃ sasaṃbuddhiṃ varaṃ ca varadaṃ tathā |
tataḥ sarvajanaṃ me ca vaśamānaya śabdakam || 1,21.64 ||
agnipatnīṃ samālikhya cāṣṭāviṃśativarṇakam |
mantraṃ mahāgaṇapatermṛtyudāridryanāśanam || 1,21.65 ||
trikoṇe bījamālikhya satāre ca gaṇeśituḥ |
dikṣu śrīśaktimadanabhūbījāni bahirlikhet || 1,21.66 ||
ṣaṭkoṇe bījaṣaṭkaṃ ca tatsaṃdhiṣvaṅgamantrakam |
tato'ṣṭadalamadhyeṣu mantrāṇi gaṇaśo likhet || 1,21.67 ||
antyākṣare cāntyadale mātṛkāmanulomataḥ |
lekhe ca pratilomenāṅkuśapāśāvṛtaṃ tataḥ || 1,21.68 ||
bhūmandireṇa subhagaṃ yantraṃ gaṇapateḥ śubham |
gajāntaṃ śrīpadaṃ divyamāyuṣyārogyavardhanam || 1,21.69 ||
etāni yantrajālāni kuṭyantardevatā likhet |
kuṭībhittibahirbhāge bhairavaṃ varṇamālikhet || 1,21.70 ||
asitāṅgaṃ ruruṃ caṇḍaṃ krodhamunmattabhairavam |
kapālinaṃ bhīṣaṇaṃ ca saṃhāraṃ bhairavaṃ kramāt || 1,21.71 ||
dvitīyāvaraṇasyāntarbhittāvekādaśa kramāt |
rudrāṃstrilocanāṃścandrakalājūṭajaṭān likhet || 1,21.72 ||
tadbāhye nava nāthāṃśca nava siddhāṃśca ṣoḍaśa |
sanatkumārasanakasanandādīn likhettataḥ || 1,21.73 ||
tṛtīyāvaraṇasyāntarbhittau brāhmyādimātaraḥ |
catuḥṣaṣṭiśca yoginyo lekhanīyā yathāvidhi || 1,21.74 ||
tadbhittibāhye devendramukhā dikpatayaḥ kramāt |
grahāśca candrasūryādyā aśvinyādyāśca tārakāḥ || 1,21.75 ||
meṣādyā rāśayo lekhyāḥ sālaṅkārāśca sāyudhāḥ |
kuṭīdvāravipārśve ca gaṇeśaṃ bhairavaṃ likhet || 1,21.76 ||
dvitīyāvaraṇadvārapārśve dvau śaṅkhapadmakau |
garutmantaṃ hanūmantaṃ tṛtīyāvaraṇasya ca || 1,21.77 ||
dvārasya pārśvayordevi vilikhedbhayabhañjanau |
yasmindeśe kuṭī divyā kalpitā yogisattamaiḥ || 1,21.78 ||
deśo dhanyataraḥ ślāghyaḥ puṇyakṣetraṃ ca pāvanam |
tatratyāśca prajā dhanyāḥ sphītārthāḥ puṇyakarmiṇaḥ || 1,21.79 ||
rāṣṭraṃ subhikṣamārogyamanāmayasukhāvaham |
ītihīnaṃ kālavṛṣṭisahitaṃ dhānyasaṅkulam || 1,21.80 ||
taskaropadravavyāghrasarpādibhayavarjitam |
tatra tīrthāni sarvāṇi gaṅgādīni vasanti ca || 1,21.81 ||
indrādayo'pi vibudhāḥ sūryacandrādayo grahāḥ |
yoginyo bhairavāḥ siddhā gaṇeśaguhamātaraḥ || 1,21.82 ||
tatrasthasya mahīpasya jayārthāvāptirāyuṣaḥ |
sambhaveccakravartitvaṃ punarnityotsavojjvalam || 1,21.83 ||
vandhyānāṃ putrasampattiḥ kuṭīsaṃdarśanādbhavet |
kuṣṭhāpasmārabhūtādimahāvyādhivināśanam || 1,21.84 ||
kuṭīgatā bhaveyuste ye sevante rasāyanam |
siddhiṃ yānti sukhenaiva devānāmapi durlabhām || 1,21.85 ||
<amarīkalpaḥ>
devi citraṃ pravakṣyāmi divyaṃ guhyatamaṃ śivam |
amarīkalpamanaghaṃ sulabhaṃ nijadehajam || 1,21.86 ||
aṇimādyaṣṭakaiśvaryadehalohādisiddhidam |
vamanādiviśuddhāṅgo lavaṇāmlavivarjitaḥ || 1,21.87 ||
kuṭīgatastu deveśi śivatoyamudāradhīḥ |
sthūlasya katakasyaiva dāruṇā pānapātrakam || 1,21.88 ||
kuryācca triphalāṃ piṣṭvā lepayeccaṣakāntare |
karṣaṃ śivāmbunā rātrau pātraṃ cādhomukhaṃ priye || 1,21.89 ||
sthāpayitvā punaḥ prātarādyantaṃ prasravaṃ tyajet |
madhyadhārāśca tatpātre kṣipettattriphalāṃ tataḥ || 1,21.90 ||
loḍayedamarīyuktaṃ pibetprāgānanaḥ sadā |
iṣṭadevāngurūnvṛddhānnatvārcitagaṇeśvaraḥ || 1,21.91 ||
mitāhāro yuktaceṣṭo vātātapavivarjitaḥ |
ekamaṇḍalamātreṇa bāhyāntaḥ śucibhāgbhavet || 1,21.92 ||
maṇḍalena dvitīyena sarvakuṣṭhavināśanam |
maṇḍalena tṛtīyena caturvidhaviṣāpaham || 1,21.93 ||
maṇḍalena caturthena nālikerāmbuvadbhavet |
ātmatoyaṃ pañcamena maladaurgandhyanāśanam || 1,21.94 ||
ṣaṣṭhamaṇḍalayogena dehadaurgandhyanāśanam |
saptamaṇḍalayogena bhavedindriyapāṭavam || 1,21.95 ||
tato'ṣṭame maṇḍale tu rajanīcūrṇakarṣayuk |
seveta śivatoyaṃ ca yāvaddviḥ saptamaṇḍalam || 1,21.96 ||
valīpalitahīnaḥ syānmahāviṣabhayojjhitaḥ |
maṇḍale pañcadaśake karṣāṃśau śṛṅgagandhakau || 1,21.97 ||
śivatoyena sammiśraṃ prapibedekaviṃśatim |
maṇḍalaṃ ca tadante syātsiddhibhāgdehalohayoḥ || 1,21.98 ||
dvāviṃśanmaṇḍale devi cābhrakaṃ varayā yutam |
evaṃ bhajedyamī yāvadaṣṭāviṃśatimaṇḍalam || 1,21.99 ||
mattahastibalopeto gṛdhradṛṣṭiranāmayaḥ |
ekonatriṃśati prāpte maṇḍale varayā yutām || 1,21.100 ||
sakāntacūrṇavimalāṃ godhāmapi pibetpriye |
pañcatriṃśanmaṇḍalāntaṃ vajrakāyo bhavennaraḥ || 1,21.101 ||
ṣaṭtriṃśanmaṇḍalādi svarṇadhātrīrajo'nvitam |
ātmagodhānvitaṃ peyaṃ tattu yāvaddvyadhikam || 1,21.102 ||
catvāriṃśanmaṇḍalāntaṃ saśarīraḥ khago bhavet |
tricatvāriṃśanmaṇḍale tu prāpte pāradasaṃyutam || 1,21.103 ||
varāyutaṃ pibedūnapañcāśanmaṇḍalāvadhiḥ |
aṇimādiguṇopeto vajrakāyaśca khecaraḥ || 1,21.104 ||
siddhairyukto nirātaṅko nirapāyo nirañjanaḥ |
yadṛcchayā sarvaloke viharatyeva sarvadā || 1,21.105 ||
ārabhya prathamaṃ devi maṇḍalānnityamācaret |
ātmatoyena dhuttūrarasena pariloḍayet || 1,21.106 ||
bhasma tenaiva sarvāṅgamasakṛtparimardayet |
anyakālasamāyātamātmatoyaṃ viśeṣataḥ || 1,21.107 ||
mūrdhni nāsāpuṭe karṇe dṛśi pādakaradvaye |
śīlayetsatataṃ devi śuddhadeho bhavennaraḥ || 1,21.108 ||
kadācidapyātmagodhāṃ bhūmau na visṛjetpriye |
amarīsevinaḥ puṃsaḥ śivatoyena jāyate || 1,21.109 ||
sarvalohaṃ ca kanakaṃ divyaṃ manujadurlabham |
vijayāsahitāṃ godhāṃ prativāsaramāpibet || 1,21.110 ||


āk, 1, 22
<vandākakalpaḥ>
vandāko dvividhaḥ proktaḥ puruṣastrīvibhedataḥ |
kharjūrīpatravatpatraḥ puruṣaḥ sarvasiddhidaḥ || 1,22.1 ||
strīsaṃjñastu guṇairalpo vṛttapatraḥ pratāpavān |
vandākaḥ pādaparuhaḥ śikharī tarurohiṇī || 1,22.2 ||
vṛkṣādanī kāminī ca vṛkṣarugbandhabandhakam |
vandākastiktatuvaraḥ kaphapittaśramāpahaḥ || 1,22.3 ||
vaśyādisiddhido vṛṣyo viṣaghnaśca rasāyanam |
<vandākāharaṇavidhiḥ>
kṛtopavāsaḥ susnāto raktamālyānulepanaḥ || 1,22.4 ||
muktakeśāmbaro bhūtvā rātrau saṃyatamānasaḥ |
vṛkṣaṃ pradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā gandhapuṣpākṣatādibhiḥ || 1,22.5 ||
pūjādravyaiḥ samabhyarcya baliṃ dadhyodanaiḥ kiret |
cintāmaṇiṃ nṛsiṃhaṃ ca manumaṣṭottaraṃ śatam || 1,22.6 ||
japtvā khaḍgena saṃchidya vandākaṃ vidhināharet |
vandākānāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ vidhireṣa udāhṛtaḥ || 1,22.7 ||
aśvinyāṃ śuciraśvatthavandākaṃ vidhināhṛtam |
kṣīreṇa piṣṭamāloḍya pibedaśvabalo bhavet || 1,22.8 ||
tathaivāśvatthavandākamuparāge'rkacandrayoḥ |
nṛsiṃhamantraṃ prajapan khaḍgenācchidya cāharet || 1,22.9 ||
ghṛṣṭvāṃbhasā prakoṣṭheṇa liptvā gorocanānvitam |
vidhāya tilakaṃ paśyetsarvavaśyo bhaveddhruvam || 1,22.10 ||
nyagrodhasya tu vandākamaśvinyāṃ vidhināharet |
sūtreṇa bandhayeddhaste hyadṛśyo jāyate naraḥ || 1,22.11 ||
sarvavaśyaṃ bhavetkṣīraiḥ piṣṭvā pāne gadāñjayet |
aśvinyāṃ tu śirīṣasya vandākaṃ vidhināharet || 1,22.12 ||
sūtreṇa bandhayeddhaste vīryastambho bhaveddhruvam |
aśvinyāmāhareddhīmān palāśasya tu bandhakam || 1,22.13 ||
haste baddhvā spṛśedyastu sā nārī vaśagā bhavet |
aṅkolabandhamaśvinyāṃ kare baddhvā jagatpriyaḥ || 1,22.14 ||
bharaṇyāṃ kuśavandākaṃ gṛhītvā śubhayogataḥ |
badhnīyāddakṣiṇe haste tenādṛśyo bhavennaraḥ || 1,22.15 ||
bharaṇyāṃ phalguvandākaṃ dhānyarāśau vinikṣipet |
tena vai dhānyavṛddhiḥ syānnātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 1,22.16 ||
bharaṇyāṃ badarīṇāṃ ca vandākaṃ vidhināharet |
bandhayeddakṣiṇe haste saṃlabhedīpsitaṃ phalam || 1,22.17 ||
kṛttikāyāṃ tu katakavandākaṃ vidhināharet |
vartimadhye kṣipettaṃ ca tena saṃgṛhya kajjalam || 1,22.18 ||
strīṇāmañjanamātreṇa patirvaśyo bhaveddhruvam |
kṛttikāyāṃ śucirbhūtvā jambūvandākamāharet || 1,22.19 ||
kṣīreṇa piṣṭaṃ tatkalkaṃ pītvā rogairvimucyate |
rohiṇyāṃ tintriṇīkasya vandākaṃ vidhināharet || 1,22.20 ||
baddhvā haste spṛśedyaṃ yaṃ sarvavaśyo bhavennaraḥ |
timirādiṣu śastaṃ tadasādhyaṃ ghṛtamañjanāt || 1,22.21 ||
kṣaudraghṛṣṭena tenaiva kṛtaṃ cakṣuṣyamañjanam |
bandhakamudumbarabhavaṃ rohiṇyāṃ gṛhya valayakaṃ kuryāt |
tadvalayopari vinihitabhāṇḍasthitamodakaṃ kṣayaṃ naiti || 1,22.22 ||
aśvatthasya tu vandākaṃ rohiṇyāṃ vidhināharet || 1,22.23 ||
gṛhe sthite ca vandāke nityaiśvaryaṃ prajāyate |
katakasya tu vandākaṃ rohiṇyāṃ vidhināharet || 1,22.24 ||
añjane netrayugale nidhiṃ paśyati niścitam |
tadeva bandhayeddhaste sarvavaśyo bhaveddhruvam || 1,22.25 ||
udumbarasya vandākaṃ rohiṇyāṃ vidhināharet |
haste baddhvā nihantyāśu jvaraṃ cāturthikaṃ priye || 1,22.26 ||
rohiṇyāṃ mātuluṅgasya bandhakaṃ tu samāharet |
dhārayeddakṣiṇe karṇe jagadvaśyakaraṃ param || 1,22.27 ||
rohiṇyāṃ bilvavandākaṃ kare baddhvā jagatpriyaḥ |
mṛgaśīrṣe śirīṣasya vandākaṃ samyagāhṛtam || 1,22.28 ||
haste baddhvā spṛśennārīṃ naraṃ vā vaśayeddhruvam |
mṛgaśīrṣe tu vandākaṃ tintriṇīvṛkṣasambhavam || 1,22.29 ||
kṣīreṇa piṣṭvā prapibedadṛśyo jāyate naraḥ |
taddadhnā yaḥ pibetprātaḥ sarvavyādhiharo bhavet || 1,22.30 ||
mahiṣītakrapiṣṭena tena sarvāṅgalepanam |
kṛtvā vahnigato yastu vahninā ca na dahyate || 1,22.31 ||
tintriṇīkasya vandākaṃ gṛhe yasya pratiṣṭhitam |
tasya corabhayaṃ nāsti kare dyūtajayo bhavet || 1,22.32 ||
udumbarasya vandākaṃ mṛgaśīrṣe samāharet |
haste baddhvā spṛśennārīṃ sā nārī vaśagā bhavet || 1,22.33 ||
strīṇāṃ haste tu badhnīyādannamadhye vinikṣipet |
jāyate cānnavṛddhistu nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 1,22.34 ||
nyagrodhasya tu vandākaṃ mṛgaśīrṣe samāharet |
badhnīyāddakṣiṇe haste janavaśyo bhaveddhruvam || 1,22.35 ||
ādāya māṭarūṣasya vandākaṃ samudāhṛtam |
baddhvā haste janairdīvyan syāddyūteṣvaparājitaḥ || 1,22.36 ||
ārdrāyāṃ taumburaṃ grāhyaṃ vandākaṃ vidhinā haret |
kṣīreṇa prapibedyastu tasya syāllohamoṭanam || 1,22.37 ||
ārdrārke vā puṣyaravau vandākaṃ tumburorharet |
badhnīyāddakṣiṇe haste bāṇastambhaḥ prajāyate || 1,22.38 ||
punarvasorhṛtaṃ vaṃśavandākaṃ kṣīrapeṣitam |
pītvā strīpuruṣau vandhyau prasuvāte sutānbahūn || 1,22.39 ||
naktamālasya vandākamāharecca punarvasau |
kṣīreṇa piṣṭvā prapibedakhilāmayanāśanam || 1,22.40 ||
taccūrṇasya mātreṇa naśyanti graharākṣasāḥ |
puṣye bandhūkavandākaṃ gṛhītvā taṃ nidhāpayet || 1,22.41 ||
kṣetramadhye ripostatra sasyanāśaśca jāyate |
āśleṣāyāṃ karṇikāravandākaṃ samyagāhṛtam || 1,22.42 ||
baddhvā haste dṛḍhaṃ tena spṛśan vaśati tejanāt |
madhūkasya ca vandākamāśleṣāyāṃ samāharet || 1,22.43 ||
bandhayeddakṣiṇe haste vyāghrādibhayanāśanam |
maghāsu mucukundasya vandākaṃ vidhināhṛtam || 1,22.44 ||
nidhāpayeddhānyamadhye taddhānyaṃ tvakṣayaṃ bhavet |
vibhītakasya vandākaṃ phalgunyoḥ pūrvayorhṛtam || 1,22.45 ||
sthāpayeddhānyamadhyena tadakṣayyaṃ kṣaṇādbhavet |
phalgunyoranyayorhaste'pyayameva vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ || 1,22.46 ||
śākavṛkṣasya vandākaṃ maghāyāṃ vidhināharet |
dhānyasaṃcayakṛddvāre dhānyavṛddhiśca jāyate || 1,22.47 ||
mauñjīvṛkṣasya vandākaṃ maghārke vidhināharet |
śirasā dhārayennityaṃ tasya śrīrvaśamāpnuyāt || 1,22.48 ||
palāśasya tu vandākaṃ hastarkṣe vidhināharet |
kare śirasi badhnīyādvyāghrādigrahahṛdbhavet || 1,22.49 ||
tindukasya ca vandākaṃ citrāyāmāhṛtaṃ tathā |
baddhvā haste samastānāṃ sadhvadho bhavati dviṣām || 1,22.50 ||
kuṭajasya tu vandākaṃ citrāyāṃ vidhināharet |
badhnīyāddakṣiṇe haste durlabhaṃ cepsitaṃ labhet || 1,22.51 ||
svātyāṃ likucavandākaṃ gṛhītvā yatra vastuni |
nidhāpayati tatsarvamakṣayaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ || 1,22.52 ||
babbūlabandhakaṃ svātyāṃ badaryāstvanurādhake |
badhnanyo dhārayeddhaste tatspṛṣṭā strī vaśā bhavet || 1,22.53 ||
āhṛtya dhātrīvandākaṃ viśākhāyāṃ yathāvidhi |
śuktakārasya bhavane viruddhasya nidhāpayet || 1,22.54 ||
sarvaṃ madyamapeyaṃ syānmokṣastasminsamuddhṛte |
anurādhāsu vidhinā aindrīvandākamāhṛtam || 1,22.55 ||
dhānyamadhye vinikṣiptaṃ dhānyamakṣayatāṃ nayet |
jyeṣṭhāyāmāmravandākaṃ hṛtvā veśyāgṛhe khanet || 1,22.56 ||
sā durbhagā bhavetsatyamuddhṛte mokṣa ucyate |
mūle khadiravandākaṃ gṛhītvā vidhipūrvakam || 1,22.57 ||
baddhvā haste naraḥ kṣipraṃ durbhagaḥ subhago bhavet |
tadeva piṣṭaṃ kṣīreṇa pītaṃ kuṣṭhādināśanam || 1,22.58 ||
mūlārke goṭṭikāyāśca vandākaṃ vidhināharet |
dakṣiṇe bandhayeddhaste strīvaśyaṃ bhavati dhruvam || 1,22.59 ||
tadeva śirasā dhāryaṃ sarvasiddhirbhaveddhruvam |
karavīrasya vandākaṃ pūrvāṣāḍhāsu sādhitam || 1,22.60 ||
dhārayecchirasā yuddhe sa bhavedaparājitaḥ |
pūrvāṣāḍhāsu vandākaṃ badarīvṛkṣasambhavam || 1,22.61 ||
pibetkṣīreṇa yā vandhyā sā bahūṃśca sutāṃllabhet |
kuravasya tu vandākaṃ pūrvāṣāḍhārkavārake || 1,22.62 ||
bandhayeddakṣiṇe haste hyadṛśyo jāyate naraḥ |
uttarāṣāḍhanakṣatre grāhyaṃ mandārabandhakam || 1,22.63 ||
palāśabandhakaṃ vātha kare baddhvā vaśaṃkaram |
śravaṇe citrakodbhūtaṃ vandākaṃ prāpyate na tu || 1,22.64 ||
kaṅkuṇītailaghṛṣṭena tāmrapatraṃ ca lepayet |
puṭapākavidhānena tatsvarṇaṃ bhavati dhruvam || 1,22.65 ||
eraṇḍasya tu vandākaṃ śravaṇārke samāharet |
bandhayeddakṣiṇe haste sadā dyūtajayo bhavet || 1,22.66 ||
dhaniṣṭhāyāṃ tu badarīvandākaṃ vidhināhṛtam |
baddhvā kare spṛśedyaṃ yaṃ sa sa dāso bhaveddhruvam || 1,22.67 ||
hṛtaṃ punnāgavandākaṃ vāruṇeṣu yathāvidhi |
kṣīreṇa piṣṭvā prapibanmahānāgabalo bhavet || 1,22.68 ||
naktamālasya vandākaṃ maghārke vidhināharet |
vidhinā dhārayedbāhau piśācānāṃ ca darśanam || 1,22.69 ||
tintriṇīkasya vandākaṃ punnarkṣe vidhināharet |
bandhayeddakṣiṇe haste nityaṃ dyūtajayo bhavet || 1,22.70 ||
punnarkṣe vaṃśavandākamadṛśyo jāyate kare |
śirīṣasya tu vandākamuttare vidhināharet || 1,22.71 ||
kare śirasi badhnīyādvyāghrādigrahahṛdbhavet |
maghāyāṃ sthāpayetkṣetre vandākaṃ madhukodbhavam || 1,22.72 ||
pakṣiṇāṃ mūṣikānāṃ ca jāyate tuṇḍabandhanam |
palāśasya tu vandākaṃ hastarkṣe vidhināharet || 1,22.73 ||
liṅgalepaṃ prakurvīta vīryastambho bhaveddhruvam |
kṣīreṇāloḍya vallīkaṃ kṣaudraṃ piṣṭvā niṣecayet || 1,22.74 ||
vaṅgaṃ tu saptadhā devi tadvaṅgaṃ rajataṃ bhavet |
palāśasya tu vandākaṃ hastarkṣe vidhināharet || 1,22.75 ||
kṣīreṇa prapibedyastu hyadṛśyo jāyate naraḥ |
tindukasya ca vandākaṃ citrāyāmāhṛtaṃ tathā || 1,22.76 ||
baddhvā haste samastānāmavadhyo bhavati dviṣām |
harītakyāstu vandākaṃ pūrvabhādrapadāhṛtam || 1,22.77 ||
kṣīreṇa kalkitaṃ pītvā caredvararuceḥ samaḥ |
uttarāsvarkavandākaṃ hṛtaṃ bhādrapadāsu tat || 1,22.78 ||
pralepādyaiḥ paraṃ hanyādviṣaṃ sthāvarajaṅgamam |
revatyāṃ bodhivandākalatayā kṛtakautukā || 1,22.79 ||
vandhyāpi labhate garbhaṃ pītvā gopayasā ca tat |
revatyāṃ vaṭavandākaṃ valmīkamadhugharṣitam || 1,22.80 ||
akṣidoṣeṣvatiśreṣṭhamakṣarāvaraṇādiṣu |
revatyāṃ vaṭavandākaṃ gṛhītvā dhārayedbhuje || 1,22.81 ||
mahānāgabalopeto mahāgaṇaśca jāyate |
samuddhṛtaṃ viśākhāyāṃ vandākaṃ rājavṛkṣakam || 1,22.82 ||
nivārayati gehasthaṃ vaiśvānarabhayaṃ gṛhe |
rohiṇyāṃ vaṭavandākaṃ kaṭisthaṃ vīryavardhanam || 1,22.83 ||
aśvatthabhavavandākaṃ revatyāṃ garbhadaṃ smṛtam |
viṣavṛkṣotthavandākaṃ pūrvāṣāḍhoddhṛtaṃ yadi || 1,22.84 ||
praliptaṃ tanute bhūtapiśācādiprabhāṣaṇam |
cullikāntargataṃ kuryātsabhaktāṃ pānasantatim || 1,22.85 ||
dyūte jāmbavavandākaṃ revatyāṃ jayakārakam |
kuryātkuravavandākaṃ hastasthaṃ bāṇavāraṇam || 1,22.86 ||
madhūkavṛkṣavandākaṃ dhānyasthaṃ dhānyavṛddhidam |
palāśataruvandākaṃ vākpradaṃ kṣīrasevitam || 1,22.87 ||
veśyāyāṃ nimbavandākaṃ gṛhāntarnihitaṃ yadi |
vaśyaṃ karoti sāścaryamā janmamaraṇāntikam || 1,22.88 ||


āk, 1, 23
<viśiṣṭā rasasaṃskārāḥ>
śrībhairavī |
śrutaṃ sarvaṃ mayā deva divyaṃ sarvarasāyanam |
śaṃbho tava prasādena kṛpāṃbhodhe sureśvara || 1,23.1 ||
vijñāpayiṣyāmyaparaṃ sarvalokahitaṅkaram |
vṛddhastrībālaṣaṇḍhānāmanyeṣāṃ rogiṇāmapi || 1,23.2 ||
rasāyaneṣvaśaktānāṃ kathaṃ saukhyaṃ bhavetprabho |
sarvānugrāhaka śrīman tadājñāpaya bhairava || 1,23.3 ||
śrutvā devyāḥ stutiparaṃ sarvalokahitapradam |
śrutvā mandasmitaṃ devo jagādetthaṃ paraṃ vacaḥ || 1,23.4 ||
śrībhairavaḥ |
sādhu sādhu mahābhāge lokānāṃ jananī yataḥ |
tasmāllokahitaṃ pṛṣṭaṃ tadvakṣyāmyahamīśvari || 1,23.5 ||
rasādisaṃskāravidhiṃ sarvaroganibarhaṇam |
yathākramaṃ pravakṣyāmi śrūyatāmavadhānataḥ || 1,23.6 ||
rasendro rasarājaśca rasaḥ sūtaśca pāradaḥ |
śivabījaṃ śivo jaitro rasaloho mahārasaḥ || 1,23.7 ||
rasottamo mahātejāḥ sūtarāṭcapalo'mṛtaḥ |
dhutturo lokanāthaśca prabhurindro bhavastathā || 1,23.8 ||
rudratejāḥ khecaraśca rasadhāturacintyajaḥ |
amaro dehadaḥ skandaḥ skandeśo mṛtyunāśanaḥ || 1,23.9 ||
devo rasāyanaḥ śreṣṭho yaśodaḥ pāvanaḥ smṛtaḥ |
proktā divyarasāścaiva trayastriṃśacca nāma ca || 1,23.10 ||
doṣayuktaḥ sūtarājo viṣameva varānane |
doṣahīno rasaḥ sākṣādamṛtaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ || 1,23.11 ||
tasmātpāradasaṃskāraṃ doṣaghnaṃ śṛṇu pārvati |
rasācāryo bhiṣakśreṣṭho yatātmāghoramantravit || 1,23.12 ||
<rasaśodhanasaṃskārāḥ; prathamaḥ prakāraḥ>
śubharkṣe śubhalagneṣu sumuhūrte suvāsare |
pūrvoktavatsūtapūjāṃ kuryādādau śucisthale || 1,23.13 ||
śataṃ palānāṃ pañcāśatpañcaviṃśati vā punaḥ |
daśa vā pañca vā devi naitasmādūnamiṣyate || 1,23.14 ||
lohaje vā śilotthe vā khalve sūtaṃ vinikṣipet |
kalāṃśaṃ sūtarājasya cūrṇeṣṭakaniśārajaḥ || 1,23.15 ||
jambīrāmlena saṃmardya taptakhalve dinaṃ priye |
kṣālayeduṣṇasauvīrairnāgadoṣo vinaśyati || 1,23.16 ||
aṅkolenendravāruṇyā vaṅgadoṣo vinaśyati |
āragvadhena ca malaṃ citrake nāgadūṣaṇam || 1,23.17 ||
kṛṣṇadhūrtena cāñcalyaṃ triphalābhirviṣaṃ haret |
giridoṣaṃ trikaṭukairasahyāgnistu gokṣuraiḥ || 1,23.18 ||
vaṅgādisaptadoṣāṇāṃ nāśārthaṃ saptavāsaram |
tattaccūrṇaiḥ kalāṃśaiśca kumāryā rasasaṃyutaiḥ || 1,23.19 ||
mardayitvā rasaṃ paścātkṣālayeduṣṇakāṃjikaiḥ |
pāradaḥ sakalairdoṣairmucyate saptakañcukaiḥ || 1,23.20 ||
vaidyakarmaṇi yojyaścedrasaḥ syātsarvarogahā |
<dvitīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
devadārumalayajajayāvāyasatuṇḍikā || 1,23.21 ||
kumārīmusalīvandhyākarkoṭīrasasaṃyutam |
sūtaṃ dinaṃ mardayecca punaḥ pātanayantrake || 1,23.22 ||
pātayedyojayetsūtaṃ śuddhaṃ vaidyasya karmaṇi |
<tṛtīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
dagdhaṃ pāṣāṇacūrṇaṃ ca niśākanyārasai rasam || 1,23.23 ||
mardayeddinamekaṃ ca pūrvayantre ca pātayet |
evaṃ saṃskārasaṃśuddhaṃ yojayedvaidyakarmaṇi || 1,23.24 ||
<caturthaḥ prakāraḥ>
daradaṃ yāmamātraṃ tu pāribhadradravaiḥ priye |
athavā jambīrarasairmardayitvā tu pācayet || 1,23.25 ||
yantre pātanake devi doṣakañcukavarjitaḥ |
<pañcamaḥ prakāraḥ>
punarnavārasaiḥ peṣyaṃ dhānyābhraṃ pāradaṃ samam || 1,23.26 ||
taptakhalve dinaṃ kṛtvā vajramūṣāgataṃ rasam |
pacedbhūdharayantre ca punaḥ saṃmardayecca tam || 1,23.27 ||
pūrvadravairyathāpūrvaṃ mardanaṃ pācanaṃ punaḥ |
kṛtvaitaṃ daśavāraṃ taṃ pātyaṃ pātanayantrake || 1,23.28 ||
śuddhaḥ syātpārado devi yojyo yoge rasāyane |
<ṣaṣṭhaḥ prakāraḥ>
daśamāṃśaṃ rasādgandhaṃ taptakhalve vinikṣipet || 1,23.29 ||
varājambīrakanyāgnidravairyāmaṃ vimardayet |
pātayetpātanāyantre kuryādevaṃ tu saptadhā || 1,23.30 ||
sagandhakaṃ mardanaṃ ca pātanaṃ bhavati priye |
śuddhaḥ syātpārado devi yojyaḥ pāradakarmaṇi || 1,23.31 ||
<saptamaḥ prakāraḥ>
tilatailairmāhiṣikairmūtrairmadyāmlakena ca |
gomāṃsairhiṅgulaṃ pācyaṃ lohapātre kramāgninā || 1,23.32 ||
saptāhaṃ lohadaṇḍena cālayettaddravaṃ muhuḥ |
sārdraṃ mayūrapittena bhāvayedātape dinam || 1,23.33 ||
pātayetpātanāyantre daradaṃ kharavahninā |
śuddho bhaveccaturyāmātpārado yogavāhakaḥ || 1,23.34 ||
<jāraṇārho vaḍabānalaviḍaḥ>
śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi jāraṇārhaṃ biḍaṃ priye |
cūrṇaṃ kṛtvā dagdhaśaṅkhaṃ gharme'rkakṣīrabhāvitam || 1,23.35 ||
kuryāddinaṃ tato devi dhūmasāraṃ ca bhāvayet |
dinaṃ jambīrakarasairātape cātitīvrake || 1,23.36 ||
caturyāmamajāmūtraiḥ ṣoḍaśāṃśaṃ suvarcalam |
yathā sāndratvamāpnoti tāvatkvāthyaṃ varānane || 1,23.37 ||
naramūtre ṣoḍaśāṃśe kaṇṭakārīṃ samūlakām |
yāvadghanībhavettāvatkvāthanīyā prayatnataḥ || 1,23.38 ||
tintriṇīkṣārakāsīsasarjakṣārāḥ śilājatu |
jambīrasya rasaiḥ sarvaṃ caturyāmaṃ pṛthakpṛthak || 1,23.39 ||
jepālaṃ tattvacāhīnaṃ mūlakakṣārasaindhavam |
guñjāṃ ca ṭaṅkaṇaṃ śigrudravairbhāvyaṃ pṛthakpṛthak || 1,23.40 ||
jambīrāṇāṃ dravairbhāvyaṃ śaṅkhādyaṃ tattrayodaśa |
sarvaṃ samāṃśaṃ ca dinamekīkṛtya tu gālitam || 1,23.41 ||
rakṣayetsarvadā jñeyo nāmnā ca vaḍabānalaḥ |
suvarṇādimalohānāṃ ratnānāṃ jāraṇe tathā || 1,23.42 ||
abhrādyuparasānāṃ ca yojanīyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
<pāradabhasmavidhiḥ; prathamaḥ prakāraḥ>
śuddhasūtaṃ śuddhagandhaṃ mardayedgoghṛtaiḥ samam || 1,23.43 ||
piṇḍībhūtaṃ kumāryāśca dalagarbhe niveśayet |
samyaksūtreṇa saṃveṣṭya tamayaskāntasaṃpuṭe || 1,23.44 ||
nirudhya taṃ mṛdupuṭe tridhā pācyaṃ punaḥ priye |
ādāya dṛḍhamūṣāyāmandhayitvā dhametsudhīḥ || 1,23.45 ||
bhasmībhavetsūtarājo yojyo yoge rasāyane |
<dvitīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
chāyāśuṣkāṇi kurvīta śākapakvaphalāni ca || 1,23.46 ||
mardayedarkapayasā tena mūṣodaraṃ lipet |
agraprasūtagojātajarāyoḥ śiṣitaṃ rajaḥ || 1,23.47 ||
mūṣāmadhye kṣipetpaścātsūtaṃ gandhaśca tadrajaḥ |
kṣiptvā nirudhya ca dhamedbhasmībhavati pāradaḥ || 1,23.48 ||
<tṛtīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
vandhyā kārkoṭakī kākatuṇḍī ca kaṭutumbikā |
kañcukī nalikā kākamācī vai kālamañjarī || 1,23.49 ||
kākajaṅghāstvimāḥ sarvāḥ piṣṭvā mūṣāntare kṣipet |
pūrvoktavandhyāmukhyābhiraṣṭābhirmardayedrasam || 1,23.50 ||
tadrasaṃ liptamūṣāyāṃ kṣiptvā ruddhvā ca bhūdhare |
pacedevaṃ cāṣṭavāramevaṃ mūṣāpralepanam || 1,23.51 ||
mardanaṃ dhamanaṃ kuryādbhūyo bhūyaḥ sureśvari |
mṛto bhavati sūtendro yogyo roge rasāyane || 1,23.52 ||
<caturthaḥ prakāraḥ>
niyāmakauṣadhairmardyaṃ caturyāmaṃ rasaṃ dṛḍham |
dviguṇe gandhataile ca śanairmandāgninā pacet || 1,23.53 ||
yāvatkhoṭatvamāpnoti tāvadevaṃ pacedrasam |
tatkhoṭaṃ saṃpuṭe lauhe kṣiptvā rundhyāddṛḍhaṃ sudhīḥ || 1,23.54 ||
pathyājalairlohakiṭṭaṃ piṣṭvā sampuṭamālikhet |
tasyordhvaṃ śrāvake kācaṃ kṛtvā nāgaṃ vinikṣipet || 1,23.55 ||
sa nāgo dravate yāvattāvadevaṃ dhametpriye |
yāvanna yāti kāṭhinyaṃ tāvannaiva dhametsudhīḥ || 1,23.56 ||
punaḥ kaṭhinatāṃ prāpte dhametpūrvoktavanmuhuḥ |
triyāmadhamanādevaṃ bhasmībhavati pāradaḥ || 1,23.57 ||
bhasmīkṛte mūlikābhirjāraṇārahito rasaḥ |
dehe lohe na yojyaḥ syādyojyo bheṣajakarmaṇi || 1,23.58 ||
<pañcamaḥ prakāraḥ>
dviguṇe gandhataile ca śuddhaṃ sūtaṃ vimardayet |
ekāhaṃ taṃ punarmardyaṃ sarpākṣībhṛṅgarāḍapi || 1,23.59 ||
viṣṇukrāntā tryahaṃ caiṣāṃ rasaiḥ ślakṣṇaṃ vimardayet |
pacedyantre trisaṃghaṭṭe hyaṣṭavāramiti priye || 1,23.60 ||
kuryādbhasmati sūtendro rogasaṃghātanāśanaḥ |
<ṣaṣṭhaḥ prakāraḥ>
śvetāṅkolasya mūlottharasairmardyastryahaṃ rasaḥ || 1,23.61 ||
mūṣāyāṃ nikṣipedruddhvā pacedbhūdharayantrake |
pārado bhasmatāṃ yāti sarvarogaharaḥ paraḥ || 1,23.62 ||
<saptamaḥ prakāraḥ>
viṣṇukrāntāṃ vedikāṃ ca kāñjikena vimardayet |
tatkalkena raso mardyaḥ saptadhā mūrchitotthitaḥ || 1,23.63 ||
taṃ rasaṃ śrāvake kṣiptvā siñcayettadrasairmuhuḥ |
dīpāgninā dinaṃ pacyādbhasma syāllavaṇākṛtiḥ || 1,23.64 ||
<aṣṭamaḥ prakāraḥ>
kākoduṃbarikākṣīrairbhāvayetsomarāmaṭham |
punaḥ punaḥ saptadhaivaṃ śoṣayenmardayetsudhīḥ || 1,23.65 ||
kākodumbarapañcāṅgaṃ ṣoḍaśāṃśe jale kṣipet |
yathaikāṃśo'vaśiṣṭaḥ syāttena taddhiṅgu mardayet || 1,23.66 ||
tadgolake rasaṃ kṣiptvā mūṣāyāṃ taṃ ca rodhayet |
bhūdhare ca puṭedevamaṣṭavāraṃ punaḥ punaḥ || 1,23.67 ||
puṭe puṭe ca taddhiṅgu dadyātsūto mṛto bhavet |
<navamaḥ prakāraḥ>
urubūkasya bījāni tathāpāmārgajāni ca || 1,23.68 ||
pūrṇayettacca mūṣāyāṃ kṣiptvā sūtaṃ tataḥ kṣipet |
tadūrdhvaṃ pūrvacūrṇaṃ ca samyaṅmūṣāṃ nirodhayet || 1,23.69 ||
pacellaghupuṭairevaṃ caturbhirbhasmatāṃ vrajet |
<daśamaḥ prakāraḥ>
kuḍuhuñcyāḥ kandamadhye kāntāstanyapariplute || 1,23.70 ||
rasaṃ kṣiptvā mukhaṃ ruddhvā tanmajjakalkataḥ sudhīḥ |
taṃ gomayaiḥ samālipya svedayedgomayāgninā || 1,23.71 ||
evaṃ kṛte saptavāraṃ raso bhasmatvamāpnuyāt |
<ekādaśaḥ prakāraḥ>
aṅkolasya śiphānīraiḥ sūtaṃ gandhaṃ samaṃ samam || 1,23.72 ||
dinamekaṃ mardayecca mūṣāgarbhaṃ nirodhayet |
puṭedbhūdharayantre ca dinānte bhasma jāyate || 1,23.73 ||
<dvādaśaḥ prakāraḥ>
sūtaṃ dhānyābhrakaṃ tulyaṃ mārakauṣadhijai rasaiḥ |
mardayeddinamekaṃ tu tatkalkairvastralepanam || 1,23.74 ||
tadvastraṃ vartikāṃ kṛtvā dhṛtvā daṃśena dīpayet |
taddrutiṃ patitāṃ kāṃsye kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ ca tadbhavet || 1,23.75 ||
tatpunarmārakairmardyaṃ pātanāyantrake pacet |
mṛto bhaveddinaikena tadbhasmākhilarogahṛt || 1,23.76 ||
<trayodaśaḥ prakāraḥ>
kāladhuttūratailena mardanīyaśca pāradaḥ |
tato niyāmakairmardyāddinaikaṃ kūrmayantrake || 1,23.77 ||
pācayedbhasmatāṃ yāti śubhraḥ syātsarvarogahā |
<caturdaśaḥ prakāraḥ>
śuddhasūtādardhabhāgaṃ śuddhaṃ gandhaṃ vimardayet || 1,23.78 ||
mārakauṣadhajairdrāvairdinaṃ mūṣāgataṃ pacet |
yantre bhūdharasaṃjñe ca dinenaikena bhasmati || 1,23.79 ||
<pañcadaśaḥ prakāraḥ>
sūtamabhraṃ vaṭakṣīraistriyāmaṃ mardayetpriye |
mūṣāgarbhe vinikṣipya karīṣāgnau dinaṃ pacet || 1,23.80 ||
bhasmībhavati sūtendraḥ śubhraḥ sarvārtināśanaḥ |
<ṣoḍaśaḥ prakāraḥ>
mukhīkṛte vāsite ca pārade samakāñcanam || 1,23.81 ||
jārayetpūrvayogena tato gandhaṃ samaṃ kṣipet |
divyauṣadhidravairmardyaṃ dinaṃ mūṣādhṛtaṃ rasam || 1,23.82 ||
divānaktaṃ karīṣāgnau pacedvā tuṣavahninā |
svedayettaṃ samuddhṛtya bhūyo divyauṣadhodbhavaiḥ || 1,23.83 ||
bījaiḥ samāṃśaiḥ saṃmardyaṃ caturyāmaṃ sureśvari |
vajramūṣāgataṃ dhāmyaṃ bhasmībhavati pāradaḥ || 1,23.84 ||
tadbhasma divyaṃ yuñjīta sadā roge rasāyane |
<saptadaśaḥ prakāraḥ>
svajīrṇe pārade svarṇaṃ samamamlena mardayet || 1,23.85 ||
piṣṭībhūtaṃ kāñjikena prakṣālyādāya tāṃ punaḥ |
