piṣṭyardhaṃ śuddhagandhaṃ ca tadardhaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ kṣipet || 1,23.86 ||
sarvatulyāṃ niśāṃ nārīpuṣparaktadravairdinam |
mardayettāṃ dinānte ca kuryāttadgolakaṃ tataḥ || 1,23.87 ||
pacedgaḍḍukayantre ca dinaṃ mandāgninā sudhīḥ |
samādāya vicūrṇyaiva dattvā tatsamagandhakam || 1,23.88 ||
garbhayantre tryahaṃ pācyaṃ laghunā tattuṣāgninā |
raso bhasma bhaveddevi nirutthaḥ syādrasāyanam || 1,23.89 ||
<aṣṭādaśaḥ prakāraḥ>
karṣatrayaṃ śuddhasūtaṃ karṣaṃ tāmrarajaḥ priye |
amlaiḥ saṃmardayedgāḍhaṃ dinaṃ khalve tato bhavet || 1,23.90 ||
piṣṭistāṃ kṣālayettoyaistata ādāya nirmalam |
mākṣīkasatvaṃ tatsarvaṃ cakramardacchadadravaiḥ || 1,23.91 ||
tridinaṃ mardayedgāḍhaṃ tāṃ piṣṭiṃ garbhayantrake |
tuṣāgninā paceddevi tridinaṃ vā divāniśam || 1,23.92 ||
karīṣāgnau bhavetsūtabhasma rogajarāpaham |
<ekonaviṃśaḥ prakāraḥ>
sūtaṃ svarṇaṃ vyomasatvaṃ samaṃ svarṇasamaṃ biḍam || 1,23.93 ||
rambhādaṇḍadravaiḥ sarvaṃ tatkhalve mardayeddinam |
tato divyauṣadhodbhūtairbījaistulyaṃ dinadvayam || 1,23.94 ||
saha saṃmardayedrambhātoyaistadgarbhayantrake |
pūrvakrameṇa vipacedrasabhasma bhavecchubham || 1,23.95 ||
jarāmaraṇarogaghnaṃ sarvasiddhipradāyakam |
<viṃśaḥ prakāraḥ>
rasendraṃ vimalāsatvaṃ tulyaṃ mardya dinatrayam || 1,23.96 ||
sinduvārachadarasaiḥ piṣṭiḥ syāttāṃ vinikṣipet |
kācakūpyantare kūpīṃ saptamṛtkarpaṭairlipet || 1,23.97 ||
śoṣayedvālukāyantre dinaṃ mandāgninā pacet |
rasabhasma bhaveddivyaṃ rugjarāmaraṇāpaham || 1,23.98 ||
<ekaviṃśaḥ prakāraḥ>
karṣadvayaṃ rasendraṃ ca tadardhaṃ śuddhagandhakam |
mākṣīkasatvaṃ gandhāṃśaṃ tatsarvaṃ mardayeddinam || 1,23.99 ||
nirguṇḍīpatrasāraiśca taṃ golaṃ nikṣipetpriye |
mūṣāmadhye tato mūṣāvaktraṃ ruddhvā vinikṣipet || 1,23.100 ||
tāṃ mūṣāṃ gaḍḍukāyantre pacenmandāgninā dinam |
rasabhasma bhaveddivyaṃ sindūrāruṇasannibham || 1,23.101 ||
jarāmaraṇarogaghnaṃ sarvasiddhipradaṃ śubham |
<dvāviṃśaḥ prakāraḥ>
pakvamūṣodare tulyagandhakāntaritaṃ rasam || 1,23.102 ||
tayoścaturguṇaṃ dattvā kākamācīrasaṃ punaḥ |
ācchādya gaḍḍukāyantre tāṃ nidhāya pacetkramāt || 1,23.103 ||
mṛdumadhyamacaṇḍākhyavahnau yāmacatuṣṭayam |
tataśca sarvarogaghnaṃ rasabhasma bhavecchubham || 1,23.104 ||
<trayoviṃśaḥ prakāraḥ>
snuhīkṣīrairdinaṃ sūtaṃ mardayettadabhāvataḥ |
amlavallyā rasaireva taṃ rasaṃ gandhakaiḥ samam || 1,23.105 ||
garbhayantre vinikṣipya pūrvavadvipacedbudhaḥ |
mṛto bhavedrasaḥ so'yaṃ sarvarogaharo bhavet || 1,23.106 ||
<caturviṃśaḥ prakāraḥ>
guñjāphalaṃ rasasamaṃ madhuṭaṅkaṇayāvakaiḥ |
bhṛṅgapatrarasairyuktaṃ dinamekaṃ vimardayet || 1,23.107 ||
chāditaṃ vajramūṣāyāṃ dhamayenmṛduvahninā |
ayaṃ rasāyano vṛṣyo rasaḥ syācchaśisannibhaḥ || 1,23.108 ||
<pañcaviṃśaḥ prakāraḥ>
samaṃ gandharasaṃ śuddhaṃ kīṭamāriṇikādravaiḥ |
ajamāryahimāryorvā śvetāṅkolarasena vā || 1,23.109 ||
mardayettridinaṃ kṣiptvā mṛṇmaye saṃpuṭe tataḥ |
dinamekaṃ karīṣāgnau tuṣāgnau vā dinatrayam || 1,23.110 ||
pacettataḥ sūtabhasma jāyate rugjarāpaham |
<ṣaḍviṃśaḥ prakāraḥ>
tāmrābhrapātanāyogācchodhitaṃ pāradaṃ priye || 1,23.111 ||
vajrabhasma samaṃ haṃsapādīdrāvairvimardayet |
divyauṣadhibhavairbījairvajramūṣāntaraṃ lipet || 1,23.112 ||
tasminpūrvarasaṃ kṣiptvā ruddhvātha tridinaṃ pacet |
tuṣāgninā tata uddhṛtya tattulyaṃ drutapāradam || 1,23.113 ||
haṃsapādīrasaiḥ sarvaṃ mardayettridinaṃ tataḥ |
pūrvavattaṃ pacetso'yaṃ raso bhasmati niścayaḥ || 1,23.114 ||
jarāmaraṇarogaghnaḥ sarvasiddhipradaḥ śubhaḥ |
<saptaviṃśaḥ prakāraḥ>
vāsitaṃ pāradaṃ karṣamaṣṭaguñjaṃ suvarṇakam || 1,23.115 ||
mṛtavajraṃ caturguñjaṃ mardyaṃ haṃsapadīrasaiḥ |
taptakhalve vajramūṣāgataṃ kṛtvā nirodhayet || 1,23.116 ||
pacedbhūdharayantre ca punarādāya taṃ rasam |
haṃsapādīdravairmardyaṃ taptakhalve dinatrayam || 1,23.117 ||
pūrvavadbhūdhare yantre pacettaṃ ca punaḥ punaḥ |
evaṃ śatapuṭaṃ kṛtvā bhasma syādraktavarṇakam || 1,23.118 ||
jarāmaraṇadāridryanāśanaṃ bhavanāśanam |
<aṣṭāviṃśaḥ prakāraḥ>
yugāṃśaḥ śuddhasūtaḥ syādekāṃśaṃ tāmracūrṇakam || 1,23.119 ||
amle dinaṃ mardayettaṃ prakṣālyādāya piṣṭikām |
tāpyasatvaṃ piṣṭisamaṃ cakramardadaladravaiḥ || 1,23.120 ||
mardayettridinaṃ sarvaṃ golakaṃ garbhayantrake |
nikṣipya tridinaṃ pācyaṃ tuṣāgnau vā sureśvari || 1,23.121 ||
divārātraṃ karīṣāgnau pacedbhasma bhavedrasaḥ |
<ekonatriṃśaḥ prakāraḥ>
mukhīkṛtarasaṃ cābhrasatvaṃ svarṇaṃ trayaṃ samam || 1,23.122 ||
raṃbhādraveṇa saṃmardyaṃ dinaṃ sarvasamaṃ biḍam |
tato divyauṣadhīnāṃ ca bījairmardyaṃ dinatrayam || 1,23.123 ||
garbhayantre pacetpaścātkarīṣāgnau mṛto bhavet |
<triṃśaḥ prakāraḥ>
khajīrṇasūtaṃ vimalāṃ samaṃ nirguṇḍikārasaiḥ || 1,23.124 ||
mardayitvā tryahaṃ kācakūpikāyāṃ vinikṣipet |
sikatāyantrake pacyāccaturyāmena bhasmitaḥ || 1,23.125 ||
<ekatriṃśaḥ prakāraḥ>
tāpyasatvaṃ samaṃ gandhaṃ dvābhyāṃ tulyaṃ ca jāritam |
sūtaṃ nirguṇḍikāṃ mardya dinaṃ tadgolakaṃ punaḥ || 1,23.126 ||
vajramūṣāndhitaṃ kṛtvā dhamedvā gaḍḍuyantrake |
pacettadraktavarṇaṃ syātsa rasaḥ sarvarogahā || 1,23.127 ||
<rasabhasmalakṣaṇam>
akṣayitvaṃ nirutthatvaṃ nirlepatvaṃ subhasmatā |
mārakatvaṃ ca lohānāṃ lakṣayedrasabhasmataḥ || 1,23.128 ||
<1. gandhapiṣṭī; prathamaḥ prakāraḥ>
saṃcūrṇya śodhitaṃ gandhaṃ pūrvapatrarasena ca |
saptadhā bhāvayedgharme strīṇāṃ ca rajasā tathā || 1,23.129 ||
tathā mānavapittena lolayedgandhakaṃ punaḥ |
palamekaṃ śuddharasaṃ karpare cātape nyaset || 1,23.130 ||
tadūrdhvaṃ lolitaṃ gandhaṃ vinyasyāṅguṣṭhamarditam |
kuryātsūto bhavetpiṣṭiḥ sarvakarmasu siddhidaḥ || 1,23.131 ||
<dvitīyaḥ prakāra, svarṇagandhapiṣṭī>
gandhakaṃ śodhitaṃ devi tilaparṇīrasena ca |
saptadhā mardayecchoṣyaṃ chāyāyāṃ bhāvayetkramāt || 1,23.132 ||
mūṣāyāṃ pāradaṃ śuddhaṃ palamātraṃ vinikṣipet |
suvarṇaniṣkagulikāṃ mūṣāyāṃ nikṣipettataḥ || 1,23.133 ||
tatra nārīrajomūtramalamātraṃ vinikṣipet |
vālukāyantramadhye ca tāṃ mūṣāṃ sthāpayecchive || 1,23.134 ||
alpamalpaṃ pūrvagandhaṃ vāraṃ vāraṃ vinikṣipet |
mṛdvagninā pacedyāvatkarṣagandhaṃ ca jīryate || 1,23.135 ||
avatārya svāṅgaśītamāharetsvarṇagolakam |
svarṇapiṣṭirbhaveddivyā sarvavāñchitadāyinī || 1,23.136 ||
<tṛtīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
bhāvayenmarkaṭītoyaiḥ śatadhā śuddhagandhakam |
chāyāyāṃ śuddhasūtaṃ ca gharme mṛtkarpare kṣipet || 1,23.137 ||
kiṃcitkiṃcitpūrvagandhaṃ nikṣipenmarkaṭīrasam |
jīrṇe gandhe dravaṃ deyaṃ muhurgandhaṃ muhurdravam || 1,23.138 ||
yāvatpādāṃśakaṃ jīrṇaṃ gandhaṃ ca piṣṭikā bhavet |
divyā sā sarvakarmārhā devānāmapi durlabhā || 1,23.139 ||
<caturthaḥ prakāraḥ>
śuddhagandhaṃ cūrṇayitvā trivāraṃ kāñjikena ca |
jambīrāmlaistrivāraṃ ca haṃsapādīrasena ca || 1,23.140 ||
saptadhā kṣālayedevaṃ karpūraṃ bhāvayetpṛthak |
śvetādrikarṇikātoyairātape ca trivārakam || 1,23.141 ||
dviguñjamātraṃ karpūraṃ yantrāntaḥ parilepayet |
tatra gandhaṃ sārdhaniṣkaṃ bhāvitaṃ cūrṇitaṃ kṣipet || 1,23.142 ||
tadūrdhvaṃ ca palaṃ sūtaṃ nikṣipettasya pṛṣṭhataḥ |
pūrvamātraṃ ca karpūraṃ tadūrdhvaṃ sārdhaniṣkakam || 1,23.143 ||
gandhakaṃ ca punaḥ kṣiptvā tato gomūtrakena ca |
śvetādrikarṇikāmūlaṃ piṣṭvā tatkalkakena ca || 1,23.144 ||
ācchādya taccharāveṇa rodhayetpācayetkramāt |
jālikāyantramadhye ca divyaṃ paścāttamuddharet || 1,23.145 ||
evaṃ kuryāttrivāraṃ ca gandhapiṣṭirbhaveddhruvam |
<pañcamaḥ prakāraḥ>
snehalipte khalvamadhye śuddhasūtaṃ palaṃ nyaset || 1,23.146 ||
karṣaṃ ca śodhitaṃ gandhaṃ devadālīdravaṃ kṣipet |
karāṅgulyā khare gharme mardayetpiṣṭikā bhavet || 1,23.147 ||
<ṣaṣṭhaḥ prakāraḥ>
tāmrakhalve palaṃ sūtaṃ karṣārdhaṃ gandhakaṃ kṣipet |
mṛdvagninā pacedyāmaṃ karāṅguṣṭhena cālayet || 1,23.148 ||
gandhapiṣṭirbhaveddivyā sarvakarmakarī śubhā |
<piṣṭīstambhanam (2)>
piṣṭīnāṃ stambhanaṃ vakṣye tiktakośātakībhavam || 1,23.149 ||
bījaṃ caṇḍālinīkandaṃ tulyaṃ kāntāstanodbhavaiḥ |
kṣīraiḥ piṣṭvā ca tāṃ piṣṭīṃ lepayedaṅgulaṃ dṛḍham || 1,23.150 ||
vandhyākande'thavā kṣīrakande vā sūraṇodbhave |
kande vā vajrakande vā kande vā kuḍuhuñcije || 1,23.151 ||
tāṃ piṣṭiṃ nikṣipettattanmajjayā rodhayenmukham |
tatkandaṃ ca mṛdā liptvā puṭedbhūdharayantrake || 1,23.152 ||
divānaktaṃ karīṣāgnāvūrdhvādhaḥ parivartanam |
yathā kandaṃ tu na dahettathā pākakramaḥ smṛtaḥ || 1,23.153 ||
atha piṣṭiṃ samānīyādbhavetsā stambhitā priye |
<piṣṭījāraṇam (2)>
piṣṭīnāṃ stambhitānāṃ ca jāraṇā vakṣyate śive || 1,23.154 ||
stanayantre lohakṛte gandhakaṃ piṣṭitulyakam |
kṣipedūrdhvaṃ ca deveśi stambhitāṃ gandhapiṣṭikām || 1,23.155 ||
tadūrdhvaṃ piṣṭitulyaṃ ca gandhakaṃ cūrṇitaṃ kṣipet |
nirudhya bhūdhare yantre pācayejjārayetkramāt || 1,23.156 ||
gandhaṃ punaḥpunardeyamevaṃ śataguṇaṃ priye |
jīrṇe śataguṇe gandhe yantrātpiṣṭiṃ samāharet || 1,23.157 ||
sarvāsāṃ gandhapiṣṭīnāṃ jāraṇaṃ syācca rañjanam |
<piṣṭīmāraṇam (2)>
jāritānāṃ ca piṣṭīnāṃ pravakṣye māraṇakramam || 1,23.158 ||
taptakhalve vinikṣipya jāritāṃ gandhapiṣṭikām |
divyauṣadhirasaiḥ piṣṭvā dinaṃ kuryācca golakam || 1,23.159 ||
divyauṣadhīnāṃ bījāni piṣṭvā divyauṣadhodbhavaiḥ |
svarasairvajramūṣāntarlepayetpūrvagolakam || 1,23.160 ||
kṣiptvā nirudhya mūṣāsyaṃ pacedbhūdharayantrake |
pravartayaṃścordhvamadho dinamekaṃ punaḥ priye || 1,23.161 ||
samāhṛtya yathāpūrvaṃ pūrvatoyaiśca mardayet |
puṭayetpūrvavaddevi daśavāramiti kramāt || 1,23.162 ||
asaṃśayaṃ gandhapiṣṭirmriyate sarvakāryakṛt |
gandhapiṣṭikramājjātarasabhasmāni bhairavi || 1,23.163 ||
sarvakarmasu mukhyāni viśeṣādvādakarmaṇi |
<rasabandhāḥ; prathamaḥ prakāraḥ: vaikrāntabandhaḥ>
vakṣyāmi rasabandhāni śṛṇu bhairavi samprati || 1,23.164 ||
śodhitaṃ pāradaṃ khalve daśaniṣkaṃ vinikṣipet |
niṣkaikaṃ śuddhagandhaṃ ca svalpaṃ svalpaṃ vinikṣipet || 1,23.165 ||
kuṭṭayenmardayedgrāvṇā piṣṭiḥ syādyāmamātrake |
kṣīrakande'thavā vandhyākande vā kuḍuhuñcije || 1,23.166 ||
kande vā nikṣipetpakve śubhe gandhakapiṣṭikām |
niṣkārdhaṃ bhasma vaikrāntamūrdhvādho nikṣipetsudhīḥ || 1,23.167 ||
tatkandamajjayā vaktraṃ nirudhya ca mṛdā bahiḥ |
limpedaṅgulimātreṇa śoṣayitvātha sarvataḥ || 1,23.168 ||
aṣṭavāraṃ pacedyantre bhūdhare kaukkuṭe puṭe |
karīṣāgnau punaḥ kuryādūrdhvabhāgamadhaḥ priye || 1,23.169 ||
adhobhāgaṃ tathordhvaṃ ca bhūyo bhūyaḥ pravartanam |
evamekadinaṃ paścātpacedyugakarīṣakaiḥ || 1,23.170 ||
pakvadāḍimabījābho baddho bhavati pāradaḥ |
vaikrāntabaddhanāmā syāccūrṇito yogavāhakaḥ || 1,23.171 ||
<dvitīyaḥ prakāraḥ: gandhakabandhaḥ>
pūrvoktāṃ gandhapiṣṭīṃ tāṃ vastre baddhvātha saṃpuṭe |
lohaje poṭṭalīṃ sthāpyacordhvādhaḥ samagandhakam || 1,23.172 ||
nirudhya saṃpuṭaṃ samyakpacetbhūdharayantrake |
yathā jīrṇo bhavedgandho bhūyo bhūyastathāpi ca || 1,23.173 ||
eva ṣaḍguṇagandhastu jāraṇīyo maheśvari |
evaṃ gandhakabaddho'yaṃ rasaḥ sarvāmayāpahaḥ || 1,23.174 ||
<tṛtīyaḥ prakāraḥ: gandhakabandhaḥ>
ṣoḍaśāṅguladīrghā ca jambīraphalavistṛtā |
pakvamūṣā dṛḍhatarā vālukāyantramadhyataḥ || 1,23.175 ||
tribhāgamagnāṃ kurvīta bahiḥ pādāṃśasaṃsthitām |
tasyāṃ kṣipedgandhasūtaṃ palamekaṃ sureśvari || 1,23.176 ||
sūtādathordhvabhāge tu dvipalaṃ śuddhagandhakam |
nikṣipettanmukhaṃ samyagrodhayenmandavahninā || 1,23.177 ||
pacennirdhūmatā yāvattāvaddhūme gate punaḥ |
kākamācīdravaiḥ pūryā taddrave jīrṇatāṃ gate || 1,23.178 ||
rasena nāgavallyāśca pūraṇīyā punaḥ priye |
unmattakarasaiḥ pūryā śanairmandāgninā pacet || 1,23.179 ||
gandhakaṃ jīryate yāvatkākamācyādikadravaiḥ |
dhattūrāntaiḥ pacedevaṃ baddho bhavati pāradaḥ || 1,23.180 ||
nāmnā gandhakabaddho'yaṃ sarvayogeṣu yojayet |
<caturthaḥ prakāraḥ gandhakasvarṇabandhaḥ>
pūrvavadgandhapiṣṭiṃ ca vidhāyādau vicakṣaṇaḥ || 1,23.181 ||
saptārdhaniṣkasūtaḥ syādadhyardhaṃ śuddhahāṭakam |
yāmamamlena saṃmardyaṃ khyāto'yaṃ hemapiṣṭikā || 1,23.182 ||
gandhapiṣṭiṃ hemapiṣṭyā samayāveṣṭya bāhyataḥ |
stanākāre lohamaye saṃpuṭe vastrabandhitām || 1,23.183 ||
kṛtvā tāṃ piṣṭikāṃ kṣiptvā piṣṭyūrdhvādhaśca gandhakam |
sarvatulyaṃ kṣipetsandhiṃ ruddhvāmlalavaṇaiḥ sudhīḥ || 1,23.184 ||
pacedbhūdharayantre ca jīrṇe jīrṇe punaḥ punaḥ |
ṣaḍguṇaṃ gandhakaṃ dadyāttato vastraṃ śanairharet || 1,23.185 ||
samāṃśaṃ gandhakaṃ bhūyo bhūyo jāryaṃ śanaiḥ śanaiḥ |
niḥśeṣaṃ gandhakaṃ naiva kuryāccetpāradacyutiḥ || 1,23.186 ||
evaṃ śataguṇe jīrṇe gandhapiṣṭiṃ samāharet |
tatsamāṃśasuvarṇasya saṃpuṭe piṣṭikāṃ kṣipet || 1,23.187 ||
kācaṃ kanyādravaiḥ piṣṭvā lepayedbāhyato'ṅgulam |
tataṣṭaṅkaṇakairliptvā paścānmṛllavaṇaiḥ kramāt || 1,23.188 ||
ekaikaṃ lepanaṃ kāryaṃ veṣṭyamaṅgulamānakam |
pratilepaṃ śoṣayecca koṣṭhīyantragataṃ dhamet || 1,23.189 ||
vaṅkanālena tīvreṇa vahninā praharaṃ bhavet |
udayādityasaṅkāśaṃ khoṭaṃ divyarasāyanam || 1,23.190 ||
gandhahāṭakabandho'yaṃ jarāvyādhidaridrahā |
<pañcamaḥ prakāraḥ gandhakabandhaḥ>
athavā gandhapiṣṭiṃ tāṃ vastre baddhvātha gandhakam || 1,23.191 ||
tulyaṃ dattvā nirudhyātha saṃpuṭe lohaje dṛḍham |
puṭettadbhūdhare tāvadyāvajjīryati gandhakam || 1,23.192 ||
evaṃ punaḥ punardeyaṃ yāvadgandhaṃ tu ṣaḍguṇam |
ityevaṃ gandhake baddhaḥ sūtaḥ syātsarvarogajit || 1,23.193 ||
<ṣaṣṭhaḥ prakāraḥ mūlikābandhaḥ>
kārkoṭīmūlajairdrāvaiḥ pāradaṃ mardayeddinam |
markaṭīmūlaje piṇḍe kṣipettaṃ marditaṃ rasam || 1,23.194 ||
taṃ piṇḍaṃ vajramūṣāyāṃ ruddhvā tīvrāgninā pacet |
jāyate khoṭabaddho'yaṃ sarvakāryakaraḥ śubhaḥ || 1,23.195 ||
<saptamaḥ prakāraḥ mūlikābandhaḥ>
arkamūlaṃ ravikṣīraiḥ piṣṭvā mūṣāṃ pralepayet |
tanmadhye rañjitaṃ sūtaṃ kṣiptvā baddhvātha rodhayet || 1,23.196 ||
mṛṇmaye saṃpuṭe taṃ ca nirundhyāllohasaṃpuṭe |
pacedgajapuṭe paścātpārado bandhamāpnuyāt || 1,23.197 ||
<aṣṭamaḥ prakāraḥ mūlikābandhaḥ>
jalakumbhīrasaiḥ sūtaṃ mardayeddivasatrayam |
jalakumbhīdalairmūṣāṃ kṛtvā tatra kṣipedrasam || 1,23.198 ||
ruddhvā tāṃ vajramūṣāyāṃ chāyāśuṣkaṃ puṭellaghu |
chagaṇairekavṛddhyā tu triṃśadvāraṃ puṭaiḥ pacet || 1,23.199 ||
tato gajapuṭe deyaṃ samyagbaddho bhavedrasaḥ |
<navamaḥ prakāraḥ mūlikābandhaḥ>
ekavīrādravairmardyaṃ dinaṃ śuddhaṃ tu sūtakam || 1,23.200 ||
ekavīrākandakalkairvajramūṣāṃ pralepayet |
tasyāṃ pūrvarasaṃ kṣiptvā dhmāte baddho bhavedrasaḥ || 1,23.201 ||
<daśamaḥ prakāraḥ mūlikābandhaḥ>
āraktakṣīrakandotthadravaiḥ strīstanyasaṃyutaiḥ |
tridinaṃ pāradaṃ mardyaṃ vajrakandadravaistryaham || 1,23.202 ||
kṣīrakandasya kalkena vajramūṣāṃ pralepayet |
tatra pūrvarasaṃ baddhvā dhmāte baddho bhavedrasaḥ || 1,23.203 ||
<ekādaśaḥ prakāraḥ mūlikābandhaḥ>
arkamūlaphalaṃ piṣṭvā mūṣāṃ tena pralepayet |
tanmadhye nikṣipenmūṣāṃ puṅkhāmūlavinirmitām || 1,23.204 ||
tanmadhye sūtakaṃ kṣiptvā puṅkhāmūlasamudbhavaiḥ |
pūrayitvā rasairmūṣāṃ nirundhyāttanmukhaṃ dṛḍham || 1,23.205 ||
evamandhīkṛtāṃ mūṣāṃ kṣipetsampuṭamadhyataḥ |
tatastaṃ lepayedyatnāllavaṇena mṛdā tathā || 1,23.206 ||
tato'sau puṭayogena sūto bandhamavāpnuyāt |
rājikādvayamātreṇa citrakadravasaindhavaiḥ || 1,23.207 ||
cūrṇito bhakṣitaḥ prātaḥ sarvarogavināśakaḥ |
<dvādaśaḥ prakāraḥ mūlikābandhaḥ>
kapitthasya śiphānīrairyāmaṃ sūtaṃ vimardayet || 1,23.208 ||
anyasyāmandhamūṣāyāṃ sūtamūṣāṃ nirodhayet |
tathā dhamettato mūṣāṃ yathā sindūravadbhavet || 1,23.209 ||
evaṃ kṛte rasendro'sau badhyate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
mūlikābandhanaṃ hyetadrasendrasya prakīrtitam || 1,23.210 ||
ityete māritāḥ sūtā mūrchitā bandhamāgatāḥ |
pratyekaṃ yojitāstatra prayogairyogavāhinaḥ || 1,23.211 ||
<rasamūrcchanāvidhiḥ; prathamaḥ prakāraḥ>
atha śuddhasya sūtasya mūrcchanāvidhirucyate |
meghanādavacāhiṅgulaśunairmardayedrasam || 1,23.212 ||
dinaṃ piṣṭaṃ tu tadgolaṃ hiṅgunā veṣṭayedbahiḥ |
pacellavaṇayantrasthaṃ dinaikaṃ caṇḍavahninā || 1,23.213 ||
ūrdhvalagnaṃ samādāya dṛḍhaṃ vastreṇa bandhayet |
ūrdhvādho gandhakaṃ tulyaṃ dattvā somānale pacet || 1,23.214 ||
jīrṇe gandhaṃ punardeyaṃ ṣaḍbhirvāraiḥ samaṃ samam |
ṣaḍguṇe gandhake jīrṇe mūrchito rogahā bhavet || 1,23.215 ||
<dvitīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
gandhakaṃ dhūmasāraṃ ca śuddhaṃ sūtaṃ samaṃ samam |
yāmaikaṃ cūrṇayetkhalve kācakupyāṃ niveśayet || 1,23.216 ||
ruddhvā dvādaśayāmāntaṃ vālukāyantrake pacet |
sphoṭayetsvāṃgaśītaṃ tamūrdhvasthaṃ gandhakaṃ tyajet || 1,23.217 ||
adhaḥsthaṃ rasamādāya sarvayogeṣu yojayet |
<tṛtīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
śuddhasūtaṃ tathā gandhaṃ sūtārdhaṃ saindhave kṣipet || 1,23.218 ||
dravaiḥ sitajayantyāśca mardayeddivasatrayam |
kṛtvā golaṃ tu saṃśoṣya mūṣāyāṃ taṃ nirodhayet || 1,23.219 ||
śoṣayitvā dhametkiṃcitsutapte'tha jale kṣipet |
tasmādrasaṃ samuddhṛtya trikandarasabhāvitam || 1,23.220 ||
yojayetsarvarogeṣu dhamedvā bhūdhare pacet |
<caturthaḥ prakāraḥ>
rasārdhaṃ gandhakaṃ mardyaṃ ghṛtairyuktaṃ tu golakam || 1,23.221 ||
kṛtvā tadbandhayedvastre ḍolāyantragataṃ pacet |
gomūtre tadgataṃ yāmaṃ naramūtrairdinatrayam || 1,23.222 ||
śoṣayettatpunarvastrairbaddhvā veṣṭyaṃ mṛdā dṛḍham |
śuṣkaṃ nirudhya mūṣāyāṃ pacedatha tuṣāgninā || 1,23.223 ||
ūrdhvabhāgamadhaḥ kṛtvā tvadhobhāgamathordhvagam |
ityādiparivartena svedayeddivasatrayam || 1,23.224 ||
paścāduddhṛtya taṃ sūtaṃ yogavāhaṃ rujāpaham |
sadyojātasya bālasya viṣṭhāṃ pālāśabījakam || 1,23.225 ||
caṇḍālīrudhiraṃ sūtaṃ sūtapādaṃ ca ṭaṅkaṇam |
jayantyā mardayeddrāvairdinamekaṃ tu golakam || 1,23.226 ||
piṣṭayā sahadevyātha lepayettāmrasampuṭam |
tanmadhye golakaṃ kṣiptvā dviyāmaṃ svedayellaghu || 1,23.227 ||
vālukāyantramadhye tu samuddhṛtya tataḥ punaḥ |
citrakaiḥ sahadevyā ca gandhakaṃ lepayedbahiḥ || 1,23.228 ||
sampuṭaṃ bandhayedvastre mṛdā lepyaṃ ca śoṣayet |
taṃ ruddhvā cānyamūṣāyāṃ dhmāte sampuṭamāharet || 1,23.229 ||
sūkṣmacūrṇaṃ haredrogānyogavāho mahārasaḥ |
sampuṭaṃ sūtatulyaṃ syācchāstradṛṣṭena karmaṇā || 1,23.230 ||
<pañcamaḥ prakāraḥ>
dhuttūrakadravairmardyaṃ dinaṃ gandhāṃśasūtakam |
andhamūṣāgataṃ svedyaṃ bhūdhare mūrchito dināt || 1,23.231 ||
<ṣaṣṭhaḥ prakāraḥ>
kṛtvā ṣaḍaṅgulāṃ mūṣāṃ supakvāṃ mṛṇmayīṃ dṛḍhām |
mūṣāgarbhe vilipyātha mūlairvartulapattrajaiḥ || 1,23.232 ||
tanmadhye sūtakaṃ kṣiptvā mūṣāṃ pūryāttu taddravaiḥ |
ruddhvā tāṃ vālukāyantre cullyāṃ dīpāgninā pacet || 1,23.233 ||
saptāhānte samuddhṛtya yojayettaṃ jarāpaham |
<saptamaḥ prakāraḥ>
kuraṇḍakarasaiḥ sāndramātape mardayedrasam || 1,23.234 ||
latākarañjapatrotthaiḥ pādāṃguṣṭhena mardayet |
dinaikaṃ mūrchitaṃ samyaksarvayogeṣu yojayet || 1,23.235 ||
<aṣṭamaḥ prakāraḥ>
kāsīsaṃ saindhavaṃ sūtaṃ tulyaṃ tulyaṃ vimardayet |
kāsīsasyāpyabhāve tu dātavyā phullatūrikā || 1,23.236 ||
stokaṃ stokaṃ kṣipetkhalve trayamekatra mūrchayet |
pratyekaṃ śataniṣkaṃ syādūnaṃ naivādhikaṃ kvacit || 1,23.237 ||
sthālīsampuṭayantreṇa dinaṃ caṇḍāgninā pacet |
ūrdhvalagnaṃ tataḥ śubhraṃ mūrchitaṃ cāharecchubham || 1,23.238 ||
<navamaḥ prakāraḥ>
atha śuddhasya sūtasya mūrchitasyāparo vidhiḥ |
sūtatulyaṃ mṛtaṃ svarṇaṃ dvābhyāṃ tulyaṃ ca gandhakam || 1,23.239 ||
ravikṣīrairdinaṃ mardyamandhayedbhūdhare puṭe |
dinaikena bhavetsiddho raso hairaṇyagarbhakaḥ || 1,23.240 ||
śubhraḥ śoṇo'thavā kṛṣṇavarṇo gurutaro rasaḥ |
punarutthānavānyastu mūrchitaḥ sa udāhṛtaḥ || 1,23.241 ||
<mūlikābandhāḥ>
śrībhairavī |
kīdṛśī oṣadhī nātha rasamūrcchākarī śubhā |
kena vā bhasma sūtaśca kena vā khoṭabandhanam || 1,23.242 ||
śrībhairavaḥ |
śṛṇu bhairavi tattvena rahasyaṃ rasabandhanam |
brahmaviṣṇusurendrādyairna jñātaṃ suravandite || 1,23.243 ||
<niśācaramūlikākalpaḥ>
gaṅgāyamunayormadhye prayāgo nāma rākṣasaḥ |
tasyāsane varārohe kṣaṇādbadhyeta sūtakaḥ || 1,23.244 ||
niśācarasya patrāṇi gṛhṇīyātsādhakottamaḥ |
tato nipīḍyate devi raso bhavati cottamaḥ || 1,23.245 ||
rasaṃ saṃmardya tenaiva dināni trīṇi vārtikaḥ |
āroṭaṃ bandhayetkṣipraṃ gaganaṃ tatra jārayet || 1,23.246 ||
tena patrarasenaiva sādhayedgandhakaṃ punaḥ |
saptadhā bhāvitaṃ tena tryūṣaṇena sahaikataḥ || 1,23.247 ||
yantre vidyādhare devi gaganaṃ tatra jārayet |
māsamātreṇa deveśi jīryate tu samaṃ samam || 1,23.248 ||
samajīrṇe tu gagane śatavedhī bhavedrasaḥ |
niśācararase devi gandhakaṃ bhāvayettataḥ || 1,23.249 ||
bhāvayetsaptavāraṃ tu dvipadyāśca rasena tu |
tārasya patralepena ardhārdhe kāñcanottamam || 1,23.250 ||
gandhake samajīrṇe'smin śatavedhī bhavedrasaḥ |
niśācararasairbhāvyaṃ saptavāraṃ tu tālakam || 1,23.251 ||
tenaiva ghātayedvaṅgaṃ nāgaṃ tāre tu nirvahet |
tattāraṃ jārayetsūte tatsūtaṃ bandhitaṃ bhavet || 1,23.252 ||
catuḥ ṣaṣṭitame bhāge śulbavedhaṃ tu dāpayet |
tadaṃśaṃ jāyate śreṣṭhaṃ dharmakāmārthamokṣadam || 1,23.253 ||
niśācarasya puṣpāṇi sūkṣmacūrṇāni kārayet |
palāni daśacūrṇasya rasairdhātryāstu bhāvayet || 1,23.254 ||
ghṛtena madhunāloḍyaṃ navabhāṇḍe vinikṣipet |
dhānyarāśau nidhātavyaṃ triḥ saptāhaṃ sureśvari || 1,23.255 ||
tena bhakṣitamātreṇa valīpalitavarjitaḥ |
valkalaṃ sūkṣmacūrṇaṃ tu madhunā sahitaṃ lihet || 1,23.256 ||
ardhamāsaprayogeṇa pratyayogaṃ bhavetpriye |
tasya mūtrapurīṣeṇa śulbaṃ bhavati kāñcanam || 1,23.257 ||
māsamātraprayogeṇa pannagaḥ kāñcanaṃ bhavet |
<mercury:: paṭṭabandha>
grāhyaṃ tatphalatailaṃ tu yantre pātālasaṃjñake || 1,23.258 ||
tena tailena deveśi rasaṃ saṃkocayedbudhaḥ |
tatkṣaṇājjāyate devi pāṭabandho mahārasaḥ || 1,23.259 ||
kaṭakaṃ kaṅkaṇaṃ kāryaṃ rasaliṅgaṃ varānane |
saṃkocamaraṇaṃ tena kartavyaṃ paramādbhutam || 1,23.260 ||
<aṅganāyikākalpaḥ>
punaranyaṃ pravakṣyāmi rasabandhaṃ sudurlabham |
trailokyajananī yā sā oṣadhī aṅganāyikā || 1,23.261 ||
tayā saṃparkamātreṇa baddhastiṣṭhati pāradaḥ |
saptāhaṃ marditasyāsya mahauṣadhyā rasai rasaḥ || 1,23.262 ||
śatāṃśenaiva vedhena kurute divyakāñcanam |
triḥ saptāhaṃ rase tasyā mardanādvaravarṇini || 1,23.263 ||
lakṣavedhī rasaḥ sākṣādaṣṭau lohāni vidhyati |
trisaptāhena deveśi daśalakṣāṇi vidhyati || 1,23.264 ||
caturthe caiva saptāhe koṭivedhī bhavedrasaḥ |
svedatāpanigharṣeṇa mahauṣadhyā rasena tu || 1,23.265 ||
dadāti khecarīṃ siddhimanivāritagocaraḥ |
kāmayetkāminīnāṃ tu sahasraṃ divasāntare || 1,23.266 ||
naṣṭacchāyo hyadṛśyaśca trailokyaṃ ca bhramedasau |
mahauṣadhyā rasenaiva mṛtasaṃjīvanaṃ bhavet || 1,23.267 ||
anena ghātayetsūtaṃ pañcāvasthaṃ kuru priye |
mṛtasya dāpayennasyaṃ hastau pādau tu mardayet || 1,23.268 ||
tasya tu praviśejjīvo mṛtasyāpi varānane |
<narasārakalpaḥ>
punaranyaṃ pravakṣyāmi rasabandhaṃ sudurlabham || 1,23.269 ||
narasārasparśanena kṣaṇādbadhyeta sūtakaḥ |
narasārarasaṃ dattvā dvipadīrajasā rase || 1,23.270 ||
dinānte bandhamāyāti sarvalohāni rañjati |
narasārarasenaiva bhāvayettu manaḥśilām || 1,23.271 ||
nirgandhā jāyate sā tu ghātayettena pannagam |
dvipadīrajasā sārdhaṃ niruddhaḥ pannago bhavet || 1,23.272 ||
narasārarasenaiva jīrṇe ṣaḍguṇapannage |
tāre tāmre'pi vā devi koṭivedhī bhavedrasaḥ || 1,23.273 ||
narasārarase stanye bhāvitaṃ saptadhā pṛthak |
rasasya dāpayedgrāsaṃ yantre vidyādharāhvaye || 1,23.274 ||
jīryate gaganaṃ devi nirmukhe ca varānane |
narasāraraseneśi kīṭanārīrasena ca || 1,23.275 ||
drāvayedgaganaṃ devi tīkṣṇalohaṃ ca pannagam |
narasāraraseneśi hanumatyā rasena ca || 1,23.276 ||
jāyate kāñcanaṃ divyaṃ niṣekādbhāskaraḥ priye |
narasārarase dattvā mañjiṣṭhāṃ raktacandanam || 1,23.277 ||
svarasairmadayantyāśca pannagaṃ devi secayet |
tatkṣaṇātkāñcanaṃ divyaṃ saptavārā niṣecitam || 1,23.278 ||
aṣṭamāṃśayutaṃ hema hemakarmaṇi cauṣadham |
narasārarase bhāvyaṃ saptavāraṃ tu hiṅgulam || 1,23.279 ||
tenaiva ghātayettīkṣṇaṃ bhasmībhūtatvamāpnuyāt |
tadbhasma tāmrapiṣṭe tu triguṇaṃ tena nirvahet || 1,23.280 ||
tacchulbaṃ hemasaṅkāśaṃ tāre vāṣṭāṃśayojitam |
tāraṃ hemasamāṃśaṃ tu dvivarṇaṃ patitaṃ bhavet || 1,23.281 ||
narasārarase bhāvyaṃ rasakaṃ saptavārataḥ |
narasārarasenaiva rasendraṃ saptavārataḥ || 1,23.282 ||
taṃ rasaṃ rasakaṃ caiva tīkṣṇalohaṃ ca pannagam |
narasārarasenaiva tenaikatra vimardayet || 1,23.283 ||
tatkṣaṇājjāyate baddho rasasya rasakasya ca |
tīkṣṇanāgaṃ tathā śulbaṃ rasakena tu rañjayet || 1,23.284 ||
haṭhāttajjāyate hema kūṣmāṇḍakusumaprabham |
<kaṅkālakhecarīkalpaḥ>
punaranyaṃ pravakṣyāmi rasabandhaṃ sudurlabham || 1,23.285 ||
kaṅkālakhecarī nāmnā oṣadhī parameśvari |
tasya tailaṃ tu saṃgrāhyamādyakhecarisaṃyutam || 1,23.286 ||
sthāpayeddinamekaṃ tu pātre bhāskaranirmite |
dvitīye vāsare prāpte vajraratnaṃ tu ghātayet || 1,23.287 ||
anale dhāmayettaṃ tu sutaptaṃ prajvalatprabham |
<mantrasihmāsanīkalpaḥ>
sabījā cauṣadhī grāhyā kācidgulmalatā priye || 1,23.288 ||
mantrasiṃhāsanī nāma tṛtīyā devi khecarī |
pātālayantre tattailaṃ gṛhṇīyāttāmrabhājane || 1,23.289 ||
tasya tailasya madhye tu prakṣipetkhecarīrasam |
medinīyantramadhye tu sthāpayecca varānane || 1,23.290 ||
pūrvauṣadhyāṃ tu taddevi gaganaṃ medinījale |
rase māsaṃ tato dattvā mardanādgolakaṃ kuru || 1,23.291 ||
baddhvā poṭṭalikāṃ tena gaganaṃ jārayetpriye |
same tu gagane jīrṇe baddhastiṣṭhati pāradaḥ || 1,23.292 ||
bhastrāphūtkārayuktena dhāmyamāno na naśyati |
kākaviṣṭhāsamaṃ rūpaṃ samajīrṇasya jāyate || 1,23.293 ||
dviguṇe gagane jīrṇe hyaṣṭalohāni saṃharet |
<irindirīkalpaḥ>
punaranyaṃ pravakṣyāmi rasabandhaṃ sudurlabham || 1,23.294 ||
śivadehātsamutpannā oṣadhī tu irindirī |
jārayedgandhakaṃ sā tu jārayetsāpi tālakam || 1,23.295 ||
kāñcanaṃ jārayetsā tu rasendraṃ sā ca bandhayet |
pravālaṃ drāvayetsā tu drāvayedgaganaṃ tathā || 1,23.296 ||
vajraṃ ca ghātayetsā tu sarvasatvaṃ ca ghātayet |
jārayetsarvalohāni satvānyapi ca jārayet || 1,23.297 ||
irindirīrase nyasya gośṛṅge tu varānane |
dhānyarāśau nidhātavyaṃ drutastiṣṭhati pāradaḥ || 1,23.298 ||
divyauṣadhyā rasenaiva rasendraḥ suravandite |
same tu kanake jīrṇe daśakoṭiṃ tu vedhayet || 1,23.299 ||
pañcame lakṣakoṭiṃ tu ṣaḍguṇe sparśavedhakaḥ |
saptame dhūpavedhī syādaṣṭame tvavalokataḥ || 1,23.300 ||
navame śabdavedhī syādata ūrdhvaṃ na vidyate |
bhramanti paśavo mūḍhāḥ kulauṣadhivivarjitāḥ || 1,23.301 ||
tṛṇauṣadhirasānāṃ ca naiva siddhiḥ prajāyate |
tasmātsarvaprayatnena jñātavyā tu kulauṣadhiḥ || 1,23.302 ||
divyauṣadhyaścatuḥ ṣaṣṭiḥ kulamadhye vyavasthitāḥ |
naiva jānāti mūḍhāstāḥ śivamohena mohitāḥ || 1,23.303 ||
adivyāstu tṛṇauṣadhyo jāyante girigahvare |
tṛṇauṣadhyā rase sūto naiva baddhaḥ kadācana || 1,23.304 ||
akṣayo naiva tiṣṭheta kulauṣadhivivarjitaḥ |
kulauṣadhyā vihīnāstu gaganaṃ cārayanti ye || 1,23.305 ||
sa rasastu varārohe vahnimadhye na tiṣṭhati |
na khoṭaṃ na ca vā bhasma naiva dravyaṃ karoti saḥ || 1,23.306 ||
kiṃciddravyaṃ prakurvanti dhāmyamānaṃ na tiṣṭhati |
patre pāke kaṣe chede naiva tiṣṭhati kāñcanam || 1,23.307 ||
na vedhaṃ ca śatādūrdhvaṃ karoti sa rasaḥ priye |
yāvanna cābdamekaṃ tu vikrāntaṃ trapu tattu kāñcanam || 1,23.308 ||
dharmārthakāmamokṣeṣu naiva dadyāttataḥ priye |
śrībhairavī |
nirjīvatvaṃ gataḥ sūtaḥ kathaṃ jīvaṃ dadāti saḥ || 1,23.309 ||
nirjīvena tu nirjīvaṃ kathaṃ jīvati śaṃkaraḥ |
śrībhairavaḥ |
divyauṣadhyā yadā devi rasendro mardito bhavet || 1,23.310 ||
kālikārahitaḥ sūtastadā bhavati pārvati |
parasya harate kālaṃ kālikārahito rasaḥ || 1,23.311 ||
aṣṭānāṃ caiva lohānāṃ malaṃ śamayati kṣaṇāt |
mahāmūrcchāgataṃ sūtaṃ ko vā vikathayenmṛtam || 1,23.312 ||
divyauṣadhyā rasenaiva jāyate naṣṭacetanaḥ |
pañcabhūtātmakaḥ sūtastiṣṭhatyeva sadāśivaḥ || 1,23.313 ||
<kṣmāpālakalpaḥ>
punaranyaṃ pravakṣyāmi rasabandhanamīśvari |
kṣmāpālena haredvajramanenaiva tu kāñcanam || 1,23.314 ||
vajrabhasma hemabhasma tadvā ekatra bandhayet |
<anye kulamūlikākalpāḥ>
niśācararase jāryaṃ narajīvena jārayet || 1,23.315 ||
taṃ sūtaṃ mārayedbhadre jāgarirdivya oṣadhiḥ |
bhakṣitaḥ sa raso yena so'pi sākṣātsadāśivaḥ || 1,23.316 ||
bhakṣite tolakaikena sparśavedhī bhavennaraḥ |
prasvedāttasya gātrasya aṣṭau lohāni kāñcanam || 1,23.317 ||
lakṣavarṣasahasrāṇi sa jīvetsādhakottamaḥ |
prasvedāttasya gātrasya rasarājaśca badhyate || 1,23.318 ||
ajāpatiḥ kṛṣṇatejāḥ kṣaṇādbadhnāti sūtakam |
jāgarīsparśanāddevi kṣmāpālena ca badhyate || 1,23.319 ||
turuvallyā rasenaiva bhāvitaṃ gaganaṃ priye |
jārayedvālukāyantre khoṭo bhavati tatkṣaṇāt || 1,23.320 ||
drutagolakamāṣaikaṃ māṣaikaṃ hemagolakam |
ekīkṛtya tu saṃmardya luṅgāmlena dinatrayam || 1,23.321 ||
karṣaikaṃ nāgapatrāṇi rasakalkena lepayet |
veṣṭayedvṛścikālīṃ ca tatpiṇḍaṃ lepayettataḥ || 1,23.322 ||
mārayetpannagaṃ devi śakragopanibhaṃ bhavet |
karṣaikaṃ tāraparṇāni mṛtanāgena lepayet || 1,23.323 ||
lepayedvṛścikālīṃ ca tatpatraṃ lepayettataḥ |
tattāraṃ mriyate devi sindūrāruṇasannibham || 1,23.324 ||
sahasrāṃśena tenaiva śulbavedhaṃ pradāpayet |
jāyate kanakaṃ divyaṃ devābharabhūṣaṇam || 1,23.325 ||
kṣīrayuktā bahuphalā granthiyuktā ca pārvati |
nāmnā vartulapattrāṇi śasyate rasabandhane || 1,23.326 ||
ekavāraṃ kandakalke mūkamūṣāgataṃ rasam |
dhamenmukhānilairbaddho bhakṣaṇāya praśasyate || 1,23.327 ||
raktakañcukikandaṃ tu strīstanyena tu peṣitam |
mūṣāyāṃ pūrvayogena kurute sūtabandhanam || 1,23.328 ||
vṛścikāpattrikābījaṃ nārīkṣīrasamanvitam |
dhamayetpūrvavatsūtaṃ bhakṣaṇārthāya vārtikaḥ || 1,23.329 ||
vajrakandaṃ samādāya rasaṃ madhye vinikṣipet |
gajendrākhyaṃ puṭaṃ dadyātsaptadhā bandhatāṃ nayet || 1,23.330 ||
bhakṣayettaṃ rasaṃ prājñaḥ ṣaṇmāsādamaro bhavet |
lāṅgalīkandamādāya karkoṭīkandameva ca || 1,23.331 ||
rasaṃ tanmadhyagaṃ kṛtvā svedayenmardayetpunaḥ |
mriyate nātra sandeho dhmātastīvrānalena tu || 1,23.332 ||
śukacañcugataṃ sūtaṃ puṭayeddhāmayettataḥ |
śatāṃśavedhakartāyaṃ dehasiddhikaro bhavet || 1,23.333 ||
haṃsapādīrasaṃ sūtaṃ śuṣkakandodare kṣipet |
gajendrapuṭanaṃ dadyānmriyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 1,23.334 ||
sahasravedhakartā ca jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
haṃsāṅghriṃ śukacañcuṃ ca gṛhītvā mardayedrasam || 1,23.335 ||
krauñcapādodare kṣiptvā tato dadyātpuṭatrayam |
mriyate nātra sandeho lakṣavedhī mahārasaḥ || 1,23.336 ||
<tṛṇajyotiḥkalpaḥ>
tṛṇajyotiriti khyātāṃ śṛṇu divyauṣadhiṃ priye |
niśā tu prajvalennityaṃ nāhnā jvalati pārvati || 1,23.337 ||
tasya mūle tu saṃkṣipte kṣīraṃ raktaṃ bhavetkṣaṇāt |
tanmūlarasagandhābhrairmātuluṅgāmlapeṣitaiḥ || 1,23.338 ||
śulbapatraṃ viliptaṃ tu bhaveddhema puṭatrayāt |
tanmūlacūrṇasaṃyukto rasarājaḥ sureśvari || 1,23.339 ||
mātuluṅgarase ghṛṣṭamabhrakaṃ carati kṣaṇāt |
<uccāṭākalpaḥ>
athoccāṭāṃ pravakṣyāmi rasabandhakarīṃ priye || 1,23.340 ||
ekameva bhavennālaṃ tasyā romapraveṣṭanam |
tasyāgre ca bhavetpuṣpaṃ śukatuṇḍasya sannibham || 1,23.341 ||
tatpatrāṇi ca deveśi śukapiñchanibhāni ca |
tatkandaṃ kūrmasaṃsthānaṃ kṣīraṃ sindūrasannibham || 1,23.342 ||
jalaṃ sravenmadhūcchiṣṭe tatsamādāya pārvati |
vedhayetsarvalohāni kāñcanāni bhavanti ca || 1,23.343 ||
rasatālakatutthāni mardayeduccaṭārasaiḥ |
ātape mriyate tapto raso divyauṣadhībalāt || 1,23.344 ||
vedhayetsarvalohāni lakṣāṃśena varānane |
āvartitaṃ bhavedyāvajjāyate'rkasamaprabham || 1,23.345 ||
<raktasnuhīkalpaḥ>
rasaṃ raktasnuhīkṣīraṃ kunaṭīṃ gandhakābhrakam |
daradaṃ caiva lohāni sahasrāṃśena vedhayet || 1,23.346 ||
snuhīkṣīraṃ samādāya niśācūrṇena veṣṭayet |
guṭikīkṛtya tenaiva nāgaṃ vidhyati tatkṣaṇāt || 1,23.347 ||
<sthalapadminīkalpaḥ>
athātaḥ sthalapadminyā divyauṣadhyā vidhiṃ śṛṇu |
padminīsadṛśā patraiḥ puṣpairapi ca tādṛśī || 1,23.348 ||
bhaṅge caiva sravetkṣīraṃ raktavarṇā suśobhanā |
ākramya vāmapādena paśyedgaganamaṇḍalam || 1,23.349 ||
paśyecca tārakāyuktaṃ grahanakṣatramaṇḍalam |
lakṣayojanato devi sā jñeyā sthalapadminī || 1,23.350 ||
tasyāḥ pañcāṅgamādāya haragaurīsamanvitam |
manaḥśilātālayuktaṃ mākṣikeṇa samanvitam || 1,23.351 ||
mardayetsaptarātraṃ tu tena śulbaṃ ca vedhayet |
sahasrāṃśena deveśi viddhaṃ bhavati kāñcanam || 1,23.352 ||
tasyāḥ pañcāṅgamādāya pūrvoktavidhinā priye |
cārayetsūtarājaṃ tu mūkamūṣāgataṃ dhamet || 1,23.353 ||
mriyate mūṣikāmadhye saṃkocena na saṃśayaḥ |
tenaiva sarvalohāni sahasrāṃśena vedhayet || 1,23.354 ||
<citrakakalpaḥ>
citrakasya yathā guhyaṃ kathayāmi samāsataḥ |
trividhaścitrako jñeyaḥ kṛṣṇo rakto rasāyanam || 1,23.355 ||
śuklo vyādhipraśamanaḥ śreṣṭhamadhyakanīyasāḥ |
kṛṣṇaṃ raktaṃ sitaṃ vāpi hemante noddharedbudhaḥ || 1,23.356 ||
kṛṣṇacitrakamutpāṭya gobhirnāghrātamīśvari |
kṣīramadhye kṣipetkṣīraṃ kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ bhavetkṣaṇāt || 1,23.357 ||
tasya pañcāṅgacūrṇena pāradaṃ saha mardayet |
dhamecca mūkamūṣāyāṃ khoṭo bhavati tatkṣaṇāt || 1,23.358 ||
raktāṃbaradharo bhūtvā raktamālyānulepanaḥ |
kṛṣṇapakṣe tu pañcamyāṃ raktamālyaudanena tu || 1,23.359 ||
baliṃ dattvā mahādevi raktacitrakamuddharet |
raktacitrakacūrṇena vaṅgaṃ pāyaistribhistribhiḥ || 1,23.360 ||
sarvadoṣavinirmuktaḥ stambhamāyāti tatkṣaṇāt |
tanmūlaṃ sūtakaṃ tāmraṃ kuṅkuṇītailasecanāt || 1,23.361 ||
ekaviṃśativāreṇa śuddhaṃ śulbaṃ bhaviṣyati |
raktacitrakabhallātatailaliptaṃ puṭena tu || 1,23.362 ||
candrārkapatraṃ deveśi jāyate divyakāñcanam |
nāginīkandasūtendraraktacitrasaṃyutam || 1,23.363 ||
patralepapratīvāpaiścandrārkaṃ kāñcanaṃ bhavet |
<jyotiṣmatītailakalpaḥ>
jyotiṣmatītailavidhiṃ vakṣyāmi śṛṇu pārvati || 1,23.364 ||
jyotiṣmatī nāma latā yā ca kāñcanasannibhā |
vallīvitānabahulā hemavarṇaphalā śubhā || 1,23.365 ||
āṣāḍhapūrvapakṣe'syā gṛhītvā bījamuttamam |
tilavatkvāthayitvā vā hastapādairathāpi vā || 1,23.366 ||
tasyāstailaṃ samādāya kumbhe tāmramaye kṣipet |
sthāpayedbhūgataṃ kumbhaṃ kramādūrdhvatuṣāgninā || 1,23.367 ||
ṣaṇmāse tu vyatikrānte sa ghaṭaḥ kāñcanaṃ bhavet |
tāmraṃ hemasamaṃ kṛtvā tailamākṣīkamiśritam || 1,23.368 ||
prativāpena siñcettaddhema tāmrasamaṃ bhavet |
tathā ca śatavedhī syādvidyāratnamanuttamam || 1,23.369 ||
<dagdharuhākalpaḥ>
dagdharuhāṃ pravakṣyāmi rasabandhakarīṃ priye |
sparśauṣadhīti sā jñeyā sarvakāmārthasādhanī || 1,23.370 ||
śaśvacchinnā mahādevi dagdhā sā pāvakena tu |
prarohati kṣaṇāddivyā dagdhā sā tu mahauṣadhī || 1,23.371 ||
raktaṃ pītaṃ sitaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ tasyāḥ puṣpaṃ prajāyate |
caṇakasyeva patrāṇi suprasūtāni lakṣayet || 1,23.372 ||
sā sthitā gomatītīre gaṅgāyāmarbude girau |
ujjayinyā dakṣiṇato vanānteṣu ca dṛśyate || 1,23.373 ||
tasyāḥ kandarasaṃ divye kṛṣṇarājīsamanvitam |
tāmbūlena samaṃ kṛtvā ghuṭikāṃ kārayedbudhaḥ || 1,23.374 ||
sarveṣāmeva lohānāṃ drutānāṃ vahnimadhyataḥ |
sahasraṃ vedhayitvā tu kāñcanaṃ kurute kṣaṇāt || 1,23.375 ||
tathaiva mriyate sūtaḥ kāntahemābhrasaṃyutaḥ |
<kaṭutumbīkalpaḥ>
kaṭutuṃbī tu vikhyātā devi divyauṣadhiṃ śṛṇu || 1,23.376 ||
tasyā bījāni saṃgṛhya sūkṣmacūrṇaṃ tu kārayet |
ekaviṃśativārāṇi bhāvyaṃ dhātrīrasena ca || 1,23.377 ||
payasā saha tenaiva viśvabheṣajasaṃyutam |
bījaṃ yantre vinikṣipya tailaṃ saṃgṛhya piṇḍitaḥ || 1,23.378 ||
rasaṃ mūrchāpayettena cakramardena mardayet |
<lohadaṇḍakalpaḥ>
gopittaṃ śikhipittaṃ ca kāṃkṣīkāsīsasaṃyutam || 1,23.379 ||
tāratulyāni caitāni sarveṣāṃ sūtakaṃ samam |
meṣaśṛṅge nidhātavyaṃ māsamekaṃ nirantaram || 1,23.380 ||
lohadaṇḍena saṃsiktaṃ sarvalohāni vedhayet |
gandhakaṃ lohadaṇḍena ekaviṃśatibhāvitam || 1,23.381 ||
yuktaṃ lohakulenaiva jambīrarasasaṃyutam |
sabījaṃ sūtakopetamandhamūṣāniveśitam || 1,23.382 ||
bhūgataṃ māsamekaṃ tu tāraṃ kāñcanatāṃ nayet |
dalasya bhāgamekaṃ tu tārapañcāṃśameva ca || 1,23.383 ||
śulbaṃ ca pañcabhāgaṃ ca bījasyaikaṃ ca yojayet |
ete dvādaśabhāgāḥ syuḥ sarvaṃ taddhārayetkṣitau || 1,23.384 ||
sthānasyāsya niṣekaṃ tu sudaṇḍena tu kārayet |
pañcaviṃśaddinānte tu jāyate kanakottamam || 1,23.385 ||
<kṣīrakandakalpaḥ>
kṣīrakandavidhiṃ vakṣye sarvasiddhikaraṃ tathā |
caturvarṇaṃ viṣaṃ tatra raktakandaṃ praśasyate || 1,23.386 ||
bhagnametatsravetkṣīraṃ raktavarṇaṃ suśobhanam |
meghānāṃ tu ninādena saṃjātairupaśobhitam || 1,23.387 ||
patraiḥ snuhīsamaiḥ snigdhaiḥ samabhirhemasatprabhaiḥ |
bandhanaṃ rasarājasya sarvasatvavaśaṃkaram || 1,23.388 ||
tasya kṣīraṃ tu saṃgṛhya tāraṃ nirvāhayedbudhaḥ |
dhameddhaṭhāgninā caiva jāyate hema śobhanam || 1,23.389 ||
tintriṇīpatraniryāsamīṣattāmrarajoyutam |
mardayetpāradaṃ prājño rasabandho bhaviṣyati || 1,23.390 ||
toyamadhye vinikṣipya gulikā vajravadbhavet |
<śākavṛkṣakalpaḥ>
śākavṛkṣasya deveśi niṣpīḍya rasamuttamam || 1,23.391 ||
raktacandanasaṃyuktaṃ sarvalohāni jārayet |
milanti sarvalohāni dravanti salilaṃ yathā || 1,23.392 ||
gandhakaṃ rasakaṃ tāpyaṃ pāradaṃ raktacandanam |
rudantyā rasasaṃyuktaṃ tāramāyāti kāñcanam || 1,23.393 ||
śākavṛkṣasya niryāsaṃ yatnataḥ parigālayet |
śigrumūlasya cūrṇaṃ tu tadrasena tu mardayet || 1,23.394 ||
praliptaśulbapatrāṇi puṭetkṣiptvā vipācayet |
taddrutaṃ kāñcanaṃ divyaṃ bhavellakṣaṇasaṃyutam || 1,23.395 ||
phalāni śākavṛkṣasya paripakvāni saṃgṛhet |
tadrasena tu samprājñaḥ saptarātraṃ tu bhāvayet || 1,23.396 ||
tadrasena samāyuktaṃ mañjiṣṭhāmiśritaṃ tathā |
lepayettārapatrāṇi dhmātaṃ bhavati kāñcanam || 1,23.397 ||
<devadālīkalpaḥ>
devadālyā mahauṣadhyā vidhiṃ vakṣyāmyataḥ param |
sā śvetā vyādhiśamane kṛṣṇā pītā rasāyane || 1,23.398 ||
paurṇamāsyāṃ trayodaśyāṃ rāhugraste divākare |
athavā kṛṣṇapañcamyāmimāṃ vidhivaduddharet || 1,23.399 ||
devadālīphalaṃ devi viṣṇukrāntāṃ ca sūtakam |
mūrchayedbandhayetkṣipraṃ śulbaṃ hema karoti ca || 1,23.400 ||
devadālīphalaṃ mūlamīśvarīrasameva ca |
toyena marditaṃ kṛtvā vaṅgaṃ stambhayati kṣaṇāt || 1,23.401 ||
<śvetaguñjākalpaḥ>
athātaḥ sampravakṣyāmi śvetaguñjāvidhiṃ priye |
kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśyāmaṣṭamyāṃ ca surārcite || 1,23.402 ||
kapāle mṛttikāṃ nyasya secayetsalilena tu |
bījāni sitaguñjāyāḥ puṣyayoge tu vāpayet || 1,23.403 ||
vakṣyamāṇena yogena kuryātsaṃgrahaṇaṃ tathā |
oṃ namo bhagavati śvetavalli śvetaparvatavāsini || 1,23.404 ||
sarvakāryāṇi kuru kuru apratihataṃ namo namaḥ svāhā |
śuddhaśulbaṃ tu saṃgṛhya mūṣāmadhye tu saṃsthitam || 1,23.405 ||
tripañcapalasaṃkhyā tu karṣārdhasitaguñjayā |
sahaikatra bhavaṃ tāraṃ tasya gandhavivarjitam || 1,23.406 ||
brahmarītisamāyuktaṃ guñjācūrṇaṃ sahaikataḥ |
devīnāṃ bhūṣaṇaṃ devi jāyate hema śobhanam || 1,23.407 ||
<kartarīrasabandhaḥ>
athātaḥ sampravakṣyāmi kartarīrasabandhanam |
asurāṇāṃ samāyoge krodhāviṣṭena cetasā || 1,23.408 ||
sudarśanaṃ mahācakraṃ preṣitaṃ muravairiṇā |
bhālapaṭṭāttatastasya nipetuḥ svedabindavaḥ || 1,23.409 ||
te bhūmau patitā divyāḥ saṃjātāḥ kartarīrasaḥ |
rakṣārthaṃ sthāpitaṃ tatra viṣṇunā ca sudarśanam || 1,23.410 ||
cakratulyaṃ bhramatyetadāyudhāni nikṛntati |
kurute garjanaṃ nādaṃ dhūmajvālāṃ vimuñcati || 1,23.411 ||
kartarīdṛṣṭimātreṇa tathānyā śabdakartarī |
lokānāṃ tu hitārthāya ghoraśaktirvyavasthitā || 1,23.412 ||
rasarūpā mahāghorā sā siddhānāṃ tu vedinī |
tasya kṣetraṃ yadā gacchedaghorāstraṃ japettadā || 1,23.413 ||
punarghoraṃ nyasettatra athānyaṃ vinyasedbudhaḥ |
anulomavilomena dehe'dhiṣṭhāpya kartarīm || 1,23.414 ||
mudrayā mudrayettāṃ tu aghorāstreṇa yojitām |
dīptena rodhayettāṃ tu stambhayeddīpanena tu || 1,23.415 ||
niṣṭhayā mudrayā tāṃ tu sthānayogena yojayet |
<udakabandhāḥ; candrodakabandhaḥ>
atha candrodakeneśi vakṣyāmi rasabandhanam || 1,23.416 ||
dṛṣṭvā candrodakaṃ mantrī paurṇamāsyāṃ viśeṣataḥ |
grahaṇaṃ tatra kartavyaṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ prayatnataḥ || 1,23.417 ||
nirgacchati mahīṃ bhittvā candravṛddhyā tu vardhate |
kṣetrabandhaṃ purā kṛtvā devamabhyarcya śaṅkaram || 1,23.418 ||
caturdaśyāṃ tu tatkṣetraṃ pūjayitvā vicakṣaṇaḥ |
ahorātroṣito bhūtvā baliṃ tatra nivedayet || 1,23.419 ||
paurṇamāsyāṃ ca rātrau ca gatvā tasya samīpataḥ |
candrodakaṃ tu saṃgṛhya mantrayuktaḥ sumantritam || 1,23.420 ||
āloḍya madhusarpirbhyāṃ pibettattu samāhitaḥ |
pītamātreṇa tenaiva mūrchito bhavati kṣaṇāt || 1,23.421 ||
candrodaye tathottiṣṭhetkṣāraṃ tasya tu dāpayet |
saptarātraprayogeṇa candravannirmalo bhavet || 1,23.422 ||
ekaviṃśatirātreṇa jīvedbrahmadinatrayam |
ekamāsaprayogeṇa brahmāyuḥ sa bhavennaraḥ || 1,23.423 ||
candrodakena gaganaṃ rasaṃ hema ca mardayet |
mūṣāmadhyagataṃ dhmātaṃ tatkṣaṇādghuṭikā bhavet || 1,23.424 ||
ayaṃ tu sparśamātreṇa lohānyaṣṭau ca vedhayet |
tadrasaṃ tu rasaṃ cānyaṃ vajreṇa samajāritam || 1,23.425 ||
catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśato vidhyeddviguṇena sahasrakam |
daśasaṅkalikābaddhaṃ guñjāmātraṃ rasaṃ tataḥ || 1,23.426 ||
trilohaveṣṭitaṃ vaktre dhṛtvā cādṛśyatāṃ vrajet |
oṃ namo rudrāya daṃṣṭrotkaṭāya vighnaṃ nāśaya nāśaya diśo rakṣa rakṣa rudro jñāpayati huṃ phaṭsvāhā |
iti digbandhamantraḥ |
oṃ namo bhagavate rudrāya triśūlahastāya amṛtodbhava rakṣa rakṣa huṃ phaṭsvāhā |
iti pānamantraḥ |
<viṣodakabandhaḥ>
athātaḥ sampravakṣyāmi viṣodarasabandhanam || 1,23.427 ||
sitapītādivarṇāḍhyaṃ tatra devi rasottamam |
tatra gatvācaloddeśe smaredghoraṃ sahasrakam || 1,23.428 ||
keśāḥ kṣiptāḥ sphuṭantyasminnātmacchāyāṃ na dṛśyate |
tailaṃ ca golakākāraṃ ghṛtaṃ tena visarpati || 1,23.429 ||
gandhakasya haredgandhaṃ palalaṃ lavaṇāyate |
jñātvā palāśapatreṇa kaṭukālābuke kṣipet || 1,23.430 ||
viṣodakaṃ gandhakaṃ ca harabījaṃ ca tatsamam |
ajākṣīreṇa piṣṭvā tu śulbapātre tu lepayet || 1,23.431 ||
tatpuṭena bhaveddevi sindūrāruṇasannibham |
śatāṃśenaiva taddevi sarvalohāni vedhayet || 1,23.432 ||
anena vidhinā devi nāgaḥ sindūratāṃ vrajet |
sahasrāṃśena tasyaiva tāraṃ bhavati kāñcanam || 1,23.433 ||
raktaṃ pītaṃ tathā kṛṣṇamuttarottarakāryakṛt |
triphalākāntapātre vā pātre'lābumaye'pi vā || 1,23.434 ||
gṛhītvā pūrvavatpatraiḥ pālāśairveṣṭayedbahiḥ |
sthāpayeddhānyamadhye tu divasānekaviṃśatim || 1,23.435 ||
mahiṣīkṣīramadhye tu bindumekaṃ tu sādhayet |
pāyasaṃ bhakṣayedyastu payomadhvājyasaṃyutam || 1,23.436 ||
yāvatpūrṇaṃ balaṃ devi jīvettadbindusaṃkhyayā |
lāṅgalī gṛhadhūmaśca sindūraṃ rajanīdvayam || 1,23.437 ||
meṣasya śṛṅgaṃ śṛṅgīṃ ca kṛṣṇonmattāśvamārakam |
sabījasūtakaṃ caiva viṣatoyena mardayet || 1,23.438 ||
viṣatoyena medhāvī saptavārāṇi bhāvayet |
athavā bhāvayettaṃ tu yāvaccūrṇaṃ tu mardayettadbhavet || 1,23.439 ||
tena nāgaṃ pratīvāpya ṣoḍaśāṃśena khaṃ bhavet |
mūṣāsthaṃ veṇuyantre ca trīṇi vārāṇi bhāvayet || 1,23.440 ||
dhūmaṃ pariharettasya aṅgavyādhikaraṃ param |
sthāpayennāgasindūraṃ pātre'lābumaye ca tat || 1,23.441 ||
taccūrṇaṃ tu śatāṃśena tāratāmrādi vedhayet |
viṣapānīyamādāya vaṅgamāvartitaṃ śubham || 1,23.442 ||
niṣiktaṃ caiva tattoyaistāraṃ bhavati kāñcanam |
viṣapānīyamādāya prakṣipecca rasottame || 1,23.443 ||
kunaṭīgandhapāṣāṇaviṣaṭaṅkaṇalāṅgalīḥ |
naṣṭapiṣṭaṃ kṛtaṃ khalve tārapatrāṇi lepayet || 1,23.444 ||
andhamūṣāgataṃ dhmātaṃ nirbījaṃ kanakaṃ bhavet |
oṃ hrīṃ nīlakaṇṭhāya ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ |
asya ayutaṃ japet |
iti viṣodakagrahaṇapānamantraḥ |
<sañjīvanījalakalpaḥ>
saṃjīvanījalasyātha vidhiṃ vakṣyāmi pārvati || 1,23.445 ||
śukreṇārādhito devi prāgahaṃ suravandite |
dānavānāṃ hitārthāya mṛtānāṃ devasaṅgare || 1,23.446 ||
mayā saṃjīvanī vidyā dattā codakarūpiṇī |
tayā saṃjīvitā daityā ye mṛtā devasaṅgare || 1,23.447 ||
nikṣiptā martyaloke sā samyakte kathayāmyaham |
asti martyā mahāpuṇyā pavitrā dakṣiṇāpathe || 1,23.448 ||
dakṣiṇe tu taṭe tasyā utpalīnagaraṃ param |
tasya dakṣiṇataḥ śailaḥ sarvalokeṣu viśrutaḥ || 1,23.449 ||
nāmnā kṛṣṇagiriḥ ceti dṛśyate sarvamaṅgale |
suprasiddhāmbikā nāma grāmastasyāsti sannidhau || 1,23.450 ||
tatrāpyudakamālokya parīkṣeta surārcite |
gṛhītvā śuṣkavaṃśaṃ tu kṣipettoyasya madhyataḥ || 1,23.451 ||
jāyate haritaṃ snigdhamahorātreṇa niścitam |
muñcatyaṅkurapatrāni dṛśyate'timanoharam || 1,23.452 ||
balipuṣpopahāreṇa tato devi samarcayet |
kṣetrādhipaṃ gaṇeśaṃ ca candraṃ yogigaṇaṃ tathā || 1,23.453 ||
oṃ candrāya pinākine śūlapāṇaye oṃ diśo bandha bandhaya diśo bandha bandhaya ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ |
tilāṃśca sarṣapāṃścaiva mantreṇānena sarṣapān |
saptābhimantritānkṛtvā sādhako dikṣu nikṣipet || 1,23.454 ||
kaṭukālābuke toyaṃ kṛtarakṣaḥ samāhitaḥ |
gṛhītvā tatprayatnena nijaṃ sthānaṃ samāśrayet || 1,23.455 ||
oṃ namo'mṛte amṛtarūpiṇi amṛtaṃ kuru kuru evaṃ rudra ājñāpayati svāhā |
saptābhimantritaṃ kṛtvā mantreṇānena tajjalam |
dinamekaṃ tathā sūtaṃ svarṇamāṣadvayānvitam || 1,23.456 ||
mardayettena toyena pibettattu vicakṣaṇaḥ |
ekaviṃśatirātraṃ tu kṣīrāhāro'tha yatnataḥ || 1,23.457 ||
jīvetkalpāyutaṃ sāgraṃ kāmarūpo mahābalaḥ |
yojanānāṃ śataṃ gatvā punareva nivartate || 1,23.458 ||
avadhyaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ svecchāhāraḥ sa khecaraḥ |
kanakaṃ pāradaṃ vyoma samamekatra yojayet || 1,23.459 ||
mardayettena toyena saptāhaṃ svedayettataḥ |
sa rasaḥ sarvalohāni ṣaṣṭyaṃśena tu vedhayet || 1,23.460 ||
athavā taṃ rasaṃ divyaṃ madhunā saha bhakṣayet |
māsamātraprayogena jīvedbrahmadināyutam || 1,23.461 ||
tasya mūtramalasvedaiḥ śulbaṃ bhavati kāñcanam |
nirvāte toyamādāya pāradaṃ ca manaḥśilām || 1,23.462 ||
mardayetkhalvapāṣāṇe naṣṭapiṣṭaṃ bhavettataḥ |
svedayetsaptarātraṃ tu trilohena ca veṣṭayet || 1,23.463 ||
antardhānaṃ kṣaṇādgacchedvidyādharapatirbhavet |
siddhakanyāśatavṛto yāvatkalpāṃścaturdaśa || 1,23.464 ||
dineṣu teṣu sarveṣu dadyācchālyodanaṃ ghṛtam |
payasā ca samāyuktaṃ nityamevaṃ ca kārayet || 1,23.465 ||
<uṣṇodakakalpaḥ>
uṣṇodakavidhiṃ vakṣye samāhitamanāḥ śṛṇu |
paśyatyuṣṇodakaṃ yatra vāsaṃ tatraiva kārayet || 1,23.466 ||
śarvarīmuṣitāṃ tatra caṇakāstu dine dine |
bhakṣayenmāsamātraṃ tu jīvedvarṣaśatāṣṭakam || 1,23.467 ||
tasya mūtrapurīṣeṇa śulbaṃ bhavati kāñcanam |
uṣṇodakaṃ ca kāsīsaṃ gandhapāṣāṇasaṃyutam || 1,23.468 ||
caturthāṃśena rasakaṃ daśabhāgaṃ vinikṣipet |
śulbaṃ ca mardayetsarvaṃ naṣṭapiṣṭaṃ kṣaṇena tu || 1,23.469 ||
tena lepitamātreṇa śulvaṃ bhavati kāñcanam |
niṣiktaṃ tena toyena prativāpaṃ dadedbudhaḥ || 1,23.470 ||
śulbaṃ ca jāyate hema taruṇādityavarcasam |
tāraṃ ca tena mārgeṇa niṣiktaṃ hematāṃ vrajet || 1,23.471 ||
uṣṇodakena bhallātaṃ tilamuṣṭiṃ ca bhakṣayet |
māsadvayaprayogeṇa jīvedvarṣaśatatrayam || 1,23.472 ||
rasagandhāśmarasakatutthaṃ daradamākṣikam |
yāmamuṣṇāmbunā ghṛṣṭvā tārapatrāṇi lepayet || 1,23.473 ||
vibhrāmya tu dhameddevi syāccaturdaśavarṇakam |
krameṇānena deveśi śulbaṃ ṣoḍaśavarṇakam || 1,23.474 ||
ekaikaṃ hematārāṃśaṃ dvayaṃ kāṃtābhrayoḥ pṛthak |
uṣṇodakena saṃmardya dhamanātkhoṭatāṃ nayet || 1,23.475 ||
taṃ mukhe dhārayenmāsaṃ vajrakāyo bhavettataḥ |
taccūrṇaṃ yavamātraṃ tu bhakṣayenmadhusarpiṣā || 1,23.476 ||
yāvatpalaṃ tasya malaiḥ śulbaṃ bhavati kāñcanam |
uṣṇodapācitān khādetkulutthānkṣīrapo bhavet || 1,23.477 ||
snānamuṣṇāṃbhasā kuryādvarṣādvarṣācchatāyuṣaḥ |
kṣīramuṣṇodakaṃ kvāthaṃ triphalāyāśca pācayet || 1,23.478 ||
pācayetpāyasaṃ kāntapātre bhuktvā mahāyuṣaḥ |
<śailodakakalpaḥ>
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi śailodakavidhiṃ priye || 1,23.479 ||
kardamāpo mahīśailaśilā ceti caturvidham |
kānicitkṣaṇavedhīni dinavedhīni kāni ca || 1,23.480 ||
pakṣamāsādiṣaṇmāsavedhanāni mahītale |
kṣiptaṃ jale yadā kāṣṭhaṃ śailībhūtaṃ ca dṛśyate || 1,23.481 ||
bahirantaśca deveśi vedhakaṃ tatprakīrtitam |
hiṅgulaṃ haritālaṃ ca gandhakaṃ ca manaḥśilām || 1,23.482 ||
eṣāṃ gandhāpahāraṃ tu kurute tacca vedhakam |
anyathā ceṣṭakaṃ devi tadagrāhyaṃ nirarthakam || 1,23.483 ||
śrīśaile śrīvanaprānte paryaṅkākhye śilātale |
tatrasthaṃ kṣaṇavedhi syānnadyāṃ bhagavatītaṭe || 1,23.484 ||
ekāhe vedhakaṃ tatra gokarṇe tu dinatrayam |
bhadrāṅge dinavedhi syāttristhalānte trivatsaram || 1,23.485 ||
dhāreśvare pākṣikaṃ syātkarṣāpuryāṃ dinaikataḥ |
brahmeśvare māsikaṃ syādvyāghrapuryāṃ tu vāsaram || 1,23.486 ||
aghoreśe māsikaṃ syātsiṃhadvīpe tathā punaḥ |
dinamekaṃ brahmagirau vindhye tu kṣaṇavedhakam || 1,23.487 ||
vāsaraṃ mālyavante tu kṣaṇavedhī tu tatra ca |
kiṣkindhe parvate ramye pampātīre kṣaṇodakam || 1,23.488 ||
tasya paścimato devi yojanadvitaye punaḥ |
bhūśailamasti tatraiva tridinaṃ vedaparvate || 1,23.489 ||
anyatra yatra yatrāpi brahmaviṣṇuśivodbhavam |
amṛtaṃ tatra tatrāpi vajrīkaraṇamuttamam || 1,23.490 ||
tasyotpattiṃ pravakṣyāmi yathā jānāti sādhakaḥ |
mahīṃ samuddhṛtavato varāhasya kalevarāt || 1,23.491 ||
yaḥ svedaḥ patitastasmājjātaṃ śailodakaṃ param |
taṃ mukhe kṣaṇikaṃ jātaṃ karṇadeśe tu vāsaram || 1,23.492 ||
bāhubhyāṃ tryahavedhī syānmāsavedhī tu pārśvayoḥ |
ṣaṇmāsamaparāṅge ca sarvaṃ samaphalaṃ ca tat || 1,23.493 ||
aghorāstreṇa tatkṣetre rakṣāṃ kṛtvā diśāṃ balim |
dattvā lakṣaṃ japitvā tu gṛhṇīyādamṛtaṃ param || 1,23.494 ||
śaradgrīṣmavasanteṣu hemante vā surārcite |
āyase tāmrapātre vā kāntalohamaye'thavā || 1,23.495 ||
śilāmbupalamaṣṭau tu palaṃ kṣīrasya nikṣipet |
kṣīrāvaśeṣaṃ saṃkvāthyaṃ trisaptāhaṃ pibennaraḥ || 1,23.496 ||
jīvedvarṣasahasraṃ tu valīpalitavarjitaḥ |
athavāṣṭapalaṃ kṣīraṃ palaikenāmbumiśritam || 1,23.497 ||
kṣīrāvaśeṣaṃ seveta pūrvoktaṃ labhate phalam |
kulutthāṣṭaguṇaṃ vāri pacedaṣṭāvaśeṣitam || 1,23.498 ||
caturguṇena tenājyaṃ pācayedghṛtaśeṣitam |
lihyānmadhusitopetaṃ trisaptāhādbṛhaspatiḥ || 1,23.499 ||
dviraṣṭavārṣikākāraḥ sahasrāyurna saṃśayaḥ |
avaśiṣṭakulutthaṃ tu pādāṃśamadhusarpiṣā || 1,23.500 ||
bhakṣayetkarṣamekaṃ tu māsenāyutajīvitaḥ |
tatsiddhatailenābhyaṅgaṃ mrakṣaṇaṃ caiva kārayet || 1,23.501 ||
pāmāvicarcikādadrukuṣṭhāni sahasā jayet |
valīpalitanirmuktaḥ sahasrāyuśca jāyate || 1,23.502 ||
yaḥ pibetprātarutthāya śailāmbu culukaṃ payaḥ |
ṣaṇmāsātsyātsahasrāyurnirvalīpalitaśca saḥ || 1,23.503 ||
athavā sūtakaṃ devi vāriṇā saha mardayet |
māsenaikena deveśi naṣṭapiṣṭirbhaviṣyati || 1,23.504 ||
māsamātraṃ samaśnīyātsa bhavedajarāmaraḥ |
athavā taṃ rasaṃ hemnā dhāmayetkhadirāgninā || 1,23.505 ||
gulikā sundarī nāma sarvāyudhanivāriṇī |
kartā hartā svayaṃ siddho jīveccandrārkatārakam || 1,23.506 ||
atha tenodakenaiva kṣīrārdhaṃ pāyasaṃ pacet |
māsamātraprayogeṇa valīpalitavarjitaḥ || 1,23.507 ||
paktvā tenāmbhasā pathyāḥ ṣaṣṭistrīṇi śatāni ca |
madhu saṃyojya bhāṇḍasthaṃ bhūmau sarvaṃ nidhāpayet || 1,23.508 ||
dine dine tadekaikaṃ bhakṣayetprātarutthitaḥ |
valīpalitanirmukto jīvedvarṣasahasrakam || 1,23.509 ||
śailībhūtaṃ kulutthaṃ vā bhakṣayenmadhusarpiṣā |
ṣaṇmāsāttu prayogeṇa jīvedvarṣasahasrakam || 1,23.510 ||
kūṣmāṇḍamāditaḥ kṛtvā yāni kāni phalāni ca |
jale kṣiptāni lohāni śailabhūtāni bhakṣayet || 1,23.511 ||
kṣīrāhāraśca jīrṇānte vajrakāyo bhavennaraḥ |
tenodakena saṃmardyamabhrakaṃ kvāthayetpriye || 1,23.512 ||
kaṭutrayayutaṃ khādejjīvedvarṣasahasrakam |
athavā rasakarṣaikaṃ tajjalena tu marditam || 1,23.513 ||
iṅgudīphalamadhyasthaṃ tacchailodakamadhyagaḥ |
kālena triguṇenaiva kāṭhinyaṃ tasya jāyate || 1,23.514 ||
ṣaṇmāsaṃ tanmukhe dhāryaṃ vajrakāyaṃ karoti tat |
daśanāgasamaprāṇo devaiḥ saha sa modate || 1,23.515 ||
gṛhītvā triphalāṃ tatra śailavāriṇi nikṣipet |
yadā bhavati tacchailaṃ gṛhītvā cūrṇayettataḥ || 1,23.516 ||
kāntajīrṇarasaṃ tena sārdhaṃ ghṛtamadhuplutam |
bhakṣayedvarṣamekaṃ tu tataḥ kṣīrāśano bhavet || 1,23.517 ||
udayādityasaṅkāśo medhāvī priyadarśanaḥ |
nīlakuñcitakeśaśca jīveccandrārkatārakam || 1,23.518 ||
pāradaṃ haritālaṃ ca śilāṃ mākṣikameva ca |
daradaṃ ca viṣaṃ caiva sarvamekatra kārayet || 1,23.519 ||
mardayetkhalvapāṣāṇe mātuluṅgarasena tu |
golakaṃ kārayitvā tu vārimadhye vinikṣipet || 1,23.520 ||
tena tāraṃ ca śulbaṃ ca kāṃcanaṃ bhavati dhruvam |
upayuñjīta māsaikaṃ valīpalitavarjitaḥ || 1,23.521 ||
sahasraṃ jīvitaṃ tasya mahābalaparākramaḥ |
śailībhūtaharidrāṃ tu taccūrṇāvāpamātrataḥ || 1,23.522 ||
hematvaṃ labhate nāgo bālārkasadṛśaprabhaḥ |
śailodake vinikṣipya bhūśaile kardame'pi vā || 1,23.523 ||
jñātvā kālapramāṇena bandhayetpāradaṃ tataḥ |
raktakṣārayutaṃ dhmātaṃ suvarṇasamasāritam || 1,23.524 ||
śatāṃśena tu lohānāṃ sarveṣāṃ hemakārakam |
dvitīyasāraṇāṃ prāpya sahasrāṃśena vidhyati || 1,23.525 ||
taṃ khoṭaṃ dhārayedvaktre divyatvaṃ labhate dhruvam |
<kecana ghuṭikāyogāḥ>
nicule kakubhe caiva kiṃśuke madhuke'pi vā || 1,23.526 ||
iṅgudīphalamadhye vā rajanīdvayamārdrake |
amṛte kandake vātha uktakandauṣadhīṣu ca || 1,23.527 ||
vidhāya koṭaraṃ tatra kṣiptvā tenaiva ḍolayet |
triphalāvyoṣakalkena veṣṭayitvā prayatnataḥ || 1,23.528 ||
pādena kanakaṃ dattvā pāradaṃ tatra yojayet |
śailodake kṣipettatra gulikā vajravadbhavet || 1,23.529 ||
pūrvavatsāraṇā kāryā pūrvavatsiddhidā bhavet |
dhāryamāṇā mukhe sā tu sahasrāyuṣkarī bhavet || 1,23.530 ||
dvitīyasāraṇāyogādayutaṃ vedhayettu sā |
dhāryamāṇā mukhe saivamayutāyuṣyadā bhavet || 1,23.531 ||
tṛtīyasāraṇāyogājjāyate lakṣavedhinī |
taṃ khoṭaṃ dhārayedvaktre lakṣāyurjāyate naraḥ || 1,23.532 ||
caturthī sāraṇā devi koṭivedhī na saṃśayaḥ |
koṭyāyurjīvitaṃ tasya khecaratvaṃ ca labhyate || 1,23.533 ||
pañcabhirdaśakoṭiḥ syātṣaḍbhiḥ koṭiśataṃ bhavet |
yāvaccandrārkajīvitvamanantabalavīryavān || 1,23.534 ||
dadāti saptamī cāpi sāraṇā gulikā parā |
khecarī nāma vikhyātā bhairaveṇa pracoditā || 1,23.535 ||
yastu tadrājikāmātraṃ māsamekaṃ tu bhakṣayet |
vajradehaḥ sa siddhaḥ syāddivyastrījanavallabhaḥ || 1,23.536 ||
krīḍate khecarairbhogaiḥ svecchayā śivatāṃ vrajet |
nānāvidhaphalāścāsyā ghuṭikāṃ śṛṇu sundari || 1,23.537 ||
śuddhabaddhaṃ rasendraṃ tu gandhakaṃ tatra jārayet |
triguṇe gandhake jīrṇe tena hema tu kārayet || 1,23.538 ||
kārayedbhasma sūtaṃ tu kāñcanaṃ tena sūtakam |
tadbhasma sūtake jāryaṃ rasendrasya same samam || 1,23.539 ||
tena sūtakajīrṇena vajraratnaṃ tu ghātayet |
tadvajraṃ jāyate bhasma sindūrāruṇasannibham || 1,23.540 ||
tadbhasma jārayetsūte triguṇe tu surārcite |
hāṭakaṃ sārayettaṃ tu ghuṭikāṃ tena kārayet || 1,23.541 ||
trilohāveṣṭitaṃ taṃ tu mukhe prakṣipya sādhakaḥ |
naṣṭacchāyo bhavetso'yamadṛśyo devadānavaiḥ || 1,23.542 ||
lakṣavarṣasahasrāṇi nirvalīpalito bhavet |
śūlinaṃ śaktisaṃyuktaṃ ratnādiguṇabhūṣitam || 1,23.543 ||
vaktre kare ca bibhṛyātsarvāyudhanivāraṇam |
vyoma mākṣikasatvaṃ ca tāraṃ tāmraṃ surāyudham || 1,23.544 ||
sāralohaṃ sūtakaṃ ca ratnādiguṇabhūṣitam |
ghuṭikā sā varārohe madhuratrayasaṃyutā || 1,23.545 ||
vaktrasthā nāśayetsākṣātpalitaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ |
śivaḥ śaktiśca deveśi ratnāni sitagonasā || 1,23.546 ||
hema tāraṃ tathā bhānuḥ samabhāgāni kārayet |
strīrajo vyāghramadhyasthaṃ padmasūtreṇa veṣṭayet || 1,23.547 ||
sitayena tathā veṣṭyaṃ guhyasthāne niveśayet |
raṇe rājakule dyūte divye kāme jayo bhavet || 1,23.548 ||
vaikrāntābhrakakāntaṃ tu sasyakaṃ tu surāyudham |
vibhītakādisambhūtakāṃjikasya samaṃ bhavet || 1,23.549 ||
samāvartya tataḥ sūte yojayetpādayogataḥ |
kumārīrasasaṃghṛṣṭā kṛtaiṣā ghuṭikā śubhā || 1,23.550 ||
rogamṛtyujarā hanti vaktrasthā nātra saṃśayaḥ |
pañca tāraṃ varārohe sūtakadvayameva ca || 1,23.551 ||
trayo gaganabhāgāḥ syurekaikaṃ hemakāntayoḥ |
ardhaṃ śulbaṃ vibhāgena gulikāmarasundarī || 1,23.552 ||
akṣayo hyajaraścaiva bhavettena mahābalaḥ |
bhasma sūtapalaikaṃ ca mṛtakāntapalaṃ tathā || 1,23.553 ||
mākṣikasya palaṃ caiva śilājatu palaṃ tathā |
palamekaṃ viḍaṅgasya pathyācūrṇapalaṃ tathā || 1,23.554 ||
ekīkṛtya tu tatsarvaṃ madhvājyena tu peṣayet |
gulikāḥ kārayettena ṣaṣṭyādhikaśatatrayam || 1,23.555 ||
ekaikāṃ bhakṣayennityaṃ varṣamekaṃ nirantaram |
jīvedvarṣaśatāyuḥ sa yathā rudro mahābalaḥ || 1,23.556 ||
<kecana rasāyanakalpāḥ>
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi rasabhasmarasāyanam |
vijñeyaṃ niṣparihāraṃ sākṣāddivyauṣadhaṃ param || 1,23.557 ||
āmalakyādi kāntaṃ ca pāradaṃ ca manaḥśilām |
vākucīsamabhāgāni kṣīriṇīrasapeṣitam || 1,23.558 ||
meghanādarasopetaṃ mūkamūṣāgataṃ pacet |
māṣaṃ dvimāṣaṃ triguṇaṃ bhakṣayettatkrameṇa tu || 1,23.559 ||
varṣatrayaṃ paraṃ devi pādaniṣkārdhakaṃ kramāt |
ṣaṭsaptāṣṭau ca varṣāṇi kramānniṣkapramāṇataḥ || 1,23.560 ||
bhuñjīta sa ca divyānnaṃ jarāvairūpyavarjitaḥ |
kiṃcitkāñcanasaṃyuktaṃ niṣkaṃ niṣkārdhameva vā || 1,23.561 ||
yo bhakṣayettribhirvarṣaiḥ sarvavyādhīñjayatyayam |
aṣṭavarṣasahasrāyurdvādaśe lakṣavedhakaḥ || 1,23.562 ||
ṣoḍaśe vatsare devi divyarūpaḥ sa jāyate |
uttamo mūlabandhastu madhyamaṃ sārabandhanam || 1,23.563 ||
adhamaḥ pākabandhastu evaṃ trividhabandhanam |
śatapalamabhayānāmakṣadhātryostathaiva kvathitajalasamāṣṭau bhāgamaṣṭāvaśiṣṭam |
ghṛtamadhusitayāḍhyaṃ vyoṣacitraṃ daśaiva rasapaladaśasiddhaṃ lohajīrṇaṃ mṛtaṃ ca || 1,23.564 ||
giriyutasamamabhraṃ kāntabhṛṅgaṃ viḍaṅgaṃ rasasahitavibhāvyaṃ taṇḍulairbilvamajjaiḥ |
himakarakṛtakalkaṃ lohapātrasthamāsaṃ tridinatanuviśuddhaṃ kalkamenaṃ variṣṭham || 1,23.565 ||
lihati śayanakāle vāmanetrārdhasevī ghananibiḍasamādhirmattamātaṅgadarpaḥ |
vigatasakaladoṣaḥ sarvadṛkdivyacakṣuḥ madana iva sukāntiḥ kāminīnāṃ pravīraḥ || 1,23.566 ||
jalada iva vapuṣmānkuñcitāgrāgrakeśaḥ turaga iva viśuddhaḥ satkaviścitrakārī |
vṛṣabhagativiceṣṭo mandagambhīraghoṣaḥ suragaja iva loke candratārārkajīvī || 1,23.567 ||
kāntahemaravicandramabhrakairgolakaṃ nihitamiṅgudīphale |
śailavārivarisiddhagolakaṃ sundarī hyamarasaṃjñikā śubhā || 1,23.568 ||
kāntahemaravicandramabhrakaṃ vajraratnarasamahirājagolakam |
kṣiptamāmalakakāṣṭhakodare bhūmiśailanihitaṃ samuddhṛtam || 1,23.569 ||
śailatāṃ gatamathāhitaṃ mukhe vajrakāyakarakalpavāsaraiḥ |
tārahemavaraśulbasūtakairgolakaṃ varaṇakāṣṭhayantritam || 1,23.570 ||
śailavārikṛtasundarīrasaiḥ khecarīti gulikā nigadyate || 1,23.571 ||
śailāmbunikṣiptapalāśabījaṃ śailīkṛtaṃ kṣaudraghṛtena khādet |
trisaptarātraṃ dinamekamekaṃ sahasrajīvī vidito naraḥ syāt || 1,23.572 ||
sūtakaṃ cābhrakaṃ caiva vajratīkṣṇasamanvitam |
hāṭakena samāyuktaṃ gulikā khecarī bhavet || 1,23.573 ||
karañjaphalamadhyasthaṃ sūtaṃ tatraiva nikṣipet |
dhṛtaḥ śailāmbumadhyasthaḥ sahasrāyuḥ prayacchati || 1,23.574 ||
tinduke dvisahasrāyurjambīre trisahasrakam |
mātuluṃge ca nāraṅge catuḥpañcasahasrakam || 1,23.575 ||
rambhāphale ṣaṭsahasraṃ panase saptasaṃkhyakam |
vibhītakaphale caiva daśasāhasrasaṃkhyakam || 1,23.576 ||
nālikere mahābhāge sahasrāṇi caturdaśa |
triṃśatsahasraṃ pathyāyāṃ lakṣamāmalake punaḥ || 1,23.577 ||
abhrapatrabhavātkvāthādahorātraṃ śilodake |
badhnāti coddhṛtaṃ sūtaṃ mṛtyudāridryanāśanam || 1,23.578 ||
sāraṇākramayogena vajravajjāyate vapuḥ |
rase rasāyane caiva lakṣavedhī na saṃśayaḥ || 1,23.579 ||
kardamaṃ tu kumāryāśca rasena kṛtapiṇḍikam |
dhamanātpatate sattvaṃ mukhasthaṃ dhārayennaraḥ || 1,23.580 ||
ṣaṇmāsopaprayogeṇa hyajarāmaratāṃ vrajet |
srotoñjanayutaṃ dhmātaṃ sattvaṃ pāradamiśritam || 1,23.581 ||
tatkhoṭaṃ dhārayedvaktre hyadṛśyo bhavati dhruvam |
yasya yo vidhirāmnāta udakasya śivāgame || 1,23.582 ||
śatena bata kālena kuryāddehe rasāyanam |
<baddhajāraṇavidhiḥ>
yā pūrvā nirmitā seyamadhamā bālajāraṇā || 1,23.583 ||
uttamā durlabhā caiva śrūyatāṃ baddhajāraṇā |
abaddhaṃ jārayedyastu kṣīyamāṇaḥ kṣayaṃ vrajet || 1,23.584 ||
baddhasya jīryate grāso jīrṇasya ca mukhaṃ bhavet |
samukho durmukhaṃ datte sāmānyottamalakṣaṇam || 1,23.585 ||
<bandhaprakārāḥ>
sāmānyo'gnisahatvena mahāratnādijārakaḥ |
sāmānyaḥ prathamaḥ kāryaḥ sagrāsastu samantataḥ || 1,23.586 ||
vasudehakaro devi sāmānyo hi bhavedayam |
grāsahīnastu yo baddho divyasiddhikaro bhavet || 1,23.587 ||
uttamo mūlabandhastu madhyamaṃ sārabandhanam |
adhamaḥ pākabandhastu evaṃ trividhabandhanam || 1,23.588 ||
mūlabandhastu yo bandho mūlasaṃkucitaṃ mahat |
sārabandhastu yo bandho vāsanābandha ucyate || 1,23.589 ||
syāccatuḥ ṣaṣṭimūlebhyaḥ kiṃcinmūlena bandhanam |
prāṇyaṅgaṃ daityādīnāṃ mūlāṅgaṃ devatāmayam || 1,23.590 ||
pāṣāṇaṃ caiva siddhānāṃ mānuṣāṇāṃ ca pūjitam |
pīṭhikādrutisaṃkocaistrividhaṃ bandhanaṃ bhavet || 1,23.591 ||
divyābhirauṣadhībhiḥ prāguktaṃ saṃkocabandhanam |
drutibhirbadhyate sūtaḥ kṣaṇabandha udāhṛtaḥ || 1,23.592 ||
abhrakaṃ harabījaṃ ca ṣoḍaśāṃśena kāñcanam |
dhmātaṃ prakāśamūṣāyāṃ śodhayetkācaṭaṅkaṇaiḥ || 1,23.593 ||
dakṣiṇāvartitaṃ dhmātaṃ harabījena melakam |
mūṣāṃ tyaktvā varārohe tiṣṭhate khagabaddhavadrasaḥ || 1,23.594 ||
raktikārdhārdhamātreṇa parvatānapi vedhayet |
bhakṣaṇāttasya deveśi rudratulyo bhavennaraḥ || 1,23.595 ||
krīḍate saptalokeṣu śivatulyaparākramaḥ |
<drutimelanavidhiḥ>
vajrakandaṃ guḍūcī ca uccaṭādisamanvitam || 1,23.596 ||
abhrakaṃ kramate śīghramanyathā nāsti saṅgamaḥ |
kṛṣṇāgarunābhisitaiḥ rasonapitarāmaṭhaiḥ || 1,23.597 ||
nārīkusumapālāśabījatailasamanvitaiḥ |
soṣṇairmilanti mṛditā drutayaḥ sakalā rase || 1,23.598 ||
<vajrabandhaḥ; daśasaṅkalikāvidhisiddhaḥ>
punaranyaṃ pravakṣyāmi vajrabandhaṃ surārcite |
gandhakaṃ bhakṣayennārī dinānāmekaviṃśatim || 1,23.599 ||
tadrajo rasarājasya bandhane jāraṇe hitam |
vajrabhasma tu bhāgaikaṃ bhāgāḥ śuddharasāttrayaḥ || 1,23.600 ||
dvipadīrajasā mardyaṃ yāvattatkalkatāṃ gatam |
pādāṃśena suvarṇena pattralepaṃ tu kārayet || 1,23.601 ||
somavallīrasaṃ kāntaṃ ṭaṅkaṇālaṃ sucūrṇitam |
dadyāttamaṣṭamāṃśena mardayecca prayatnataḥ || 1,23.602 ||
naṣṭapiṣṭaṃ ca śuṣkaṃ tadandhayitvā puṭe tataḥ |
andhamūṣāgataṃ dhmātaṃ krāmaṇena samanvitam || 1,23.603 ||
khoṭastu jāyate devi śatavedhī mahārasaḥ |
sauvīraṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ kācaṃ dattvā dattvā viśodhayet || 1,23.604 ||
akṣīṇo milate hemnā samāvartaśca jāyate |
ekottarakrame vṛddhyā saṃkalaiḥ krāmayettataḥ || 1,23.605 ||
ekaguṇena sūtena ekā saṅkalikocyate |
triguṇena tu sūtena dvitīyā saṃkalocyate || 1,23.606 ||
ṣaḍguṇena tu sūtena tṛtīyā saṅkalī bhavet |
daśaguṇena sūtena caturthī saṅkalī bhavet || 1,23.607 ||
pañcādaśaguṇeneśi pañcamī saṅkalī bhavet |
ekaviṃśadguṇeneśi ṣaṣṭhī saṅkalikā bhavet || 1,23.608 ||
aṣṭāviṃśadguṇeneśi saptamī saṅkalī smṛtā |
ṣaṭtriṃśadguṇite baddhā bhavetsaṅkalikāṣṭamī || 1,23.609 ||
pañcacatvāriṃśaguṇe saṅkalī navamī matā |
pañcādhyadhikapañcāśaddaśasaṅkalikā smṛtā || 1,23.610 ||
evaṃ ca kramavṛddhyā tu saṃkalī daśabandhitā |
prathame daśavedhī ca śatavedhī dvitīyake || 1,23.611 ||
tṛtīye sahasravedhī syāccaturthe'yutavedhikaḥ |
pañcame lakṣavedhī syāddaśalakṣaṃ tu ṣaṣṭhake || 1,23.612 ||
saptame koṭivedhī syāddaśakoṭiṃ tathāṣṭame |
dhūmavedhī tu navame daśame śabdavedhakaḥ || 1,23.613 ||
saṃkalaiḥ sakalairbaddhe vedho daśaguṇo bhavet |
daśasaṃkalikābaddhaḥ śabdavedhī mahārasaḥ || 1,23.614 ||
yathā lohe tathā dehe krāmate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
vedhayettatpramāṇena dhātūṃścaiva śarīrakam || 1,23.615 ||
kārayedghuṭikāṃ divyāṃ badarāsthipramāṇataḥ |
mahākālīṃ pūjayitvā dhārayetsatataṃ budhaḥ || 1,23.616 ||
omaiṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ kāli kāli mahākāli māṃsaśoṇitabhojani |
hrāṃ hrīṃ hraṃ rakṣa kṛṣṇamukhi devi rasasiddhiṃ dadasva me |
śrīṃ hrīmaim || 1,23.617 ||
pūjayitvā tato devīṃ siddhacakraṃ viśeṣataḥ |
tāṃ kṣipeccakramadhye tu ghuṭikāṃ divyarūpiṇīm || 1,23.618 ||
śatavedhena yā baddhā rasena ghuṭikā priye |
māsamekaṃ tu vaktrasthā jīveccaiva yathā vidhiḥ || 1,23.619 ||
tathā sahasravedhena baddhā yā ghuṭikā śubhā |
māsadvayaṃ tu vaktrasthā jīveccandrārkatārakam || 1,23.620 ||
daśasahasravedhena baddhā yā ghuṭikā yadi |
śakratulyaṃ tadāyuṣyaṃ tribhirmāsaistu jāyate || 1,23.621 ||
lakṣavedhena yā baddhā ghuṭikā divyarūpiṇī |
caturmāsaṃ tu vaktrasthā brahmāyuṣyaṃ prayacchati || 1,23.622 ||
daśalakṣeṇa yā baddhā ghuṭikā divyarūpiṇī |
saptamāsaṃ tu vaktrasthā vaiṣṇavaṃ labhate phalam || 1,23.623 ||
koṭivedhena yā baddhā ghuṭikā divyarūpiṇī |
ṣaṇmāsasaṃsthitā vaktre sākṣādvai rudratāṃ nayet || 1,23.624 ||
daśakoṭiprabhedena ghuṭikā divyarūpiṇī |
saptamāsaṃ tu vaktrasthā sa bhavedvyāpako naraḥ || 1,23.625 ||
kartā hartā svayaṃ bhoktā śāpānugrahakārakaḥ |
sarvajñaḥ sarvakartā ca sūkṣmarūpo nirañjanaḥ || 1,23.626 ||
icchayā kurute sṛṣṭimicchayā saṃharejjagat |
svacchandagamano bhūtvā śivarūpo bhavennaraḥ || 1,23.627 ||
pūjyate sarvadevaiśca brahmaviṣṇumaheśvaraiḥ |
<anyo vajrabandhaḥ>
punaranyaṃ pravakṣyāmi vajrabandhaṃ sudurlabham || 1,23.628 ||
samāṃśabhakṣaṇaṃ hema śuddhasūtena kārayet |
mṛtavajraṃ kalāṃśena mardayeddvipadīrasaiḥ || 1,23.629 ||
prāgvaccheṣaṃ kriyājātaṃ pūrvavacca phalaṃ bhavet |
vajrabandho bhavetsiddho devadānavadurjayaḥ || 1,23.630 ||
caturviṃśatisiddhānāṃ nāyakaḥ sarvasiddhimān |
<anyo vajrabandhaḥ>
vajracūrṇasamaṃ sūtaṃ haṃsapādyā vimarditam || 1,23.631 ||
puṭitvā mārayettatra punastulyaṃ rasaṃ kṣipet |
dhmātaḥ khoṭo bhavatyāśu śodhitaḥ kācaṭaṅkaṇaiḥ || 1,23.632 ||
yāvacchukrodayaprakhyo jāyate ca rasaḥ priye |
<anyo vajrabandhaḥ>
vajrasattvaṃ ca gaganaṃ rasaṃ hema ca melayet || 1,23.633 ||
amaratvamavāpnoti vaktrasthena surādhipe |
jārayitvā rasaṃ taddhi punastenaiva jārayet || 1,23.634 ||
koṭivedhī na sandeho vaktrasthaṃ khecaraṃ param |
vedhayettatpramāṇena dhātūṃścaiva śarīrakam || 1,23.635 ||
kārayedghuṭikāṃ divyāṃ vajrasiddhena kāñcane |
anena kramayogena yāvacchakyaṃ tu mārayet || 1,23.636 ||
tadbhasma sūtakaṃ devi sarvaroganibarhaṇam |
hemnā ca sārayitvā tu candrārkaṃ lepayettataḥ || 1,23.637 ||
dvātriṃśāṃśena hemārdhaṃ mātṛkāryadhikaṃ bhavet |
badarāsthipramāṇena kārayedgulikāṃ budhaḥ || 1,23.638 ||
yathā lohe tathā dehe kramate nānyathā kvacit |
<anyo vajrabandhaḥ>
vajreṇa dvaṃdvitaṃ hema kāntaśulbakapālinā || 1,23.639 ||
rañjayetsaptavārāṇi taṃ khoṭaṃ sūkṣmacūrṇitam |
bhāvitaṃ strīrajenaiva bhūrjapatre niveśitam || 1,23.640 ||
drutasūtena saṃyuktaṃ baddhvā vastreṇa poṭṭalīm |
svedayeddevadeveśi yāvadbhavati golakaḥ || 1,23.641 ||
lāṅgalī jīvanī caiva gandhāryuttaravāruṇī |
eteṣāṃ nikṣipetpiṇḍe vajragolaṃ tu veṣṭayet || 1,23.642 ||
mūṣāmadhye vinikṣipya saṃdhayitvā prayatnataḥ |
bhūmau mūṣāṃ vinikṣipya puṭaṃ mūrdhni pradāpayet || 1,23.643 ||
anenaiva pratāpena bandhameti mahārasaḥ |
golakaṃ dhārayedvaktre varṣamekaṃ yadā priye || 1,23.644 ||
jīvetkalpasahasrāṇi yathā nāgo mahābalaḥ |
varṣairdvādaśabhiḥ sākṣājjāyate rasapūruṣaḥ || 1,23.645 ||
gātrasya tasya prasvedādaṣṭau lohāstu kāñcanam |
vajrabhasma tathā sūtaṃ kāñcanena samanvitam || 1,23.646 ||
vaktrasthaṃ kurute yastu abdātpalitavarjitaḥ |
<anye vedhopayogino bandhāḥ>
kṛṣṇābhrakasya satvaṃ ca tāraṃ tāmraṃ ca hāṭakam || 1,23.647 ||
mākṣikaṃ kāntatīkṣṇaṃ ca samabhāgāni kārayet |
andhamūṣāgataṃ dhmātaṃ khoṭaṃ bhavati tatkṣaṇāt || 1,23.648 ||
taṃ khoṭaṃ sūkṣmacūrṇaṃ tu drutasūtena golayet |
mṛtasattvasya bhāgaikaṃ bhāgāścatvāri golakam || 1,23.649 ||
mardayettaptakhalvena bhasmībhavati sūtakaḥ |
mārayedbhūdhare yantre saptasaṅkalikākramāt || 1,23.650 ||
guñjāmātraṃ tu tadbhasma madhvājyena tu lehayet |
saṃvatsaraprayogeṇa hyayutāyurbhavennaraḥ || 1,23.651 ||
valīpalitanirmukto mahābalaparākramaḥ |
tadbhasma palamekaṃ tu palaikaṃ gandhakasya ca || 1,23.652 ||
andhamūṣāgataṃ dhmātaṃ khoṭaṃ bhavati tatkṣaṇāt |
jīvetkalpasahasrāṇi yathā nāgo mahābalaḥ || 1,23.653 ||
tasya mūtrapurīṣeṇa lohānyaṣṭau ca kāñcanam |
tatkhoṭaṃ rañjayetpaścādvaṅgābhrakakapālinā || 1,23.654 ||
rañjayetsaptavārāṇi bhavetkuṅkumasannibhaḥ |
hemnā saha samāvartya sāraṇātrayasāritam || 1,23.655 ||
śatāṃśena tu tenaiva śulbamadhye pradāpayet |
jāyate kanakaṃ divyaṃ devābharaṇabhūṣaṇam || 1,23.656 ||
baddhasūtasya bhāgaikaṃ bhāgaikaṃ kuṭilasya ca |
andhamūṣāgataṃ dhmātaṃ yāvadvaṅgāvaśeṣitam || 1,23.657 ||
anena kramayogena vaṅgaṃ nirvāhya ṣaḍguṇam |
anena kramayogena vahannāgaṃ ca ṣaḍguṇam || 1,23.658 ||
tatastaṃ rañjayetpaścāttīkṣṇaśulbakapālinā |
pakvasūtasya bhāgaikaṃ bhāgaikaṃ drutasūtakam || 1,23.659 ||
mardayenmadhyamāmlena golakaṃ bhavati kṣaṇāt |
mṛtavajrasya bhāgaikaṃ bhāgāścatvāri golakam || 1,23.660 ||
mardayettaptakhalvena bhasmībhavati sūtakaḥ |
mārayedbhūdhare yantre saptasaṃkalikākramāt || 1,23.661 ||
tadbhasma tu punaḥ paścānmadhyamāmlena mardayet |
puṭaṃ dadyātprayatnena ṣaṣṭyādhikaśatatrayam || 1,23.662 ||
tadbhasma jāyate divyaṃ sindūrāruṇasannibham |
tenaiva vedhayettāraṃ sahasrāṃśena kāñcanam || 1,23.663 ||
mṛtavajrasya bhāgaikaṃ bhāgāḥ ṣoḍaśa sūtakam |
devadālīśaṅkhapuṣpīrasena mṛditaṃ kramāt || 1,23.664 ||
mārayedbhūdhare yantre bhasmībhavati tatkṣaṇāt |
pūtkārāṇāṃ sahasreṇa dhāmyamānaṃ na gacchati || 1,23.665 ||
īdṛśaṃ bhasma sūtaṃ ca dehe lohe ca yojayet |
tadbhasma palamekaṃ tu palamekaṃ ca gandhakam || 1,23.666 ||
andhamūṣāgataṃ dhmātaṃ khoṭaṃ bhavati tatkṣaṇāt |
taṃ khoṭaṃ rañjayetpaścātkapālikramayogataḥ || 1,23.667 ||
sāraṇātrayayogena śulbavedhaṃ pradāpayet |
bhasmasūtapalaikaṃ ca palaikaṃ pannagasya ca || 1,23.668 ||
kāntapātreṇa taṃ kṛtvā mardayellohamuṣṭinā |
mṛdvagninā tataḥ pācyaṃ yāvannāgena melakam || 1,23.669 ||
puṭena jāyate bhasma sindūrāruṇasaprabham |
tadbhasma tu punaḥ paścādgopittena tu mardayet || 1,23.670 ||
taccaturdaśavārāṃstu puṭayedbhasmayettataḥ |
tadbhasma bhāgamekaṃ tu bhāgaikaṃ hemagolakam || 1,23.671 ||
ekīkṛtyātha saṃmardya krāmaṇena sahaikataḥ |
tārapiṣṭaṃ tu tenaiva sahasrāṃśena kāñcanam || 1,23.672 ||
anena kramayogena vaṅgabhasma prajāyate |
sahasrāṃśena tenaiva śulbavedhaṃ pradāpayet || 1,23.673 ||
tacchulbaṃ jāyate tāraṃ śaṅkhakundendusannibham |
śvetābhrakaṃ ca sattvaṃ ca kāṅkṣī kāntaṃ tathāyasam || 1,23.674 ||
vaṅgaṃ kāntaṃ tāraṃ tathā śvetaṃ vaikrāntaṃ ca kadambakam |
andhamūṣāgataṃ dhmātaṃ khoṭaṃ kundendusannibham || 1,23.675 ||
taṃ khoṭaṃ sūkṣmacūrṇaṃ tu drutasūtena golakam |
mṛtavajrasya bhāgaikaṃ bhāgāścatvāri golakaḥ || 1,23.676 ||
mardayettaptakhalvena bhasmībhavati sūtakaḥ |
mārayedbhūdhare yantre saptasaṅkalikākramāt || 1,23.677 ||
tataśca jāyate bhasma śaṅkhakundendusannibham |
tadbhasma palamekaṃ tu palamekaṃ tu gandhakam || 1,23.678 ||
andhamūṣāgataṃ dhmātaṃ khoṭaṃ bhavati tadrasaḥ |
tāreṇa ca samāvartya sāraṇātrayasāritam || 1,23.679 ||
sahasrāṃśena tenaiva śulbavedhaṃ pradāpayet |
tīkṣṇacūrṇapalānyaṣṭau palāṣṭau drutasūtakam || 1,23.680 ||
mardayettaptakhalvena ṭeṭañcilvīrasena ca |
tatkṣaṇājjāyate piṇḍaṃ tīkṣṇena saha golakam || 1,23.681 ||
mṛtavajrasya bhāgaikaṃ bhāgāścatvāri golakam |
mardayettaptakhalvena bhasmībhavati tatkṣaṇāt || 1,23.682 ||
mārayedbhūdhare yantre saptasaṅkalikākramāt |
ṣoḍaśāṃśena tenaiva vaṅgavedhaṃ pradāpayet || 1,23.683 ||
tadbhasma palamekaṃ tu palaikaṃ gandhakasya ca |
andhamūṣāgataṃ dhmātaṃ khoṭaṃ bhavati tatkṣaṇāt || 1,23.684 ||
tasya khoṭasya bhāgaṃ ca tīkṣṇacūrṇaṃ ca tatsamam |
dvau bhāgau drutasūtasya sarvamekatra mardayet || 1,23.685 ||
taptakhalve tu saṃmardya golakaṃ bhavati kṣaṇāt |
mārayedbhūdhare yantre saptasaṅkalikākramāt || 1,23.686 ||
tatsarvaṃ jārayedbhasma śaṅkhakundendusannibham |
tadbhasma kārayetkhoṭaṃ tīkṣṇena dvaṃdvitaṃ saha || 1,23.687 ||
anena kramayogena saptasaṅkalikākramāt |
taṃ khoṭaṃ jārayetpaścātsāraṇātrayasāritam || 1,23.688 ||
lakṣāṃśena tu tenaiva vaṅgavedhaṃ pradāpayet |
bhasmasūtapalaikaṃ tu śuddhavaṅgaṃ pradāpayet || 1,23.689 ||
mardayedāyase pātre vaṅgaṃ tu mriyate kṣaṇāt |
dvipalaṃ mṛtavaṅgasya tāracūrṇapaladvayam || 1,23.690 ||
dvipalaṃ gandhakaṃ dadyātpalaikaṃ ṭaṅkaṇasya ca |
andhamūṣāgataṃ dhmātaṃ khoṭaṃ bhavati tatkṣaṇāt || 1,23.691 ||
palaikaṃ khoṭacūrṇasya palaikaṃ drutasūtakam |
mardayenmātuluṅgāmlairgolako bhavati kṣaṇāt || 1,23.692 ||
ṣoḍaśāṃśena tenaiva tārāriṣṭaṃ tu vedhayet |
mṛtavaṅgapalaikaṃ tu palaikaṃ sūtakasya ca || 1,23.693 ||
sarvacūrṇaṃ palaikaṃ tu trayamekatra kārayet |
mardayenmātuluṅgāmlairgolako bhavati kṣaṇāt || 1,23.694 ||
mṛtavajrasya bhāgaikaṃ bhāgaścatvāri golakam |
ekatra mardayedbhadre oṣadhīdravasaṃyutam || 1,23.695 ||
mārayedbhūdhare yantre puṭānāṃ saptakena tu |
tatsarvaṃ jāyate bhasma vajrasyaiva prabhāvataḥ || 1,23.696 ||
sāmudraṃ tripalaṃ devi bhasmamadhye pradāpayet |
ekīkṛtyātha saṃmardya vajrīkṣīre dinatrayam || 1,23.697 ||
viśvāmitrakapālasthaṃ puṭaṃ dadyāttu bhūdhare |
tatsarvaṃ jāyate bhasma śaṅkhakundendusannibham || 1,23.698 ||
tenaiva yavamātreṇa vaṅgaṃ stambhayati kṣaṇāt |
vaṅgaṃ tāraṃ tathā sattvaṃ samasūtena golakam || 1,23.699 ||
anena kramayogena mārayecca pṛthakpṛthak |
tatsarvaṃ jāyate bhasma vajrasyaiva prabhāvataḥ || 1,23.700 ||
bhasmasūtapalaikaṃ ca vaṅgabhasma paladvayam |
dve pale mṛtatārasya sattvabhasma paladvayam || 1,23.701 ||
śaṅkhacūrṇaṃ palaṃ pañca sāmudrasya palāṣṭakam |
ṭaṅkaṇasya palānyaṣṭau sarvamekatra jārayet || 1,23.702 ||
vajrīkṣīreṇa saṃveṣṭya puṭaṃ dadyāccaturdaśa |
eṣa siddharasaḥ sākṣāddurlabhastridaśairapi || 1,23.703 ||
vaṅgaṃ tu dāpayetpaścādbhāṇḍe caiva tu mṛṇmaye |
siddhaṃ bhasma bhavellohaśalākena ca cālayet || 1,23.704 ||
vāpayecca prayatnena yāvatkaṭhinatāṃ vrajet |
andhamūṣāgataṃ dhmātaṃ tāraṃ bhavati śobhanam || 1,23.705 ||
kadācitsphāṭite tāre punarvaṅgaṃ pradāpayet |
patre dāhe kaṣe chede tattāraṃ śāśvataṃ bhavet || 1,23.706 ||
saṃtitaṃ danugolaṃ ca ṣoḍaśāṃśasamanvitam |
mṛtavajrasya bhagaikamekatraiva tu kārayet || 1,23.707 ||
devadālīśaṅkhapuṣpaṃ tadrasena tu mardayet |
mārayedbhūdhare yantre puṭānāṃ saptakena tu || 1,23.708 ||
tadbhasma tu punaḥ paścāddīpayantreṇa pācayet |
bhasmasūtapalaikaṃ ca śvetābhrakadaladvayam || 1,23.709 ||
śaṅkhacūrṇapalaikaṃ tu palaikaṃ ṭaṅkaṇasya tu |
ekīkṛtyātha tatsarvaṃ vajrīkṣīreṇa peṣayet || 1,23.710 ||
araṇyotpalakaiḥ paścātpuṭaṃ dadyāccaturdaśa |
raso'yaṃ yavamātreṇa vaṅgaṃ stambhayati kṣaṇāt || 1,23.711 ||
kṛṣṇābhrakapalaikaṃ tu dvipalenaiva sūtakam |
gandhakasya palaikaṃ ca ekīkṛtyātha mardayet || 1,23.712 ||
mardyamānaṃ prayatnena golakaṃ bhavati kṣaṇāt |
mṛtavajrasya bhāgaikaṃ bhāgāścatvāri golakam || 1,23.713 ||
mardayetpraharaikaṃ tu bhasmībhavati sūtakaḥ |
āraṇyagomayenaiva puṭaṃ dadyāttu bhūdhare || 1,23.714 ||
anena kramayogena saptasaṅkalikāṃ kuru |
śatāṃśena tu tenaiva nāgavedhaṃ pradāpayet || 1,23.715 ||
taṃ khoṭaṃ rañjayetpaścācchulbābhrakakapālinā |
punastāṃ rañjayetpaścāttīkṣṇaśulbakapālinā || 1,23.716 ||
punastaṃ rañjayetpaścānnāgābhrakakapālinā |
hemnā saha samāvartya sāraṇātrayasāritam || 1,23.717 ||
sahasrāṃśena tenaiva śulvavedhaṃ pradāpayet |
jāyate kanakaṃ divyaṃ devābharaṇabhūṣaṇam || 1,23.718 ||
vajravallībhasmasnuhīkṣīre puṭitaṃ daśabhiḥ puṭaiḥ |
tattulyaṃ mardayetsūtaṃ devadālyā rasaiḥ puṭet || 1,23.719 ||
tattulyaṃ puṭayettīkṣṇaṃ triphalāyā rasena tu |
tattulyaṃ puṭayennāgamahimārāṭarūṣakaiḥ || 1,23.720 ||
hemavarṇapradaṃ taddhi viṃśatyaṃśena yojitam |
catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśakenedaṃ śuddhatāraṃ ca rañjayet || 1,23.721 ||
aṣṭamāṃśena tenaiva nāgapatrāṇi lepayet |
puṭayenmārayennāgaṃ sindūrāruṇasannibham || 1,23.722 ||
tattulyaṃ mārayeddhema kāñcanārarase puṭet |
tattulyaṃ mārayecchulbaṃ gṛhakanyārasena ca || 1,23.723 ||
paścādamlena puṭayedyāvatsindūrasannibham |
candrārkau rañjayettena śatāṃśena tu pācitam || 1,23.724 ||
sabījaṃ bījavarjaṃ vā vajreṇa saha sūtakaḥ |
tadbhasma mardayetpaścātsvarṇapatrarasena ca || 1,23.725 ||
tenaiva vedhayecchulbaṃ śulbaṃ tāreṇa yojayet |
<vajrahemadvandvamelāpanam>
vajreṇa dvaṃdvayeddhema hemnā ca dvaṃdvayedrasam || 1,23.726 ||
rasena dvaṃdvayeddehaṃ sa deho hyajarāmaraḥ |
kadalīṭaṅkasauvīraṃ kaṇṭakārīrasaplutam || 1,23.727 ||
krāmaṇaṃ sarvadhātūnāṃ sarvadvandveṣu melanam |
timirasya tu pañcāṅgaṃ peṭārībījasaṃyutam || 1,23.728 ||
ekīkṛtyātha saṃmardya vajraṃ tenaiva veṣṭayet |
vajramūṣāgataṃ dhmātaṃ hemadvandvaṃ tu kārayet || 1,23.729 ||
kāntapāṣāṇacūrṇe tu bhūlatā rāmaṭhaṃ madhu |
guñjāṭaṅkaṇakācaṃ ca karkaṭāsthi snuhīpayaḥ || 1,23.730 ||
tatsarvaṃ tu samaṃ yojyaṃ strīstanyena tu mardayet |
mūṣālepaḥ kṛtaḥ prājño vajramelāpakaḥ sukham || 1,23.731 ||
haṭhāgnau vajramūṣābhirdṛḍhaṃ vajrā milanti ca |
susūkṣmā ravakā bhūtvā hyekībhāvaṃ vrajanti ca || 1,23.732 ||
kācaṭaṅkaṇayogena dhmātaḥ śuddhimavāpnuyāt |
bahubhiścaiva mūṣābhistejaḥpuñjo'pi jāyate || 1,23.733 ||
mṛtavajrasya bhāgaikaṃ bhāgāḥ ṣoḍaśa hāṭakam |
śvetakācasya sūtaṃ tu bhāgaṃ ṣoḍaśa dāpayet || 1,23.734 ||
andhamūṣāgataṃ dhmātaṃ vajradvandvaṃ tu kārayet |
anena kramayogena saptavārāṃśca dāpayet || 1,23.735 ||
milate tatkṣaṇādvajraṃ hemnā tu salilaṃ yathā |
cūrṇe narakapālasya mṛtavajraṃ ca dāpayet || 1,23.736 ||
andhamūṣāgataṃ dhmātaṃ hemnā milati tatkṣaṇāt |
bhṛṅgapatraṃ nṛkeśaṃ ca mukhaṃ kāntasya ṭaṅkaṇam || 1,23.737 ||
bālavatsapurīṣaṃ ca strīstanyena tu peṣayet |
andhamūṣāgataṃ dhmātaṃ vajraṃ milati tatkṣaṇāt || 1,23.738 ||
kṣīreṇotpalasāriṇyā mṛtavajraṃ tu bhāvayet |
hemapiṣṭikayā madhye vajraṃ tatraiva nikṣipet || 1,23.739 ||
veṣṭayedbhūrjapatreṇa bāhye vastreṇa veṣṭayet |
dhānyamadhye tu saṃsthāpya pakṣamekaṃ nirantaram || 1,23.740 ||
uddharettatprayatnena vajrabandhaṃ tu kārayet |
<ratnadrutibandhaḥ>
drutā vajrāstu sūtena melanīyāstu pārvati || 1,23.741 ||
drutibandhaḥ sa vijñeyaḥ śatasāhasravedhakaḥ |
ratnānāṃ drutayaḥ sarvā melayitvā yathāvidhi || 1,23.742 ||
śatasahasravedhī ca dehasiddhipradāyakaḥ |
musalī citrakaṃ vandhyā kukkuṭīkandapadminī || 1,23.743 ||
kañcukī nīlisindūrī pāṭhā nāgabalā yathā |
kaṃsapātre rasaścaiva ratnānāṃ drutayastathā || 1,23.744 ||
ātape dhārayitvā vai adhaḥ karṣānalaṃ yathā |
oṣadhīnāṃ rasaṃ dattvā svacchaṃ kṛtvā punaḥ punaḥ || 1,23.745 ||
yāmamātraṃ tu gharme ca drutirmilati vai rasam |
na teṣāṃ krāmaṇe śaktirvaktuṃ vaktraśatairapi || 1,23.746 ||
saindhavaṃ niṃbapatrāṇi vākucī dveṣiṇījake |
drutābhrasya rasenaiva melanaṃ paramaṃ matam || 1,23.747 ||
vajradvandvānamīśāni vajreṇa rasamāraṇam |
sabījaṃ sāraṇaṃ proktaṃ khoṭabandhanameva ca || 1,23.748 ||
tanmamācakṣva deveśi kimanyacchrotumarhasi || 1,23.749 ||


āk, 1, 24
<mahārasairuparasairlohaiśca rasasya vedhopayogino bandhāḥ>
śrībhairavaḥ |
mahārasairuparasairlohaiśca parameśvari |
ājñāpaya samastaṃ taṃ rasarājasya bandhanam || 1,24.1 ||
<vaikrāntabandhāḥ; prathamaḥ prakāraḥ>
vaikrāntasya tu bhāgaikamaṣṭabhāgaṃ tu sūtakam |
kanakasya tu saptāṃśaṃ dvipadīrasaṭaṅkaṇam || 1,24.2 ||
naṣṭapiṣṭaṃ ca śuṣkaṃ ca dhmātaṃ khoṭo bhavetpriye |
sauvīraṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ kācaṃ dattvā dattvā tu śodhayet || 1,24.3 ||
samahemni samāvartya mūṣāgataṃ tataḥ |
samāṃśabhakṣaṇaṃ tattu śuddhasūtena kārayet || 1,24.4 ||
vaikrāntaṃ ṣoḍaśāṃśena pūrvayogena dhāmayet |
daśasaṅkalikāyogādvedho daśaguṇottaraḥ || 1,24.5 ||
<dvitīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
punaranyaṃ pravakṣyāmi prayogaṃ bhuvi durlabham |
vaikrāntasattvaṃ deveśi pāradena samanvitam || 1,24.6 ||
jāritaṃ samahemnā tu śilābhāṇḍe nidhāpayet |
māsamātroṣitaṃ bhūmau samuddhṛtya prayatnataḥ || 1,24.7 ||
eṣa devi raso divyo dehadravyakaro bhavet |
<tṛtīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
vaikrāntakāstu ye kecittriphalāyā rasena ca || 1,24.8 ||
ekaikaṃ devi saptāhaṃ sveditā marditāstathā |
sudhmātā mūkamūṣāyāṃ khoṭo bhavati cākṣayaḥ || 1,24.9 ||
kāntaṃ rūpyaṃ ca kanakaṃ pāradaṃ caiva yojayet |
śulbaṃ tṛtīyabhāgaṃ tu srotoñjanasamanvitam || 1,24.10 ||
taddhmātaṃ khoṭatāṃ yātaṃ dehalohakaraṃ bhavet |
<caturthaḥ prakāraḥ śvetavaikrāntasiddhaḥ>
śvetavaikrāntacūrṇaṃ tu hayamūtreṇa mardayet || 1,24.11 ||
ādau susvinnamādāya pale palaśataṃ kṣipet |
tārasya jāyate bhasma viśuddhasphaṭikākṛti || 1,24.12 ||
tadbhasma melayetsūte samabhāgaṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ |
cārayedrajataṃ sūte hayamūtreṇa mardayet || 1,24.13 ||
puṭayedandhamūṣāyāṃ krameṇa mṛduvahninā |
ahorātraṃ trirātraṃ vā bhavedagnisaho rasaḥ || 1,24.14 ||
sparśanātsarvalohāni rajataṃ ca kariṣyati |
<pañcamaḥ prakāraḥ raktavaikrāntasiddhaḥ>
raktasya vakṣyate karma jarādāridryanāśanam || 1,24.15 ||
saptadhā bhāvayettasya vyāghrīkandāṃbhasā rajaḥ |
palaṃ tasya palaṃ hemnaḥ palaṃ śuddharasasya ca || 1,24.16 ||
śuddhabhasma bhavetsarvaṃ punarhemnaḥ śataṃ kṣipet |
tadbhasma jāyate sarvaṃ śuddhahemasamaprabham || 1,24.17 ||
tadbhasma rasarāje tu punarhemnā tu melayet |
bhavedagnisaho devi tato rasavaro bhavet || 1,24.18 ||
sarvavedhī bhavetsūtaḥ koṭivedhī mahārasaḥ |
<ṣaṣṭhaḥ prakāraḥ>
raktavaikrāntasattvaṃ tu hemnā tu saha melayet || 1,24.19 ||
samaṃ tu jārayetsūte sārayitvā samena tu |
sahasrāṃśena lohāni vedhayennātra saṃśayaḥ || 1,24.20 ||
<saptamaḥ prakāraḥ kṛṣṇavaikrāntasiddhaḥ>
kṛṣṇavaikrāntabhāgaikaṃ śuddhasūtaṃ śatāṃśakam |
ekatra mardayetkhalve cūrṇaṃ bhavati taddvayam || 1,24.21 ||
asya cūrṇasya bhāgaikaṃ hemabhāgasahasrakam |
ekatra mardayettāvadyāvadbhasma tu jāyate || 1,24.22 ||
dhamettadandhamūṣāyāṃ yāvatkhoṭo bhaviṣyati |
samāṃśabhakṣaṇaṃ tasya pīṭhikāṃ tasya kārayet || 1,24.23 ||
vedhayetsarvalohāni sparśamātreṇa hematām |
taccūrṇamabhrakaṃ caiva rasena saha mardayet || 1,24.24 ||
svedayejjārayeccaiva tato vahnisaho bhavet |
sa rasaścāritaścaiva sarvalohāni vidhyati || 1,24.25 ||
<aṣṭamaḥ prakāraḥ pītavarṇavaikrāntasiddhaḥ>
pītavarṇe'pi vaikrānte raktakṛṣṇavidhiḥ smṛtaḥ |
pītavaikrāntacūrṇaṃ tu hemacūrṇasamanvitam || 1,24.26 ||
pītābhrakasya cūrṇaṃ tu melayitvā mahārasaḥ |
svedito marditaścaiva māsādagnisaho bhavet || 1,24.27 ||
<nīlavaikrāntabhasmarasāyanam>
nīlavarṇaṃ tu vaikrāntaṃ mriyate rasasaṃyutam |
kṛṣṇābhrakeṇa sahitaṃ madhusarpiryutaṃ ca tat || 1,24.28 ||
bhakṣayenmaṇḍalaṃ devi jīveccandrārkatārakam |
yasya yasya hi yo yogastasya tasya prayogataḥ || 1,24.29 ||
melayitvā rasaṃ guñjāmānaṃ trimadhusaṃyutam |
khādetkṣīrājyaśālyannaṃ bhojyamanyacca varjayet || 1,24.30 ||
<navamaḥ prakāraḥ>
vaikrāntasatvasaṃyuktaṃ luṅgāmle mardayedrasam |
tāpayeduṣṇatoyena jalena paripūrayet || 1,24.31 ||
sattvaṃ sūtaṃ ca saṃmiśraṃ dhametsyādrasabandhanam |
baddhaṃ rasaṃ mukhe kṣiptvā bhūmicchidrāṇi paśyati || 1,24.32 ||
<daśamaḥ prakāraḥ>
niṣkamekaṃ rasaṃ krāntamaśvamūtreṇa mardayet |
dinamekamidaṃ devi mardayitvā mṛto bhavet || 1,24.33 ||
caturdinamidaṃ kṛtvā samasūtaṃ samaṃ nayet |
mardanaṃ svedanaṃ caiva pūrvavacchuddhamānasaḥ || 1,24.34 ||
saptadvandvajamekaikaṃ saptame'ṣṭapalaṃ bhavet |
śalyāviśalyāmūlasya vāriṇā mardayeddinam || 1,24.35 ||
bhūdharīyantramadhyasthaṃ puṭaṃ saptadinaṃ dadet |
tadbhasma śuddhaṃ sevyaṃ syādguñjāmātraṃ tu maṇḍalam || 1,24.36 ||
ekādyaṃ pañcamaṃ madhyaṃ punarekaṃ praśasyate |
pūrvavadbandhanaṃ devi koṭivedhī bhavedrasaḥ || 1,24.37 ||
<kāntabandhaḥ>
raktavarṇamayaskāntaṃ lākṣārasasamaprabham |
bhinnastrīraktasaṃkāśaṃ taccūrṇaṃ sūtasaṃyutam || 1,24.38 ||
mardayecchāgaraktena dhmātaṃ khoṭo bhavetpriye |
sa sūtaḥ śatavedhī tu sa tu vyādhiharo bhavet || 1,24.39 ||
gulikāṃ dhārayedvaktre jīvedvarṣasahasrakam |
pītavarṇamayaskāntaṃ bhinnahemasamaprabham || 1,24.40 ||
vedhayetsparśamātreṇa sa tu lohāni sundari |
<capalabandhaḥ; prathamaḥ prakāraḥ>
lāṅgalīṃ karavīraṃ ca citrakaṃ girikarṇikām || 1,24.41 ||
strīstanyaṭaṅkasauvīraṃ mūṣālepaṃ tu kārayet |
capalāddviguṇaṃ sūtaṃ sūtāddviguṇakāñcanam || 1,24.42 ||
naṣṭapiṣṭaṃ ca tatkuryādandhamūṣāgataṃ dhamet |
tatra sthito rasendro'yaṃ khoṭo bhavati śobhanaḥ || 1,24.43 ||
śatāṃśaṃ vedhayennāgaṃ guñjāvarṇastu jāyate |
tena nāgaśatāṃśena śulbaṃ raktanibhaṃ bhavet || 1,24.44 ||
tena śulbaśatāṃśena tāraṃ vidhyati kāñcanam |
<dvitīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
capalasya tu ṣaḍbhāgāstārabhāgāstu sapta ca || 1,24.45 ||
aṣṭau kanakabhāgāstu nava bhāgā rasasya ca |
triṃśadbhāgā militvā tu bhavanti suravandite || 1,24.46 ||
citrakaṃ kaṇavīraṃ ca lāṅgalī gṛdhraviṭtathā |
marditaṃ mātuluṅgāmlairmūṣālepaṃ tu kārayet || 1,24.47 ||
andhayitvā dhameddevi khoṭo bhavati śobhanaḥ |
tena khoṭadaśāṃśena viddho nāgo'ruṇo bhavet || 1,24.48 ||
tena nāgena viddhaṃ tu śulbaṃ guñjāruṇaṃ bhavet |
tena śulbena tāraṃ tu viddhaṃ bhavati kāñcanam || 1,24.49 ||
<tṛtīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
hemābhraṃ capalaṃ devi pāradārdhena saṃyutam |
pāradena kanakaṃ dattvā kunaṭyā mardayetkṣaṇam || 1,24.50 ||
lāṅgalī citrakaṃ caiva strīstanyaṃ kaṇavīrakam |
gṛdhraviṣṭhā tathā sarvaṃ mūṣālepaṃ tu kārayet || 1,24.51 ||
tanmadhye tu sthitaṃ dhmātaṃ khoṭo bhavati śobhanaḥ |
pūrvoktaṃ vedhayedetannirbījaṃ kanakaṃ bhavet || 1,24.52 ||
sutaptalohapātre ca kṣipeccapalacūrṇakam |
sabījaṃ pāradaṃ kṛtvā capalasya tu vāpayet || 1,24.53 ||
lākṣābho badhyate sūto'ṅkuśeneva mahāgajaḥ |
<gandhakabandhaḥ; prathamaḥ prakāraḥ>
śuddhasūtapalaikaṃ ca palaikaṃ gandhakasya ca || 1,24.54 ||
ekīkṛtyātha saṃmardya dhuttūrakarasena ca |
