mārayeccakrayogena bhasmībhavati sūtakaḥ || 1,24.55 ||
andhamūṣāgato dhmātaḥ khoṭo bhavati śobhanaḥ |
sitaṃ hema ca nāgaṃ ca candrārkaṃ cāpi vedhayet || 1,24.56 ||
<dvitīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
palaikaṃ śuddhasūtasya karṣaikaṃ gandhakasya ca |
mardayetsnigdhakhalvena devadālīrasaplutam || 1,24.57 ||
mardayettu karāṅgulyā gandhapiṣṭistu jāyate |
jambīrasya rasenaiva dinamekaṃ tu mardayet || 1,24.58 ||
pañcadrāvakasaṃyuktāṃ vaṭikāṃ kārayecchubhām |
palāśamūlakalkena vaṭikāṃ tāṃ pralepayet || 1,24.59 ||
dhametkhoṭo bhavecchvetakācaṭaṅkaṇayogataḥ |
śodhayettaṃ prayatnena yāvannirmalatāṃ vrajet || 1,24.60 ||
sa khoṭo jāyate devi triguṇaṃ pannagaṃ tataḥ |
śataśo rañjayetpaścācchulbābhrakakapālinā || 1,24.61 ||
śulbe tāre ca khoṭo'yaṃ sahasrāṃśena vedhakaḥ |
<tṛtīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
gandhakena hate sūte mṛtalohāni vāhayet || 1,24.62 ||
punarhema samāvartya samāṃśaṃ bhakṣaṇaṃ kuru |
sāritaḥ śulbatārairghoṣaṃ vidhyati sūtakaḥ || 1,24.63 ||
<caturthaḥ prakāraḥ>
rasaṃ hemasamaṃ kṛtvā piṣṭikārdhena gandhakam |
dvipadīrajasā yuktaṃ mardayeṭṭaṅkaṇānvitam || 1,24.64 ||
naṣṭapiṣṭaṃ ca śuṣkaṃ ca dhmātaḥ khoṭo bhavettataḥ |
candrārkaṣoḍaśāṃśena viddhaṃ bhavati kāñcanam || 1,24.65 ||
hemārdhamilitaṃ taṃ tu mātṛkāsamatāṃ vrajet |
kuryātsaṅkalikāyogādvedho daśaguṇottaraḥ || 1,24.66 ||
yathā hemni tathā tāre vyāṭibījena yojitam |
tṛtīyasaṅkalābaddhaṃ ṣaṭśate nāgavedhakam || 1,24.67 ||
caturguṇena tenaiva sahasrāṃśena kāñcanam |
anena kramayogena sapta saṅkalikā yadi || 1,24.68 ||
kurute kāñcanaṃ divyamaṣṭalohāni pārvati |
taṃ punaścūrṇayitvā tu puṭayedbhasma jāyate || 1,24.69 ||
sarvavyādhijayo devi palaikena subhakṣite |
dvipale brāhmamāyuṣyaṃ tripale vaiṣṇavaṃ bhavet || 1,24.70 ||
catuṣpale ca pañcatvamīśaḥ pañcapale bhavet |
ṣaṭpale bhakṣite devi sadāśivatanurbhavet || 1,24.71 ||
<pañcamaḥ prakāraḥ>
sūtakaṃ gandhakaṃ tāraṃ meṣavallīvasā samā |
tridinaṃ mardayellakṣaṃ vaṅgaṃ pādena lepayet || 1,24.72 ||
andhamūṣāgataṃ dhmātaṃ vaṅgaṃ stambhayati kṣaṇāt |
<ṣaṣṭhaḥ prakāraḥ, gandhapiṣṭividhiḥ>
cūrṇitaṃ gandhakaṃ devi markaṭīrasabhāvitam || 1,24.73 ||
bhāvayecchatavārāṇi jīvabhasma tu gacchati |
dāpayenmṛṇmaye pātre rasena saha saṃyutam || 1,24.74 ||
tāpayedravitāpena markaṭīrasasaṃyutam |
gandhakaṃ grasate sūtaḥ piṣṭikā bhavati kṣaṇāt || 1,24.75 ||
tilaparṇīrasenaiva gandhakaṃ bhāvayettataḥ |
saptavārāṃstu deveśi chāyāśuṣkaṃ punaḥpunaḥ || 1,24.76 ||
śodhitaṃ pātitaṃ sūtaṃ palaikapramitaṃ priye |
mūṣāmadhye vinikṣipya narendrīrasasaṃyutam || 1,24.77 ||
jārayedvālukāyantre bhāvitaṃ gandhakaṃ punaḥ |
truṭi truṭi pradātavyaṃ gandhakaṃ ca punaḥ punaḥ || 1,24.78 ||
anena kramayogena jāyate gandhapiṣṭikā |
gandhapāṣāṇacūrṇaṃ tu kanakasya rasena tu || 1,24.79 ||
bhāvayetsaptavārāṃstu strīrajena tu saptadhā |
śuddhasūtapalaikaṃ ca kharpare dāpayettataḥ || 1,24.80 ||
bhāvitaṃ gandhakaṃ dadyānnarapittena saṃyutam |
dolayedravitāpena piṣṭikā bhavati kṣaṇāt || 1,24.81 ||
gandhakaṃ sūkṣmacūrṇaṃ tu caṇakāmlena pārvati |
bhāvayetsaptavārāṇi strīpuṣpeṇa ca saptadhā || 1,24.82 ||
drutasūtakamadhye tu karpūraṃ gandhakaṃ rasam |
dāpayennikṣipedgoṣṭhe saptāhādgandhapiṣṭikā || 1,24.83 ||
<gandhakapiṣṭīstaṃbhanaṃ vedhaśca>
kaṭukośātakībījaṃ cāṇḍālīkandameva ca |
stanyakṣīreṇa saṃpeṣya piṣṭikāṃ tena lepayet || 1,24.84 ||
puṭayedbhūdhare yantre stambhate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
hemasampuṭamadhye tu samāvartaṃ tu kārayet || 1,24.85 ||
aṣṭamāṃśena tenaiva nāgavedhaṃ pradāpayet |
ṣoḍaśāṃśena tenaiva tāravedhaṃ pradāpayet || 1,24.86 ||
<saptamaḥ prakāraḥ>
gandhapiṣṭipalaikaṃ tu nāgapiṣṭipalāṣṭakam |
lepayennāgapatrāṇi chāyāyāṃ śoṣayettataḥ || 1,24.87 ||
āṭarūṣakapiṇḍena nāgapatrāṇi lepayet |
āraṇyotpalakairdevi dāpayecca puṭatrayam || 1,24.88 ||
tannāgaṃ mriyate divyaṃ sindūrāruṇasaprabham |
tannāgaṃ palamekaṃ tu śulbacūrṇapalāṣṭakam || 1,24.89 ||
vāsakasya rasenaiva praharaikaṃ tu mardayet |
mārayetpātanāyantre śulbaṃ tanmriyate kṣaṇāt || 1,24.90 ||
ṣoḍaśāṃśena tenaiva tāravedhaṃ pradāpayet |
jāyate kanakaṃ divyaṃ devābharaṇabhūṣaṇam || 1,24.91 ||
yā kācidgandhapiṣṭī tu ādau nāgaṃ tu mārayet |
nāgena vedhayecchulbaṃ śulbe tāraṃ tu vedhayet || 1,24.92 ||
kanakaṃ jāyate divyaṃ siddhayoga udāhṛtaḥ |
<aṣṭamaḥ prakāraḥ>
gandhakaṃ madhusaṃyuktaṃ harabījena marditam || 1,24.93 ||
bhūmisthaṃ māsamekaṃ tu tāramāyāti kāñcanam |
udvartanena tenaiva sarvakuṣṭhavināśanam || 1,24.94 ||
ghanena saha saṃyuktaṃ vraṇarogavināśanam |
saṃvatsaraprayogeṇa sahasrāyurbhavennaraḥ || 1,24.95 ||
<tālakabandhaḥ>
śuddhasūtapalaikaṃ tu palaikaṃ tālakasya ca |
ekīkṛtyātha saṃmardya unmattakarasena ca || 1,24.96 ||
mārayeccakrayantreṇa bhasmībhavati sūtakaḥ |
andhamūṣāgataṃ dhmātaṃ khoṭo bhavati śobhanaḥ || 1,24.97 ||
vaṅgaṃ tāraṃ ca śulbaṃ ca kramaśo vedhayedrasaḥ |
śuddhavaṅgapalaikaṃ ca palaikaṃ sūtakasya ca || 1,24.98 ||
dve pale tālakaṃ caitadunmattarasamarditam |
mārayetpātanāyantre dhamanātkhoṭatāṃ nayet || 1,24.99 ||
śvetābhrakasya sattvaṃ ca tāraṃ tīkṣṇaṃ ca mākṣikam |
andhamūṣāgataṃ dhmātaṃ khoṭo bhavati tatkṣaṇāt || 1,24.100 ||
tatkhoṭapalamekaṃ ca palaikaṃ sūtakasya ca |
tālakasya palaṃ sarvamekīkṛtyātha mardayet || 1,24.101 ||
mārayetpātanāyantre dhamanātkhoṭatāṃ nayet |
<anye laghubandhāḥ>
śuddhasūtapalaikaṃ ca palaikaṃ pannagasya ca || 1,24.102 ||
paladvayaṃ kunaṭyāśca sarvamekatra mardayet |
mārayetpātanāyantre dhamanātkhoṭatāṃ nayet || 1,24.103 ||
hemapiṣṭipalaikaṃ ca palaikaṃ gandhakasya tu |
ekīkṛtyātha saṃmardya dhuttūrakarasena ca || 1,24.104 ||
mārayetpātanāyantre dhamanātkhoṭatāṃ nayet |
kṛṣṇābhrakasya satvaṃ ca kāntaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ ca hāṭakam || 1,24.105 ||
śuddhaṃ tāraṃ ca mākṣīkaṃ samabhāgāni kārayet |
andhamūṣāgataṃ dhmātaṃ khoṭo bhavati tatkṣaṇāt || 1,24.106 ||
tatkhoṭapalamekaṃ tu palaikaṃ sūtakasya ca |
paladvayaṃ kunaṭyāśca sarvamekatra mardayet || 1,24.107 ||
mārayetpātanāyantre dhamanātkhoṭatāṃ nayet |
kṛṣṇābhrakapalaikaṃ tu rasakasya palaṃ tathā || 1,24.108 ||
sūtakasya palaikaṃ tu sarvamekīkṛtaṃ priye |
unmattakarasenaiva mardayetpraharadvayam || 1,24.109 ||
mardayeddinamekaṃ vā ṭaṅkaṇena samanvitam |
ghuṭikāṃ kārayetpaścācchāyāśuṣkaṃ tu kārayet || 1,24.110 ||
mahāvartigataṃ dhmātaṃ khoṭaṃ bhavati sūtakaḥ |
taṃ khoṭaṃ śodhayetkācaṭaṅkaṇadravayogataḥ || 1,24.111 ||
hemnā saha samāvartya sāraṇātrayasāritam |
sahasrāṃśena tenaiva śulbavedhaṃ pradāpayet || 1,24.112 ||
anena kramayogena koṭivedhī bhavedrasaḥ |
bījadvayaṃ palāśasya palamekaṃ tu sūtakam || 1,24.113 ||
jambīrāmlena saṃmardya chāyāśuṣkaṃ tu kārayet |
tumbīkā meghanādī ca kākajaṅghā ca bhūlikā || 1,24.114 ||
stanyaṃ ca taiḥ praliptāyāṃ mūṣāyāṃ caiva nikṣipet |
dhamayetkhadirāṅgāraiḥ khoṭo bhavati cākṣayaḥ || 1,24.115 ||
palāśataile saṃmardyaṃ yāvatsyāddravapiṣṭikā |
andhamūṣāgataṃ dhmātaṃ khoṭo bhavati tatkṣaṇāt || 1,24.116 ||
pūrvaśuddhena sūtena samahemnā ca pārvati |
golakaṃ kārayettena mardayitvā dṛḍhaṃ kṛtam || 1,24.117 ||
brahmavṛkṣasya bījāni kārpāsāsthi vibhītakam |
yavaciñcā ca vandhyā ca rājikā ca samanvitam || 1,24.118 ||
sūkṣmapiṇḍīkṛtaṃ sarvaṃ tena liptvā tu golakam |
puṭanaiḥ saptabhirdevi piṣṭikāstambhanaṃ bhavet || 1,24.119 ||
bṛhatpuṭaṃ tato dattvā mūṣāyāṃ tu dhamettataḥ |
samāvartya tu taṃ sūtaṃ samahemnā niyojitam || 1,24.120 ||
śatāṃśena tu candrārkaṃ vedhayetsuravandite |
punastenaiva yogena piṣṭīstaṃbhaṃ tu kārayet || 1,24.121 ||
sārayitvā tato hemnā vedhaścaiva sahasrakaḥ |
evaṃ lakṣāṇi koṭiṃ ca vedhayetkramayogataḥ || 1,24.122 ||
saptasaṃkalikādūrdhvaṃ kṛtvā vaktraṃ tu golakam |
varṣeṇaikena sa bhavedvalīpalitavarjitaḥ || 1,24.123 ||
cāṇḍālīrākṣasīpuṣpairatha madhvājyaṭaṅkaṇaiḥ |
mahārasāṣṭamadhye tamabhrakaṃ vāpi yojayet || 1,24.124 ||
nāgavaṅgasamaṃ sūtaṃ hematāramathāpi vā |
abhrakaṃ drutisattvaṃ vā mardayetpraharadvayam || 1,24.125 ||
chāyāśuṣkaṃ tato golaṃ mūkamūṣāgataṃ priye |
dattvā laghupuṭaṃ dhmātaḥ khoṭo bhavati śobhanaḥ || 1,24.126 ||
raṃbhā vīrā snuhīkṣīraṃ kañcukī yavaciñcikā |
dīnārī caiva gorambhā mīnākṣī kākamācikā || 1,24.127 ||
ebhirmarditasūtasya punarjanma na vidyate |
pūrvavatkramayogena khoṭo bhavati śobhanaḥ || 1,24.128 ||
viṣṇukrāntā ca vakrāṅkābalā ca tulasī tathā |
mahāsomāhivallī ca sūryāvartaṃ ca sundari || 1,24.129 ||
ebhistu marditaḥ sūtaḥ pūrvavatkhoṭatāṃ nayet |
snuhīkṣīraṃ ca kāñjīraṃ bījāni kanakasya ca || 1,24.130 ||
kañcukī lāṅgalī cendravāruṇī viṣamuṣṭikā |
pālāśamūlatoyaṃ ca mardayettena sūtakam || 1,24.131 ||
samahemni samaṃ sūtaṃ piṣṭikāṃ kārayedbudhaḥ |
mahārasānpiṣṭikārdhaṃ mardayedoṣadhīrasaiḥ || 1,24.132 ||
yāmatrayaṃ mardayitvā golakaṃ kārayedbudhaḥ |
piṣṭikāṃ bandhayitvā tu gandhataile vipācayet || 1,24.133 ||
andhamūṣāgataṃ golaṃ chāyāśuṣkaṃ tu kārayet |
tato laghupuṭaṃ dattvā khadirāgnau tu dhāmayet || 1,24.134 ||
khoṭavajjāyate devi saha hemnā tu dhāmayet |
akṣīṇo milate hemni samāvartaśca jāyate || 1,24.135 ||
tatkṣaṇādeva sūtasya divyadeho bhavennaraḥ |
vedhayetsarvalohāni rañjitaḥ krāmito rasaḥ || 1,24.136 ||
samāṃśabhakṣaṇe sūte mardayedoṣadhīrasaiḥ |
naṣṭapiṣṭaṃ tu taṃ kṛtvā pūrvayogena dhāmayet || 1,24.137 ||
khoṭastu jāyate divyaḥ ṣoḍaśāṃśena vedhakaḥ |
baddhaḥ saṅkalikāyogādvidhyeddaśaguṇottaram || 1,24.138 ||
athavā cūrṇabaddhastu vidhyeddaśaguṇottaram |
mṛgadūrvā candravallī pakvabimbā tathaiva ca || 1,24.139 ||
kāṅkṣīlā karavīraṃ ca bījaṃ conmattakasya ca |
kākāṇḍīpalasaṃyuktaṃ mardayetsurasundari || 1,24.140 ||
samahemni samaṃ sūtaṃ piṣṭikāṃ kārayedbudhaḥ |
athavā sārayitvā tu samena samasūtakam || 1,24.141 ||
mahārasapiṣṭikāṃ ca mardayedoṣadhīrasaiḥ |
yāmatrayaṃ mardayitvā golakaṃ kārayedbudhaḥ || 1,24.142 ||
andhamūṣāgataṃ golaṃ chāyāśuṣkaṃ tu kārayet |
tato laghupuṭaṃ dattvā dhmātaḥ khoṭo bhavetpriye || 1,24.143 ||
mṛgadūrvottamāsomārasaiḥ sūtakasāraṇam |
mūlaistrayāṇāṃ lāṅgalyā rāmaṭhena ca hanyate || 1,24.144 ||
samena hemnā saṃyuktaṃ piṣṭikāṃ kārayedbudhaḥ |
athavā tārapiṣṭiṃ ca samasūtena kārayet || 1,24.145 ||
pūrvavatkramayogena khoṭo bhavati śobhanaḥ |
andhamūṣāgataṃ bhūmau svedayetkarṣakāgninā || 1,24.146 ||
ahorātraṃ trirātraṃ vā cūrṇabandhaṃ bhavettataḥ |
taccūrṇabandhaṃ kurute vedhaṃ daśaguṇottaram || 1,24.147 ||
śūlinīrasasūtaṃ ca srotoñjanasamanvitam |
pūrvavatpiṣṭikāyogātkhoṭo bhavati śobhanaḥ || 1,24.148 ||
srotoñjanaṃ satagaraṃ sṛṣṭitrayayutaṃ rasam |
bhavedvahnisahaḥ kṣipraṃ sūtakaḥ sarvakarmakṛt || 1,24.149 ||
hemābhraṃ caiva tārābhraṃ śulbābhraṃ cābhratīkṣṇakam |
vaṅgābhraṃ caiva nāgābhraṃ saha sūtena bandhayet || 1,24.150 ||
yathālābhauṣadhīghṛṣṭaṃ mahārasasamanvitam |
hemnā tāreṇa śulvena tīkṣṇavaṅgoragaistathā || 1,24.151 ||
ebhirvyastaiḥ samastairvā piṣṭiṃ kṛtvā same samām |
mārayetpūrvavidhinā garbhayantre tuṣāgninā || 1,24.152 ||
samāṃśaṃ sūtakaṃ dattvā vāraṃ vāraṃ punaḥ punaḥ |
jāyate vividhaḥ khoṭaḥ kāntabaddho mahārasaḥ || 1,24.153 ||
bhavetsaṅkalikāyogādvedho daśaguṇottaraḥ |
<nigalabandhaḥ>
khoṭaḥ poṭastathā bhasma dhūlīkalpaśca pañcamaḥ || 1,24.154 ||
ete nigalayogābhyāṃ sarvabandhaphalodayaḥ |
snuhyarkasaṃbhavaṃ kṣīraṃ bandhabījāni gugguluḥ || 1,24.155 ||
saindhavaṃ dviguṇaṃ dattvā mardayeta vicakṣaṇaḥ |
piṣṭikābandhanaṃ kṛtvā kalkenānena sundari || 1,24.156 ||
bilvapramāṇaṃ kṛtvā tu mūṣāmatidṛḍhāṃ śubhām |
ūrdhvādho lavaṇaṃ dattvā mūṣāmadhye rasaṃ kṣipet || 1,24.157 ||
mūṣāmadhye pradātavyaṃ dagdhaśaṅkhādicūrṇakam |
mukhaṃ tasya dṛḍhaṃ baddhvā loṇamṛttikayā budhaḥ || 1,24.158 ||
kārayetsandhiloṇaṃ ca chāyāśuṣkaṃ tu kārayet |
ukto nigalabandho'yaṃ putrasyāpi na kathyate || 1,24.159 ||
tuṣakarṣāgninā bhūmau mṛdusvedaṃ tu kārayet |
ahorātraṃ trirātraṃ vā puṭaṃ dattvā prayatnataḥ || 1,24.160 ||
sudhmātaṃ khadirāṅgārai rasendraṃ khoṭatāṃ nayet |
sauvīraṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ kācaṃ dattvā dattvā tu śodhayet || 1,24.161 ||
akṣīṇo milate hemni samāvartaśca jāyate |
samāṃśabhakṣaṇaṃ tattu śuddhasūtena kārayet || 1,24.162 ||
dhamayetpūrvayogena rasendraṃ khoṭatāṃ nayet |
bhavetsaṅkalikāyogādvedho daśaguṇottaraḥ || 1,24.163 ||
palāśabījaniryāsaṃ kokilonmattavāruṇī |
śūlinīrasasaṃyuktaṃ peṣayetsaindhavānvitam || 1,24.164 ||
piṣṭikāveṣṭanaṃ kṛtvā nigalena tu bandhayet |
abhrakasya tu patreṇa vajrārkakṣīrasindhunā || 1,24.165 ||
tāpyena lohakiṭṭena sikatāmṛṇmayena ca |
ebhistu nigale baddhaḥ pāradīyo mahārasaḥ || 1,24.166 ||
nātikrāmati maryādā velāmiva mahodadhiḥ |
tailārkakṣīrakatakalāṅgalyā nigalottamaḥ || 1,24.167 ||
kākaviṭbrahmabījāni lāṅgalī nigalo'paraḥ |
vākucī brahmabījāni karkaṭāsthīni sundari || 1,24.168 ||
sāmudraṃ śāmbaraṃ caiva lavaṇaṃ nigalottamaḥ |
snuhyarkasambhavaṃ kṣīraṃ brahmabījāni kārayet || 1,24.169 ||
kanakasya ca bījāni loṇāṣṭena ca mardayet |
nigalo'nyastu gojihvāmūlāni strīrajo'paraḥ || 1,24.170 ||
vākucī brahmabījāni snuhyarkakṣīrasaindhavam |
jvālinī kākaviṣṭhā ca praśasto nigalottamaḥ || 1,24.171 ||
lavaṇaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ kṣāraṃ śilātālakagandhakam |
tathāmlavetasaṃ tāpyaṃ hiṅgulaṃ samabhāgikam || 1,24.172 ||
snuhyarkapayasā yuktaṃ peṣayennigalottamam |
piṣṭikāṃ veṣṭayedeṣāmekena nigalena tu || 1,24.173 ||
loṇamūṣāgataṃ prāgvatkhoṭaṃ kṛtvā tu vedhayet |
dvitīyaṃ golakaṃ vakṣye piṣṭikāstambhamuttamam || 1,24.174 ||
dvipadīrajamūtreṇa saindhavābhraṃ saguggulam |
piṣṭīṃ saṃveṣṭya kalkena mṛdā tu punaraṣṭadhā || 1,24.175 ||
tuṣakarṣāgninā bhūmau mṛdusvedaṃ tu kārayet |
ahorātraṃ trirātraṃ vā pūrvavatkhoṭatāṃ nayet || 1,24.176 ||
vākucī brahmabījāni gaganaṃ vimalaṃ maṇim |
sauvarcalaṃ saindhavaṃ ca ṭaṅkaṇaṃ gugguluṃ tathā || 1,24.177 ||
dvipadīrajasā mūtraṃ suślakṣṇaṃ taṃ ca mardayet |
piṣṭīmāveṣṭya kalkena pūrvavatkhoṭatāṃ nayet || 1,24.178 ||
<rasamūrchāvidhiḥ, prathamaḥ prakāraḥ>
atha mūrcchāṃ pravakṣyāmi rasasya parameśvari |
bāhlīkaṃ saindhavaṃ kanyā dhūśaro laśunaṃ vacā || 1,24.179 ||
meghanādā kākamācī sarvāṃśaṃ mardayedrasam |
ślakṣṇaṃ taṃ golakaṃ kṛtvā hiṃgunā veṣṭayedbahiḥ || 1,24.180 ||
samena loṇayantrasthaṃ kṛtvā tadvipaceddinam |
caṇḍāgninā svāṅgaśītamadhaḥ pātre sthitaṃ rasam || 1,24.181 ||
cūrṇībhūtaṃ samādāya sāndradviguṇavāsasā |
baddhvā somānale yantre hyathordhvaṃ gandhakaṃ samam || 1,24.182 ||
dattvā pacetpunargandhamevaṃ gandhaṃ tu ṣaḍguṇam |
jārayetsa raso devi mūrchitaḥ sarvarogahā || 1,24.183 ||
<dvitīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
gandhakaṃ navasāraṃ ca śuddhasūtasamaṃ samam |
yāmaṃ saṃmardayecchlakṣṇaṃ kācakūpyāṃ vinikṣipet || 1,24.184 ||
saptamṛtkarpaṭairliptvā kūpīṃ gharme viśoṣayet |
kūpikāṃ vālukāyantre dvādaśapraharaṃ pacet || 1,24.185 ||
sa raso jāyate devi mūrchito rañjito bhavet |
<tṛtīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
rasārdhaṃ gandhakaṃ mardyaṃ yāmayugmaṃ kharātape || 1,24.186 ||
tataḥ sitajayantyāśca rasaiḥ saṃmardayettryaham |
tatastu golakaṃ kṛtvā mūṣāyāṃ saṃnirodhayet || 1,24.187 ||
śoṣayitvā dhametkiṃcitsaṃtaptāṃ tāṃ jale kṣipet |
taṃ rasendraṃ samādāya bhāvayedgokṣuradravaiḥ || 1,24.188 ||
mūrchito jāyate sūtaḥ sarvarogaharaḥ śubhaḥ |
<baddharasalakṣaṇam>
akṣayaśca laghudrāvī tejasvī nirmalo guruḥ || 1,24.189 ||
sphuṭanaḥ punarāvarto baddhaḥ sūtaḥ sa ucyate |
<jalūkābandhāḥ; mardanajalūkā>
rasasyāṣṭamabhāgaṃ tu svarṇaṃ vā nāgameva vā || 1,24.190 ||
śleṣmātakaphalaṃ pakvaṃ kokilākṣakabījakam |
tilapiṇyākacūrṇaṃ ca taptakhalve vimardayet || 1,24.191 ||
śālmalyāścaiva pañcāṅgaṃ rasaṃ tatra vinikṣipet |
jalūkā jāyate yāvattāvanmardyāttataḥ kṣipet || 1,24.192 ||
karpūraṃ vākucītailaṃ saindhavaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ kaṇām |
kapikacchukaromāṇi tatsarvaṃ peṣayejjalaiḥ || 1,24.193 ||
etatpūrvajalūkāṃ trisaptāhaṃ taptakhalvake |
jalūkā jāyate divyā mardanākhyā surārcite || 1,24.194 ||
nārīṇāmṛtukāle tu sā yojyā yonigahvare |
madadarpaharā tāsāṃ madavihvalakārakā || 1,24.195 ||
bālye cāṣṭāṅgulā yojyā yauvane sā daśāṅgulā |
dvādaśāṅgulikā yojyā pragalbhānāṃ jalūkakā || 1,24.196 ||
yastu tāṃ dhārayenmūrdhni vīryaṃ tasya sthiraṃ bhavet |
<manmathajalūkā>
rasendrasyāṣṭamaṃ bhāgaṃ svarṇaṃ vā nāgameva vā || 1,24.197 ||
śālmalītvagghastikandanīlāmūlamunicchadam |
eṣāṃ dravaistaptakhalve sarvaṃ mardyamatandritaḥ || 1,24.198 ||
śleṣmātakaphalaṃ pakvaṃ kokilākṣakabījakam |
supakvabilvamajjāṃ ca tasminkṣiptvā vimardayet || 1,24.199 ||
jalūkā jāyate yāvatkarpūrādyaṃ ca pūrvavat |
tasminkṣiptvā trisaptāhaṃ mardayettaptakhalvake || 1,24.200 ||
manmathākhyā jalūkā syātpūrvavatphaladāyinī |
<kandarpajalūkā>
sūtakasyāṣṭamaṃ bhāgaṃ hāṭakaṃ sīsameva vā || 1,24.201 ||
śuṇṭhī bhṛṅgī varā kṣaudrī chāgakṣīraghṛtaṃ gavām |
mūtraṃ triḥ saptadivasaṃ mardayettaptakhalvake || 1,24.202 ||
jalūkā jāyate yāvatkarpūrādyaṃ ca pūrvavat |
ekaviṃśaddinaṃ mardyaṃ jalūkā jāyate śubhā || 1,24.203 ||
kandarpākhyā sureśāni pūrvavatphaladāyinī |
<madanajalūkā>
tridinaṃ mardayetkhalve sūtaniṣkacatuṣṭayam || 1,24.204 ||
tripalaṃ cāmraniryāsaṃ stokaṃ stokaṃ vinikṣipet |
payaścaiva mahāśamyā dātavyaṃ mardanakṣamam || 1,24.205 ||
jalūkā madanākhyeyaṃ jāyate śubhadā nṛṇām |
rāmāṇāṃ madadarpāṇāṃ drāvikāgnighṛtau yathā || 1,24.206 ||
pūrvavatkramayogena vīryastambhakaraṃ bhavet |
nānāvarṇaṃ tathā svacchaṃ dhṛtaṃ yonau jalūkavat || 1,24.207 ||
badhyate sūtako yastu jalūkābandhalakṣaṇam || 1,24.208 ||


āk, 1, 25
<paribhāṣā>
paribhāṣāṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu tvaṃ sāvadhānataḥ |
<vaidyabhāgaḥ>
ardhaṃ siddharasaṃ devi triṣvekaṃ hemabhasma ca || 1,25.1 ||
pādāṃśakaṃ rūpyabhasma ṣaṣṭhāṃśaṃ tāmrabhasma ca |
aṣṭamāṃśaṃ hi kāntābhraṃ kalāṃśaṃ navaratnakam || 1,25.2 ||
raseśvaraṃ samuddiśya rasavaidyāya dhīmate |
rasācāryāya siddhāya dadyādiṣṭārthasiddhaye || 1,25.3 ||
<kajjalī paṅkarasaśca>
dhātubhirgandhakādyaiśca nirdravairmardito rasaḥ |
suślakṣṇaḥ kajjalābho'sau kajjalī sābhidhīyate || 1,25.4 ||
sadrave marditaḥ so'pi iti pākarasaḥ smṛtaḥ |
<piṣṭī>
arkāṃśatulyādrasato'tha gandhānniṣkārdhatulyāttruṭiśo'pi khalve |
arkātape tīvratare vimardyātpiṣṭirbhavetsā navanītarūpā || 1,25.5 ||
khalve vimardya gandhena dugdhena saha pāradam || 1,25.6 ||
peṣaṇātpiṣṭitāṃ yāti sā piṣṭīti matā paraiḥ |
<pātanapiṣṭī>
caturthāṃśasuvarṇena rasena kṛtapiṣṭikā || 1,25.7 ||
bhavetpātanapiṣṭī sā tathā rūpyādibhiḥ kṛtā |
<hemapiṣṭī tārapiṣṭī ca>
rūpyaṃ vā jātarūpaṃ vā rasagandhādibhirhatam || 1,25.8 ||
samutthitaṃ ca bahuśaḥ sā kṛṣṭī hematārayoḥ |
kṛṣṭīṃ kṣiptvā suvarṇānte na varṇo hīyate tayā || 1,25.9 ||
svarṇakṛṣṭyā kṛtaṃ bījaṃ rasasya parirañjanam |
<lohakam>
tāmraṃ tīkṣṇasamāyuktaṃ drutaṃ nikṣipya bhūriśaḥ || 1,25.10 ||
sagandhe likucadrāve nirgataṃ varalohakam |
<hemaraktī tāraraktī ca>
tena raktīkṛtaṃ svarṇaṃ hemaraktītyudāhṛtā || 1,25.11 ||
nikṣiptā sā drute svarṇe varṇotkarṣavidhāyinī |
tārasya rañjanī cāpi bījarāgavidhāyinī || 1,25.12 ||
evameva prakartavyā tāraraktī manoharā |
rañjanī khalu rūpyasya bījānāmapi rañjanī || 1,25.13 ||
<candrānaladalam>
mṛtena vā baddharasena vānyalohena vā sādhitamanyaloham |
sitaṃ hi pītatvamupāgataṃ taddalaṃ hi candrānalayoḥ prasiddham || 1,25.14 ||
ābhāsamṛtabandhena rasena saha yojitam |
sādhitaṃ vānyalohena sitaṃ pītaṃ hi taddalam || 1,25.15 ||
<ayonāgam>
tāpyena nihataṃ kāntaṃ saptavāraṃ samutthitam |
taddvayaṃ dvipalaṃ vātha tāmrabhasma paladvayam || 1,25.16 ||
sarvaṃ nikṣipya mūṣāyāṃ saptavāraṃ dhameddṛḍham |
tadayonāgamityuktaṃ sādhakaṃ dehalohayoḥ || 1,25.17 ||
rasena sāraṇāyantre tadīyā gulikā kṛtā |
sā dhṛtā vadane hanti meharogānaśeṣataḥ || 1,25.18 ||
kurute dantadārḍhyaṃ ca dṛśau gṛdhradṛśāviva |
tathānyān kṣetrajānrogān rogāñjatrūrdhvasaṃbhavān || 1,25.19 ||
<śulvanāgam>
mākṣikeṇa hataṃ tāmraṃ daśavāraṃ samutthitam |
tadvadviśuddhanāgaṃ hi dvitayaṃ taccatuṣpalam || 1,25.20 ||
nīlāñjanahataṃ bhūyaḥ saptavāraṃ samutthitam |
iti saṃsiddhametaddhi śulbanāgaṃ prakīrtitam || 1,25.21 ||
sādhitastena sūtendro vadane vidhṛto nṛṇām |
nihanti māsamātreṇa mehavyūhamaśeṣataḥ || 1,25.22 ||
pathyāśanasya varṣeṇa palitaṃ valibhiḥ saha |
gṛdhradṛṣṭirlasaddṛṣṭiḥ sarvārogyasamanvitaḥ || 1,25.23 ||
<piñjarī>
lohaṃ lohāntare kṣiptaṃ dhmātaṃ nirvāpitaṃ drave |
pāṇḍupītaprabhaṃ jātaṃ piñjarītyabhidhīyate || 1,25.24 ||
<candrārkam>
bhāgāḥ ṣoḍaśa tārasya tathā dvādaśa bhāsvataḥ |
ekatrāvartitāste tu candrārkamiti kathyate || 1,25.25 ||
<nirvāpahaṇam>
dhāryalohe'nyalohaścetprakṣipto vaṅkanālataḥ |
nirvāpaṇaṃ tu tatproktaṃ vaidyairnirvāhaṇaṃ khalu || 1,25.26 ||
kṣipennirvāhaṇaṃ dravye nirvāhye samabhāgikam |
āvāpyaṃ vāpanīyaṃ ca bhāge dṛṣṭe ca dṛṣṭavat || 1,25.27 ||
<vārtitara (ḍef.)>
mṛtaṃ tarati yattoye lohaṃ vāritaraṃ hi tat |
<rekhāpūrṇa (ḍef.)>
aṅguṣṭhatarjanīghṛṣṭaṃ yattadrekhāntare viśet || 1,25.28 ||
mṛtaṃ lohaṃ taduddiṣṭaṃ rekhāpūrṇābhidhānataḥ |
<nirutthabhasmalakṣaṇam>
guḍaguñjāsukhasparśamadhvājyaiḥ saha yojitam || 1,25.29 ||
na yāti prakṛtiṃ dhmānādapunarbhavamucyate |
<niruttha>
lohena saha saṃyuktaṃ dhmātaṃ rūpyeṇa cellihet || 1,25.30 ||
tadā nirutthamityuktaṃ lohaṃ tadapunarbhavam |
evaṃ rūpyaṃ satāmraṃ ceddhmātaṃ tāmre lagenna hi || 1,25.31 ||
tadā nirutthaṃ mantavyaṃ rajataṃ ca bhiṣagvaraiḥ |
<bījam>
nirvāhaṇaviśeṣeṇa tadvadvarṇaṃ bhavedyadā || 1,25.32 ||
mṛdulaṃ citrasaṅkāśaṃ tadbījamiti kathyate |
<uttāraṇam>
idameva hi nirdiṣṭaṃ vaidyairuttāraṇaṃ khalu || 1,25.33 ||
saṃsṛṣṭalohayorekalohasya parināśanam |
<tāḍanam>
pradhmātaṃ vaṅkanālena tattāḍanamudāhṛtam || 1,25.34 ||
<dhānyābhram>
cūrṇābhraṃ śālisaṃyuktaṃ vastrabaddhaṃ hi kāñjike |
niryātaṃ mardanādvastrāddhānyābhramiti kathyate || 1,25.35 ||
<sattvam>
kṣārāmladrāvakairyuktaṃ dhmātamākarakoṣṭhake |
yastato nirgataḥ sāraḥ sattvamityabhidhīyate || 1,25.36 ||
<ekakolīsakaḥ>
koṣṭhikāśikharāpūrṇaiḥ kokilādhmānayogataḥ |
mūṣākarṇamanuprāptairekakolīśako mataḥ || 1,25.37 ||
<svedanādyarhāḥ kokilāḥ>
śikhitrāḥ pāvakocchiṣṭā mṛtāṅgārāśca kokilāḥ |
kṛṣṇāṅgāḥ kokilāśceti paryāyāste parasparam || 1,25.38 ||
<charcoal:: wood>
drāvaṇe sattvapāte ca mādhūkāḥ khādirāḥ śubhāḥ |
durdrāve vaṃśajāpestu svedane bādarāḥ śubhāḥ || 1,25.39 ||
<hiṅgulākṛṣṭarasaḥ>
vidyādharākhyayantrasthādārdrakadravamarditāt |
samākṛṣṭo raso yo'sau hiṅgulākṛṣṭa ucyate || 1,25.40 ||
<ghoṣākṛṣṭaṃ vaṅgam>
svalpatālayutaṃ kāṃsyaṃ vaṅkanālena tāḍitam |
muktavaṅgaṃ hi tattāmraṃ ghoṣākṛṣṭamudāhṛtam || 1,25.41 ||
<guhyanāgam>
palaviṃśati nāgasya śuddhasya kṛtacakrikam |
rūpikādugdhasampiṣṭaśilāyāṃ parilepitam || 1,25.42 ||
śarāvasaṃpuṭe ruddhvā pacetkroḍapuṭena tām |
tāvadvāraṃ pacedyatnādyāvadbhasma prajāyate || 1,25.43 ||
guḍagugguluguñjājyaṭaṅkaṇaiḥ parimardya tam |
mūṣāmadhye nirudhyātha dhmānādutthāpitaṃ punaḥ || 1,25.44 ||
cakrīṃ tena punaḥ kṛtvā palapramitapāradaiḥ |
liptvā limpetsitārkasya payasā śilayāpi ca || 1,25.45 ||
pacedgajapuṭairevaṃ vārāṇāṃ khalu viṃśatiḥ |
puṭe puṭe ca nāgasya kuryādutthāpanaṃ khalu || 1,25.46 ||
nīlajyotirdravaiḥ samyagdaśavārāṇi ḍhālayet |
iti siddhaṃ hi tatsīsaṃ karṣamātrāvaśeṣitam || 1,25.47 ||
guhyanāgākhyayā proktaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ rasarasāyane |
niṣkamātraṃ tu nāge'smin lohākhye yā drute sati || 1,25.48 ||
svato lakṣaguṇāṃ haimīṃ śalākāṃ grasate dhruvam |
lagettailaprataptaṃ tatsvarṇamudgirati dhruvam || 1,25.49 ||
guhyanāgo'yamuddiṣṭaḥ śaktisvacchandabhairavaḥ |
<varanāgam>
tīkṣṇaṃ nīlāñjanopetaṃ dhmātaṃ hi bahuśo dṛḍham || 1,25.50 ||
mṛdu kṛṣṇaṃ drutadrāvaṃ varanāgaṃ taducyate |
<utthāpanam>
mṛtasya punarudbhūtiḥ sā proktotthāpanākhyayā || 1,25.51 ||
<ḍhālanam>
drutadravyasya nikṣepo drave tatḍhālanaṃ matam |
<capalaḥ>
triṃśatpalamitaṃ nāgaṃ bhānudugdhena marditam || 1,25.52 ||
vimṛdya puṭayettāvadyāvatkarṣāvaśeṣakam |
na tatpuṭasahasreṇa kṣayamāyāti sarvadā || 1,25.53 ||
capalo'yaṃ samādiṣṭo vārtikairnāgasambhavaḥ |
itthaṃ hi capalaḥ kāryo vaṅgasyāpi na saṃśayaḥ || 1,25.54 ||
tatspṛṣṭahastasaṃspṛṣṭaḥ kevalo badhyate rasaḥ |
sa raso dhātuvādeṣu śasyate na rasāyane || 1,25.55 ||
ayaṃ hi karpaṇākhyena lokanāthena kīrtitaḥ |
bhrāmakāśmarajaḥ sūkṣmaṃ pañcamāṃśasamanvitam || 1,25.56 ||
kumārīmūlatoyena mardayedekavāsaram |
śārṅgerīsvarase vāpi dinamekamanāratam || 1,25.57 ||
evaṃ bhūnāgadhautaṃ ca mardayeddivasadvayam |
athaikapalanāgena tāvatā trapuṇāpi ca || 1,25.58 ||
daśaniṣkarasendreṇa ślakṣṇapiṣṭaṃ samācaret |
yojayitvādyakalkena yathāpūrvaṃ vimardayet || 1,25.59 ||
tataḥ śāṇarasendreṇa sattvena rasakasya ca |
piṣṭīṃ kṛtvā tu pūrveṇa pūrvakalkaiśca yojayet || 1,25.60 ||
atha prakṣālya soṣṇena kāñjikena praśoṣayet |
palārdhaṃ śuddhasasyena aṣṭaguñjārasena ca || 1,25.61 ||
vimardya kāñjikaiḥ kuryānmaricapramitā vaṭīḥ |
nirudhya vajramūṣāyāṃ sandhibandhaṃ vidhāya ca || 1,25.62 ||
śikhitrairnavabhiḥ samyagbhastrābhyāṃ pradhametkhalu |
tato mūṣāgataṃ sattvaṃ samādāya samantataḥ || 1,25.63 ||
dhametprakaṭamūṣāyāṃ vaṅkanālena śodhayet |
daśaśāṇaṃ hi tatsatvaṃ bhasmanā lavaṇena ca || 1,25.64 ||
sakāñjikena saṃpeṣya puṭayogena śodhayet |
triniṣkapramite tasminpūrvaproktena bhasmanā || 1,25.65 ||
aśītiguṇitaṃ nāgaṃ dhmātvā nirvāhayetkhalu |
iyatā pūrvasūto'sau kṣīyate na kathaṃcana || 1,25.66 ||
capalo'yaṃ samuddiṣṭo lokanāthena śambhunā |
anenāpi rasaḥ śīghraṃ pūrvavadbadhyate sukham || 1,25.67 ||
kāravallījaṭācūrṇairdaśadhā puṭito hi saḥ |
bhavennāgavinirmukto grāsaṃ gṛhṇātyaśeṣataḥ || 1,25.68 ||
sukhaṃ prakaṭamūṣāyāṃ bhaveccātiguṇottaraḥ |
jīrṇagrāso raso hyeṣāṃ dehalohakaro bhavet || 1,25.69 ||
<bhūnāgadhautam>
bhūbhujaṅgaśakṛttoyaiḥ prakṣālyāpahṛtaṃ malam |
kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ hi tatproktaṃ dhautākhyaṃ rasavādibhiḥ || 1,25.70 ||
<dvandvānam>
dravyayormelanaṃ dhmānāddvaṃdvānaṃ parikīrtitam |
<bhañjanī>
bhāgādrūpyādhike kṣepamanuvarṇasavarṇakam || 1,25.71 ||
dalairvā varṇikāgrāso bhañjanī vādinirmitā |
<phullikā>
pataṅgīkalkato jātā lohe tāratvahematā || 1,25.72 ||
dināni katicitsthitvā yātyasau phullikā matā |
<pataṅgīrāgaḥ>
rañjitārdharasāllohādanyadvā cirakālataḥ || 1,25.73 ||
viniryāti sa nirdiṣṭaḥ pataṅgīrāgasaṃjñakaḥ |
<āvāpaḥ>
drute dravyāntarakṣepāllohādyaiḥ kriyate hi yaḥ || 1,25.74 ||
sa āvāpaḥ pratīvāpastadevācchādanaṃ matam |
<abhiṣekaḥ>
drute vahnisthite lohe viramyāṣṭanimeṣakam || 1,25.75 ||
salilasya parikṣepaḥ so'bhiṣeka iti smṛtaḥ |
nāgaṃ vā vaṅgaṃ vā pradrāvya niṣecayecchataṃ vārān || 1,25.76 ||
uktadravye taddravatāḍanametaddhi so'bhiṣekastu |
<nirvāpa>
taptasyāpsu parikṣepo nirvāpastapanaṃ ca tat || 1,25.77 ||
prativāpyādikaṃ kāryaṃ drutalohe sunirmale |
<śuddhāvartaḥ>
yadā hutāśo dīptārciḥ śuklotthānasamanvitaḥ || 1,25.78 ||
śuddhāvartastadā jñeyaḥ sa kālaḥ sattvanirgame |
<rekhāvartaḥ>
drāvyadravyanibhā jvālā dṛśyate dhamane yadā || 1,25.79 ||
dravasyonmukhatā seyaṃ rekhāvartaḥ sa kathyate |
<svāṅgaśītaṃ vahni(bahiḥ)śītaṃ ca>
vahnisthameva śītaṃ yattaduktaṃ svāṅgaśītalam || 1,25.80 ||
agnerākṛṣṭaśītaṃ yadvahniśītaṃ tadīritam |
<svedanam>
kṣārāmlairauṣadhairvāpi ḍolāyantre sthitasya hi || 1,25.81 ||
pacanaṃ svedanākhyaṃ syānmalaśaithilyakārakam |
<mardanam>
uddiṣṭairauṣadhaiḥ sārdhaṃ sarvāmlaiḥ kāñjikairapi || 1,25.82 ||
peṣaṇaṃ mardanākhyaṃ syādbahirmalavināśanam |
<mūrchanam>
mardanādiṣṭabhaiṣajyairnaṣṭapiṣṭitvakārakam || 1,25.83 ||
tanmūrcchanaṃ hi vāryadribhūjakañcukanāśanam |
<naṣṭapiṣṭiḥ>
svarūpasya vināśena piṣṭitvāpādanaṃ hi yat || 1,25.84 ||
vivṛddhirjitasūtena naṣṭapiṣṭiḥ sa ucyate |
<utthāpanam>
svedatāpādiyogena svarūpāpādanaṃ punaḥ || 1,25.85 ||
tadutthāpanamityuktaṃ mūrcchāvyāpattināśanam |
<pātanam>
uktauṣadhairmarditapāradasya yantrasthitasyordhvamadhaśca tiryak |
niryāpanaṃ pātanasaṃjñayoktaṃ vaṅgāhisaṃparkajakañcukaghnam || 1,25.86 ||
<rodhanam>
jalasaindhavayuktasya rasasya divasatrayam || 1,25.87 ||
sthitirāpyāyinī kumbhe yo'sau rodhanamucyate |
<niyamanam>
rodhanāllabdhavīryasya capalatvanivṛttaye || 1,25.88 ||
kriyate yo ghaṭe svedaḥ proktaṃ niyamanaṃ hi tat |
<dīpanam>
dhātupāṣāṇamūlādyaiḥ saṃyukto ghaṭamadhyataḥ || 1,25.89 ||
grāsārthaṃ tridinaṃ svedo dīpanaṃ tanmataṃ budhaiḥ |
<grāsamānam>
iyanmānasya sūtasya bhojyadravyātmikā mitiḥ || 1,25.90 ||
iyatītyucyate yo'sau grāsamānamitīritam |
<jāraṇā>
grāsasya cāraṇaṃ garbhadrāvaṇaṃ jāraṇaṃ tathā || 1,25.91 ||
iti trirūpā nirdiṣṭā jāraṇā varavārtikaiḥ |
grāsaḥ piṇḍaḥ parīṇāmastisraścākhyāḥ parāḥ punaḥ || 1,25.92 ||
samukhā nirmukhā ceti jāraṇā dvividhā matā |
nirmukhā jāraṇā proktā bījādānena bhāgataḥ || 1,25.93 ||
<samukhajāraṇā>
śuddhaṃ svarṇaṃ ca rūpyaṃ ca bījamityabhidhīyate |
catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśato bījaprakṣepo mukhamucyate || 1,25.94 ||
evaṃ kṛte raso grāsalolupo mukhavān bhavet |
kaṭhinānyapi lohāni kṣamo bhavati bhakṣitum || 1,25.95 ||
iyaṃ hi samukhī proktā jāraṇāmṛtajāraṇā |
<nirmukhacāraṇā>
divyauṣadhisamāyogātsthitaḥ prakaṭakoṣṭhiṣu || 1,25.96 ||
bhuṅkte nikhilalohādyānyo'sau rākṣasavaktravān |
<cāraṇā>
rasasya jaṭhare grāsakṣepaṇaṃ cāraṇā matā || 1,25.97 ||
<garbhadrutiḥ>
grastasya drāvaṇaṃ garbhe garbhadrutirudāhṛtā |
<bāhyadrutiḥ>
bahireva drutīkṛtya ghanasatvādikaṃ khalu || 1,25.98 ||
jāraṇāya rasendrasya sā bāhyadrutirucyate |
<druti (substance)>
nirlepitvaṃ dravatvaṃ ca tejastvaṃ laghutā tathā || 1,25.99 ||
asaṃyogaśca sūtena pañcadhā drutilakṣaṇam |
<druti (process)>
auṣadhājyādiyogena lohadhātvādikaṃ sadā || 1,25.100 ||
uttiṣṭhate dravākārā sā drutiḥ parikīrtitā |
<jāraṇā>
drutagrāsaparīṇāmo biḍayantrādiyogataḥ || 1,25.101 ||
jāraṇetyucyate tasyāḥ prakārāḥ santi koṭiśaḥ |
<viḍam>
kṣārairamlaiśca gandhādyairmūtraiḥ sapaṭubhistathā || 1,25.102 ||
rasagrāsasya jīrṇārthaṃ tadbiḍaṃ parikīrtitam |
<rañjanam>
saṃsiddhabījasattvādijāraṇena rasasya hi || 1,25.103 ||
pītādirāgajananaṃ rañjanaṃ samudīritam |
<sāraṇā>
sūte satailayantrasthe svarṇādikṣepaṇaṃ hi yat || 1,25.104 ||
vedhādhikyakaraṃ lohe sāraṇā sā prakīrtitā |
<vedhaḥ>
vyavāyibheṣajopeto dravye kṣipto rasaḥ khalu || 1,25.105 ||
vedha ityucyate tajjñaiḥ sa cānekavidhaḥ smṛtaḥ |
<vedhabhedāḥ>
lepaḥ kṣepaśca kuntaśca śabdākhyo dhūmasaṃjñakaḥ || 1,25.106 ||
<lepavedhaḥ>
lepena kurute lohaṃ svarṇaṃ vā rajataṃ tathā |
lepavedhaḥ sa vijñeyaḥ puṭamatra ca saukaram || 1,25.107 ||
<kṣepavedhaḥ>
prakṣepaṇaṃ drute lohe vedhaḥ syātkṣepasaṃjñakaḥ |
<kuntavedhaḥ>
saṃdaṃśadhṛtasūtena drutadravyāhatiśca yaḥ || 1,25.108 ||
suvarṇatvādikaraṇaḥ kuntavedhaḥ sa ucyate |
<dhūpavedhaḥ>
vahnau dhūmāyamāne'ntaḥprakṣiptarasadhūmataḥ || 1,25.109 ||
svarṇādyāpādanaṃ lohe dhūmavedhaḥ sa īritaḥ |
<śabdavedhaḥ>
mukhasthite rase nālyā lohasya dhamanātkhalu || 1,25.110 ||
svarṇarūpyatvajananaṃ śabdavedhaḥ sa kīrtitaḥ |
<utpāṭanam>
piṇḍadravyasya sūtena kāluṣyādinivāraṇam || 1,25.111 ||
prakāśanaṃ ca varṇasya tadutpāṭanamīritam |
<svedanam>
kṣārāmlairauṣadhaiḥ sārdhaṃ bhāṇḍe ruddhvātiyatnataḥ || 1,25.112 ||
bhūmau nikhanyate yattatsvedanaṃ samudīritam |
<saṃnyāsaḥ>
rasasyauṣadhayuktasya bhāṇḍe ruddhasya yatnataḥ || 1,25.113 ||
sadāgniyutacullyantaḥkṣepaḥ saṃnyāsa ucyate |
<svedanasaṃnyāsaguṇāḥ>
dvāvetau svedasaṃnyāsau rasarājasya niścitam || 1,25.114 ||
guṇaprabhāvajananau śīghravyāptikarau tathā || 1,25.115 ||


āk, 1, 26
<yantrāṇi>
raso niyantryate yena yantraṃ taditi kathyate |
<1. khalvayantram>
khalvayantraṃ dvidhā proktaṃ mardanādikakarmaṇi || 1,26.1 ||
khalvaṃ lohamayaṃ devi pāṣāṇotthamathāpi vā |
khalvayogyā śilā nīlā śyāmā snigdhā dṛḍhā guruḥ || 1,26.2 ||
khalvayantraṃ dvidhā proktaṃ rasādimukhamardane |
ṣoḍaśāṅgulikotsedhā navāṅgulisuvistarā || 1,26.3 ||
caturviṃśāṅgulīdīrghagharṣaṇī dvādaśāṅgulā |
viṃśatyaṅguladīrghā vā syādutsedhe daśāṅgulā || 1,26.4 ||
khalvapramāṇaṃ tajjñeyaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ syādrasamardane |
ṣoḍaśāṅgulavistāraḥ khalvo bhavati vartulaḥ || 1,26.5 ||
caturaṅgulanimnaṃ ca madhye'timasṛṇīkṛtam |
mardanī cipiṭādhastātsugrahā ca śikhopari || 1,26.6 ||
ayaṃ hi vartulaḥ khalvo mardane'tisukhapradaḥ |
ayasā kāntalohena lohakhalvamapīdṛśam || 1,26.7 ||
adhastāddroṇikā kāryā vahniprajvālanocitā |
utsedhena navāṅgulaḥ khalu kalātulyāṅgulāyāmavān vistāreṇa navāṅgulo rasamitairnimnaistathaivāṅgulaiḥ |
kaṇṭhe dvyaṅgulavistaro'timasṛṇo droṇārdhacandrākṛtirgharṣaścaiva daśāṅgulaśca tadidaṃ khalvākhyayantraṃ matam || 1,26.8 ||
asminpañcapalaḥ sūto mardanīyo viśuddhaye || 1,26.9 ||
tattadaucityayogena khalveṣvanyeṣu śodhayet |
<taptakhalvaḥ>
lohe navāṅgulaḥ khalvo nimnaścaiva ṣaḍaṅgulaḥ || 1,26.10 ||
mardako'ṣṭāṅgulaścaiva taptakhalvābhidho hyayam |
kṛtvā khalvākṛtiṃ cullīmaṅgāraiḥ paripūrya tām || 1,26.11 ||
tasminniveśya taṃ khalvaṃ pārśve bhastrikayā dhamet |
tasminvimarditā piṣṭiḥ kṣārāmlaiśca susaṃyutā || 1,26.12 ||
pradravatyativegena sveditā nātra saṃśayaḥ |
kṛtaḥ kāntāyasā so'yaṃ bhavetkoṭiguṇo rasaḥ || 1,26.13 ||
<2. khavalabhīyantram (valabhīyantraṃ vā)>
yantre lohamaye pātre pārśvayorvalayadvayam |
tādṛksvalpataraṃ pātraṃ valayadyotakoṣṭhakam || 1,26.14 ||
pūrvapātropari nyasya svalpapātropari kṣipet |
rasaṃ saṃmūrchitaṃ sthūlapātramāpūrya kāñjikaiḥ || 1,26.15 ||
dviyāmaṃ svedayedevaṃ rasotthāpanahetave |
tatsyātkhavalabhīyantraṃ rasasādguṇyakārakam || 1,26.16 ||
sūkṣmakāntamaye pātre rasaḥ syādguṇavattaraḥ |
<3. ūrdhvapātanayantram>
aṣṭāṅgulamitā samyakvartulā cipiṭā tale || 1,26.17 ||
caturaṅgulataḥ kaṇṭhādadho droṇyā samanvitā |
caturaṅgulavistārā nimnayā dṛḍhabaddhayā || 1,26.18 ||
tadvidhā ca ghaṭī mūle ṣoḍaśāṅgulavistṛtā |
navāṅgulakavistārakarṇena ca samanvitā || 1,26.19 ||
pūrve ghaṭe rasaṃ kṣiptvā nyubjāṃ dadyātparāṃ ghaṭīm |
sordhvaṃ nimnā ca parito dṛḍhapālikayānvitā || 1,26.20 ||
pālyāṃ droṇyāṃ kṣipettoyaṃ pāvakaṃ jvālayedadhaḥ |
ūrdhvapātanayantraṃ hi nandinā parikīrtitam || 1,26.21 ||
<4. adhaḥpātanayantram>
upariṣṭāttu tatsthālyāṃ kṣipedanyāmadhomukhīm |
sthālikāṃ cipiṭībhūtāṃ talāntarliptapāradām || 1,26.22 ||
kṣiptvā tatpaṅkile garte jvālayenmūrdhni pāvakam |
adhaḥpātanayantraṃ hi tadetatparikīrtitam || 1,26.23 ||
<5. tiryakpātanayantram>
kṣipedrasaṃ ghaṭe dīrghe natādhonālasaṃyute |
tannālaṃ nikṣipedanyaghaṭakukṣyantare khalu || 1,26.24 ||
tatra ruddhvā mṛdā samyagvadane ghaṭayoradhaḥ |
adhastādrasakumbhasya jvālayettīvrapāvakam || 1,26.25 ||
itarasmin ghaṭe toyaṃ prakṣipetsvāduśītalam |
tiryakpātanametaddhi vārtikairabhidhīyate || 1,26.26 ||
pātanātritayasyoktaṃ yantrāṇāṃ tritayaṃ khalu |
pātanaiśca vinā sūto nitarāṃ doṣamṛcchati || 1,26.27 ||
tribhirevordhvapātaiḥ sa kasmāddoṣairna mucyate |
dvivibhāgena vipākena dravyānanyonyayogataḥ || 1,26.28 ||
pātrāntaraparikṣepādguṇāḥ syurvividhāḥ khalu |
khaṇḍānyulūkhalāṃbhobhistaṇḍulāsyurjalojjhitāḥ || 1,26.29 ||
pātanaiva mahāśuddhistaṇḍulī parikīrtitā |
<6. kacchapayantram>
viśālavadane bhāṇḍe toyapūrṇe niveśayet || 1,26.30 ||
aparaṃ pṛthulaṃ samyakpratarastasya madhyame |
ālavālaṃ biḍaiḥ kṛtvā tanmadhye pāradaṃ kṣipet || 1,26.31 ||
ūrdhvādhaśca biḍaṃ dattvā mallenārudhya yatnataḥ |
puṭamaucityayogena dīyate tannigadyate || 1,26.32 ||
yantraṃ kacchapasaṃjñaṃ hi taduktaṃ rasajāraṇe |
<7. āntarālikayantram>
kṛtvā lohamayīṃ mūṣāṃ vartulādhārakāriṇīm || 1,26.33 ||
vitastyā samitāṃ kāntalohena parinirmitām |
muṇḍalohodbhavāṃ vāpi kaṇṭhādho dvyaṅgulādadhaḥ || 1,26.34 ||
dvyaṅgulaṃ valayaṃ dadyānmadhyadeśe ca kaṇṭhataḥ |
pidhānadhārakaṃ ciñcāpatravistīrṇakaṅkaṇam || 1,26.35 ||
pidhānamantarāviṣṭaṃ saśikhaṃ śliṣṭasandhikam |
tale pravihatacchidraṃ bhāṇḍaṃ kṛtvā hyadhomukham || 1,26.36 ||
yantreṇālambayenmūrdhni nirudhya ca viśoṣya ca |
sthālīkaṇṭhaṃ tato dadyātpuṭānalavidhāraṇam || 1,26.37 ||
evaṃrūpaṃ bhavedyantramāntarālikasaṃjñakam |
anena jārayedgandhaṃ drutiṃ garbhakṛtāmapi || 1,26.38 ||
<8. tāpikāyantram>
tāpīmūṣāṃ mṛdā kṛtvā dṛḍhāṃ cāratnimātrikām |
sudṛḍhāṃ madhyadeśe ca dvyaṅgulacchidrasaṃyutām || 1,26.39 ||
kāntalohamayīṃ khārīṃ dadyāddravyasya copari |
tāpikāṃ pūrayecchuddhasikatābhiḥ samantataḥ || 1,26.40 ||
tāṃ ca cullyāṃ samāropya kṣepaṃ kṣepaṃ biḍadravam |
pādāṅguṣṭhamitāṃ jvālāṃ jvālayedanalaṃ tataḥ || 1,26.41 ||
lohābhrakādikaṃ sarvaṃ rasasyopari jārayet |
tāpikāyantramityuktaṃ sukaraṃ rasajāraṇe || 1,26.42 ||
<9. garbhayantram>
sthālyāṃ vinikṣipya rasādivastu svarṇādi khoryāṃ pravidhāya bhūyaḥ |
amlena cordhvaṃ lavaṇāni dattvā cullyāṃ pacettatpratigarbhayantram || 1,26.43 ||
<garbhayantra>
khorīmallaṃ tataḥ sthālīṃ nirundhyādatiyatnataḥ |
sthālyāṃ mallena vā khoryāṃ kṣiptvā vastu nirudhya ca || 1,26.44 ||
kṣiptvā paṭṭādikaṃ ruddhvā pākaḥ syādgarbhayantrakam |
<10. pālikāyantram>
caṣakaṃ vartulaṃ lohaṃ vinatāgrordhvadaṇḍakam || 1,26.45 ||
etaddhi pālikāyantraṃ balijāraṇahetave |
<11. ghaṭīyantram>
catuḥprasthajalādhāraṃ caturaṅgulakānanam || 1,26.46 ||
ghaṭīyantramidaṃ proktaṃ tadāpyāyanake smṛtam |
<12. iṣṭikāyantram>
vidhāya vartulaṃ gartaṃ mallamatra nidhāya ca || 1,26.47 ||
vinidhāyeṣṭakāṃ tatra madhyagartavatīṃ śubhām |
gartasya paritaḥ kuryātpālikāmaṅgulocchrayām || 1,26.48 ||
tatra sūtaṃ vinikṣipya gartāsye vasanaṃ kṣipet |
nikṣipedgandhakaṃ tatra mallenāsyaṃ nirudhya ca || 1,26.49 ||
mallapālikayormadhye mṛdā samyaṅnirudhya ca |
vanotpalaiḥ puṭaṃ deyaṃ kapotākhyaṃ na cādhikam || 1,26.50 ||
iṣṭikāyantrametatsyādgandhakaṃ tena jārayet |
<13. vidyādharayantram>
sthālikopari vinyasya sthālīṃ samyaṅnirudhya ca || 1,26.51 ||
ūrdhvasthālyāṃ jalaṃ kṣiptvā vahniṃ prajvālayedadhaḥ |
etadvidyādharaṃ yantraṃ hiṅgulākṛṣṭihetave || 1,26.52 ||
<14. ḍamarukayantram>
yantraṃ sthālyupari sthālīṃ nyubjāṃ dattvā nirudhyate |
yantraṃ ḍamarukākhyaṃ tadrasabandhakṛte hitam || 1,26.53 ||
<15. nābhiyantram>
mallamadhye caredgartaṃ tatra sūtaṃ sagandhakam |
gartasya paritaḥ kuḍyaṃ prakuryāttryaṅgulocchritam || 1,26.54 ||
tataścācchādayetsamyaggostanākāramūṣayā |
samyaktoyamṛdā ruddhvā samyagatrocyamānayā || 1,26.55 ||
<jalamṛtsnā>
lehavatkṛtabarbūrakvāthena parimiśritam |
jīrṇakiṭṭarajaḥ sūkṣmaṃ guḍacūrṇaiḥ samanvitam || 1,26.56 ||
iyaṃ hi jalamṛtproktā durbhedyā salilaiḥ khalu |
<vahnimṛtsnā>
khaṭikāpaṭukiṭṭaiśca mahiṣīdugdhamarditaiḥ || 1,26.57 ||
vahnimṛtsā bhavedghoravahnitāpasahā khalu |
etayormṛtsnayo ruddho na gantuṃ kṣamate rasaḥ || 1,26.58 ||
<nābhiyantra (cont.)>
tato jalaṃ vinikṣipya vahniṃ prajvālayedadhaḥ |
nābhiyantramidaṃ proktaṃ nandinā tattvavedinā || 1,26.59 ||
anena jīryate sūte nirdhūmaḥ śuddhagandhakaḥ |
<16. grastayantram>
mūṣā mūṣodarāviṣṭā ādyantasamavartulā || 1,26.60 ||
cipiṭā ca tale proktaṃ grastayantraṃ manīṣibhiḥ |
sūtendrabandhanārthaṃ hi rasavidbhirudīritam || 1,26.61 ||
<17. tulāyantram>
vṛntākākāramūṣe dve tayoḥ kaṇṭhādadhaḥ khalu |
prādeśamātrāṃ nalikāmṛjvīṃ lagnāṃ sagandhakām || 1,26.62 ||
tatraikasyāṃ kṣipetsūtamanyasyāṃ gandhacūrṇakam |
nirudhya mūṣayorvaktraṃ vālukāyantrake kṣipet || 1,26.63 ||
adho'gniṃ jvālayedetattulāyantramudāhṛtam |
śilātālakagandhāśmajāraṇāya prakīrtitam || 1,26.64 ||
<18. sthālīyantram>
sthālyāṃ tāmrādi nikṣipya mallenāsyaṃ nirudhya ca |
pacyate sthālisaṃsthaṃ yatsthālīyantramiti smṛtam || 1,26.65 ||
<19 koṣṭhikāyantram>
sthūlabhāṇḍodarasyāntarvālukāṃ nikṣipecchubhām |
vitastipramitotsedhāṃ tatastatra niveśayet || 1,26.66 ||
supakvāṃ mṛṇmayīṃ koṣṭhīṃ dvādaśāṅgulakocchrayām |
ṣaḍaṅgulakavistīrṇāṃ madhye'timasṛṇīkṛtām || 1,26.67 ||
pañcāṅgulapidhānaṃ ca tīkṣṇāgraṃ mukulīkṛtam |
na nyūnaṃ nādhikaṃ koṣṭhe kaṇṭhato masṛṇā bahiḥ || 1,26.68 ||
koṣṭhyāṃ ca nikṣipedgandhaṃ ṣaṭpalaṃ ślakṣṇacūrṇitam |
tatastraipādikīṃ lauhīṃ niveśya ca sthirīkṛtām || 1,26.69 ||
tasyāṃ ca vinyasetkhorīṃ lauhīṃ vā kāntalohajām |
tasyāṃ sūtaṃ kṣipecchuddhaṃ palaviṃśatimānataḥ || 1,26.70 ||
ṭaṅkagandhakasūtaṃ ca bhāvayellaśunadravaiḥ |
adhaḥśikhena pūrvoktapidhānena pidhāya ca || 1,26.71 ||
saṃdhibandhaṃ prakurvīta sudhāmṛtsnāguḍādibhiḥ |
sandhibandhe viśuṣke ca kṣipedupari vālukām || 1,26.72 ||
bhāṇḍavaktraṃ nirudhyātha jvālayettadadho'nalam |
evaṃ yāmatrayaṃ yāvattato gandhakasambhavaḥ || 1,26.73 ||
pidhānalagnadhūmo'sau galitvā nipatedrase |
evaṃ ca ṣaḍguṇaṃ gandhaṃ bhuktvā sūto'ruṇo bhavet || 1,26.74 ||
karoti kalpanirdiṣṭānviśiṣṭānakhilānguṇān |
koṣṭhikāyantrametaddhi nandinā parikīrtitam || 1,26.75 ||
<20. vālukāyantram>
pañcāḍhavālukācūrṇaṃ bhāṇḍe nikṣipya yatnataḥ |
pacyate rasagolādyaṃ vālukāyantrakaṃ hi tat || 1,26.76 ||
<21. lavaṇayantram>
evaṃ lavaṇanikṣepātproktaṃ lavaṇayantrakam |
<22. dhūpayantram>
vidhāyāṣṭāṅgulaṃ pātraṃ lohamaṣṭāṃgulocchrayam || 1,26.77 ||
kaṇṭhādho dvyaṅgule deśe jātādhāraṃ ca tatra ca |
tiryaglohaśalākāṃ ca tasmiṃstiryagvinikṣipet || 1,26.78 ||
tanūni svarṇapatrāṇi tasyāmupari vinyaset |
pātrādho nikṣipeddhūpaṃ vakṣyamāṇamihaiva hi || 1,26.79 ||
tatpātraṃ nyubjapātreṇa chādayedapareṇa hi |
mṛdā vilipya sandhiṃ ca vahniṃ prajvālayedadhaḥ || 1,26.80 ||
tena kṛṣṇāni patrāṇi hatānyuktavidhānataḥ |
rasaścarati vegena drutiṃ garbhe dravanti ca || 1,26.81 ||
gandhālakaśilānāṃ hi kajjalyā vā mṛtāhinā |
dhūpanaṃ svarṇapatrāṇāṃ paramaṃ parikīrtitam || 1,26.82 ||
tārārthaṃ tārapatrāṇi mṛtavaṅgena dhūpayet |
dhūpayecca yathāyogaṃ rasairuparasairapi || 1,26.83 ||
dhūpayantramidaṃ proktaṃ jāraṇādravyaśodhane |
<23. kandukayantram>
sthūlasthālyāṃ dravaṃ kṣiptvā vāso baddhvā mukhe dṛḍham || 1,26.84 ||
tatra svedyaṃ vinikṣipya mṛdāsyaṃ prapidhāya ca |
adhastājjvālayedagniṃ yantraṃ tatkandukāhvayam || 1,26.85 ||
<24. ḍhekīyantram>
bhāṇḍakaṇṭhādadhaśchidre veṇunālaṃ vinikṣipet |
kāntakāṃsyapātradvayaṃ kṛtvā sampuṭaṃ jalagarbhitam || 1,26.86 ||
nālāsyaṃ tatra saṃyojyaṃ dṛḍhaṃ taccāpi kārayet |
yuktadravyairvinikṣiptaḥ pūrvaṃ tatra puṭe rasaḥ || 1,26.87 ||
agninā tāpito nālāttoye tasminpatatyadhaḥ |
yāvaduṣṇaṃ bhavetsarvaṃ bhājanaṃ tāvadeva hi || 1,26.88 ||
jāyate rasasaṃdhānaṃ ḍhekīyantramidaṃ bhavet |
<25. somānalayantram>
ūrdhvaṃ vahniradhaścāpo madhye tu rasasaṅgrahaḥ || 1,26.89 ||
somānalamidaṃ proktaṃ jārayedgaganādikam |
<26. nālikāyantram>
lohanālagataṃ sūtaṃ bhāṇḍe lavaṇapūritam || 1,26.90 ||
niruddhaṃ vipacedetannālikāyantramīritam |
<27. pātālayantram>
susaṃdhisaṃdhitaṃ kṛtvā divyabhāṇḍe tu sammukham || 1,26.91 ||
aṣṭāṅgulamukhaṃ taṃ tu dīrghaṃ syātṣoḍaśāṅgulam |
susaṃdhisaṃdhitaṃ kṛtvā vastramṛttikālepanam || 1,26.92 ||
tatra pātālayantre tu sūtakādi nipātayet |
<28. dīpikāyantram>
kacchapayantrāntargatamṛṇmayapīṭhasthadīpikāsaṃsthaḥ || 1,26.93 ||
yasminnipatati sūtaḥ proktaṃ taddīpikāyantram |
<29. gaṅgāsāgara(bhaṭṭī)yantram>
dvādaśāṅgulamutsedhaṃ ṣoḍaśāṅgulamāyatam || 1,26.94 ||
tāmrīyaṃ mṛṇmayaṃ vātha suślakṣṇaṃ cipiṭaṃ śubham |
pātraṃ karṇādadho droṇīṃ dvyaṅgulotsedhamātrakām || 1,26.95 ||
droṇyāṃ pātraṃ nyasedanyattāvanmātraṃ susaṃdhitam |
nyubjaṃ sandhau tayornālaṃ kuryādgomukhasannibham || 1,26.96 ||
pācyadravyamadhaḥ pātre dravadravyeṇa yojitam |
kṣiptvā nidhāya mṛtsaṃdhiṃ yantrordhve jalasecanam || 1,26.97 ||
cullyāmāropya tadyantraṃ caṇḍāgniṃ jvālayedadhaḥ |
tasmānnānyadviniryāti tattaddravyāśrito rasaḥ || 1,26.98 ||
gaṅgāsāgarayantraṃ hi bhaṭṭiyantramidaṃ smṛtam |
guḍapuṣpaphalādīnāmāhareddrutimuttamām || 1,26.99 ||
<30. ḍo(do)lāyantram>
dravadravyeṇa bhāṇḍasya pūritārdhodarasya ca |
mukhe tiryakkṛte daṇḍe rasaṃ sūtreṇa lambitam || 1,26.100 ||
svedayettattalagataṃ ḍolāyantramiti smṛtam |
<31. koṣṭhīyantram>
cullīṃ caturmukhīṃ kṛtvā tatra bhāṇḍaṃ niveśayet || 1,26.101 ||
tatrauṣadhaṃ vinikṣipya ruddhvā tadbhāṇḍakānanam |
koṣṭhīyantramidaṃ nāmnā tatratyaiḥ parikīrtitam || 1,26.102 ||
<32. garbhayantram>
tryaṅgulāṃ pariṇāhena dairghyeṇa caturaṅgulām |
mṛṇmayīṃ sudṛḍhāṃ mūṣāṃ vartulaṃ kārayenmukham || 1,26.103 ||
loṇasya viṃśatiṃ bhāgānbhāgamekaṃ tu gugguloḥ |
suślakṣṇaṃ peṣayitvā tu vāraṃ vāraṃ punaḥ punaḥ || 1,26.104 ||
mūṣālepaṃ dṛḍhaṃ kṛtvā lavaṇārdhamṛdā budhaḥ |
kārīṣe vā tuṣāgnau vā bhūmau tu svedayenmṛdu || 1,26.105 ||
ahorātraṃ trirātraṃ vā rasendro bhasmatāṃ vrajet |
garbhayantramidaṃ proktaṃ piṣṭikābhasmakārakam || 1,26.106 ||
<33. haṃsapākayantram>
kharparaṃ sikatāpūrṇaṃ kṛtvā tasyopari nyaset |
aparaṃ kharparaṃ tatra śanairmṛdvagninā pacet || 1,26.107 ||
pañcakṣāraistathā mūtrairlavaṇaiśca biḍaṃ tataḥ |
haṃsapākaḥ samākhyāto yantraṃ tadvārtikottamaiḥ || 1,26.108 ||
<34. mūṣāyantram>
lohamūṣādvayaṃ kṛtvā dvādaśāṅgulamānataḥ |
mūṣayormukhayornālaṃ dattvā samyaṅnirodhayet || 1,26.109 ||
ekasyāṃ sūtakaṃ śuddhamanyasyāṃ śuddhagandhakam |
deyaṃ jalaṃ sūtakādho vahniṃ gandhopari kṣipet || 1,26.110 ||
jārayetṣaḍguṇaṃ gandhamanenaiva krameṇa hi |
mūṣāyantramidaṃ jñeyaṃ siddhanāgārjuneritam || 1,26.111 ||
<35. stanayantram>
kāntalohamayaṃ pātramāyataṃ dvādaśāṅgulam |
dīrghamaṣṭāṅgulaṃ devi pātrādhastryaṅgulaṃ śubham || 1,26.112 ||
nimnaṃ pātraṃ pidhānīṃ ca lohotthāṃ cipiṭāṃ śubhām |
stanayantramidaṃ sūtapiṣṭīnāṃ jāraṇe varam || 1,26.113 ||
<36. nāgamāyūrayantram>
vṛntākamūṣāyugalaṃ padmavartalohena kārayet |
ekasyāṃ nikṣipetsūtamanyasyāṃ garalaṃ kṣipet || 1,26.114 ||
nāgākāraṃ vaktranālaṃ viṣamūṣāmukhe nyaset |
mayūrākāranālaṃ hi rasamūṣāmukhe nyaset || 1,26.115 ||
mayūravadane nāgamukhaṃ saṃyojayetsudhīḥ |
sandhitrayaṃ vajramṛdā lepaṃ kuryādyathā dṛḍham || 1,26.116 ||
salilaṃ rasamūṣādho viṣādho'ṅgārapāvakaḥ |
nāgamāyūrayantraṃ hi viṣadhūpe varaṃ priye || 1,26.117 ||
<37. cakrayantram>
hastamātrāyataṃ gartaṃ vitastidvayanimnakam |
koṣṭhīṃ gartasya madhye tu dvādaśāṃgulamāyatām || 1,26.118 ||
vitastidvayamutsedhāṃ koṣṭhyāmāpūrayecchubhām |
vālukāṃ tadbahiśchāṇavahniṃ koṣṭhyāṃ rasaṃ priye || 1,26.119 ||
cakrayantramidaṃ sūtabhasmakarmaṇi śasyate |
<38. khecarayantram>
susthālyāṃ kadalīpuṣpanibhāṃ sacchidramūṣikām || 1,26.120 ||
adhomukhīṃ prakurvīta lipedvajramṛdā dṛḍham |
upariṣṭādadhovaktrāṃ sthālīmanyāṃ susaṃdhitām || 1,26.121 ||
mūṣāyāṃ vatsanābhaṃ tu nikṣipedūrdhvabhājane |
rasaṃ vilepayedyuktyā yantrordhvaṃ kaitavo rasaḥ || 1,26.122 ||
adho mṛdvagninā pākastvetatkhecarayantrakam |
prāyaḥ siddharasendrasya viṣadhūpe varaṃ priye || 1,26.123 ||
<39. kāpāliyantram>
sthālyāṃ sūtādikānkṣiptvā hemarūpyādi khorikām |
nyubjāṃ sandhiṃ mṛdā liptvā vālukāṃ khorikāntagām || 1,26.124 ||
kṛtvā mṛdvagninā pākastvetatkāpāliyantrakam |
<40. vālukāyantram>
sarasāṃ gūḍhavaktrāṃ ca mṛdvastrāṅgulasaṃyutām || 1,26.125 ||
śoṣitāṃ kācakalaśīṃ pūrayettriṣu bhāgayoḥ |
bhāṇḍe vitastigambhīre vālukāsu pratiṣṭhitā || 1,26.126 ||
bhāgasya pūrayitrībhiranyābhiravakuṇṭhayet |
bhāṇḍavaktraṃ maṇikayā sandhiṃ limpenmṛdā pacet || 1,26.127 ||
cullyāṃ tṛṇasya cādāhānmaṇikāpṛṣṭhavartinaḥ |
etaddhi vālukāyantraṃ rasagolādikānpacet || 1,26.128 ||
<41. lavaṇayantram>
evaṃ lavaṇanikṣepātproktaṃ lavaṇayantrakam |
antaḥkṛtarasālepatāmrapātramukhasya ca || 1,26.129 ||
liptvā mṛllavaṇenaiva sandhiṃ bhāṇḍatalasya ca |
tadbhāṇḍaṃ paṭunāpūrya kṣārairvā pūrvavatpacet || 1,26.130 ||
<42. bhūdharayantram>
vālukāgūḍhasarvāṅgāṃ yantre mūṣāṃ rasānvitām |
dīptotpalaiḥ saṃvṛṇuyādyantraṃ tadbhūdharāhvayam || 1,26.131 ||
<43. nālikāyantram>
kumbhasya pārśve suṣiraṃ kuryādaṅguṣṭhamātrakam |
tāvatsthūlamayonālaṃ veṇunālamathāpi vā || 1,26.132 ||
chidre saṃyojayennālaṃ nālāgre ghaṭikāṃ nyaset |
kumbhe sarjādiniryāsaṃ kṣiptvā vaktraṃ nirodhayet || 1,26.133 ||
adho'gniṃ jvālayedetannālikāyantramucyate |
<44. puṭayantram>
śarāvasampuṭāntasthaṃ karīṣeṣvagnimānavit || 1,26.134 ||
paceta cullyāṃ yāmaṃ vā rasaṃ tatpuṭayantrakam |
<45. pātālayantram>
viśālavadanāṃ sthālīṃ garte sajalagomaye || 1,26.135 ||
vinyasya vadanāntaśca pūrayedaparaṃ ghaṭam |
pañcasacchidrasahitāṃ sthālīṃ vaktre niveśayet || 1,26.136 ||
tadghaṭaṃ pūrayettailapātyaṃdravyairnirodhayet |
bhāṇḍavaktraṃ śarāveṇa puṭaṃ dadyātsamantataḥ || 1,26.137 ||
chidrebhyaḥ patitaṃ tailaṃ tattadyogeṣu yojayet |
idaṃ pātālayantraṃ hi sarvatailaṃ nipātayet || 1,26.138 ||
kāṣṭhatvagbījamāṃsāsthivaṃśatalaṃ samāharet |
<46. dhūpayantram>
sthālyāṃ laddiṃ kharādīnāṃ kṣiptvāsye kāṃsyapātrakam || 1,26.139 ||
sajalaṃ vinyaseddevi vahniṃ prajvālayedadhaḥ |
kāntapātrasthitaṃ tailaṃ sarvavraṇaviropaṇam || 1,26.140 ||
dhūpayantramidaṃ devi nandinā parikīrtitam |
<47. adhaḥpātanayantram>
vṛttālābusamasthūlaṃ dīrghanālaṃ sarandhrakam || 1,26.141 ||
tasminkṣipettailapātyadravyaṃ bījādikaṃ priye |
tanmukhe nikṣipetkeśānvinyasettadadhomukham || 1,26.142 ||
sacchidre ca ghaṭe nālaṃ bhāṇḍacchidre niveśayet |
ghaṭamadhye puṭaṃ dadyānnālādho ghaṭikāṃ nyaset || 1,26.143 ||
adhaḥpātanayantraṃ hi śreṣṭhaṃ syāttailapātane |
<48. anyatadhaḥpātanayantram>
viśālakāṃsyapātrāntarnyaseduttambhanaṃ samam || 1,26.144 ||
tadūrdhve mṛṇmayaṃ pātraṃ sudṛḍhaṃ caturaṅgulam |
vinyasedaparaṃ pātraṃ saṃtaptaṃ pūrvapātrake || 1,26.145 ||
vinyasya tasmin śrīkhaṇḍakṛṣṇāgarumadhuplutam |
evaṃ dvitīyaṃ pātraṃ tu tṛtīyamapi tadvidham || 1,26.146 ||
uparyupari vinyasya tadūrdhvaṃ mṛtkaṭāhakam |
nyubjaṃ nyasetkāṃsyapātre kaṭāhaṃ mārjayejjalaiḥ || 1,26.147 ||
adhaḥpātanayantraṃ hi gandhatailaṃ nipātayet |
<mūṣā>
mūṣā hi krauñcikā proktā kumudī karahāṭikā || 1,26.148 ||
pācanī vahnimitrā ca rasavādibhiriṣyate |
muṣṇāti doṣānmūṣeyaṃ sā mūṣeti nigadyate || 1,26.149 ||
upādānaṃ bhavettasyā mṛttikā lohameva ca |
mūṣāmukhaviniṣkrāntā varamekāpi kākinī || 1,26.150 ||
durjanapraṇipātena dhiglakṣamapi māninām |
<saṃdhilepa>
mūṣāpidhānayorbandhe randhrāṇaṃ suvilepayet || 1,26.151 ||
andhraṇaṃ randhanaṃ caiva saṃśliṣṭaṃ sandhibandhanam |
<mūṣārthe śreṣṭhā mṛt>
mṛttikā pāṇḍurasthūlaśoṇapāṇḍuramūṣarā || 1,26.152 ||
cirādhmānasahā sā hi mūṣārthamatiśasyate |
tadabhāve ca vālmīkī kaulālī vā samīryate || 1,26.153 ||
yā mṛttikā dagdhatuṣaiḥ śaṇena śikhitrakairvā hayaladdinā ca |
lohena daṇḍena ca kuṭṭitā sā sādhāraṇā syātkhalu mūṣikārtham || 1,26.154 ||
śvetāśmānastuṣā dagdhāḥ śikhitrāḥ śaṇakarpaṭam |
laddikiṭṭaṃ yathāyogaṃ saṃyojyā mūṣikāmṛdi || 1,26.155 ||
<vajramūṣā>
mṛdastribhāgaṃ śaṇaladdibhāgau nāgaśca nirdagdhatuṣopalādeḥ |
kiṭṭārdhabhāgaṃ parikhaṇḍya vajramūṣāṃ vidadhyātkhalu sattvapāte || 1,26.156 ||
<yogamūṣā>
dagdhāṅgāratuṣopetamṛtsnā valmīkasambhavā |
tattadbiḍasamāyuktā tattadbiḍavilepitā || 1,26.157 ||
tayā yā vihitā mūṣā yogamūṣeti kathyate |
anayā sādhitaḥ sūto jāyate guṇavattaraḥ || 1,26.158 ||
<vajradrāvaṇamūṣā>
gārabhūnāgadhautābhyāṃ śaṇairdagdhatuṣairapi |
samaiḥ samā ca mūṣāmṛnmahiṣīdugdhasaṃyutā || 1,26.159 ||
krauñcikā vakṣyamāṇā hi bahudhā parikīrtitā |
tayā viracitā mūṣā vajradrāvaṇake hitā || 1,26.160 ||
yāmayugmaparidhmānānnāsau dravati vahninā |
<gāramūṣā>
dagdhaṣaḍguṇagārāḍhyā kiṭṭāṅgāraśaṇānvitā || 1,26.161 ||
kṛṣṇamṛdbhiḥ kṛtā mūṣā gāramūṣetyudāhṛtā |
<vajramūṣā>
vastrāṅgāratuṣāstulyāstaccaturguṇamṛttikā || 1,26.162 ||
gārāśca mṛttikā tulyā sarvairetairvinirmitā |
vajramūṣeti nirdiṣṭā yāmamagniṃ saheta sā || 1,26.163 ||
<svarṇa(vara)mūṣā>
raktavargarajoyuktā raktavargāmbusādhitā |
varamūṣeti nirdiṣṭā svarṇamūṣetyudāhṛtā || 1,26.164 ||
<varṇamūṣā>
mṛṇmayā sādhitā mūṣā kṣitikhecaralepitā |
varṇamūṣeti sā proktā varṇotkarṣe niyujyate || 1,26.165 ||
<rūpyamūṣā>
evaṃ hi śvetavargeṇa rūpyamūṣā samīritā |
<biḍamūṣā>
tattadbiḍamṛdodbhūtā tattadbiḍavilepitā || 1,26.166 ||
dehalohārthayogārthaṃ biḍamūṣetyudāhṛtā |
sahate'gniṃ caturyāmaṃ draveṇa vyathitā satī || 1,26.167 ||
<mūṣāpyāyanam>
dravībhāvamupeyośca mūṣāyāṃ dhmānayogataḥ |
kṣaṇamuddharaṇaṃ yattanmūṣāpyāyanamucyate || 1,26.168 ||
<vṛntākamūṣā>
vṛntākākāramūṣāyāṃ nālaṃ dvādaśakāṅgulam |
dhuttūrapuṣpavaccordhvaṃ sudṛḍhaṃ śliṣṭasandhikam || 1,26.169 ||
aṣṭāṅgulaṃ ca sacchidraṃ sā syādvṛntākamūṣikā |
anayā kharparādīnāṃ mṛdūnāṃ sattvamāharet || 1,26.170 ||
<gostanī mūṣā>
mūṣā yā gostanākārā śikhāyuktapidhānakā |
sattvānāṃ drāvaṇe śuddhau mūṣā sā gostanī bhavet || 1,26.171 ||
<mallamūṣā>
nirdiṣṭā mallamūṣā yā malladvitayasampuṭāt |
parpaṭyādirasādīnāṃ svedanāya prakīrtitā || 1,26.172 ||
<pakvamūṣā>
kulālabhāṇḍarūpā yā dṛḍhā ca paripācitā |
pakvamūṣeti sā proktā poṭṭalyādivipācane || 1,26.173 ||
<golamūṣā>
nirvaktrā golakākārā puṭanadravyagarbhiṇī |
golamūṣeti sā proktā gatvaradravyarodhinī || 1,26.174 ||
<mañjumūṣā>
tale yā kūrparākārā kramādupari vistṛtā |
sthūlavṛntākavatsthūlā mañjumūṣeti saṃsmṛtā || 1,26.175 ||
sā cāyo'bhrakasatvādeḥ puṭāya drāvaṇāya ca |
<mañjūṣāmūṣā>
mañjūṣākāramūṣā yā nimnatāyāmavistarā || 1,26.176 ||
ṣaḍaṅgulapramāṇena mūṣā mañjūṣāsaṃjñakā |
bhūmau nikhanya tāṃ mūṣāṃ dadyātpuṭamathopari || 1,26.177 ||
<musalamūṣā>
mūṣā yā cipiṭā mūle vartulāṣṭāṅgulocchrayā |
mūṣā sā musalākhyā ca cakribaddharase tathā || 1,26.178 ||
<anyā vajramūṣā>
dagdhāṅgārasya ṣaḍbhāgā gairikaṃ kṛṣṇamṛttikā |
gāramaṅgārakiṭṭaṃ ca vajramūṣā prakīrtitā || 1,26.179 ||
gārā dagdhāstuṣā dagdhā valmīkamṛttikā |
śaṇatvakca samāyuktā mūṣā vajropamā matā || 1,26.180 ||
<andhamūṣā prakāśamūṣā ca>
prakāśā cāndhramūṣā ca mūṣā ca dvividhā smṛtā |
prakāśamūṣā vijñeyā śarāvākārasaṃyutā || 1,26.181 ||
dravyanirvahaṇe sā ca vārtikaistu praśasyate |
andhramūṣā ca kartavyā gostanākārasannibhā || 1,26.182 ||
pidhānena samāyuktā kiṃcidunnatamastakā |
patralepe tathā raṅge dvandvamelāpake hitam || 1,26.183 ||
saiva chidrānvitā nandagambhīrā sāraṇocitā |
<bhasmamūṣā>
tilabhasma dviraṃśaṃ tu iṣṭakāṃśasamanvitam || 1,26.184 ||
bhasmamūṣeti vijñeyā tārasaṃśodhane hitā |
<anyā vajramūṣā>
tuṣaṃ vastraṃ samaṃ dagdhaṃ mṛttikā caturaṃśikā || 1,26.185 ||
kūpīpāṣāṇasaṃyuktā vajramūṣā prakīrtitā |
<mūṣāvaṅkanālakoṣṭhikopayuktamṛllakṣaṇam>
kṛṣṇā raktā ca pītā ca śuklavarṇā ca mṛttikā || 1,26.186 ||
ādyā śreṣṭhā kaniṣṭhāntyā madhyame madhyame mate |
gajāśvānāṃ malaṃ dagdhvā yāvatkṛṣṇatvatāṃ gatam || 1,26.187 ||
vāśā vajralatā patraṃ valmīkasya mṛdā saha |
peṣayedvajratoyena yāvacchuklatvatāṃ gatam || 1,26.188 ||
mardayettena badhnīyādvaṅkanālaṃ ca koṣṭhikām |
valmīkamṛttikālohakiṭṭaśvetāśmanāṃ pṛthak || 1,26.189 ||
ekāṃśau dvau tu dagdhasya tuṣasya strīśiroruhām |
samāṃśastatsamastaṃ tu chāgīdugdhena mardayet || 1,26.190 ||
yāmadvayaṃ dṛḍhaṃ tena kuryānmūṣāṃ ca sampuṭam |
śoṣayitvā rasaṃ kṣiptvā tatra kaṃsaṃ nirodhayet || 1,26.191 ||
vajramūṣādikaṃ proktaṃ samyaksūtasya māraṇe |
mokṣakṣārasya bhāgau dvāviṣṭikakāṃśasaṃyutau || 1,26.192 ||
yatkṛtau sā tu mūṣā syāduttamā tāraśodhane |
raktavargeṇa saṃmiśrā raktavargapariplutā || 1,26.193 ||
raktavargakṛtālepā samuktā svarṇakarmasu |
śuklavargeṇa saṃmiśrā śuklavargapariplutā || 1,26.194 ||
śuklavargakṛtālepā śuklaśuddhiṣu śasyate |
viḍvargeṇa tu saṃmiśrā viḍvargeṇa pariplutā || 1,26.195 ||
viḍvargeṇa kṛtālepā mūṣā syāddrutijāraṇe |
kṣāravargeṇa saṃmiśrā kṣāravargapariplutā || 1,26.196 ||
kṣāravargakṛtālepā mūṣā nirvahaṇe hitā |
<mūṣāyāmāvartanavidhiḥ; varṇapuṭa (!)>
viṣaṭaṅkaṇaguñjābhirmūṣālepaṃ tu kārayet || 1,26.197 ||
prakāśāyāṃ prakurvīta yadi vāṅgāralepanam |
tasyāṃ vinyasya mūṣāyāṃ dravyamāvartayedbudhaḥ || 1,26.198 ||
lepavarṇe puṭe yojyā raktamṛtpaṭubhūkhagāḥ |
<āvartitadravyasvarūpam>
āvartamāne kanake pītā tāre sitaprabhā || 1,26.199 ||
śulbe jalanibhā tīkṣṇe śuklavarṇā praśasyate |
yantramevauṣadhībhyaḥ syācchreṣṭhaṃ sūtasya yantraṇe || 1,26.200 ||
oṣadhīsahite'pyeṣāṃ raso yantreṇa badhyate |
<koṣṭhī>
sattvānāṃ pātanārthāya patitānāṃ viśuddhaye || 1,26.201 ||
koṣṭhikā vividhākārāstāsāṃ lakṣaṇamucyate |
<ākarakoṣṭhī aṅgārakoṣṭhī vā>
rājahastasamutsedhā tadardhāyāmavistarā || 1,26.202 ||
caturaśrā ca kuḍyena veṣṭitā mṛṇmayena sā |
ekabhittau careddvāraṃ vitastyābhogasaṃyutam || 1,26.203 ||
dvāraṃ sārdhavitastyā ca saṃmitaṃ sudṛḍhaṃ śubham |
dohalyadho vidhātavyaṃ dhamanāya yathocitam || 1,26.204 ||
prādeśapramitā bhittiruttarāṅgasya cordhvataḥ |
dvāraṃ copari kartavyaṃ prādeśapramitaṃ khalu || 1,26.205 ||
tataśceṣṭikayā ruddhvā dvārasandhiṃ vilipya ca |
śikhitraistāṃ samāpūrya dhamedbhastrādvayena ca || 1,26.206 ||
śikhitrādhamanadravyamūrdhvadvāreṇa nikṣipet |
sattvapātanagolāṃśca pañca pañca punaḥ punaḥ || 1,26.207 ||
bhavedākarakoṣṭhīyaṃ kharāṇāṃ sattvapātane |
<pātālakoṣṭhī>
dṛḍhabhūmau caredgartaṃ vitastyā saṃmitaṃ śubham || 1,26.208 ||
vartulaṃ cātha tanmadhye gartamanyaṃ prakalpayet |
caturaṅgulavistāranimnatvena samanvitam || 1,26.209 ||
gartāddharaṇiparyantaṃ tiryagdalasamanvitam |
kiṃcitsamunnataṃ bāhyagartābhimukhanimnakam || 1,26.210 ||
mṛccakrīṃ pañcarandhrāḍhyāṃ garbhagartopari kṣipet |
āpūrya kokilaiḥ koṣṭhīṃ pradhamedekabhastrayā || 1,26.211 ||
pātālakoṣṭhikā hyeṣā mṛdūnāṃ sattvapātane |
dhmānasādhyapadārthānāṃ nandinā parikīrtitā || 1,26.212 ||
<gārakoṣṭhī>
dvādaśāṅgulanimnā yā prādeśapramitā tathā |
caturaṅgulataścordhvaṃ valayena samanvitā || 1,26.213 ||
bhūricchidravatīṃ cakrīṃ valayopari nikṣipet |
śikhitrāṃstatra nikṣipya pradhamedvaṅkanālataḥ || 1,26.214 ||
<vaṅkanālam>
mūṣāmṛdbhiḥ prakartavyamaratnipramitaṃ dṛḍham |
adhomukhaṃ ca tadvaktre nālaṃ pañcāṅgulaṃ khalu || 1,26.215 ||
vaṅkanālamiti proktaṃ dṛḍhādhmānāya kīrtitam |
<gārakoṣṭhī (cont.!)>
gāragoṣṭhīyamādiṣṭā sṛṣṭalohavināśinī || 1,26.216 ||
koṣṭhī bandharasādīnāṃ vidhānāya vidhīyate |
dvādaśāṅgulakotsedhā sā budhnā caturaṅgulā || 1,26.217 ||
tiryakpradhamanā yā sā mṛdudravyaviśodhanī |
rasādidravyapākānāṃ pramāṇajñāpanaṃ puṭam || 1,26.218 ||
neṣṭo nyūnādhikaḥ pākaḥ supākaṃ hitamauṣadham |
lohāderapunarbhāvo guṇādhikyaṃ tathogratā || 1,26.219 ||
anapsu majjanaṃ rekhāpūrṇatā puṭato bhavet |
puṭādrāgo laghutvaṃ ca śīghraṃ vyāptiśca dīpanam || 1,26.220 ||
jāritādapi sūtendrāllohānāmadhiko guṇaḥ |
yathāśmani viśedvahnirbahiḥsthaḥ puṭayogataḥ || 1,26.221 ||
cūrṇatvāddhi guṇāvāptistathā loheṣu niścitam |
pācyamānauṣadhaṃ kṣiptvā śarāvadvayasampuṭe || 1,26.222 ||
ruddhvā garuṇḍapacanaṃ puṭaṃ taditi kathyate |
<mahāpuṭam>
nimnavistarataḥ kuṇḍe dvihaste caturaśrake || 1,26.223 ||
vanotpalasahasreṇa pūrite puṭanauṣadham |
koṣṭhyāṃ ruddhaṃ prayatnena piṣṭikopari nikṣipet || 1,26.224 ||
vanotpalasahasrārdhaṃ kovikopari nikṣipet |
vahniṃ prajvālayettatra mahāpuṭamidaṃ smṛtam || 1,26.225 ||
<gajapuṭam>
rājahastapramāṇena caturaśraṃ ca nimnakam |
pūrṇaṃ copalaśāṭhībhiḥ kaṇṭhāvadhyatha vinyaset || 1,26.226 ||
vinyasetkumudīṃ tatra puṭanadravyapūritām |
pūrvacchagaṇato'rdhāni garuṇḍāni vinikṣipet || 1,26.227 ||
etadgajapuṭaṃ proktaṃ mahāguṇavidhāyakam |
<vārāhapuṭam>
itthaṃ cāratnike kuṇḍe puṭaṃ vārāhamucyate || 1,26.228 ||
<kukkuṭapuṭam>
puṭaṃ bhūmitale yattadvitastidvitayocchrayam |
tāvacca talavistīrṇaṃ tatsyātkukkuṭakaṃ puṭam || 1,26.229 ||
<kapotapuṭam>
yatpuṭaṃ dīyate bhūmāvaṣṭasaṃkhyairvanotpalaiḥ |
tadbālasūtabhasmārthaṃ kapotapuṭamucyate || 1,26.230 ||
<gorvarapuṭam>
goṣṭhāntargokhurakṣuṇṇaṃ śuṣkaṃ cūrṇitagomayam |
gorvaraṃ tatsamādiṣṭaṃ variṣṭhaṃ rasasādhane || 1,26.231 ||
<govarapuṭa>
gorvarairvā tuṣairvāpi puṭaṃ yatra pradīyate |
tadgorvarapuṭaṃ proktaṃ rasabhasmaprasiddhaye || 1,26.232 ||
<bhāṇḍapuṭam>
sthūlabhāṇḍe tuṣāpūrṇe madhye mūṣāsamanvite |
vahninā vihite pāke tadbhāṇḍapuṭamucyate || 1,26.233 ||
<vālukāpuṭam>
adhastādupariṣṭācca kovikā chādyate khalu |
vālukābhiḥ prataptābhiryantraṃ tadvālukāpuṭam || 1,26.234 ||
<bhūdharapuṭam>
vahnimatyāṃ kṣitau samyaṅnikhanyāddvyaṅgulādadhaḥ |
upariṣṭātpuṭaṃ yantraṃ puṭaṃ tadbhūdharāhvayam || 1,26.235 ||
<lāvakapuṭam>
ūrdhvaṃ ṣoḍaśikāmātraistuṣairvā gorvaraiḥ puṭam |
yantraṃ tallāvakākhyaṃ syānmṛdudravyasusādhane || 1,26.236 ||
anuktapuṭamāne tu sādhyadravyabalābalāt |
puṭaṃ vijñāya dātavyamūhāpohavicakṣaṇaiḥ || 1,26.237 ||
<kūpikādisvarūpam>
kācāyomṛdghaṭīnāṃ ca kūpikā caṣakāṇi ca |
rūpikā kūpikā siddhā golaṃ caiva karaṇḍakam || 1,26.238 ||
caṣakaṃ ca kaṭhorī ca vāṭikā khorikā tathā |
kañcolī grāhikā ceti nāmānyekārthakāni hi || 1,26.239 ||
rasoparasalohānāṃ tridhā saṃskāravahnayaḥ |
garuṇḍakāṣṭhakolīśasādhanāste pṛthaktridhā || 1,26.240 ||
garuṇḍasādhanāsteṣu puṭaṃ kukkuṭasaṃjñakam |
varāhapuṭasaṃjñaṃ hi gajasaṃjñaṃ puṭe bhavet || 1,26.241 ||
śreṣṭhā vanodbhavacchāṇā madhyamā goṣṭhasambhavāḥ |
adhamā kṛtrimaṃ kāṣṭhaṃ khadirāsanasambhavam || 1,26.242 ||
athavā sāravṛkṣotthaṃ vitastidvayadīrghakam |
sthūlaprakoṣṭhamātraṃ tu śreṣṭhaṃ sūtendrapācane || 1,26.243 ||
mṛdumadhyamacaṇḍāgnisaṃjñaṃ syāddārusādhanam |
aṅgārāḥ khadirodbhūtāstriphalāvṛkṣasambhavāḥ || 1,26.244 ||
karṣāḥ sāratarūdbhūtāḥ śreṣṭhā dhamanakarmaṇi || 1,26.245 ||


āk, 2, 1
<uparasaśodhanamāraṇavidhiḥ>
śrībhairavī |
devadeva cidānanda saccidānandadāyaka |
tvayaiva pratibuddhāsmi pūrvaṃ rasavidhānakam || 2,1.1 ||
idānīṃ tvatprasādena śrotumicchāmyahaṃ prabho |
gandhādyuparasānāṃ ca lohānāṃ hemapūrviṇām || 2,1.2 ||
padmarāgādiratnānāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ jātimāhvayam |
saṃskāraṃ ca guṇānbrūhi yathā jānāmyahaṃ prabho || 2,1.3 ||
<uparasāḥ>
śrībhairavaḥ |
gandhatālaśilātāpyaghanahiṅgulagairikāḥ |
capalāśmajabhūnāgaharidrāśmāgnijārakāḥ || 2,1.4 ||
kharparītutthakaṅkuṣṭhagirisindūraṭaṅkaṇāḥ |
kampillaviṣakāsīsagaurīpāṣāṇabhūkhagāḥ || 2,1.5 ||
poddāraśṛṅgī sindūrastuvariśca rasāñjanam |
nīlāñjanaṃ ca sauvīraṃ srotoñjanamaphenakam || 2,1.6 ||
puṣpāñjanaṃ śaṅkhaśuktiśambūkāśca varāṭakāḥ |
sābuṇī ca navakṣāracīnakṣārākhumārakāḥ || 2,1.7 ||
sarjaguggululākṣāśca kṣārāśca lavaṇāni ca |
gorocano'mlavetaśca kācacchagaṇavālukāḥ || 2,1.8 ||
ete uparasāḥ khyātā rasarājasya karmaṇi |
<dvādaśa lohāḥ>
svarṇarūpyārkakāntābhrasattvaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ ca muṇḍakam || 2,1.9 ||
bhujaṅgaṃ trapusaṃ caiva rītiḥ kāṃsyaṃ ca vartakam |
dvādaśaitāni lohāni maṇḍūro lohakiṭṭakam || 2,1.10 ||
<navaratnāni>
māṇikyamuktāphalavidrumāṇi tārkṣyaṃ ca puṣyaṃ bhiduraṃ ca nīlam |
gomedhakaṃ cātha viḍūrakaṃ ca krameṇa ratnāni navagrahāṇām || 2,1.11 ||
<nava uparatnāni>
sūryakāntaścandrakāntastārakāntastu kāntakaḥ |
vaikrāntaśca nṛpāvartaḥ sasyako vimalā tathā || 2,1.12 ||
perojaśca navaitāni hyuparatnāni nirdiśet |
<gandhakaḥ: gandhakaśuddhiḥ; prathamaḥ prakāraḥ>
utpattilakṣaṇaṃ jātiṃ gandhakaṃ śodhayedataḥ || 2,1.13 ||
sājyaṃ bhāṇḍe payaḥ kṣiptvā mukhaṃ vastreṇa bandhayet |
tatpṛṣṭhe cūrṇitaṃ gandhaṃ kṣiptvā śrāveṇa rodhayet || 2,1.14 ||
bhāṇḍaṃ nikṣipya bhūmyantarūrdhvaṃ deyaṃ puṭaṃ laghu |
tataḥ kṣīre drutaṃ gandhaṃ śuddhaṃ yogeṣu yojayet || 2,1.15 ||
<dvitīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
kaṅguṇīsarṣapairaṇḍatailaṃ vātha kusumbhajam |
meṣīkṣīraṃ ghṛtaṃ vātha gokṣīraṃ cāranālakam || 2,1.16 ||
eteṣvekaṃ tu bhāṇḍāntaḥ kiṃcidūnaṃ prapūrayet |
ruddhvā vastreṇa tadvaktraṃ gandhacūrṇaṃ tata upari || 2,1.17 ||
lohapātreṇa ruddhvātha pṛṣṭhe sthāpya ca kharparam |
sāgnimutpalakaiḥ pūrṇameva drāvya samuddharet || 2,1.18 ||
taddhuttūradravaiḥ piṣṭvā śuṣkaṃ drāvyaṃ ca pūrvavat |
punarevaṃ prakartavyaṃ suśuddho gandhako bhavet || 2,1.19 ||
<tṛtīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
yāmaikaṃ gandhakaṃ mardyaṃ bṛhatyā cājagandhayā |
bhṛṅgīdhuttūrayorvātha tilaparṇyāśca taddravaiḥ || 2,1.20 ||
tamādāya ghṛtaistulyairlohapātre kṣaṇaṃ pacet |
baddhvāgninā drutaṃ taṃ vai hyajākṣīre vinikṣipet || 2,1.21 ||
ityevaṃ saptadhā kuryācchuddhimāyāti gandhakaḥ |
<caturthaḥ prakāraḥ>
karañjairaṇḍatailaṃ ca chāgīdugdhaṃ ca bhāṇḍake || 2,1.22 ||
kṣiptvā tasya mukhaṃ baddhvā svacchavastreṇa buddhimān |
gandhakaṃ dhūrtajairdrāvairdinaṃ bhāvyaṃ viśoṣayet || 2,1.23 ||
taccūrṇaṃ pūrvabhāṇḍasya vastropari nidhāpayet |
ācchādya śrāvakenaiva pṛṣṭhe deyaṃ puṭaṃ laghu || 2,1.24 ||
drutaṃ gandhaṃ samādāya bhāvyaṃ dhuttūrajairdravaiḥ |
tadvaddrāvyaṃ punarbhāvyaṃ drāvayecca punastataḥ || 2,1.25 ||
ādāya matsyapittena saptadhā bhāvyamātape |
tataḥ kośātakībījacūrṇena saha peṣayet || 2,1.26 ||
bhāvayedbhṛṅgajairdrāvaiḥ saptāhamātape khare |
toyena kṣālitaṃ śoṣyaṃ tato mṛdvagninā kṣaṇam || 2,1.27 ||
ghṛtākte lohapātre tu drāvitaṃ ḍhālayettataḥ |
bhṛṅgarājadravāntasthaḥ samyakśuddhaḥ sa jāyate || 2,1.28 ||
<pañcamaḥ prakāraḥ>
gandhakaṃ yāmamātraṃ vā madyabrāhmyajagandhayoḥ |
bhṛṅgadhuttūrayorvāpi tilaparṇīrasena vā || 2,1.29 ||
tadādāya ghṛtopetaṃ lohapātre kṣaṇaṃ pacet |
laghvagninā drutaṃ tacca meṣīkṣīre vinikṣipet || 2,1.30 ||
evaṃ kṛtaṃ saptavāraṃ śuddhaṃ bhavati gandhakam |
<gandhakatailam; prathamaḥ prakāraḥ>
devadālyamlaparṇī vā nāraṅgaṃ vātha dāḍimam || 2,1.31 ||
mātuluṅgaṃ yathālābhaṃ dravamekasya cāharet |
gandhakasya tu pādāṃśaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ dravasaṃyutam || 2,1.32 ||
ātape tridinaṃ śuṣkaṃ dravaṃ deyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ |
dhuttūratulasīkṛṣṇalaśunaṃ devadālikā || 2,1.33 ||
śigrumūlaṃ kākamācī karpūraṃ śaṃkhinīdvayam |
kṛṣṇāgaru ca kastūrī vandhyā karkoṭakī samam || 2,1.34 ||
mātuluṅgarasaiḥ piṣṭvā kṣipederaṇḍatailake |
anena lohapātrasthaṃ bhāvayetpūrvagandhakam || 2,1.35 ||
trivāraṃ kṣaudratulyaṃ tajjāyate gandhavarjitam |
idaṃ gandhakatailaṃ syātsarvayogeṣu yojayet || 2,1.36 ||
<dvitīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
athavārkasnuhīkṣīrairvastraṃ lepyaṃ tu saptadhā |
gandhakaṃ navanītena piṣṭvā vastraṃ lipettu tām || 2,1.37 ||
tadvartiṃ jvalitāṃ daṃśe dhṛtāṃ kuryādadhomukhīm |
tailaṃ patedadho bhāṇḍe grāhyaṃ yogeṣu yojayet || 2,1.38 ||
<gandhakasattvapātanam; prathamaḥ prakāraḥ>
gandhakastu kuberākṣītailena ciramarditaḥ |
dṛḍhaṃ prakaṭamūṣāyāṃ dhmātaḥ sattvaṃ vimuñcati || 2,1.39 ||
<dvitīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
yadvā bhāṇḍodare kṣiptvā gandhakaṃ pūrvaśodhitam |
pidhāya tāmrapātreṇa tasmiñśītodakaṃ kṣipet || 2,1.40 ||
mandaṃ prajvālya tadadho vahnimuṣṇaṃ jalaṃ haret |
evaṃ punaḥ punaḥ śītajalamūrdhvaṃ vinikṣipet || 2,1.41 ||
gandhakasyāgnitaḥ sattvaṃ svarṇābhaṃ sarvakāryakṛt |
na cāsya sattvamādadyātsattvarūpo hi gandhakaḥ || 2,1.42 ||
iti gandhakatattvajñāḥ kecidanye pracakṣate |
<gandhakadrutiḥ>
yadvā saṃcūrṇitaṃ gandhaṃ kaṭutailasamanvitam || 2,1.43 ||
vastre nikṣipya tadvastraṃ kārayedvartikāṃ dṛḍhām |
tataḥ sūtreṇa saṃveṣṭya goghṛtena pariplutām || 2,1.44 ||
saṃdaṃśenoddhṛtaṃ kṛtvā vartiṃ cādhaḥ pradīpayet |
pakvāmraphalasambhūtarasavarṇā bhaveddrutiḥ || 2,1.45 ||
<gandhakasya guṇāḥ>
gandhakaḥ kaṭukaḥ pāke vīryoṣṇo vimalaḥ saraḥ |
visarpakuṣṭhakaṇḍūtikrimigulmakṣayāpahaḥ || 2,1.46 ||
akṣirogapraśamano vṛṣyo viṣagadārtijit |
sarvasiddhiprado balyastridoṣaghno rasāyanaḥ || 2,1.47 ||
<haritālam>
haritālaṃ ca godantī tālakaṃ naṭamaṇḍanam |
girijālalitaṃ pītamatigandhaṃ biḍālakam || 2,1.48 ||
<haritālabhedāḥ>
haritālaṃ dvidhā proktaṃ patrākhyaṃ piṇḍasaṃjñakam |
<pattratālaka:: phys. properties>
svarṇavarṇaṃ guru snigdhaṃ tanupattraṃ ca bhāsuram || 2,1.49 ||
tatpattratālakaṃ proktaṃ bahupatraṃ rasāyanam |
<piṇḍatālaka:: phys. properties>
niṣpatraṃ piṇḍasadṛśaṃ svalpasattvaṃ tathā guru || 2,1.50 ||
strīpuṣpaharaṇaṃ tattu guṇālpaṃ piṇḍatālakam |
<aśuddhaharitālasevane doṣāḥ>
vātaśleṣmapramehādikaramāyurnibarhaṇam || 2,1.51 ||
tāpasphoṭāṅgasaṃkocaṃ haritālamaśodhitam |
<haritālaśuddhiḥ; prathamaḥ prakāraḥ>
tālakaṃ kaṇaśaḥ kṛtvā daśāṃśena tu ṭaṅkaṇam || 2,1.52 ||
jambīrotthadravaiḥ kṣālyaṃ kāñjikaiḥ kṣālayetpunaḥ |
vastre caturguṇe baddhvā ḍolāyantre dinaṃ pacet || 2,1.53 ||
sacūrṇenāranālena dinaṃ kūṣmāṇḍajai rasaiḥ |
svedyaṃ vā śālmalītoyaistālakaṃ śuddhimāpnuyāt || 2,1.54 ||
<dvitīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
madhutulye ghanībhūte kaṣāye brahmamūlaje |
trivāraṃ tālakaṃ bhāvyaṃ piṣṭvā mūtre'tha māhiṣe || 2,1.55 ||
utpalairdaśabhirdeyaṃ puṭaṃ kuryātpunaḥ punaḥ |
evaṃ dvādaśadhā pācyaṃ śuddhaṃ yogeṣu yojayet || 2,1.56 ||
<tṛtīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
tālakaṃ poṭṭalaṃ baddhvā sacūrṇe kāñjike pacet |
dolāyantreṇa yāmaikaṃ tataḥ kūṣmāṇḍaje rase || 2,1.57 ||
tilataile pacedyāmaṃ yāmaṃ ca triphalājale |
evaṃ yantre caturyāmaṃ pācyaṃ śudhyati tālakam || 2,1.58 ||
<tālakasattvapātanam; prathamaḥ prakāraḥ>
lākṣā rājī tilāḥ śigru ṭaṅkaṇaṃ lavaṇaṃ guḍam |
tālakārghyeṇa saṃmardya chidramūṣāṃ nirodhayet || 2,1.59 ||
puṭetpātālayantreṇa sattvaṃ patati niścayam |
<dvitīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
tālakaṃ mardayeddugdhaiḥ sarpākṣyā vā kaṣāyakaiḥ || 2,1.60 ||
pūrvavajjanayetsattvaṃ chidramūṣānirodhitam |
<tṛtīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
lākṣā rājī guḍaṃ śigru ṭaṅkaṇaṃ lavaṇaṃ tilāḥ || 2,1.61 ||
ebhistulyaṃ sattvatālaṃ mardayedravidugdhakaiḥ |
dinaṃ vā vajriṇīdugdhaiḥ kūśmāṇḍasya dravaiśca vā || 2,1.62 ||
tena kalkena liptvāntaśchidramūṣāṃ nirodhayet |
puṭādvā dhamanātsattvaṃ grāhyaṃ pātālayantrake || 2,1.63 ||
<caturthaḥ prakāraḥ>
tālakādaṣṭamāṃśena deyaṃ sūtaṃ ca ṭaṅkaṇam |
kūśmāṇḍasya rasaiḥ snuhyāḥ kṣīrairmardyāddinatrayam || 2,1.64 ||
tadgolaṃ chidramūṣāyāṃ grāhyaṃ sattvaṃ ca pūrvavat |
<pañcamaḥ prakāraḥ>
bhāgāḥ ṣoḍaśa tālasya viṣapāradaṭaṅkaṇāḥ || 2,1.65 ||
śvetābhravaṅgayoścūrṇaṃ pratibhāgaṃ vimiśrayet |
samaṃ snuhyarkapayasā mardayeddivasadvayam || 2,1.66 ||
tadgolaṃ kācakupyantaḥ kṣiptvā tāṃ kācakūpikām |
sarvato'ṅgulamānena limpedvastre mṛdā dṛḍham || 2,1.67 ||
śuṣkāṃ tāṃ vālukāyantre śanairmandāgninā pacet |
śuṣke drave nirudhyātha samyaṅmṛllavaṇairmukham || 2,1.68 ||
caṇḍāgninā pacedyāvattāvaddvādaśayāmakam |
svāṅgaśītaṃ samuddhṛtya bhittvā kūpīṃ samāharet || 2,1.69 ||
ūrdhvalagnaṃ tālasattvaṃ sphaṭikopalasannibham |
<ṣaṣṭhaḥ prakāraḥ>
bhāvitaṃ snukpayaḥ siktaṃ snuhīdrāvairdvisaptadhā || 2,1.70 ||
tilasarṣapaśigrūṇi lavaṇaṃ mitrapañcakam |
ebhistulyaṃ śuddhatālaṃ dinamekaṃ vimardayet || 2,1.71 ||
bhūdhare chidramūṣāntardhmātaṃ sattvaṃ vimuñcati |
<tālakasya guṇāḥ>
tālakaṃ śodhitaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ kuṣṭhamṛtyujarāpaham || 2,1.72 ||
saubhāgyasaugandhyakaraṃ paramāyurvivardhanam |
<manaḥśilā>
manaḥśilā syātkunaṭī nāgāsyā raktagandhakaḥ || 2,1.73 ||
nepālikā nāgajihvā kalyāṇī saptanāmakā |
<manaḥśilābhedāḥ>
manaḥśilā tridhā proktā śyāmāṅgī kaṇavīrakā || 2,1.74 ||
khaṇḍākhyā caiva tadrūpaṃ vivicya parikalpyate |
śyāmā raktā kharāṅgā ca bhārāḍhyā śyāmikā matā || 2,1.75 ||
tejasvinī ca nirbhārā tāmrābhā kaṇavīrakā |
cūrṇībhūtātiraktāṅgī sabhārā khaṇḍapūrvikā || 2,1.76 ||
uttaroktā guṇaiḥ śreṣṭhā bhūrisattvā prakīrtitā |
<aśuddhamanaḥśilāsevāyāṃ doṣāḥ>
aśmarīṃ mūtrakṛcchraṃ ca mandāgniṃ malabaddhatām || 2,1.77 ||
karoti kuṣṭhaṃ tāpaṃ ca śuddhihīnā manaḥśilā |
<manaḥśilāśuddhiḥ>
ajāmūtraistryahaṃ pacyāddolāyantre manaḥśilām || 2,1.78 ||
saptadhā dadhyajāpittairgharme tāpyaṃ viśuddhaye |
jīvantībhṛṅgarāḍraktāgastyadrāvairmanaḥśilām || 2,1.79 ||
dolāyantre pacedyāmaṃ yāmaṃ chāgotthamūtrakaiḥ |
kṣālayedāranālena sarvayogeṣu yojayet || 2,1.80 ||
agastyasya rase bhāvyā saptāhācchodhitā śilā |
<manaḥśilāsattvapātanam; prathamaḥ prakāraḥ>
tālavacca śilāsattvaṃ pātanaṃ śodhanaṃ tathā || 2,1.81 ||
gomāṃsairmātuluṅgāmlairdinaṃ bhāvyā manaḥśilā |
tāṃ raktapītapuṣpāṇāṃ rasaiḥ pītaiśca bhāvayet || 2,1.82 ||
dinānte mardayedyāmaṃ mitrapañcakasaṃyutam |
gulikāṃ kācakūpyantaḥ kṣiptvā tāṃ kācakūpikām || 2,1.83 ||
sarvato'ṅgulamānena limpedvastramṛdā dṛḍham |
śuṣkāṃ tāṃ vālukāyantre śanairmandāgninā pacet || 2,1.84 ||
śuṣke drave nirudhyātha samyaṅmṛllavaṇairmukham |
caṇḍāgninā pacedyāvattāvaddvādaśayāmakam || 2,1.85 ||
svāṅgaśītaṃ samuddhṛtya bhittvā kūpīṃ samāharet |
ūrdhvalagnaṃ śilāsattvaṃ bālārkasadṛśopamam || 2,1.86 ||
<dvitīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
agastyaśigrujaistoyaistryahaṃ bhāvyā manaḥśilā |
tālakauṣadhayogena sattvaṃ hemanibhaṃ bhavet || 2,1.87 ||
<manaḥśilāyā guṇāḥ>
manaḥśilā kaṭūṣṇā ca satiktā kaphavātajit |
kaṇḍūtikṣayakāsaghnī viṣabhūtāgnimāndyahṛt || 2,1.88 ||
<mākṣikam>
mākṣikaṃ madhudhātu syānmākṣīkaṃ hemamākṣikam |
tāpyaṃ ca tāpijaṃ tārkṣyaṃ tāpīdeśasamudbhavam || 2,1.89 ||
<mākṣikabhedāḥ>
mākṣīkaṃ dvividhaṃ hemamākṣikaṃ tāramākṣikam |
tatrādyaṃ mākṣikaṃ kānyakubjotthaṃ svarṇasannibham || 2,1.90 ||
tapatītīrasambhūtaṃ pañcavarṇaṃ suvarṇavat |
pāṣāṇabahalaḥ proktastāpyākhyo'sau guṇātmakaḥ || 2,1.91 ||
suvarṇākārabhedācca pratyekaṃ tatpunastridhā |
kādambaṃ kāravellākhyaṃ taṇḍulīyakasaṃjñakam || 2,1.92 ||
<aśuddhamākṣikasevane doṣāḥ>
prāṇaviṣṭambhadaurbalyavahnisādākṣirogakṛt |
mākṣikaṃ mārayatyeva śuddhihīnaṃ surārcite || 2,1.93 ||
<mākṣikaśuddhiḥ; prathamaḥ prakāraḥ>
mākṣikaṃ naramūtreṇa kvāthaiḥ kaulutthakodbhavaiḥ |
vetasenāmlavargeṇa ṭaṅkaṇena kaṭutrikaiḥ || 2,1.94 ||
ḍolāyantre dinaṃ pācyaṃ sūraṇasyaiva madhyagam |
dinaṃ rambhādravaiḥ pacyāduddhṛtaṃ peṣayedghṛtaiḥ || 2,1.95 ||
eraṇḍatailasaṃyuktaiḥ puṭe pacyanviśodhayet |
<dvitīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
mākṣikasya trayo bhāgā bhāgaikaṃ ṭaṅkaṇasya ca || 2,1.96 ||
mātuluṅgadravairvātha jambīrotthadravaiḥ pacet |
lohapātre pacettāvadyāvatpātraṃ sulohitam || 2,1.97 ||
tāmravarṇamayo vāpi tāvacchudhyati mākṣikam |
<tṛtīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
agastipuṣpaniryāsaiḥ śigrumūlaṃ nigharṣayet || 2,1.98 ||
dravaiḥ pāṣāṇabhedyāśca peṣyamebhiśca mākṣikam |
tadvaṭīṃ cātha mūṣāyāṃ daśabhirutpalaiḥ puṭet || 2,1.99 ||
punaḥ punaśca piṣṭvātha puṭaiḥ ṣaḍbhirviśudhyati |
<caturthaḥ prakāraḥ>
kṣaudrakṣīrāranālāśca aṣṭabhāgāḥ pṛthakpṛthak || 2,1.100 ||
gavyaṃ takraṃ caturbhāgaṃ bhāgaḥ kaulutthajo rasaḥ |
sarvaṃ sampūrayedbhāṇḍe bhāgaikaṃ mākṣikaṃ pacet || 2,1.101 ||
ḍolāyantreṇa mṛdvagnau yāmamātraṃ punaḥ pacet |
uddhṛtya mātuluṅgāmlaiḥ piṣṭvā daśabhirutpalaiḥ || 2,1.102 ||
puṭe punaḥ punaḥ kuryādevaṃ dvādaśavāsaram |
śuddhaṃ bhavati mākṣīkaṃ sarvayogeṣu yojayet || 2,1.103 ||
<pañcamaḥ prakāraḥ>
mākṣikaṃ kaṇaśaḥ kṛtvā jālinīmeghanādayoḥ |
piṇḍe nikṣipya vipaceddolāyantre kulutthaje || 2,1.104 ||
kvāthe tacchuddhatāṃ yāti praśastaṃ lohamāraṇe |
<ṣaṣṭhaḥ prakāraḥ>
taile takre gavāṃ mūtre kaulutthe vāmlakāñjike || 2,1.105 ||
mākṣikaṃ śodhayetprājño giridoṣanivṛttaye |
<saptamaḥ prakāraḥ>
kulutthakodravakvāthanaramūtrāmlavetasaiḥ || 2,1.106 ||
ṭaṅkaṇenāmlavargeṇa kaṭukatritayena vā |
ḍolāyantre dinaṃ pācyaṃ sūraṇakandamadhyagam || 2,1.107 ||
dinaṃ rambhādravaiḥ pācyaṃ dhmātamuddhṛtya peṣitam |
eraṇḍatailasarpirbhyāṃ puṭaiḥ śudhyati mākṣikam || 2,1.108 ||
<aṣṭamaḥ prakāraḥ>
suvarṇavarṇavimalaṃ tāpyaṃ vā kaṇaśaḥ kṛtam |
punarnavāyāḥ kalkasthaṃ kaulutthe svedayedrase || 2,1.109 ||
saindhavairbījapūrāktairyuktaṃ vā poṭṭalīkṛtam |
ḍolāyantre dinaṃ svedyaṃ śuddhimāyāti niścitam || 2,1.110 ||
<mākṣikasattvapātanavidhiḥ; prathamaḥ prakāraḥ>
godugdhaiśca snuhīkṣīrairbhāvyameraṇḍatailakaiḥ |
mākṣikaṃ dinamekaṃ tu marditaṃ vaṭakīkṛtam || 2,1.111 ||
abhravaddhamane satvaṃ sasyakasyāpyayaṃ vidhiḥ |
<dvitīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
dolāyantre sāranāle mākṣikaṃ svedayeddinam || 2,1.112 ||
cūrṇitaṃ madhusarpirbhyāṃ lohapātre paceddinam |
ādāya bhāvayedgharme vajrīkṣīrairdināvadhi || 2,1.113 ||
gṛhadhūmairghṛtaiḥ kṣaudraiḥ saṃyuktairmardayeddinam |
andhamūṣāgataṃ dhmātaṃ sattvaṃ guñjānibhaṃ bhavet || 2,1.114 ||
<tṛtīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
kadalīkandatoyena mākṣikaṃ śatadhātape |
bhāvitaṃ pācayedyāmaṃ sājyairvātāritailakaiḥ || 2,1.115 ||
pūrvavaddhamanātsattvamindragopasamaṃ bhavet |
<caturthaḥ prakāraḥ>
snuhyarkapayasā stanyairmākṣikaṃ mardayeddinam || 2,1.116 ||
kaṅkuṣṭhaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ caiva pratipādāṃśamiśritam |
mūkamūṣāgataṃ dhmātaṃ satvaṃ maṇinibhaṃ bhavet || 2,1.117 ||
<pañcamaḥ prakāraḥ>
kadalīkandatulasījambīrāṇāṃ dravaiḥ kramāt |
bhāvayenmākṣikaṃ ślakṣṇaṃ pratidrāveṇa saptadhā || 2,1.118 ||
ruddhvā dhmāte patetsattvaṃ śukatuṇḍanibhaṃ śubham |
<ṣaṣṭhaḥ prakāraḥ>
mūtravargāmlavargābhyāṃ dvisaptāhaṃ vibhāvayet || 2,1.119 ||
mākṣīkaṃ tīvragharmeṇa dinamamlaiśca mardayet |
mitrapañcakasaṃyuktaṃ vaṭīkṛtya dhameddṛḍham || 2,1.120 ||
vyomavadvaṅkanālena sattvaṃ śulbanibhaṃ bhavet |
<saptamaḥ prakāraḥ>
snuhīkṣīrairgavāṃ kṣīrairbhāvyameraṇḍatailakaiḥ || 2,1.121 ||
mākṣikaṃ pañcamitrāktaṃ saptāhānte vaṭīkṛtam |
pūrvavadvidhamettena sattvaṃ lākṣānibhaṃ bhavet || 2,1.122 ||
<aṣṭamaḥ prakāraḥ>
stanyaiḥ kaṅkuṣṭhakaiścaiva kadalītoyasaṃyutaiḥ |
mitrapañcakasaṃyuktairmākṣikaṃ dinasaptakam || 2,1.123 ||
bhāvitaṃ mardayedyāmaṃ dinaṃ vātāritailakaiḥ |
mṛdvagninā pacettāvadyāvaddravati golakam || 2,1.124 ||
sattvaṃ kiṃśukapuṣpābhaṃ vyomavaddhamanādbhavet |
vimalānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ sasyakasyāpyayaṃ vidhiḥ || 2,1.125 ||
<navamaḥ prakāraḥ>
saṃcūrṇya mākṣikaṃ śuddhaṃ mardyamamlena kenacit |
kṣālayedāranālena hyadhaḥsthaṃ svarṇacūrṇavat || 2,1.126 ||
jārayettatsamāhṛtya dhametsattvaṃ vimuñcati |
yojayedvāpane cedaṃ bījānāṃ yatra yatra vai || 2,1.127 ||
<daśamaḥ prakāraḥ>
kadalīpatrajairnīrairmākṣikaṃ bhāvayeddrutam |
gandharvatailamadhvājyaiḥ pakvamekadinaṃ tataḥ || 2,1.128 ||
tattāpyaṃ vajramūṣāyāṃ pakvāyāṃ nikṣipettataḥ |
lohasaṃdhānakaraṇaṃ tatsamaṃ tatra nikṣipet || 2,1.129 ||
dṛḍhaṃ pramūkamūṣāyāṃ koṣṭhikāyāṃ niveśayet |
aṅgāraiḥ khadirodbhūtairdhamedbhastrādvayena vai || 2,1.130 ||
vaṅkanālayujā sattvaṃ tāpyasya patati dhruvam |
<ekādaśaḥ prakāraḥ>
māṇivandhyaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ ca gṛhṇīyātsamabhāgataḥ || 2,1.131 ||
phalapūrarasaiḥ piṣṭvā sampuṭe sudṛḍhaṃ kṣipet |
samyagliptvā vanodbhūtaiśchāṇakaiḥ puṭayettataḥ || 2,1.132 ||
kukuptotthaṃ tadākṛṣyaṃ svāṃgaśītaṃ pramardayet |
phalapūrarasaiḥ pakvairmardayitvātha pūrvavat || 2,1.133 ||
puṭanaṃ chagaṇenaiva tāvatkuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ |
yāvattatpuṭitaṃ kalkaṃ phalapūrāmlamarditam || 2,1.134 ||
śāṇottejitanistriṃśaliptaṃ tattāmratāmiyāt |
itthaṃ śuddhaṃ ca garuḍaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ nīrajaṃ rasam || 2,1.135 ||
ghṛtena madhunā yuktaṃ saguḍaṃ guñjayā samam |
prāgvanmūṣāgataṃ kṛtvā dhamettatkhadirāgninā || 2,1.136 ||
mṛdusattvaṃ nāgasamamindragopakasannibham |
patatyeva na saṃdehaḥ sarvajñavacanaṃ tathā || 2,1.137 ||
śukladīptiraśabdaśca yadā vaiśvānaro bhavet |
tadā tu sattvaṃ patitaṃ jānīyānnānyathā kvacit || 2,1.138 ||
<mākṣikasya guṇāḥ>
kaṣāyatiktamadhuraṃ kaṭukaṃ mākṣikadvayam |
uṣṇaṃ rasāyanaṃ kuṣṭhaśoṣahidhmāvamipraṇut || 2,1.139 ||
mākṣīkadhātuḥ sakalāmayaghnaḥ prāṇo rasendrasya paraṃ hi vṛṣyaḥ |
durmelalohadvayamelakaśca guṇottaraḥ pūrvarasāyanāgryaḥ || 2,1.140 ||
<abhrakam>
utpattyādi ghanasyādau kathitaṃ tadrasāyane |
adhunā sampravakṣyāmi tatkriyāstadguṇānapi || 2,1.141 ||
<abhrakabhasmavidhiḥ; prathamaḥ prakāraḥ>
dhānyābhrakaṃ meghanādadravaiḥ saṃmardayeddinam |
śatāṃśaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ dattvā tato gajapuṭe pacet || 2,1.142 ||
mustāsūraṇavarṣābhūrasairvyastaiḥ puṭaṃ tridhā |
kāsamardarasaiḥ pañca varāgomūtrakairapi || 2,1.143 ||
nyagrodhasya jaṭākvāthairmardyaṃ daśapuṭaṃ punaḥ |
ṣaṭca jambīranīreṇa gokṣīreṇa puṭatrayam || 2,1.144 ||
peṣaṇaṃ gharmapākaṃ ca sthālīpākaṃ puṭaṃ kramāt |
kuryāccatvāri karmāṇi ṭaṅkaṇaṃ ca puṭe puṭe || 2,1.145 ||
niścandraṃ jāyate hyabhraṃ sarvayogeṣu yojayet |
ayaṃ sāmānyasaṃskāro viśeṣastu nigadyate || 2,1.146 ||
<bhasmīkaraṇe viśiṣṭasaṃskāraḥ>
niścandrakaṃ mṛtavyoma sāmānyasaṃskṛtam |
tattadrogaharadravyakvāthaiḥ piṣṭvā puṭe pacet || 2,1.147 ||
yantraistriḥ saptadhā kuryāttattadrogaharaṃ bhavet |
ayaṃ viśeṣasaṃskārastattadrogaharo bhavet || 2,1.148 ||
pūrvāhne peṣaṇaṃ kāryaṃ madhyāhne gharmapācanam |
sāyāhne sthālikāpākaṃ kuryādrātrau puṭaṃ kramāt || 2,1.149 ||
saṃskāraḥ pañcadhā prokto ghanasya parameśvari |
dhānyābhrakaraṇaṃ sattvapātanaṃ nirmalakriyā || 2,1.150 ||
sumṛtīkaraṇaṃ caiva tvamṛtīkaraṇaṃ tathā |
māraṇe ghanasattvasya ghanapatrasya māraṇe || 2,1.151 ||
kramācchreṣṭhatamau proktau śastau daradaṭaṅkaṇau |
<dvitīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
mustākvāthena dhānyābhraṃ pañcaviṃśatpuṭe pacet || 2,1.152 ||
gomūtraiśca tathā kvāthaistriphalāyāḥ sureśvari |
kāsamardadravairekaṃ gokṣīreṇa puṭaṃ tridhā || 2,1.153 ||
niścandraṃ jāyate hyabhraṃ sarvayogeṣu yojayet |
viśeṣasaṃskārayutaṃ tattadrogaharaṃ bhavet || 2,1.154 ||
<tṛtīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
dhānyābhrakasya bhāgaikaṃ bhāgau dvau ṭaṅkaṇasya ca |
piṣṭvā tadandhamūṣāyāṃ ruddhvā tīvrāgninā dhamet || 2,1.155 ||
svabhāvaśītalaṃ cūrṇaṃ sarvayogeṣu yojayet |
viśeṣasaṃskārayutaṃ tattadrogaharaṃ bhavet || 2,1.156 ||
<caturthaḥ prakāraḥ>
dhānyābhraṃ mardayedamlairgharme saṃsthāpayettataḥ |
puṭaṃ kuryāttato'mlena secanaṃ mardanaṃ punaḥ || 2,1.157 ||
viṃśadvāraṃ tato dugdhe puṭanistabdhamabhrakam |
kṣipettaṃ mardayeddugdhairdugdhe kṣiptvātape nyaset || 2,1.158 ||
puṭaṃ triḥ saptavārāṇi kuryādevaṃ punaḥ punaḥ |
taṇḍulaṃ vajravallī ca tālamūlī punarnavā || 2,1.159 ||
śārṅgerī maricaṃ caiva balā ca payasā saha |
pūrvābhraṃ peṣayedetaiḥ pratyekaiśca tryahaṃ tryaham || 2,1.160 ||
kṣiptvātape puṭe pacyātpratyekena punaḥ punaḥ |
evaṃ niścandrakaṃ vyoma kajjalābhaṃ mṛtaṃ bhavet || 2,1.161 ||
<pañcamaḥ prakāraḥ>
dhānyābhrakasya śuṣkasya daśāṃśaṃ maricaṃ kṣipet |
peṣayedamlavargeṇa amlairbhāvyaṃ dinatrayam || 2,1.162 ||
tacchuṣkaṃ sampuṭe dhāmyaṃ khadirāṅgārakairdṛḍham |
ūrdhvapātre nivāryātha siñcedamlena kena tam || 2,1.163 ||
dhānyaṃ tatraiva ṣaḍvāramamlaiḥ siñcyātpunaḥ punaḥ |
agastyaśigruvarṣābhūmūlapatrabhavai rasaiḥ || 2,1.164 ||
piṣṭvābhraṃ secayettena ṣaḍdhā dhāmyaṃ ca secayet |
sitāmadhvājyagokṣīraistaddhautaṃ peṣyamabhrakam || 2,1.165 ||
ruddhvā ṣaḍbhiḥ puṭaiḥ pācyaṃ piṣṭvā caiva punaḥ punaḥ |
matsyākṣyāścaikavīrāyā dravaiḥ piṣṭvā tridhā pacet || 2,1.166 ||
evaṃ gajapuṭaiḥ pācyaṃ niścandramayate'bhrakam |
pattrābhrakasya sindūraṃ sarvayogeṣu yojayet || 2,1.167 ||
<ṣaṣṭhaḥ prakāraḥ>
dhānyābhraṃ mardayedyāmaṃ matsyākṣīsvarasaistataḥ |
pacedgajapuṭairevaṃ saptadhā tulasīrasaiḥ || 2,1.168 ||
kokilākṣarasaiḥ sapta kumārīsvarasaistathā |
śvetadūrvārasaistadvadvyāghrīkandarasaistathā || 2,1.169 ||
punarnavārasaiḥ sapta tadvatpañcāmṛtaistataḥ |
niścandraṃ jāyate hyabhraṃ sarvayogeṣu yojayet || 2,1.170 ||
<saptamaḥ prakāraḥ>
dhānyābhraṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ tulyaṃ gomūtraistulasīdravaiḥ |
vākucyā sūraṇairnālyā dinaṃ piṣṭvā puṭe pacet || 2,1.171 ||
dravaiḥ punaḥ punaḥ piṣṭvā dinānteṣu puṭe pacet || 2,1.172 ||
ruddhvā ruddhvā puṭaistvevaṃ niścandraṃ cābhrakaṃ bhavet |
<aṣṭamaḥ prakāraḥ>
piṣṭvā sāmlāranālena peṭālīmūlajatvacam || 2,1.173 ||
taddravairmardayedabhraṃ dinaṃ gajapuṭe pacet |
evaṃ saptapuṭaṃ kāryaṃ dadhnā ca puṭasaptakam || 2,1.174 ||
yavaciñcādravaistadvanniścandraṃ jāyate'bhrakam |
<navamaḥ prakāraḥ>
dhānyābhrakaṃ ravikṣīrai ravimūladravaiśca vā || 2,1.175 ||
mardyaṃ mardyaṃ puṭe pacyātsaptadhā mriyate dhruvam |
<daśamaḥ prakāraḥ>
dhānyābhrakaṃ tuṣāmlāntarātape sthāpayeddinam || 2,1.176 ||
yāmaṃ mardyaṃ tu tadgolaṃ ruddhvā gajapuṭe pacet |
evaṃ kārpāsatoyāntaḥ sthāpyaṃ pācyaṃ puṭe pacet || 2,1.177 ||
evaṃ gokṣīramadhyasthaṃ sthāpyaṃ peṣyaṃ puṭe pacet |
paścādamlaiśca gokṣīraiḥ kārpāsaiśca punaḥ punaḥ || 2,1.178 ||
gharmapākaṃ mardanaṃ ca sthālīpākaṃ puṭaṃ kramāt |
ekaviṃśatpuṭe prāpte niścandraṃ jāyate'bhrakam || 2,1.179 ||
sarveṣāṃ ghātitābhrāṇāmamṛtīkaraṇe vidhiḥ |
<abhrakasya guṇāḥ>
abhrakaṃ gaganaṃ bhṛṅgaṃ bahuputramumābhavam || 2,1.180 ||
patrābhrakasya sindūramamṛtaṃ paramaṃ hitam |
tridoṣaghnaṃ balakaraṃ vṛṣyamārogyadaṃ śuci || 2,1.181 ||
sarvarogaharaṃ saumyaṃ viṣaghnaṃ ca rasāyanam |
<cinnabar:: synonyms>
hiṅgulo daradaṃ cūrṇapāradaśca rasodbhavaḥ || 2,1.182 ||
rasagarbhaḥ suraṅgaśca lohaghnaḥ siddhipāradaḥ |
<cinnabar:: subtypes>
hiṅgulastrividho jñeyaścarmāraḥ śukatuṇḍakaḥ || 2,1.183 ||
haṃsapādaśca tatrādyaṃ tārakarmaṇi yojayet |
adhamaṃ taṃ vijānīyācchukatuṇḍaṃ ca madhyamam || 2,1.184 ||
hematārakriyāmārge yojayetparameśvari |
haṃsapādo mahāśreṣṭhaḥ sarvakarmakaro hi saḥ || 2,1.185 ||
tasya sattvaṃ sūta eva daradasya tribhedataḥ |
<śukatuṇḍa:: phys. properties>
hemakiṭṭasya sadṛśastadrūpastīkṣṇamārakaḥ || 2,1.186 ||
carmārastīkṣṇarūpaḥ syātsuprītaḥ śukatuṇḍakaḥ |
<haṃsapāda:: properties>
japākusumasaṅkāśo haṃsapādo mahottamaḥ || 2,1.187 ||
rasāyane sarvasūtaharaṇe sarvarañjane |
lohānāṃ māraṇe śreṣṭho varṇotkarṣaṇakarmaṇi || 2,1.188 ||
rasakasya tathā rāgabandhe dhaureyakaḥ smṛtaḥ |
maladoṣādikaṃ nāsti sarvakāryeṣu pūjyate || 2,1.189 ||
<hiṅgulaśuddhiḥ>
meṣīkṣīreṇa daradamamlavargaiśca bhāvitam |
saptavāraṃ prayatnena śuddhimāyāti niścitam || 2,1.190 ||
