<hiṅgulasattvapātanam>
daradaṃ pātanāyantre pātitaṃ ca jalāśaye |
sattvaṃ sūtakasaṅkāśaṃ vimuñcati na saṃśayaḥ || 2,1.191 ||
<hiṅgulasya guṇāḥ>
ye guṇāḥ pārade proktāste guṇāḥ santi hiṅgule |
navajvaraharaḥ proktaḥ sampātajvaranāśanaḥ || 2,1.192 ||
<gairikam>
gairikaṃ giridhātuḥ syādraktadhāturgavedhukam |
<gairika:: subtypes>
gairikaṃ dvividhaṃ proktaṃ tatrādyaṃ svarṇagairikam || 2,1.193 ||
pāṣāṇagairikaṃ cānyatpūrvaṃ śreṣṭhatamaṃ guṇaiḥ |
<gairikasya guṇāḥ>
śoṇitaṃ masṛṇaṃ snigdhaṃ svādu vṛṣyaṃ himaṃ laghu || 2,1.194 ||
cakṣuṣyaṃ raktapittaghnaṃ viṣahidhmāvamipraṇut |
tāpajvaraharaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ hemaghnaṃ raktagairikam || 2,1.195 ||
<pāṣāṇagairika:: phys. properties>
pāṣāṇagairikaṃ proktaṃ kaṭhinaṃ tāmravarṇakam |
<capalaḥ>
dehavedhī lohavedhī capalā rasabandhinī || 2,1.196 ||
capalā bahubhedā ca sarvalohasvarūpataḥ |
hemastāro ravimayaḥ sīsātmā vaṅgarūpadhṛk || 2,1.197 ||
tīkṣṇarūpaḥ kāṃsyarūpo rakto viṣamayastathā |
<capalasvarūpam>
sattvalohasvarūpāste viṣo haritalohabhāk || 2,1.198 ||
viṣākhyaṃ capalaṃ prāpya nirjīve tasya dāpayet |
puṭenāraṇyajaiśchāṇaiḥ sadhūmaṃ sarvathā tyajet || 2,1.199 ||
naśyeta tasya dhūmaṃ vai sparśanaṃ dūratastyajet |
aśanāttasya saṃsparśānmriyate sadya eva hi || 2,1.200 ||
dhūmāvalokanācchīrṣapīḍā saṃjāyate jvaraḥ |
evaṃ dattapuṭe śānte gṛhṇīyāccapalaṃ tataḥ || 2,1.201 ||
<capalasya guṇāḥ>
tasya saṃsparśamātreṇa hīrako mriyate kṣaṇāt |
sarveṣāṃ capalānāṃ vai svabhāvaḥ samudāhṛtaḥ || 2,1.202 ||
vaṅgastambhe nāgarāje krame vātīva śasyate |
sarvalohāni kurvanti suvarṇaṃ tārameva ca || 2,1.203 ||
yuktyātha śodhitaḥ sūte vinā bījaṃ ca vā tathā |
śataṃ vāpi sahasraṃ vā lakṣaṃ koṭiṃ ca vedhayet || 2,1.204 ||
vajreṇa rasarājena bījena ca samāśritā |
dhūmato vedhayellohān rasajīrṇaḥ svayaṃ galet || 2,1.205 ||
niṣpatya tena dehasya capalena mahātmanā |
<śilājatu>
aśmajaṃ girijaṃ śailamaśmalākṣā śilājatu || 2,1.206 ||
<śilājatu:: subtypes>
śilādhāturdvidhā prokto gomūtrādyo rasāyanam |
karpūrapūrvakaścānyastatrādyo dvividhaḥ smṛtaḥ || 2,1.207 ||
sasattvaścaiva niḥsattvastayoḥ pūrvo guṇādhikaḥ |
grīṣme tīvrārkataptebhyaḥ pādebhyo himabhūbhṛtaḥ || 2,1.208 ||
svarṇarūpyākagarbhebhyaḥ śilādhāturviniḥsaret |
<śilājatorguṇāḥ>
svarṇagarbhagirerjātaṃ japāpuṣpanibhaṃ guru || 2,1.209 ||
sasattvaṃ svādu paramaṃ paramaṃ tadrasāyanam |
rūpyagarbhagirerjātaṃ madhuraṃ pāṇḍuraṃ guru || 2,1.210 ||
śilājaṃ pāṇḍurogaghnaṃ viśeṣātpittarogajit |
tāmragarbhagirerjātaṃ nīlavarṇaṃ ghanaṃ guru || 2,1.211 ||
śilājaṃ kaphavātaghnaṃ tīkṣṇoṣṇaṃ dīpanaṃ param |
vahnau kṣiptaṃ bhavedyattalliṅgākāraṃ hyadhūmakam || 2,1.212 ||
salile'pyavalīnaṃ ca tatsiddhaṃ hi śilājatu |
gomūtragandhi kṛṣṇaṃ guggulukābhaṃ saśarkaraṃ mṛtsnam |
snigdhamanamlakaṣāyaṃ mṛdu guru ca śilājatu śreṣṭham || 2,1.213 ||
sarvaṃ ca tiktakaṭukaṃ svādu nātyuṣṇaśītalam || 2,1.214 ||
vṛṣyaṃ tridoṣajidbhedi cakṣuṣyaṃ ca rasāyanam |
kṣayaśophodarārśoghnaṃ mehamūtragrahāpaham || 2,1.215 ||
nūnaṃ sajvarapāṇḍuśophaśamanaṃ mehāgnimāndyāpahaṃ medaśchedakaraṃ ca yakṣmaśamanaṃ mūlāmayonmūlanam |
plīhavināśanaṃ jaṭharahṛcchūlaghnamāmāpahaṃ sarvatvaggadanāśanaṃ kimaparaṃ dehe ca lohe hitam || 2,1.216 ||
rasoparasasūtendraratnaloheṣu ye guṇāḥ |
vasanti te śilādhātau jarāmṛtyujigīṣayā || 2,1.217 ||
<bhūnāgaḥ>
bhūnāgaḥ kṣitināgaśca bhūlatā raktajantukaḥ |
kṣitijaḥ kṣitijantuśca viṣaghno raktatuṇḍakaḥ || 2,1.218 ||
yāsāṃ chede na raktaṃ prabhavati satataṃ prāyaśo raktabhūmau |
saṃgrāhyā bhūlatāstā viṣaharadhanadaṃ pātayettāsu sattvam |
tadyuktyā pāradendraścarati yadi samaṃ sāraṇāyantrayogaiḥ |
baddho'yaṃ koṭivedhī samaravirajate yojayedbhāskare vā || 2,1.219 ||
<bhūnāgasattvasya guṇāḥ>
sattvaṃ bhūnāgajaṃ kṣvelayakṣarākṣasamṛtyujit |
sarvavaśyakaraṃ sarvarogaghnaṃ ca rasāyanam || 2,1.220 ||
rasendre jāraṇākarmajāritaṃ koṭivedhakṛt |
suvarṇādīni lohāni raktāni grasati kṣaṇāt || 2,1.221 ||
<bhūnāgasattvapātanam; prathamaḥ prakāraḥ>
raktabhūjātabhūnāgamṛttikāṃ kṣālayejjalaiḥ |
yāvatsattvāvaśeṣaṃ syāddhautasattvaṃ tadeva hi || 2,1.222 ||
mitrapañcakayuktaṃ tanmūṣāyāṃ dhamayeddṛḍham |
nirbhidya mūṣāṃ tatsattvaṃ gṛhītvā kiṭṭakaṃ punaḥ || 2,1.223 ||
evaṃ trivāraṃ dhamanātsattvaśeṣaṃ samāharet |
<dvitīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
bhūnāgamṛttikāṃ bhāṇḍe bhūnāgaiḥ saha nikṣipet || 2,1.224 ||
ṣaṇmāsāntaṃ samāntaṃ vā jalaiḥ siñcenmuhurmuhuḥ |
asya sattvaṃ vidhānena gṛhṇīyādabhrasattvavat || 2,1.225 ||
<tṛtīyaḥ prakāraḥ>
raktabhūmijabhūnāgān pañjarasthena barhiṇā |
bhakṣayettu śaratkāle nityaṃ tanmalamāharet || 2,1.226 ||
lākṣāsarjarasaḥ sarjī guggulurmitrapañcakam |
ūrṇā kṣārāśca paṭavo nīlasarpendragopakau || 2,1.227 ||
matsyaśca nīlasaraṭaḥ kūrmapṛṣṭhaṃ śaśāsthi ca |
mākṣīkaṃ śikhiśaṃkhatutthaṃ ca sarvāṃśaṃ barhiṇo malam || 2,1.228 ||
sarvaṃ nikṣipya mūṣāyāṃ dhamettīvrāgninā dṛḍham |
nirbhidya mūṣāṃ tatsattvaṃ gṛhītvā kiṭṭakaṃ punaḥ || 2,1.229 ||
evaṃ trivāradhamanātsattvaśeṣaṃ samāharet |
<haridrāśmā>
haridrāśmā niśāgrāvaḥ pītāṅgaḥ pītakarṣaṇaḥ || 2,1.230 ||
tatsattvakaṃ sitaṃ svarṇaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ rasarasāyane |
<agnijāraḥ>
agnijāro'gniniryāso'pyagnigarbho'gnijaḥ smṛtaḥ || 2,1.231 ||
vaḍabāgnimalo jñeyo jarāyuścārṇavodbhavaḥ |
agnijvālo'gnijāraśca proktaḥ sindhuplavo daśa || 2,1.232 ||
sāmudrasyāgninakrasya jarāyurbahirujjhitaḥ |
saṃśuṣko bhānutāpena so'gnijāra iti smṛtaḥ || 2,1.233 ||
jārābhaṃ dahati sparśātpicchilaṃ sāgarotplavam |
jarāyustaccaturvarṇaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ teṣu salohitam || 2,1.234 ||
<agnijārasya guṇāḥ>
syādagnijāraḥ kaṭukoṣṇavīryaḥ tundāmayaghnaḥ kaphavātahārī |
pittapradaḥ śoṇitasannipātaśūlādivātāmayanāśakaśca || 2,1.235 ||
agnijārastridoṣaghno dhanurvātādivātanut |
mardano rasavīryasya dīpano jāraṇastathā || 2,1.236 ||
<kharparī (rasakam)>
kharparī rasakaṃ tutthakharparyamṛtasambhavā |
rasako dvividhaḥ prokto darduraḥ kāravellakaḥ || 2,1.237 ||
sadaṃśo darduraḥ prokto nirdaṃśaḥ kāravellakaḥ |
sattvapāte śubhaḥ pūrvo dvitīyaścauṣadhādiṣu || 2,1.238 ||
ghanasattvanibhaṃ bāhye śubhaṃ hāridravattataḥ |
acchaṃ kharparavattutthamuttamaṃ satodaram || 2,1.239 ||
<kharparyā guṇāḥ>
rasakaḥ sarvadoṣaghnaḥ kaphapittavināśanaḥ |
cakṣūrogakṣayaghnaśca lohapāradarañjanaḥ || 2,1.240 ||
nāgārjunena nirdiṣṭau rasasya rasakāvubhau |
śreṣṭhau siddharasau khyātau dehalohakarau parau || 2,1.241 ||
rasaśca rasakaścobhau yenāgnisahanau kṛtau |
dehalohamayīṃ siddhiṃ dāsyatastu na saṃśayaḥ || 2,1.242 ||
<kharparīsattvapātanam>
jayantītriphalācūrṇaṃ haridrāguḍaṭaṅkaṇam |
pādāṃśaṃ rasakasyedaṃ piṣṭvā mūṣāḥ pralepayet || 2,1.243 ||
nalikāsampuṭaṃ baddhvā śoṣayedātape khare |
grāhyaṃ pātālayantreṇa sattvaṃ dhmāte puṭe'tha vā || 2,1.244 ||
<kharparīśuddhiḥ>
rajasvalārajomūtrai rasakaṃ bhāvayeddinam |
taireva dinamekaṃ tu mardayecchuddhimāpnuyāt || 2,1.245 ||
<mayūratuttham>
mayūratutthaṃ tutthaṃ ca nīlāśmā tāmrabhasma ca |
mayūragrīvakaṃ kṣveḍanāśanaṃ sasyakaṃ priye || 2,1.246 ||
mayūragrīvasaṅkāśaṃ ghṛṣṭe gokṣīrasannibham |
apsu ca plavate kṣiptametanmāyūratutthakam || 2,1.247 ||
athavā śukapicchābhamantaḥ kāñcanabindubhiḥ |
aṅkitaṃ gharṣayettutthamāyase cāmlasaṃyute || 2,1.248 ||
bhavedayastāmranibhametanmāyūratutthakam |
<mayūratutthaśuddhiḥ>
viṣṭhayā mardayettutthaṃ mārjārakakapotayoḥ || 2,1.249 ||
daśāṃśaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ dattvā pācyaṃ mṛdupuṭe tataḥ |
puṭaṃ dadhnā puṭaṃ kṣaudrairdeyaṃ tutthaviśuddhaye || 2,1.250 ||
<mayūratutthasattvapātanam>
tutthaṃ ca ṭaṅkaṇaṃ caiva tulyaṃ ślakṣṇaṃ vimardayet |
mūṣāyāṃ taṃ vinikṣipya ruddhvā tīvrāgninā dhamet || 2,1.251 ||
indragopanibhaṃ sattvaṃ patatyeva na saṃśayaḥ |
<mayūratutthasya guṇāḥ>
tutthaṃ kaṭukaṣāyoṣṇaṃ śvitranetrāmayāpaham || 2,1.252 ||
viṣadoṣeṣu sarveṣu praśastaṃ kāntikārakam |
<kaṅkuṣṭha:: synonyms>
kaṅkuṣṭhaṃ kākakuṣṭhaṃ ca recakaṃ rāgadāyakam || 2,1.253 ||
<kaṅkuṣṭha:: subtypes>
kaṃkuṣṭhaṃ ca dvidhā proktaṃ hematārātmakaṃ tathā |
<kaṅkuṣṭha:: medic. properties>
vraṇajantujvaraghnaṃ ca śūlodāvartagulmahṛt || 2,1.254 ||
<girisindūram>
mahāgiriṣu cāllova pāṣāṇāntasthito rasaḥ |
śuṣkaḥ śoṇaḥ sa nirdiṣṭo girisindūrasaṃjñayā || 2,1.255 ||
tridoṣaśamanaṃ bhedi rasabandhanakārakam |
dehalohakaraṃ netryaṃ girisindūramīritam || 2,1.256 ||
<ṭaṅkaṇam>
ṭaṅkaṇaṃ ṭaṅkaṇakṣāro rasakṣāro rasādhikaḥ |
lohadrāvī rasaghnaśca subhago raṅgadaśca saḥ || 2,1.257 ||
mālatītīrasambhūtaḥ kṣāraśreṣṭho navāhvayaḥ |
aśmarīmatisāraṃ ca nihanyātsthāvaraṃ viṣam || 2,1.258 ||
rasasiddhikaraḥ prokto nāgārjunapuraḥsaraiḥ |
<kampillakaḥ>
iṣṭacūrṇasya saṅkāśaścandrikāḍhyo'tirekaḥ || 2,1.259 ||
saurāṣṭradeśakhanijaḥ sa hi kampillako mataḥ |
pittavraṇādhmānavibandhanaghnaḥ śleṣmodarārtikrimigulmahārī |
mūlāmaśūlajvaraśophahārī kaṃpillako ricya malāpahārī || 2,1.260 ||
<vatsanābhiḥ>
utpatyādi viṣasyādau kathitaṃ hi rasāyanam || 2,1.261 ||
amṛtaṃ syādvatsanābho viṣamugraṃ mahauṣadham |
garalaṃ maraṇaṃ nāgaḥ stokakaṃ prāṇahārakam || 2,1.262 ||
sarvakuṣṭhaharaṃ proktaṃ sarvavyādhivināśanam |
sannipātādirogāṇāṃ vinivṛttikaraṃ priye || 2,1.263 ||
<kāsīsa:: synonyms>
kāsīsaṃ dhātukāsīsaṃ kesaraṃ haṃsalomaśam |
śodhanaṃ pāṃśukāsīsaṃ śubhraṃ sattvāhvayaṃ matam || 2,1.264 ||
<kāsīsa:: subtypes>
kāsīsaṃ trividhaṃ śubhraṃ kṛṣṇaṃ pītamiti smṛtam |
kāsīsaṃ puṣpakāsīsaṃ hīrakāsīsamityatha || 2,1.265 ||
pītaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ sitaṃ raktaṃ caturdheti pare jaguḥ |
<kāsīsa:: medic. properties>
kāsīsamuṣṇaṃ sakṣārakaṣāyāmlaṃ satiktakam || 2,1.266 ||
vātaśleṣmapraśamanaṃ cakṣuṣyaṃ rañjakaṃ param |
mūtrakṛcchrāśmarīkuṣṭhakaṇḍūvraṇaviṣāpaham || 2,1.267 ||
<gaurīpāṣāṇakaḥ>
gaurīpāṣāṇakaḥ pīto vyaktadehaśca cūrṇakaḥ |
rasabandhakaraḥ snigdho doṣaghno rasavīryakṛt || 2,1.268 ||
<tuvarī phaṭakī ca>
bhūmistuvarikā phullatuvarī rañjikā kṣitiḥ |
citrabhūścīnakāraśca mañjiṣṭhārāgadāyinī || 2,1.269 ||
khagastu phaṭakī dugdhapāṣāṇo netrarogahā |
karpūrākhyaśilādhāturmañjiṣṭhārāgarañjakaḥ || 2,1.270 ||
<podāraśṛṅgī>
sadalaṃ pītavarṇaṃ ca bhavedgurjaramaṇḍale |
arjunasya gireḥ pārśve jātaṃ podāraśṛṅgikam || 2,1.271 ||
sīsasattvaṃ marucchleṣmaśamanaṃ puṅgadāpaham |
rasabandhanamutkṛṣṭaṃ keśarañjanamuttamam || 2,1.272 ||
<sindūra:: synonyms>
sindūranāgagarbhe saimantikavīrapāṃsu nāgabhavam |
raktaṃ ca nāgareṇuḥ gaṇapatibhūṣā suraṅga ityākhyā || 2,1.273 ||
saubhāgyaṃ caiva śṛṅgāraṃ maṅgalyamaruṇaṃ rajaḥ |
<sindūra:: medic. properties>
sindūraṃ kaṭukaṃ tiktamuṣṇaṃ vraṇaviropaṇam || 2,1.274 ||
kilāsaviṣakaṇḍūtivisarpaśamanaṃ param |
hiṅgule ye guṇāḥ santi te guṇāstimurau priye || 2,1.275 ||
<rasāñjanam>
rasāñjanaṃ rasodbhūtaṃ rasagarbhaṃ rasāgrajam |
rītyāṃ tu dhāmyamānāyāṃ tatkiṭṭaṃ tu rasāñjanam || 2,1.276 ||
tadabhāve tu kartavyaṃ dārvīkvāthasamudbhavam |
kṛtakaṃ bālabhaiṣajyaṃ dārvīkvāthodbhavaṃ tathā || 2,1.277 ||
dārvīkvāthabhavaṃ kaṇṭhavikāsi kṣiptamakṣṇi yat |
tadrasāñjanamuddiṣṭaṃ kaṣāyaṃ kaṭu tiktakam || 2,1.278 ||
kiṃciduṣṇaṃ kaphaharaṃ chedanaṃ lekhanaṃ laghu |
raktapittapraśamanaṃ netrarogavināśanam || 2,1.279 ||
rasāñjanaṃ tārkṣyaśailaṃ jñeyaṃ varyañjanaṃ tathā |
rasanābhaṃ cāgnisāraṃ dvādaśāhvayakīrtitam || 2,1.280 ||
<nīlāñjanam>
nīlaṃ nīlāñjanaṃ caiva cakṣuṣyaṃ vārisambhavam |
kapotakaṃ ca kāpotaṃ samproktaṃ śakrabhūmijam || 2,1.281 ||
śītaṃ nīlāñjanaṃ proktaṃ kaṭutiktakaṣāyakam |
cakṣuṣyaṃ kaphavātaghnaṃ viṣaghnaṃ ca rasāyanam || 2,1.282 ||
<sauvīrāñjanam>
sauvīramañjanaṃ snigdhamavaghṛṣṭaṃ śilātale |
añjanābhaṃ śilārūpametacchreṣṭhamakṛtrimam || 2,1.283 ||
sauvīramañjanaṃ caiva raktapittaharaṃ hitam |
viṣahidhmākṣirogaghnaṃ vraṇaśodhanaropaṇam || 2,1.284 ||
<srotoñjana:: synonyms>
srotodbhavaṃ srotanadībhavaṃ ca srotoñjanaṃ vāribhavaṃ tathānyam |
sauvīrasāraṃ ca kapotasāraṃ valmīkaśīrṣaṃ munisaṃmitāhvam || 2,1.285 ||
valmīkaśikharākāraṃ bhaṅge nīlotpaladyutiḥ |
gharṣaṇe gairikacchāyaṃ srotoñjanamidaṃ bhavet || 2,1.286 ||
<srotoñjana:: medic. properties>
sroto'ñjanaṃ śītakaṭukaṣāyaṃ krimināśanam |
rasāyanaṃ rase yojyaṃ stanyavṛddhikaraṃ param || 2,1.287 ||
sroto'ñjanaṃ himaṃ snigdhaṃ tṛṣṇāhṛtsvādu lekhanam |
netryaṃ hidhmāvamicchardikaphapittāsrakopanut || 2,1.288 ||
<ahiphenam>
śrāntasya mathanākṣobhādvāsukervadanotthitāḥ |
dvīpāntare patanti sma saviṣāḥ svedabindavaḥ || 2,1.289 ||
yatra yatra patanti sma prarūḍhā gulmarūpataḥ |
teṣu jātaṃ tu niryāsamaphenaṃ bruvate janāḥ || 2,1.290 ||
caturvidhamaphenaṃ syājjāraṇaṃ māraṇaṃ tathā |
dhāraṇaṃ sāraṇaṃ caiva kramādvakṣyāmi lakṣaṇam || 2,1.291 ||
śvetaṃ tu jāraṇaṃ proktaṃ kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ tu māraṇam |
dhāraṇaṃ pītavarṇaṃ ca karburaṃ sāraṇaṃ tathā || 2,1.292 ||
jāraṇaṃ jarayedannaṃ māraṇaṃ mṛtyudāyakam |
dhāraṇaṃ ca vayaḥstambhaṃ sāraṇaṃ malasārakam || 2,1.293 ||
aphenaṃ sannipātaghnaṃ vṛṣyaṃ balyaṃ ca mohadam |
grahaṇīmatisāraṃ ca nāśayetṣaṇḍatāmapi || 2,1.294 ||
<puṣpāñjana:: synonyms>
puṣpāñjanaṃ puṣpaketuḥ kaustubhaḥ kusumāñjanam |
rītikaṃ rītikusumaṃ rītipuṣpaṃ ca puṣpakam || 2,1.295 ||
<puṣpāñjana:: medic. properties>
puṣpāñjanaṃ hitaṃ proktaṃ pittahikkāpradāhanut |
nāśayedviṣakāsārtisarvanetrāmayāpaham || 2,1.296 ||
<śaṅkha>
śaṅkho'rṇavabhavaḥ kamburjalajaḥ pāvanadhvaniḥ |
kuṭilo'ntarmahānādaḥ śvetapītaḥ sunādakaḥ || 2,1.297 ||
sasvano dīrghanādaśca bahunādo harapriyaḥ |
kṣullakaḥ kṣudraśaṅkhaḥ syācchaṃbūko nakhaśaṅkhakaḥ || 2,1.298 ||
śaṅkhaḥ kaṭurasaḥ śītaḥ puṣṭivīryabalapradaḥ |
gulmaśūlakaphaśvāsanāśano viṣadoṣahā || 2,1.299 ||
kṣullakaḥ kaṭukaḥ snigdhaḥ śūlahārī ca dīpanaḥ |
śuktirmuktāprasūścaiva mahāśuktiśca śuktikā || 2,1.300 ||
muktāsphoṭastautikastu mauktikaprasavā ca sā |
jñeyā mauktikasūścaiva muktāmātā tathā smṛtā || 2,1.301 ||
muktāśuktiḥ kaṭuḥ snigdhā śvāsahṛdrogahāriṇī |
śūlapraśamanī rucyā madhurā dīpanī parā || 2,1.302 ||
jalaśuktiḥ kṣudraśuktiḥ krimisusphuṭikā ca sā |
jalaśuktiḥ kaṭuḥ snigdhā dīpanī gulmaśūlanut || 2,1.303 ||
tathā viṣaharā rucyā pācanī baladāyinī |
<kaparda>
kapardako varāṭaśca kapardaśca varāṭikā || 2,1.304 ||
carācaraścaro varyo bālakrīḍanakaśca sa |
pītābhā granthilā pṛṣṭhe dīrghavṛttā varāṭikā || 2,1.305 ||
rasavaidyairvinirdiṣṭā sā carācarasaṃjñakā |
sārdhaniṣkabharā śreṣṭhā niṣkabhārā ca madhyamā || 2,1.306 ||
pādonaniṣkabhārā ca kaniṣṭhā parikīrtitā |
pariṇāmādiśūlaghnī grahaṇīkṣayanāśanī || 2,1.307 ||
kaṭūṣṇā dīpanī vṛṣyā netryā vātakaphāpahā |
rasendrajāraṇe proktā biḍadravyeṣu śasyate || 2,1.308 ||
tadanye puṃvarāṭāḥ syurguravaḥ śleṣmapittalāḥ |
kapardaḥ kaṭutiktoṣṇaḥ karṇaśūlavraṇāpahaḥ || 2,1.309 ||
gulmaśūlāmayaghnaśca netradoṣanikṛntanaḥ |
<sābuṇi>
sarvakṣāro bahukṣāraḥ samūhakṣārasābuṇiḥ || 2,1.310 ||
stomakṣāro mahākṣāro malāriḥ kṣāramelakaḥ |
sarvakṣāramatikṣāraṃ cakṣuṣyaṃ bastiśodhanam || 2,1.311 ||
udāvartakrimighnaṃ ca biḍavadvastraśodhanam |
<navasāra>
karīrapīlukāṣṭheṣu pacyamāneṣṭakodbhavaḥ || 2,1.312 ||
kṣāro'sau navasāraḥ syāccūlikālavaṇābhidhaḥ |
rasendrajāraṇaṃ lohadrāvaṇaṃ jaṭharāgnikṛt || 2,1.313 ||
gulmaplīhāsyaśoṣaghnaṃ bhuktamāṃsādijāraṇam |
<saurāṣṭrī>
gopittena śataṃ vārānsaurāṣṭrīṃ bhāvayettataḥ || 2,1.314 ||
dhamitvā pātayetsatvaṃ krāmaṇaṃ cātiguhyakam |
<ākhupāṣāṇa>
mūṣakasyābhidhā pūrvaṃ pāṣāṇasyābhidhā tataḥ || 2,1.315 ||
ākhupāṣāṇanāmāyaṃ lohasaṅkarakārakaḥ |
ākhugrāvā saro rūkṣo vamiśītajvarāpahaḥ || 2,1.316 ||
tārakarmaṇi saṃpūjyo medodurmāṃsakṛntanaḥ |
<sarjarasa>
rālaḥ sarjarasaścaiva yakṣadhūpo'gnivallabhaḥ || 2,1.317 ||
deveṣṭaḥ śālaniryāsaḥ surabhirdhūpavallabhaḥ |
rālastu pañcadhā prokto raktaḥ pītaḥ sito'sitaḥ || 2,1.318 ||
nānāvarṇaśca vijñeyaḥ kṛṣṇasteṣu guṇottaraḥ |
rālastu śiśiraḥ snigdhaḥ kaṣāyastiktako rase || 2,1.319 ||
vātapittaharaḥ sphoṭakaṇḍūtivraṇanāśanaḥ |
<guggulu>
dānavendravijitānpurā surān bhraṣṭakāntidhṛtidhairyatejasaḥ |
vīkṣya viṣṇuramṛtaṃ kilāsṛjatgugguluṃ balavapurjayapradam || 2,1.320 ||
marubhūmiṣu jāyante prāyaśaḥ purapādapāḥ || 2,1.321 ||
bhānormayūkhaiḥ saṃtaptā grīṣme muñcanti guggulum |
gugguluḥ pañcadhā prokto mahiṣākṣaśca nīlakaḥ || 2,1.322 ||
padmastu kumudaścaiva suvarṇaḥ pañcamaḥ priye |
mahiṣākṣaśca nīlaśca dantināṃ guṇadāyakau || 2,1.323 ||
vājināṃ kumudaḥ padmo narāṇāṃ svarṇavarṇakaḥ |
eraṇḍabījataile vā tilataile'thavā ghṛte || 2,1.324 ||
pañcatiktaśṛtaiḥ kvāthaiḥ śuddhiṃ kuryātsuguggulum |
dolāyantre pacettāvadyāvannirmalatā bhavet || 2,1.325 ||
evaṃ viśuddhaṃ saṃgrāhyaṃ tattadyogeṣu yojayet |
piṭakāgaṇḍamālādyā naśyanti ca marudgadāḥ || 2,1.326 ||
<yavakṣāra>
yavakṣāro'mṛtaḥ pākyo yavajo yavaśūkajaḥ |
yavaśūko yavāhvaśca yavapākyo yavārujaḥ || 2,1.327 ||
yavakṣāraḥ kaṭūṣṇaśca kaphavātodarārtinut |
āmaśūlāśmarīkṛcchraviṣadoṣaharaḥ paraḥ || 2,1.328 ||
<sarjakṣāra>
sadyaḥkṣāraḥ sarjikaśca kṣāraḥ sajjī suvarjikaḥ |
sarjīkaḥ kaṭurūkṣaśca tīkṣṇo vātakaphārtinut || 2,1.329 ||
gulmānāhavamighnaśca mehajāṭhararogahṛt |
<lavaṇakṣāra>
loṇāraṃ lavaṇotthaṃ lavaṇāsuraṃ ca lavaṇamedaśca || 2,1.330 ||
jatujaṃ lavaṇakṣāraṃ lavaṇaṃ ca kṣāralavaṇaṃ ca |
loṇakakṣāramatyuṣṇaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ pittapravṛddhidam || 2,1.331 ||
kṣāraṃ lavaṇamīṣacca vātagulmādiśūlanut |
<vajrakakṣāra>
vajrakaṃ vajrakakṣāraṃ kṣāraśreṣṭhaṃ vidārakam || 2,1.332 ||
sāraṃ candanasāraṃ ca dhūmotthaṃ dhūmajaṃ gajāḥ |
vajrakakṣāramatyuṣṇaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ kṣāraṃ ca rodhanam || 2,1.333 ||
gulmodarārtiviṣṭambhaśūlapraśamanaṃ param |
<sāmudralavaṇa>
sāmudrakaṃ tu sāmudraṃ sāmudralavaṇaṃ śivam || 2,1.334 ||
<sea salt:: medic. properties>
sāmudraṃ laghu hṛdyaṃ ca vāritāsṛjapittalam |
vidāhi kaphavātaghnaṃ dīpanaṃ rucikṛtpriyam || 2,1.335 ||
<saindhava>
saindhavaṃ syācchītaśivaṃ nādeyaṃ sindhujaṃ śivam |
śuddhaṃ śivātmakaṃ pathyaṃ māṇimanthaṃ navābhidham || 2,1.336 ||
saindhavaṃ lavaṇaṃ vṛṣyaṃ cakṣuṣyaṃ dīpanaṃ ruci |
pūtaṃ nasyāttridoṣaghnaṃ vraṇadoṣavibandhajit || 2,1.337 ||
saindhavaṃ dvividhaṃ jñeyaṃ sitaṃ raktamiti kramāt |
rasavīryavipākeṣu guṇāḍhyaṃ pūtanaṃ sitam || 2,1.338 ||
<kācalavaṇa>
nīlakācodbhavaṃ kācatilakaṃ caiva kācasambhavam |
kākasauvarcalaṃ kācalavaṇaṃ pākyajaṃ smṛtam || 2,1.339 ||
kācotthaṃ hṛdyagandhaṃ ca tatkālalavaṇaṃ tathā |
kuruvindaṃ kācamalaṃ katimaṃ ca caturdaśa || 2,1.340 ||
kācākhyaṃ lavaṇaṃ rucyamīṣatkṣāraṃ sapittalam |
dāhadaṃ kaphavātaghnaṃ dīpanaṃ gulmaśūlahṛt || 2,1.341 ||
<biḍalavaṇa>
biḍaṃ ca biḍakaṃ khaṇḍaṃ kṛtakṣāraṃ ca āsuram |
supākyaṃ lavaṇaṃ khaṇḍaṃ dhūrtaṃ kṛtrimakaṃ daśa || 2,1.342 ||
biḍamuṣṇaṃ salavaṇaṃ dīpanaṃ vātanāśanam |
rucyaṃ cājīrṇaśūlaghnaṃ gulmamehavināśanam || 2,1.343 ||
<sauvarcala>
sauvarcalaṃ ca rucakaṃ tilakaṃ hṛdyagandhakam |
akṣaṃ ca kṛṣṇalavaṇaṃ rucyaṃ kodravikaṃ tathā || 2,1.344 ||
<sauvarcala:: medic. properties>
sauvarcalaṃ laghu kṣāraṃ kaṭūṣṇaṃ gulmaśūlanut |
ūrdhvavātāmaśūlārtivibandhārocakaṃ jayet || 2,1.345 ||
<amlavetasa>
amlo'mlavetaso vedhī rasāmlo vītavetasaḥ |
vetasāraścāmlasāraḥ śaravedhī ca vedhakaḥ || 2,1.346 ||
nīlaśca bhedano bhedī rājāmlaścāmlabhedanaḥ |
amlāṅkuśo raktasāraḥ phalāmlaścāmlanāyakaḥ || 2,1.347 ||
sahasravedhī vīrāmlo gulmaketurdharākṣidhā |
śaṅkhamāṃsādidrāvī syaddvidhā caivāmlavetasaḥ || 2,1.348 ||
amlavetasamatyamlaṃ kaṣāyoṣṇāmavātajit |
kaphārśaḥsamagulmāmamarocakaharaṃ param || 2,1.349 ||
<kāca>
kācastuṣārasāraśca rasakhoṭamalāpahaḥ |
śṛṅgārī cābhradhārī ca sarvanetrāmayāpahā || 2,1.350 ||
<chagaṇa>
piṣṭakaṃ chagaṇaśchāṇamutpalaṃ ca vanotpalam |
kariṇḍopalaśāṭhī ca varaṭī chagaṇābhidhā || 2,1.351 ||
rasalohadoṣahāri khyātaṃ tadbhasma duritasaṃhṛtaye |
<aṅgāra>
śikhitrāḥ pāvakocchiṣṭā aṅgārāḥ koliśā matāḥ || 2,1.352 ||
kokilāśceti cāṅgārā nirvāṇāḥ payasā vinā |
<sikatā>
sikatā pravāhajanitā siktā pānīyacūrṇakā sūkṣmā || 2,1.353 ||
sā vālukā śramaghnī saṃsekātsannipātaghnī |
<uparasaśodhana>
kāsīsaṃ bhāvayedgharme dinaṃ jambīrajairdravaiḥ || 2,1.354 ||
śudhyate ṭaṅkaṇaṃ gairī kaṅkuṣṭhaṃ ca varāṭikā |
śaṅkhaṃ nīlāñjanaṃ caiva pṛthakśodhyaṃ dine dine || 2,1.355 ||
godugdhaistriphalākvāthairbhṛṅgadrāvaiḥ śilājatu |
mardayedāyase pātre dinaikaṃ tacca śudhyati || 2,1.356 ||
meṣīkṣīreṇa daradamamlavargaiśca bhāvitam |
saptavāraṃ prayatnena śuddhimāyāti niścayam || 2,1.357 ||
sūryāvartaṃ vajrakandaṃ kadalī devadālikā |
śigruḥ kośātakī vandhyā kākamācī ca vālukā || 2,1.358 ||
āsāmekarasenaiva trikṣārairlavaṇaiḥ saha |
bhāvayedamlavargaiśca dinamekaṃ prayatnataḥ || 2,1.359 ||
sauvīraṃ kāntapāṣāṇaḥ śuddhā bhūnāgamṛttikā |
sarve uparasāścātha pṛthagbhāvyaṃ dinaṃ dinam || 2,1.360 ||
tataḥ pācyaṃ ca taddrāvairḍolāyantre dinaṃ sudhīḥ |
śudhyante nātra sandehaḥ sarve uparasāḥ pṛthak || 2,1.361 ||
punarnavāmeghanādakapijambīratindukaiḥ |
agastipuṣpakumudayavaciñcāmlavetasaiḥ || 2,1.362 ||
vanasūraṇabhūdhātrīmaṇḍūkīkaravīrakaiḥ |
kāravallīkṣīrakandaraktotpalaśamīghanaiḥ || 2,1.363 ||
meṣaśṛṅgīśaśavasāśakravāruṇiṭaṅkaṇaiḥ |
tailamatsyavasāvyoṣadravairetaiḥ sakāñjikaiḥ || 2,1.364 ||
etaiḥ samastairvyastairvā ḍolāyantre dinatrayam |
abhrapatrādyuparasān śuddhihetoḥ prapācayet || 2,1.365 ||


āk, 2, 2
śrībhairavaḥ |
svarṇādisarvalohānāmutpattyādikramaṃ bruve |
<gold:: synonyms>
svarṇaṃ suvarṇakanakojjvalakāñcanāni kalyāṇahāṭakahiraṇyamanoharāṇi |
gāṅgeyagairikamahārajatāgnivīryarukmāni hematapanīyakabhāsvarāṇi || 2,2.1 ||
jāmbūnadāṣṭāpadajātarūpapiñjānacāmīkarakarburāṇi |
kārtasvarāpiñjaravarṇabhūritejāṃsi dīptāmaladīpipītakāni || 2,2.2 ||
maṅgalyasaumeravaśātakumbhaśṛṅgāracandrārajajāmbavāni |
āgneyaniṣkāgniśikhāni ceti netrābdhinirdhāritahemanāma || 2,2.3 ||
<svarṇabhedāḥ>
prākṛtaṃ sahajaṃ vahnisambhūtaṃ khanisambhavam || 2,2.4 ||
rasendravedhasaṃjātaṃ svarṇaṃ pañcavidhaṃ smṛtam |
brahmāṇḍaṃ saṃvṛtaṃ yena rajoguṇabhuvā khalu || 2,2.5 ||
tatprākṛtamiti proktaṃ devānāmapi durlabham |
brahmā yenāvṛto jātaḥ suvarṇaṃ sahajaṃ hi tat || 2,2.6 ||
visṛṣṭamagninā śaivaṃ tejaḥ pītaṃ suduḥsaham |
abhūtsvarṇaṃ taduddiṣṭaṃ suvarṇaṃ vahnisaṃbhavam || 2,2.7 ||
etatsvarṇatrayaṃ divyaṃ varṇaiḥ ṣoḍaśabhiryutam |
dhāraṇādeva tatkuryāccharīramajarāmayam || 2,2.8 ||
girīṇāṃ khanisambhūtaṃ tatsvarṇaṃ khanijaṃ smṛtam |
taccaturdaśavarṇāḍhyaṃ bhakṣitaṃ sarvarogahṛt || 2,2.9 ||
rasendravedhasambhūtaṃ tadvedhajamudāhṛtam |
rasāyanaṃ mahāśreṣṭhaṃ pavitraṃ vedhajaṃ hi tat || 2,2.10 ||
raktābhaṃ pītavarṇaṃ ca dvividhaṃ kāñcanaṃ bhavet |
<grāhyāgrāhyasvarṇarūpa>
dāhe raktaṃ sitaṃ chede nikaṣe kuṅkumaprabham || 2,2.11 ||
guru snigdhaṃ mṛdu svacchaṃ nirdalaṃ raktapītakam |
hema ṣoḍaśavarṇāḍhyaṃ śasyate dehalohayoḥ || 2,2.12 ||
śvetāṅgaṃ kaṭhinaṃ rūkṣaṃ vivarṇaṃ samalaṃ dalam |
dāhe śvetāsitaṃ śvetaṃ nikaṣe laghu ca vrajet || 2,2.13 ||
na śuddhaṃ na mṛtaṃ svarṇaṃ rogavargaṃ karoti ca |
saukhyaṃ vīryaṃ balaṃ hanti tasmācchuddhirudīryate || 2,2.14 ||
<svarṇaśuddhi>
valmīkamṛttikā dhūmaṃ gairikaṃ ceṣṭakā paṭu |
ityete mṛttikāḥ pañca jambīrairvāranālakaiḥ || 2,2.15 ||
piṣṭvā limpetsvarṇapatraṃ bhasmacchannaṃ tu karpare |
yāvaddravaṃ puṭaṃ tāvatkuryāttena viśudhyati || 2,2.16 ||
mṛttikāṃ mātuluṅgāmlaiḥ pañcavāsarabhāvitām |
sabhasmalavaṇaṃ hema śodhayetpuṭapākataḥ || 2,2.17 ||
bhāvayenmātuluṅgāmlaistridinaṃ pañcamṛttikām |
saindhavaṃ bhūmibhasmābhiḥ svarṇaṃ śudhyati pūrvavat || 2,2.18 ||
taile takre gavāṃ mūtre hyāranāle kulutthake |
kramānniṣecayettaptaṃ saptavāraṃ dravairdravaiḥ || 2,2.19 ||
svarṇādilohapatrāṇāṃ śuddhireṣā praśasyate |
drāvitau nāgavaṅgau ca tadvatsiñcedviśuddhaye || 2,2.20 ||
suvarṇaṃ ḍhālayetpūrvaṃ kāñcanārodbhave rase |
saptavāraṃ tataḥ karmayogyaṃ hema bhaveddhruvam || 2,2.21 ||
<svarṇabhasma (1)>
nāgacūrṇaṃ śilāyuktaṃ vajrīkṣīrasamanvitam |
lepanātpuṭayogācca hemapatrāṇi lepayet || 2,2.22 ||
mṛtaṃ nāgaṃ snuhīkṣīrairathavāmlena kenacit |
piṣṭvā lepyaṃ svarṇapatraṃ ruddhvā gajapuṭe pacet || 2,2.23 ||
ādāya peṣayedamle mṛtanāgaṃ cāṣṭamāṃśakam |
pacedgajapuṭe ruddhvā pūrvavannāgasaṃyutam || 2,2.24 ||
evaṃ punaḥ punaḥ pākādaṣṭadhā mriyate dhruvam |
<svarṇabhasma (2)>
śuddhasūtasamaṃ gandhaṃ mākṣikaṃ ca sahāmlakaiḥ || 2,2.25 ||
aṣṭabhiśca puṭairhema mriyate pūrvavatkriyā |
<svarṇabhasma (3)>
śuddhasūtasamaṃ svarṇaṃ khalve kuryācca golakam || 2,2.26 ||
ūrdhvādho gandhakaṃ dattvā sarvatulyaṃ nirudhya ca |
triṃśadvanotpalairdadyātpuṭānevaṃ caturdaśa || 2,2.27 ||
nirutthaṃ jāyate bhasma gandhaṃ deyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ |
<svarṇabhasma (4)>
svarṇamāvartya tolaikaṃ māṣaikaṃ śuddhanāgakam || 2,2.28 ||
kṣiptvādāya tu taccūrṇaṃ tattulye gandhamākṣike |
amlena mardayedyāmaṃ ruddhvā laghupuṭe pacet || 2,2.29 ||
gandhaṃ punaḥ punardeyaṃ mriyate daśabhiḥ puṭaiḥ |
<rasādibhiḥ svarṇamāraṇam>
hemapatrāṇi kurvīta vilimpedrasabhasmanā || 2,2.30 ||
amlapiṣṭena puṭayeddhema nirjīvatāṃ vrajet |
athavā tāpyapuṭitaṃ svarṇaṃ gacchati bhasmatām || 2,2.31 ||
yadvā mṛtena kariṇā śilāyogena bhasmayet |
athavā mṛtavajreṇa yoginyaṃśavilepitam || 2,2.32 ||
bhasmatāṃ yātyuparasai rasaiścaiva mahārasaiḥ |
amlapiṣṭairviliptaṃ ca puṭitaṃ yāti bhasmatām || 2,2.33 ||
<svarṇamāraṇa (5)>
svarṇasya dviguṇaṃ sūtaṃ yāmamamlena mardayet |
āroṭaṃ mākṣikaṃ kṣiptvā mūṣāyāṃ svarṇatulyakam || 2,2.34 ||
tatpṛṣṭhe marditaṃ hema tatpṛṣṭhe śuddhamākṣikam |
deyaṃ svarṇasamaṃ tacca pṛṣṭhe gandhaṃ ca tatsamam || 2,2.35 ||
sarvaṃ ca cūrṇitaṃ dadyādruddhvā mūṣāṃ dhameddṛḍham |
svabhāvaśītalaṃ grāhyaṃ bhasma tadbhāgapañcakam || 2,2.36 ||
ṭaṅkaṇaṃ śvetakācaṃ ca bhāgaikaikaṃ prapeṣayet |
tritayaṃ madhunājyena melakaṃ golakīkṛtam || 2,2.37 ||
dhānyābhrasya ca bhāgaikamadhaścordhvaṃ ca dāpayet |
nirudhya taddhamedgāḍhaṃ mūṣāyāṃ ghaṭikādvayam || 2,2.38 ||
nirutthaṃ jāyate bhasma tattadyogeṣu yojayet |
<svarṇamāraṇa (6)>
hemnaḥ pādaṃ mṛtaṃ sūtaṃ piṣṭamamlena kenacit || 2,2.39 ||
patre liptvā puṭe pacyādaṣṭabhirmriyate dhruvam |
śuddhānāṃ sarvalohānāṃ māraṇe rītirīdṛśī || 2,2.40 ||
<svarṇamāraṇa (7)>
śuddhamākṣīkabhāgaikaṃ bhāgaṃ cāroṭamākṣikam |
dvibhāgaṃ sūtakaṃ kṣiptvā trayamamlena mardayet || 2,2.41 ||
bhāgaikaṃ hemapatraṃ ca liptvā cāmlena mardayet |
tadgolaṃ pātanāyantre haṭhādyāmatrayaṃ pacet || 2,2.42 ||
ityevaṃ mriyate svarṇaṃ nirutthaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ |
<svarṇamāraṇa (8)>
yavaciñcārajovṛkṣabhallātaiṣṭaṅkaṇena ca || 2,2.43 ||
liptvā svarṇasya patrāṇi ruddhvā gajapuṭe pacet |
tairdravaiḥ piṣṭyā mriyate saptadhā puṭaiḥ || 2,2.44 ||
pīlukaṅkuṣṭhabukkāṇasauvarcalamapeṣitam |
liptvā svarṇasya patrāṇi mriyate saptabhiḥ puṭaiḥ || 2,2.45 ||
<svarṇasya guṇāḥ>
suvarṇaṃ bṛṃhaṇaṃ snigdhaṃ madhuraṃ rasapākayoḥ |
viṣadoṣaharaṃ śītaṃ sakaṣāyaṃ satiktakam || 2,2.46 ||
hṛdyaṃ kāntipradaṃ balyaṃ saṃrasanaṃ guru lekhanam |
buddhimedhāsmṛtikaraṃ vraṇaghnaṃ vāgviśuddhidam || 2,2.47 ||
kṣayonmādapraśamanaṃ cakṣuṣyaṃ ca rasāyanam |
tridoṣaśamanaṃ saumyamāyuṣyaṃ ruciraṃ śuci || 2,2.48 ||
prajñāvīryasvarakaramaiśvaryaṃ dhāraṇādbhavet || 2,2.49 ||


āk, 2, 3
śrībhairavaḥ |
<silver:: synonyms>
raupyaṃ śubhraṃ vasuśreṣṭhaṃ ruciraṃ candralohakam |
rajataṃ taptarūpyaṃ ca candrabhūtistu raupyakam || 2,3.1 ||
kaladhautaṃ ca saudhaṃ ca candrahāsaṃ ca tārakam |
<rajatabhedāḥ>
sahajaṃ khanisaṃjātaṃ kṛtrimaṃ ca tridhā matam || 2,3.2 ||
rajataṃ pūrvapūrvaṃ hi svaguṇairuttarottaram |
kailāsādyadrisambhūtaṃ sahajaṃ rajataṃ bhavet || 2,3.3 ||
tatspṛṣṭaṃ hi mahāvyādhināśanaṃ dehināṃ bhavet |
himācalādikūṭeṣu yadrūpyaṃ jāyate hi tat || 2,3.4 ||
khanijaṃ kathyate tajjñaiḥ paramaṃ hi rasāyanam |
śrīrāmapādukānyastaṃ vaṅgaṃ yadrūpyatāṃ gatam || 2,3.5 ||
tatpādarūpyamityuktaṃ kṛtrimaṃ sarvaroganut |
<grāhyāgrāhyarajatasvarūpam>
ghanaṃ snigdhaṃ guru snigdhaṃ dāhe chede sitaṃ mṛdu || 2,3.6 ||
varṇāḍhyaṃ candravatsvacchaṃ dāhe chede samaprabham |
śaṅkhābhaṃ masṛṇaṃ sphoṭarahitaṃ rajataṃ śubham || 2,3.7 ||
dāhe raktaṃ ca pītaṃ ca kṛṣṇaṃ rūkṣaṃ sphuṭaṃ laghu |
sthūlāṅgaṃ karkaśāṅgaṃ ca rajataṃ tyājyamaṣṭadhā || 2,3.8 ||
kaṭhinaṃ kṛtrimaṃ rūkṣaṃ vivarṇaṃ samalaṃ dalam |
dāhe chede ghane naṣṭaṃ madhyamaṃ rajataṃ matam || 2,3.9 ||
karoti tāpaṃ viḍbhedaṃ kṣayaṃ śuklabalāyuṣām |
na śuddhaṃ na mṛtaṃ tāraṃ tasmācchuddhaṃ ca mārayet || 2,3.10 ||
<rajataśuddhi>
nāgena ṭaṅkaṇenaiva drāvitaṃ śuddhimṛcchati |
tāraṃ trivāraṃ nikṣiptaṃ taile jyotiṣmatībhave || 2,3.11 ||
taptaṃ taptaṃ tārapatraṃ secayecchuddhimāpnuyāt |
piśācasaṃbhave yadvā prakṣiptaṃ śuddhimāpnuyāt || 2,3.12 ||
rajataṃ doṣanirmuktaṃ nāgottīrṇaṃ samāharet |
śvetakumbhodbhave nīre ḍhālayetsaptavārakān || 2,3.13 ||
<rajatabhasma (1, 2, 3)>
kuraṇḍamunipuṣpotthasalilaiḥ saṃpramardayet |
tārapatrāṇi liptāni puṭayecca vanotpalaiḥ || 2,3.14 ||
mriyate nātra sandehaḥ liptaṃ vā rasabhasmanā |
amlavargapraliptena pūrvavatpuṭayogataḥ || 2,3.15 ||
mriyate tālakaṃ sūtaṃ vaṅgamamlena peṣayet |
tārapatrāṇi saṃlipya puṭitvā bhasmatāṃ nayet || 2,3.16 ||
mriyate gandhayogādyairvaiṣṇavena vipadyate |
<rajatabhasma (4)>
snuhīkṣīraiḥ pacettāpyaṃ tārapatrāṇi lepayet || 2,3.17 ||
ruddhvā gajapuṭe paktvā pūrvoktaiḥ pācayetpunaḥ |
catvāriṃśatpuṭairevaṃ pacettāraṃ mṛtaṃ bhavet || 2,3.18 ||
<rajatabhasma (5)>
bhūdhātrīṃ mākṣikaṃ tulyaṃ pippalīṃ saindhavāmlakaiḥ |
liptvā tārasya patrāṇi ruddhvā saptapuṭaiḥ pacet || 2,3.19 ||
dravaiḥ punaḥ punaḥ piṣṭvā mriyate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
<rajatabhasma (6)>
likucadravasūtābhyāṃ tārapiṣṭīṃ prakalpayet || 2,3.20 ||
ruddhvādho gandhakaṃ dattvā mūṣāgarbhe nirudhya ca |
svedayedvālukāyantre dinamekaṃ dṛḍhāgninā || 2,3.21 ||
svāṅgaśītalatāṃ piṣṭiṃ sāmlatālena marditam |
puṭeddvādaśavārāṇi bhasmībhavati rūpyakam || 2,3.22 ||
<rajatabhasma (7)>
mākṣikaṃ cūrṇaluṅgāmlamarditaṃ puṭitaṃ śanaiḥ |
triṃśadvāreṇa tattāraṃ nirutthaṃ bhasma jāyate || 2,3.23 ||
rāgaḥ syātsarvalohānāṃ puṭādhikye na saṃśayaḥ |
<rajatabhasma (8)>
tārapattraṃ caturbhāgā bhāgaikaṃ śuddhatālakam || 2,3.24 ||
mardyaṃ jambīrajairdrāvaistārapatrāṇi lepayet |
śoṣayedandhayettaṃ ca triṃśadvanotpalaiḥ puṭet || 2,3.25 ||
caturdaśapuṭairevaṃ nirutthaṃ mriyate dhruvam |
<rajatabhasma (9)>
raupyapatraṃ caturbhāgā bhāgaikaṃ mṛtavaṅgakam || 2,3.26 ||
athavā gandhabhāgaikaṃ lepyaṃ jambīrapeṣitaiḥ |
ruddhvā tribhiḥ puṭaiḥ pācyaṃ pañcaviṃśadvanotpalaiḥ || 2,3.27 ||
mriyate nātra sandeho gandho deyaḥ punaḥ punaḥ |
<rajatabhasma (10)>
rasagandhau samaṃ kṛtvā kākatuṇḍasya mūlakam || 2,3.28 ||
mardayenmahiṣīkṣīraiḥ piṣṭiṃ tāṃ kṣālayejjalaiḥ |
haridrāgolake kṣiptvā golaṃ hayapurīṣake || 2,3.29 ||
kṣiptvā dinaikaviṃśacca tadgarbhāduddharetpunaḥ |
tatpiṣṭvā tārapatrāṇi lepyamamlena kenacit || 2,3.30 ||
puṭairviṃśatibhirbhasma jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
bhasmanā hyamlapiṣṭena limpettāmramayaṃ kṣuram || 2,3.31 ||
jāyate taṃ mṛtaṃ vidyādbhasma hāṭakatārayoḥ |
<rajatasya guṇāḥ>
raupyaṃ snigdhaṃ kaṣāyāmlaṃ vipāke madhuraṃ rasam || 2,3.32 ||
vayasaḥ sthāpanaṃ śītaṃ lekhanaṃ vātapittajit |
vṛṣyaṃ rucikaraṃ balyaṃ jaṭharāgnipradīpanam || 2,3.33 ||


āk, 2, 4
śrībhairavaḥ |
<tāmra>
tāmraṃ mlecchamukhaṃ śulbaṃ tapaneṣṭamudumbaram |
tryambakaṃ cāravindaṃ ca ravilohaṃ ravipriyam || 2,4.1 ||
raktaṃ naipālakaṃ caiva raktadhātuḥ karendudhā |
mlecchaṃ nepālakaṃ ceti tayornepālamuttamam || 2,4.2 ||
nepālādanyakhanyutthaṃ mlecchamityabhidhīyate |
susnigdhaṃ mṛdulaṃ śoṇaṃ ghanaghātakṣayaṃ guru || 2,4.3 ||
nirvikāraṃ guru śreṣṭhaṃ tāmraṃ nepālamucyate |
sitakṛṣṇāruṇacchāyaṃ vāmabhedi kaṭhorakam || 2,4.4 ||
kṣālitaṃ ca punaḥ kṛṣṇametanmlecchakatāmrakam |
pāṇḍuraṃ kṛṣṇaśoṇaṃ ca laghu sphuṭanasaṃyutam || 2,4.5 ||
rūkṣāṅgaṃ sadalaṃ tāmraṃ neṣyate rasakarmaṇi |
utkledamohabhramadāhabhedāḥ tāmrasya doṣāḥ khalu durdharāste |
viśodhanāttadvigatasvadoṣaṃ sudhāsamaṃ syādrasavīryapāke || 2,4.6 ||
vāntimūrcchābhramonmādanānārukkuṣṭhaśūlakṛt || 2,4.7 ||
āyuḥkāntibalabhraṃśakaraṃ dhātupradūṣaṇam |
nu śuddhaṃ na mṛtaṃ tāmraṃ tena saṃśodhya mārayet || 2,4.8 ||
<tāmraśuddhi>
tāmraṃ kṣārāmlasaṃyuktaṃ drāvitaṃ taptagairikam |
nikṣiptaṃ mahiṣītakrachagaṇe saptavārakam || 2,4.9 ||
pañcadoṣavinirmuktaṃ saptavāreṇa jāyate |
tāmranirdalapattrāṇi liptvā nimbavasindhunā || 2,4.10 ||
dhmātvā sauvīrake kṣepādviśudhyatyaṣṭavārataḥ |
nimbvambupaṭuliptāni tāpitānyaṣṭavārakam || 2,4.11 ||
viśudhyantyarkapatrāṇi nirguṇḍīrasamardanāt |
kumārīpatramadhye tu śulvapatraṃ niveśitam || 2,4.12 ||
puṭitaṃ doṣanirmuktaṃ pāṇḍuraṃ ca prajāyate |
itthaṃ viśodhitaṃ tāmraṃ sarvadoṣavivarjitam || 2,4.13 ||
bhavedrasāyanakaraṃ dehalohakaraṃ param |
imāṃ śuddhiṃ vijānīyācchivo vā nandikeśvaraḥ || 2,4.14 ||
balinā nihataṃ tāmraṃ saptavāraṃ samutthitam |
sarvadoṣavinirmuktaṃ bhavedamṛtasannibham || 2,4.15 ||
snuhyarkakṣīralavaṇakāñjikaistāmrapatrakam |
liptvā pratāpya nirguṇḍīrasaiḥ siñcyātpunaḥ punaḥ || 2,4.16 ||
vārān dvādaśataḥ śuddhaṃ lepāttāpācca secanāt |
khaṭikāṃ lavaṇaṃ takrairāranālaiśca peṣayet || 2,4.17 ||
tena liptaṃ tāmrapatraṃ taptaṃ taptaṃ niṣecayet |
ṣaḍvāramamlatakrāntarnirguṇḍyāśca viśuddhaye || 2,4.18 ||
taptāni tāmrapatrāṇi secayettiktakārasaiḥ |
liptvāmlatakralavaṇakāñjikena punaḥ punaḥ || 2,4.19 ||
taptaṃ taptaṃ tridhā siñcyācchuddhimāyāti niścayam |
gomūtreṇa pacedyāmaṃ tāmrapātraṃ dṛḍhāgninā || 2,4.20 ||
śudhyate nātra sandeho māraṇaṃ cāpyathocyate |
<tāmrabhasma (1)>
gandhena tāmratulyena hyamlapiṣṭena lepayet || 2,4.21 ||
kaṇṭavedhīkṛtaṃ patramandhayitvā puṭe pacet |
uddhṛtya cūrṇayettasminpādāṃśaṃ gandhakaṃ kṣipet || 2,4.22 ||
jambīrairāranālairvā mṛgadūrvāthavā dravaiḥ |
piṣṭvā piṣṭvā pacedruddhaṃ sagandhaṃ ca catuṣpuṭaiḥ || 2,4.23 ||
mātuluṅgadravaiḥ piṣṭvā puṭamekaṃ pradāpayet |
sitaśarkarayāpyekaṃ puṭaṃ deyaṃ mṛtaṃ bhavet || 2,4.24 ||
<tāmrabhasma (2)>
pāṣāṇabhedimatsyākṣīdravairdviguṇagandhakam |
tāmrasya lepayetpiṣṭiṃ ruddhvā gajapuṭe pacet || 2,4.25 ||
samāṃśena punargandhaṃ dattvā drāvaiśca lolayet |
evaṃ saptapuṭaiḥ pakvaṃ tāmrabhasma bhaveddhruvam || 2,4.26 ||
<tāmrabhasma (3)>
tāmrasya triguṇaṃ sūtaṃ jambīrāmlena mardayet |
ādau mūṣāntare kṣiptvā dhuttūrasya tu patrakam || 2,4.27 ||
tatpṛṣṭhe tāmratulyaṃ ca gandhakaṃ cūrṇitaṃ kṣipet |
tatpṛṣṭhe marditaṃ tāmraṃ pūrvatulyaṃ ca gandhakam || 2,4.28 ||
ācchādya dhūrtapatraistu ruddhvā gajapuṭe pacet |
svāṅgaśītaṃ tataścūrṇaṃ mṛtaṃ bhavati niścitam || 2,4.29 ||
<tāmrabhasma (4)>
kiṃcidgandhena vāmlena kṣālayettāmrapatrakam |
tāmrapādena sūtena sārdraṃ patraṃ pralepayet || 2,4.30 ||
tāmradviguṇagandhena cāmlapiṣṭena tatpunaḥ |
liptvā hyadhordhvagaṃ deyā supiṣṭā cāmlaparṇikā || 2,4.31 ||
tatpiṇḍaṃ bhāṇḍagarbhe tu ruddhvā cullyāṃ vipācayet |
yāmaikaṃ tāmrapākena bhasmībhavati niścitam || 2,4.32 ||
<tāmrabhasma (5)>
sūtamekaṃ dvidhā gandhaṃ yāmaṃ kanyā vimardayet |
dvayostulye tāmrapatre sthālyā garbhe nirodhayet || 2,4.33 ||
samyaṅmṛllavaṇaiḥ sandhiṃ pārśve bhasma nidhāpayet |
caturyāmaṃ paceccullyāṃ pātrapṛṣṭhe sagomayam || 2,4.34 ||
jalaṃ punaḥ punardeyaṃ svāṅgaśītaṃ vicūrṇayet |
mriyate nātra sandehaḥ sarvayogeṣu yojayet || 2,4.35 ||
<tāmrabhasma (6)>
jīrṇaṃ tāmraṃ samādāya tatra caitānvinikṣipet |
vaṅgaṃ ghoṣaṃ gajaṃ tīkṣṇasāraṃ kāntaṃ ca ṣaṭsamān || 2,4.36 ||
pratyekaṃ tāmramānena sarvānekatra dhāmayet |
vaṅkanālena tāvattadyāvadarko'vaśiṣyate || 2,4.37 ||
tamarkaṃ ḍhālayetpūrvaṃ nirguṇḍīsalilāntare |
saptavārāṃśca tanmūlacūrṇamarke pravāpayet || 2,4.38 ||
tena tāmreṇa kurvīta vaṭikāmavraṇāṃ śubhām |
dviguṇaṃ gandhakaṃ liptvā balestryaṃśaṃ ca pāradam || 2,4.39 ||
śuddhaṃ hiṅgulajaṃ vāpi mardayedbījapūrakaiḥ |
jambīrajairvā likucanīraiḥ samyakpramardayet || 2,4.40 ||
tridinaṃ tadviśoṣyātha bhāṇḍāntastanniveśayet |
upariṣṭāttāmraghaṭīmadhovaktrāṃ nidhāya ca || 2,4.41 ||
dṛḍhaṃ vilepayetpaścātkaṭorīṃ mṛṇmayīṃ kṣipet |
atiprayatnāllimpettāṃ yathā vārā na bhidyate || 2,4.42 ||
adhiculli tato dattvā pūrayitvā jalairadhaḥ |
prajvālayedvītihotraṃ mṛdumadhyottamakramāt || 2,4.43 ||
dināni ṣaṭsamādāya svāṅgaśītaṃ tu pātrakam |
nirbhidya śulvaṃ gṛhṇīta mṛtaṃ sūtena yatnataḥ || 2,4.44 ||
tataḥ khalve vicūrṇyaitadyatheṣṭaṃ viniyojayet |
<tāmrabhasmaśuddhiḥ>
nānāvidhaṃ mṛtaṃ tāmraṃ śuddhyarthaṃ bhāgapañcakam || 2,4.45 ||
bhāgaikaṃ śvetakācaṃ ca bhāgaṃ śvetaṃ ca ṭaṅkaṇam |
mūṣāyāṃ miśritaṃ kṣiptvā bhāgaikaṃ cābhrapatrakam || 2,4.46 ||
ūrdhvaṃ dattvā nirudhyātha dhmāte grāhyaṃ suśītalam |
nirdoṣaṃ tu bhavettāmraṃ sarvarogaharaṃ bhavet || 2,4.47 ||
athavā māritaṃ tāmramamlenaikena mardayet |
tadgolaṃ sūraṇasyāntaḥ ruddhvā sarvatra lepayet || 2,4.48 ||
śuṣkaṃ gajapuṭe pacyātsarvadoṣaharaṃ bhavet |
vāntiṃ bhrāntiṃ virekaṃ ca na karoti kadācana || 2,4.49 ||
<tāmrabhasma (7)>
vilipya likucadrāvapiṣṭagandhāśmapaṅkataḥ |
tāmrapatrāṇi saṃchādya sthālīmadhye nirudhya ca || 2,4.50 ||
yāmamātraṃ pacetsamyakśītānyākṛṣya cūrṇayet |
tadbhasma vallamātraṃ hi tāmbūlīdalaveṣṭitam || 2,4.51 ||
bhakṣitaṃ vāmayitvātha recayitvā dviyāmataḥ |
jvaraṃ vināśayennḥṇāṃ śūlādhmānasamanvitam || 2,4.52 ||
viṣaṃ garaṃ ca vegena vāmayatyeva niścitam |
pathyamatra pradātavyaṃ gavyaṃ takraṃ ca bhaktakam || 2,4.53 ||
atireke'tivāntau ca samohe cātimātrake |
tattadaucityayogena kuryācchītāṃ pratikriyām || 2,4.54 ||
ativāntau bhajedbhraṣṭamikṣudaṇḍaṃ suśītalam |
yadvā bilvabhavaṃ kvāthaṃ sitayā saha pācayet || 2,4.55 ||
barbūratvagrasaḥ peyo vireke takrasaṃyutaḥ |
<tāmrabhasma (8)>
śulbatulyena sūtena balinā tatsamena ca || 2,4.56 ||
tadardhāṃśena tālena śilayā ca tadardhayā |
vidhāya kajjalīṃ ślakṣṇāṃ bhinnakajjalasannibhām || 2,4.57 ||
yantrādhyāyavinirdiṣṭagarbhayantrodarāntare |
kajjalīṃ tāmrapatrāṇi paryāyeṇa vinikṣipet || 2,4.58 ||
prapacedyāmaparyantaṃ svāṅgaśītaṃ vicūrṇayet |
tattadrogaharānupānasahitaṃ tāmraṃ dvivallonmitaṃ sallīḍhaṃ pariṇāmaśūlamudaraṃ śūlaṃ ca pāṇḍujvaram |
gulmaplīhayakṛtkṣayāgnisadanaṃ mehaṃ ca mūlāmayaṃ duṣṭāṃ ca grahaṇīṃ hareddhruvamidaṃ sarvāmayadhvaṃsanam || 2,4.59 ||
tāmraṃ tiktakaṣāyakaṃ ca madhuraṃ pāke'tha vīryoṣṇakaṃ koṣṇaṃ pittakaphāpahaṃ jaṭhararukkuṣṭhāmajūrtyantakṛt |
ūrdhvādhaḥpariśodhanaṃ ca viṣahṛtsthaulyāpahaṃ kṣutkaraṃ durnāmakṣayapāṇḍurogaśamanaṃ netryaṃ paraṃ lekhanam || 2,4.60 ||


āk, 2, 5
śrībhairavaḥ |
<lohotpattiḥ>
kāntāyastīkṣṇamuṇḍākhyalohotpattirathocyate |
devāsurasamūhena mathyamāne mahodadhau || 2,5.1 ||
samutpannaṃ purā tasminnamṛtaṃ devajīvanam |
pīyamānātsuraistasmānnipetuḥ kṣudrabindavaḥ || 2,5.2 ||
jñātvā tān bhūgatān yantā gopayāmāsa dhūrjaṭiḥ |
lohapāṣāṇarūpeṇa kṛtvā tānvasudhātale || 2,5.3 ||
pāparogābhibhūtā ye mānavā na bhajantvamūn |
ityevaṃ śivaguptā ye sudhāyā bindavaḥ purā || 2,5.4 ||
teṣāṃ mayā samāsena kathyate sādhanaṃ sphuṭam |
kuṣṭhādiroganāśārthaṃ yathābuddhyanusārataḥ || 2,5.5 ||
<iron:: subtypes>
kāntāyastīkṣṇamuṇḍākhyaṃ lohamevamapi tridhā |
rasāyanebhyaḥ sarvebhyo vidyācchataguṇādhikam || 2,5.6 ||
<kāntaloha:: synonyms>
ayaskāntaṃ kāntalohaṃ kāntaṃ syātkṛṣṇalohakam |
kāntāyasaṃ mahālohaṃ kālalohaṃ ca saptadhā || 2,5.7 ||
<aśuddhakāntalohasevanajā doṣāḥ>
hṛtpīḍāṃ vahnidaurbalyaṃ mahārogānmṛtiṃ tathā |
karoti sevanāllohamaśodhitamamāritam || 2,5.8 ||
<kāntagrahaṇavidhiḥ>
tasmātkāntasya saṃśuddhirmāraṇaṃ ca vidhīyate |
madonmattagajaḥ sūtaḥ kāntamaṅkuśamucyate || 2,5.9 ||
kṣetraṃ jñātvā gṛhītavyaṃ tatprayatnena bhūyasā |
mārutātapanikṣiptaṃ varjayetsurasundari || 2,5.10 ||
bhūmisthitaṃ ca yatkāntaṃ chāgaraktena bhāvayet |
chāgaraktapraliptena carmaṇā tatpraveṣṭayet || 2,5.11 ||
chāgacarma parīveṣṭya vinyasetpūrvavatkṣitau |
uddhṛtaṃ saptabhirmāsaistoyakumbhe vinikṣipet || 2,5.12 ||
raktapuṣpaiḥ sadā pūjyaṃ raktagandhānulepanaiḥ |
pūritaṃ madyamāṃsaiśca yojyaṃ rasarasāyane || 2,5.13 ||
saṃskṛtaṃ chāgaraktena bhrāmakaṃ cumbakaṃ bhavet |
anena kramayogena drāvakaṃ bhavati priye || 2,5.14 ||
kāntalohaṃ vinā sūto dehe na krāmati kvacit |
na kāntena vinā sūtaḥ kāntaḥ sūtavivarjitaḥ || 2,5.15 ||
kāntasūtasamāyuktaḥ prayogo dehadhārakaḥ |
purā proktaṃ mayā sarvaṃ kāntasatvaṃ yathāvidhi || 2,5.16 ||
adhunā sampravakṣyāmi kāntasaṃskāratadguṇān |
<kāntaloha:: śodhana:: naisargika>
śaśaraktena saṃliptaṃ kāntapatraṃ sudhāmitam || 2,5.17 ||
trivāraṃ cāgninā devi doṣaṃ naisargikaṃ tyajet |
<kāntaloha:: śodhana:: adri>
triphalāṣṭaguṇe toye triphalā ṣoḍaśaṃ palam || 2,5.18 ||
tatkvāthapādaśeṣaṃ ca kāntasya palapañcakam |
kṛtvā patrāṇi taptāni saptavāraṃ niṣecayet || 2,5.19 ||
eva pralīyate doṣo girijo lohasaṃbhavaḥ |
kāntalohamayaṃ cūrṇaṃ gomūtre'ṣṭaguṇe pacet || 2,5.20 ||
dhānyāmlaiḥ kṣālitaṃ bhūyaḥ śoṣitaṃ śuddhimāpnuyāt |
ratnamālā haṃsapādī gojihvā triphalāmṛtā || 2,5.21 ||
gopālī tumbururdantī gomūtre peṣayedimāḥ |
tatkalke kāntapatraṃ tu taptaṃ taptaṃ dvisaptadhā || 2,5.22 ||
secayetkāntalohaṃ tu sarvadoṣanivṛttaye |
<kāntasiddhikramaḥ>
kramaḥ kāntasya siddhyarthaṃ kartavyo mantrapūrvakam || 2,5.23 ||
udbhavodbhavaśabdaḥ syāccaturthyanto'mṛtātparam |
svāhāntaḥ praṇavaścādau mantro'yaṃ mardane sthitaḥ || 2,5.24 ||
rakṣāyai loharasayorayamevaikamudbhavam |
vinā svāheti tasyānte phaḍantaṃ yojayetpriye || 2,5.25 ||
praṇavordhvaṃ namaścaṇḍaṃ vajrapāṣāṇa ityapi |
tataḥ paraṃ mahāvajrasenādhipataye namaḥ || 2,5.26 ||
dviruktasaruśabdaḥ syānmahāvidyābalāya ca |
tatastvāheti mantro'yaṃ balikarmaṇi pūjitaḥ || 2,5.27 ||
anenaiva baliṃ kṛtvā yathoktāmācaretkriyām |
<lohabhasmavidhiḥ (1)>
stanyena hiṅgulasyātha peṣayetpalapañcakam || 2,5.28 ||
tenaiva patraṃ kāntasya limpetpañcapalonmitam |
ruddhvā gajapuṭe pācyaṃ kaṣāyaistraiphalaiḥ punaḥ || 2,5.29 ||
jambīrairāranalairvā viṃśatyaṃśena hiṅgulam |
piṣṭvālipya puṭe ruddhvā tallohe pācayetpunaḥ || 2,5.30 ||
ruddhaṃ puṭetpacedrātrau prātardrāvaiśca bhāvayet |
evamaṣṭadinaṃ kuryāttrividhaṃ mriyate hyayaḥ || 2,5.31 ||
<lohabhasmavidhiḥ (2)>
ciñcāpatranibhaṃ kuryātkāntaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ ca muṇḍakam |
mṛtpātrasthaṃ kṣipedgharme dantīdrāvaiḥ prapūrayet || 2,5.32 ||
patraṃ punaḥ punastāvadyāvattarati tatsvayam |
mriyate tīvragharmeṇa taccūrṇīkṛtya yojayet || 2,5.33 ||
<lohabhasmavidhiḥ (3)>
kāntāyastīkṣṇamuṇḍānāṃ cūrṇaṃ matsyākṣijairdravaiḥ |
ātape tridinaṃ bhāvyaṃ tridinaṃ citrakadravaiḥ || 2,5.34 ||
tryahaṃ trikaṇṭakarasaiḥ sahadevīdravairdinam |
gomūtraistriphalākvāthairbhāvayecca tryahaṃ tryaham || 2,5.35 ||
uktadrāvaistato mardyaṃ kramāddeyaṃ puṭaṃ puṭam |
ruddhvā gajapuṭairevaṃ mṛtaṃ yogeṣu yojayet || 2,5.36 ||
<lohabhasmavidhiḥ (4)>
sthālyāṃ vā lohapātre vā lohadarvyā vicālayan |
pācayettriphalākvāthairdinaṃ lohacūrṇakam || 2,5.37 ||
tatpiṇḍaṃ triphalātoyaiḥ piṣṭvā ruddhvā puṭe pacet |
ṣoḍaśāṅgulagartāntarnirvāte'harniśaṃ pacet || 2,5.38 ||
evaṃ tridhā prakartavyaṃ sthālīpākaṃ puṭāntakam |
bhṛṅgārdrakaṃ tālamūlaṃ hastikarṇyāśca mūlakam || 2,5.39 ||
śatāvarīvidāryośca mūlaṃ tattriphalāmbhasi |
saṃpeṣya pūrvavatsthālyāṃ pācyaṃ peṣyaṃ tridhā puṭaiḥ || 2,5.40 ||
pācyaṃ tataḥ punarnavyāḥ kvāthaiśca daśamūlataḥ |
bṛhatyāśca kaṣāyeṇa bījapūrasya ca dravaiḥ || 2,5.41 ||
brahmabījarasaiḥ śigrukvāthairgopayasāpi ca |
pratyekaṃ saṃprapeṣyādau pūrvagarte puṭe pacet || 2,5.42 ||
bhāvayettaddraveṇaiva puṭānte yāmamātrakam |
pratyekamevaṃ saṃpeṣya puṭayedbhāvayetkramāt || 2,5.43 ||
mriyate nātra sandehaḥ kāntaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ ca muṇḍakam |
<lohabhasmavidhiḥ (5)>
śuddhasūtaṃ dvidhā gandhaṃ khalve kṛtvā tu kajjalīm || 2,5.44 ||
dvayoḥ samaṃ kāntacūrṇaṃ mardayetkanyakādravaiḥ |
yāmadvayātsamuddhṛtya tadgolaṃ tāmrapātrake || 2,5.45 ||
ācchādyairaṇḍapatraiśca yāmārdhe'tyuṣṇatāṃ gatam |
dhānyarāśau nyasetpaścāttridinānte samuddharet || 2,5.46 ||
saṃpeṣya gālayedvastre satyaṃ vāritaraṃ bhavet |
kāntaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ ca muṇḍaṃ ca nirutthaṃ jāyate mṛtam || 2,5.47 ||
svarṇādīnmārayedevaṃ cūrṇīkuryācca lohavat |
siddhayogamathākhyātaṃ siddhānāṃ saṃmukhāgatam || 2,5.48 ||
anubhūtaṃ mayā devi sarvarogāpahārakam |
nāyaḥ pacetpañcapalapramāṇādadho na cordhvaṃ tridaśapramāṇāt || 2,5.49 ||
<lohabhasmavidhiḥ (6)>
arjunasya tvacaḥ peṣyāḥ kāñjikenātilolitāḥ |
tanmadhye kāntacūrṇaṃ ca kāṃsyapātre vinikṣipet || 2,5.50 ||
dinaikaṃ dhārayedgharme dravaiḥ pūryaṃ punaḥ punaḥ |
arjunaiḥ sāranālairvā trividhaṃ mārayedayaḥ || 2,5.51 ||
dravaiḥ kukkuṭapatrotthaiḥ kāntacūrṇaṃ vimardayet |
dinaṃ ca hyātape tīvre dravairmardyaṃ trikaṇṭajaiḥ || 2,5.52 ||
vandhyābhṛṅgapunarnavyāḥ sagomūtrairdinaṃ punaḥ |
gomūtraistriphalā kvāthyā tatkaṣāyeṇa bhāvayet || 2,5.53 ||
trisaptāhaṃ prayatnena dinaikaṃ mardayettataḥ |
ruddhvā gajapuṭe pacyāddinaṃ kvāthena mardayet || 2,5.54 ||
divā mardyaṃ puṭedrātrāvekaviṃśaddināvadhi |
ekaviṃśatpuṭairevaṃ mriyate trividhaṃ hyayaḥ || 2,5.55 ||
<lohabhasmavidhiḥ (8)>
mākṣikaṃ ca śilā hyamlairharidrā maricāṅghri ca |
piṣṭvā lepyaṃ kāntapatraṃ taptaṃ taptaṃ niṣecayet || 2,5.56 ||
saptadhā traiphale kvāthe jalena kṣālayetpunaḥ |
kuṭṭayellohadaṇḍena peṣayettraiphale jale || 2,5.57 ||
ṣoḍaśāṃśena lohasya dātavyaṃ mākṣikaṃ śilā |
amlena marditaṃ ruddhvā gajākhyaikapuṭe pacet || 2,5.58 ||
nirutthaṃ jāyate bhasma kāntaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ ca muṇḍakam |
<lohabhasmavidhiḥ (9)>
tindūphalasya majjāyāṃ khaḍgaṃ liptvātape khare || 2,5.59 ||
dhārayetkāṃsyapātrāntardinaikena sphuṭatyalam |
lepaṃ punaḥ punaḥ kuryāddinānte taṃ prapeṣayet || 2,5.60 ||
triphalākvāthasaṃyuktaṃ dinaikena mṛto bhavet |
toyāṣṭabhāgaśeṣeṇa triphalā palapañcakam || 2,5.61 ||
ghṛtaṃ kvāthasya tulyaṃ syādghṛtatulyaṃ mṛtāyasam |
pācayettāmrapātre tu lohadarvyā vicālayan || 2,5.62 ||
mṛdvagninā pacedyāvattāvajjīryati taddravam |
lohatulyā sitā yojyā supakvāmavatārayet || 2,5.63 ||
yogavāhamidaṃ khyātaṃ mṛtalohaṃ mahāmṛtam |
itthaṃ kāntasya tīkṣṇasya muṇḍasyāpi hyayaṃ vidhiḥ || 2,5.64 ||
ghṛtatulyaṃ mṛtaṃ lohaṃ lohapātragataṃ pacet |
jīrṇe ghṛte samādāya sarvayogeṣu yojayet || 2,5.65 ||
omomamṛtendra bhakṣayāmi namaḥ |
pātre yasminpraviśati jale tailabindurna sarpethiṅgurgandhaṃ visṛjati nijāṃ tiktatāṃ nimbakalkaḥ |
pāke dugdhaṃ bhavati śikharākāratā naiva bhūmau kāntaṃ lohaṃ tadidamuditaṃ lakṣaṇoktaṃ na cānyat || 2,5.66 ||
<lohapākasvarūpam>
lohapākastridhā prokto mṛdumadhyakharātmakaḥ |
paṅkotkarasamaḥ pūrvaḥ piṇḍapākastu madhyamaḥ || 2,5.67 ||
tṛtīyaḥ kharapākaḥ syādvālukāsadṛśaḥ priye |
<nirutthabhasmaparīkṣā>
pākaṃ varṇaṃ tathā gandhaṃ jñātvā lohasya vahnitaḥ || 2,5.68 ||
uttāryātha tataḥ śuddhaṃ lohapātrāntare nyaset |
lohamātaṅgapañcāsyā gandhamākṣīkahiṅgulāḥ || 2,5.69 ||
<loha:: checkingthe vāritara state>
sarvametanmṛtaṃ lohaṃ dhmātavyaṃ mitrapañcakaiḥ |
yadyevaṃ syānnirutthānaṃ sevyaṃ vāritaraṃ hitam || 2,5.70 ||
madhvājyena samāyuktaṃ lohaṃ tāreṇa saṃyutam |
andhamūṣāgataṃ dhmātaṃ gṛhṇīyātsvāṅgaśītalam || 2,5.71 ||
lohamadhyagataṃ tāraṃ pūrvamānaṃ bhavedyadi |
tadā lohaṃ nirutthaṃ syādanyathā sādhayetpunaḥ || 2,5.72 ||
gandhakaṃ cotthitaṃ lohaṃ tulyaṃ khalve vimardayet |
dinaikaṃ kanyakādrāvai ruddhvā gajapuṭe pacet || 2,5.73 ||
ityevaṃ sarvalohānāṃ kartavyetthaṃ nirutthitiḥ |
<kāntādibhasmaguṇāḥ>
kāntasindūramāyuṣyamārogyaṃ balavīryadam || 2,5.74 ||
dehadārḍhyakaraṃ śreṣṭhaṃ kuryādindriyapāṭavam |
tridoṣaśamanaṃ rājayakṣmarogavināśanam || 2,5.75 ||
jvarāsṛkpittapāṇḍvādīn hanyādaṣṭamahāgadān |
yadyadrogaharairyogaistattadrogaharaṃ bhavet || 2,5.76 ||
<tīkṣṇaloha:: synonyms>
tīkṣṇaṃ śastrāyasaṃ śastraṃ piṇḍaṃ piṇḍāyasaṃ śaṭham |
āyasaṃ niśitaṃ tīvraṃ lohakhaḍgaṃ ca muṇḍajam || 2,5.77 ||
ayaścitrāyasaṃ proktaṃ cīnajaṃ ca tripañcadhā |
<muṇḍaloha:: synonyms>
muṇḍaṃ muṇḍāyasaṃ lohaṃ kṛṣṇalauhaṃ śilodbhavam || 2,5.78 ||
<tīkṣṇa-, muṇḍaloha:: māraṇa>
kāntalohaprakāreṇa māraṇaṃ tīkṣṇamuṇḍayoḥ |
<tīkṣṇaloha:: medic. properties>
tīkṣṇalohaṃ kaṣāyāmlaṃ tiktakaṃ kaṭukaṃ laghu || 2,5.79 ||
śītalaṃ lekhanaṃ rūkṣaṃ kaphapittāsrapāṇḍujit |
pramehasarvaśūlaghnaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ muṇḍādhikaṃ guṇaiḥ || 2,5.80 ||
<muṇḍa:: medic. properties>
cakṣuṣyaṃ muṇḍalohaṃ tu kaṣāyaṃ svādu tiktakam |
lekhanaṃ vātahṛcchītaṃ kṛmipittakaphapraṇut || 2,5.81 ||
śvayathūdaraśūlārśaḥkuṣṭhapāṇḍupramehajit || 2,5.82 ||


āk, 2, 6
śrībhairavaḥ |
<tin:: subtypes>
khurakaṃ miśrakaṃ ceti dvividhaṃ vaṅgamucyate |
khuraṃ tatra guṇaiḥ śreṣṭhaṃ guṇahīnaṃ tu miśrakam || 2,6.1 ||
<khura:: phys. properties>
dhavalaṃ mṛdulaṃ snigdhaṃ drutadrāvaṃ sagauravam |
<miśraka:: phys. properties>
niḥśabdaṃ khuravaṅgaṃ syānmiśrakaṃ śyāmaśubhrakam || 2,6.2 ||
<tin:: synonyms>
trapu trapusamārūpaṃ vaṅgaṃ ca kuṭilaṃ himam |
kurūpyaṃ viccaṭaṃ raṅgaṃ pūtigandhaṃ rasāhvayam || 2,6.3 ||
<tin:: parīkṣā:: goodṝuality>
svacchamuṣṇāsahaṃ śītaṃ sūkṣmapatrakaraṃ laghu |
ruṅnāśe rūpyakaraṇe tadvaṅgaṃ śreṣṭhamucyate || 2,6.4 ||
<vaṅgaśuddhi (1)>
drāvayitvā niśāyukte kṣiptaṃ nirguṇḍikārasaiḥ |
viśudhyati trivāreṇa khuravaṅgaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 2,6.5 ||
amlatakravinipiṣṭavarṣābhūvṛṣasindhubhiḥ |
kaṭvalābugataṃ vaṅgaṃ dvitayaṃ pariśudhyati || 2,6.6 ||
satālenārkadugdhena liptvā vaṅgadalānyatha |
bodhiciñcātvacaḥ kṣārairdadyāllaghupuṭāni ca || 2,6.7 ||
mardayitvā caredbhasma tadrasādiṣu kīrtitam |
<vaṅgaśuddhi (2)>
nāgavacchodhayedvaṅgaṃ tadvadaśvatthaciñcayoḥ || 2,6.8 ||
tadbhasma haritālaṃ ca tulyamamlena mardayet |
palāśotthadravairvātha lolayitvāndhrayetpuṭe || 2,6.9 ||
uddhṛtya daśamāṃśena tālena saha mardayet |
<vaṅgaśuddhi (3)>
pūrvadrāvaiḥ sahāloḍya ruddhvā gajapuṭe pacet || 2,6.10 ||
ekaviṃśatpuṭairvaṅgo mṛto bhavati rogahā |
śirīṣarajanīcūrṇaiḥ kumāryā yutagolakam || 2,6.11 ||
sūtaliptaṃ vaṅgapatraṃ golakaṃ samamarditam |
ruddhvā laghupuṭaiḥ pakvaṃ mṛtaṃ syātpūrvasaṃkhyayā || 2,6.12 ||
akṣabhallātakītoyaiḥ piṣṭvā patrāṇi lepayet |
tatastilakharīmadhye kṣiptvā ruddhvā puṭaiḥ pacet || 2,6.13 ||
catvāriṃśadgajapuṭairvaṅgakaṃ bhasma jāyate |
<vaṅgaguṇāḥ>
vaṅgaṃ tīkṣṇoṣṇakaṭukamīṣadvātaprakopanam || 2,6.14 ||
medaḥśleṣmāmayaghnaṃ ca krimighnaṃ mehanāśanam |
himaṃ kaṣāyalavaṇaṃ pāṇḍudāhaharaṃ saram || 2,6.15 ||
lekhanaṃ pittalaṃ kiṃcittrapu sīsaṃ ca tadguṇam |
<sīsaka>
sīsakaṃ tu jaḍaṃ śītaṃ yavaneṣṭaṃ bhujaṅgamam || 2,6.16 ||
yogīṣṭaṃ nāgamuragaṃ kuvaṅgaṃ paripiṣṭakam |
mṛdu kṛṣṇāyasaṃ pakṣmatāraśuddhikaraṃ smṛtam || 2,6.17 ||
sirāvṛttaṃ ca vaṅgaṃ syāccīnapiṣṭaṃ ca ṣoḍaśa |
sīsaṃ tu vaṅgasāmyaṃ syādrasavīryavipākataḥ || 2,6.18 ||
<aśuddhavaṅganāgasevane doṣāḥ>
pākahīnau nāgavaṅgau kuṣṭhagulmarujākarau |
mehapāṇḍujvaraśleṣmavātapittapradau smṛtau || 2,6.19 ||
drutadrāvaṃ mahābhāraṃ chede kṛṣṇaṃ samujjvalam |
pūtigandhaṃ bahiḥ kṛṣṇaṃ śuddhaṃ sīsamato'nyathā || 2,6.20 ||
nirguṇḍīmūlacūrṇena sārkadugdhena lepayet |
nāgapatraṃ tataḥ śuṣkaṃ drāvayitvā niṣecayet || 2,6.21 ||
liptvā drāvyaṃ punaḥ secyaṃ saptavāraṃ viśuddhaye |
niśātumburubījāni kokilākṣīkuberakam || 2,6.22 ||
gaurīphalaṃ mallikā ca mokṣo brahmā mayūrakam |
yathālābhaṃ tu bhasmaiṣāṃ vajrīkṣīreṇa bhāvayet || 2,6.23 ||
tanmadhye drāvitaṃ nāgaṃ śuddhaṃ secyaṃ tu saptadhā |
mahiṣasyāsthicūrṇena supākaṃ mūtrasecanāt || 2,6.24 ||
vaṅgaṃ śuddhaṃ bhavettadvannāgo nāgāsthimūtrataḥ |
<nāgabhasma (1)>
aśvatthaciñcātvagbhasma nāgasya caturaṃśataḥ || 2,6.25 ||
kṣiptvā cullyāṃ pacedagnau cālayellohacaṭṭunā |
yāvadbhasma tamuddhṛtya bhasmatulyāṃ manaḥśilām || 2,6.26 ||
jambīrairāranālairvā piṣṭvā ruddhvā puṭe pacet |
svāṅgaśītaṃ punaḥ piṣṭvā viṃśatyaṃśe śilāmlake || 2,6.27 ||
evaṃ ṣaṣṭipuṭaiḥ paktvā nāgaḥ syāttu nirutthitaḥ |
<nāgabhasma (2)>
athavā nāgapatrāṇi cūrṇaliptāni kharpare || 2,6.28 ||
alpāgnau pācayedyāmaṃ bhasma taccitrakadravaiḥ |
bharjayellohapātre tatpārthadaṇḍena cālayan || 2,6.29 ||
yāmaṣoḍaśaparyantaṃ dravaṃ deyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ |
daṇḍena mardayedgāḍhamuddhṛtaṃ citrakadavaiḥ || 2,6.30 ||
lolayitvā nirudhyātha ṣaṭpuṭairmriyate laghu |
<nāgabhasma (3)>
ciñcākṣamikṣubhallātavāśāvajralatābhavaiḥ || 2,6.31 ||
apāmārgārjunāśvatthasamudbhūtaiśca bhasmabhiḥ |
dṛḍhaṃ pālāśadaṇḍena lohapātre tu bharjayet || 2,6.32 ||
saptabhirdivasaireva mriyate nātra saṃśaya |
<nāgabhasma (4)>
piṣṭvāgastyaṃ ca bhūnāgaṃ liptvā bhāṇḍaṃ viśodhayet || 2,6.33 ||
tadbhāṇḍe drāvayennāgaṃ drute nāge vinikṣipet |
vāśāciñcaṭayoḥ kṣāraṃ vāśākāṣṭhena ghaṭṭayet || 2,6.34 ||
yāmaikaṃ pācayeccullyāṃ samuddhṛtya vimiśrayet |
saṃcūrṇya ca śilātāpye vāśakakṣārasaṃyutam || 2,6.35 ||
catustulyaṃ pūrvanāgaṃ viṃśatyekapuṭaiḥ pacet |
dvipuṭaṃ ciñcikakṣārairdeyaṃ vāśārasānvitaiḥ || 2,6.36 ||
nāgaḥ sindūravarṇābho mriyate sarvakāryakṛt |
atyuṣṇaṃ sīsakaṃ snigdhaṃ tiktaṃ vātakaphāpaham || 2,6.37 ||
pramehatoyadoṣaghnaṃ dīpanaṃ cāmavātanut || 2,6.38 ||


āk, 2, 7
śrībhairavaḥ |
<brass:: synonyms>
rītiḥ kṣudrasuvarṇaṃ sitakanakaṃ piṅgalaṃ ca pittalakam |
lohitakamārakūṭaṃ piṅgalalohaṃ ca pītakaṃ navadhā || 2,7.1 ||
<brass:: subtypes>
rītikā kākatuṇḍīti dvividhaṃ pittalaṃ bhavet |
<brass:: parīkṣā>
saṃtaptā kāñjike kṣiptā tāmrābhā rītikā matā || 2,7.2 ||
evaṃ yā jāyate kṛṣṇā kākatuṇḍīti sā matā |
<brass:: parīkṣā:: goodṝuality>
gurvī mṛdvī ca pītākapotābhā sārāṅgī tāḍanakṣamā || 2,7.3 ||
susnigdhā masṛṇāṅgī ca rītiretādṛśī śubhā |
śuddhā snigdhā mṛduḥ śītā suraṅgā sūtrapattriṇī || 2,7.4 ||
hemopamā śubhāśvatthā jātyā rītiḥ prakīrtitā |
<brass:: parīkṣā:: badṝuality>
pāṇḍuḥ pītā kharā rūkṣā barbarā ghaṭṭanākṣamā || 2,7.5 ||
pūtigandhā tathā laghvī rītirneṣṭā rasādiṣu |
<rītiguṇāḥ>
rītistiktarasā rūkṣā jantughnī sāsrapittanut || 2,7.6 ||
kṛṣṇā kuṣṭhaharā yogāduṣṇavīryā ca śītalā |
<rājarīti:: properties>
kākatuṇḍī kṛtasnehā rājarītiguṇānugā || 2,7.7 ||
rājarītistu nirdiṣṭā rītikāsadṛśī guṇaiḥ |
<brass:: medic. properties>
rītikāyugalaṃ rūkṣaṃ satiktaṃ lavaṇaṃ saram || 2,7.8 ||
śobhanaṃ pāṇḍuvātaghnaṃ balavīryāyurvardhanam |
rītikā kaṭutiktoṣṇā plīhānāhanibarhaṇī || 2,7.9 ||
rūkṣā kaphāsrapittaṃ ca hanyātsvādu prayojitā |
<rājarīti:: synonyms>
rājarītiḥ kākatuṇḍī rājaputrī maheśvarī || 2,7.10 ||
brāhmaṇī brahmarītiśca kapilā piṅgalāpi ca |
<kāṃsya>
kāṃsyaṃ saurāṣṭrikaṃ ghoṣaṃ kaṃsīyaṃ vahnilohakam || 2,7.11 ||
dīptalohaṃ ghoṣayuṣyaṃ dīptakaṃ ca navāhvayam |
<bronśe:: production>
aṣṭabhāgena tāmreṇa dvibhāgakuṭilena ca || 2,7.12 ||
vidrutena bhavetkāṃsyaṃ tatsaurāṣṭrabhavaṃ śubham |
<bronśe:: parīkṣā:: goodṝuality>
tīkṣṇaśabdaṃ mṛdu snigdhamīṣacchyāmalaśubhrakam || 2,7.13 ||
nirmalaṃ dāharaktaṃ ca ṣoḍhā kāṃsyaṃ praśasyate |
śvetadīptaṃ mṛdujyoti śabdāḍhyaṃ snigdhanirmalam || 2,7.14 ||
ghanāgnisahasūtrāṅgaṃ kāṃsyamuttamamīritam |
<bronśe:: parīkṣā:: badṝuality>
tatpītaṃ dahane tāmraṃ ghanaṃ rūkṣaṃ ghanāsaham || 2,7.15 ||
mandanādaṃ gatajyoti saptadhā kāṃsyamutsṛjet |
<bronśe:: medic. properties>
ghṛtamekaṃ vinā cānyatsarvaṃ kāṃsyagataṃ nṛṇām || 2,7.16 ||
bhuktamārogyasukhadaṃ hitaṃ sātmyakaraṃ tathā |
kāṃsyaṃ tu laghutiktoṣṇaṃ lekhanaṃ dṛkprasādanam || 2,7.17 ||
kṛmikoṣṭhaharaṃ vātapittaghnaṃ bhojane hitam |
<vartaloha>
vartalohaṃ vartatīkṣṇaṃ vartakaṃ lohasaṃkaraḥ || 2,7.18 ||
nīlikā nīlalohaṃ ca lohakaṃ vaṭṭalohakam |
kāṃsyārkarītilohāhijātaṃ tadvartalohakam || 2,7.19 ||
tadeva pañcalohākhyaṃ lohavidbhirudāhṛtam |
tadbhāṇḍasādhitaṃ sarvamanyavyañjanapūrvakam || 2,7.20 ||
amlena varjitaṃ cātidīpanaṃ pācanaṃ śubham |
<vartalohaśuddhimāraṇam>
drutamaśvajale kṣiptaṃ vartalohaṃ viśudhyati || 2,7.21 ||
mriyate gandhatālābhyāṃ puṭitaṃ vartalohakam |
teṣu teṣu vibhāgeṣu yojanīyaṃ yathāvidhi || 2,7.22 ||
vartalohaṃ kaṭūṣṇaṃ ca tiktaṃ ca śiśiraṃ tathā |
kaphajitpittaśamanaṃ madhuraṃ dāhamehanut || 2,7.23 ||
rūkṣaṃ rucyaṃ kṛmighnaṃ ca netryaṃ malaviśodhanam |
<pittalādīnāṃ śodhanabhasmavidhiḥ>
trikṣāraṃ pañcalavaṇaṃ saptadhāmlena bhāvayet || 2,7.24 ||
kāṃsyāraghoṣapatrāṇi tena kalkena lepayet |
ruddhvā puṭedgajapuṭe śuddhimāyāti nānyathā || 2,7.25 ||
tāmravanmāraṇaṃ teṣāṃ kṛtvā sarvatra yojayet |
mṛtāni lohānyamṛtībhavanti nighnanti yuktāni mahāmayāṃśca |
abhyāsayogāddṛḍhadehasiddhiṃ kurvanti rugjanmajarāvināśam || 2,7.26 ||
nāgena svarṇaṃ rajataṃ ca tāpyaiḥ gandhena tāmraṃ śilayā ca nāgam |
tālena vaṅgaṃ trividhaṃ tu lohaṃ nārīpayo ghnanti ca hiṅgulena || 2,7.27 ||
tathābhrasatvaṃ balinopalohaṃ vajreṇa sūtaṃ vinihanti sadyaḥ || 2,7.28 ||
<abhrakasattva>
vajrābhrakasya satsatvaṃ vakṣyāmi śṛṇu bhairavi |
caturdhābhrakasatvaṃ syātkaṭhinaṃ mṛdulaṃ drutiḥ || 2,7.29 ||
bījaṃ ca guṇavajjñeyaṃ tatkramāduttarottaram |
<sattvapātanāyābhrakasiddhiḥ>
dhānyābhrakaṃ ravikṣīre gharme sthāpyaṃ dināvadhi || 2,7.30 ||
tacchlakṣṇaṃ peṣayetpaṭṭe śarāve taṃ nirodhayet |
samyakpacedgajapuṭe ravikṣīre pacetpunaḥ || 2,7.31 ||
mardanaṃ puṭapākaṃ ca kuryādevaṃ tu saptadhā |
tathā jambīranīre ca niculasya rase tathā || 2,7.32 ||
etatsiddhaghanaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ niścandraṃ sattvapātane |
ghanamārakasārairvā vyastairvātha samastakaiḥ || 2,7.33 ||
piṣṭvā dhānyābhrakaṃ ślakṣṇaṃ ruddhvā gajapuṭe pacet |
lākṣāgugguludagdhorṇāsarjasarjarasaṃ paṭu || 2,7.34 ||
śaśāsthi kṣudramatsyāśca haridrā mitrapañcakam |
pañcamāhiṣakaṃ cāpi bhallātaṃ ca samaṃ samam || 2,7.35 ||
sarvatulyaṃ siddhaghanaṃ niścandraṃ peṣayeddinam |
tatsarvaṃ tena vaṭakāḥ kāryāste karṣamātrakāḥ || 2,7.36 ||
koṣṭhīyantre dhamedgāḍhamaṅgāraiḥ khadirodbhavaiḥ |
triyāmadhamanādeva sattvaṃ patati nirmalam || 2,7.37 ||
kaṭhinaṃ sūkṣmaravakaṃ kācaṭaṅkaṇavarjitam |
trivāramevaṃ kurvīta tatkiṭṭaiḥ sattvapātane || 2,7.38 ||
<abhrakamṛdusattva (1)>
punaranyaṃ pravakṣyāmi mṛdusattvaṃ sureśvari |
gugguluṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ guñjāṃ sarjasarjarasaṃ guḍam || 2,7.39 ||
kṣudramīnaṃ yavakṣāraṃ kācapiṇyākasūraṇam |
bhūlatā triphalā vahniḥ kṣīrakandaṃ punarnavam || 2,7.40 ||
dhuttūraṃ lāṅgalī pārā balā gandhakasevakam |
gokṣīraṃ pañcalavaṇaṃ sarvaṃ ca dviguṇaṃ madhu || 2,7.41 ||
ṣaḍbindu kṣudraśaṃbūkamasthīni śaśakasya ca |
pārāvatamalaṃ tryūṣamindragopaṃ saśigrukam || 2,7.42 ||
godhūmasarṣapaṃ tāpyaṃ chāgakṣīreṇa mardayet |
evaṃ vyastaṃ samastaṃ vā yāmamātreṇa marditam || 2,7.43 ||
asya piṇḍasya bhāgaikaṃ dvibhāgaṃ siddhamabhrakam |
pañcamāhiṣabhāgaikaṃ sarvamekatra loḍayet || 2,7.44 ||
karṣābhā vaṭikāḥ kāryāḥ kiṃcicchāyāviśoṣitāḥ |
khadirāṅgārasaṃtapte koṣṭhīyantre kṣipetkṣipet || 2,7.45 ||
ghuṭīpañcakapañcaiva vaṅkanāle dhameddṛḍham |
mṛdusattvaṃ bhavecchubhraṃ rugjarāmṛtyunāśanam || 2,7.46 ||
trivāramevaṃ kurvīta tatkiṭṭaṃ sattvapātanam |
anena kramayogena kāntasasyakamākṣikam || 2,7.47 ||
kaṭhinoparasāścānyaiḥ śuddhā bhūnāgamṛttikā |
muñcanti drutisaṅghātaṃ gṛhṇantīmaṃ pṛthakpṛthak || 2,7.48 ||
<abhrakamṛdusattva (2)>
abhrasatvaṃ samādāya samāṃśaṃ kācaṭaṅkaṇam |
dattvā dattvā trivāraṃ tadvajramūṣāgataṃ dhamet || 2,7.49 ||
amlavargaṃ snuhīpatraṃ bimbībījaṃ savalkalam |
kalkayettatra saṃtaptaṃ saptavāraṃ niṣecayet || 2,7.50 ||
mṛdu śubhraṃ bhavettasya rugjarāmṛtyunāśanam |
<abhrakamṛdusattvam (3)>
siddhābhraṃ daśabhāgaṃ syācchuddhabhāgaṃ tribhāgakam || 2,7.51 ||
ṭaṅkaṇaṃ mākṣikaṃ sūtaṃ bhāgaikaṃ ca suśodhitam |
ūrṇāsarjayavakṣāraṃ bhāgaṃ bhāgaṃ vimiśrayet || 2,7.52 ||
mardyaṃ mūtrāmlavargābhyāṃ yathā proktaṃ dināvadhi |
ajāpañcāṅgasaṃyuktaṃ pūrvavatsattvapātanam || 2,7.53 ||
kṛtvādāya mṛtaṃ sākṣānnirmalaṃ mṛdu jāyate |
<abhrakabījapāka>
bījapākaṃ pravakṣyāmi ghanasyaitatparaṃ hitam || 2,7.54 ||
siddhābhrakaṃ śatapalaṃ svarṇarūpyārkakāntakam |
rasatrigandhamākṣīkaṃ vimalābhūlatādrijam || 2,7.55 ||
nīlāñjanaṃ ca kaṅkuṣṭhaṃ kāsīsaṃ daradaṃ tathā |
pratyekaṃ daśaniṣkaṃ syāttato gairikaṭaṅkaṇam || 2,7.56 ||
indragopaṃ guḍaṃ guñjā madhu sarpiśca gugguluḥ |
sarjakṣāro yavakṣāro matkuṇā navapañcakam || 2,7.57 ||
kṣudramīnaśaśāsthīni māhiṣaṃ śṛṅgamālakam |
pṛthakpṛthakpañcamūlaṃ pañcamāhiṣaṃ mardayet || 2,7.58 ||
teṣāṃ ca vaṭikā kāryā kiṃcicchāyāviśoṣitā |
pūrvavatpātayetsattvaṃ rugjarādainyamṛtyuhṛt || 2,7.59 ||
bījasatvamidaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ vaidye vāde rasāyane |
<abhrakasattvanirmalīkaraṇa>
nirmalīkaraṇaṃ vakṣye ghanasatvasya pārvati || 2,7.60 ||
tatsatvaṃ kaṇaśaḥ kṛtvā mitrapañcakasaṃyutam |
mūṣāyāṃ tadvinikṣipya kācaṃ tadadharottaram || 2,7.61 ||
ruddhvā haṭhāgnau dhamayetkhadirāṅgārayogataḥ |
evaṃ krameṇa dhamayetsaptadhā nirmalaṃ bhavet || 2,7.62 ||
sasatvaṃ nirmalībhūtaṃ lohakhalve vicūrṇayet |
mardayettriphalākvāthairyāmaṃ gharme viśoṣayet || 2,7.63 ||
pākaṃ puṭaṃ ca vidhivatkuryādevaṃ punaḥ punaḥ |
catvāriṃśatpuṭaṃ kuryādevaṃ mārkavajairdravaiḥ || 2,7.64 ||
pṛthakpañcāmṛtaiḥ pañcapuṭaṃ cekṣurasaistridhā |
udayādityasaṅkāśaṃ ghanasatvaṃ susiddhidam || 2,7.65 ||
<abhrakasattvasindūravidhi (1)>
cūrṇīkuryāllohakhalve nirmalīkṛtamabhrakam |
niṣkāṇāṃ śatakaṃ tasminniṣkaikaṃ gandhakaṃ kṣipet || 2,7.66 ||
peṣayeddinamekaṃ tu kumārīrasayogataḥ |
yāmaṃ tattāpayedgharme tato gajapuṭe pacet || 2,7.67 ||
evaṃ śatapuṭaṃ kuryādgandhaṃ dadyātpunaḥ punaḥ |
sindūrapāṭalacchāyamabhrasatvaṃ bhavecchive || 2,7.68 ||
<abhrakasattvasindūravidhi (2)>
ghanasatvaṃ suvimalaṃ lohakhalve vicūrṇayet |
śataniṣkaṃ tatastasminniṣkārdhaṃ mākṣikaṃ kṣipet || 2,7.69 ||
arkakṣīrairdinaṃ mardya ruddhvā gajapuṭe pacet |
evaṃ śatapuṭaṃ kuryānmākṣīkaṃ ca puṭe puṭe || 2,7.70 ||
etatsyātsattvasindūraṃ rugjarāmṛtyunāśanam |
<abhrakasattvasindūravidhi (3)>
tatsatvaṃ ravakānhitvā lohakhalve subuddhimān || 2,7.71 ||
mardayellohadaṇḍena varākvāthasamanvitam |
yāmamātraṃ khare gharme sthāpayellohapātragam || 2,7.72 ||
sthālīpākaṃ kharaṃ kṛtvā rātrau gajapuṭe pacet |
evaṃ saptapuṭaṃ kuryānmārkavasvarasaistathā || 2,7.73 ||
kumārīsvarasaistadvaccitramūlarasaistathā |
nīlīpatrarasaistadvatpunarnavarasaistathā || 2,7.74 ||
meghanādadravaiḥ sapta kvāthairvaṭajaṭodbhavaiḥ |
evaṃ susūkṣmacūrṇaṃ tu śatāṃśaṃ gandhakaṃ kṣipet || 2,7.75 ||
mardayedāranālena gharme sthālyāṃ puṭe pacet |
evaṃ daśadinaṃ kuryātpunastāpyaṃ śatāṃśataḥ || 2,7.76 ||
kṣiptvāranālaiḥ saṃmardya gharme sthālyāṃ puṭe pacet |
evaṃ daśadinaṃ kuryātpunaḥ pañcāmṛtaiḥ pacet || 2,7.77 ||
pañcapañcekṣujarasaiḥ pañcadhā puṭamācaret |
sindūrābhe'bhrabhasite nikṣipecchuddhahiṅgulam || 2,7.78 ||
viṃśatyaṃśaṃ ghanasyātha stanyaiḥ saṃmardya saṃpuṭe |
ruddhvātha bhūdhare yantre kṣiptvā laghupuṭaṃ vidhet || 2,7.79 ||
evaṃ viṃśatpuṭaṃ kuryāddattvā dattvātha hiṅgulam |
anena kramayogena satvaṃ sindūrasannibham || 2,7.80 ||
ayaṃ sāmānyasaṃskāro viśeṣastu nigadyate |
yadyadrogaharairdravyaistattadrogaharaṃ bhavet || 2,7.81 ||
<abhrakasattvasindūravidhi (4)>
ghanasatvaṃ suvimalaṃ lohakhalve vicūrṇayet |
muṇḍīpatrarasairmardyaṃ dinaṃ gajapuṭe pacet || 2,7.82 ||
evaṃ daśapuṭaṃ kāryaṃ nirguṇḍīsvarasaistathā |
tathā vaṭajaṭākvāthaiḥ peṭārīmūlajai rasaiḥ || 2,7.83 ||
kakubhasya rasaistadvajjambūtvaksvarasaistathā |
evaṃ ṣaṣṭipuṭaṃ kāryaṃ tatastasminvinikṣipet || 2,7.84 ||
viṃśatyaṃśena daradaṃ stanyaiḥ saṃmardayeddinam |
tatastatsampuṭe ruddhvā bhūdhare tu puṭaṃ laghu || 2,7.85 ||
evaṃ viṃśatidhā kuryātsindūrābhaṃ bhaveddhruvam |
<sthālīpāka>
sthālīpākastridhā prokto mṛdurmadhyaḥ kharātmakaḥ || 2,7.86 ||
paṅkotkarasamaḥ pūrvaḥ piṇḍapākastu madhyamaḥ |
tṛtīyaḥ kharapākaḥ syādvālukāsadṛśaḥ priye || 2,7.87 ||
sthālīpāko rase yuktaḥ saṃyukto daradena vā |
mṛdumadhyamapākābhyāṃ kartavyo bhūdhare tataḥ || 2,7.88 ||
kṣiptvā laghupuṭe deyaṃ rasahiṅgulavarjitaḥ |
kharapākaḥ prakartavyo vidhirgajapuṭe pacet || 2,7.89 ||
<lohabhasmanāmamṛtīkaraṇavidhiḥ>
sarveṣāṃ māritānāṃ ca lohānāmabhrakasya ca |
vakṣyāmi paramaṃ guhyamamṛtīkaraṇaṃ śṛṇu || 2,7.90 ||
triphalotthakaṣāyasya bhāgānādāya ṣoḍaśa |
goghṛtasya palānyaṣṭau mṛtābhrakapalāndaśa || 2,7.91 ||
ekīkṛtvā lohapātre pācayenmṛduvahninā |
drave jīrṇe samādāya sarvayogeṣu yojayet || 2,7.92 ||
anupānaṃ vinā hyabhraṃ jarāmṛtyurujāpaham |
yojayedanupānairvā tattadrogaharaṃ bhavet || 2,7.93 ||
kaṭhinasyābhrasatvasya sindūraṃ palitaṃ valim |
indriyāṇāṃ paṭutvaṃ ca patrābhrakaguṇā api || 2,7.94 ||
mṛdusatvasya sindūraṃ sarvāmayanibarhaṇam |
valīpalitadāridryamṛtyughnaṃ surasāyanam || 2,7.95 ||
tridoṣaśamanaṃ saumyaṃ dīpanaṃ pācanaṃ śubham |
nihanti jvarajālaṃ ca grahaṇīmatisārakam || 2,7.96 ||
aśmarīṃ mūtrakṛcchraṃ ca kṣayaṃ pāṇḍuhalīmakam |
durnāmamehakuṣṭhāni vātajān pittajānapi || 2,7.97 ||
kaphajāndustarānrogān vidradhyādivraṇānapi |
nānāvidhāni śūlāni puṃstrīvandhyatvabhañjanam || 2,7.98 ||
yadyadrogaharairyogaistattadroganibarhaṇam |
pārade ye guṇāḥ santi bījasatve'pi te guṇāḥ || 2,7.99 ||
<maṇḍūra, śuddhi>
akṣāṅgārairdhametkiṭṭaṃ lohajaṃ tadgavāṃ jalaiḥ |
secayedakṣapātrāntaḥ ṣaḍvāraṃ ca punaḥ punaḥ || 2,7.100 ||
maṇḍūro'yaṃ samākhyātaḥ śuddhaṃ ślakṣṇaṃ niyojayet |
gomūtre triphalā kvāthyā tatkvāthe secayecchanaiḥ || 2,7.101 ||
<maṇḍūrabhasma>
lohakiṭṭaṃ sutaptaṃ ca yāvacchīryati tatsvayam |
tacchīrṇaṃ grāhayetpeṣyaṃ maṇḍūraṃ yaṃ prayojayet || 2,7.102 ||
ye guṇā mārite muṇḍe te guṇā muṇḍakiṭṭake |
tasmātsarvatra maṇḍūraṃ rogaśāntyai prayojayet || 2,7.103 ||
kiṭṭāddaśaguṇaṃ muṇḍaṃ muṇḍāttīkṣṇaṃ śatonmitam |
tīkṣṇāllakṣaguṇaṃ kāntaṃ bhakṣaṇātkurute guṇam || 2,7.104 ||
maṇḍūraṃ pāṇḍuśoṣārśograhaṇīkāmalāpaham |
sarvaśūlapramehaghnaṃ dīpanaṃ paramaṃ hitam || 2,7.105 ||
śītaṃ tiktaṃ kaṣāyāmlaṃ hṛdrogaplīhanāśanam |
<rasādimāraṇāyadravyamānam>
rasendraṃ śodhayeddevi palānāṃ dvisahasrakam || 2,7.106 ||
sahasraṃ vā śataṃ vāpi pañcāśadvā tadardhakam |
na kuryādrasakarmāṇi hīnaṃ vai pañcaviṃśateḥ || 2,7.107 ||
lohasya pacanaṃ triṃśatpalādūrdhvaṃ na kārayet |
arvākpañcapalāddevi ghanasatvaṃ tathā pacet || 2,7.108 ||
svarṇaṃ pañcapalādūrdhvaṃ palādarvāṅna śodhayet |
tathā rūpyaṃ ca tāmraṃ ca śodhayenmārayetpriye || 2,7.109 ||
lohavadghanasatvaṃ ca ghanapatraṃ tathaiva ca |
hīnaṃ pañcapalādūrdhvaṃ na kuryātpalaviṃśateḥ || 2,7.110 ||
drāvaṇaṃ śodhanaṃ devi māraṇaṃ nāgavaṅgayoḥ |
vajraṃ niṣkādadhaścordhvaṃ na kuryācchodhanaṃ mṛtim || 2,7.111 ||
padmarāgādiratnāni tathā kuryātsureśvari || 2,7.112 ||


āk, 2, 8
śrībhairavaḥ |
mahāmburāśau sariti parvate kānane'pi vā |
ratnānāmākaraṃ devi sthānamādheyagauravāt || 2,8.1 ||
teṣu rakṣoviṣavyālavyādhidoṣaharāṇi ca |
prādurbhavanti ratnāni tathaiva viguṇāni ca || 2,8.2 ||
praduṣṭenopajātāni jantunopahatāni ca |
doṣaistānyupacīyante hīyante guṇasaṃpadā || 2,8.3 ||
<ruby:: synonyms>
māṇikyaṃ śoṇaratnaṃ ca ratnarāṭraviratnakam |
śṛṅgāri raṅgamāṇikyaṃ taralo ratnanāyakaḥ || 2,8.4 ||
rāgadṛkpadmarāgaśca ratnaṃ śoṇopalastathā |
saugandhikaṃ lohitakaṃ kuruvindaṃ śarendukam || 2,8.5 ||
<nīlagandhika>
māṇikyaṃ padmarāgākhyaṃ dvitīyaṃ nīlagandhi ca |
<ruby:: parīkṣā:: goodṝuality>
kuśeśayadalacchāyaṃ svacchaṃ snigdhaṃ guru sphuṭam || 2,8.6 ||
vṛttāyataṃ samaṃ gātraṃ māṇikyaṃ śreṣṭhamucyate |
parīkṣāpariśuddhānāṃ ratnānāṃ pṛthivībhṛtā || 2,8.7 ||
dhāraṇaṃ sarvadā kāryaṃ śreyaḥśrīkīrtikāṅkṣiṇā |
tatastu śāstratasteṣāṃ parīkṣādikamucyate || 2,8.8 ||
<māṇikyabhedāḥ>
yatsiṃhalaṃ kālapuramandhraṃ tumburusaṃsthitam |
kṣetraṃ caturvidhaṃ tasya māṇikyasya samudbhave || 2,8.9 ||
siṃhale tu bhavedraktaṃ padmarāgamanuttamam |
pītaṃ kālapurodbhūtaṃ kuruvindamiti smṛtam || 2,8.10 ||
aśokapallavacchāyamandhraṃ saugandhikaṃ priye |
tumburau chāyamānīlaṃ nīlagandhi ca kīrtitam || 2,8.11 ||
madhyame madhyamaṃ jñeyaṃ māṇikyaṃ kṣetrabhedataḥ |
māṇikyasya guṇāḥ proktāścatvāro munipuṅgavaiḥ || 2,8.12 ||
snigdhacchāyā gurutvaṃ ca nairmalyamatiraktatā |
<māṇikyaguṇāḥ>
māṇikyaṃ dīpanaṃ vṛṣyaṃ kaphavātakṣayārtihṛt || 2,8.13 ||
bhūtavetālapāpaghnaṃ karmajavyādhināśanam |
māṇikyaṃ ca mahāpuṇyaṃ mahābhāgyakaraṃ param || 2,8.14 ||
sarvabhūtagrahonmādaviṣaghnaṃ doṣajitparam |
<muktā>
muktā saumyā mauktikaṃ śauktikeyaṃ tāraṃ tārā śauktikaṃ tārakā ca |
ambhaḥsāraṃ śītalaṃ nīrajaṃ ca nakṣatraṃ syādinduratnaṃ valakṣam || 2,8.15 ||
muktāphalaṃ binduphalaṃ ca muktā śaukteyakaṃ śuktimaṇiḥ śaśipriyam |
svacchaṃ himaṃ haimavataṃ ca śubhraṃ sudhāṃśuratnaṃ ca bhasaṃkhyakāhvam || 2,8.16 ||
<pearl:: origin>
mātaṅgoragamīnapotriśirasastvaksāraśaṅkhāmbubhṛcchuktīnāmudarācca mauktikamaṇiḥ spaṣṭaṃ bhavedaṣṭadhā |
chāyāḥ pāṭalanīlapītadhavalāstatrāpi sāmānyataḥ saptānāṃ bahuśo na labdhiritaracchaukteyakaṃ tūlvaṇam || 2,8.17 ||
<pearl:: parīkṣā:: goodṝuality>
nakṣatrābhaṃ śuddhamatyantamuktaṃ snigdhaṃ sthūlaṃ nirmalaṃ nirvraṇaṃ ca |
yastaṃ dhatte gauravaṃ yattulāyāṃ tannirmaulyaṃ mauktikaṃ saukhyadāyi || 2,8.18 ||
<pearl:: medic. properties>
mauktikaṃ ca madhuraṃ suśītalaṃ dṛṣṭirogaśamanaṃ viṣāpaham |
rājayakṣaparikopanāśanaṃ kṣīṇavīryabalapuṣṭivardhakam || 2,8.19 ||
mauktikaṃ śiśiraṃ snigdhaṃ viśadaṃ kāntivardhanam || 2,8.20 ||
dāhatṛḍbhramamūrcchāsṛkpittajvaraviṣāpaham |
cakṣuṣyaṃ pavanāsrapittaviṣajitsarvendriyāhlādanaṃ tṛḍdāhajvaraśokamohaśamanaṃ śītaṃ śramaghnaṃ hitam |
dhanyaṃ maṅgalamāyuṣaḥ sthitikaraṃ saubhāgyakāntipradaṃ muktā hāravibhūṣaṇaṃ tadakhilaṃ mūlaṃ śaśī prīyate || 2,8.21 ||
<coral:: synonyms>
pravālo'ṅgārakamaṇirvidrumo'mbhodhipallavaḥ || 2,8.22 ||
bhaumaratnaṃ ca raktāṅgo raktāṅkuralatāmaṇiḥ |
<coral:: place of origin>
setau sāgaramadhye yā jāyate vallarī śubhā || 2,8.23 ||
vidrumākhyā suraktā sā durlabhā dīptarūpiṇī |
<coral:: parīkṣā:: goodṝuality>
pāṣāṇatvaṃ bhajatyeṣā pākataḥ kaṭhinā satī || 2,8.24 ||
pravālanāma tadraktaṃ varṇasaubhāgyakāntidam |
siddhaṃ svacchaṃ snigdharūpaṃ vṛttaṃ kāntaṃ samaṃ guru || 2,8.25 ||
raṅgagātraṃ dṛḍhaṃ piṇḍaṃ pravālaṃ śreṣṭhamucyate |
pakvabiṃbaphalābhāsaṃ japākusumasaṃnibham || 2,8.26 ||
śukatuṇḍasamacchāyaṃ pravālamatiśobhanam |
<coral:: parīkṣā:: badṝuality>
śāradābhaṃ dalaṃ sūkṣmaṃ vakraṃ rūkṣaṃ sakoṭaram || 2,8.27 ||
viddhaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ laghutamaṃ pravālaṃ doṣakṛdbhavet |
gauraraṅgaṃ jalākrāntaṃ pravālamaśubhaṃ tyajet || 2,8.28 ||
<coral:: parīkṣā:: goodṝuality>
pravālaṃ komalaṃ snigdhaṃ surāgaṃ vidrumaṃ hitam |
dhanadhānyakaraṃ medhyaṃ viṣāhibhayanāśanam || 2,8.29 ||
<coral:: medic. properties>
pravālo madhurāmlaśca kaphapittādidoṣanut |
vīryakāntikaraḥ strīṇāṃ dhṛto maṅgaladāyakaḥ || 2,8.30 ||
<marakata>
marakataṃ rauhiṇeyaṃ gārutmantaṃ harinmaṇiḥ |
sauparṇaṃ garuḍodgīrṇaṃ budharatnāśmagarbhaje || 2,8.31 ||
garalārirvāyavīryaṃ gāruḍaṃ rudrasaṃmitam |
turuṣkaviṣayāṃbhodhau samīrārohaṇācale || 2,8.32 ||
tacchāyāśyāmalo deśaḥ so'bhūnmarakatākaraḥ |
tadviśeṣānmarakataṃ jātaṃ doṣaguṇānvitam || 2,8.33 ||
doṣāḥ saptavidhāstasya guṇāḥ pañcavidhāḥ smṛtāḥ |
bhavedaṣṭavidhā chāyā maṇermarakatasya ca || 2,8.34 ||
asnigdhaṃ rūkṣamityuktaṃ visphoṭaṃ piṭakaṃ tathā |
jaṭharaṃ kāntihīnaṃ ca vicchāyaṃ malinaṃ tathā || 2,8.35 ||
saśarkaraṃ sapāṣāṇaṃ karkaśaṃ sūtrasaṃyutam |
kalmāṣavarṇaṃ śabalaṃ samastabhayadāyakam || 2,8.36 ||
nirmalaṃ kathitaṃ svacchaṃ guru syādgurudāyutam |
snigdhaṃ rūkṣaṃ vinirmuktamarajaskamareṇukam || 2,8.37 ||
surāgaṃ rāgabahulamiti pañca guṇāḥ smṛtāḥ |
etairyuktaṃ marakataṃ sarvapāpaharaṃ param || 2,8.38 ||
marakataṃ viṣaghnaṃ ca śītalaṃ madhuraṃ rase |
āmapittaharaṃ rucyaṃ puṣṭidaṃ bhūtanāśanam || 2,8.39 ||
snigdhaṃ marakataṃ svacchaṃ sarvadoṣaharaṃ śubham |
kāntisaubhāgyadaṃ medhyaṃ bhūtagrahaviṣāpaham || 2,8.40 ||
<puṣparāga:: synonyms>
pītastu puṣparāgaḥ pītasphuṭikaṃ ca pītaratnaṃ ca |
pītāśmā gururatnaṃ pītamaṇiḥ puṣyarāgaśca || 2,8.41 ||
<puṣparāga:: parīkṣā:: true>
īṣatpītaṃ pavicchāyaṃ svacchaṃ kāntyā manoharam |
puṣyarāga iti khyātaṃ ratnaṃ ratnaparīkṣakaiḥ || 2,8.42 ||
<puṣparāga:: parīkṣā:: goodṝuality>
hemacchāyaṃ śirovṛttaṃ jyotiraṅgāranirmalam |
pītagātraṃ guru snigdhaṃ puṣyarāgaṃ praśasyate || 2,8.43 ||
<puṣparāga:: parīkṣā:: badṝuality>
rūkṣaṃ kṣatalaghu śvetaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ gauraṃ saśarkaram |
malinaṃ bindumadratnaṃ puṣyarāgaṃ na śasyate || 2,8.44 ||
<puṣparāga:: medic. properties>
puṣyarāgo'mlaśītaśca vātajiddīpanaḥ param |
āyuḥ śriyaṃ ca prajñāṃ ca dhāraṇātkurute nṛṇām || 2,8.45 ||
puṣyarāgaṃ śubhaṃ medhyaṃ vastusaubhāgyakīrtidam |
vākpaṭutvakaraṃ hṛdyaṃ viṣaghnaṃ doṣajitparam || 2,8.46 ||
<vajra>
vajramindrāyudhaṃ vīraṃ bhiduraṃ kuliśaṃ paviḥ |
abhedyamasiraṃ ratnaṃ dṛḍhaṃ bhārgavakaṃ smṛtam || 2,8.47 ||
<vajra:: parīkṣā:: goodṝuality>
svacchaṃ vidyutprabhaṃ snigdhaṃ sundaraṃ laghu lekhanam |
ṣaḍāraṃ tīkṣṇadhāraṃ ca susaumyāraṃ śriyaṃ diśet || 2,8.48 ||
<vajrākarā vajrabhedāśca>
pauṇḍramataṅgahimācalasaurāṣṭrasupārakosalakaliṅgāḥ |
peṇṇānadītaṭaścetyaṣṭau vajrākarā vinirdiṣṭāḥ || 2,8.49 ||
śyāmaṃ prapauṇḍraprabhavaṃ kiṃcitpītaṃ mataṅgagirijātam |
himavatsurāṣṭrasaṃbhavamātāmraṃ kṛṣṇakānti saupāram || 2,8.50 ||
phullaśirīṣacchāyaṃ kosalajaṃ kanakakānti kāliṅgam |
peṇṇānadītaṭodbhavamāhurvajraṃ sudhāṃśunibham || 2,8.51 ||
kaliṅgakosalau deśau mataṅgādrihimālayau |
saurāṣṭrapauṇḍrakauverākarasūpārakau tathā || 2,8.52 ||
kṛtādiṣu yugeṣvetau vajrāṇāmākarau smṛtau |
ākareṣveva vajrāṇāṃ khaniḥ srotaḥ prakīrṇakam || 2,8.53 ||
samutpattisthalaṃ tredhā nirdiṣṭaṃ suranāyike |
koṭyaḥ pārśvāni dhārāśca ṣaḍaṣṭau dvādaśaiva ca || 2,8.54 ||
ityacchatīkṣṇadhārāgrā vajrasyāhuratho guṇān |
<vajra:: 5 doṣas>
malo binduśca rekhā ca trāsaḥ kākapadaṃ tathā || 2,8.55 ||
ete doṣāḥ samākhyātāḥ pañca vajreṣu saṃsthitāḥ |
kuryādaśodhitaṃ vajraṃ kuṣṭhadāhāṅgagauravam || 2,8.56 ||
hṛtpārśvapīḍāṃ pāṇḍuṃ ca tatastacchuddhirucyate |
<vajraśuddhi>
gṛhītvātha śubhraṃ vajraṃ vyāghrīkandodare kṣipet || 2,8.57 ||
mahiṣīśakṛdālipya karīṣāgnau vipācayet |
ahorātrātsamuddhṛtya hayamūtreṇa secayet || 2,8.58 ||
evaṃ punaḥ punaḥ pakvaṃ saptarātrādviśudhyati |
meghanādāśamīśyāmāśṛṅgīmadanakodbhavaiḥ || 2,8.59 ||
kulutthavetasāgastyasindhuvārākhukarṇikāḥ |
eteṣāṃ hayamūtreṇa kaṣāyaṃ sādhitaṃ punaḥ || 2,8.60 ||
jambīre kuliśaṃ kṣiptvā hayamūtraistryahaṃ pacet |
ḍolāyantravidhānena tataḥ śuddhimavāpnuyāt || 2,8.61 ||
kulutthakodravakvāthairḍolāyantre vipācayet |
vyāghrīkandagataṃ vajraṃ saptāhācchuddhimāpnuyāt || 2,8.62 ||
kulatthakodravakvāthaṃ hayamūtraṃ snuhīpayaḥ |
kṣiptvā bhāṇḍe pacettasmin vyāghrīkandagataṃ pavim || 2,8.63 ||
ḍolāyantre divārātraṃ samuddhṛtya punaḥ kṣipet |
vajrakande mṛdā kandaṃ liptvā gajapuṭe pacet || 2,8.64 ||
tatpakvaṃ pācanadrāvaiḥ secitaṃ śuddhimāpnuyāt |
vyāghrīkandagataṃ vajraṃ mṛdā liptaṃ puṭe pacet || 2,8.65 ||
ahorātrātsamuddhṛtya hayamūtreṇa secayet |
vajrīkṣīreṇa vā siñcyādevaṃ śuddhaṃ tu mārayet || 2,8.66 ||
<viprajātīyavajramāraṇam>
vakṣyate viprajātyādeḥ śuddhavajrasya māraṇam |
aśvatthabadarībhaṇḍīmākṣikaṃ karkaṭāsthi ca || 2,8.67 ||
samaṃ snukpayasā piṣṭvā mūṣāmadhye pralepayet |
tanmadhye prakṣipedvajramauṣadhaistaistataḥ param || 2,8.68 ||
vajramācchādya yatnena tato mūṣāṃ nirodhayet |
kuñjarākhyena puṭayetpuṭena mahatā punaḥ || 2,8.69 ||
viprajātīyakaṃ vajraṃ puṭenaikena sidhyati |
karavīraṃ meṣaśṛṅgī daradaṃ ca hyudumbaram || 2,8.70 ||
arkadugdhaiḥ samaṃ piṣṭvā tatkṛte golake kṣipet |
vajraṃ kṣatriyajātīyaṃ pūrvavanmārayeddhruvam || 2,8.71 ||
<vaiśyajātīyavajramāraṇam>
sūraṇaṃ laśunaṃ śaṃkhaṃ śilāyāṃ peṣayetsamam |
balāmatibalāṃ gandhaṃ kacchapāsthi ca peṣayet || 2,8.72 ||
uttarāvāruṇīdugdhaistatkṛte golake kṣipet |
vajraṃ tu vaiśyajātīyaṃ pūrvavattanmṛtaṃ bhavet || 2,8.73 ||
<śūdrajātīyavajramāraṇam>
sūraṇaṃ laśunaṃ śaṅkhaṃ śilāṃ saṃpeṣayetsamam |
vaṭakṣīreṇa tatkḷpte golake pūrvavatpacet || 2,8.74 ||
vajraṃ tacchūdrajātīyaṃ tena samyagbhavenmṛtam |
strīpuṃvajraṃ tu pūrvoktairmriyate tattadauṣadhaiḥ || 2,8.75 ||
caturjātyauṣadhaireva mṛtirvajre napuṃsake |
<vajramāraṇa (1)>
sāmānyaḥ sarvavajrāṇāṃ vakṣyate māraṇakramaḥ || 2,8.76 ||
śuddhaṃ vajraṃ matkuṇānāṃ raktairliptvā dhametpunaḥ |
agnivarṇaṃ kṣipenmūtre gardabhasya punaḥ punaḥ || 2,8.77 ||
lepitaṃ dhmāpitaṃ tadvadevaṃ kuryāttrisaptadhā |
tālakaṃ matkuṇaiḥ piṣṭvā tasmingole kṣipettu tam || 2,8.78 ||
ruddhvā mūṣāṃ dhamedgāḍhaṃ hayamūtre vinikṣipet |
samuddhṛtya punastadvatsaptavārairmṛto bhavet || 2,8.79 ||
<vajramāraṇa (2)>
meṣaśṛṅgaṃ bhujaṅgāsthi kūrmapṛṣṭhaṃ śilājatu |
gandhakaṃ kāntapāṣāṇaṃ munipuṣpaṃ satālakam || 2,8.80 ||
trikṣāraṃ pañcalavaṇaṃ meṣaśṛṅgīndravāruṇī |
vajravallī mūṣakarṇī badarī kuḍmalāni ca || 2,8.81 ||
mūṣakasya malaṃ stanyaṃ snuhyarkakṣīramatkuṇam |
pañcāṅgaṃ śarapuṃkhasya hyasthinī kharameṣayoḥ || 2,8.82 ||
peṭāribījaṃ strīpuṣpaṃ pārāvatamalaṃ śilā |
puṣpāṇi caiva vākucyāḥ pañcāṅgaṃ timirasya ca || 2,8.83 ||
dhātrīvṛkṣasya pañcāṅgaṃ gorambhā vājimūtrakam |
haṃsapādī vajrakandaṃ bṛhatīphalasūraṇam || 2,8.84 ||
gojihvā karkaṭaṃ māṃsaṃ mūtravargaṃ ca miśrayet |
etatsamastaṃ vyastaṃ vā yathālābhaṃ supiṇḍitam || 2,8.85 ||
tatpiṇḍe nikṣipedvajramandhamūṣāgataṃ puṭet |
kulutthakodravaṃ piṣṭvā hayamūtre vilolayet || 2,8.86 ||
tanmadhye secayettaptāṃ mūṣāpuṭavinirgatām |
evaṃ punaḥ punaḥ kuryādekaviṃśativārakam || 2,8.87 ||
ādāya tatpunarvajraṃ tāle matkuṇapeṣite |
golake nikṣipedruddhvā mūṣāṃ tīvrānale dhamet || 2,8.88 ||
ityevaṃ saptadhā dhmātaṃ hayamūtre niṣecayet |
anena kramayogena mṛto bhavati niścitam || 2,8.89 ||
tanmṛtaṃ cūrṇayetkhalve siddhayoga udāhṛtaḥ |
<vajramāraṇa (3)>
bhrāmakaṃ ca mṛtaṃ tāpyaṃ peṭālībījaṭaṅkaṇam || 2,8.90 ||
kṣīraṃ cottaravāruṇyā gandhakaṃ tālakaṃ śilā |
ciñcābījaṃ meṣaśṛṅgī strīpuṣpaṃ cāmlavetasaḥ || 2,8.91 ||
pañcāṅgaṃ śarapuṃkhāyāḥ śaśadantaṃ śilājatu |
etatsamastaṃ vyastaṃ vā yathālābhaṃ sucūrṇayet || 2,8.92 ||
snuhyarkottaravāruṇyāḥ kṣīraiḥ stanyairvimardayet |
tadgolake kṣipedvajraṃ ruddhvā caitāndhameddṛḍham || 2,8.93 ||
guḍūcī saindhavaṃ hiṅgu samustottaravāruṇī |
kvāthaiḥ kaulutthakaiḥ piṣṭvā tasminkvāthe vinikṣipet || 2,8.94 ||
taṃ vajraṃ pūrvavadgole kṛtvā ruddhvā dhamettathā |
secanāntaṃ tataḥ kuryādekaviṃśativārakam || 2,8.95 ||
tālakaṃ matkuṇāyoge saptavāraṃ punardhamet |
secayedaśvamūtreṇa tadvajraṃ mriyate dhruvam || 2,8.96 ||
<vajramāraṇa (4)>
trivarṣīyotthakārpāsamūlamādāya peṣayet |
trivarṣanāgavallyā vā nijadrāvaiḥ prapeṣayet || 2,8.97 ||
tadgolake kṣipedvajraṃ ruddhvā gajapuṭe pacet |
evaṃ saptapuṭaiḥ pakvamekaikena kṛtaṃ bhavet || 2,8.98 ||
<vajramāraṇa (5)>
tālakāsīsasaurāṣṭrīmapāmārgasya bhasma ca |
piṣṭvā kaulutthakaiḥ kvāthaistasminvajraṃ sutāpitam || 2,8.99 ||
kṣipettrisaptavārāṇi mriyate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
vaikrāntabhasmanā sārdhaṃ peṣayedamlavetasam || 2,8.100 ||
tadgole nikṣipedvajramandhamūṣāgataṃ dhamet |
secayedaśvamūtreṇa pūrvagole punaḥ kṣipet || 2,8.101 ||
ruddhvā dhmātaḥ punaḥ secyamevaṃ kuryāttrisaptadhā |
mriyate nātra sandehaḥ sarvayogeṣu yojayet || 2,8.102 ||
<vajramāraṇa (7)>
uttarāvāruṇīkṣīraiḥ kāntapāṣāṇajaṃ mukham |
kṣaṇaṃ piṣṭvā tu tadgolaṃ kṣiptvā tasminpaceddinam || 2,8.103 ||
nṛtaile gandhatailena mriyate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
<vajramāraṇa (8)>
bhūnāgaṃ gandhakaṃ cātha nārīstanyena peṣayet || 2,8.104 ||
tadgolasthaṃ pacedvajraṃ pūrvataile mṛtaṃ bhavet |
<vajramāraṇa (9)>
snuhīkṣīreṇa vimalāṃ piṣṭvā tadgolake kṣipet || 2,8.105 ||
vajraṃ nirudhya mūṣāṃ tu śuṣkāṃ tīvrāgninā dhamet |
kṣiptāmaśvasya mūtre tu kṣiptvā vajraṃ samāharet || 2,8.106 ||
ityevaṃ saptadhā kuryāttatastālakamatkuṇaiḥ |
piṣṭvā golaṃ kṣipettasminvajramūṣāṃ nirudhya ca || 2,8.107 ||
dhmāpitaṃ pūrvavatsecyaṃ saptavārairmṛtaṃ bhavet |
<vajramāraṇa (10)>
vajraṃ matkuṇaraktena liptvā liptvātape kṣipet || 2,8.108 ||
śuṣkaṃ lepyaṃ punaḥ śoṣyaṃ yāvatsaptadināvadhi |
viṣṇukrāntāpeṭakāryordravaiḥ siñcetpunaḥ punaḥ || 2,8.109 ||
taptaṃ taptaṃ ca tadvajraṃ śatavārairmṛtaṃ bhavet |
<vajramāraṇa (11)>
gandhakaṃ cūrṇitaṃ bhāvyaṃ strīpuṣpeṇa tu saptadhā || 2,8.110 ||
punaḥ strīrajasāloḍyaṃ tasmin vajraṃ sutāpitam |
secayettāpayedekaviṃśadvārānmṛtaṃ bhavet || 2,8.111 ||
<vajramāraṇa (12)>
vajraṃ nadīmahāśuktau kṣiptvā bhāvyaṃ muhurmuhuḥ |
snuhyarkottamakanyānāṃ draveṇaikena cātape || 2,8.112 ||
kṛṣṇakakaṭamāṃsena peṣitaṃ veṣṭayetpunaḥ |
bhūnāgasya mṛdā samyagdhṛtaṃ bhasmatvamāpnuyāt || 2,8.113 ||
<vajramāraṇa (13)>
raktamūlasya mūlaiśca meghanādasya kuḍmalaiḥ |
peṣitairveṣṭitaṃ dhmātaṃ vajraṃ bhasma bhavatyalam || 2,8.114 ||
<vajramāraṇa (14)>
mātuluṅgagataṃ vajraṃ ruddhvā limpenmṛdā bahiḥ |
puṭe pacetsamuddhṛtya tadvacchatapuṭe pacet || 2,8.115 ||
<vajramṛdūkaraṇa (1)>
nāgaparṇīdravairliptaṃ tatpatreṇaiva veṣṭitam |
jānumadhye sthitaṃ yāmaṃ tadvajraṃ mṛdutāṃ vrajet || 2,8.116 ||
<vajramṛdūkaraṇa (2)>
mātṛvāhakabījasya madhye vajraṃ vinikṣipet |
jambīrodaragaṃ vātha dolāyantre dinaṃ pacet || 2,8.117 ||
kulutthakodravakvāthe traiphale vā kaṣāyake |
ahorātrātsamuddhṛtya jambīrāntaḥ punaḥ kṣipet || 2,8.118 ||
mātṛvāhakabīje vā pacetprakṣipya pūrvavat |
punaḥ kṣepyaṃ punaḥ pācyaṃ tridinānte samuddharet || 2,8.119 ||
badarīvaṭanimbānāmaṅkurāṇi samāharet |
piṣṭvā tadgolake vajraṃ pūrvapakvaṃ vinikṣipet || 2,8.120 ||
aśvatthapatrake veṣṭya tadgolaṃ jānumadhyagam |
dinaṃ vā dhārayetkakṣe mṛdurbhavati niścitam || 2,8.121 ||
<vajramṛdūkaraṇa (3)>
pāradaṃ tīkṣṇacūrṇaṃ ca dinamamlena mardayet |
tadgole nikṣipedvajraṃ sūtreṇāveṣṭayedbahiḥ || 2,8.122 ||
nāgavallīdalaiścaiva veṣṭitaṃ dhānyarāśigam |
māsānte tatsamuddhṛtya limpennāgalatādravaiḥ || 2,8.123 ||
taddalairveṣṭitaṃ jānumadhyasthaṃ mṛdutāṃ vrajet |
<vajramṛdūkaraṇa (4)>
kāntapāṣāṇavaktraṃ vā cūrṇaṃ vā kāntalohajam || 2,8.124 ||
sasūtamamlayogena dinamekaṃ vimardayet |
tadgole nikṣipedvajraṃ nimbakārpāsakodravaiḥ || 2,8.125 ||
patraiḥ piṣṭaiḥ susaṃpeṣya nāgavallīdalaistataḥ |
veṣṭitaṃ jānumadhyasthaṃ dinānte mṛdutāṃ vrajet || 2,8.126 ||
<vajramṛdūkaraṇa (5)>
eraṇḍavṛkṣamadhye tu tatphale vā kṣipetpavim |
māsamātrātsamuddhṛtya jānumadhye ca pūrvavat || 2,8.127 ||
komalaṃ jāyate vajraṃ dinānte nātra saṃśayaḥ |
<vajramṛdūkaraṇa (6)>
vajraṃ tittirimāṃsena veṣṭitaṃ nikṣipenmukhe || 2,8.128 ||
atisthūlasya bhekasya mukhaṃ sūtreṇa veṣṭayet |
nikhaneddhastamātrāyāṃ kṣoṇyāṃ māsātsamuddharet || 2,8.129 ||
maṇḍūkaṃ sampuṭe ruddhvā samyaggajapuṭe pacet |
tadvajraṃ pūrvagolasthaṃ jānumadhyagataṃ dinam || 2,8.130 ||
bhavedvajraudanaṃ sākṣātpaścādāhārya yojayet |
sarvavajraudanānāṃ tu māraṇaṃ pūrvavadbhavet || 2,8.131 ||
<vajradruti>
kṣāratrayaṃ rāmaṭhaṃ ca caṇakāmlāmlavetasam |
jvālāmukhīṃ cekṣurakaṃ sthalakumbhīphalāni ca || 2,8.132 ||
snuhyarkapayasā ślakṣṇaṃ piṣṭvā tadgolake kṣipet |
vajraṃ viśodhitaṃ samyagvastre baddhvā haṭhātpacet || 2,8.133 ||
ḍolāyantreṇa dhānyāmle drutaṃ māsāṣṭakādbhavet |
vajravallyantarasthaṃ vā kṛtvā vajraṃ nirodhitam || 2,8.134 ||
amlabhāṇḍagataṃ secyaṃ saptāhāddravatāṃ vrajet |
bhasmaudanaṃ drutiśceti trividhaṃ vajramāraṇam || 2,8.135 ||
rasabandhakaraṃ proktaṃ nāgārjunapuraḥsaraiḥ |
<vajraguṇāḥ>
vajraṃ ca ṣaḍrasopetaṃ sarvarogāpahārakam || 2,8.136 ||
sarvāghaśamanaṃ saukhyaṃ dehadārḍhyaṃ rasāyanam |
vajraṃ ratnottamaṃ puṇyaṃ śrīsaubhāgyavivardhanam || 2,8.137 ||
āyuṣyaṃ dhanyamojasyaṃ yaśasyaṃ sarvapāpmajit |
āyurbalaṃ dehasaukhyaṃ rūpaṃ kāntiṃ karoti ca || 2,8.138 ||
sevitaṃ hanti rogāṃśca mṛtaṃ vajraṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
<nīla>
nīlastu sauriratnaṃ syānnīlāśmā nīlaratnakaḥ || 2,8.139 ||
nīlopalastṛṇagrāhī mahānīlaḥ sunīlakaḥ |
indranīlamaṇistatra caturdhā jātibhedataḥ || 2,8.140 ||
<sapphire:: subtypes:: caste>
sitacchāyo bhavedvipro raktaḥ kṣatriyajātikaḥ |
pītastu vaiśyajātiryo vṛṣalaḥ kṛṣṇadīdhitiḥ || 2,8.141 ||
<nīlasya guṇadoṣāḥ>
nīlīrasasamābhāsā vaiṣṇavīpuṣpasaṃnibhā |
lavalīpuṣpasaṅkāśā nīlendīvarasaprabhā || 2,8.142 ||
atasīpuṣpasaṅkāśā cāṣapakṣasamadyutiḥ |
kṛṣṇatrikarṇikāpuṣpasamānadyutidhāriṇī || 2,8.143 ||
mayūrakaṇṭhasacchāyā śambhoḥ kaṇṭhanibhā tathā |
viṣṇudehasamābhāsā bhṛṅgapatrasamaprabhā || 2,8.144 ||
etāśchāyāḥ śubhakarā indranīlamahāmaṇeḥ |
gurutvaṃ snigdhakāntitvaṃ surāgaṃ pārśvarañjanam || 2,8.145 ||
tṛṇagrāhitvamityete guṇāḥ pañca prakīrtitāḥ |
na vimlo nirmalo gātro masṛṇo gurudīptikaḥ || 2,8.146 ||
tṛṇagrāhī mṛdurnīlo durlabho lakṣaṇānvitaḥ |
indranīlaṃ śubhaṃ varṇyaṃ sarvapāpanibarhaṇam || 2,8.147 ||
